'ARZ-E-NAASHIR (PUBLISHER'S NOTE)

Islam ek mustaqil nizaam-e-hayaat hai. Is se kisi Musalman ko inkaar nahi ho sakta ke deen ki asal da'awat yeh hai ke sirf Allah Ta'aala aur Allah ke Rasool ﷺ ki ita'at ki jaye. Huzoor ﷺ ne apne qawl-o-fe'l se ahkaam-e-Ilahi ki tarjumaanee farmaayi hai – kaun si cheez halaal hai? Kaun si cheez haraam? Kya jaa'iz hai? Kya najaa'iz? Lehaza har Musalman ke liye zaroori hai ke woh Qur-aan Wa Sunnat ke ahkaam ki ita'at kare. Yahi wajah hai ke a'maal ki durusti ke liye masa-il ka 'ilm hona zaroori hai aur is 'ilm ka haasil karna farz hai.

Allah Tabaraka wa Ta'aala ka irshaad hai:

“Allah se us ke bandon mein wahi darte hain jo samajh ('ilm) rakhte hain” (Surah Faatir: 28).

Aur ek jagah irshaad hai:

“Kya woh log jo 'ilm rakhte hain aur jo nahi rakhte, barabar ho sakte hain?” (Surah Zumar: 9).

Aap ﷺ ka irshaad hai: “Allah Ta'aala jis ke saath bhalai ka iraadah farmaata hai, use deen ki gehri samajh ata farma deta hai” (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 2645 – Hasan Saheeh).

Is liye ba-haisiyat-e-Musalman yeh zaroori hai ke hum apne mu'atabar 'Ulama se kitaab o sunnat ki roshni mein apne masa-il ka hal talaash karein.

Aap ﷺ ka irshaad hai: “Allah Ta'aala 'ilm ko bandon se chheen kar nahi uthaye ga, balke 'Ulama ko wafaat de kar 'ilm ko uthayega. Yahan tak ke jab Allah kisi bhi 'aalim ko baaqi nahi chodega, to log jaahil logon ko apna rahnuma bana lenge, jo bina 'ilm ke fatwe denge, khud bhi gumrah honge aur doosron ko bhi gumrah karenge” (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 100).

Aaj kal social media ka daur hai, jismein WhatsApp, Facebook, YouTube, Twitter, Skype wagayrah shaamil hain. Is se deeni, akhlaaqi aur ta'aleemi nuqtah-e-nazar se mufeed (useful) cheezein bhi pahunchayi ja sakti hain, aur insaani wa akhlaaqi qadr-o-qeemat ko tabah karne wali cheezein bhi.

Social media ke positive aur mufeed pehlu bhi hain, aur is ka sahi isti'emaal kar ke Islami aur insani nuqtah-e-nazar se bahut se achhe kaam kiye ja sakte hain. Magar is ke kaee (many) manfi (negative) aur nuqsaan-deh pehlu bhi hain. Yeh jhooti khabrein phailane ka ek bada network ban gaya hai. Aksar baatein bagair tahqiqat ke share kar di jaati hain.

Islam ne humein jhoot to jhoot, har suni hui baat ko bagair tahqiqat ke aage badhane se bhi mana'a kiya hai. Chunanche Allah Ta'aala ka irshaad hai:

“Aey imaan walon! Agar koi faasiq tumhare paas koi khabar laye jis mein kisi ki shikaayat ho, to sirf us ki baat par bharosa na karo, balke tahqiqat karo ke woh sach hai ya nahi, taake kahin kisi qawm ko an-jaane mein nuqsaan na pohncha do, phir baad mein jab unki bara'at zaahir ho to tumhein apne kiye par sharmainda hona pade” (Surah Hujuraat: 6).

Isi tarah aksar ta'aleem-yafta log 'Ulama se door ho kar “Mufti Google” se apne masa-il ka hal dhoondhte hain, jahan bahut se galat masa-il hote hain, jin ki tahqiqat nahi hoti. Allah Ummat-e-Muslimah ki hifaazat farmaaye aur unhein sahi samajh ata kare, taake woh apne mu'atabar 'Ulama se masa-il ka 'ilm haasil karein.

Isi wajah se social media ke sahi isti'emaal ke zariye WhatsApp par Islamic Knowledge Group banakar, nawjawano mein tahqiqat ka jazba paida kiya gaya. Haalaat-e-haazira, roz marrah ke masa-il, aur jadeed fiqhi masa-il Sawalaat ki shakal mein pooche gaye, jin ke jawabat ko kaamil tahqiqat ke baad mashhoor muftiyan aur 'Ulama ne de kar 'awaam ko mustafeed kiya.

Chunanche Hindustan mein Hanafī maslak par 'amal karne wale log ziyadah hain aur bade bade madaaris fiqh-e-Hanafi ki ta'aleem dete hain, is liye Hanafi maslak mein bahut si kitaabein Urdu aur English mein dastiyab (available) hain. Magar Urdu mein Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee mein sabse behtareen kitaab Hazrat Shaikh-ul-Hadees Maulana Ibrahim Khatib ki “Tuhfatul-Bari” hai – jise mere naqis 'ilm ke mutabiq Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee ka Urdu mein encyclopedia kehna ghalat na hoga. Is kitaab mein tafseel se masa-il ka zikr hai, magar aaj kal English medium ke badhte rujhan ki wajah se naajawaan tabqe ko Urdu padhne mein dilchaspi nahi hoti, jis ki wajah se mustanad kitaab hone ke bawajood 'awaam padhne mein susti aur kaahili karti hai.

Isi wajah se WhatsApp group mein pooche gaye masa-il ko English script (transliteration) mein kitaabi shakal dene ka iraadah kiya gaya taake English medium se ta'aleem yafta nawjawanon ko aasaan zubaan mein haalaat-e-haazira aur jadeed fiqhi masa-il ka 'ilm, Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee ke a'itebaar se Sawal jawab ki shakal mein haasil ho.

Lehaza pehla edition Urdu zubaan mein shaay'e (published) kiya gaya tha albatta ab ise English zubaan jaanne walon ke sahulat ke liye English script (transliteration – roman urdu) mein shaye kiya jaa raha hai – taake har shakhs masa-il ka sahih 'ilm haasil kar sake, In Shaa Allah. Alhamdulillah, taqreeban 165 se zaa-id (excess) mu'atabar kitabon k hawalajaat - jin mein aksar 'Arabi ki mu'atabar aur mashhoor kitabein shamil hain, iske 'alaawah kuch mu'atabar Urdu kitabein aur chand mu'atabar websites bhi — in sab ke hawale kitaab ke aakhir mein “Maraajê wa Masaadir” ke zer-e-'unwaan darj kiye gaye hain.

Agarcheh is kitaab mein ziyadah tar aasan aur samajh me aane wale Urdu alfaaz isti'emaal kiye gaye hain, uske baa-wajood baaz alfaaz aise hain jo 'aam Qaari-een, ya wo log jo urdu maadari zubaan to rakhte hain lekin unki Ta'aleem English medium me hone ki wajah se kabhi urdu alfaaz ka matlab samajhna unke liye mushkil ho sakta hain. Is baat ko dhyaan me rakhte hue humne poori koshish ki hai ke mushkil urdu alfaaz ke sath qausain (bracket) mein unka aasan Urdu tarjama ya phir English ma'anaa likh diya hai, taake qaari-een ba-aasaani baat ko samajh sakein.

Is 'azeem khidmat ke silsile mein hum dil ki gehraaiyon se Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Maulana Shabbir Tambe, Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb, Mufti Ishaq Patel, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare, aur Mufti Saeed Ghare ke tehe-dil se shukr guzaar hain, jinhon ne apni masroofiyat ke bawajood waqt nikaal kar is kitaab ko paya-e-takmeel tak pahunchaya.

Aakhir mein tamam 'Ulama-e-kiraam (Mufti Muzaffar Sain, Maulana Muzaffar Sange, Mufti Husain Muqadam, Mufti Abdul Basit Muqadam, Maulana Habibur Rahman Qasmi, Mufti Mahmood Mahadik, Mufti Fauzan Murudkar, Maulana Ibrahim Dongarkar, Mufti Anas Patel wagayrah) aur doston aur ahbaab ka shukriya ada karte hain jinhon ne jis kisi bhi andaaz mein is kitaab ki isha-at mein madad ki hai.

Allah hum sab ki khidmaat ko qabool kare, is mein kami kotaahi aur galatiyon ko mu'aaf kare aur ummat ko is se bharpoor istifaada ki taufeeq ata farmaaye. Aameen.

Maktabah Dar-E-Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra - 6361759091 / 9028286370

MARAAJÊ WA MASAADIR 361

TAQREEZ - MAULANA YASEEN QASMI PATEL, TALOJA

ألْحَمْدُ لِلّٰهِ وَحْدَه وَالصَّلَاةُ وَالسَّلَامُ عَلىٰ مَن لَّا نَبِيَّ بَعْدَه

Alhamdulillahi wahdahu wass-salaatu wass-salaamu

'ala mal-laa nabiyya ba'dahu.

Fiqh-e-Islami Ummat-e-Muslimah ke liye ek aisa giraan qadr (precious) sarmaayah hai jiske zariye Musalmaan apni infiraadi aur ijtimaa'i zindagi ko shari-'at ke mutabiq dhalne ki koshish karte hain. Ahd-e-Sahaba-e-Kiraam se le kar aaj tak 'Ulama-e-Ummat ne fiqhi abwaab mein be-shumaar khidmaat anjaam di hain.

Zer-e-nazar kitab (Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il Wa 'Ilmi Baatein – Jild Awwal Roman English) bhi fiqh ke usi 'azeem zakhere mein ek nihayat aham izafah hai.

Dar-asal is kitaab ko Dar-e-Arqam Lower Tudill ne maujoodah halaat mein badi koshishon ke saath khaalis Urdu zubaan mein likkhi hui kitaab ko Urdu se Roman Urdu ki shakal mein dhalaa hai, jiske sabab un logon ke liye jo kisi wajah se Urdu padhna nahi jaante, bahut sahulat ho gai hai.

Is kitaab ke mu-allifeen ne masa-il ko nihayat baarik beeni aur mehnat-e-shaaqah (intense efforts) ke saath murattab kiya hai. 'Ibaarat mein salaasat (fluency & clarity) hai, aur kitaab ko sawal-wa-jawab ki shakal mein murattab karke 'aam se 'aam insaan ke liye bhi aasaan aur qabil-e-istifaadah (useful) banaya gaya hai.

Is kitab ki sabse badi khubi yeh hai ke mu-allifeen ne sirf qadeem (old) masa-il par hi iktifa (limited) nahi kiya, balke 'asr-e-haazir ke pesh aamadah masa-il ko bhi fiqhi usoolon ki roshni mein bayaan kiya hai. Is se yeh kitab na sirf tullab-e-'ilm ke liye mufeed (beneficial) hai balke 'aam Musalmaanon ke liye bhi rahnuma ki haisiyat rakhti hai.

Bande ne dono kitaabon mein se kuch safhaat dekhe hain, qabil-e-nafa paaye. Main mu-allifeen aur naashir ki is 'azeem koshish par dili masarrat aur qalbi khushi ka izhaar karta hoon, aur du'aa karta hoon ke Allah Ta'aala is kitab ko qabool farmaaye, aur Allah Ta'aala mu-allifeen wa naashir ko "Khayrunnas anfa'uhum linnas" (Sab se behtar insaan woh hai jo doosron ke liye sab se ziyadah nafa'a mand ho) ka misdaaq banaaye, aur ise ahl-e-'ilm aur 'aammatul-muslimeen ke liye nafa'a bakhsh banaaye, aur mu-allifeen wa naashir ko mazeed 'ilmi wa deeni khidmaat ki taufeeq 'ata farmaaye. Aameen Ya Rabbal 'Aalameen.

Wa sallallahu Ta'aala 'ala khayri khalqihi Muhammadinw wa aalihi wa ashaabihi ajma'een.

Bandah: Muhammad Yaseen Qasmi – Taloja (Sabiq Shaikhul-Hadees, Darul 'Uloom Islamia 'Arabiya, Taloja)

TAQREEZ – QAZI KHAWAJA MU'EENUDDIN AKRAMI NADWI MADANI, BHATKAL

بِسْمِ اللّٰهِ الرَّحْمٰنِ الرَّحِيْمْ

Bismillahir Rahmaanir Raheem

اَلْحَمْدُ لِلّٰهِ رَبِّ الْعَالَمِيْنْ، وَصَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلىٰ سَيِّدِنَا مُحَمَّدٍ خَاتَمِ النَّبِيِّنْ، وَعَلٰى آلِهِ وَصَحْبِهِ اَجْمَعِيْنْ،أمَّابَعْد

Alhamdulillahi Rabbil 'Aalameen, wa sallallahu 'ala Sayyidina Muhammad Khaatimun-Nabiyyeen, wa 'ala aalihi wa sahbihi ajma'een, amma ba'ad!

Fiqh-e-Islami, Shari'at-e-Mutahhara ka aisa 'amali mazhar hai jo zindagi ke har goshe mein insaan ki rahnumai karta hai.

Aimma-E-Mujtahideen ne Qur-aan wa Sunnat se jo usool wa qawa'id akhaz farmaaye hain, woh har zamane ke liye ummat ke hidayat ka minar rahe hain. Inhi mein Hazrat Imam Shafi'ee (RA) ka fiqhi maktab ek roshan aur mutawazan nizam-e-fiqh ki haisiyat rakhta hai. Is mein nusoos-e-shar'iyyah ki gehrai aur ijtihadi baseerat (deep Islamic knowledge and wise thinking) ko nihayat haseen imtizaj (combination) ke saath pesh kiya gaya hai.

Jis daur mein bhi fikri intishar (Intellectual confusion) aur fiqhi sathiyat (shallow understanding of Islamic jurisprudence) 'aam hui, us zamane ke 'ulama aur ashaab-e-fiqh ne apni 'umdah tasaneef (authored books) ke zariye in fitnon ka muqabalah kiya aur Shari'at-e-Mutahharah ki baala-dasti (dominance) aur is ke har zamane ke liye mufeed aur zaruri hone ko wazeh kiya. Neez is se dast bardari (give up) ko zehr-e-qatil bataya. Isi tarah har zamane ke fitne ka muqabalah fiqh ke usoolon (jo dar asl Shari'at ke usool the) ke mutabiq kiya.

Maujoodah daur social media ka daur hai. Is daur mein minutes mein nahi balki seconds mein kaam wajood mein aane lage aur aisi barq raftari nazar aane lagi ke ab logon ke paas apne masa-il ke hal ke liye kisi 'aalim ke paas jaane ki fursat kahan hoti. Unhon ne apni zarurat ka hal social media, bil khusus Google, se hasil karna shuru kar diya. Aise mein agar 'ulama-e-kiraam ya muftiyan-e-'ezaam 'awamun naas ki zarurat ke mutabiq in taraqqi yafta wasa-il ko isti'emaal karte hue unke masa-il aur unke sawal ke jawabat aur hal na pesh karte to yeh badi mehroomi ki baat hoti.

Isi 'ilmi tasalsul mein Mohtaram Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla aur unke rufaqa-e-'ilm ne jo 'ilmi khidmat “Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il Aur 'Ilmi Baatein” ke 'unwan se pesh ki hai, woh nihayat mufeed aur qabil-e-qadr hai. Is majmu'eh mein Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee ke nuqtah-e-nazar se bahut se qadeem aur jadeed masa-il ko nihayat husn-e-tarteeb, tahqiqat aur wazahat ke saath jama'a kiya gaya hai. Main ne kuch masa-il ka mutaala'ah kiya aur kitab ko mukhtalif jaghon se padha, Alhamdulillah main ne murattibeen ko apni puri koshish ke saath is majmu'eh ko tarteeb dene wala paya.

Mazeed bar-aa'n, is par nazare sani do nihayat qabil-e-ehtram wa laa'iq wa fazil muftiyan ne anjam di hai. Isi tarah Faqih-ul-'Asr Hazrat Maulana Khalid Saifullah Rahmani, mudda zilluhul-'aali, wagayrah ki taqreezaat ne is ki qeemat mein izafah kar diya hai. Ab Masha Allah is kitab ko Roman Urdu (transliteration) mein bhi murattab kiya gaya hai, is se iska faidah do guna ho jaayega.

Is tarah yeh majmu'aah 'ulama, talaba (students) aur 'aam ahl-e-'ilm balkeh 'awamun naas sab ke liye mufeed ban kar unke liye istifaadeh ka mauqa faraham karta hai. Murattibeen ne apne husn-e-zann se mujhe chand jumle tahreer karne ki ba-israar (persistently) darkhast ki, halaankeh main is ka apne ko hargiz ahl nahi paata, magar in buzurgon ki taqreezat ke saath meri yeh sutoor bhi is mein shamil ho jaayein to yeh mere liye kisi bade in'aam wa sharaf se kam nahi. Is liye isi baat ko zahan mein rakh kar yeh sutoor tahreer kar raha hoon.

Main jumla murattibeen kiraam, bil khusus Mufti Faiz Mulla ko is mubarak aur mufeed kawish par dil ki gehrai se mubarakbaad deta hoon. Allah Ta'aala se du'aa hai ke woh is khidmat ko sharaf-e-qabooliyat bakhshe aur ise sadaqah-e-jariyah banaye, aur mazeed 'ilmi wa deeni khidmaat ki taufeeq ata farmaye. Wama taufeeqi illa billah, 'alayhi tawakkaltu wa ilayhi uneeb.

Wa sallallahu 'alaa Sayyidina Muhammad wa aalihi wa sahbihi wa sallama tasleeman kaseera.

Khawaja Mu'eenuddin Akrami Nadwi Madani,

Ustaad-e-Hadith wa Fiqh, Jamia Islamia, Bhatkal Wa Qazi, Sheher Bhatkal

URDU TAQAREEZ KA KHULASA

Faqihul 'Asr Mufti Khalid Saifullah Rahmani

Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee 'ilmi e'etibaar se imtiyazi haisiyat ki haamil hai, jitne Muhaddiseen Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee mein paida huwe shayad hi kisi aur fiqh mein paida huwe hon; magar afsos ke Urdu zubaan mein Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee par bahut kam kaam hua. Allah jaza-e-khair ata farmae azeez mukarram Maulana Faiz Ahmed Mulla sallamahullahu Ta'aala ko ke unhon ne 'Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur 'Ilmi Baatein' ke naam se ek qabil-e-tahseen koshish ki hai, jisme tamam fiqhi abwaab ke muntakhab masa-il ko jama'a kiya gaya hai. Unhon ne ziyadah tar to khud kaavish ki hai aur baaz fatawa maujoodah daur ke buzurg Shafi'ee 'Ulama ke naql kiye hain, neez Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee ki mustanad kitabon khaas kar Imam Nawawi rahmatullah alaih ki tahreeron se bhi faidah uthaya hai. Qaari-een ki sahulat ke liye sawal-wa-jawab ka andaaz rakhkha gaya hai, zubaan salees (simple), 'aam faham (easily understandable) aur wazeh hai. Masa-il ka intekhab bahut behtareen hai jisme jadeed masa-il ko bhi shaamil karne ki qabil-e-sataish (praiseworthy effort) koshish ki gai hai.

Maulana Nisar Daroge Sahab

Zer-e-nazar kitaab Qur-aan Wa Sunnat se mustafad aur fiqh ke aham Maraajê, khususaan Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee ki mu'atabar (reliable) aur mutadawal (commonly used) kitabon se makhuz (taken from) wa manqool (quoted) masa-il par mushtamil 'umda kitaab hai. Baaz masa-il naql-e-tahreer ke aitbaar se sahi lekin tahqeeq talab hain. Azeezam Faiz Khatib sallamahu ne mujh se jab kitaab ke bare mein chand kalimaat ya taqreez likhne ko kaha to maine arz kiya ke 'alaqa ke bade bade 'Ulama wa muftiyan-e-kiraam mein se koi taqreez likhe to ziyadah mufeed hoga, magar woh musir rahe to tabarrukan deen aur 'ilm-e-deen ki nashr wa isha'at karne walon mein shaamil hone aur un 'Ulama ki juhud-e-mubarakah wa nafa'a bakhsh khidmaat ki qadardani ke liye yeh chand kalimaat likh raha hoon. Kitaab ki khaas baat yeh hai ke ba-qaul Faiz aksar sawalaat ka mustafti Faiz hai aur ziyda tar jawabaat Mufti Faiz aur Maulana Shabbir ka faiz hai. Goya kitaab mein 'ilm wa fiqh ke fuyooz jari hain jin se 'awaam wa khawas mustafeed honge.

Maulana Abdus Salaam Hajju Sahab

In hazraat ne mobile WhatsApp ke zariye kiye gaye sawalaat ke jawabaat ko amali jaama pehnakar kitabi shakal di aur Asatiza-e-jamia Husainiya 'Arabiya Shrivardhan ne is ki tauseeq ki aur Sarparast-e-jamia wa Madrasa 'Arabiya Taloja ki taa'eed hasil hui, jiska zikr Maulana Khalid Saifullah Rahmani sahab ki taqreez mein hai. Raqimus-sutoor se darkhwast ki gai ke kuch kalimaat tahreer karen, to bator-e-ta'meel-e-irshaad is kitaab ke musawwade (draft) par jagah jagah nazar daali. Alhamdulillah yeh kitaab beshtar masa-il par mushtamil hai, khususaan jadeed masa-il ko bhi shaamil kiya hai aur ab yeh kitaab mashaaheer 'Ulama ki tauseeq wa taqariz se aarasta hokar manassa-e-shuhud mein aane ki tayyari mein hai.

Mufti Rafeeque Poorkar Madni Sahab

Zer-e-nazar kitaab mein aqaa'id, taharat, ibadaat, tijarat wa qarz, nikah wa mu'asharat, qurbani, aqeeqa, khel kood, libaas wa poshak, viraasat, ahadees ki tahqeeq, aadaab-e-zindagi jaise deegar mauzu'aat ke muta-'alliq sawalaat ke jawab maujood hain. Khaas taur par beshumar jadeed masa-il ka hal is kitaab mein pesh kiya gaya hai. Is kitaab ki khususiyaat yeh hai ke iska musannif wa mu'allif koi ek fard nahi balki muta'addad afraad ki mehnat wa koshishon ka is kitaab ki tayyari mein dakhal hai. Chunanche aap is kitaab mein Maulana Shabbir Tambe Sahab Husaini ki faqahat, Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb Nadwi Sahab ki 'ilmi tahqeeq wa justaju, Mufti Ishaq Patel Sahab Husaini ki zakaawat, Mufti Saeed Ghare Sahab Husaini ki fatwa naveesi aur khaas taur par Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla Sahab Husaini ke baaran-e-faiz ka nazaara karenge. Saath hi Mufti Muzffar Sain sahab, Mufti Fauzan Murudkar Sahab, Mufti Mahmood Mahadik Sahab, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare Sahab, Maulana Muzffar Sange Sahab ki qadray mehnat ka bhi mushahada hoga. Lekin haqeeqat yeh hai ke is kaam mein sab se ziyadah mehnat Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla sahab ki hai. Yeh sab sa'adatmand hazraat Azhar-e-Kokan Jamia Husainiya 'Arabiya Shrivardhan ke tarbiyat yafta aur faiz yafta hain aur is waqt mukhtalif ilmi kaamon mein masroof hain. Aisa mehsoos ho raha hai ke in sab ne mil kar Urdu zubaan mein "Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah" tayyar kiya hai. Main ne is kitaab ka shuru' se aakhir tak mushahada kiya to andaaza hua ke andaaz aasaan hai, usloob dilkash hai, zubaan bhi salees hai, jawabaat dene mein ihtiyaat ke pehlu ko pesh-e-nazar rakhkha gaya hai. Nez bahut si jaghon par jawab ke baad 'Arabi ibaarat aur Maraajê ka tazkirah kiya gaya hai, baaz maqamaat par dalail bhi zikr kiye gaye hain, munaaqasha ka andaaz bhi apnaya gaya hai, tahqiqi ja'iza bhi jaga jaga naql kiya gaya hai, bahut si ahadees ki tahqeeq bhi pesh ki gai hai. Jadeed aur zaroori masa-il ke zikr se bhi is kitaab ki ahmiyat mein izafah ho gaya hai. Is kitaab ke taqreeban tamam masa-il ko hawalon aur Maraajê ke saath jama'a kiya gaya hai jis ki wajah se kitaab ki ahmiyat mein kafi izafah ho gaya hai. Albata bahut kam hi masa-il aise hain jo hawalon se khaali hain lekin mera gumaan hai ke in hazraat ne mu'atabar kutub se ruju karne ke baad hi in jawabaat ko zabt kiya hoga. Majmoo'ee taur par yeh kitaab ek fiqhi dastavez ban gai hai jis se 'awaam wa khawaas ko ba-sahulat istifaadah aasaan hoga. Main is 'azeem kaam par in hazraat ko dil ki gehraa'i se mubarakbaad pesh karte hue ummeed karta hoon ke yeh kitaab be intiha mufeed sabit hogi.

Is Kitaab Ke Bare Mein Akabireen Ke Ta'assuraat

Yeh kitaab bahut behtarin hai aur har ghar mein hona chahiye. Maulana Sadique Mukadam, Roha

Yeh kitaab bahut achchi kitaab hai, hum is ka Malyalam mein Tarjamah (translation) karenge, In Sha Allah. Maulana Abdush Shakur, Kerala

Is kitaab se hamein bahut 'ilmi faidah ho raha hai. Maulana 'Abdullah Ba'naeem, Hyderabad

Aaj kal ke naye zamane ke naye masa-il bade salees (simple) aur aasan andaaz me tarteeb diye hai. Maulana Dawood Murudkar, Chandwa

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur 'Ilmi Batein - Jild Duwam

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il ki doosri jild anqareeb (soon) Insha Allah manzar-e-'aam par aane ki kagaar par hai. Pecheeda, jadeed aur nit naye masa-il par nazar-e-saani jaari hai. Yeh doosri jild Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare aur Mufti Ishaq Patel ki koshishon ka samarah hai.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

PESHE LAFZ: MUFTI FAIZ AHMED MULLA

بِسْمِ اللّٰهِ الرَّحْمٰنِ الرَّحِیْمِ

وَقُل رَّبِّ زِدْنِى عِلْمًا

Allah tabarak wa Ta'aala ka paak irshaad hai: "Ae Nabi ﷺ aap yeh du'aa karte rahiye ke, Ae mere Parwardigaar! Mera 'ilm badha dijiye". Khitaab khaas taur par Nabiye Kareem ﷺ se hai, lekin mutaalaba puri Ummat se hai, ke woh apne Rab se 'ilm mein izaafa ki du'aa karte rahein aur us ke liye koshish bhi karte rahein.

Kainaat-e-insani ke sabse pehle fard Hazrat Adam 'Alaihis-salaam ki takhleeq ke baad unhein jo 'azeem ni'ematein ata hui, un mein se sabse badi ni'emat 'ilm hi thi. Isi 'ilm ki wajah se Hazrat Adam (AS) ko malaika ka masjood banaya gaya. Unke baad puri bani aadam ke liye bulandi aur fazilat ka asal me'eyaar bhi 'ilm hi qarar paya.

Hazrat Adam (AS) ko Allah Ta'aala ne 'ilm-ul-asma ke saath saath 'ilm-e-hidayat bhi ata farmaya. Yeh silsila Adam (AS) se le kar Nabi Akhiruz-Zaman ﷺ tak jari raha. Aap ﷺ ne yeh 'ilm Sahaba-e-Kiraam tak pohanchaya, aur phir Sahaba-e-Kiraam ke zariye yeh 'ilm hum tak aaya aur qayamat tak aane wale insano tak jaata rahega.

Log is 'ilm ki roshni mein Allah ki ma'rifat hasil karte rahenge, gunaahon se bachte rahenge aur jannat ke liye kaam karte rahenge.

Isi silsileh ki ek adna koshish yeh kitaab “Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur Ilmi Baatein” bhi hai.

Is kitaab ke pehle muharrik, Lower Tudil ke rehne wale Mohtaram Faiz bin Ismail Khatib hain. Inki lagan, 'ilm se muhabbat, aur naye masa-il ke hal ki talash in sawalon aur jawabon ko kitaabi shakal dene ka sabab bana.

Sabse pehle unhone WhatsApp par “Islamic Knowledge” ke naam se ek group banaya aur Konkan ke mashahir ulema aur muftiyan-e-kiraam ko shaamil kiya. Log masa-il poochte aur ulema jawabaat dete rahe. Jab achcha khaasa mawad (a substantial amount of content) ikattha ho gaya to unhon ne Kuwait mein apne farig auqaat mein in masa-il ko computer mein jama'a kiya.

Phir in masa-il ko tarteeb dene aur tasheeh karne ke liye group ke ulema se darkhwast ki. Magar mashghooliyat ki wajah se kisi ne zimmedari nahi uthai. Phir yeh koshish mujh adna bande se bhi ki gai. Pehle to maine bhi inkar kiya, magar unke israar par maine deen ki khidmat samajh kar is zimmedari ko qabool kiya.

Masa-il ki tasheeh ke baad jawabaat un ulema ke paas bheje gaye unhon ne ne apne jawabaat mein islaah karke musawwadah wapas bheja. Uske baad yeh musawwadah Hazratul Ustaz Maulana Nisar Ahmed Sahab Daroge (DB) ke nazar hua, jinhon ne islahat ki aur apne qeemti kalimaat likhe.

Phir dobara masai'l ka jaaiza liya gaya aur dobara yeh musawwada Hazrat Shaikh-ul-Hadees Maulana Ibrahim Sahab DB (Jamia Husainia 'Arabiya, Shrivardhan) ke paas bheja gaya. Aap ne busy hone ke bawajood is musawwade ko dekha, islahat ki aur research ke liye nishan lagaya. Phir teesri baar kutub-e-fiqh ki taraf muraja'at (Refer) kar ke un nishan zad masa-il ki tahqiq hui, aur Hazrat Shaikhul-Hadees Hazrat Maulana Ibrahim Sahab mudda zilluhul-'aali se phone call ke zariye jawabaat ki tasdeeq ki gai aur itminan ke baad un jawabat ko kitaab mein likkha gaya. Wazeh rahe ke in dono shuyookh ke hawalay jis musawwade (Draft) ko kiya gaya thaa woh is kitaab ke ibtidaa'ee awraaq (pages) the jin par in asateen-e-'uloom-e- nubuwwat ne apni qeemti nazrein daali thien, iske baad bhi is musawwade mein halaat ke aitebaar se masa-il ka izafah kiya gaya, khususan jadeed masa-il ka, jin par in hazaraat ki masrufiyaat ki wajah se nazar-e-inayat nahi ho paai.

Teesri martaba musawwada Fakhre Konkan Hazrat Mufti Rafeeq Sahab Madani (DB) ke paas bheja gaya. Aap ne safar wa hazar dono haalat mein musawwada (Draft) dekha aur humein apne qeemti kalimaat se nawaza. Chunanche Mufti Sahab ke mashware par kuch masa-il ke aakhir mein hawalajat ka izafah kiya gaya taake 'awaam-o-khawas dono ke liye mufeed ho.

Aakhir mein yeh musawwad (Draft) Shaikh-ul-Hadees Hazrat Mufti Ishaq Sahab Patel (Darul 'Uloom Islamia 'Arabiya, Taloja) aur phir Hazrat Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Sahab Barmare (Madrasa Zia-ul-Uloom, Kundloor Karnataka) ke hawale kiya gaya. In dono buzurgon ne az awwal ta aakhir Musawwada dekha, hawalajaat shaamil kiye aur jawabat ko daleel ke saath mazboot banaya.

Allah Ta'aala in tamaam asateza aur ahl-e-'ilm hazaraat ko behtareen ajar ata farmaye, imaan ki salamati, sihhat wa aafiyat ke sath umren daraaz kare aur mustaqbil me unki qabron ko raushan kare aur jannat-ul-firdous mein a'alaa maqam ata kare. Aameen.

Agar mein Mufti Abdul Basit Muqadam Sahab (Imam Wa Khateeb, Jame Masjid, Upper Tudil) aur Hazrat Maulana Habibur Rahman Sahab Qasmi (Ustaz-e- Adabe 'Arabi wa Angrezi, Darul 'Uloom Islamia 'Arabiya, Taloja) ka zikr na karoon to yeh na-shukri hogi. In dono ne kitaab ki composing ki aur adabi islahat ki.

Is kitaab ki tashih wa tadveen mein humne bharpoor koshish ki hai. Phir bhi agar kisi mas-ale mein koi islah ki zarurat ho to guzarish aur iltija hai ke ek adna talib-e-'ilm jaankar humein muttale' (inform) kare'. InshaAllah agle edition mein uski islah ki jayegi.

Wallahul-Musta'aan wa alayhit-tuklaan, wa la hawla wa la quwwata illa billahil aliyyil 'azeem.

Mufti Faiz Ahmed Bin Adam Mulla (Haal Khurd, Khopoli).

TAHAARAT KE MASA-IL

Musta'mal Paani (Used Water)

Sawal: Musta'mal paani kise kehte hain? Neez wuzoo aur gusl me is paani ke isti'emaal ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Jo paani farz wuzoo ya gusl me isti'emaal huwa ho use musta'mal paani kehte hain, yeh paani paak to zaroor hota hai lekin paak karne wala nahi hota, ya'ani dobara use wuzoo ya gusl me isti'emaal nahi kar sakte, lekin agar yeh paani doosre paani ke saath milkar 192 litre ho jaaye to phir isti'emaal kar sakte hain. (Al-Majmoo':1/150). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs farz gusl ke dauran paani se bhari balti me haath daale to kya wo paani musta'mal ho jaayega? Kya us paani se us ka wuzoo ya gusl karna sahi hai?

Jawab: Haath daalne ki do (2) sooratein hain:

Note: Agar koi shakhs balti mein sirf chullu bhar paani nikaalne ki niyyat se haath daalta hai, aur phir woh nikaala hua paani le kar wuzoo ya farz gusl karta hai, to aysi soorat mein balti ka baaqi paani musta'mal nahi hoga, kyunke haath daalne ka maqsad sirf paani nikaalna tha, na ke wuzoo ya gusl ka aaghaz karna. Albattah agar koi shakhs balti mein haath is niyyat se daalta hai ke main farz wuzoo ke liye haath dho raha hoon ya farz gusl ke liye haath dho raha hoon, to ab chhunkay haath daalne ke waqt niyyat farz wuzoo ya gusl ki hai, lihaza balti ka paani musta'mal shumar hoga.

Sooraj Ki Tapish Se Garam Hone Wale Paani Se Taharat

Sawal: Kya Sooraj ki tapish (heat) se garam hone wale paani se taharat haasil karna durust hai?

Jawab: Maa-e-mushammas (Sun-exposed water - water that has been heated by direct sunlight): Hauz aur taalaab ka paani sooraj ki tapish (heat) se garam ho to bil-ittifaaq is ka isti'emaal makrooh nahi hai. Albatta kisi bartan ka paani sooraj ki tapish aur dhoop se garam ho to in sharaait ke saath makrooh-e-tanzeehi hai (sooraj ki tapish se garam hone wale paani ko mushammas paani kahenge).

Peshaab Lagi Hui Cheez Ko Paak Karne Ka Tareeqa

Sawal: Chote bachche ya bachchi ki peshaab lagi hui cheez ko paak karne ka tareeqa kya hai?

Jawab: Agar ladke (boy) ki 'umr qamri (Islami) do saal se kam hai aur wo doodh ke 'alaawah koi cheez bataure giza na khaata ho to uski peshaab par paani chidakna is tarah ke paani peshab par gaalib aajaaye kaafi hai ya'ani kapda aur badan paak ho jaayega, lekin agar ladki (girl) ki peshaab lagi ho to us jagah ya kapde ko mukammal paani baha kar dhona zaroori hai yahan tak ke naapaaki ka asar khatm ho jaaye (Hashiyatul-Jamal: 1/189). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Kapde Ko Kutta Chu Jaaye To Paak Karne Ka Tareeqa

Sawal: Agar kisi ke kapde ko kutta chu jaaye to kya kapda naapaak ho jaata hai? Neez usko paak karne ka tareeqa kya hai?

Jawab: Agar kutte ne apna mu kisi shakhs ke kapde ya badan ko lagaaya ya kutte ka geela badan kapde ya badan ko lag gaya ho ya kutta to sookha ho lekin kapda ya badan geela ho to wo kapda ya badan naapaak ho jaayega, is ko paak karne ka tareeqa yeh hai ke use saat martaba khaalis paani se dhoya jaaye, in saat martaba me ek bar paak mitti mila kar gadle paani se dhona bhi zaroori hai (Hashiyatul-Jamal: 1/186). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Paani Me Makkhi, Machchar Ke Marne Se Paani Ka Hukm

Sawal: Jis jandar me behne wala khoon na ho jaise Makkhi, Machchar to kya us ke paani me gir kar marne ki wajah se paani naapaak ho jaayega?

Jawab: Jis jaandar me behne wala khoon na ho uske paani me gir kar marne se ya mar kar girne se wo paani naapaak nahi hoga jab tak ke us ki wajah se paani ka rang, boo (smell) ya maza (taste) badal na gaya ho. (Al-Minhaaj-ul-Qawim: 1/15). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Attached Baitul Khala Aur Bathroom Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Aaj kal bathroom me baitul khala rehte hain kya wahan Wuzoo kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Ji han. Wuzoo karne me koi haraj nahi, albatta is baat ka ehtimaam aur ehtiyat karna zaroori hai ke wahan naapaaki ke cheenten badan par na ude warna namaz nahi hogi (Hashiyatul-Jamal: 1/389). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Wuzoo ke ta'alluq se suna tha ke wuzoo karne ke baad girne waala paani naapaak hota hai kyunke is se gunah jhadte hain to kya yeh sahi hai?

Jawab: Gunah jhadne wali baat to sahi hai albatta jab tak 'uzwa (body part) par naapaaki na lagi ho Wuzoo me musta'mal paani naapaak nahi hota (Al-Majmoo': 1/158). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Bathroom me baitul khala hai, kya wahan wuzoo ki masnoon du'aayein padh sakte hain ya nahi?

Jawab: Bathroom aur baitul khala dono ki jagah alag alag hoti hai agarche kamra ek ho isliye agar bathroom ki jagah par du'aayein padhe to koi haraj nahi hona chahiye (Al-bayan: 1/355, Al-Majmoo':2/167). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Ghar me bhi baitul khala hote hain to du'aa kab padhni chaahiye?

Jawab: Baitul Khala me jaane ki du'aa baitul Khala me daakhil hone se pehle padhna chahiye aur nikalne ki du'aa baitul khala se bahar nikal kar padhe. (Al-bayan: 1/204). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Peshaab & Paakhana (Urine & Stool) Ke Liye Simt (Direction)

Sawal: India ke e'etibaar se Peshaab aur Paakhana kis simt (Direction) me karna chahiye?

Jawab: India ke aitebaar se qiblah Magrib ki janib hai, is liye agar khule maydaan me Peshaab - Pakhaana karna ho to peshaab - pakhaana karte waqt magrib ki simt (direction) me naa to chehra kare aur naa hi peeth ('Umdatus-Salik:1/20). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya aysi koi hadees hai jis me khule maydaan me Peshaab - Pakhaana ke liye mashriq ya Magrib ki janib rukh karne ka hukm hai. Agar hai to us ki kya wajah? Main ne yeh sawal isliye kiya hai taa'ke is ki asal wajah / haqeeqat zaahir ho kyunke kuch log is hadees ke mutabiq India me bhi 'amal karte hain.

Jawab: Ek hadees mein “Mashriq ya Magrib ki janib rukh kare” alfaaz aaye hain. 'Ulama ne likkha hai yeh hukm Madinah ke liye hai isliye ke unka qiblah us janib nahi hai albatta India ke e'etibaar se qiblah Magrib ki janib hai isliye khule maydaan me Peshaab – Pakhaana karna ho to wo Magrib chod kar kisi aur janib kare (Mustakhraj Abu 'Awwanah:1/170, Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah: 1/44). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

English Bathroom (Toilet) Isti'emaal Karna

Sawal: Kya English Bathroom/Toilet isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: English bathroom ya Toilet ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai, albatta istinja se faragat ke baad jism ke deegar hisson par ya kapde par naapaaki lag jaaye to use dhona waajib hoga. Lihaaza aise baitul Khala me jab jaane ka ittifaq ho jaaye to ehtiyatan apna tehband (lungi, pants etc.) utaar kar baith jaaye phir faragat ke baad kamar se neeche paani baha kar kapde pehen le. (Al-Majmoo':2/78).

Hadees-e mubarakah me lafz (Muhajjarun 'alayhi bil-labin) mahalle istishhaad hai aur Majmoo' ki ibaarat me (Fil bunyan) mahalle istishhaad hai. Is riwaayat se fuqahaa-e-muhaddiseen ne kaee (many) ek masa-il aur fawaaid par istidlaal kiya hai. Minjumla unke taamiraat me istiqbaale qiblah aur istidbaare qiblah ka jawaaz hai. Isi tarah is riwaayat se taamiraat me qaza-e-haajat ke jawaaz par bhi daleel pakdi hai. Zaahir si baat hai ke Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) aur Sahaba-e-Kiraam (RA) ke daur me baitul Khala ki jo bhi shakl wa soorat rahi hogi yaqeenan is me taraqqi hi hui hai, yahan tak ke maujoodah daur ke Indian Bhandey aur Kamod is ki taraqqiyaafta soorat hai. Is riwaayat aur ibaarat se mahaz taameerat me qazaye haajat ke jawaaz ko batlaana maqsood hai, chaahe taameerat me bane huwe Bhande ki shakal o soorat jo bhi ho ya usme aur taraqqi ho kar koi aur soorat ho jaaye. Wallahu A'alam. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Note: Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (R.A) Nabi-e-Akram (ﷺ) ki har baat mein payravi (follow) karte the, aur jo ta'aleemaat unhon ne Nabi ﷺ se haasil ki thin, unhein baad walon tak muntqil karte the. Is hadees mein 'Abdullah Bin 'Umar (R.A) bayan karte hain: "Main ne Nabi ﷺ ko dekha", ya'ani main ne Nabi ﷺ ko qaza-e-haajat (peshaab ya pakhaana) karte hue dekha — "eenton (bricks) se ghira hua", ya'ani aap ﷺ ke ird-gird mitti se bani huee eenton ki deewar thi taa'ke logon ki nigahon se parda ho.

Bukhari ki riwayat mein Ibn-e-'Umar (R.A) ke Nabi ﷺ ko dekhne ka sabab yeh bayan hua hai: "Main Hafsah (R.A) ke ghar ki chhat par chadha, to main ne Nabi ﷺ ko qaza-e-haajat karte hue dekha ke aap ﷺ Ka'abah ki simt peeth kiye hue the aur Shaam ki taraf muh kiye hue the". Ibn-e-'Umar (R.A) ne Nabi ﷺ ka woh hissa dekha jo zaahir tha, ya'ani aap ke jism-e-mubarak ka upar wala hissa, aur aysi koi cheez nahi dekhi jo shar'an dekhna jaaiz na ho, jaise ke satr wagayrah.

Office Me Urinal Me Khade Ho Kar Peshab Karna

Sawal: Aaj kal aksar office me Urinal lagaye hote hain, is me khade ho kar peshab karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Peshab baith kar karna Sunnat hai, lekin majboori ki wajah se khade hokar bhi peshab karna jaaiz hai, isliye Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se khade ho kar peshab karna bhi saabit hai. (Bukhari Kitabul Wuzoo, babul baul qaiman wa qaidan:224, Muslim: 273, An-Nasa'i:1/19). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Aap (ﷺ) ne ek baar jo khade reh kar peshab kiya tha uski asal waja kya thi? Warna log apni sahoolat ke liye majboori ka isti'emaal karenge.

Jawab: Allah ne deen me aasani aur sahoolat rakhi hai koi tangi nahi rakhi taake har kisi ke liye deen par chalna aasan ho. Aap (ﷺ) ne jo kooda dalne ki jagah par khade ho kar peshab kiya tha 'Ulama ne uski mukhtalif wujoohaat bayan ki hain.

Qur-aan Download Kiya Huwa Mobile Phone Baitul Khala Le Jana

Sawal: Aaj kal aksar musalmano ke smart phone (Mobile) me Qur-aan download kiya huwa hota hai. Aise mobile ko baitul khala le jaane ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar mobile phone ki screen par Qur-aan-e-kareem ki aayat zaahir ho rahi ho to is soorat me mobile phone baitul khala le jaane ki ijazat nahi hogi kyunke is me Qur-aan ki be hurmati hai, lekin agar aayat screen par zaahir na ho rahi ho ya'ani sirf mobile ke memory ya software me store ho to aysi soorat me mobile phone baitul khala le jaane me koi haraj nahi hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:1/161, Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 1/196). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Baitul Khala Me Khane Peene Aur Padhne Ke Liye Akhbar Le Jaane Ka Hukm

Sawal: Aaj kal log Magribi tehzeeb se mutassir ho kar waqt bachane ke liye subha uthne ke baad toilet me peene ke liye chai aur padhne ke liye akhbar le jate hain. Kya yeh 'amal durust hai?

Jawab: Baitul Khala me khana ya peena, ziyadah der tak baithe rehna ya aysi cheez saath le jana jis me Allah Ta'aala ke Asma-e-husna ho ya Rasool-e-Akram (ﷺ) ke ba-barkat naam ho isi tarah batein karna aur zikr karna sab shara'an (sharee'at me) makrooh hai. Soorat-e-mas'oula me jo chai noshi aur akhbar beeni (news paper reading) ka tazkira kiya gaya hai, yeh bhi az ruye shara'a (sharee'at ki nazar me) makrooh hai. Aysi cheezon se ijtinaab aur parhez karna chahiye. Yeh sab batein kiraaman kaatibeen farishton ki aziyyat ka baa'is ban sakti hain. “Chunanche Ibn-e-kaseer (Rh) ne is silsile me ek hadees-e mursal tehreer ki hai, jis me Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ka irshade giraami hai ke: “Tum kiraaman kaatibeen ka ikram kiya karo kyunke wo tum se kisi waqt bhi alag nahi hote, siwaye do haalaton ke, ek to biwi se sohbat karte waqt aur doosre peshab ya pakhana karte waqt aur ek riwaayat me gusl ka bhi tazkira milta hai.” Lihaaza hame apni aysi gair sharaee harkaton se ijtinab karna chahiye jo Allah Ta'aala ki paakeeza makhlooq ki aziyyat ka sabab bane. (Al-Jamiu's-Sageer wa Ziyadatuhu: 6644, Al-Majmoo':2/73, Kifaayatul Akhyar: 1/35, Saheeh ligairih. Akhrajahu Abu Dawood (15), An-Nasa'i fi “al-sunanul Kubra” (32) Bilikhtilaf yaseer, Ib-ne Kaseer Al-bidayah wan-Nihayah: 1/45-46). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qay (Vomit) Naapaak Hai

Sawal: Qay ka paani agar badan par gir jaaye to wo paani paak hai ya naapaak?

Jawab: Qay naapaak hai lihaaza kapde ya badan par ya kisi jagah lag jaaye to use itna dho kar saaf karna zaroori hai ke najaasat ka asar khatm ho jaaye (At-Tambeeh:1/23). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Miswak Ke Isti'emaal Ki Muddat

Sawal: Miswak kitne dino tak isti'emaal kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Ahadees me Miswak ke mukhtalif maqaasid wa fawaaid waarid huwe hain, miswak mu ki pakeezgi hai, Rabb Ta'aala ki raza ka zariya hai wagayrah. Isi tarah fuqahaa-e-kiraam ne miswak ke fawaaid me likkha hai ke miswak ke zariye daant saaf hote hain aur muh ki badbu door hoti hai lihaaza jab tak miswak se mazkoora maqaasid haasil hote rahen us waqt tak miswak isti'emaal kare aur agar miswak is qadar chota ho jaaye ke daant saaf karna aur miswak karna mumkin na ho to munaasib hai ke miswak ko tabdeel kare. Ahadees wa fiqh ki kitaabon me aysi koi had (limit) meri nazar se nahi guzri ke miswak kitne dino tak aur kitna khatam hone tak isti'emaal karna chahiye. (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/85). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Wuzoo Ke Faraaiz

Sawal: Wuzoo ke kitne Faraaiz hain?

Jawab: Wuzoo ke 6 Faraaiz hain: (1) Farz Wuzoo ki niyyat karna. (2) Peshani ke baalon se thuddi (Chin) ke neeche aur ek kaan ki lau (earlobe) se doosre kaan ki lau tak poora chehra dhona. (3) dono haath kohniyo (elbows) samet dhona. (4) Sir ke kuch hisse ka masah karna. (5) dono paanw takhno samet dhona. (6) aur isi tarteeb se wuzoo karna (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/47). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dauran-E-Wuzoo Kisi 'Uzwa (Part) Ke Bare Me Shak Ho Jaaye

Sawal: Agar kisi Shakhs ko dauran-e-wuzoo kisi 'uzwa (part) ke baare me shak ho jaaye ke dhoya nahi to aysi soorat me kya karna chahiye?

Jawab: Agar kisi Shakhs ko dauran-e-wuzoo kisi 'uzwa ki tahaarat ke baare me shak ho jaaye masalan sar ke masah ke waqt yeh shak paida ho jaaye ke dono haath dhoya ya nahi to is soorat me wo yeh maan lega ke us ne hathon ko nahi dhoya hai, lihaaza use hathon ko dobara dhona waajib hoga, phir uske baad ke aa'aza (parts) ko bit-tarteeb dhoyega (Al-Majmoo':1/529). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Qur-aan Ko Choone Ya Pakadne Ke Liye Wuzoo Zaroori

Sawal: Kya Qur-aan ko choone ke liye Wuzoo zaroori hai? Isi tarah 'aurat haiz ki haalat me ho to kya wo Qur-aan ko choo sakti hai?

Jawab: Qur-aan ko choone ke liye Wuzoo lazimi aur zaroori hai. Lihaaza haiz ki haalat me 'aurat na Qur-aan padh sakti hai aur na choo sakti hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/190, 1/238). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar kisi kitaab me Qur-aan ki Aayat likhi ho to kya Haizah (Menstruation) 'aurat bataure zikr padh sakti hai?

Jawab: Haizah (Menstruation) 'aurat ke liye Qur-aan ki Aayatein bataure zikr ya du'aa ke padhna jaaiz hai, jaise bismillah, subhanallazi sakhkhara lana…… Rabbana Aatina fiddunya Hasana…. Wagayrah (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/86). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Kuch logon ka kehna hai ke haiz ki haalat me Qur-aan agar kapde se pakadna chaahe to pakad sakte hain. Kya yeh baat sahi hai?

Jawab: Yeh durust nahi hai. Jis tarah Qur-aan-e-kareem ko choona haraam hai usi tarah use uthana bhi haraam hai. (Al-Majmoo':2/65). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Mobile Me Download Kiye Huwe Qur-aan Ka Hukm

Sawal: Mobile me download kiye huwe Qur-aan ka kya hukm hai? Kya use bagair wuzoo haath laga sakte hain?

Jawab: Agar Qur-aan screen par display ho jaaye tab Wuzoo zaroori ya'ani farz hoga lekin agar mobile ya memory card me store ho lekin screen par display na huwa ho to Wuzoo zaroori nahi (Nihayatuz-Zain:34). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Qur-aane majeed ki aayaton ko be Wuzoo choona isi tarah se mobile ke screen on hone ke baad Qur-aan ki aayaton ko choo kar tilaawat karna jaaiz hai ya nahi? Neez Haizah (Menstruation) 'auratein Qur-aan choo sakti hain ya nahi?

Jawab: Qur-aane majeed ki aayaton ko be Wuzoo choone ke silsile me chaahe mobile ki screen par ho ya Qur-aan se tilaawat ki jaarahi ho bahar soorat bagair Wuzoo ke choo kar tilaawat karna mana'a hai. Isi tarah se Haizah (Menstruation) 'auraton ke liye bhi Qur-aan ka choona ya padhna jaaiz nahi. Han! Agar kisi shaikh se mureed ho ya phir talaba (students) ko 'arabi padhati ho to is soorat me lafz ko tod tod kar padhaye isi tarah se kuch apna khas tasbeehaat ka ma'amul ho ya waqt waqt ki du'aa mein Qur-aan ki aayat aa jaaye to zikr aur du'aa ki niyyat se aayat padhne ki gunjaish hai. (Al-Majmoo':2/65). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mobile Se Qur-aan Sunne Ke Liye Wuzoo Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya mobile phone se audio ya video ki shakal me Qur-aan paak sunne ke liye ba-wuzoo hona zaroori hai?

Jawab: Agar mobile phone se Qur-aane majeed audio ya video ki shakl me suna jaaye aur screen par aayat-e-mubaraka zaahir na horahi ho to is mobile phone ko bila Wuzoo choona aur Qur-aan sunna jaaiz hai lekin agar mobile phone ki screen par Qur-aan-e-kareem ki aayat zaahir ho rahi ho to is soorat me mobile phone ko bila Wuzoo choona jaaiz nahi. (Al-Majmoo':2/70, Nihayatuz-Zain:1/32, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:1/123). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dauran-E-Wuzoo Agar Azaan Shuru' Ho Jaaye

Sawal: Dauran-e-wuzoo agar azaan shuru' ho jaaye to kya kare? Wuzoo karta rahe ya ruk kar azaan ka Jawab de?

Jawab: azaan hote waqt agar wuzoo kar rahe hon to Wuzoo karte huwe bhi azaan ka Jawab diya ja sakta hai, Wuzoo rokne ki zaroorat nahi, Wuzoo ke dauran azaan ka Jawab dena saabit hai. (Al-fatawa Al-fiqhiyyatul Kubra Li-Ibn Hajar: 1/60). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Barhana (Necked) Wuzoo Karna

Sawal: Agar koi gusl khana me barhana (nanga ho kar) wuzoo kare to kya us ka Wuzoo ho jaayega?

Jawab: Wuzoo ke sahi hone ke liye satar chupana shart nahi hai. Satar ka chupana to namaz me shart hai lihaaza Wuzoo ho jaayega, neez Wuzoo ke baad satar ka khulna ya kholna naaqize wuzoo (wuzoo ko todne wala) bhi nahi hai. (At-Tambeeh: 1/17-28). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Hauz (Water Pool) Ke Paani Se Wuzoo

Sawal: Bahut saari masajid me hauz hote hain jin me wuzoo ka paani wapas usi hauz me hi jaata hai, Wuzoo kiya huwa paani is qabil nahi hota ke wapas Wuzoo karen to kya hauz ke paani se kiya huwa Wuzoo sahi hoga?

Jawab: Wuzoo me isti'emaal kiya huwa paani musta'mal ho jaata hai jo paak to hai lekin paak karne wala nahi hota, agar yeh paani doosre paak paani ke saath mil kar qullatain ya'ani (192 Litre) tak pohonche aur paani ke rang, boo (smell) aur maza (taste) me kuch tagayyur / tabdeeli (changes) na ho to wo paani tahoor hoga, ya'ani khud paak hoga aur paak karne wala bhi hoga, lihaaza is hauz ke paani ko Wuzoo ke liye phir se isti'emaal kar sakte hain. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 1/40). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dettol Ya Itar Daale Huwe Paani Se Wuzoo Aur Gusl

Sawal: Kya Dettol ya Itar wagayrah daale huwe paani se Wuzoo aur gusl kiya ja sakta hai?

Jawab: Wuzoo aur gusl ke liye khalis paani zaroori hai, kisi paak cheez ke milne ki wajah se uski teen sifat (Rang - color, boo - smell, maza - taste) me se kisi ek me tabdeeli na ho to us paani se wuzoo gusl durust hai warna wo paani mutagayyar (badla hua) ho jaayega, agarche yeh mutagayyar paani paak hai lekin is paani se Wuzoo aur gusl durust nahi, Soorate mas-uoolah me mazkoora paani dettol ya itar shaamil karne ki wajah se mutagayyar ho gaya hai lihaaza aise paani se Wuzoo aur gusl nahi kiya ja sakta. (Kifaayatul Akhyar: 1/15). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Makeup (Cosmetics) Par Wuzoo

Sawal: Kya makeup (cosmetics) par Wuzoo ho jaata hai?

Jawab: Makeup agar aysi cheez se bana ho jo paani ko aazaye wuzoo (parts of ablution) tak pahonchne se maane'e (rokne wala) ho to wuzoo aur gusl durust nahi hoga. Iske 'alaawah aysi cheezon se makeup karna jo paani ko jild (skin) tak pahonchne se maa'ne na ho to uske rehte huwe wuzoo aur gusl durust ho jaata hai. (Al-Majmoo': 1/426). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Lipstick, Nail Polish, Tel Aur Mehndi Laga Kar Wuzoo

Sawal: Kya Lipstick aur Nail Polish ke maujood hote huwe Wuzoo sahi hoga?

Jawab: Wuzoo sahi hone ke sharaait me se ek shart yeh bhi hai ke aazaye Wuzoo (parts of ablution) par koi aysi cheez na ho jo paani ko chamdi (skin) tak pahonchne me rukaawat bane, isliye lip stick, nail polish ya in jaisi har wo cheez jo maa'n-e-Wuzoo ho aur aazaye Wuzoo par paani pahonchne me rukaawat bane to Wuzoo karne se pehle unko zaayil (khatm) karna aur mitana zaroori hoga. Inke rehte huwe wuzoo sahi nahi hoga. (Al-Majmoo': 1/426). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya sar ke baalo me tel (Oil) lagane ke baad wuzoo sahi hoga?

Jawab: Ji, Wuzoo sahih ho jaata hai lekin yeh zaroori hai ke tel jama'a huwa na ho, jaise Dalda, Ghee, Charbi wagayrah chunke yeh cheezen paani ko jild (skin) tak pahonchne ke liye rukaawat banti hain to in cheezon ke maujood rehte huwe wuzoo sahih nahi hoga. (Al-Majmoo': 1/426). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'Aurat ka hatho me mehndi laga kar wuzoo karne ka shara'an (sharee'at me) kya hukm hai?

Jawab: 'Umooman mehndi ka rang aazaye wuzoo (parts of ablution) par paani ke pahonchne ke liye maane'e (rukaawat) aur rukaawat nahi hota. Lihaaza mehndi ka rang hote huwe wuzoo sahih hoga, lekin agar koi mehndi aysi ho ke jiski the (layer) chamdi par jam jati hai to is soorat me Wuzoo sahih nahi hoga. (Al-Majmoo': 1/426-467). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ungli Par Voting Ki Siyahi (Ink) Se Wuzoo Aur Gusl

Sawal: Voting ke waqt ungli par jo siyahi lagaee jati hai uske rehte huwe Wuzoo aur gusl ka kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Vote dene ke waqt par bataure alamat siyahi ka isti'emaal kiya jaata hai jo hukoomat ki taraf se ek lazimi amr (mandatory) hai. Tehqeeq ke baad ma'aloom huwa ke yeh nishan sukhne ke baad jirm (cover) ki shakal ikhtiyaar kar leta hai aur paani ke pahonchne ke liye rukaawat banta hai jis ke rehte Wuzoo aur gusl durust nahi hoga isliye is ko fauri taur par saaf karne ki koshish ki jaaye. Is ka aasan tareeqa yeh hai ke voting ko jaane se qabl ungli par vaseline ya Grease ya us jaisi chikni cheez lagali jaaye phir siyahi lagate hi usi waqt kapde ki madad se use saf kar diya jaaye, phir apne maqam par jaakar kapde dhone ka liquid “Aala ya Bliching water ya Toilet ke bartan ki safa'ee me isti'emaal hone wala liquid” se fauri taur par saf kar diya jaaye to nishan usi waqt mit jaata hai, yeh mujarrab (tajarba kiya huwa) hai. Lekin agar is ko nikalna dushwar aur takleefdah (painful) ho to Maslak-e-Hanafi par 'amal karte huwe wuzoo aur gusl ko sahih aur durust samjha jaayega. (Al-Majmoo': 3/148). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Wuzoo Ke Baad Aazaye Wuzoo Ko Pochna

Sawal: Wuzoo ke baad aazaye Wuzoo (parts of ablution) ko pochne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Mubaah (Jaaiz) hai lekin saaf na karna aula aur mustahab hai. (Al-Majmoo': 1/42). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Wuzoo Tootne Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Wuzoo kin baaton se toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Char asbaab me se kisi ek sabab ke paaye jaane ki soorat me Wuzoo toot jaayega.

Agli pichli sharamgaah se kisi cheez ka nikalna. (2) Aqal ka zaayil (Khatam) hona, chahe sone hi ki wajah se waqti taur par ho. (3) 'Aurat ya mard ki chamdi (skin) ka kisi naa-mahram mard ya 'aurat ki chamdi se choo jana. (4) Hatheli ya ungliyon ke pet ki janib (inner palm or the inner side of the fingers) se apni ya kisi doosre insan ki sharamgaah ko choona (Rauzatul Taalibeen: 1/72). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Sone ki wajah se Wuzoo ke tootne ki kya kaifiyat hai?

Jawab: Aysi neend jisme badan dheela pad jaaye, paas waale ka kalaam (speech) na samajh sake aur shu'oor (consciousness / sense) khatam ho Jaaye Wuzoo ko tod degi. Sirf unghne se wuzoo nahi tootega. Neez apni sureen (buttock / hips) ko niche ki taraf achchi tarah jamaye huwe baith kar sone se bhi Wuzoo nahi tootega, chahe sahara le kar soye (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/74). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya namaz me hasne se Wuzoo Toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek hasne se Wuzoo nahi toot-taa albatta namaz toot jati hai jabke do harf zaahir ho jaaye (Al-Majmoo': 2/60). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya gaana sunne ya gaali Dene se Wuzoo toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Gaana sunne ya gaali Dene se Wuzoo nahi toot-taa (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/72). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya kisi naa-mahram 'aurat ko bila shahwat choone se Wuzoo toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Kisi naa-mahram 'aurat ko bila hayil (bagair kisi cheez ki aadh ke) choone se wuzoo toot jaata hai, chahe bagair shahwat ke chooye, hatta ke apni biwi ko choone se bhi wuzoo toot jaata hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/72). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya kisi choti bachchi ko choone se bhi Wuzoo toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Ladki agar Kam 'umr ho aur hadd-e-shahwat ko na pohonchi ho ya'ani us ko dekhne se jinsi khwahish ubharti na ho to use choone se Wuzoo nahi tootega warna toot jaayega (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/74). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Wuzoo ke baad nakhun kaate to kya dobara Wuzoo karna zaroori hai? Ya Kam az Kam nakhun hi dobara dhona lazim hai?

Jawab: Wuzoo ko todne wali cheezen char hain, inke 'alaawah kisi cheez se Wuzoo nahi toot-taa lihaaza nakhun aur baal wagayrah zaayil karne ki soorat me Wuzoo ya gusl par koi asar nahi padega isliye na to dobara Wuzoo karne ki zaroorat hai aur naa hi nakhun dhone ki (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/72). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Biwi Aur Saas Ko Haath Lagane Se Wuzoo Par Asar

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek biwi ko haath (skin Touch) lagane se Wuzoo Toot jaata hai lekin saas ko haath lagane se Wuzoo nahi toot-taa hai. Is ki kya waja hai?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek naa-mahram 'aurat ki chamdi (skin) ko naa-mahram mard ki chamdi choo Jaaye to wuzoo toot jaata hai, biwi bhi naa-mahram hoti hai isliye use choone se Wuzoo toot jaata hai, nikah ki wajah se biwi mahram nahi ho jaati wo to naa-mahram hi reh jaati hai, sirf is se istimta'a ya'ani faidah haasil karna halal ho jaata hai. Jabke saas hameshah ke liye haraam ho jati hai isliye unhe choone se Wuzoo nahi toot-taa (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/63). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muh Boli Bahan Ko Haath Lagane Se Wuzoo Par Asar

Sawal: Agar koi apni muh boli bahan ko haath lagaye to kya uska Wuzoo toot jaayega?

Jawab: Muh boli beti, bhai ya bahan haqeeqi ki tarah nahi hote inke ahkam ajnabi hi ki tarah hote hain lihaaza muh boli bahan ko choone se wuzoo toot jaayega (Sooratul Ahzab Aayat 5, Kitabul Um: 5/25). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Baap Ya Maa Sharik Bhanji Aur Bhatiji Se Wuzoo

Sawal: Kya allati (baap sharik) aur akhyafi (maa sharik) bhanji ko haath lagane se wuzoo toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Bhanji aur bhatiji (chahe woh allati ho ya akhyafi) mahram rishtedaron me se hai lihaaza inko choone se wuzoo nahi tootega (Kitabul Um:5/25). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Safar Me Gair Mahram Ko Haath Lagne Se Wuzoo Par Asar

Sawal: Agar Safar me train wagayrah me Wuzoo ki haalat me galati se kisi gair mahram ko ya uske baalon ko haath lag jaaye to kya is soorat me Wuzoo baqi rahega?

Jawab: Baalon ko haath lagne se wuzoo nahi tutta, albatta jism ke deegar a'aza ko bila hayil choone se wuzoo toot jaayega (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:1/187). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khurooj-E-Mani (Sperm) Se Wuzoo Nahi Toot-taa

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee RA ke nazdeek mani (sperm) ke kharij hone se Wuzoo nahi toot-taa, Albatta gusl farz hota hai, Lekin Qur-aan ko choo nahi sakte. To is mas'ale ka kya faidah ke mani kharij hone se Wuzoo nahi tootega? Baraye meherbani 'Ulama rehbari farmaye.

Jawab: Peshab ya paakhane ke raste se koi bhi cheez nikal jaaye usse Wuzoo toot jaata hai siwaye mani ke, ke jab kisi baWuzoo shakhs ko bedari me ya baith kar sone ki haalat me ehtilaam (nocturnal emission or wet dream) ho jaaye (mani kharij hojaaye) to is par gusl to waajib ho jaata hai lekin Wuzoo nahi toot-taa kyunke mani ke nikalne se do umoor me se (ya'ani Wuzoo aur gusl me se) bada amr ya'ani gusl waajib ho gaya to kam darje wala amr ya'ani Wuzoo waajib nahi hota ya'ani Wuzoo nahi toot-taa, iska faidah yeh hai ki is haalat me agar wuzoo toot jaaye to wuzoo karna sahih hoga barkhilaf haiz aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) wali janaabat ke, ke is haalat me Wuzoo sahih nahi hota (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:1/111). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ki Sharamgah Se Nikalne Wale Paani Ka Hukm

Sawal: 'Auraton ki sharamgah se safed rang ki Jo rutoobat (Liquid) girta hai uska kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar yeh rutoobat 'aurat ki sharamgah ke zahiri hisse se nikle to wo paak hai aur uske nikalne se wuzoo nahi tootega, zahiri hisse se murad 'aurat ki sharamgah ka woh hissa hai jo qazaye haajat ke waqt khul jaata hai aur gusl-e-janaabat me jahan tak dhona waajib hota hai, lekin agar yeh rutoobat andar se ya'ani reham se ya reham ke qareeb se nikal rahi hai to naapaak hogi aur uske nikalne se wuzoo bhi toot jaayega (Bugyatul Mustarshideen:1/201). Bataure 'ilaaj atibbaa (Doctors) chand dawaiyan tajweez karte hain, Jo is beemaari me mufeed saabit huwe hain, jaise hamdard remedies ki Anise Niswa ya Anise Khawaateen ya Niswani wagayrah.

Ehtiyaat: In dawaiyon ka isti'emaal ayyam-e-hamal aur ayyam-e-haiz wa Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) mein nahi karna chahiye. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mardon Ki Sharamgah Se Mazi (Pre-Cum) Ka Kharij Hona

Sawal: Biwi se mohabbat ki batein ya izhaar karte waqt mard ki agli sharamgah se jo Mazi (chikna safed paani) kharij hota hai, is ki wajah se Wuzoo aur gusl karne ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Mardon ki agli sharamgah se "Mazi (Pre-cum)" ya'ani chikna safed paani nikalta hai hukman yeh naapaak hai, iske nikalne se Wuzoo to toot jaayega lekin gusl waajib nahi hoga, aur agar kapdon ya badan par lag jaaye to dhona zaroori hoga (Al-Majmoo':2/143). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Rahal (Book Stand) Ya Bag Me Rakhe Qur-aan Ko Uthane Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya Qur-aan rakhe huwe rahal ya Qur-aan ke Box ko jis me Qur-aan maujood ho ya woh tipaayi jis par Qur-aan rakhkha huwa ho bagair Wuzoo utha ya choo sakte hain?

Jawab: Nahi, in cheezon ko choone ya uthane ke liye Wuzoo zaroori hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/79). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Safar ke dauran agar kisi bag me Qur-aan rakhkha Jaaye to use uthane ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Qur-aan ko choone ya uthane ke liye Wuzoo shart hai, agar Qur-aan bag me rakhkha huwa ho aur saath me dusra saamaan bhi ho to us bag ko bagair Wuzoo ke utha sakte hain, lekin agar bag me sirf Qur-aan rakhkha huwa ho to use choone ya uthane ke liye Wuzoo zaroori hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/80). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi maa'zur shakhs Qur-aan ke auraq ko lakdi se palte to kya aisa karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek raajeh (ziyadah sahi) mas-alah yahi hai ke tilaawat karne wala shakhs lakdi se Qur-aan-E-Kareem ke auraaq (pages) ko palat sakta hai kyunke is kaifiyat ke saath na to wo Qur-aan ko uthane wala shumaar hoga aur na hi choone wala (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/80). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gusl Ke Faraaiz

Sawal: Gusl ke kitne Faraaiz hain?

Jawab: Gusl ke do farz hain: (1) Farz ya sunnat gusl ki niyyat karna. (2) Poore badan par is tarah Paani bahana ke baal barabar bhi koi jagah sookhi na rahe (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/88). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Farz Gusl Barhana (Nanga) Ho Kar Karna

Sawal: Kya farz gusl barhana ho kar karna durust hai?

Jawab: Khuli fiza (open atmosphere) me aur logon ki maujoodgi me barhana gusl karna jaaiz nahi hai, lihaaza us waqt satar ke saath gusl karna waajib hai, albatta agar band gusl khane me tanha gusl kar rahe hain to bila satar barhana gusl karna jaaiz hai aur satar ke saath gusl karna afzal hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/90). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gusl Ke Mauqe Par Ki Gai Wuzoo

Sawal: Kya Farz o sunnat gusl ke mauqe par ki gai wuzoo se namaz padhi ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Ji ha: Farz Wuzoo ki niyyat se ki gai Wuzoo ke baad agar nawaqiz-e-wuzoo (wuzoo todne wali cheezon) me se koi baat pesh na aayi hoto us Wuzoo se namaz ada ki ja sakti hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/90, Fathul-Wahhab:1/21). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Sunnat gusl masalan Jumu'ah ke gusl me ki gai Wuzoo se namaz Ada karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar Wuzoo Farz ki niyyat se kare aur nawaqiz-e-Wuzoo pesh na aayi to us Wuzoo se namazen padh sakte hain (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/53). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Farz Gusl ke baad agar koi shakhs Wuzoo na kare to kya woh bila Wuzoo kiye usi gusl se namaz Ada kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Chunke gusl yeh badi taharat hai aur Wuzoo choti taharat, lihaaza agar koi Farz Gusl ke dauran Wuzoo na kare aur gusl ke daoran nawaqiz-e-Wuzoo (Wuzoo todne wali cheezon) se bache to usi farz gusl (Badi taharat) me Wuzoo (Choti taharat) dakhil ho jaayegi, aur gusl se faraagat ke baad wo baWuzoo shumaar hoga, albatta sunnat gusl me Farz ki niyyat se Wuzoo na kare to is sunnat gusl se namaz nahi padh sakte, kyunke sunnat gusl me Farz Wuzoo dakhil nahi hota (Al-Majmoo':2/224-225). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

So Kar Uthne Ke Baad Ehtilaam (Wet Dream) Ke Shak Par Gusl

Sawal: Kisi shakhs ko neend se bedaar hone ke baad kapdon par geele dhabbe nazar aaye lekin use neend me ehtilaam ho jaane ka ehsas nahi huwa to kya us par gusl Farz hoga jab ke alamat se zaahir ho ke yeh mani ke dhabbe Hain?

Jawab: Alamat se waazeh hai aur yaqeen bhi hai ke yeh mani (sperm) hai to gusl karna Farz hoga chahe ehtilaam hone ka ehsas huwa Ho ya nahi (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/83). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar kisi ne khwab me dekha ke ehtilaam ho gaya lekin bedaar hone ke baad use ehtilaam ki koi 'alaamat nazar na aayi to kya is par gusl Farz hoga?

Jawab: Ji Nahi, is soorat me uspar gusl Farz nahi hoga (Al-Majmoo':2/142). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Ehtilaam (Wet Dream) Aur Biwi Se Sohbat Par Gusl Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar kisi ko raat me ehtilaam ho jaaye phir woh biwi se sohbat (Humbistari) kare to use gusl me kya niyyat karni hogi? Sirf janaabat, sirf ehtilaam ya dono ke gusl ki?

Jawab: Farz gusl ke qasd ke saath koi ek niyyat kare tab bhi kaafi hoga (Kifaayatul Akhyar:1/42). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Husband & Wife Ke Barhana (Necked) Sone Par Gusl Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya khawind (husband) aur biwi ke mahaz barhana (necked) sone par gusl waajib hoga?

Jawab: Mahaz barhana sone ki wajah se gusl waajib nahi hota jab tak ke dukhool na huwa ho ya mani ka Khurooj na huwa ho, albatta agar aapas me bos-wa-kinar (kissing) ki wajah se mani khaarij ho to gusl waajib hoga aur agar mazi khaarij ho jaaye to jism ya kapde ke jis hisse par lag jaaye use dhona zaroori hoga aur gusl ke bagair sirf Wuzoo karke namaz ada karna durust hoga. (Bidayatul Muhtaaj:1/149). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Condom Pehen Kar Humbistari Karne Se Gusl Ke Waajib Hone Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya Condom pehen kar humbistari karne se gusl waajib hoga, agarche mani (Sperm) ka khurooj na ho?

Jawab: Gusl waajib hone ki ek soorat to yeh hai ke mard ya 'aurat ka maadda-e-manviya khaarij hojaaye aur doosri soorat yeh hai ki mard ki sharmgaah ke Hashfa (Sharmgaah ka agla ubhra huwa hissa jise Supari kaha jaata hai:"glans") ka ya Hashfa cut chuka hai to kati hui sharmgaah ke Hashfa ke baqadr hissa ka 'aurat ki sharmgaah me daakhil hona, chahe mani nikle ya na nikle, gusl farz ho jaata hai condom pehenne ki soorat me dukhool paya jaata hai, isliye is kaifiyat se humbistari karne se bhi mard aur 'aurat dono par gusl waajib ho jaayega. (Nihaayatul Matlab:1/149). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ki Andarooni (Internal) Sonography Se Gusl Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat ki andarooni (Internal) sonography ki gai to kya us par gusl farz hoga?

Jawab: Nahi. Gusl Farz nahi hoga. (Al-muhimmat:2/252). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Aurat Ki Sharmgaah Me Dawai (Medicine) Rakhne Se Gusl Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ki sharmgaah me bataure ilaj kisi qism ki goli ya dawai (medicine) rakhne se gusl waajib hoga?

Jawab: 'Aurat par Gusl-e-Janaabat ke waajib hone ke liye mard ki sharmgaah (Hashfah ke baqadr Hissa – uzwe Tanaasul ki Supari/Agla ubhara huwa hissa) ka 'aurat ki sharmgaah me dakhil hona shart hai, iske 'alaawah kisi aur cheez masalan ungli ya Ala (instrument) wagayrah ke daakhil hone ya karne ki wajah se gusl waajib nahi hoga, albatta jab dawa (medicine) ki kuch miqdaar baahar aajayegi to Wuzoo toot jaayega. (Al-muhimmat: 2/252, Al-Minhajul-Qaweem: 1/36). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bandage, Plaster Par Wuzoo Aur Gusl

Sawal: Agar aazaye wuzoo (parts of ablution) ki jagah par plaster kiya huwa ho to wuzoo ke liye kya kare?

Jawab: Agar aazaye Tayammum ke 'alaawah deegar aazaye Wuzoo par plaster ho jise mukammal taharat haasil karne ke baad bandha ho to baqi jitne hisse par plaster na ho usko dhoye aur phir tayammum kare (Al-Minhajul-Qaweem: 1/61). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar Aazaye Wuzoo ki jagah plaster kiya huwa ho aur gusl waajib ho jaaye to kya kare?

Jawab: Pehle sahih saalim badan ko farz gusl ki niyyat se dho le phir plaster wale hisse par masah kar ke tayammum kare (At-T'aliqatul Qazi Husain: 1/433). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar wuzoo ke kisi 'uzwa par bataure ilaj patti (Bandage) lagaee gai ho to taharat aur namaz ka kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Chand sharait ke saath uski taharat aur namaz sahih ho jaayegi.

Sawal: Agar haath ki ungli ka naakhun kata ho aur us par batur-e-ilaj patti lagaya ho to Wuzoo aur namaz ka kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Haath me kohni (elbow) ke upar Patti ho to wahi mas-alah hai Jo abhi upar zikr kiya gaya hai. Lekin kohni ke andar hai jaha taymmum me mitti pohchayi jaati hai to wuzoo kar ke namaz to padh le, lekin uska hisab rakhe aur shifayabi ke baad qaza karle (Asnal-Matalib:1/82). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar haath ki ungli ka naakhun kata ho aur Patti naapaaki ki haalat me bandhi ho to?

Jawab: Tayammum sahih nahi hoga. Patti kholna zaroori hai. Is Shart ke saath ke Patti kholne se us zakhm ke badh jaane ya us 'uzwa ke kharab hone ka andesha na ho. Warna patti usi haalat par rakh kar Patti ke ird gird ka hissa dho kar patti par masah kar ke namazen ada kare aur shifayabi ke baad namazon ka i'aadah kare (ya'ani dobara padhe). (Asnal-Matalib: 1/82). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jabeerah (Bandage, Plaster) Ke Ahkaam Wa Masa-il

(1) Saahib-e-jabeerah wuzoo ya gusl karte waqt jabeerah par paani se masah karne ke saath saath ba-sahulat agar tayammum kar sakta ho to tayammum zaroori hai, aur agar tayammum karne mein mashaqqat Laahiq ho to jabeerah par sirf paani ka masah kaafi hai, tayammum zaroori nahi. Aur us shakhs ko paak hi samjha jaayega.

(2) Saahib-e-jabeerah hatta al-maqdoor (jitna mumkin ho) tahaarat ke husool ki koshish kare, lekin jabeerah baandhne se pehle tahaarat ke husool mein mashaqqat-e-shadeedah Laahiq ho ya tahaarat ke baqi rakhne mein dushwari ho to aysi haalat mein patti bandhne se pehle tahaarat shart nahi hai.

(3) Jin sooraton mein namaz ka i'aadah wajib hai, un sooraton mein agar namaz ka i'aadah mein mashaqqat na ho to i'aadah wajib hai, aur agar mashaqqat ho to i'aadah wajib nahi hai. (1st Fiqh-e-Shafi'ee Seminar, 2019, Taloja).

Mozon (Socks) Par Masah

Sawal: Kya mozon par masah ke liye chamde ke moze (leather socks) hona zaroori hai? Aur kya kapde ke mozon par masah sahih nahi hota?

Jawab: Masah ke liye mozon ka aisa hona zaroori hai jin me dono pair takhne ke saath chup sake, neez moze mazboot ho is taur par ke musaafir ke utarne aur sawar hone ke waqt baqadre zaroorat bagair chappal ke khalis moze pehen kar musalsal (continue) chal sake ya'ani chalna aasaan bhi ho aur musalsal chalne ki wajah se moze bhi na phate, itne mazboot ho, isi tarah moze aise ho ke paani in me sarayat na kar sake ya'ani mozon me pani absorb na ho. Jin mozon me yeh sharait payi jaaye un par masah durust hai, bazaar me milne wale kapdon ke mozon me yeh sharten paai nahi jaati is liye un par masah sahih nahi hai, aur chamde ke mozon me 'umooman yeh sharte paayi jaati hain is liye un par masah sahih hai (Gaayatul Bayan: 1/50). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Sawal: Agar koi chamde ke moze bina Wuzoo ke pehne to kya Wuzoo me uspar bagair pair dhoye masah karna sahih hoga?

Jawab: Mozon par masah ke jaaiz hone ke liye shart yeh hai ke dono moze mukammal paaki haasil karne ke baad pehne ho ya'ani gusl aur Wuzoo ki zaroorat hoto gusl aur Wuzoo se mukammal faarig hone ke baad pehne ho, lihaaza agar moze bagair Wuzoo kiye huwe pehne ho to uspar masah karna sahih nahi hoga. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 1/202). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Tayammum Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Agar Wuzoo ya gusl ke liye paani na ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Agar Wuzoo ya gusl ke liye paani na mile to tayammum kare. Chehra aur dono haathon par makhsoos sharait ke saath wuzoo aur gusl ya kisi 'uzwa par patti bandhi hui hone ki soorat me niyyat aur qasd ke saath chehra aur dono hathon par mitti pahunchane ko tayammum kehte hain (Asnal-Matalib: 1/72, Al-Aziz:1/196). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kin Halaat me tayammum karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar paani isti'emaal karna dushwar ho jaaye to tayammum karna jaaiz hai. Is majboori ke chand asbaab hain:

(1) Safar me talash karne ke bawajood paani na mile. (2) Paani tak jaane me jaan ya maal par khauf wa khatra ho. (3) Pyas jaise umoor ke liye paani ki zaroorat ho. (4) Paani ke muta-'alliq la ilmi ki wajah se paani mayassar na ho. (5) Beemaari badhne ka andesha ho. (6) Jabeera baandhna (Haddi Tootne par plaster aur zakhm par kabhi patti chipkane ki zaroorat ho (Al-Aziz:1/196). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Tayammum ke arkaan kya hain?

Jawab: Tayammum ke arkaan saat (7) hain:

(1) Paak, Khaalis, aur gair-musta'ammul (un-used) mitti ka hona. (2) Mitti ka qasd wa iraadah karna, ya'ani apne iraade se mitti muntaqil karna. (3) Jis se masah karna hai use aazaye tayammum (chehra aur dono hath) ki taraf muntaqil karna. (4) Niyyat (Namaz wagayrah umoor ke mubaah hone ki niyyat karna is tarah ke niyyat mitti ke muntaqil karne se lekar chehra ka masah shuru' hone tak baaqi rahe). (5) Chehra ka masah karna. (6) dono hathon ka masah karna. (7) Tarteeb ya'ani pehle chehre ka phir hathon ka masah karna (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/94). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya ek tayammum se do namazen ada kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Ek tayammum se sirf ek farz namaz ada kar sakte hain albatta usi tayammum se sunnaten jitni chaahe ada kar sakte hain (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/96). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya ek tayammum se imaam mukammal Jumu'ah (ya'ani dono khutbe aur namaz) ada kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Jumu'ah ka khateeb Jumu'ah ke khutba aur namaz ko ek tayammum se ada nahi kar sakta kyunki namaz ki tarah khutba bhi farz hai, ek tayammum se sirf ek farz ada kar sakte hain lihazaa khutbe ke liye alag tayammum aur namaze jumu'aa ke liye alag tayammum karna zaruri hai (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 1/219). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Tayammum kin cheezon se toot-taa hai?

Jawab: Wo asbaab (reasons) Jin se Wuzoo toot jaata hai tayammum bhi toot jaayega. Isi tarah paak paani ke isti'emaal par qudrat haasil hona is tarah ke bimar shakhs namaz ki adaigi se qabl sihhatyaab ho jaaye to tayammum baatil ho jaayega, isi tarah paani maujood na ho aur tayammum kiya ho, ya paani ko haasil karne me jaan ya maal ko khatra ho aur tayammum kiya ho, isi tarah paani ki zaroorat hone ki soorat ya in jaisi sooraton me tayammum kiya ho aur namaz shuru' karne se pehle paani par qudrat ka wehem bhi haasil ho to tayammum baatil hoga bashart yeh ke paani ke isti'emaal se koi rukaawat na ho (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/97). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Baraf Baari (Snowfall) Wale 'Alaqe Me Tayammum

Sawal: Agar kisi 'alaqe me baraf baari (snowfall) hoti hai aur sakht Sardi jo naaqabile bardaasht hoti hai to kya aysi soorat me tayammum ki gunjaish hai?

Jawab: Fuqahaa-e-kiraam hadees ki roshni me farmate hain ke shadeed thandi ke waqt tayammum karna jaaiz hai lekin yeh us waqt hai jabke thande paani ke isti'emaal se jaan ki halaqat ka khadshah ho ya jism ke kisi 'uzwa ke kharab aur bekar hone ka khatra ho ya faalij ka khatra ho aur paani garam karne par qudrat bhi na ho, yeh halaat us waqt pesh aate hain jab insaan safar me ho, 'aam halaat me to insaan apne ghar par hi hota hai jahaan paani garam karne par use qudrat haasil hoti hai to is soorat me uske liye paani garam kar ke Wuzoo aur gusl karna hi zaroori hoga tayammum karna sahih nahi hoga (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 1/282).

Hazrat Amr bin Aas (RA) farmate hain ke Gazwa-e-zaatus-salaasil ki ek thandi raat me mujhe ehtilaam (wet dream) huwa aur mujhe dar laga ke agar mai ne gusl kar liya to mai mar jaunga, chunanche main ne tayammum kar ke apne saathiyon ko fajr ki namaz padhai to logon ne Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se iska zikr kiya to Aap ﷺ ne farmaya: Amr! Tum ne janaabat ki haalat me apne saathiyon ko fajr ki namaz padhai? Maine Aap (ﷺ) ko gusl na karne ka sabab batlaya aur kaha ke "Main ne Allah ka yeh farman suna hai ke tum apne aap ko qatl na karo beshak Allah tum par reham karne wala hai." yeh sun kar Rasoolullah (ﷺ) muskuraye aur kuch nahi kaha. (Abu Daud:335). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haiz Ka Khoon Aane Ki Kam Se Kam 'umr, Kam Se Kam Aur Ziyadah Se Ziyadah Muddat

Sawal: Shar'an haiz ke shuru' hone ki Kam se kam 'umr kitni hai? Neez har maah haiz ke aane ki Kam se kam aur ziyadah se ziyadah Muddat kitni hoti hai?

Jawab: Haiz ke shuru' hone ki Kam se kam 'umr taqreeban naw (9) saal hai, aur haiz shuru' hone ke baad har maah kam se kam ek din aur ek raat aa sakta hai aur ziyadah se ziyadah Pandhrah 15 raaten aur 15 din aa sakta hai (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/360). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Kale Rang Ki Spotting

Sawal: Kuch 'auraton ko daurane haiz pehle 4-5 din blooding (khoon behta hai) aur baad me khoon girna band ho jaata hai magar agle 3-4 din kale rang ki Spotting hoti hai, is ka shumaar kis me hoga?

Jawab: Is khoon ka shumaar bhi haiz hi me hoga aur haiz ke ahkam jaari rahenge. (Sharhu Saheeh Al-Bukhari Li Ibne Battal:1/456, Al-Bayan: 1/350). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khoon-E-Haiz Ke Kapdon Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ko Khoon-e-Haiz ke kapde dhokar phenkna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Khoon-e-Haiz ka kapda phenkna zaroori nahi balke bawaqte zaroorat paak saaf dhokar isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/46). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Pandra (15) Din Se Ziyadah Haiz Ka Khoon

Sawal: Agar pandra din se ziyadah Khoon-e-Haiz aaye to is khoon ko kya shumaar karenge?

Jawab: Pandra roz ke baad jo khoon aayega wo Istihaza ya'ani beemaari ka khoon shumaar hoga, is me namaz wagayrah mu'aaf nahi aur haalate haiz ke saare mamnoo'aa (prohibited) umoor bhi jaaiz hain. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/369, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/80). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Janaabat Ke Gusl Se Pehle Haiz Ka Shuru' Hona

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat par janaabat ka gusl farz ho jaaye aur gusl se pehle hi use haiz shuru' ho jaaye:

Jawab: Janaabat ke baad agar haiz shuru' ho jaaye phir farz gusl haasil nahi ho sakta jab tak ke haiz ka khoon khatam na ho.

Wilaadat Ka Khoon 40 Din Se Kam Me Band Hona

Sawal: Wilaadat ka khoon (Dum-e-Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding)) chalees din se kam me band ho jaaye to kya use gusl kar ke namaz padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Dum-e-Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki kam se kam muddat ek Lehza (Thodi der – ek second bhi) aur ziyadah se ziyadah muddat saath din hai aur gaalib muddat chalees roz hai, is muddat ke darmiyan jab bhi khoon band ho jaaye to gusl karke namazen shuru' karna zaroori hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/83). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) Ka Khoon Chalees Din Se Ziyadah

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat ko wilaadat ke chalees din guzar jaane ke baad bhi Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ka khoon aa raha ho to us waqt us 'aurat ke liye kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Bachhe ki paidaish ke baad 'aurat ko jo khoon aata hai use “Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding)” kehte hain. Uski ziyadah se ziyadah muddat saath (sixty days:60) din hai aur gaalib muddat chalees din hai ya'ani 'umoomi taur par 'auraton ko chalees din khoon aata hai aur kam se kam muddat ek Lehza ya'ani ek second hai, agar khoon chalees dino ke baad bhi jaari rahe to saath dino tak wo Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) hi ka khoon shumaar hoga aur wo 'aurat hukman naapaak shumaar hogi, saath dino se tajawuz karne ki soorat me ab yeh khoon istihazah ya'ani beemaari ka khoon shumaar hoga. Ab is soorat me wo 'aurat hukman paak hogi, lihaaza ab wo 'aurat paki ki niyyat se gusl karegi. Ramdhan ke ayyam chal rahe ho to roza bhi rakhegi aur har farz namaz ke liye safai karke sharmgaah par paak kapda baandh kar baWuzoo ho kar namazen ada karegi (Nihayatul Matlab:1/344). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haiz Ya Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) Ki Haalat Me Mehndi

Sawal: Kya 'aurat Haiz ya Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki haalat me mehndi laga sakti hai?

Jawab: Sog (shohar ke intiqal ke baad gam me rehna) wali 'aurat ke liye mehndi lagana haraam hai, uske 'alaawah shaadi shuda 'aurat ke liye tamaam halaat me chaahe paaki ho ya naapaaki mukammal hatho aur pairo me mehndi lagana masnoon hai, albatta ungliyon ke poron par ya hatho aur pairo par naqsh-o-nigari karna, kala khizab karna to yeh shohar ki ijaazat se jaaiz hoga warna nahi, jis 'aurat ka shohar na ho uske liye ihram ki haalat ke 'alaawah poore haath paanw me mehndi lagana makrooh hai aur naqsh o nigari karna to haraam hai (Al-Majmoo':3/140, Tuhfatul-Bari:3/223). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Naapaaki Me Qur-aan Aur Hadees Padhna

Sawal: 'Aurat agar haddas (naapaaki) ki haalat me hai to kya wo hadees ki kitaab padh sakti hai?

Jawab: Haddas-e-Akbar (Haiz, Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding), Janaabat) ki halat me Qur-aan ko choona aur padhna mamnoo' (prohibited) hai, Haddas-e-Asgar (wuzoo ka toot jana) me Qur-aan bagair chooye padh sakti hai, isi tarah Qur-aan ke 'alaawah deegar kitaaben in dono Haddas ke pesh aane ki soorat me padhna mamnoo' (prohibited) nahi agar us kitaab me Qur-aan ki aayatein ho to unhe na padhe (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/77). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kisi kitaab ke mutala'a (reading) ke dauran agar Qur-aan ki koi aayat aaye to haiza ya junubi 'aurat kya kare?

Jawab: Daurane mutala'a (reading) agar Qur-aan ki aayat aa jaaye to aysi 'aurat use dil hi me padh le zubaan se uska talaffuz na kare (Al-Majmoo':2/358). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Haddas-e-Akbar me bagair haath lagaye Qur-aan ki tilaawat ka kya Hukm hai?

Jawab: Qur-aan ki tilaawat chahe dekh kar ya bagair dekhe ho, haath me lekar ho ya bagair haath lagaye ho Haddas-e-Akbar ki Haalat me mamnoo' (prohibited) hai albatta Haddas-e-Asgar me bagair chooye Qur-aan ki tilaawat kar sakte hain (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/78). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Daurane Haiz Aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) Me 'Aurat Ka Bahar Jana

Sawal: Hamare 'alaqe ki 'aurato me yeh mash'hoor hai ke haiz aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki halat me ghar se bahar nahi ja sakte hatta ke kisi ki mayyit par, taziyat ke liye aur isi tarah 'iyaadat (visiting the sick) ke liye bhi nahi ja sakte kyunke in ayyam me 'aurat naapaak hoti hai aur naapaak jism par jinnat ka jald asar hota hai aur isi tarah jitne naapaak qadam zameen par lagte hain utne gunah ziyadah hote hain. Is ki shara'an kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Yeh sab aglaatul 'awaam (people's miscoceptions) me se hain. Haqeeqat yeh hai ke haiz aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki haalat me 'aurat Hukman naapaak hoti hai ya'ani in haalat me wo namaz nahi padh sakti, Qur-aan nahi padh sakti aur na hi choo sakti hai, isi tarah masjid me bhi nahi thahar sakti, Aazaye makhsusa ke 'alaawah 'auraton ka zahiri jism paak hota hai. Lihaaza shara'ee hudood ka lihaz rakh kar aur masnoon du'aaon ke ehtimam ke saath aur parda ki pabandiyo me reh kar in dino me ghar se bahar ja sakti hai. (Saheeh Muslim: 298, Al-Bayan:1/338). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Naapaaki Ki Haalat Me Zere Naaf Baal Nikaalna

Sawal: Kya naapaaki (Haddas-e-Akbar) ki Haalat me Zere Naaf Baal nikaal sakte hain?

Jawab: Farz Gusl se pehle jism ka koi hissa (Baal, Nakhun) wagayrah jism se alag karna jaaiz to hai lekin munasub nahi (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj fi Sharhil-Minhaaj wa Hawaashi Ash Sharwaani Wa 'Abbaadi: 4/56). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bahut Jald Manzar-E-'Aam Par Aane Wali Kitaab 📘 "Islami Bank-Kaari: Haqiqat, Zarurat Aur Ishqalaat Ka Izaalah"

Aaj ke daur mein jab ma'eeshat ka pahiyyah soodi nizaam ke gird ghoom raha hai, aisey mein Islami Bank-kaari sirf ek mutabadil nahi balke aik shara'ee taqaza bhi hai.

Isi aham zarurat ke pehlu ko madde nazar rakhte hue Jami'aa Zia-ul-'Uloom Kundloor – Karnataka ke Shu'bah Tadreebe Iftah ke ek taalib-e- 'ilm Maulavi 'Usman Sadi Papa ko yeh mauzoo' baraye tahqeeq diya gaya tha. Unhon ne apne ustad Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Mahmood Barmare Husaini aur Maulana Jasim Mahadik ki nigrani mein is mauzoo' par gehre mutaala'aah, 'ilmi tajziye aur mustanad dalaeelon ke saath ek jaame'e maqalah tahreer kiya tha.

Yeh maqalah na sirf Islami Bank-kaari ki bunyadon aur usoolon ko wazeh karta hai balke un ishqalaat aur e'etiraazaat ka mudallal aur 'ilmi jawab bhi pesh karta hai jo 'aam taor par logon ke zehno mein paida hote hain.

Ab yeh maqalah kuch zaroori aur jadeed masa-il ke izafe ke saath, 'awaami mafaad ke pehlu ko dekhte hue, kitaabi shakal mein shaaye'e kiya jaayega taake 'aam log bhi Islami Bank-kaari ke usooolon ko samajh sakein aur is ke muta'alliq paaye janae wale ishqalaat aur galat fahmiyon ka izaalah kar sakein.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

AZAAN AUR IQAAMAT KE MASA-IL

Azaan Wa Iqaamat Ki Ahmiyat

Sawal: Farz namaz ke liye azaan aur iqaamat ki kya ahmiyat hai?

Jawab: Azaan aur iqaamat sunnat 'alal kifayah hai (koi ek anjaam de to baqi logon se iska mutalaba saaqit / khatam ho jaayega), paancho waqt ki farz namazon ke liye chahe namaz jamaa-'at ke saath padhi ja rahi ho ya koi tanha (alone) padh raha ho. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/115). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bagair Wuzoo Ke Azaan Dena

Sawal: Kya bagair wuzoo ke azaan sahih hoti hai?

Jawab: Bagair wuzoo azaan dena jaaiz aur sahih hai, kyunke azaan deegar azkaar ki tarah ek zikr hai aur zikr karne ke liye tahaarat (paaki) shart nahi, albatta sunnat aur afzal yahi hai ke baWuzoo azaan di jaaye. Bila zaroorat bagair wuzoo azaan dena makrooh hai. (Al-Umm:1/105). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Masjid Me Haalate Janaabat Me Azaan

Sawal: Mu'azzin masjid me taakheer (delay) se aaye aur fajr ki azaan ka waqt ho jaaye aur imaam sahab haalate janaabat me ho aur masjid me dusra koi azaan dene wala maujood na ho to kya imaam Sahab haalate janaabat me azaan de sakte hain?

Jawab: Masjid me imaam ke liye haalate janaabat me azaan dena jaaiz nahi hai. Albatta masjid se bahar sakht karaahat ke saath jaaiz hai.

Chunanche Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Beshak mai masjid ko Haizah (Menstruation) 'aurat aur kisi junubi ke liye Halaal nahi karta.” (Sunane Abu Dawood: 232, Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah: 1/148). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Tape Recorder Se Record Shuda Azaan Dena

Sawal: Tape Recorder se record shuda azaan dene ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Azaan shi'aar-e-islam (Bunyadi 'alaamat, pehchan) me se hai, aur mumayyiz (Jo achche aur bure me tameez kar sake) Mard azaan ke kalimaat kahe yeh shart hai, lihaaza tape recorder ke zariye di gai azaan mu'atabar nahi hogi, yeh qadiyani firqa ki iejaad (paida kiya huwa) aur un ka tareeqa hai aur qadiyani firqa Ba'ittifaqe 'Ulama-e-Haqq Wal Muslimeen Kafir hain ('Ujalatul Muhtaaj: 1/190, Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 6/66-67). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ke Liye Azaan Aur Iqaamat Ka Hukm

Sawal: 'Auraton ko farz namaz ke liye azaan aur iqaamat ka kya Hukm hai?

Jawab: 'Auraton ke liye sirf Iqaamat mustahab hai, azaan mustahab nahi, lekin agar koi 'aurat zikr ki niyyat se dheemi aawaz me azaan ke alfaz kehti hai is tarah ke sirf 'auratain sun saken to yeh makrooh nahi aur naa hi mustahab hai, albatta buland aawaz se azaan dena ya sharai azaan ki niyyat se azaan dena haraam hai (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 1/299). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Infiraadi (Individual) & Ijtimaa'i Namaz Me Azaan Aur Iqaamat

Sawal: Kya infiraadi (individual) (Tanha) farz namaz padhte waqt iqaamat padhna zaroori hai? Agar koi na padhe to kya mas-alah hoga?
