Jawab: Jamaa-'at ki namaz ke liye Iqaamat kehna sunnate kifaya hai aur Infiraadi (individual) namaz padhne wale ke liye sunnat-e-ain hai lihaaza agar iqaamat kehna bhool jaaye ya jaan boojh kar chod de to namaz ki Sihhat par koi asar nahi padega lekin jaan boojh kar chodna napasandida aur makrooh hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/120). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Office Me Namaz Ke Liye Azaan Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kisi namaz ka waqt ho aur office me do ya teen log mil kar namaz jamaa-'at ke saath padhna chahe to kya namaz ke liye azaan dena zaroori hai?

Jawab: Agar kisi office ya company me char (4) log mil kar jamaa-'at ke saath ya koi tanha shakhs namaz padhna chahta ho to un ke liye azaan dena sunnat hai, agarche un logon ko koi doosri azaan sunayi di ho (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 2/195). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qaza Namaz Ke Liye Azaan Aur Iqaamat

Sawal: Qaza Namaz ke liye azaan aur Iqaamat ka kya Hukm hai?

Jawab: Musalsal kayi (bahut) namazon ki qaza kar rahe hain to sirf pehli namaz ke shuru' me azaan kahe lekin iqaamat har namaz ke liye kahe (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/121). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jama'a Bainas-Salatain Me Azaan Aur Iqaamat

Sawal: Safar ya baarish ki wajah se jama'a Bainas-Salatain kare to azaan aur Iqaamat ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Safar ya baarish ki wajah se jama'a bainas-salatain kare to jama'a taqdeem ho ya taakheer, pehle namaz ke shuru' me azaan kahe lekin iqaamat dono namazon ke liye kahe (Al-Bayan: 2/61). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Azaan Ke Baad Darood Shareef Padhna

Sawal: Azaan ke baad Darood Shareef padhne ka kya Hukm hai?

Jawab: Azaan ke baad khud Mu'azzin aur azaan sunne wala dono ke liye azaan ke baad ki du'aa se pehle Nabiye Kareem ﷺ par Darood wa Salaam aahista aawaz me padhna masnoon hai aur Darood wa Salaam ke baad azaan ke baad ki du'aa padhna masnoon hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/121). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Azaan Ke Baad Masjid Se Bahar Nikalne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya azaan ke baad Masjid se bahar jana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar koi Shakhs masjid me maujood ho aur azaan ho jaaye to bila kisi 'uzr ke namaz ada kiye bagair masjid se bahar nikalna makrooh hai. (Al-Majmoo': 2/149, Sunan At-Tirmizi:204, Hasan Saheeh). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Aandhi, Toofaan, Sailaab Aur Salaatul Istisqa Me Azaan

Sawal: Kya aandhi, toofaan ya sailaab ya wabaai amraz ke phailaw ke waqt me is niyyat se azaan dena ke azaab ya shadeed masaa'il khatam ho jaaye, kya aisa karna sahih hai?

Jawab: Fuqahaa-e-Shawafe' ne baaz musibaton, sakht haalat aur 'umoomi pareshaniyon ke waqt azaan dene ki gunjaish ko zikr kiya hai, un par qiyas karte huwe asbaab ke darje me aur sunnat hone ka aitiqaad na rakhte huwe azaan de to mubaah hona chahiye. Wallahu A'alam (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 1/461). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Salatul Is'tisqa ya'ani baarish maangne ke liye namaz me azaan di jati hai?

Jawab: Salatul Is'tisqa me azaan nahi hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/196). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sakht Baarish Me Fajr Ki Azaan

Sawal: Madinah Munawwara me bahut ziyadah baarish hone ki wajah se fajr ki azaan me “Hayya alal-falaah” ki jagah “Sallu fi buyutikum” kaha gaya. Jis ka matlab apne gharon me hi namaz padh lo. Kya yeh hamare pyaare Nabi ﷺ ki sunnat hai?

Jawab: Baarish ki raat ya sakht toofaan wa aandhi ki raat ho to azaan ke baad Ala Sallu fi Rihalikum ya Ala sallu fi buyutikum kehna mustahab hai, baazo ne azaan mukammal hone ke baad in alfaz ke izaafa ko behtar kaha hai aur baaz 'Ulama-e-shawafe' ne Hayya alas-salaah aur Hayya alal-falaah ko tark kar ke unki jagah in kalimaat ko padhna kafi samjha hai, Albatta in kalimaat ko Hayya alal-falaah ke baad kehna ziyadah behtar hai, uske Jawab me la haula wala quwata illa billah padhna mustahab hai.(Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/208). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

NAMAZ KE MASA-IL

Bachchon Ke Liye Namaz Ka Hukm

Sawal: Bachchon ke liye namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Bachchon ke sarparaston par yeh lazim hai ke jab unki 'umr 7 saal ho jaaye to unko namaz sikhaye aur namaz padhne ka hukm dey aur jab who 10 saal ki 'umr ko pohonch jaaye to namaz ke chodne par unhe maare (Al-Majmoo':3/10). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Makrooh Auqaat

Sawal: Sooraj tulu' (sunrise) hone ke waqt isi tarah ain zawaal ke waqt aur ain sooraj guroob hote waqt namaz padhna makrooh hai ya haraam?

Jawab: Makrooh-e-Tahrimi hai, ya'ani aisa makrooh jo haraam ke qareeb hai. (Asnal-Matalib:1/123). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Jahan tak mere 'ilm me hai, India me Tulu'-e-Aaftab ke waqt 15-20 minutes, Zawal ke waqt taqreeban 5 minutes aur guroob ke waqt 3 minutes aur is me ehtiyaatan 3 minutes ziyaadah, in auqaat me namaz nahi padh sakte, kya bairooni ya sab mamaalik me yeh time frame alag hota hai kyunke mai ne kisi se suna ke Kuwait me zawaal se Zuhr ki azaan ke waqt ke darmiyaan 25-40 minutes hota hai, main ne bahut pehle suna tha ke imaam Shafi'ee RA ke nazdeek Jumu'ah ke din Jumu'ah ki azaan zawaal se pehle bhi de sakte hain? Jumu'ah ki azaan zawaal se pehle di jaaye to Jumu'ah ki sunnat ka kya? Zawaal tak intizaar karna zaroori hai kya?

Jawab: (1) Tulu'-e-Shams ke baad taqriban 15-20 minutes waqte karaahat rehta hai. (2) Tulu', Guroob ke waqt poora sooraj nikalne ya Guroob hone tak waqte karaahat hai. (3) Zawaal ke waqt shayad 5 minutes se bhi kam mana'a rahega. (4) Zawaal se pehle Zuhr ya Jumu'ah ki na azaan sahih hai na namaz, albatta Jumu'ah ke din nawafil, mamnoo' (prohibited) waqt (Zawaal se pehle Istawa ke waqt) me bhi padh sakte hain (Fiqhul Manhaji: 1/110). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Makrooh-E-Tahrimi Waqt Me Farz Namaz

Sawal: Agar kisi ki fajr ki namaz qaza ho jaaye to kya Tulu'-e-Shams ke baad taqreeban 20 minutes tak jo Makrooh-e-Tahrimi waqt hota hai, is me namaz padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee RA ke nazdeek agar kisi shakhs ki fajr ki farz namaz qaza ho jaaye to us namaz ko ain Tulu'-e-Shams ke waqt bhi padh sakte hain, us ke liye 20 minutes intzar karne ki zaroorat nahi (Asnal-Matalib:1/124).

Chunanche Hazrat Anas RA se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolulallah ﷺ ne irshad farmaya: Jo shakhs koi namaz bhool jaaye ya wo namaz ke waqt so jaaye to uska kaffara yeh hai ke jab use yaad aajaaye wo us namaz ko fauran padh le. (Saheeh Muslim: 684). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haram Me Kisi Bhi Waqt Namaz Makrooh Nahi

Sawal: Kya Makka Mukarrama me makrooh auqaat me bhi namaz padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Makka Mukarrama ya'ani poore haram Shareef me kisi bhi waqt koi bhi namz padh sakte hain, chahe Tawaaf hi ki do rak'at kyun na ho aur chahe sabab wali ho ya sabab wali na ho, makrooh nahi (Asnal-Matalib:1/124). Mufti Faiz Ahmed bi Aadm Mulla

Namaz Ke Sharaait

Sawal: Namaz ki adaigi ke liye kya sharaait hain?

Jawab: Namaz sahi hone ke liye paanch (5) cheezen namaz me dakhil hone se pehle shart hai. (1) Chote Bade Haddas se badan ka paak hona ya'ani Wuzoo ki zaroorat ho to baWuzoo hona aur farz gusl ki zaroorat ho to gusl se farig hona. (2) Badan, kapdon aur jis jagah namaz padhna hai uska najasat se paak hona. (3) Satar ka chupana (4) Namaz ke waqt ke dakhil hone ka 'ilm hona. (5) Qiblah ki taraf rukh karna (Kifaayatul Akhyar:113).

Note: Mard ka satar naaf se lekar ghutne tak aur namaz me 'aurat ka satar poora jism hai siwaye chehra aur hatheli ke. (Minhaju-Taalibeen: 1/31). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Me 'Aurat Ka Kitna Jism Dhanpna

Sawal: 'auraton ko namaz me kitna jism dhanpna zaroori hai?

Jawab: 'aurat ka namaz me siwaye chehre aur hatheliyon ke mukammal badan satar hai. (Bidayatul Muhtaaj:1/266). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Bagair Topi Ke Namaz Ada Karna

Sawal: Kya bagair topi namaz padhna sahih hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek sar dhaank kar namaz padhna masnoon hai, kaahili, susti, laparwahi aur fashion ki wajah se bagair sir dhaanke nange sar namaz padhna makrooh hai aur khilafe adab hai. Iske bawajood namaz durust ho jaayegi, namazon ke 'alaawah baahar bazaaro me bhi khule sir ghoomna phirna khilafe muruwwat hai. (Bushral-Kareem:1/282, Zaadul Ma'aad fi Hadyi Khairil 'Ibaad:1/130). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Takhno Ke Neeche Pant Latka Kar Namaz Padhna

Sawal: Takhnon ke neeche pant latka kar namaz padhne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Takhnon ke neeche kapda, pant wagayrah latka kar namaz padhna agar takabbur ki wajah se ho to haraam hai aur agar is me takabbur na ho to makrooh hai, neez takhne dhanp kar padhi hui namaaz adaa to ho jati hai lekin us namaz ka kamil (complete) ajar wo sawaab nahi milta (Khulasatul ahkaam lin Nawawi: 1/ 331, Abu Dawood: 638, Mirqatul Mafateeh: 2/634, Al-Majmoo': 1/377). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Ba-Jamaa-'at Namaz Ke Liye Khade Hone Ka Waqt

Sawal: Farz Ba-jamaa-'at namaz ke liye kab khada hona chahiye?

Jawab: Ba-jamaa-'at namaz ke liye kab khada hona chahiye is baare me Ualma Hazraat ke ahaadees ki roshni me mukhtalif aqwaal hain.

Muqtadi Ka Imaam Se Aage Khada Rehna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs imaam se aage khada ho jaaye to kya us ki namaz durust hogi?

Jawab: Iqtidaa ki sharto me se ek shart yeh bhi hai ke muqtadi imaam se aage na badhe, ya to saath khada ho jaaye ya peeche. Lihaaza agar koi shakhs jaan boojh kar imaam se aage badh jaaye to us muqtadi ki namaz baatil ho jaayegi (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab:1/538). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ek martaba ek Kuwaiti ke peeche namaz padhne ke liye uske 2-4 inch peeche khada hua. Us ne kaha ke is tarah khada rehna bid'at hai. Dono ke pair ek hi Qataar (line) me ho. 'Ulama baraye meherbani rehbari farmaye.

Jawab: Iqtidaa ke sharaait me se yeh bhi hai ke muqtadi imaam ke age na badhe. Khade hone ki haalat me aidiyon (heel) se baithne ki haalat me sureeno (buttocks / hips) se. lihaaza ya to imaam ke saath saath khada ho ya kuch peeche kyun ke ehtiyatan yahi behtar hai taake be khayali me imaam se aage na badh jaaye aur namaz faasid (invalid) naa ho jaaye (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab:1/538). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Ki Niyyat Zubaan Se Ya Dil Se

Sawal: Namaz me zubaan se niyyat ke baare me kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Niyyat ki jagah Dil hai ya'ani dil se niyyat karna farz aur zaroori hai aur zubaan se is ke talaffuz karna mustahab hai, agar koi shakhs dil se niyyat kare aur zubaan se uske tallaffuz ada na kare tab bhi yeh kafi hai uski namaz sahih ho jaayegi (Al-Muhazzab:1/134). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Zuhr ki namaz ki dil se niyyat aur qasd karta hai aur zubaan se asar keh diya to kya us ki zuhr ki namaz sahih hogi?

Jawab: Niyyat me asal aitibar dil ka kiya jaata hai dil ki gaflat ke saath sirf zubaan se kehna kafi nahi, lihaaza dil se jis namaz ki niyyat kiya tha usi namaz ki niyyat shumaar hogi aur zubaan ke alfaz ka aitibar nahi hoga, lihaaza Soorate mas-uoolah me zuhr ki namaz sahih hogi (Fathul-'Aziz:3/263). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Me Do Pairon Ke Darmiyan Faasla

Sawal: Namaz me khade rehne ke liye do pairon ke darmiyan kitna faasla hona chahiye? Koi bahut faasla rakhta hai to koi bahut kam, kya har imaam ke nazdeek alag tareeqa hai? Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek konsa tareeqa sahih aur afzal hai?

Jawab: Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) jab bhi namaz ke liye khade hote to Sahaba-e-Kiraam ki taraf mutawajjah hote aur taakeed ke sath safon ko durust aur seedha karne ka hukum dete. Is ka asar Sahaba-e-Kiraam par yeh hota ke har ek doosray ke kandhay se kandha milata aur ediyon se ediyan milata taa ke shaitaan safon ke darmiyan ghus kar ek doosray ke dilon mein ikhtilaf aur nafrat paida na kare. Lihaza taqreeban tamam aimma-e-kiraam ke nazdeek namaz mein pairon ko milana aur chipkana makrooh hai. Hazrat Imam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek dono qadamon ke darmiyan ek balisht ke barabar faasla rakhna sunnat hai, is se ziyadah faslah (distance) rakhna makrooh hai, Ahnaf ke nazdeek chaar ungli ke baqadr faasla rakhna masnoon hai is miqdar se kam karna ya ziyadah karna makrooh hai. Imaam Malik (R) aur imaam Ahmed Bin Hanbal (R) ke nazdeek koi miqdar muta-'ayyan nahi, namazi mutawassit haalat me apni sahoolat se khada rahe yeh mandoob hai ya'ani na to pairon ko milaye aur na hi itna kushada rakhe ke fahash ma'aloom ho (Saheeh Al-Bukhari:719, Al-Majmoo': 3/431, Raddul Mohtar/Fiqhul 'Ibadaat Maliki/Al-Mugni Ibne Qudama Hambali). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Har Rak'at Me Ta'awwuz (أَعُوذُ بِاللّٰهِ مِنَ الشَّيْطَانِ الرَّجِيمِ ) Padhna

Sawal: Kya har Rak'at me ta'awwuz - A'uoozu billahi minasshaitanir-rajeem (أَعُوذُ بِاللّٰهِ مِنَ الشَّيْطَانِ الرَّجِيمِ) Padhna Sunnat hai?

Jawab: Namaz ki har rak'at me ta'awwuz (A'uoozu billahi minash shaitanir-rajeem) Padhna sunnat hai albatta namaz ki pehli rak'at me ta'awwuz padhne ka takidan hukm kiya gaya hai (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:1/188). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Namaz Me Taujeeh (Wajjahtu) Padhna

Sawal: Agar imaam ne surah fatiha shuru' ki aur hamari taujeeh khatam nahi hui to kya kare? Taujeeh mukammal kare ya Taujeeh ko padhna rok de aur imaam ki surah fatiha sune?

Jawab: Taujeeh ka padhna sunane Hai'aat me se hai. Agar imaam surah Fatiha padh raha ho to muqtadi (Imam ke peeche namaz padhne wala) apni taujeeh poori kar le aur agar soorat shuru' kiya ho aur muqtadi ko yaqeen ho ke taujeeh padh kar apni surah fatiha bhi padh sakta hai to taujeeh padhe warna taujeeh ko tark kar de aur apni surah fatiha mukammal kar le. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/372). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya sunnat namazon me bhi taujeeh (Wajjahtu) padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Sirf Janaze ki namaz me taujeeh nahi padhi jaayegi baqiyyah tamaam namazon me taujeeh padhna sunnat hai, jaan boojh kar namaz ki kisi sunnat ko chodna makrooh hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:2/125). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bagair Surah Fatiha Ke Namaz

Sawal: Agar koi Zuhr, Asar, Magrib ki teesri rak'at ya 'Isha ki teesri aur chauthi rak'at padh raha hai, lekin uska dhyan chala jaaye aur wo surah fatiha na padhe to kya uski namaz qabool hogi? Aur agar koi fajr, Magrib aur 'Isha (pehli do rak'at) padhte waqt, waqt milne ke bawajood surah fatiha na padhe?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek surah fatiha padhna farz hai surah fatiha padhe bagair namaz nahi hoti, lihaaza agar usne jaan boojh kar surah fatiha nahi padha tha to us ke namaz sahih nahi hogi, imaam ke ruku karne se pehle padhne ka mauqa mile to padh le wo rak'at aur namaz sahih hogi warna imaam se alag hone ki niyyat karke apni namaz mukammal karle aur agar kisi rak'at me surah fatiha padhna bhool jaaye aur imaam ke ruku karne se pehle padh sakta ho to padh le warna imaam ke salaam pherne ke baad us rak'at ka iaada kare(dohraye). (Asnal-Matalib:1/230). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi ne jis rak'at me bhool ki wajah se surah fatiha nahi padhi thi us rak'at ke imaam ke salaam ke baad fauran padhe to kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Ek poori rak'at padh le to us ki namaz sahih hogi (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/509). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi muqtadi jehri ya sirri namaz ke kisi rak'at me surah fatiha na padhe (Waqt na mile ya bhool jaaye ya koi aur waja ho) to kya imaam Shafi'ee ke maslak ke aitibar se wo rak'at shumaar hogi?

Jawab: Agar muqtadi masbooq (jis ki rak'at chooti ho) ho aur uski pehli rak'at me utna waqt na mil sake ke jis me wo apni surah fatiha mukammal kar sake aur Surah Fatiha mukammal na karne me wo kotaahi karne wala bhi nahi tha to wo namaz chahe sirri ho ya jehri jitni miqdaar padh sakta ho utni padh le baqiyyah ka imaam zamin hoga. Agar darmiyani rak'at ho aur imaam tez qiraat wala ho aur muqtadi sust raftaar ho to jaldi jaldi apni surah fatiha mukammal karne ki koshish kare agarche imaam ruku me chala jaaye aur ruku se uth khada ho, jaan boojh kar surah fatiha chod de to namaz nahi hogi, namaz ko dohrana zaruri hoga. Agar kisi rak'at mein Surah Fatiha padhna bhool jaye aur Tashahhud mein yaad aaye, to Imam ke salam pherne ke baad khada ho kar Masbooq ki tarah ek rak'at dohraye. Puri namaz dobara padhne ki zarurat nahi. (Asnal-Matalib:1/230). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hanafi Ya Hanbali Imaam Ke Peeche Surah Fatiha

Sawal: Kya Hanafi imaam ke peeche namaz ada karte waqt bhi surah fatiha padhna zaroori hai kyunke surah fatiha aur qiraat ke darmiyan bahut mukhtasar waqt hota hai.

Jawab: imaam Shafi'ee (RA) ke nazdeek har namaz ki har rak'at me surah fatiha padhna zaroori hai chahe imaam koi bhi ho (Al-Umm:1/129). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Hanafi ya Hanbali imaam ke peeche namaz ada karte waqt Shafi'ee muqtadi surah fatiha kab padhe?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (RA) ke nazdeek namaz ki har rak'at me surah fatiha padhna farz hai uske bagair wo rak'at shumaar nahi hoti. Lihaaza agar waqt mil sakta ho to Aameen ke baad surah fatiha padhe warna imaam ke saath saath bhi surah fatiha padh sakte hain. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:2/231). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Hanafi imaam ke peeche namaz padhte waqt aksar muqtadi ko apni karne aur padhne ki cheezein imaam ke baad karna chahiye siwaye tahiyyat aur du'aa ke to kya imaam ke saath surah fatiha padhna theek hai ya baad me padhna ziyadah behtar hoga jab imaam doosri surah padhe?

Jawab: Qur-aan ka hukm hai ke jab uski tilaawat ki ja rahi ho to us ko dhyan aur gaur se suno aur khaamoshi ikhtiyar karo (surah Aaraf:204) yeh hukm poore Qur-aan ke liye hai aur surah fatiha padhe bagair namaz nahi hoti aur hanafi imaam chunke waqfa ziyadah nahi dete lihaaza Shafi'ee muqtadi agar imaam ke saath saath apni surah fatiha padh le to yeh jaaiz hai (I'aanatut-Taalibeen:1/176). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Namaz Me Qur-aan Bagair Tarteeb Aur Ulta Padhna

Sawal: Agar koi namaz me Qur-aan tarteeb se na padhe to kya us ki namaz durust hogi? Jaise pehle Qul huwallahu Ahadd…padhe aur baad me Inna A'atainakal Kausar padhe.

Jawab: Namaz me Qur-aan-e-kareem ko tarteeb ke mutabiq tilaawat karna masnoon hai, tarteeb ki mukhalafat karna khilafe aula hai, uske bawajood uski namaz durust ho jaayegi. (Al-Majmoo':2/165). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Magrib ki namaz me doosri jamaa-'at me ek misri ne ek rak'at me Qul A'oozu birabbinnaas (قُلْ أَعُوْذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسْ) aur dursi rak'at me Iza ja'a (اِذَا جَاءَ ) padhi. Mai ne mere ek saathi ko kaha ke namaz me Qur-aan bagair tarteeb se padhna makrooh hai to us saathi ne kaha ke aaj misri Mu'azzin ne bhi is tarah padha hai. ek bar kuwaiti imaam sahab ne bhi padhaye the tab unhon ne kaha tha ke Huzoor (ﷺ) ne 'Eid ki namaz is tarah padhai thi.

Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek namaz me is tarah bina tarteeb ke padhna kaisa hai? Bhool se padhe to alag baat hai lekin Huzoor (ﷺ) ka wo ek bar 'amal ko daleel banana kaisa hai?

Jawab: Huzoor (ﷺ) se jahan jis tarah padhna saabit hai wahan usi tarah padha jaayega warna phir 'aam jo mamool hai ya'ani tarteeb aur doosri rak'at me pehli rak'at ke muqabil choti qiraat padhna uske khilaf karna khilafe aula hai (Al-Majmoo':3/385). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi namaz me ulta Qur-aan padhe ya'ani pehle minal jinnati wannaas padhe phir usse pehle wali aayat phir usse pehle wali aayat to kya is tarah sooraton ko padhne se uski namaz sahih hogi?

Jawab: Qur-aan-e-kareem ki aayat ko usi tarteeb se padhna zaroori hai jis tarteeb se Qur-aan-e-kareem me darj hai kyunke aayaton aur sooraton ki maujoodah tarteeb tauqifi (Allah ki taraf se muqarrar ki hui) hai. Har aayat baad wali aayat se marboot (judi hui) hai, agar tarteeb ko ulat diya jaaye to ma'anaa wa mafhoom bigad jaayega aur gunah lazim hoga, isi wajah se imaam Nawawi (R) wagayrah ne is tarah Qur-aan-e-kareem padhne ko shadeed mamnoo' (prohibited) kaha hai aur imaam qurtubi (R) ne to is tarah padhne ko haraam kaha hai, surah fatiha ki tamam aayaat ko tarteeb se padhna waajib hai, 'amadan (jaan boojh kar) (jaan bhooj kar) khilafe tarteeb padhne se qiraat baatil ho jaayegi, dobara padhna waajib hoga aur agar khilafe tarteeb ki soorat me maa'na (meaning) wa mafhoom badal jaaye to namaz baatil ho jaayegi (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:1/481,495 , Tafseer Qurtubi: 1/61). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sirri Namaz (Zuhr Ya Asr) Me Zor Se Tilaawat Karna

Sawal: Agar koi sirri (Zuhr ya Asr) me ba-aawaze buland qiraat kare to kya us ki namaz durust hogi?

Jawab: Sirri namazon me qiraat ko aahista padhna aur jahri namazon me qiraat ko buland aawaz se padhna sunane hai'at me se hai, jis ke tark ki soorat me namaz bhi durust aur sahih ho jaayegi aur sajda-e-sahwa se talafi ki bhi zaroorat nahi, lekin namaz ki sunnaton me se kisi sunnat ko jaan boojh kar chodna makrooh hai. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:2/150). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Namaz Me Thook Aur Balgam Ka Mas-alah

Sawal: Namaz padhte waqt khaansi ki wajah se mu me balgam utar aaya aur use thookna mumkin bhi tha lekin use nigal liya to kya aysi soorat me namaz baaqi rahegi ya toot jaayegi?

Jawab: Mazkoora soorat me namaz baatil ho jaayegi kyunke jab balgam mu me is had (limit) tak utar aaye ke use thookna mumkin ho to daaman ya aastin ya rumaal par thookna zaroori hai agar use nigal liya to namaz baatil ho jaayegi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/296). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Daurane Namaz Daant Se Kuch Khaee Hui Cheez Nikalna

Sawal: Namaz ke dauran daant se kuch khaee hui cheez nikal aaye aur wo nigal le to us namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Namaz me kuch khaane peene se namaz baatil ho jati hai, agar daurane namaz khaya huwa kuch daanton se nikal jaaye to us ko nigalna nahi chahiye balke us ko phenk dena chahiye, masjid jaisi jaghon par Tissue wagayrah me nikaal ke rakhna chahiye, agar phenkne par qudrat ke bawajood daanton se nikal kar halaq me utar jaaye to namaz baatil ho jaayegi, albatta gair iradi taur par andar chali jaaye to mu'aaf hai. Behtar aur ehtiyat yeh hai ke namaz se pehle mu ko achchi tarah saaf kare ya kulli (Khilal ki zaroorat ho to Khilal) kare. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 1/259). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Namaz Me Ziyadah Der Tak Khaamosh Rehna

Sawal: Kya namaz me ziyadah der tak khaamosh rehna namaz ko faut karta hai?

Jawab: Namaz me ziyadah der tak khaamosh nahi rehna chahe is khaamoshi me koi garz wabistah na bhi ho to us se namaz baatil nahi hoti (Minhajul Taalibeen:1/32). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Humne suna hai ke namaz me khaamosh rehne ke bajaaye us waqt Allah ka zikr kare (Teesra kalma wagayrah) masalan:(1) agar hamari tahiyyat, darood shareef aur du'aa khatam ho aur imaam ziyadah waqt le to us waqt khaamosh rehne ki bajaaye Allah ka zikr kare.

(2) Teen ya chaar rak'at wali namaz me do rak'at ke baad tahiyyat me agar imaam ziyadah waqt le to tahiyyat ke baad khaamosh rehne ki bajaaye darood shareef bhi padhna chahiye ya zikr kare. 'Ulama se guzarish hai ke batayen ke kya upar ke aamal sahih hain?

Jawab: Imaam ki jehri qiraat ke waqt ke 'alaawah kisi jagah muqtadi ko khaamosh rehna nahi chahiye ya to zikrullah karta rahe ya du'aa karta rahe ya darood shareef padhta rahe ya tilaawate Qur-aan karta rahe kyunke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaya ke in namazon me insaani kalaam munaasib nahi yeh sirf tasbeeh takbeer aur tilaawate Qur-aan hai. (Muslim: 537, Abu Dawood: 930). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Ke Dauran Nigahain

Sawal: Namaz ke dauran nigahain kahan honi chahiye?

Jawab: Namaz me khushu' ke husool ke liye tashahhud aur sajda ke 'alaawah mukammal namaz me nighahon ko sajde ki jagah rakhna masnoon hai isi tarah sajda me naak ki taraf aur tashahhud me apni god (Lap) ki taraf nazar rakhe aur jab shahaadat ki ungli uthai jaaye to shahaadat ki ungli par nigaah rakhe yeh masnoon hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/390). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sajda-E-Tilaawat Ke Baad Khada Hona Aur Phir Tilaawat

Sawal: Agar imaam ne fajr ki doosri rak'at me Aayate sajda par sajda kiya phir qiyam kar ke kuch der khaamosh reh kar bagair tilaawat ke ruku karke namaz mukammal kar li to kya namaz durust hogi?

Jawab: Agar koi namaz me aayate sajdah padhe to sajda tilaawat ke baad khada hona waajib hai, chahe aayat sajda soorat ke aakhir me ho ya darmiyan mein, bahar haal mustahab yeh hai ke khade hone ke baad kuch padhe phir ruku kare, albatta agar koi khade hone ke baad kuch padhe bagair hi ruku karta hai tab bhi jaaiz hai lekin is tarah karna gair mustahab hai lihaaza namaz to durust ho jaayegi. (Al-Majmoo':4/63). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sajda Ki Aayat Par Muqtadi Galati Se Ruku Kare

Sawal: Agar namaz ke dauran imaam sajda ki aayat padh kar sajda me jaaye aur peeche galati se muqtadi ruku samajh kar ruku me chala jaaye ya'ani sajda na karte huwe namaz me ek ruku ziyadah ho jaaye aysi soorat me muqtadi ki wo namaz ada ho jaayegi ya use namaz dohrana padegi?

Jawab: Us ki namaz durust hogi. Usko namaz dohrane ki ya sajda-e-sahwu karne ki zaroorat nahi. (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/166). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Teesri Ya Chauthi Rak'at Me Koi Surah Padhna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs infiraadi (individual) ya ba-jamaa-'at namaz me bhool kar teesri ya chauthi rak'at me surah fatiha ke baad koi surah padh le to sajda-e-sahwu ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Teen aur char rak'at wali namaz me ibtidayi do rak'at me surah fatiha ke baad koi doosri soorat milana aur padhna masnoon hai, albatta teesri aur chauthi rak'at me soorat padhna sunnat nahi lekin koi padh le to jaaiz hai is liye ke basa auqaat Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se bhi bayane jawaaz (Jaaiz hona) ke liye padhna saabit hai lihaaza agar koi teesri ya chauthi rak'at ke qiyam me soorat padh le to sajda-e-sahwu na kare, kyunke sajda-e-sahwu ke asbaab me yeh sabab dakhil nahi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/166). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Me Biwi Ya Kisi Na Mahram Ke Jism Ko Touch Karna

Sawal: Agar koi namaz padh raha ho aur daurane namaz biwi ya kisi na mahram ka haath, paanw ya jism ka koi hissa mas (touch) kare to Shafi'ee maslak ke e'etibaar se kya namaz ko mukammal kare, kya namaz hogi? Ya use namaz ko tod kar phir se Wuzoo kar ke namaz padhna hogi?

Jawab: Agar kisi mard ki chamdi (skin) kisi na mahram 'aurat ki chamdi se bila hayil (bagair kapde wagayrah) chooye to us se Wuzoo toot jaata hai aur namaz ke liye wuzoo shart hai, bagair Wuzoo ke namaz nahi hoti, mazkoora soorat me wo khud-ba-khud namaz se nikal gaya lihaaza dobara wuzoo karke namaz padhna zaroori hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/65). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Namaz Me Harkat

Sawal: Namaz me kis darja ki harkat se namaz toot jati hai? Masalan: agar kisi insaan ne apna phone neeche rakhkha huwa hai, aur daurane namaz us ka phone baja, aur usne neeche jhuk kar apna phone uthaaya use band kiya aur apni jaib me rakhkha to kya yeh durust hai?

Jawab: Jo 'amal aur harkat namaz ke aamal wa af'aal me se na ho agar wo kaseer (ziyadah) ho to namaz baatil hogi aur qaleel (kam) ho to namaz baatil nahi hogi (tutegi nahi). Qaleel wa kaseer ke farq ke liye 'urf-o-'aadat ki taraf ruju karenge, lihaaza jitni harkat ko 'umooman log kaseer tasawwur karte hain wo kaseer shumaar hogi aur namaz baatil hogi, jis harkat ko log qaleel tasawwur karte ho wo qaleel shumaar hogi aur namaz baatil nahi hogi. Khafeef (halki) aur choti harkaton se namaz baatil nahi hoti aur badi harkaton se namaz baatil ho jati hai, lihaaza soorate mazkoora me us ki namaz baatil ho jaayegi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/166). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ruku Me Kis Qadar Jhukna Chahiye

Sawal: Ruku me kis qadar jhukna chahiye?

Jawab: Ruku me is qadar jhukna waajib hai ke hatheliyan ghutnon tak pohonch jaayein aur mustahab tareeqa yeh hai ke sir aur peeth barabar seedh me humwaar rahe, chunanche Hazrat Wabisah Bin Ma'bad (R) se riwaayat hai farmate hain ke: “Main ne Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ko namaz padhte dekha jab Aap ruku karte to apni pusht aysi sidhee rakhte ke agar us par paani dala jaata to wahin ruk jata.” (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/133). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zameen Par Sajda Karne Se Aajiz Ho

Sawal: Jo aadmi khade hone par qaadir ho aur zameen par sajda karne se aajiz (majboor) ho to kursi par baithe bagair namaz padhne ka kya tareeqa hai?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs khade ho kar namaz padhne par qaadir ho magar peshani sajda ke liye zameen par rakhne aur peeth ko ruku ke liye ziyadah jhukane par qaadir na ho to ruku aur sajda ke liye apni garden (neck) ko apni taaqat ke baqadr jitna mumkin ho jhuka kar ruku aur sajda ka isharah kare, albatta ruku ke muqabale me sajda ke waqt garden (neck) kuch ziyadah jhukaye (Ash-Sharhul-Kabeer:3/468). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sajda Me Peshaani Ka Hissa Zameen Par Tekna

Sawal: Kya sajda me peshaani ka kuch hissa zameen par tekna kafi hoga?

Jawab: Peshaani ka kuch hissa sajda ki jagah se bagair hayil ke choo jaaye to sajda durust hoga. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/136). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar sajda me peshaani aur zameen ke darmiyan baal ya topi wagayrah hayil ho to kya sajda durust hoga?

Jawab: Sajda me peshani ka khula rakhna zaroori hai, agar koi aysi cheez peshani aur zameen ke darmiyan hayil ho jo namaz padhne wale ke jism se muttasil hai aur jo namazi ki harkat se harkat karti hai, masalan topi, baal, amama (turban) wagayrah to sajda durust nahi hoga. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/346). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Peshaani Par Zakhm Ki Wajah se Patti Par Sajda

Sawal: Agar kisi shakhs ki peshaani par zakhm ho jis ki wajah se patti bandhi hui ho jis ki wajah se sajda me peshaani ko zameen par tekna mushkil ho to kya is soorat me peshaani par teke bagair sajda karna durust hoga?

Jawab: Peshaani par agar zakhm ho aur zakhm par patti bandhi ho to us ke liye jaaiz hai ke zameen par peshaani teke bagair zakhm ki us patti par hi sajda kare is soorat me namaz durust hogi aur us namaz ka iaada bhi nahi hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/362). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Tashahhud Me Ungli Hilaana

Sawal: Kya tashahhud me ungli hilaate rehna sunnate rasool hai?

Jawab: Ahaadeese rasool se tashahhud me ungli ko harkat dena aur na dena balke sirf isharah karna dono saabit hai shawafe' ke nazdeek wo hadees raajeh (ziyadah sahi) hai jis me sirf isharah ka zikr hai, is liye shahadat ki ungli ko uthaakar sirf isharah karna masnoon hai, mustaqil harkat dete rehna masnoon nahi hai (Muslim shareef:58, An-Nasa'i:1268, Ibne Maajah: 754, Al-Majmoo':3/454, Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 309). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Me Salaam Ka Jawab Dena

Sawal: Agar koi ghar me namaz padh raha ho aur ghar me koi dakhil ho jaaye aur salaam kare, to aysi haalat me namaz padhne wale ko kya karna chahiye?

Jawab: Aysi soorat me Jawab dena waajib nahi, haath ke ishare se Jawab dena jaaiz hai, zubaan se Jawab dega to namaz toot jaayegi, behtar yeh hai ke baad az salaam (salaam pherne ke baad) zubaani Jawab de. (Fathul-Mu'een: 57). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Daurane Namaz Waalidain Ke Pukarne Par Namaz Todne Ka Hukm

Sawal: hadeese paak me warid hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaya kash meri maa zinda hoti, aur main 'Isha ki namaz ke liye musalle par khada hota aur surah fatiha shuru' kar chuka hota, udhar se mere ghar ka darwaza khulta aur meri maa pukarti: Muhammed! To main un ke liye namaz tod deta aur main kehta: Labbaik aye maa.

Is hadees ko bunyad bana kar aksar logon ki soch hai ke maa ka maqam itna bada hai ke uski aawaz par namaz todne ka hukm hai, iski kya haqiqat hai, baraye meherbani tafseeli mas-alah bayan kijiye.

Jawab: Agar sunnat namaz ho aur waalidain me se koi aulad ko pukaare to unki pukaar par labbaik kehna aur unki pukaar ka Jawab dena waajib hai kyunke namaz sunnat hai aur waalidain ki itaa'at waajib hai aur nafarmani karna haram, lekin agar koi farz namaz padh raha ho aur waalidain me se koi pukaare to un ki pukaar ka Jawab dena waajib nahi, kyunke Allah Ta'aala ka Haq waalidain ke haq par muqaddam hai, albatta agar namaz me takhfeef ki jaaye aur mahaz arkan wa waajibaat ada karke namaz mukammal karke unki pukaar ka Jawab diya jaaye to yeh behtar hai isi tarah zikr ki niyyat se unchi aawaz me subhanallah keh kar bhi un ko mutanabbeh karna behtrareen soorat hai ke wo samajh jaayenge ke beta/beti namaz me hai. Lekin agar waalidain ki pukaar se yeh mehsoos ho ke wo kisi musibat me hai to jis tarah naabeena (blind) wagayrah ki jaan bachane ke liye namaz todna waajib hai, unki jaan bachane ke liye bhi farz namaz ko todna waajib hai. (Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 16/105, Fathul-'Aziz: 4/115, Kifaayatul Akhyar: 1/97). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Daurane Namaz Rak'ato Aur Sajda Ki Ta'daad Me Shak

Sawal: Daurane namaz agar rak'ato ki ta'daad me shak ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Daurane namaz agar ta'daad me shak ho to kam par 'amal kare ya'ani teen ya chaar rak'atein hui is ka shak ho to teen par 'amal kar ke baqiyyah rak'atein puri kare aur aakhir me sajda-e-sahwu kar ke namaz mukammal kare. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/434). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi ko sajda ki ta'adaad me shak ho to kya mas-alah hai? Neez aakhri rak'at me tahiyyat me darood padhne ke baad shak ho ke aakhri rak'at me ek ya do sajde kiye hain phir kya kare?

Jawab: Usi waqt ek sajda kare phir mukammal tahiyyat padhe phir sajda-e-sahwu karke salaam phere ('Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik: 60). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi Shakhs ko aakhri rak'at ke sajda ki ta'adaad me shak ho aur tahiyyat, darood mukammal hone ke baad ek sajda kare jo shak ki wajah se choota hai aur phir fauran ek sajda-e-sahwu ka kare (darmiyan me wapas se tahiyyat, darood aur du'aa padhe) to kya namaz sahih hogi?

Jawab: Namaz sahih nahi hogi balke use jis waqt yaad aajaaye usi waqt pehle choota huwa sajda kare jis me shak huwa tha phir shuru' se aakhir tak mukammal tahiyyat padhe phir sahwu ke do sajde kare phir salaam phere ('Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik: 60). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Me Mobile Ki Ring Bajne Ka Mas-alah

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs namaz me ho aur galati se mobile silent par rakhna bhool gaya ho aur ring baje to kya kare?

Jawab: Musalmanon ko chahiye ke masjid me dakhil hone se pehle apne mobile phone band karle ya silent mode par lagaale taake namaz ki haalat me aysi soorat pesh hi na aaye ke use band karna pade, us waqt is baat ka ehtmaam nihaayat zaroori hai kyunke us se aadmi ki apni namaz me to khalal (Kharabi) waaqe hota hi hai dusron ko bhi takleef hoti hai aur un ka zehen muntashir (dispersion of mind) ho jaata hai. Albatta agar koi shakhs mobile band karna bhool jaaye aur ghanti bajne lage to use chahiye ke amale kaseer kiye bagair dheere se band kar de (Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 324-325). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs mobile band karna bhool jaaye aur namaz me ghanti bajne lage to amale kaseer kiye bagair kis tarah mobile band kar sakta hai? Agar is dauran amale kaseer wajood me aa jaaye to namaz ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Agar namaz me ghanti bajne lagi to use chahiye ke jaib me rehte huwe hi ek haath se mobile band kar de, aur is andaaz se band kare ke kisi dekhne wale ko yeh na lage ke yeh shakhs namaz me nahi hai, is tarah band karne se namaz par koi asar nahi padega. Isi tarah agar kaee (many) bar ghanti baji aur kaee (many) bar isi andaaz me band kiya to bhi namaz ho jaayegi. Lekin agar jaib se nikaal kar haath me lekar bahut ziyadah harkat ke saath mobile band kiya jiski wajah se amale kaseer wajood me aaya to namaz faasid (invalid) ho jaayegi, kyunke is soorat me har dekhne wala yahi samjhega ke yeh shakhs namaz me nahi hai, ya kam se kam use shak to zaroor ho jaayega ke shayad yeh namaz me nahi hai, is liye is soorat me namaz toot jayegi aur use namaz dobara padhni hogi (Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan:324). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Daurane Namaz Sajda Bhool Jaaye

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs chaar rak'at wali namaz ke dauran doosri rak'at me ek sajda bhool jaaye ya'ani do sajdon ke bajaaye ek hi sajda kare aur phir teesri rak'at ke qiyam me yaad aane ke baad teesri rak'at me us ki talafi kare ya'ani do ke bajaaye teen sajda kare aur salaam se pehle sajda-e-sahwa kare to kya imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek wo namaz durust hogi?

Jawab: Namaz ke arkan me tarteeb waajib hai, lihaaza agar koi shakhs jaan boojh kar koi rukn chod de to us ki namaz baatil ho jaayegi aur bhoole se koi rukn choot jaaye to us ke baad kiye huye aamal wa af'aal ka koi e'etibaar nahi hoga yahan tak ke pehle is choote huwe rukn ko ada kare. Mazkoora mas-alah me namazi ko teesri rak'at ke qiyaam me yaad aaye ke uska doosri rak'at ka ek sajda choot gaya hai to ab yeh shakhs qiyaam ki haalat se seedha zameen par baith jaayega aur itminan se baithne ke baad ek sajda karega phir tashahhude awwal padhega phir teesri rak'at ke liye khada hoga aur chauthi rak'at mukammal kar ke sajda-e-sahwa kar ke salaam pherega. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/300, Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 282). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Ke Andar Do Qism Ki Sunnat

Sawal: Sunnat hay'aat ka kya matlab hai?

Jawab: (1) Sunane Ab'aaz: is ka hukm yeh hai ke agar wo choot jaaye ya is ko jaan boojh kar chod de to namaz ki nekiyon me kami aajati hai is soorat me namazi ke liye sunnat hai ke wo is kami ko poora karne ke liye aakhir me sajda-e-sahwa kar le jis ki wajah se wo kami poori ho jati hai.

(2) Sunane Hay-aat: is ka hukm yeh hai ke agar wo choot jaaye ya jaan boojh kar chod de to sajda-e-sahwa ke zariye sawab ki is kami ko poora nahi kiya ja sakta ya'ani is sunnat ko chodne ki wajah se sajda-e-sahwa karna sunnat nahi (Fiqhul Manhaji: 1/143). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tashahhude Awwal Ka Bhool Jana

Sawal: Agar imaam do rak'at ke baad tahiyyat me baithna bhool gaya aur sidha teesri rak'at ke liye uth gaya, muqtadi subhanallah keh kar isharah de rahe hon lekin imaam use qabool na kare aur phir chauthi rak'at ki tahiyyat, darood aur du'aa ke baad sajda-e-sahwu kare to kiya namaz hogi?

Jawab: Imaam ki namaz sahih aur durust hogi aur imaam sahab ne jo 'amal kiya yahi sahih hai (Fiqhul Manhaji: 1/143, 166). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi pehle tashahhud me na baithe aur na hi aakhir me sajda-e-sahwa kare to namaz ada hogi?

Jawab: Namaz to ho jaayegi albatta sawab me kami aur naqs baqi rahega lekin namaz ko dobara lautane ki zaroorat nahi (Fiqhul Manhaji: 1/166). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sajda-e-Sahwa Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Agar koi namaz me sajda-e-sahwa kare to kya use dobara tahiyyat, darood aur du'aa padhna chahiye?

Jawab: Sajda-e-sahwa ke baad fauran salaam pherni chahiye kuch padhne ki zaroorat nahi (Fiqhul Manhaji: 1/168). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs, teen ya chaar rak'at wali namaz ka pehla tashahhud chod de to ab kya kare?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah ke mutabiq agar koi shakhs pehla tashahhud bhool jaaye to is ke liye namaz ke aakhir me salaam se pehle do sajde (Sajda-e-sahwa) karna sunnat hai, yaad rahe ke namaz me pehla tashahhud sunnate baaz hai us ki talafi sajda-e-sahwu se ho jati hai (Fiqhul Manhaji:1/143,166). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Sawal: Agar muqtadi ki ek rak'at choot jaaye aur la ilmi me muqtadi ne imaam ke saath hi salaam pher diya to use kya karna hoga? Agar muqtadi ko mas-alah ka 'ilm na ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Agar waqt ziyadah na guzra ho to uth kar ek rak'at puri kare aur aakhir me sajda-e-sahwa kare. Agar bahut der ho chuki ho to use namaz ko dobara padhna zaroori hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/168). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sajda-E-Shukr Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek shukrane ka sirf sajda hai ya namaz bhi padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek shukrane ka sirf sajda masnoon hai, lekin sajda-e-shukr ke saath kuch sadqah kare aur namaz padhain to yeh ziyadah behtar hoga (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:2/217). Maulana Shabbir Tambe aur Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya bila Wuzoo sajda-e-shukr ada ho sakta hai?

Jawab: Bagair Wuzoo ke sajda-e-shukr ada nahi hoga, sajda-e-shukr ke liye bhi wahi sharait hain jo namaz ke durust hone ki sharait hain (Al-Bayan: 2/295). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Ba-Jamaa-'at Namaz Ke Liye Daudna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs ba-jamaa-'at namaz ke liye daud kar aakhri tahiyyat me masjid me pahonche phir niyyat bandhte waqt ek salaam phere lekin wo doosri salaam se pehle tahiyyat me baithe to kiya ba-jamaa-'at ke sawab ko haasil karne ki niyyat se daudna sahih hai? Kya use namaz ba-jamaa-'at ka sawab milega kyun ke ek salaam ke baad wo jamaa-'at me dakhil hua?

Jawab: Namaz ke liye daud kar jaane se hadeese paak me mana'a kiya gaya hai (Al-bayan: 2/372). Agar koi shakhs imaam ke pehla salaam pherne se pehle niyyat ke saath takbeer kahe to use jamaa-'at ki fazeelat haasil ho jaayegi lekin agar imaam pehla salaam pher chuka ho aur muqtadi niyyat ke saath takbeere tahrimah kahe to jamaa-'at haasil nahi hogi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/177). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Namaz me imaam se milne ka matlab kya hai?

Jawab: Imaam se milne ka matlab yeh hai ke muqtadi ki niyyat aur takbeer tahreema imaam ke pehle salaam se pehle honi chahiye. Isliye ke namazi takbeere tahrimah se namaz me dakhil hota hai aur salaam se namaz se bahar nikalta hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/177). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Namaz ke liye daud kar jana hadees se mana'a hai, lekin agar koi namaze ba-jamaa-'at ka sawab haasil karne ki niyyat se kabhi kabhar daude to kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Hadees me waqar aur sukun ke saath chalna mazkoor hai aur daud kar jaane se mana'a kiya gaya hai (Al-bayan: 2/372). Lekin jaisa ke Sawal me bayan kiya gaya hai agar kabhi jamaa-'at ka sawab haasil karne ke liye 'aam rafter se zara tez chalna pade to koi haraj nahi hona chahiye. Isliye ke yeh ek nek maqsad ke liye hai aur daudna mana'a to hai lekin haraam nahi balke imaam agar muqtadi ki aahat sune to use ruku aur tahiyyat ko zara taweel karne ka hukm hai kyun ke ruku me imaam ko paa-lene se rak'at haasil ho jaayegi aur tahiyyat me imaam ko paa-lene se jamaa-'at ki namaz haasil ho jaayegi. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Imam Ke Pehle Salaam Ke Mukammal Hone Se Pehle Salaam Pherna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs imaam ke pehle salaam ke mukammal hone se pehle salaam pher de to namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: imaam ke saath namaz padhne wale ke liye mustahab hai ke imaam ke dono salaam mukammal hone ke baad apne salaam ki ibtida kare albatta agar koi imaam ke ek salaam ke baad ya imaam ke saath hi salaam phere tab bhi jaaiz hai lekin agar koi jan boojh kar imaam ke salaam pherne se pehle salaam pher de aur imam se juda hone ki niyyat na kare to is soorat me namaz baatil ho jaayegi. (Al-Majmoo': 3/483). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Masbooq, Imaam Ke Dono Salaam Mukammal Hone Ke Baad Khada Ho

Sawal: Jis shakhs ki rak'at choot jaaye (Masbooq) to imaam ke salaam pherne ke fauran baad khade hoga ya dono salaam mukammal hone ka intizar karega?

Jawab: Jis shakhs ki rak'at choot jaaye us ke liye mustahab yeh hai ke imaam ke dono salaam se farig hone ke baad khada ho, agar koi shakhs imaam ki pehle salaam ke baad khada ho jaaye to namaz durust hogi. Albatta imaam ke assalaamu alaikum mukammal karne se pehle imaam se alag hone ki niyyat kiye bagair khada ho jaaye to namaz baatil ho jaayegi (Al-Majmoo':3/483). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Masbooq Ke Liye Namaze Fajr Me Du'aa-E-Qunoot Padhna

Sawal: Agar kisi ki fajr ki ek rak'at choot jaaye aur imaam ke salaam pherne ke baad masbooq ek rak'at padhe to kya apni aakhri rak'at me bhi du'aa-e-qunoot padhna hoga?

Jawab: imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek imaam ke saath jab koi muqtadi kisi rak'at me shaamil ho jaata hai to wo rak'at us ki pehli rak'at shumaar hoti hai, lihaaza imaam ki Iqtidaa me imaam ke saath du'aa-e-qunoot to padhega lekin chunke is du'aa ka asal waqt aakhri rak'at hai. Isliye apni doosri rak'at ke ruku ke baad bhi dobara padhega. (Kifayatun Nabeeh-Sharhut Tambeeh:3/588). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imam Ki 5 Rak'atein Aur Masbooq Ki 4 Rak'atein

Sawal: Agar 'Isha ki namaz me muqtadi imaam se doosri rak'at me mile aur imaam ne be khayali me sahwan (bhool kar) paanch rak'atein padhai aur kisi ka dhyan us taraf nahi gaya aur tashahhud me imaam ko samajh aaya ke panch rak'at hui hai to imaam ne sajda-e-sahwa ada kiya, aysi soorat me masbooq ko kya karna hai?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah me chunke bekhayali me masbooq ki chaar rak'atein mukammal huein is liye imaam ke salaam pherne ke baad us ne bhi salaam pher diya lihaaza is ki yeh namaz mukammal aur sahih hui (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/170). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Farz Namaz Ke Baad Jahran (Zore Se) Ijtimaa'i Du'aa

Sawal: Kuch log kehte hain ke farz namaz ke baad jahran Ijtimaa'i du'aa saabit nahi aur hamare yahan jo har farz namaz ke baad jis tarah ijtimaa'i du'aa ka ehtimam kiya jaata hai wo ek bid'at hai. Is ki shara'ee haisiyat kya hai?

Jawab: Farz namazon ke baad ke auqaat hadees shareef ke mutabiq qabooliyate du'aa ke auqaat hote hain. Salaam ke baad imaam wa muqtadi ka ta'alluq khatam ho jaata hai is liye har ek ko apni apni infiraadi (individual) du'aa karna mustahab hai Shawaafe' ke nazdeek aayate Kareema “wala tajhar bisalatika wala tukhafit biha” ("وَلَا تَجْهَرْ بِصَلَاتِكَ وَلَا تُخَافِتْ بِهَا") (Al-Isra'a:110) ki wajah se sirran (Aahista) du'aa karna mustahab hai, agar muqtadi hazraat aise hain ke un ko namaz ke baad du'aa yaad nahi hai to un ki ta'aleem ki garz se imaam jahran (zor se - loudly) du'aa kare aur muqtadi aameen kahe, muqtadiyon ko du'aa yaad ho jane ke baad har ek apni apni du'aa sirran padhe agar ta'aleem ki zaroorat na ho to imaam muqtadiyon ki taraf mutawajjah ho kar sirran du'aa kare aur muqtadi hazraat bhi apni apni du'aa sirran kare (Al-Muhimmaat fi sharhir- Rawzati war-Rafi'ee:3/122).

Hazrat Allama Muhammad Anwar Shah Kashmiri (rh) ne Faizul bari me aur muhaddisul 'Asr Hazrat Maulana Muhammad Yusuf binnori nawwarallhu marqadahu ne apne fatawah me likkha hai ke Faraaiz ke baad maujoodah hai'at ke mutabiq ijtimaa'i du'aa karna sunnat mustamirrah to nahi hai, lekin is ki asal saabit hai; is liye ise bid'at kehna guloo hai. (Darool Ifta: Jamiya 'Uloom-e-Islamiya Allama Muhammad Yusuf Binnori Tawn). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Chaar Rak'at Ki Bajaaye Teen Rak'at Ke Baad Salaam Pherna

Sawal: Imaam Sahab ne zuhr me chaar rak'at ki bajaaye teen rak'at ke baad salaam pher diya, imaam sahab ne baat nahi ki aur khade hokar ek rak'at padhai, albatta muqtadiyon ne kalam kiya tha, to un muqtadiyon ki namaz ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Mazkoora mas-alah me imaam ka yeh 'amal sahih hai, ya'ani muqtadi ki yaad dahani ke baad imaam ko jaise hi apne sahwa (an unintentional mistake) ka yaqeen ho jaayega fauran khade ho kar baqiyyah namaz mukammal kar ke sajda-e-sahwu kar ke salaam pherega, muqtadi hazrat bhi imaam ki ittiba'a me ek rak'at padh kar sajda-e-sahwu kar ke salaam pherenge.

Is mas-alah me yeh shart lagaee jaayegi ke is dauran jaan boojh kar aur namaz me hone ka 'ilm hone ke bawajood kalam na kiya gaya ho, aur ziyadah waqt aur bhool kar ziyadah kalam bhi na kiya ho ya'ani cheh (6) kalmaat se ziyadah na bola ho, warna az sare nau namaz ka e'aada zaroori hoga, chunke muqtadi hazraat ne jis waqt baat ki us waqt wo yeh samajh rahe the ke hum namaz me nahi hai, aur jo namaz me na ho us ke liye baat karna mubaah hai, lihaaza mazkoora mas-alah me muqtadi hazraat ki namaz sahih hogai.(Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:2/140). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muqtadi Ka Imaam Ke Saath Shaamil Hone Ka Intizaar Karna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs masjid me us waqt dakhil ho jab imaam ruku se uth chuka ho ya sajde me gaya ho to wo shakhs kya kare? Kya imaam ka agli rak'at ke liye khada hone tak ka intizaar kare (kyunke us ki wo rak'at shumaar nahi hogi) ya usi haalat me jamaa-'at me shaamil ho jaaye?

Jawab: Sunnat tareeqa yahi hai ke namaz ke dauran muqtadi imaam ko jis haal me paaye Iqtidaa ki niyyat ke saath takbeere tahrimah keh kar imaam ke saath shaamil ho jaaye aur intizaar karta na rahe.

Chunanche Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Jab tum me se koi namaz ke liye aaye aur imaam jis bhi haalat me hoto wo waisa hi kare jaisa imaam kar raha hai.”

Isi tarah ek aur hadees me irshad farmaya ke: “Jab tum namaz ke liye aao aur hum sajde me ho to tum bhi sajda karo aur use kuch shumaar na karo aur jis ne rak'at ko paa liya us ne namaz ko paa liya. (Sahih Abu Dawood: 893). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imaamat Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya nabaalig bachcha imamat kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Nabaalig bachcha imamat kar sakta hai bashart-e-ke us me tameez paida hogai ho aur Qur-aan tajweed ki riayat ke saath padhta ho, neez namaz ke zaroori ahkaam wa masa-il se waqif bhi ho (Kitabul Umm:1/193). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya bagair daadhi wala shakhs imamat kar sakta hai jabke wo Qur-aan sahih padhna janta ho aur namaz ke ahkaam wa masa-il se bhi khoob waqif ho? Aur kya us ki Iqtidaa me namaz padhne walon ko jamaa-'at ka sawab milega?

Jawab: Daadhi rakhna mashru wa sunnat hai aur is ka ehtimam karna chahiye. Behtar yeh hai ke koi daadhi wala shakhs hi imamat karaye jo namaz ke ahkaam wa masa-il se waqif ho, lekin agar koi bagair daadhi wala shakhs bhi imamat kare to uski Iqtidaa me namaz durust ho jaayegi aur in sha Allah jamaa-'at ka sawab bhi milega. (Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah:4/206). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya koi ladki ya 'aurat 'auraton ki ba-jamaa-'at namaz ki imaamat kar sakti hai?

Jawab: 'aurat ka imaamat karna durust hai jabke Iqtidaa karne wali 'auratein hi hon. Kisi 'aurat ke liye mardon ki jamaa-'at ki imaamat karna jaaiz nahi, agar mard hazraat 'aurat ki Iqtidaa me namaz padhain to un mardon ki namaz dursut nahi hogi. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/425, Al-Majmoo':4/255). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hanafi Aur Hanbali Muqtadiyon Ki Imaamat

Sawal: Berooni mulk me imaamat karte waqt agar hum du'aa-e-qunoot na padhe aur surah fatiha se pehle jahran bismillah na padhe to kya hamaari imamat sahih hogi aur hamaari Iqtidaa me namaz padhne walon ki namaz sahih ho jaayegi jab ke hum maslake Shafi'ee ke muqallid hain aur muqtadiyon ki aksariyat hanafi ya hambali hai?

Jawab: Aap ka imaamat karna bhi sahih hoga aur muqdadiyon ki namaz bhi sahih ho jaayegi lekin aap imamat kar rahe hain to aap ko muqtadiyon ke maslak ke muqabale apne maslak ko tarjeeh aur badapan dena chahiye aur apne maslak ke e'etibaar se hi namaz padhani chahiye (Al-Majmoo':4/290). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tanha Namaz Padhne Wale Ko Imaam Banana

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs tanha (akele) namaz padh raha ho aur hamein uski Iqtidaa karna ho to uska sahih tareeqa kya hai?

Jawab: pehle niyyat karke takbeere tahrimah kahe phir haath se isharah kare is baat ko batlane ke liye ke hum tumhari Iqtidaa kar rahe hain taake wo imamat ki niyyat kar le aur takbeeraate intiqali ko buland aawaz se kahe (Nihaayatul Matlab:2/384). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Aksar log dusra tareeqa Ikhtiyar karte hain “pehle haath se isharah karte hain phir rak'at baandhte hain” kya yeh sahih tareeqa hai?

Jawab: Yeh tareeqa bhi sahih hai albatta behtar yahi hai ke rak'at baandh kar phir isharah kare. Waise isharah karne se muta'alliq koi baat kitabon me manqool nahi hai (Nihaayatul Matlab:2/384). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imaam Ki Bhool Par Muqtadi Ka Luqma

Sawal: Ba-jamaa-'at namaz me 'aurat ka imaam ko luqma dena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Agar koi 'aurat imaam ko aagah karna chahti ho to us ke liye mustahab hai ke wo tasfeeq kare ya'ani apne daahine haath ki hatheli ke pusht ko bayein haath ki hatheli ke andaruni hissa par mare ya us ke bar-aqs kare. Albatta aysi jagah jahan fitna na ho ya tamam mahram hon to subhanallah kahne ki bhi gunjaish hai lekin yeh khilafe sunnat hai. (Al-Majmoo':4/82).

Hazrat sahal bin saad as-saa'idi (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Namaz me jab imaam bhool jaaye to us ko yaad dilane ke liye mardon ke liye “Subhannallah” kehna hai, aur 'auraton ke liye taali bajana hai.” (Ibne Maajah: 1035). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar daurane namaz muqtadi ko shak ho jaaye ke imaam ne rak'aton me galati ki hai, haqiqat me imaam sahih ho aur muqtadi apne shak ki bunyad par luqma de (subhanallah kahe) to kya wo namaz faut hogi? Aysi soorat me muqtadi ko kya karna chahiye?

Jawab: Jab imaam se koi galati sarzad ho jaaye ya'ani rak'at ki tadad bhool jaaye ya qiraat bhool jaaye to muqtadi ke liye mustahab hai ke zikr ki niyyat se ya qiraat ki soorat me tilaawat ki niyyat se luqma de ya zikr wa tilaawat ki niyyat ke saath imaam ko intibaah karne ki niyyat kare to namaz par koi asar nahi padega, sirf intibaah ki garz se luqma de to namaz baatil ho jaayegi, mazkoora soorat me muqtadi ki niyyat par namaz ki sihhat aur adame sihhat ka daro madar hoga ya'ani mas-alah ko janta nahi hoga to namaz baatil nahi hogi albatta 'ilm ke bawajood mahaz intibaah ki niyyat se luqma diya hoga to namaz baatil ho jaayegi warna nahi. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/47). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imam Se Alag Hone Ki Niyyat Karna

Sawal: Agar hum ba-jamaa-'at namaz padh rahe hain imaam ko rak'aton me shak ho jaaye to wo kam par a'atimad kar ke ziyadah padhega, agar muqtadi ko yaqeen ho ke imaam ek rak'at ziyadah padh raha hai aur luqma dene ke bawajood imaam aur ek rak'at padhne ke liye khada hua, us waqt muqtadi ko kya karna chahiye kyunke muqtadi ko shak nahi hai to wo ziyadah kyun padhe? Kya muqtadi ko imaam se alag ho kar salaam pherna chahiye? Ya imaam ki mutaba'at me ek rak'at ziyadah ada kare?

Jawab: Aysi soorat me muqtadi ko chahiye ke wo imaam se alag hone ki niyyat karke tahiyyat poori kar ke apne taur par salaam pher de ya imaam ke fauran laut aane ka intazaar kare aur imaam ke saath salaam phere (Fathul-'Aziz:4/177, An-Najmul Wahhaaj:3/44). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar hum railway ya bus station par ba-jamaa-'at namaz padh rahe hain aur imaam ke lambi rak'aton ki wajah se train ya bus chootane ka andesha ho to hum kya kare?

Jawab: Aysi soorat me imaam se alag ho kar apni baqiyyah rak'atein mukhtasaran ada kare, chunke aap ko shuru' jamaa-'at ka kuch hissa mil gaya tha, phir 'uzr ki wajah se imaam se alag hone ke sabab aap ko jamaa-'at ki fazeelat bhi haasil hogi, albatta Jumu'ah ki namaz hai to kam se kam ek rak'at ka imaam ke saath milna zaroori hai, to aap ko Jumu'ah mil jaayegi, warna nahi. (Fathul-Mu'een:1/174). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Imaam Ke Liye Wuzoo Ko Rokna Mummkin Na Ho

Sawal: Agar imaam ke liye Wuzoo ko rokna mumkin na ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Namaz mukhtasar kar sakte hain. Agar wuzoo na ruk sake aur tahiyyat me “Allahumma Salli 'Alaa Muhammadinw Wa 'Alaa Aali Muhammad ( أللّٰهُمَّ صَلِّى عَلىٰ مُحَمَّدٍ وَّ عَلىٰ آلِ مُحَمَّدٍ )” tak padh liya ho to fauran salaam phere (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/382,428). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla aur Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Agar muqtadi tahiyyat tak imaam ke saath hai aur us waqt Wuzoo tootne ka andeshah ho aur muqtadi salaam phere to kya us ki namaz ada hogi?

Jawab: Imaam se alag hone ki niyyat karke salaam phere to namaz mukammal hogi aur judaigi ki niyyat ke bagair salaam phere to namaz nahi hogi. Beech namaz me niyyat karni hogi. Mas-alah yeh hai ke muqtadi apni namaz me jab bhi imaam se juda hone ki niyyat kare to wo imaam se alag ho jaayega aur agli namaz wo apne hisab se ada kar sakta hai. Ab imaam ki mutabaqat (payravi) us par lazim na rahi. Mazkoora mas-alah me chunke us ki majboori hai ke us ko wuzoo tootne ka ehsas ho raha hai ke agar imaam ke salaam pherne tak intizaar karunga to wuzoo toot jaayega. Lihaaza ab agar wo imaam se juda ho kar apna salaam khud phere to namaz mukammal hui. ek baat dhyan me rahe ke niyyat zubaan se nahi karna hai beech namaz me dil me karna hai. (Bidayatul Muhtaaj:1/354). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imaam Ka Wuzoo Toot Jaaye

Sawal: Agar imaam ka Wuzoo toot gaya to phir imaam kya kare?

Jawab: Peeche muqtadiyon me se kisi ko ya kisi munfarid ko isharah kar de ke wo imaam ka naa'ib (Representative) ban kar namaz ki imamat karaye (Al-M'utamad: 1/455). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar imaam ko sajde me yaad aaye ke us ka Wuzoo nahi tha to kya kare?

Jawab: Aise waqt me imaam apni pichli saf me se kisi namaz ka 'ilm rakhne wale ko apna naa'ib (Representative) banayega, sajda se uth kar phir wo naa'ib (Representative) tamam logon ko baqiyyah namaz padhayega, is soorat me muqtadi hazraat ko namaz ke iaada aur dohrane ki zaroorat nahi rahegi, albatta imaam wuzoo kar ke aayega aur jamaa-'at me sharik ho kar masbooq ki tarah apni namaz puri karega (Al- Moatamad:1/455). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar imaam ka Wuzoo toot gaya, kisi aur ko imaam banana hai to us ka sahih tareeqa kya hai?

Jawab: imaam peeche kisi muqtadi ko isharah karega, wo thoda aage badh kar imamat ki niyyat kar ke namaz padhayega, jis rukn se imaam ne choda hai wahin se aage namaz padhayega (Al- Moatamad:1/456). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Wuzoo tootne ke bawajood bhi imaam zaahir ke e'etibaar se jamaa-'at jari rakhe to kya muqtadiyon ki namaz sahih hogi?

Jawab: Muqtadiyon ki namaz sahih hogi jabke muqtadiyon ko imaam ke be Wuzoo hone ka 'ilm na ho. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ek Hi Jagah Ka'ee Masaajid Me Speakers Par Namazen

Sawal: Jis jagah qarib me ek se ziyadah masaajid hon (Khas kar Metro cities mein, Makka Mukarrama aur Madinah Munawwara me bhi) aur wahan speaker par namazen hoti hain. Ab dono masaajid ki aawazein mil jati hain jis ki wajah se baaz muqtadi gadbada jate hain aur kabhi doosri wali masjid ke imaam ki takbeer par sajda se uth jate hain aur phir samajh me aane ke baad phir se sajda me jate hain. Aysi soorat me kya unki namaz sahih hogi?

Jawab: Agar awaaz pehchan sakte hain to aysi haalat me apne imaam ki takbeerat par hi muqtadi ki naqal wa harkat honi chahiye, bhool se huwa ho to gunah nahi, dobara imaam se mil jaaye mazkoora soorat me us muqtadi ki namaz sahih shumaar hogi (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:2/232). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muqtadi Ka Imaam Se Do Ya Teen Rukn Peeche Hona

Sawal: Agar koi muqtadi imaam ke saath pehli rak'at se shaamil ho lekin kisi 'uzr ki wajah se doosri ya kisi aur rak'at me imaam ke saath ruku me shaamil nahi hua (ek ya do rukn peeche raha) muqtadi ke ruku me jaane se pehle imaam "sami allahu li-man hamida" keh kar khada ho jaaye lekin wo jald ruku karke imaam se mile to kya wo rak'at shumaar hogi?

Jawab: Ji ha, shumaar hogi kyunke 'uzr ki wajah se teen rukn tak imaam se peeche rehne ki soorat me muqtadi ki namaz baatil nahi hoti lekin bila 'uzr do rukne fe'ali imaam se peeche rehne ki soorat me namaz baatil ho jati hai. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 2/394). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar muqtadi kisi 'uzr ki wajah se imaam se teen rukn peeche hoto kya wo rak'at shumaar hogi? Ya'ani imaam pehle sajda me aur muqtadi ruku me ho.

Jawab: Ji ha, agar 'uzr ki wajah se peeche raha tha to wo rak'at shumaar hogi (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 2/394). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Main ne yeh suna tha ke agar muqtadi imaam se teen rukn peeche rahe to wo rak'at shumaar nahi hoti?

Jawab: Yeh us waqt hai jabke imaam se peeche rehne me muqtadi ke paas koi 'uzr na ho mahaz kaahili aur susti ki wajah se imaam se peeche reh gaya ho to ek rukne fe'ali tak gunjaish hai ya'ani namaz baatil nahi hogi lekin do rukn fa'ali peeche raha to namaz baatil ho jaayegi. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 2/394). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sutra (A Physical Object) Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Namaz me sutra (a physical object to act as a barrier) ki kya ahmiyat hai? Uski uonchai kitni honi chahiye? Usko kahan rakhna chahiye?

Jawab: Namazi ko apne samne sutra rakhna mustahab hai, isliye ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne namazi ke samane se guzarne wale ke liye badi wa'Eid sunayi hai, namazi aur sutre ke darmiyan (3) haath se ziyadah faslah na ho, sutre ki uonchai ek zira'a ya ek zira'a ke do tihayi ke barabar honi chahiye, sutra bilkul saamne na rakhe balke kuch dayein ya bayein taraf rakhe yeh sunnat hai (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 510, Nihayatuz-Zain:79). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar imaam sutra rakhe to kya muqtadi ka bhi sutra hoga?

Jawab: Imaam ka sutra muqtadi ke liye kafi hoga (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 1/221). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar imaam sutra rakhe to kiya koi bhi muqtadi ke saf ke beech me se jasakta hai? Jaisa ke haram shareef me hota hai.

Jawab: Nahi guzar sakta kyunke sutra isi waste hota hai taake koi namazi ke samne se na guzre ('Ujalatul Muhtaaj: 1/25). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Imaam ka sutra muqtadi ka sutra hota hai, kya is ka matlab yeh hai ke log muqtadiyon ki safon ke darmiyan se guzar sakte hain aur un ke kandhon ko phalang kar doosri taraf ja sakte hain?

Jawab: Imaam pehli saf ke liye sutra hoga jabke teen zira'a ke fasle par ho aur pehli saf baad wali saf ke liye sutra hogi jabke qarib ho, lihaaza saf ke darmiyan se guzarna sahih nahi aur kandhon ko phalang kar doosri jagah jana bhi sahih nahi hai lekin agar log agli saf me jaga chod kar pichli saf me khade huwe hon to chunke is me un ka qusoor hai, lihaaza peeche se aane wala shakhs agli saf ko mukammal karne ke liye kandhon ko phalang kar aage ja sakta hai (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:2/56). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namazi Ke Saamne Se Guzarne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs namazi ke samne se ja raha ho to namazi ko kiya karna chahiye?

Jawab: Agar sutra rakhkha hai to usko roke agar na ruke to us ko quwwat se mana'a kare aur agar sutra na rakhe ho to yeh namazi ki kotahi hai lekin dono sooraton me namaz sahih ho jaayegi. (At-Tehzeeb: 2/167). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi zaeef aadmi namazi ke samne se guzarna chahe to kya hum unhe rok sakte hain? Dil me dar rahega ke agar rokne ke chakkar me wo gir gaye aur lag gaya to? 'Ulama se guzarish hai ke is par roshni daale kyunke ek taraf se lagta hai ke hadees me rokne ka hukm hai aur doosri taraf dil me ehteraam ka bhi khayal aa raha hai ke zaeef aadmi hai.

Jawab: Deene Islam saraapa rahmat wala deen hai aur sab se bada rishta aur ta'alluq Allah aur bande ke darmiyan ka ta'alluq hai aur ahaadees ke e'etibaar se aadmi namaz ki haalat me apne rab se sargoshi aur hum kalami karta hai aur is ta'alluq ko todne wala shaitan hi hoga isliye use shaitan kaha gaya hai aur use maarne ka hukm diya gaya hai phir chahe wo mar hi jaaye. Isliye sutre ka hukm diya gaya taake koi Allah aur uske bande ke darmiyan haayil na ho. Lekin agar koi guzre to wo gunahgaar to hoga lekin namaz par koi farq nahi hoga. Wallahu A'alam. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 2/235). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Half Sleeves T-Shirt Ya Shirt Pehen Kar Namaz

Sawal: Kya nisf aasteen (Half Sleeves) wala T-shirt ya Shirt pehen kar namaz sahih hoti hai?

Jawab: Nisf aasteen wali shirt ya T-shirt pehen kar padhi gai namaz durust hogi. (At-Tambeeh:1/28). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Distressed Denim Ya'ani Phati Hui Jeans Pehen Kar Namaz Padhne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Aaj kal Distressed Denim Jeans ya'ani phati hui jeans pant pehenne ka chalan muashre me 'aam ho gaya hai, kya use pehen kar namaz durust hogi?

Jawab: Mardon aur 'auraton par waajib hai ke wo satar chupaye, ya'ani mard par waajib hai ke itna kapda pehene ke jis se naaf aur ghutne ke darmiyan ka hissa chupa rahe, albatta jism ke deegar aaza (parts) ko chupana sunnat hai. 'auraton par waajib hai ke namaz ke 'alaawah ajnabi mardon ki maujoodgi me apna mukammal jism chupaye rakhe, aur namaz me chehre aur hatheliyon ke 'alaawah mukammal jism chupaye, neez dono ke satar me yeh bhi waajib hai ke kapda aisa ho jo jism ki rangat ko chupane wala ho, lihaaza aisa kapda pehenna ke jis se satar nazar aata ho, kapde ke baarik aur patle hone ki wajah se ya kapde ke phate huye hone ki wajah se, to yeh jaaiz nahi. Aisa kapda pehen kar padhi jaane wali namaz durust nahi hogi aur laziman gunah hoga. Lihaaza Sawal me mazkoora jeans pant ke phate huye hone ki haalat jis nauiyat ki hogi uske mutabiq faisla kiya jaayega. Agar ghutne ke upar phati hui hogi to gunah hoga aur namaz bhi sahih nahi hogi, aur ghutne ke niche phati hui hogi to usse namaz ki sihhat par to koi asar nahi padega, ya'ani namaz to sahih ho jaayegi, lekin aisa kapda pehenna khilafe sunnat hai. Seerat wa sawaneh ki kitabon me Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke libas se muta-'alliq yeh baat milti hai ke Aap (ﷺ) kapde ko phati hui haalat me nahi pehente the, balke pewand laga kar pehena karte the. (Sunane Abu Dawood: 641, Sunan At-Tirmizi:377, Al-Majmoo':3/167). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tasweer Wale Kapde Pehen Kar Namaz Padhna

Sawal: Agar T-shirt par kisi jaandar ki photo ho to kya namaz durust hogi?

Jawab: Jandar ki tasweer wala libas pehen kar namaz padhna makrooh hai lekin namaz durust ho jaayegi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/394). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Tasweer wale kapde pehen kar namaz padne ka shara'an kya hukm hai? Aur aise kapde pehen kar namaz padhne par namaz hogi ya nahi?

Jawab: Namaz ke liye Allah rabbul izzat ne zeenat ikhtiyar karne ka hukm diya hai aur khubsoorat kapde pehenna bhi zeenat me dakhil hai isliye Fuqahaa ne aadmi ke liye namaz padhte waqt khubsoorat kapde pehenne ko masnoon qarar diya hai. Lihaaza masjid jate waqt khubsoorat aur shara'ee libas pehen kar jana chahiye.

Aadmi ke liye namaz padhte waqt har aysi cheez ikhtiyaar karna makrooh hai jis se namaz me khalal waqe hota ho aur namaz se khushu' khatam ho aur yeh baat ma'aloom hai ke tasweer wale kapde mamnoo' (prohibited) hone ke saath khud us aadmi ki to kya doosron ki namaz me bhi khalal dalne ka sabab bante hain, isliye tasweer wale kapde pehen kar namaz padhna makrooh hai lekin agar koi aise kapde pehen kar namaz padhe to namaz durust ho jaayegi, albatta sawab mukammal nahi milega. (Al-Majmoo':1/130). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Jagah Badal Badal Kar Padhna

Sawal: Namaz jagah badal badal kar padhna chahiye kyunke zameen ka wo hissa hamara gawah ban jaata hai. Kya yeh baat sahih hai?

Jawab: Jagah badalna mustahab hai. Is liye ke yeh ahaadees se saabit hai, Muslim shareef me Hazrat mu'aaviyah (Rz) ki hadees me hai, Abu dawood wa Ibne Maajah me Hazrat Abu Huraira (Rz) ki hadees me bhi hai.

Zameen ka wo hissa gawah banega, yeh 'aam dalail se saabit hai, jaise Allah Ta'aala ka irshad hai: Us din wo ya'ani zameen apni sari khabrein bata degi. (Surah Az-zilzaal: 4, Muslim: 1463, Abu Dawood: 854, Ibne Maajah: 1417, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/394). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Bachcha Ke Baalig Ho Jaane Par Namaz Ka Mas-alah

Sawal: Agar koi bachcha 'Isha ki namaz padh kar so jaaye aur phir wo raat me baalig ho jaaye (Us ko pehli bar ehtilaam (wet dream) ho jaaye) wo subh fajr se pehle gusl le to use 'Isha ki namaz wapas padhna hogi ya phir fajr phadhna hogi?

Jawab: Agar koi bachcha farz namaz ada karne ke baad 'umr se ya kisi aur sabab se baalig ho jaaye to us par us namaz ka e'aada (namaz dobara padhna) waajib nahi barkhilaf hajj ke, ke agar koi bachcha hajj ki adaigi ke baad baalig ho gaya to us hajj ka eaada karna us par waajib hai, lihaaza Soorate mas-uoolah me us bachche par 'Isha ki namaz ka eaada waajib nahi. (Al-Iqna': 1/114). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Barhana (Necked) Namaz Padhna Kis Waqt Jaaiz Hai

Sawal: Agar kisi ne najis kapde pehne hon aur us ke paas paak kapde maujood na ho to kya barhana namaz padhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs sirf najis kapde par hi qadir ho aur us ke 'alaawah paak kapde maujood na ho aur farz namaz ka waqt khatam ho raha ho to hurmat-e-waqt ki wajah se us shakhs ke liye napaak kapda utaar kar barhana namaz padhna jaaiz hai aur us namaz ka eaadah bhi zaroori nahi hai. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb: 1/478). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ayyam-E-Haiz Aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) Me Namaz Ka Hukm

Sawal: Ayyam-e-haiz me Namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Ayyame haiz me namaz mu'aaf hai ya'ani qaza karne ki zaroorat nahi. (Asnal-Matalib: 1/100). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki haalat me Namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Nifaas ki haalat me bhi namaz mu'aaf hai aur baad me qaza karne ki bhi zaroorat nahi. ('Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik: 31). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Haizah (Menstruation) 'Aurat Ka Guroob-e-Aaftab Se Qabl Paak Hona

Sawal: Haizah 'aurat agar Magrib se pehle paak ho jaaye to namaz ka kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Haizah (Menstruation) 'aurat agar guroob-e-aaftab se qabl paak ho jaaye to sirf 'asr ki namaz padhna ada-an ya qaza-an zaroori hai aur agar 'asr ke awwal waqt me paak ho jati hai to zuhr qaza karegi aur 'asr adaa padhegi (Fathul-'Aziz be Sharhil-Wajeez:1/73). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Likoria (Leukorrhea) Ki Mareeza Ke liye Tahaarat Wa Namaz Ka Hukm

Sawal: Likoria ('aurat ki agli sharamgah se nikalne wala sufed paani) ki mareeza ke liye tahaarat wa namaz ke kya ahkaam hain?

Jawab: Likoria se murad agar mazi ho aur is marz me mubtala (afflicted) 'aurat ke raham se sayyal maaddah (behne wali cheez) musalsal rista rehta ho aur ek namaz ke mukammal waqt me itni gunjaish ya itna waqt (waqt: maslan panch minutes) bhi mayassar na aati ho jis me paaki mil jaaye to aysi 'aurat maa'zur shumaar hogi aur uske ahkaam mustahazah aur salasul-boul (urinary incontinence) aur salasul-mazi (incontinence of pre-cum) wale mareez ki tarah honge. Lihaaza Likoria ki mareeza har namaz ke liye Wuzoo karegi aur namaz ada karegi, daurane namaz agar sayyal maadda behta rahe tab bhi wuzoo qayam rahega kyunke wo maa'zur hai. Libas ki paaki ke liye pad ya andaruni kapda isti'emaal kare aur har namaz ke liye Wuzoo karne se pehle achchi tarah istinja karke pad ya andaruni kapda tabdeel kar le aur wuzoo kar ke namaz ada kare. Likoria aur salasul-boul ke mareezon ke liye ek hi hukm hai ke wo musalsal jari rehne wale peshab ya Likoria ke qatron se libas ko mehfooz rakhne ke liye mukammal tahaarat ke baad pad ya kapda wagayrah baandh kar aur Wuzoo kar ke namaz ada kare ('Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik:32). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Me 'Arabi Ke 'alaawah Zubaan Me Du'aa Maangna

Sawal: Kya namaz ki haalat me 'arabi ke 'alaawah kisi aur zubaan me du'aa mang sakte hain?

Jawab: Nahi, namaz ki haalat me 'arabi ke 'alaawah kisi aur zubaan me du'aa maang nahi sakte, is se namaz baatil ho jaayegi. (Fathul-Wahhab:1/83). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Du'aa Ke Liye Sajda Karna

Sawal: Kya namaz ke baad hum sajda kar ke kisi zabaan me du'aa maang sakte hai?

Jawab: Sirf du'aa ke liye sadja karna jaaiz nahi hai. Namaz me sajda ki haalat me 'arabi me du'aa jaaiz hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/326). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sunnate Mu-akkadah

Sawal: Sunnate mu-akkadah kise kehte hai?

Jawab: Sunnate mu-akkadah se muraad wo kaam jis ke karne ki Sharee'at me taakeed ki gai ho aur us kaam ko chodne wala mustahiqe azab to na ho lekin mustahiqe malamat ho un par Aap (ﷺ) ne pabandi se 'amal kiya aur unko tark nahi kiya siwaye ek do martaba ke aur yeh ek do martaba ka chodna bhi is liye tha taake ummat ko ma'aloom ho jaaye ke yeh waajib nahi hai. (Al-Tareefat:122). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Jaan Boojh Kar Namaz Qaza Karna Gunahe Kabeera Hai

Sawal: Kaam me mashgooli aur uske baad thakawat ki wajah se Magrib ki namaz ke liye masjid me nahi pahonch paata hun lekin namaz ko qaza kar ke masjid me 'Isha se pehle padh leta hun lekin ghar me waqt par padhna munaasib nahi samajhta kya mera yeh 'amal sahih hai ya mai waqt par masjid na jaa saka aur namaz qaza hone ka darr ho to kya main ghar par namaz padh sakta hun taake meri namaz qaza na ho.

Jawab: Jaan boojh kar namaz qaza karna kabeera gunah hai aur agar koi namaz ka inkar kar ke usko chodta hai to us shakhs ki saza qatal hai lihaaza kaam kaaj ki masrufiyat ki wajah se ya safar wagayrah ki wajah se masjid jaane ka mauqa na mile to jahan mayassar ho chahe ghar ho ya office, auqaate namaz me namaz adaa karna zaroori aur waajib hai. (Al-Gayatu fi ikhtisarin Nihayati:2/229). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaz Ke Mutafarriq Masa-il

Sawal: Kya namaz me cheenk ka Jawab de sakte hai?

Jawab: Namaz me cheenk ka Jawab yar-hamukallah keh kar nahi de sakte albatta yar-hamuhullah keh kar de sakte hain lekin surah fatiha ke dauran Alhamdulillah aur yar hamuhullah nahi kehna chahiye warna surah fatiha dobara padhna lazim hoga. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:1/123, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/42). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Daurane namaz agar Muhammad (ﷺ) ka naam aaye to kya sallallahu alaihi wa sallam padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Zaroori nahi balke mustahab hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 1/483). Mufti Muzaffar Sain

Sawal: Qur-aan ki wo konsi aayaat hain jin ke sunne se namaz me us ka Jawab ya du'aa padhna mustahab hai?

Jawab: Namaz me jahan aayate wa'Eid aaye wahan Allah ki panaah talab karna aur aayate ummeed par Allah se du'aa karna hadees se saabit hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen:112, Muslim: 772). Mufti Muzaffar Sain

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek 'asr me kitni sunnate hain?

Jawab: 'Asr me pehle chaar (4) rak'atein hain jo sunnate gair mu-akkadah hain (I'aanatut-Taalibeen:1/387). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek namaz me aankh band karna makrooh hai?

Jawab: Poori namaz me aankh band karke padhna yeh makrooh hai, Khushu' Khuzu' ki khaatir thodi der ke liye makrooh nahi hai. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj:2/177). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi imaam ke peeche rak'at us waqt baandhe jis waqt imaam ruku me jaaye, agar muqtadi ko imaam ke saath ruku na mile ya'ani muqtadi ke ruku me jaane se pehle imaam sami allahu liman-hamida keh kar khada ho jaaye lekin wo jaldi se ruku karke imaam se milne ki koshish kare to kya wo rak'at shumaar hogi? Kya is soorat me yeh rak'at haasil hogi?

Jawab: Muqtadi ki yeh rak'at shumaar nahi hogi (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/228). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: 'Arab mamaalik me aaj kal kuch log ruku se uthne ke baad wapas haath baandhte hain jaise qiyam ki haalat me hote hain, kya yeh tareeqa chaaron imaam me se kisi ke nazdeek saabit hai? Main ne kisi se suna hai ke wo kisi hadees ki bina par 'amal karte hain jis ka khulasa kuch is tarah se hai: logon ko yeh hukm diya jaata tha ke wo namaz me apna dahina haath bayein haath ki kalayi par rakhain (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 7740) isi tarah kuch log ek sajda se uthne ke baad jalsa me apni shahadat ki ungli harkat dete hain, yeh kis ka tareeqa hai? Kya imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek bhi aisa hai?

Jawab: Yeh gair muqallideen ke nazdeek mustahab hai, imaam Shafi'ee ka yeh maslak nahi. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar kisi andhe ke kunwe (well) me girne ka andesha ho ya saanp aaye aur aap namaz me hon to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar kisi andhe ke kunwe me girne ka andeshah ho ya saanp aaye to namazi par waajib hai ke use aawaz de kar bachaane ki koshish kare, agar wo na ruke ya gaafil ho to namaz tod kar andhe shakhs ko bachaye ya saanp ho to saanp ko maar de aur apni namaz dohraye (Fathul-Wahhab:1/60). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Fajr Ki Sunnat Choot Jaaye To Fajr Baad Padhne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Fajr se pehle ki sunnat namaz ki kya fazeelat hai? Agar kisi wajah se fajr se pehle ki sunnat choot jaaye to fajr baad padhne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) riwaayat karti hain ke Nabiye Kareem(ﷺ) ne irshad farmaya fajr ki do rak'at duniya aur jo kuch duniya me hai us se behtar hai (Muslim: 1688).

Isi tarah ek riwaayat me hai ek sahabiye Rasool ko Nabiye Kareem(ﷺ) ne fajr ke baad do rak'at namaz padhte huwe dekha to Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaya konsi do rak'at hai Aye Qais? To Sahabiye Rasool ne kaha main ne fajr ki sunnat namaz nahi padhi thi to yeh do rak'at hai to Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) khaamosh rahe. (Abu Dawood:1267).

Fuqahaa-e-kiraam ne fajr se pehle do rak'at ko sunnate mu-akkadah qaraar diya hai albatta agar kisi wajah se pehle ki sunnat choot jaaye to fajr baad bhi padhna durust hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/457, Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:2/236). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Bagair Wuzoo Ke Bhool Kar Ya 'Amadan Namaz Padhna

Sawal: Agar koi bagair wuzoo ke bhool kar ya 'amadan (jaan boojh kar) namaz padhe to kya us namaz ka eaada zaroori hai?

Jawab: Bagair Wuzoo ke namaz sahih nahi hogi chaahe jaan boojh kar ya bhool kar us ne bagair Wuzoo ke padha to kisi bhi soorat me namaz nahi hogi. Lihaaza agar kisi ko namaz ke baad be Wuzoo hone ka 'ilm ho jaaye tab bhi us namaz ko dobarah ada karna zaroori hai. (Al-Majmoo':2/281). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Witr Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Ramazan shareef me agar kisi ko tahajjud me uthne ka yaqeen ho to use ba-jamaa-'at namaze witr padhna chahiye ya subh uth kar tahajjud ke baad tanha padhna chahiye phir jamaa-'at ka sawab ka kya?

Jawab: Ramazan Shareef me witr ki namaz ko jamaa-'at se padhna sunnat hai, agar subha uth kar tahajjud ki namaz padhne ka yaqeen ho to witr ko muakkhar (delay) karna bhi masnoon hai. Imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek witr ki namaz padhne ke baad bhi tahajjud padhna jaaiz hai. Lihaaza agar kisi ne witr ki namaz taraaweeh ke baad padh liya ho aur subh sehri me tahajjud padhne ka mouqa ho to us ke liye tahajjud padhna bhi jaaiz hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/202). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek witr ki namaz teen rak'at ek saath wasl karke padh sakte hain jis me doosri rak'at ke baad jalsa na kare.

Jawab: Ji han. witr ki namaz padhne ka yeh tareeqa bhi jaaiz hai, albatta teen ya teen se ziyadah rak'at witr ki namaz padhna ho to afzal tareeqa yeh hai ke har do rak'at par salaam phere phir akhir me ek rak'at padhe. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 147). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shafi'ee Muqtadi Ka Hanbali Ya Hanafi Imaam Ke Peeche Witr Padhna

Sawal: Khaliji mamalik (Gulf Countries) me aimma-e-Kiraam poora ramazan witr ki aakhri rak'at me ruku se uth kar du'aa padhte hain, jis me kuch alfaaz aise bhi hote hain jo du'aa-e-qunoot se milte julte hain. Sawal yeh hai ke kya wo padhi jaane wali du'aa du'aa-e-qunoot hoti hai ya 'aam du'aa aur kya muqtadi hazraat jo maslak-e-Shafi'ee se ta'alluq rakhte hain, imaam ke saath du'aa me sharik ho kar aameen bol sakte hain?

Jawab: Hanaabila ke nazdeek poora saal witr ki namaz me du'aa-e-qunoot padhna masnoon hai. Aur ahnaaf ke nazdeek waajib hai. Bairuni mamalik me aksariyat hanaabila ki hai to witr ki namaz me wahan imaam jo du'aa karta hai wo du'aa-e-qunoot hi hoti hai. Agar shawafe' hazraat unke saath ba-jamaa-'at namaz padh rahe hon to unki Iqtidaa me unke saath du'aa me shariq hona aur aameen kehna bhi sahih hai. (Al-Mugni Ibne Qudama Hambali: 2/111, Ad-durrul Mukhtar: 6/2, Al-Majmoo': 4/24). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi Shafi'ee maslak par 'amal karne wala taraaweeh ki namaz Hanafi masjid me padhe to witr ka kya hukm hoga? Kya use us jamaa-'at ke saath witr padhni chahiye kyunke Hanafi hazraat 3 rak'at ek saath padhte hain aur wo teesri rak'at me du'aa-e-qunoot padhte hain?

Jawab: Shafi'ee maslak par 'amal karne wala shakhs Hanafi imaam ki Iqtidaa me witr padh sakta hai, kyunke imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek witr ki 3 rak'at ek salaam se padhna bhi sahih hai, isi tarah unke saath du'aa-e-qunoot me sharik hona bhi sahih hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/328-330). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Du'aa-Yeh-Qunoot Ke Auqaat

Sawal: Du'aa-e-qunoot kin kin auqaat me padhi jati hai?

Jawab: Du'aa-e-qunoot teen auqaat me padhi jati hai. (1) rozana namaz fajr ki doosri rak'at ke aitidal me. (2) Ramazanul Mubarak ke nisfe saani (aadhe ramazan) se akhir ramazan tak witr ki aakhri rak'at ke aitidal me use qunoote 'Umar bhi kehte hain in dono auqaat me Du'aa-e-qunoot padhna sunane ab-aaz me se hai ya'ani unke tark par sajda-e-sahwu se uski talafi ki jati hai. (3) Aafate samawi ki wajah se ya kuffar wa mushrikeen ki taraf se jab musibatein aur mazaalim musalmano par dhaye jate hain to uska dafiyeh (door karna) ke liye aur Allah Ta'aala se nusrat wa istimdaad ke liye paancho waqt ki farz namaz ki aakhri rak'at ke aitidal me Du'aa-e-qunoot padhi jati hai use qunoote naazilah kehte hain yeh du'aa sunnat hay'aat me se hai, is ko tark karne ki soorat me sajda nahi kiya jaayega. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/144,167). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bagair Soye Huwe Tahajjud Ki Namaz Padhna

Sawal: Kya bagair soye huwe tahajjud ki namaz padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Tahajjud aur qiyamul-lail ka waqt 'Isha ki namaz padhne aur kuch der so kar uthne ke baad hota hai aur fajr ka waqt shuru' hone tak rehta hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj Ma'a Hawashi: 2/131, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/217). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Bina 'Uzr Ke Zuhr Aur 'Asr Ek Saath Padhna

Sawal: Kya Rasool-e-Kareem (ﷺ) ne kabhi bina 'uzr ke zuhr aur 'Asr ki farz namaz chaar (4), chaar (4) rak'at ek saath padhi hai? Jis tarah hum qasr ki do (2), do (2) rak'at safar ke dauran padhte hain.

Jawab: ek martaba Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne zuhr aur 'Asr ko jama'a karke padha tha aur uske 'Ulama ne alag alag asbaab bayan kiye hain lekin shawafe' ke yahan bila 'uzr ke do namazon ko ek waqt me padhna gunahe kabeera hai, neez auqaate namaz ke 'alaawah padhi gai namaz sahih nahi hoti Imaam Ahmed (Rh) wagayrah kaee (many) 'Ulama ne beemaari ki wajah se do namazon ke jama'a karne ki ijazat di hai. Shawafe' ke nazdeek bhi ek qaul yeh hai ke marz ki wajah se jama'a taqdeem ya taakheer (delay) ke sharait ke saath taqdeem wa taakheer (delay) kar sakte hain lihaaza koi shakhs apni had (limit) tak is par 'amal kar sakta hai beemaari ke e'etibaar se taqdeem ya taakheer (delay) jis me sahoolat ho is par 'amal kare masalan doosri namaz ke waqt beemaari ka zor badhta ho to jama'a taqdeem ki soorat me baarish ki tarah dono namaz ki takbeere tahrimah pehli namaz ke salaam ke waqt aur dono ke darmiyan beemaari ka paya jana shart hai.
