Baaz 'Ulama ke nazdeek is masale me marz is darje ka ho ke mareez ko har namaz ke waqt me ada karne me itni mashaqqat ho jitni baarish me chalne se ho jabke kapde tar (beeghe huwe - wet) ho, aur baaz 'Ulama ke nazdeek itni mashaqqat zaroori hai ke farz namaz baith kar padhna jaaiz ho jaaye (in dono zabiton me bahut ziyadah farq bhi nahi hai) (Tuhfah m'a Hawashi: 3/274, Fathul-Mu'een: 213, Tuhfatul-Bari:1/240). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Safar Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Train ka safar hai aur train bhi logon se bhari hui hai aur qiblah ka rukh bhi ma'aloom nahi ho raha hai neez bheed ki wajah se khade ho kar namaz ada karna bhi mushkil hoto is soorat me kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Aysi soorate haal me ishare se namaz ada kare aur jab safar mukammal ho jaaye to namaz dohrayi jaaye (Fathul-'Aziz be Sharhil-Wajeez: 1/263). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Daurane safar bus ya kisi sawari me namaz ka waqt ho jaata hai aur hum bagair Wuzoo ke hon aur bus chal rahi ho, Wuzoo wa tayammum na karsakte hon aur namaz ka waqt bhi khatam ho raha ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Basa auqat aadmi sarkari gadi bus ya train me ya apni gaadi me safar karta hai aur farz namaz ka waqt ho chuka hota hai aur us ki haalat yeh hoti hai ke gaadi ko rok nahi sakta ya Wuzoo ya tayammum ke liye paani aur mitti ko talash karne ke baad bhi use na mile to waqt ki hurmat ka lihaz karte huwe jaise mumkin ho usi haalat me namaz ada kare taake use namaz chodne ka gunah na ho, phir daurane safar ya ikhtitame safar par jab mitti ya paani use mil jaaye aur wo un par qaadir ho jaaye to Wuzoo ya tayammum kar ke us namaz ka 'eaadah (repeat) kare (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit tullaab: 1/128). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Daurane Safar Qasr Ka Hukm

Sawal: Qur-aan ki aayat ke mutabiq Khauf ki haalat me qasr kar sakte hain, aaj kal yeh haalat hamare safar me nahi hoti to kya qasr nahi karna chahiye? Is ka poora matlab kya hai aur is ke bar-aks hadees me kya hai jis buniyad par qasr kar sakte hai?

Jawab: Yahi Sawal Hazrat 'Umar (RA) ne Aap (ﷺ) se kiya tha to Aap (ﷺ) ne irshad farmaya ke “yeh Allah ki taraf se sadqa hai is ko qabool karo.” Isliye mas-alah ab khauf aur mashaqqat ka nahi hai agar qasr ki masafat payi jaaye to qasr karna jaaiz hoga. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/179, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 2/361, Al-Wajeez: 80). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Imaam Abu Hanifah (Rh) aur imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek daurane safar qasr ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Imaam Abu Hanifah (Rh) ke nazdeek qasr waajib hai aur imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek ziyadah sahi qaul yahi hai ke qasr karna afzal hai lekin chand sooratein is se mustasna (alag) hain.

Qasr Ke Liye Kam Se Kam Faasla

Sawal: Qasr ke liye safar ka kam se kam kitna faasla hona chahiye? Qasr me zuhr, 'Asr aur 'Isha ki kitni rak'atein padhni chahiye?

Jawab: Qasr ke liye safar ka faasla kam se kam 48 mile (81 KM - Kilometer) hona chahiye. Qasr me Zuhr, Asar aur Isha ki 4 raka'aat ki bajaaye 2 raka'aat hogi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/190). Maulana Muzaffar Sange

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek qasr ke liye safar ka fasalah kam se kam 48 mile (81 KM) hona chahiye is ki kya daleel hai?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek masaafate qasr 4 bareed hai is liye ke Hazrat Ibne Uamr (RA) aur Hazrat Ibn Abbas (RA) chaar bareed par qasr kiya karte the aur 4 bareed ki miqdar 'Ulama wa muhaqqiqeen ne 81 KM likhi hai (Al-Muhazzab: 1/192). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qasr Ke Sharaait

Sawal: Qasr ke jaaiz hone ke liye kya sharaait hain?

Jawab: Qasr ke jaaiz hone ke liye chand sharaait hain. (1) Safar kam se kam 81 KM ka ho. (2) Safar kisi gunah ke kaam ke liye na ho. (3) Qasr karne wala kisi mukammal namaz padhne wale ki Iqtidaa na kare, ya'ani qasr karne wale ka imaam bhi aisa musaafir ho jis ke liye qasr jaaiz ho aur wo bhi qasr hi kar raha ho. (4) Takbeere tahrimah ke waqt qasr ki niyyat kare. (5) Namaz ki takbeere tahrimah se salaam pherne tak safar jari rahe. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 174, 'Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik: 70-72). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muqeem Ke Peeche Qasr

Sawal: Agar koi imaam mukammal chaar (4) rak'at namaz padha raha ho to us ke peeche qasr karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Koi imaam masalan zuhr ki chaar rak'at padha raha ho to musaafir ke liye uske peeche qasr karna jaaiz nahi hai balke mukammal chaar rak'at padhna zaroori hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/187). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Ba-Jamaa-'at Namaze Qasr

Sawal: Kya namaze Qasr jamaa-'at se padh sakte hain? Agar jamaa-'at ke saath padhe to kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Namaze Qasr jamaa-'at se padh sakte hain magar sirf musafiron ki jamaa-'at ke saath, agar muqeem ke peeche padhe to namaz mukammal padhna zaroori hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 174). Maulana Muzaffar Sange

Jama'a Taqdeem Aur Jama'a taakheer

Sawal: Kya jama'a taqdeem ki soorat me pehli namaz (Zuhr ya Magrib) ko pehle padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Jama'a taqdeem me tarteeb waajib aur zaroori hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 177). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya jama'a taakheer (delay) ki soorat me pehli namaz (Zuhr ya Magrib) ko pehle padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Jama'a taakheer me tarteeb zaroori nahi hai lekin tarteeb se namaz padhna mustahab hai ('Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik: 72). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi safar me jama'a taakheer (delay) kar raha ho aur wo masjid me 'asr ki jamaa-'at ke waqt pahonche to use kya karna chahiye? Use 'Asr ki niyyat karke 4 rak'at padhna chahiye aur phir 2 rak'at zuhr ki qasr ada kare ya zuhr ki niyyat kare aur asar ki qasr padhe?

Jawab: Jama'a taakheer me tarteeb se ya'ani pehle waqt wali namaz pehle padhna mustahab hai waajib nahi hai. Isliye agar imaam ke saath pehle 'asr ki 4 rak'at aur baad me zuhr ki niyyat se 2 rak'at padhe to durust hai, koi haraj nahi. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/532). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Kaam Poora Karne Ki Ummeed Ke Dino Me Qasr

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs safar shuru' kare aur use ummeed ho ke manzil par 4 din se kam rukna padega lekin kaam mukammal na hone ki wajah se kuch aur din rukna pade to qasr ka kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek agar safar shuru' karne ke waqt manzil par 4 din se kam rukne ki niyyat ho to qasr kar sakte hain lekin kaam poora na ho aur ummeed ho ke aaj kal me poora hoga to 18 dino tak qasr kar sakta hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/520). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jumu'ah Ki Namaz Ke Saath 'Asr Ki Qasr

Sawal: Agar daurane safar Jumu'ah ka din aajaaye to kya Jumu'ah ki namaz ke baad asar ki jama'a taqdeem aur qasr kar sakte hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek ya'ani hamari kitabon me is baat ko jaaiz qaraar diya gaya hai ke musaafir Jumu'ah ki namaz ke saath 'asr ki namaz ko jama'a kar sakta hai. (Kifaayatul Akhyar:1/140). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haajiyon Ke Liye 'arafah Me Qasr

Sawal: Kya tamaam haajiyon ke liye 'arafah me qasr aur jama'a (Zuhr aur 'Asr ko jama'a ka ke) padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Haaji agar shara'ee musaafir ho (jaise Hindustan ya Madinah wagayrah se makka aaye huwe 4 din mukammal na huwe hon) to qasr wa jama'a kar sakta hai ya'ani zuhr, 'Asr aur Magrib 'Isha ko jama'a kar sakta hai lekin shara'ee musaafir na ho to dono namazon ko apne apne waqt par mukammal ada karna zaroori hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:2/260). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Samandari Safar Me Qasr Ka Hukm

Sawal: Jo log bahri jahaaz (Sea Vessel – Ship) par kaam karte hain, un ke liye namazon ko qasr karne aur jama'a karne se muta-'alliq shara'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar safar shara'ee ho ya'ani 81 KM ya us se ziyadah ho aur halal maqsad ke liye safar ka aagaz kiya gaya ho aur jahaaz me sawaar mallah (Captain) ho aur uske ahal wa ayaal (family and dependents) uske saath kashti me hon, to uske liye itmaam afzal hai. Baqiyyah sab ke liye qasr afzal hai. Phir dekha jaayega, ke daurane safar jahaaz kisi jagah ruk raha hai to kitne din rukega, agar aane jaane ka din chod kar 4 dino tak rukne ka iraadah ho to namazen mukammal padhenge aur 4 dino se kam thahrne (stay) ka iraadah ho to safar ke mukammal hone tak jama'a aur qasr dono bhi jaaiz honge. (Fathul-'Aziz:4/475). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Safar Ke Darmiyan Me Waapasi

Sawal: Agar koi safar me nikle aur raaste me zuhr ka waqt ho jaaye to wo qasr aur jama'a kare ya'ani zuhr ki 2 aur 'asrki 2 rak'at padhe aur phir masaafate qasr (81 KM) ko poora karne ke baad kisi 'uzr ki wajah se ghar waapasi ho aur 'asr ka waqt ho to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar masaafate qasr poori hone ke baad waapas ho raha ho to koi haraj nahi hoga. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/183). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs qasr aur jama'a taqdeem kare ya'ani zuhr ki 2 aur 'asr ki 2 rak'at padhe, albatta kisi 'uzr ki wajah se masafate qasr (81 KM) se kam me waapasi ho to kya zuhr aur 'asr use dobarah ada karni hogi? Ya safar me un do namazon ko padhne ki wajah se iaada ki zaroorat nahi hai?

Jawab: Masafate qasr ko poora karne ki wajah se ya masafate qasr ka iraadah karke usko poora karne ka iraadah ho to wo musafir hoga aur phir use safar ki sahoolaten haasil hongi, lihaaza usne zuhr aur 'asr ko do (2), do (2) rak'at safar me ada kiya to use un dono namazon ka iaada zaroori hai albatta agar us ne safar me zuhr ki chaar rak'at ada ki hai to us namaz ko dobara padhne ki zaroorat nahi hai (Al-Majmoo':4/324). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Qasr Ke Mutafarriq Masa-il

Sawal: Kya daurane safar 81 KM mukammal hone se pehle jama'a aur qasr kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Aisa shara'ee musafir jiska safar masaafate qasr ke baqadr ho us ke liye apne ilaaqa ki had (limit) chodne ke baad kahin bhi qasr wa jama'a karna jaaiz hai, 81 KM mukammal karna zaroori nahi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/186). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Safar kab khatam hota hai aur kitne dino tak safar ki wajah se qasr aur jama'a kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek agar koi shakhs apne gaon (village) ya sheher se kisi ke liye safar kare aur jaane aur aane ke 2 din chod kar wahan mutlaqan ya mukammal 4 din iqaamat ki niyyat kare to aysi jagah pahonchne se safar khatam hoga. Lekin agar sire se niyyat hi nahi ki ya 4 din se kam ki niyyat ki to us jagah pahonchne se safar khatam na hoga ya'ani use chaar din poore hone se pehle tak jama'a aur qasr karna jaaiz hoga. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/186). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Tudil se Mumbai ke liye safar kare aur daurane safar Panvel me ruke phir Mumbai se aate waqt New Mumbai me ruke aur phir Tudil waapas aaye to qasr ka kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Agar koi Shakhs Tudil se Mumbai ke liye safar kare aur daurane Safar Panvel me ruke phir Mumbai se aate waqt New Mumbai me ruke aur phir Tudil waapas aaye aur har jagah 4 din se kam thahrne (stay) ka iraadah ho to yeh musafir har jagah qasr wa jama'a kar sakta hai albatta agar wo kisi jagah 4 din se ziyadah theherta hai to ab wo muqeem hone ke baad jab wo is maqam se 81 KM se ziyadah safar ka iraadah karega to daurane safar use qasr ki ijaazat hogi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/180). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mausam Ki Kharaabi Me Namaz Jama'a Karna

Sawal: Mausam ki kharabi (halki baarish, bijli ki kadak) ki wajah se kal Kuwait me Magrib ki namaz ke saath 'Isha ki namaz bhi padhi gai, be shak ek sunnat ada ho rahi hai isliye arab is ko tarjeeh dete hain lekin kya haqeeqat me aisa karna chahiye kyun ke aksar log namaz ke baad bahar jate hain bahut kam log mausam ki kharabi ki wajah se gharon me rehte hain.

Jawab: Baarish ki wajah se jama'a taqdeem jaaiz hai, sunnat nahi, balke khilafe afzal hai. Us ki shart yeh hai ke pehli namaz ke aakhir waqt aur doosri namaz ki shuru' ke waqt baarish ho rahi ho. Jab yeh jama khilafe afzal hai, aur khususan aaj kal sahoolat itni hai ke baarish ki wajah se koi ghar me thehra nahi rehta to aysi ma'mooli (ordinary) baarish ki soorat me jama'a karne ki kya zaroorat hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/184). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Mausam ki kharabi (halki baarish, bijli ki kadak) wale din Magrib ki namaz ke saath 'Isha padhi gai?

Jawab: (1) Dobarah 'Isha ki namaz padhne me koi haraj nahi. (2) Jinhon ne baad me aakar pehle 'Isha ki namaz padhi un ki 'Isha sahih nahi hui kyunke jama'a taqdeem me tarteeb waajib aur zaroori hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/182). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar baarish ki wajah se Magrib ke baad fauran 'Isha padhe to sunnat aur witr ko kab padhain?

Jawab: Sunnat aur witr 'Isha baad padhe. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/216). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Namaze Taraweeh Ki Tahqeeq

Saudi 'Arab ke naamwar 'aalim, Masjide Nabawi ke mashoor mudarris aur Madinah Munawwara ke (Saabiq) Qaazi Al-Shaikh Atiyya Muhammad Saalim (Mutawaffa:1999) ne namaze Taraweeh ki chauda-sau (1400) saala taareekh par 'arabi zubaan me ek mustaqil kitaab (At-taraweeh Aksaru min Alfi 'aam fil Masjidin Nabawi) likhi hai, kitaab ke muqaddama me tasneef ka sabab bayan karte huwe likhte hain ke masjide nabawi me namaze taraweeh ho rahi hoti hai, baaz log aath (8) rak'at padh kar hi ruk jate hain, un ka yeh guman hai ke aath (8) rak'at padhna behtar hai aur us se ziyadah jaaiz nahi hai, is tarah yeh log masjide nabawi me baqiyyah taraweeh ke sawab se mahroom rehte hain, un ki is mahrumi (deprivation or lack) ko dekh kar bahut afsos hota hai mai yeh kitaab likh raha hun taake un logon ke shukook wa shubhat khatm ho aur un ko bees (20) rak'at padhne ki taufeeq ho jaaye, is kitaab me 1200 saala taareekh par mudallal bahas (well-argued discussion) karne ke baad Shaikh Muhammad Saalim likhte hain: Is tafseeli tajziya (analysis) ke baad hum apne qurra se awwalan to yeh poochna chahenge ke kya ek hazaar saal se zaa-id (excess) is taweel 'arse me kisi ek mauqe par bhi yeh saabit hai ke masjide nabawi me mustaqil aath (8) rak'at padhi jati thi? Ya chalein, bees (20) se kam padhna hi saabit ho? Balke saabit to yeh hai ke poore chauda-sau (1400) saala daur me bees (20) ya us se zaa-id hi padhi, doosra Sawal yeh hai ke kya kisi sahabi ya maazi ke kisi ek 'aalim ne bhi yeh fatwa diya ke aath (8) se zaa-id taraweeh jaaiz nahi hai aur us ne Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ki hadees ko is fatwa ki buniyad bana diya ho?

Taraweeh lafz Tarweeha ki jama'a hai jis ke maane thaharna / ruk jana (4 Rak'at ke baad) aur 'arabi ke hisaab se koi bhi jama'a 'adad / number ki miqdar kam se kam 3 hoti hai lafze taraweeh ke hisaab se kam se kam 3 baar rukna ya'ani 12 rak'at aur 3 witr lihaaza 8 ka koi Sawal hi nahi hota neez gair muqallideen ka jhagda 8 ya 20 ka nahi hai balke wo to taraaweeh ko maante hi nahi wo use Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ki hadees ka hawala de kar qiyamul-lail padhte hain.

Taraweeh Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya Taraweeh ki namaz ghar par ada karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Taraweeh ki namaz sunnate Muakkada hai aur use jamaa-'at ke saath ada karna sunnat hai, agar masjid me jamaa-'at se taraweeh padhna mumkin na ho to ghar par bhi taraweeh ki namaz ada karna jaaiz hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/228). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Taraweeh ki jamaa-'at ke liye kitne logon ka hona zaroori hai?

Jawab: Taraweeh ki jamaa-'at 'aam namazon ki tarah do ashkhaas se bhi mun'aqid ho sakti hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/228). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Taraweeh ki namaz me imaam ka Qur-aan-e-kareem me dekh kar tilaawat karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Amale Kaseer se ihtiraaz karte huwe aur bachte huwe namaze taraweeh ke dauran Qur-aan-e-kareem me dekh kar tilaawat karne ki gunjaish hai. (Al-Majmoo': 4/95). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya taraweeh me mukammal Qur-aan Khatam karna zaroori hai? Aksar auqat dekha gaya ke Qur-aan mukammal karne ke liye tezi se padhte hain jo samajh nahi aata.

Jawab: Ramazan ka poora mahina taraweeh padhna aur taraweeh me poora Qur-aan sunna yeh do alag alag sunnatein hain aur Allah ne Qur-aan ko itminaan ke saath padhne ka hukm diya hai lihaaza Qur-aan-e-kareem ko itminaan aur tajweed ki ri'aayat ke saath padhne ki koshish karna chahiye Qur-aan-e-kareem ko tez aur tajweed ki ria'ayat ke bagair padhne se behtar tajweed ke saath kam padhna hai. (Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah: 1/248). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi majboori ki wajah se taraweeh choot jaaye aur taraweeh khud se padhna padhe ab is soorat me kya karna chahiye taake Qur-aan mukammal ho jaaye.

Jawab: Utne Hisse ko apne taur par padh lijiye. (Al-Azkarul Muntakhaba min kalami sayyidil-Abrar). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Sawal: Imaam Bukhari (R) ne apni zindagi me hamesha 20 rak'at taraweeh padhi, kya yeh sahih hai?

Jawab: Imaam Bukhari (Rh) ke baare me ab tak mai ne kahin nahi padha ke wo taraweeh bees (20) ya aath (8) rak'at padhte the. Albatta Imaam Bukhari (Rh) ke khas shaagird Imaam Tirmizi (Rh) kehte hain ke aksar ahle 'ilm bees (20) rak'at taraweeh ke qaail (manne wale) hain. (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 3/160). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar Ground floor par masjid hai aur doosre manzil par flat me mastooraat masjid ke imaam ke saath taraweeh ki namaz ada kar rahi hain to is bare me 'Ulama rehbari farmayein.

Jawab: Agar imaam ki naql wa harkat nazar aaye ya suni jaaye aur Iqtidaa ke sahih hone ke sharait paaye jaayein to taraweeh aur deegar Faraaiz wa nawaafil bhi imaam ki Iqtidaa me padh sakti hain warna nahi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/180-183). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Iqtidaa ke sahih hone ke sharaait kya hain?

Jawab: Iqtidaa ke sahih hone ki saat (7) sharten hain: (1) Muqtadi imaam se aage na badhe, khade hone ki haalat me ediyon (heels) se aur baithne ki haalat me sureen (buttock / hips) se. (2) Imaam se saadir hone wale namaz ke af'aale zahirah ka 'ilm hona chahiye imaam ko dekhne ke zariye ho ya kisi saf ko dekhne ke zariye ya imaam ki aawaz sun kar. (3) Imaam aur muqtadi ke namaz padhne ki jagah ek ho. (A) Jab dono (Imam aur muqtadi) masjid me hon to Iqtidaa sahih hogi, chahe masafat qarib ho ya door. (b) Agar dono masjid ke 'alaawah khuli fiza me namaz padh rahe hon to is shart ke saath Iqtidaa sahih hogi ke dono ke darmiyan teen-sau (300) zira (450 feet/145 mitre) se ziyadah faslah na ho. (c) Agar dono me se ek ya'ani imaam ghar ke sehan me ho aur muqtadi ghar ke andaruni hisse me ho to Iqtidaa us waqt sahih hogi jab imaam jis jagah ya tameer me khada hai wo tameer muqtadi ki tameer ya'ani muqtadi jis jagah khada hai us tamir se muttasil ho, isi tarah imaam ke peeche aakhri saf muttasil ho us pehli saf se jo ghar ke andar hai, is tarah ke dono safon ke darmiyan teen-sau (300) zira se ziyadah faasla (distance) naa ho. (d) Agar imaam masjid me ho aur muqtadi masjid se bahar sadak par ho ya kisi parking me namaz padh raha ho aur darmiyan me koi hayil na ho aur masjid ke aakhri kinara ya aakhari saf aur sadak par ya parking me namaz padhne wale ke darmiyan teen-sau (300) zira (450 feet/145 mitre) se ziyadah faasla na ho to Iqtidaa sahih ho jaayegi lekin agar dono ke darmiyan masjid ki diwar hayil ho to dekha jaayega ke darwaza khula hai ya band agar khula ho to Iqtidaa sahih hogi aur agar band ho to Iqtidaa sahih nahi hogi. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 2/375). (4) Muqtadi Iqtidaa ki ya jamaa-'at ke saath namaz padhne ki niyyat kare. (5) Af'aale zahira me aur arkaan me imaam aur muqtadi ki namaz me muwafaqat ho ikhtilaf na ho agar ikhtilaf hoga to Iqtidaa sahih nahi hogi jaise imaam janaaze ki namaz padha raha ho to us ke peeche zuhr, 'asr wagayrah panj waqta namazen sahih nahi hogi.(6) Namaz ke af'aal me imaam ki muwafaqat kare mukhalafat na kare lekin agar imaam koi aisa fe'al chodta hai jo ke sunnat ho aur use ada karne me ziyadah mukhalafat na ho balke bahut hi kam waqt me wo ada ho jaata ho to us sunnat ki adayegi ke liye peeche rehna jaaiz hai jaise jalsaye istirahat, lekin agar ziyadah taakheer ho sakti ho to peeche rehna jaaiz nahi jiase agar imaam sajda-e-tilaawat chod de to muqtadi use nahi karega aur agar imaam Faraaiz me se koi farz chod de to ab us ki muwafaqat karna jaaiz nahi balke muqtadi intizaar karega ke bhool se choda hoga to laut kar aayega aur is fe'al (Act) ko karega agar laut kar na aaye to muqtadi apne dil hi me imaam ki Iqtidaa chodne ki niyyat kar ke apni baqiyyah namaz poori karega jaise imaam aakhri tashahhud chod kar paanchwi rak'at ke liye khada ho jaaye to muqtadi us ki muwafaqat nahi karega.(7) Imaam ki mutaba'at (Ittiba kare) Ittiba ka matlab yeh hai ke kisi bhi rukne fe'ali ko imaam ke shuru' karne ke baad aur khatam karne se pehle shuru' kare ziyadah peeche na rahe aur imaam ke saath saath bhi na kare, takbeere tahreema aur salaam ko saath saath karega to is muqtadi ki namaz baatil ho jaayegi aur takbeere tahrimah ko saath saath karne ki soorat me to us ki namaz mun'aqid hi nahi hogi.(Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/358, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/174). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bagair Taujeeh (Wajjahtu) Ke Taraweeh Padhna

Sawal: Aksar Huffaz Taraweeh me Taujeeh (Wajjahatu) padhne ka mauqa nahi dete hain, takbeere tahrimah ke fauran baad surah fatiha shuru' kar dete hain to kya taraweeh me taujeeh (wajjahatu) padhna masnoon nahi?

Jawab: Janaze ki namaz ke 'alaawah baqiyah tamaam namazon me taujeeh padhna sunnat hai, bagair taujeeh (wajjahatu) ke taraweeh padhna khilafe sunnat hai aur jaan boojh kar na padhna makrooh hai, is se ijtinaab karna chahiye.

Imam Nawawi (Rh) likhte hain “Taraweeh ki namaz ke sunnat hone par 'Ulama ka ittifaq hai aur wo bees rak'atein hain. Har do rak'at ke baad salaam phera jaayega, taraweeh ki namaz deegar 'aam namazon ki tarah hi padhi jaayegi ya'ani deegar namazon ki tarah is namaz me bhi tamam azkaar padhe jaayenge, masalan du'aaye iftitah (Wajjahatu) aur ruku wa sujood, aitidal wa jalsa wagayrah ki tasbeehaat aur du'aayein aur tashahhud (Attahiyat) aur uske baad ki mukammal du'aa. Agarcha har kisi ke 'ilm me yeh tamaam baatein hoti hain lekin uske bawajood main ne in cheezon ko taakidan bayan kiya kyunke aksar log taraweeh me iski taraf tawajjuh nahi karte.” (Al-Azkaar Lin-Nawawi: 1/183). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Taraweeh Ki Namaz Me Tashahhud Ke liye Kam Rukna

Sawal: Agar imaam taraweeh ki namaz me tashahhud (Attahiyaat) ke liye itna kam ruke ke darood mukammal padhne ka bhi waqt na mile to aysi namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Attahiyyat me “Allahumma Salli 'Alaa Muhammad

(اللَّهُمَّ صَلِّ عَلَى مُحَمَّدٍ)” tak darood shareef padhna farz hai, lihaaza agar koi shakhs “Allahumma Salli 'Alaa Muhammad” tak darood padh le to us ki namaz ho jaayegi, albatta Aap(ﷺ) ki aal(family) par darood padhna sunane ab'aaz me se hai jis ke tark karne par sajda-e-sahwa karna sunnat ho jaata hai, isiliye imaam ko chahiye ke itminan se tahiyyat mukammal kare ya kam se kam waqt de ke jis me muqtadi farz ki had (limit) tak tahiyyat padh sake.(Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/139). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Taraweeh Ki Ek Rak'at Padhane Ke Baad Salaam Pherna

Sawal: Agar imaam taraweeh ki ek rak'at padhane ke baad salaam phere to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar imaam bhool kar taraweeh ki ek rak'at ke baad salaam pher de phir use is baat ka yaqeen ho jaye ke us ne ek hi rak'at padhayi hai to fauran namaz ki kaifiyat ikhtiyar karke doosri rak'at ke liye khade ho kar doosri rak'at mukammal kare aur sajda-e-sahwa kare (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 2/191).

Albatta imaam ke baithne ke waqt jis muqtadi ko yaqeen ho ke imaam galati par hai aur us ne ek hi rak'at padhayi hai to zikr ki niyyat se subhanallah keh kar imaam ko galati par mutanabbeh kare, agar imaam ko galati ka ehsas ho jaaye aur wo doosri rak'at ke liye fauran khada ho jaaye to theek warna imaam se alag hone ki niyyat karke apni namaz poori kare. (Al-Majmoo': 4/113, 161). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Isha Ki Namaz Taraweeh Ki Namaz Ke Saath

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs 'Isha ki namaz ke baad masjid pahonche aur taraweeh ki namaz jari ho to use kya karna chahiye?

Jawab: Agar 'Isha ki doosri jamaa-'at chal rahi ho to usi me shirkat kar ke pehle ba-jamaa-'at 'Isha ki namaz padh le, phir imaam ke saath taraweeh ki namaz me shareek ho jaaye. Lekin agar 'Isha ki doosri jamaa-'at khadi na ho to pehle 'Isha ki namaz padh le chahe infiradan padhe ya taraweeh padhane wale ki Iqtidaa me padhe taake ba-jamaa-'at namaz ka sawaab hasil ho, is soorat me ab imaam ke salaam pherne ke baad yeh shakhs masbook (ya'ani jis ki koi rak'at choot gai ho) ki tarah uth kar apni baqiyyah rak'atein poori kar le. (Al-Majmoo': 4/270). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Taraweeh Me Sattaeswi (27th) Ko Qur-Aan Khatam Karna

Sawal: Taraweeh me Qur-aan kis taareekh ko khatam karna chahiye, neez sattaeswi (27th) ko Qur-aan khatam karne ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Poore Ramazan taraweeh ki namaz me mukammal ek Qur-aan khatam karna sunnat hai, lihaaza rozana ek ek paara padhne ki tarteeb banayi jaaye to tees dino me itminan se mukammal Qur-aan khatam ho sakta hai, lekin choonke mahina kabhi 29 ka bhi hota hai to is soorat me ek paara choot sakta hai isliye ehtiyatan unteesvi (29th) raat hi ko khatam kare taake Qur-aan naaqis na rahe. Sattaeswi (27th) shab me Qur-aan-e-kareem khatam karne se muta-'alliq koi daleel maujood nahi hai. Lekin chunke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne Shabe Qadr ko Ramazan ke aakhari ashre ki taaq raaton me talash karne ka hukm diya hai aur Shabe Qadr ke sattaeswi (27th) shab ko hone ke imkanaat rehte hain, jis ki barkaton ke husool ke liye barre sageer me aksar jaghon par satta'eswi (27th) shab me Qur-aan mukammal kiya jaata hai, ho sakta hai yeh waja ho. Isi tarah yeh bhi mumkin hai ke taqriban 40 saal pehle jab 'alaqa-e-kokan me Qur-aan ke huffaz nahi the ya bahut kam the,taraweeh sunane ke liye Huffaz gujrat aur U.P Bihar se aaya karte the, to un ki sahulat ke liye ke wo apni 'Eid apne ghar manaye Satta'eswi (27th) raat ko Qur-aan khatam kiya jaata hoga. Wallahu A'alam. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen:1/307). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Taraweeh Padhaane Wale Ko Hadiya Dena Aur Chanda Karna

Sawal: Aksar ilaaqon me taraweeh padhaane wale Hufffaz ko jo raqam di jati hai us ka shara'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Taraweeh ke ikhtitaam se qabl ya ikhtitaam par 'aammatan huffaz ko jo raqam di jati hai use lena dena dono jaaiz hai, yeh raqam ya kapda wagayrah jo huffaz ko diya jaata hai wo ya to bataure ikram ya in'aam ya bataure ujrat ke diya jaata hai yeh sab jaaiz hai, kyun ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Jin cheezon par tum ujrat lete ho un me kitabullah sab se ziyadah mustahiq hai". (Bukhari fil Ijarah: 2276, Saheeh Muslim: 1045, Tafseere Qurtubi: 1/371). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Huffaz taraweeh me Qur-aan padhte hain jis ke 'iwaz (badle me) me gaonwale ba-khushi apni istita'at ke mutaabiq hadiya dete hain, lekin aaj kal gaonwalon ki committee ya'ani jamaa-'at ke har ghar par kuch makhsus raqam (specific amount) tay (fix) karaati hai us se zaa-id (excess) raqam agar deni ho to apni marzi. Beshak huffaz ki mehnat aur Qurbaani hai aur khushi se jitna ziyadah de utna kam hai lekin kisi par zabardasti bojh dalna kaisa hai? Ho sakta hai ke wo banda bechara jamaa-'at ki wajah se majboori me aakar de raha ho, aise hadiye ka kya faidah jo saamne wala dabaav me aakar de raha ho jis men us ki dili razamandi na ho, kya taraweeh ke badle hadiya dene ke liye gaon me ghoom kar chanda jama'a karna sahih hai? Kya sirf ailan karke nahi chal sakta ke apni khushi se dena hai wo kisi zimmedar ke pas jama'a kare taake hadiya ki adayigi me aasani ho. Baraye karam 'Ulama-e-kiraam is mas'le par roshni daalein.

Jawab: Yeh tareeqa waqa'ee (Indeed) bahut galat hai, is se bachna chahiye. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Taraweeh Padhaane Wale Huffaz Ko Iftaar Aur Sehri Ke Bajaaye Raqam Dena

Sawal: Agar kisi gaon me koi committee imaam aur taraweeh padhaane wale huffaz ko Iftaar aur sehri ka intizaam khud karne ke bajaaye us ka mubadalah raqam ki soorat me de de to kya hukm hai?

(1) Jabke saal bhar imaam ko Tiffin dena gaon ke committee ki zimmedari ho to mas-alah kya hoga? (2) Ya yeh ke baqi 11 maheene imaam ko tiffin dena gaon ke zimme na ho to kya mas-alah hoga? (3) Doosri soorat me saalon saal gaon ke log ramazan me imaam aur taraweeh padhaane wale ko iftaar aur sehri dete hon lekin ab is ke bajaaye gaon ki committee har ghar se ek muqarrara raqam (Fixed Amount) misaal ke taur par 500 Rupay har ghar se Iftaari aur sehri ki niyyat se wasool kare jo raqam jama ho use imaam aur taraweeh padhaane wale ke darmiyan Iftaar aur sehri ke 'iwaz (badle me) taqseem kare. 'Ulama se guzarish hai ke masa-il ki wazahat kare, Sawal karne ki niyyat masa-il ki wazahat hai kisi ki dil aazari nahi.

Jawab: Ba-zahir to is me koi qabahat nahi albatta committee kisi par zor zabardasti na kare, logon ki haisiyat ke aitibar se unhe ikhtiyar de. (Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah Alaa Kanzir-Ragibeen:4/304, Fayzul-Qadeer: 6/18). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qaza-E-'Umri Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek qaza-e-'umri (Aaj tak jitni namazen qaza hui hain) ki kya ahemiyat aur mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Jab tak us ke Zimme ek farz bhi baqi ho to sunnat chod kar pehle use us farz ko ada karna waajib hai, aasani ke liye har farz namaz se pehle aur baad me chooti hui farz namazon ki qaza karta rahe. Agar ta'adaad yaad na ho to itni taweel muddat tak padhta rahe ke itminan ho jaaye ke ab mere zimme koi qaza baqi nahi rahi hogi (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 1/383). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Aksar logon ki jawani me farz namazen faut hoi hoti hain, kya taraweeh ki jamaa-'at me qaza-e-'umri ki namazen padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek sunnat padhne wale ke peeche farz namaz ki niyyat karna jaaiz hai, lihaaza agar koi shakhs taraweeh me imaam ki Iqtidaa me apni farz namazon ki qaza kare to sirf farz namaz ada ho jaayegi. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 1/318). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Taraweeh Ke Bajaaye Qazaaye Umri

Sawal: Agar kisi ki jawani me farz namazen qaza hui ho to taraweeh padhaane wale imaam ki Iqtidaa me kya taraweeh ki bajaaye fajr ki qazaye 'umri padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Ji han, fajr ki qaza padh sakte hain, kyunke imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek sunnat padhne wale ki Iqtidaa me farz namaz padhna sahih aur jaaiz hai lekin is soorat me sirf qaza namaz haasil hogi, taraweeh ka sawab nahi milega (Fatawa Ibnus-Salah: 1/236). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ramazan me ba-jamaa-'at witr ki bajaaye Magrib ki qaza-e-'umri kaise padhe?

Jawab: Chunke Hanafi imaam ek salaam se teen rak'at witr padhte hain to in ki Iqtidaa me Magrib ka koi mas-alah nahi, albatta Shafi'ee imaam ke peeche witr ki namaz me Magrib ki qaza kar raha ho to do rak'at par imaam ke salaam pherne ke baad masbook ki tarah apni ek rak'at puri karega. Wazeh rahe is soorat me use sirf qaza namaz (Magrib) ki hi haasil hogi. (Al-Majmoo': 4/269-270). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Fajr Ki Qaza Padhne Wale Ki Iqtidaa Me Zuhr Ya Asar

Sawal: Aagar koi fajr ki qaza namaz padh raha hai kisi aur namaz ke baad (Zuhr, asar, Magrib ya 'Isha) is dauran koi peeche se aaye aur us waqt ki namaz (misal ke taur par zuhr) ada karne ke liye niyyat bandhe aur fajr ki qaza karne wala is ke ishare ko imamat ke liye qabool kare aur namaz padhaye aur doosri rak'at me du'aa-e-qunoot padhe to kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Fajr ki namaz padhne wale ke peeche zuhr ki namaz padhna sahih hai albatta du'aa-e-qunoot ke waqt muqtadi ko ikhtiyar hoga ya to imaam ki ittiba'a (while following) me du'aa-e-qunoot me sharik ho jaaye ya imaam se alahidah hone ki niyyat karke apni baqiyyah namaz infiradan puri kare. (Al-Majmoo':4/270). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

JUMU'AH AUR 'EidAIN KE MASA-IL

Jumu'ah Ke Din Jumu'ah Mubarak Kahna

Sawal: Kya Jumu'ah ke din Jumu'ah Mubarak kahna shara'an durust hai?

Jawab: Jumu'ah ka din yaqeenan ba barkat din hai is barkat ke izhar ke liye ya bataur du'aa kisi ko Jumu'ah Mubarak kehna fi nafsihi jaaiz to hai magar chunke sahabah aur salaf Saliheen se yeh saabit nahi hai is liye 'Ulama is ko riwaj dene se mana'a karte hain. Banda ki naaqis raye yeh hai ke is ko ma'amool na banana chahiye, albatta agar koi is niyyat se kahe ke aap ko Jumu'ah ke din ki barkatain haasil hon to is ki gunjaaish hogi, lekin agar koi is ko sunnat samajh kar kahta hai to jaaiz nahi. Wallahu A'alam (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/105). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Jumu'ah Ki Namaz Ke Shara'et

Sawal: Jumu'ah ki namaz sahih hone ke liye kya shara'et hain?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek Jumu'ah ki namaz sahih hone ke liye cheh (6) Shara'et ka paya jana zaroori hai. (1) Waqt: Jumu'ah ka waqt wahi hai jo zuhr ki namaz ka waqt hai. (2) Iqaamat: Aabadi ki jagah Jumu'ah ada ki jaaye. (3) ek hi basti me ek se zayed Jumu'ah na ho. (4) Chalis (40) aadmiyon ka hona: Chalis se kam ki soorat me Jumu'ah munaqid na hogi. (5) Jumu'ah ko ba-jamaa-'at ada kiya jaaye. (6) Namaz se pehle do khutbe hon. (Al-Fiqhul Minhijji: 1/193). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khutbah Jumu'ah Se Pehle Azaan Ki Jagah

Sawal: Khutba-e- Jumu'ah se pehle Mu'azzin kahan khada ho kar azaan de?

Jawab: Jumu'ah ke din jab imaam mimber par chadh kar salaam karke baith jaaye to mustahab hai ke Mu'azzin imaam ke bilkul samne khade ho kar azaan de. (Al-Waseet: 2/283). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khutba E Jumu'ah Ke Shara'et

Sawal: Khutba e Jumu'ah ke sahih hone ke kya shara'et hain?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazddek khutbah ke sahih hone ke liye panch (5) cheezon ka paya jana zaroori hai yeh khutbah ke arkaan kehlate hain. (1) Dono Khutbon me lafze Hamd ka maaddah aur lafze Allah ke saath Allah Ta'aala ki hamd karna “Al-hamdulillah/ Ahmadullah.” (2) Dono Khutbon me lafze Salah ka maaddah aur Aap (ﷺ) ke asmaye mubarakah me se kisi bhi isme Mubarak zaahir ke saath darood shareef padhna “Allahumma Salli 'Alaa Muhammad (اَللّٰهُمَّ صَلِّى عَلىٰ مُحَمَّدٍ) ya Usalli Ala Muhammad (أُصَلِّيْ عَلىٰ مُحَمَّدٍ)” albatta zameer ke saath darood shareef “Sallallahu alaihi wa sallam” padhna kafi nahi. (3) Dono khutbon me taqwa ki wasiyyat karna. (4) Doosre Khutbe me momineen ke liye umoore aakhirat se muta-'alliq du'aa mangna (5) Do Khutbon me se kisi ek me Qur-aan padhna kam se kam ek aayate mufhimah zaroori hai aur khutbah ke aakhir me padhna afzal aur masnoon hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/197). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khutba-E-Jumu'ah Ke Dauran Sunnat Padhna

Sawal: Kya Jumu'ah ke khutbe ke dauran sunnat namaz padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs Khutba-e-Jumu'ah ke dauran masjid me dakhil ho to use do rak'at Tahiyyatul Masjid padhna mustahab hai aur in do rak'at ko mukhtasar padhna waajib hai is tarah ke jise 'urf me taweel na kaha jaaye ya'ani namaz ke aqwaal wa af'aal me se sirf waajibaat ko ada kare. (Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah / Bushral-Kareem:1/12). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Jumu'ah ke khutbe ke dauran sunnat padh sakte hain lekin Jumu'ah farz hai aur farz ke hote waqt sunnat kaise ada kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Muslim shareef ki hadees me hai ke jab Hazrat Sulaik Gitfaani (Rz) Jumu'ah ke din masjid me dakhil huye aur baith gaye daranhaale ke Huzoore Aqdas (ﷺ) khutbah de rahe the to Huzoor Aqdas (ﷺ) ne un se farmaya ke khade ho jao aur halki phulki do rak'at padho is hadees ki bunyad par imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Jumu'ah ke khutbe ke dauran do rak'at sunnat padh sakte hain jis me sirf waajibat ko ada kare (Saheeh Muslim: 875, Bushral-Kareem:1/182). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Jumu'ah ke doosre khutbe ke dauran masjid me dakhil ho jaaye to use Tahiyyatul Masjid padhni chahiye ya nahi?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs Jumu'ah ki namaz ke liye aise waqt me masjid me dakhil ho jab ke imaam dusra khutbah de raha ho to wo dekhega ke imaam ki ibtida me ya inteha me agar khutbe ki ibtidah kiya ho aur itna waqt ho ke khutbah khatam hone se pehle halki aur mukhtasar do rak'at padh kar faarig ho sakta ho to wo do rak'at padh kar baith jaaye aur agar imaam doosre khutbe ka aakhari hissa padh raha ho to Tahiyyatul Masjid na padhe balke khade rah kar intizar kare take Jumu'ah ki takbeere woola faut na ho aur waise bhi Jumu'ah ki namaz me shaamil hone ki soorat me Tahiyyatul Masjid ka sawab khud bakhud haasil ho jaayega (Al-bayan: 2/596, Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah: 1/310, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/102). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya Hanafi Maslak me bhi yahi hukm hai?

Jawab: Ahnaaf ke nazdeek un ke apne dalayil hain jin ki buniyad par un ke yahan daurane khutbah sunnat wagayrah padhna mana'a hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Jumu'ah Me Do Khutbon Ke Darmiyan Du'aa Karna

Sawal: Do khutbon ke darmiyan jab imaam baith jaata hai to us waqt kya karna afzal hai?

Jawab: Jab imaam do khutbon ke darmiyan baith jaaye to use ikhtiyar hai ke koi zikr ya Qur-aane majeed ki tilaawat kare, albatta du'aa karna behtar hai isliye ke Jumu'ah ke din ek aysi ghadi hai jis me du'aa qabool hoti hai aur yeh ghadi imaam ke namaz ke liye nikalne se namaz mukammal hone tak ke waqt me ziyadah mumkin hai aur do khutbon ke darmiyan qabooliyate du'aa ka ziyadah imkaan hai, lekin du'aa karne me yeh khayal rahe ke aahista se kare take kisi ko tashweesh (Uljhaw) na ho (Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 6/140). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Sawal: Jumu'ah ke khutbe ke aakhir me jo du'aa hoti hai kya us waqt haath utha sakte hain?

Jawab: Imaam Nawawi (Rh) farmate hai: Khutbe ke waqt du'aa me haath na uthana sunnat hai. (Sharah Muslim: 6/162). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

English Ya Kisi Aur Zubaan Me Jumu'ah Ka Khutbah

Sawal: Kya Khutba-e-Jumu'ah Urdu ya apni maadari zabaan me de sakte hain? Kyunke kokan me aksar log 'arabi nahi jante jis ki wajah se bahut sare afraad Khutba-e-Jumu'ah ke dauran onghte hain jis se khutbah ka maqsad faut hota hai.

Jawab: Jumu'ah ke khutbon ke sahih hone ke liye dono Khutbon ke tamam arkaan ka 'arabi me hona shart hai, kyunke yeh namaz ki Takbeere tahrimah (Allahu Akbar) ki tarah farz zikr hai jaise Takbeere tahrimah ka 'arabi me hona shart hai, Jumu'ah ke dono khutbon ka 'arabi me hona shart hai, aur uske liye 'arabi zubaan padhna seekhna farze kifayah ke darje mie hai. Lihaaza khutbah sunne wale sama'een 'arabi zubaan ke tarjume se nawaqif hon, tab bhi Khutbah sahih ho jaayega, bas sunne walon me itna faham kafi hai ke wo yeh samjhein ke khateeb wa'az wa nasihhat kar raha hai. Albatta agar Jumu'ah ki namaz aise 'ajami ('arabi ke 'alaawah) 'alaqe me ada ki ja rahi ho jahan ke rehne walon ki zubaan 'arabi bolne me pala-ti na ho aur bahut girani aur mushkil mehsoos karte hon to unke liye yeh 'uzr hoga aur waqti taur par ya'ani khutbah ke arkan ki adayigi ki had (limit) tak 'arabi zubaan padhna seekhne tak in me se koi ek apni 'ajami zubaan me khutbah de to is ki gunjaish hogi, is soorat me khutbah ka ek rukn ya'ani ek aayate mufhima ki tilaawat karna to is ki jagah par khateeb apni zubaan me zikr wa du'aa karega. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/27). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kuwait ki ek masjid me har Jumu'ah ke liye duniya ke mukhtalif scholar me se koi na koi English khutbah dene wale aate hain, is masjid me aksar ta'aleem yaaftah log namaz ke liye jate hain, in ki yeh soach hoti hai ke 'arabi me khutbah dene ke baad aksar logon ko pata hi nahi chalta ke khateeb kya wa'az kar rahe hain, khutbah samajhna zaroori hai take hame sahih 'ilm haasil ho jaaye, kya daure sahabah me isi tarah taba'een ke daur me jo ajami (gair Arab) the aur jin ko 'arabi samajh nahi aati thi to kya us waqt un ki zubaan me hota tha ya arabi me?

Jawab: Jumu'ah ke khutbon ke sahih hone ke liye khutbon ke sirf arkan ka 'arabi zubaan me hona shart hai, uske 'alaawah nasihhat ki deegar batein doosri zubaan me bayan karna jaaiz hai.

Khutbon ka 'arabi zubaan me hona yeh Aslaaf ya'ani Sahaba-e-Kiraam (Rz) aur taba'een aur tabe taba'een ke daur se musalsal chala aa raha hai, jaisa ke fiqhi ibarat se wazah hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/317, I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 2/79). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jumu'ah Se Pehle Sunnat Namaz

Sawal: Ek baar mujhe mere khaalaazaad bhai jo bairun me rehta hai, us ne kaha ke Jumu'ah se pehle sunnat nahi hai, mai ne us se kaha ke imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek sunnat hai. Lekin mai ne use koi daleel nahi batayi thi. Kya kisi hadees me Jumu'ah se pehle sunnat namaz ka zikr hai? Ya yeh qiyas ki bunyad par hai?

Jawab: Dono hi hain riwaayat bhi hai aur zuhr par qiyas bhi hai. (Fathul-Mu'een: 1/169). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Middle East me zawal / Zuhr ka waqt shuru' hote hi doosri azaan khutbah ki hoti hai, is liye zawal se pehle Jumu'ah ki sunnat padhte hain kyunke phir waqt nahi milta, kya zawal se pehle Jumu'ah ki sunnat padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Aysi soorat me Jumu'ah se pehle ki sunnat baad me padhe. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Jumu'ah Ki Namaz Me Doosri Rak'at Ke Ruku Ke Baad Milna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Jumu'ah ki namaz me imaam se doosri rak'at ruku ke baad mile to us ke namaz ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) riwaayat karte hain ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne irshad farmaya jis kisi ne Jumu'ah ki ek rak'at paa li to us ko chahiye ke wo doosri rak'at padh le (Sunan -e- Ibne Maajah: 1121).

Fuqahaa ne is hadees se istidlal kiya hai ke jo shakhs Jumu'ah ki doosri rak'at ke ruku me imaam ke saath shaamil ho jaaye to use Jumu'ah ki namaz haasil ho jaayegi. Lihaaza ab yeh shakhs sirf ek rak'at padh kar apni Jumu'ah ki namaz ko mukammal karega. Albatta agar wo doosri rak'at ke ruku ke baad imaam ke saath shaamil ho raha hai to aysi soorat me wo Jumu'ah ki niyyat ke saath namaz me sharik hoga aur imaam ke salaam pherne ke baad masbook ki tarah zuhr ki chaar rak'at ada karega. (Al-Majmoo': 4/555). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Jumu'ah Milne Ke Liye Aage Ki Saf Khali Rakhna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Jumu'ah ko daud kar jaaye take use kam se kam doosri rak'at ka ruku mile aur wo aage ki saf (jo khali hai) ke bajaaye peeche rak'at bandhne take ruku mile aur doosre log bhi uske saath mil jaayein, kya uski Jumu'ah hogi?

Jawab: Us ki Jumu'ah ki namaz sahih ho jaayegi aur Jumu'ah ki namaz pane ke liye aisa karna jaaiz hai. Safon ka durust rakhna aur unhein mukammal taur par pur karna Sharee'ate mutahharah ka ek hukm hai aur mustahab hai. Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne safon ke durust karne ko namaz ki takmeel qarar diya hai aur agli saf me jagah maujood hote huye pichli saf me khade hone se mana'a farmaya hai.

Jumu'ah ke 'alaawah deegar namazon ki adayigi ke waqt sirf rak'at pane ke liye agli saf me jagah maujood hote huye peeche ki saf me niyyat bandh lena sakht makrooh 'amal hai. Sukun wa etminaan se agli saf me hi ja kar namaz padhna chahiye khwah kitni hi rak'atain nikal jaayein. Iske bawajood pichli saf me padhi gai namaz ada ho jaayegi kyunke yeh jagah masjid me hi hone ki wajah se ittisal-e-sufoof (continuity of rows) qaa-im rahega aur Iqtidaa durust hogi. (Akhrajuhul Bukhari: 783, Abu Dawood: 683, Saheeh Muslim: 438, Sunane Abu Dawood: 671, Al-Majmoo': 4/301). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ramazan Ke Aakhari Jumu'ah Aur Khutbah Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Hamare yahan ramazan ke aakhari Jumu'ah aur khutbah ko bahut ahmiyat di jati hai, is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Alwada'ayi Jumu'ah aur alwada'ayi khutbah padhne ki Sharee'at me koi haqeeqat nahi hai, bid'at hai, ise chod dena chahiye (Saheeh Muslim: 1718). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jumu'ah Ke Din 'Eid

Sawal: Kal Kuwait ki masjid me ek shaikh ne kaha ke 'Eid agar Jumu'ah ko aaye aur agar eid ke khutbah me shaamil huye to Jumu'ah ke khutbah me shaamil hone ki zaroorat nahi aur zuhr ada kar sakte hain, aisa Rasoolullah ne kiya tha. Kya yeh sahih hai? Agar sahih hai to zuhr kaise ada kare?

Jawab: Yeh baaz 'Ulama ka qaul hai, lekin aksar is ke qayil (manne wale) nahi hain, shawafe' wa ahnaaf ke nazdeek bhi Jumu'ah hi padhna zaroori hai aur jis riwaayat me wo baat aayi hai us ke mukhatab door daraz gaon se aaye huye log hain jin par Jumu'ah farz hi nahi tha, aur wo 'Eid ke liye Madinah aaye the to Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne unhein yeh ijazat marhamat farmayi ke wo chaahe to laut jaayein aur apne elaaqe me zuhr padhein, Jumu'ah tak Madinah me rukne ki zaroorat nahi. Abu Dawood: 1073 ki riwaayat me tasrih hai ke Huzoor (ﷺ) ne farmaya: Hum Jumu'ah padhainge (Kitabul Um:2/512, Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/586, Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah:3/182). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

'Eid Ke Din Musafaha Aur Mubarak Badi Dena

Sawal: 'Eid ki namaz ke baad musafaha aur Mubarak badi ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: 'Eid ke din aapas me ek doosre ko mubarakbaadi dena aur musafah karna mubaaha hai isi tarah musalman bhai ki mulaqat ke waqt khushi ka izhar karna aur khushi ke liye rishtedaron ki mulaaqat karna mandub / mustahab hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Eid-Ul-Fitr Ke Mauqe Par 'Eidi Dena

Sawal: Kya 'Eid ke dino me chote Bachchon ko 'Eidi dena shar'an durust hai?

Jawab: 'Eid ke dino me 'Eidi mangna to durust nahi, albatta bachchon, maatahton (under authority), mulazimeen wagayrah ko apni istita'at ke mutabiq apni khushi se hadiyah (gift) ke taur par 'Eidi dena sirf jaaiz hi nahi balke mustahasan (Achcha) amar hai. “Imam abu Hanifah (Rh) ke ustaad imaam Hammad (Rh) panch sau afrad ko ek ek joda aur ek ek sau dirham 'Eid ke din enaayat kiya karte the, jaisa ke “Siyaru A'alamin Nubalaa” nami kitab me mazkoor hai, isi tarah yeh maKareem-e- akhlaque (achche akhlaque) me se bhi hai, jis ke sabab ek musalman khushi mehsoos karta hai aur kisi musalman ke dil ko khush karna razaye Ilahi ka sabab hai. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mastoorat Ke Liye 'Eidain Ki Namaz Ka Hukm

Sawal: Jis tarah mard ke liye 'Eid ki namaz sunnat hai isi tarah kya hamari maa, bahnon ko bhi 'Eid ki namaz sunnat hai?

Jawab: 'auraton ke liye bhi 'Eidain ki namaz padhna mustahab hai, is ka waqt Tulu'-e-Aaftab se shuru' hota hai aur zawaal tak baaqi rahta hai. (Al-Iqna':1/258). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Hanafi Imaam Ke Peeche 'Eid-Ul-Fitr Ki Namaz

Sawal: Agar muqtadi Shafi'ee ho to Hanafi imaam ke peeche 'Eid-ul-fitr ki namaz kaise padhega?

Jawab: Hanafi imaam ki Iqtidaa me 'Eidain ki namaz padhna durust hai. Shawafe' aur ahnaaf ki namaze 'Eid me farq yeh hai ke ahnaaf ke nazdeek dono rak'aat me zaa-id (excess) takbeerat teen hain aur doosri rak'at me zaa-id takbeerat qiraat ke baad hai aur shawafe' ke nazdeek zaa-id takbeerat dono raka'aat me qiraat se qabl hain, aur pehli rak'at me saat aur doosri rak'at me panch takbeerat hain. Ab agar koi Shafi'ee shakhs hanafi imaam ke peeche 'Eid-ul-fitr ki namaz padh raha ho to Shafi'ee muqtadi hanafi imaam ki mu'afaqat me dono raka'aat me teen hi takbeerat kahega aur teen se zaa-id takbeerat nahi kahega, isi tarah doosri rak'at me bhi imaam ki mu'afaqat me qiraat ke baad hi teen takbeerein kahega albatta ehtiyat yeh kare ke takbeeron ke saath musalsal / pe dar pe bila fasal rafa' yadain na kare warna namaz baatil ho jaayegi is soorat me behtar yahi hoga ke sirf takbeerat kahe haath na uthaye (Haashiyatush Sharwani:3/42). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Eid Ke Ayyam Me Namaze Janazah Ke Baad Takbeer

Sawal: 'Eid ke ayyam me agar namaze janazah padhi jaaye to kya is ke baad bhi takbeer padhna sunnat hai?

Jawab: Ji han, 'Eid ke ayyam me padhi jaane wali namaze janazah ke baad bhi takbeerate tashreeq padhna masnoon hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:3/53, Tuhfatul-Bari). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur 'Ilmi Batein - Jild Duwam

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il ki doosri jild anqareeb (soon) Insha Allah manzar-e-'aam par aane ki kagaar par hai. Pecheeda, jadeed aur nit naye masa-il par nazar-e-saani jaari hai. Yeh doosri jild Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare aur Mufti Ishaq Patel ki koshishon ka samarah hai.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

JANAZEH KE MASA-IL

Rishteydaron Ko Maut Ki Khabar Dena

Sawal: Doosre bastiyon me mayyit ki khabar dena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Tajheez wa takfeen me shaamil hone aur du'aa-e-magfirat ke liye a'izzaa wa aqaarib (loved ones and family members) aur musalmanon ko khabar dene me koi haraj nahi hai. Muslim shareef me hai ke Nabi (ﷺ) ne najaashi ki wafaat ke din logon ko unki wafaat ki khabar bhijwayi (Minhaajut-Talibeen:1/351, Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 1245). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Wasiyyat Ke Zariye Kisi Mayyit Par Aane Se Rokna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs yeh wasiyyat kare ke mere marne ke baad meri mayyat par fulan shakhs na aaye to kya us ki wasiyyat ka e'etibaar hoga?

Jawab: Kisi shakhs ke liye yeh sahih nahi ke wo is tarah ki wasiyyat kare jis se kisi ko shara'ee hukm par 'amal karne se roka jaaye, is tarah ki wasiyyat karne walon ko mutanabbah karna chahiye ke is tarah ki wasiyyat na kare lihaaza is tarah ki wasiyyat ka koi e'etibaar nahi hoga aur jis shakhs ko aane se mana'a kiya gaya hai us ke liye mayyat par aana durust hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:3/48, Al-Muatamid: 4/541). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Gair Muslim Ki Maut Par RIP (Rest In Peace) Kehna

Sawal: Kya kisi gair Muslim ki maut par angrezi me RIP (Rest In Peace) kehna durust hai?

Jawab: Gairon ke intiqal ke baad RIP (Rest In Peace) Bhawu purna sharadhanjali ya is tarah ke du'aaiyyah kalimaat kehna jaaiz nahi albatta un ki wafaat par ta'ziyat karna, duniyawi e'etibaar se tasalli ke alfaz aur sabr ki talqeen karna jaaiz hai. Marne wale kafir ke liye du'aaiyyah kalimaat isti''amal na kare kyunke yeh islaami aqeedah ke khilaaf hai (Muat ke baad kaamyab aur pur sukun zindagi sirf Iman wale ke liye hai. (Haashiyatush Sharwani: 3/75, Al-Majmoo': 5/144). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mayyit Ke Peshaani Ko Bosah Dena

Sawal: Kya mayyit ke peshaani ko bosah dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Mayyit ke rishteydaaron aur doston ke liye mayyit ke chehre se kapda hatana aur peshani ko bosa dena jaaiz hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:4/217).

Aap (ﷺ) ne Hazrat Usman bin Maz'uoon (Rz) ko un ki wafaat ke baad bosah liya. (Tirmiz: 989)

Hazrat Abu Bakr (Rz) ne Aap (ﷺ) ke Jabeen-e-Mubarak ko wafaat ke baad bosah liya tha. (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 3667). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Miyan Biwi Ka Ek Doosre Ko Gusl Dena

Sawal: Kya miyan biwi ek doosre ko wafaat ke baad gusl de sakte hai?

Jawab: Shohar apni biwi ko wafaat ke baad gusl de sakta hai, Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) se marvi hai ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaya: Aye 'Aayesha! Agar tum mujh se pehle faut ho gai to main tumhain gusl dunga (Ibne Maajah: 1454, Musnade Ahmed).

Isi tarah biwi apne shohar ko gusl de sakti hai jaisa ke Asma Binte Umais (Rz) ne apne shohar Hazrat Abu Bakr (Rz) ko gusl diya tha. (Mu'atta Imaam Malik: 466, Kitabul Umm: 1/311). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haizah (Menstruation) Aur Junubi 'Aurat Ka Mayyit Ko Gusl Dena

Sawal: Kya Haizah aur junubi 'aurat mayyit ko gusl de sakti hai?

Jawab: Mayyit ko gusl dene wale ka janaabat aur haiz wa Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) se paak hona shart nahi, lihaaza zaroorat ke waqt haalate janaabat wale mard wa 'aurat isi tarah haiz wa Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) wali 'aurat bhi bila karaahat mayyit ko gusl de sakti hai. (Fathul-Wahhab: 1/108). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haizah (Menstruation) Aur Junubi 'Aurat Ke Intiqaal Par Gusl

Sawal: Agar haizah ya junubi 'aurat ka intiqaal ho jaaye to uske gusl me kya niyyat ki jaaye?

Jawab: Mayyit ko gusl dene wale ke liye gusl ki niyyat karna zaroori nahi hai balke sunnat hai, lihaaza haizah ya junubi 'aurat ka intiqaal ho jaaye to gusl dene wale ke liye mayyit ka gusl ya janaabat wa haiz ka gusl in me se kisi ek ki niyyat karna sunnat hai. (Al-Manhaj: 3/99). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Mayyit Ka Deedaar Karana

Sawal: Hamare 'alaqe kokan me bahut ehtimaam se mayyit ka deedaar karaya jaata hai, is ki shar'an kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) farmati hain ke (jab Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ki wafaat ho gai) Abu Bakr (Rz) apne ghar se jo maqame SUNH (jagaah ka naam) me tha ghode par sawaar ho kar aaye aur utarte hi masjid me tashreef le gaye. Phir Aap kisi se guftagu kiye bagair 'Aayesha (Rz) ke hujre me aaye (Jahan Nabi Kareen ﷺ ki naa'sh Mubarak rakhi hui thi) aur Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ki taraf gaye. Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ko Burd Hibrah (Yaman ki bani hui dharidar chaadar) se dhank diya gaya tha. Phir Aap ne Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ka chehra Mubarak khola aur jhuk kar us ka bosah liya aur rone lage. Aap ne kaha mere maa baap aap par qurbaan hon aye Allah ke Nabi! Allah Ta'aala do mautein aap par kabhi jama'a nahi karega. Siway ek maut ke jo aap ke muqaddar me thi so aap wafaat pa chuke (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 1242).

Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) bayan karti hain ke Hazrat Abu Bakr Siddique (Rz) ne Aap (ﷺ) ke wisaal ke baad Aap ki dono aankhon ke darmiyan (ya'ani mathe) par bosah diya tha. (Aizan: 1839).

Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Main ne tumhein qabron ki ziyaarat se roka tha. Ab Muhammed ko apni Maan ki qabr ki ziyaarat ki ijaazat de di gai hai. To tum bhi un ki ziyaarat karo, yeh cheez aakhirat ko yaad dilaati hai (Jaami'ut Tirmizi: 1054).

Mazkoorah ahadees se yeh baat wazeh hoti hai ke Hazrat Abu Bakr Siddique (Rz) ne Aap (ﷺ) ki naa'sh Mubarak ki ziyaarat ki, aur peshani ka bosah liya. Is hadees se mayyit ki ziyaarat ke jawaaz ka aur mustahab hone ka suboot milta hai. Neez hadees me ziyaarate quboor ka jawaaz bhi milta hai. Is ki 'illat yeh bataayi gai hai ke yeh cheez aakhirat ko yaad dilaati hai. Aur mayyit ki ziyaarat ka asal maqsad bhi yahi hai. Balke ta'seer ke e'etibaar se ziyaarate mayyit ziyaarate quboor se ablag (ziyadah asardar) hai. Lihaaza ziyaarate mayyit jaaiz hi nahi balke mustahab bhi hai. Neez fuqhaye kiraam ne taqbeele mayyit ko rishtadar aur aqaarib ke liye masnoon aur un ke 'alaawah ke liye jaaiz likkha hai. Lekin yeh waazeh rahe ke e'atidal wa tawaazun ka lihaaz rakhkha jaaye aur mayyit ke deedaar ko waajib na samjha jaaye. Neez is ka is qadar bhi ehtimaam na kiya jaaye ke guloo ka shaibah ho. Isi tarah ajnabi mardon ke liye ajnabi 'aurat ka deedaar aur us ke bar'aks soorat bhi haraam hai. Wallahu A'alamu bis sawab.

(Al-'Aziz Sharahul Wajeez: 11/378, Hashiyatut Tirmizi: 4/634, Bushral-Kareem:1-2/376). Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare

Namaze Janazah Me Do (2) Salaam

Sawal: Imaam Ahmed Bin Hambal (Rh) ke nazdeek janaaze ki namaz me ek hi salaam hai aur imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke yahan do salaam, is ki kya wajah hai?

Jawab: Har ek ke apne dala'il hain jis ki bunyad par ikhtilaaf hai. Hanabilah (Rh) ke nazdeek ek salaam Sahaba-e-Kiraam (Rz) ke asar se saabit hai taqreeban cheh Sahaba-e-Kiraam (Rz) ek (1) salaam phera karte the aur shawafe' wa ahnaaf ke nazdeek do salaam hain. Yeh Hazrat ibne Masood (Rz) ki riwaayat ki wajah se hai wo farmate hain ke teen kaam aise hain jin ko Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) kiya karte the lekin logon ne unhein chod diya un me se ek kaam Janazah par salaam hai, deegar namazon ki tarah ya'ani jaise deegar namazon me do salaam pherte hain, usi tarah Janazeh me bhi do salaam hain lekin logon ne ek salaam pherna shuru' kiya (Al-Sharahul Kabeer Aliul Maqna'a: 6/157, Al-Majmoo': 5/239). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Namaze Janazah Jahran (Zor Se) Padhna

Sawal: Kya janaze ki namaz jahran (zor se) padhi ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Janaze ki namaz jahran padhna masnoon nahi hai albatta ta'aleeme ummat ke liye jahran padhna jaaiz hai, ek martabah sirf Hazrat 'Abdullah ibne Abbas (Rz) ne qiraat ya'ani surah fatiha jahran padhi thi. Usi waqt deegar maujood logon ne pucha ke aap ne jahran kyun padhi to farmaya ke take logon ko ma'aloom ho jaye ke namaze janazah me qiraat ya'ani surah fatiha padhi jani chahiye. Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) padha karte the. Aimmaa-e-Arba'ah (Rh) ke nazdeek namaze Janazah sirran hi padhna masnoon hai. Aur yeh sirf Aimmaa-e-Arba'ah (Rh) ka maslak nahi hai balke muta'addad salafi 'Ulama (Ahle Hadees) ki tasrih bhi isi tarah ki mili hai (Ahkaamul-Jana'iz wa Bida'uhaa: 120/mas-alah 78, Fatawa Nur 'ali ad-Darb: 14/15) (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/23). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ka Namaze Janazah Padhna

Sawal: Jis tarah mard ke liye namaze Janazah padhna farz hai usi tarah kya 'aurat bhi janaaze ki namaz padh sakti hai? Jamaa-'at ke saath ya alaahidah?

Jawab: 'Auraton ke liye bhi namaze Janazah ba-jamaa-'at padhna masnoon hai (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/27, Haashiyatush Sharwani: 3/148) Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ne Hazrat Saad bin Abi Waqqas (Rz) ki namaz-e-Janazah padhi hai, wo un ke mahram bhi nahi the. Sirf Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ne nahi, balke azwaaje Mutaharat (Rz) ne ek saath Hazrat Saad bin Abi Waqqas (Rz) ki namaze Janazah padhi hai. (Muslim Shareef: 973). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Kya sirf 'auraton ka namaz-e-Janazah padhna kafi hai?

Jawab: 'Auraton ka namaze janazah padhna jaaiz hai lekin sirf 'auraton ke namaze Janazah padhne se farz kifaayah saaqit nahi hoga, mardon ka Janazah padhna zaroori hai. Agar us elaaqe me mard hazraat na ho to majboori me 'auraton ka namaz-e-janazah padhna kaafi hoga. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 3/193-194). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qabristaan Me Namaze Janazah Padhna

Sawal: Agar koi namaze Janazah me shareek na ho aur wo sidha qabristaan me dafnaane ke waqt pahonche to kya wo qabristaan me us ki qabr par namaze Janazah padh sakta hai? Kyunke bahut logon ki yeh samajh hoti hai ke qabristan me namaz padhna haraam hai.

Jawab: Qabr par namaz-e-janazah padhna ahadees se saabit hai is liye is me koi haraj nahi (Bahrul-Mazhab: 2/591, Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 458, Muslim: 956). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya qabristan me qabr ke paas ijtimayi namaz-e-janazah padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Qabr par infiraadi (individual) aur ijtimaa'i dono tarah se Janazah ki namaz padhna sahih hai chahe pehle ho chuki ho (Bahrul-Mazhab: 2/591). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Namaz-E-Janazah Gaaibanah Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya kisi doosre shahar ya mulk me gaaibanah namaz-e-janazah ada kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Jis tarah Janazah samne rakh kar us ki namaz padhna ya dafan ke baad qabr par us ke liye namaz-e-janazah padhna saabit hai usi tarah Janazah kisi shahar me ho aur us ke jaan pehechaan wale doosre shahar me hon to wo gaaibanah namaz-e-janazah ada kar sakte hain.

Saheeh Al-Bukhari me Hazrat Jabir bin 'Abdullah (Rz) se marvi hai ke unhon ne bayaan kiya ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaya: Aaj Habash ke ek mard-e-Saleh (Najaashi Habash ke badshah) ka intiqaal ho gaya hai, aao un ki namaz-e-janazah padho, Hazrat Jabir (Rz) ne bayaan kiya ke phir hum ne saff bandi kar li aur Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne un ki namaz-e-janazah padhayi (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 2/433, Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 3877). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Doosre Mamaalik me mayyit ke dafan se pehle namaz gaaibanah padhne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Namaz Gaaibanah padhne ke liye tahaarat aur gusl shart hai, agar mayyit ko gusl diya gaya ho to phir namaz-e-janazah padh sakte hain chahe mayyit dafan ki gai ho ya dafan se pehle ho albatta gusl se pehle namaz-e-janazah padhna sahi nahi hogi. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 2/207, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/485). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Dobarah Namaz-e-janazah Padhna

Sawal: Kisi shakhs ka intiqaal ho jaaye phir uski namaz-e-janazah shahar me padhi jaaye aur phir mayyit ko tadfeen ke liye us ke apne gaon laya jaaye, jahan par wapas namaz-e-janazah padhi jaaye, kya Soorate mas-uoolah me Janazah ki namaz padha huwa shakhs dobarah namaz-e-janazah padh sakta hai aur imaam ban kar padha sakta hai?

Jawab: Aise shakhs ka ke jis ki mayyit par namaz-e-janazah padhi ho dobarah us mayyit par us shakhs ka namaz-e-janazah padhna mustahab nahi lekin agar padhna chahe to jaaiz hai. Soorate mas-uoolah me agar wo shakhs jis ne shahar me namaz padhai ho ya padhi yo aur dobarah gaon me namaz-e-janazah padhe to namaz sahi hogi, albatta behtar hai ke dobarah namaz-e-janazah na padhe aur na padhaye kyunke yeh mustahab nahi hai. (Al-Majmoo': 5/246). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shi'aah Ki Mayyit Par Namaz-e-janazah Padhna

Sawal: Kya kisi shi'aah ki mayyit par jaane ke baad us ki namaz-e-janazah padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Shi'aah me bahut se firqe hain jin me se bahut so ke aqaaid kufriyah hain jin ke aqaaid kufriya hain un ka musalmanon me shumaar nahi hoga, lihaaza in ki namaz-e-janazah padhna durust nahi, albatta jin ke aqaaid kufriyah na hon un ki namaz-e-janazah padhne me koi haraj nahi. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Siqt Bachchah Ke Gusl, Kafan, Namaz Aur Dafan Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Agar cheh (6) maheene se pehle bachchah gir jaaye to gusl, kafan, namaz-e-janazah aur dafan ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Cheh (6) maheene mukammal hone se pehle bachchah gir jaaye to use siqt kehte hai. Siqt ki teen haltein hain.

Cheh (6) Maheene Ka Mara Huwa Bachchah Paida Hona

Sawal: Agar bachchah cheh (6) maheene ka mara huwa paida ho to gusl, kafan, namaz-e-janazah aur dafan ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Agar bachchah cheh (6) mah mukammal hone ke baad paida ho aur mar jaaye ya murdah haalat me paida ho jaaye to wahi hukm hoga jo ek bade shakhs ki maut ke baad hota hai ya'ani gusl, kafan, namaz-e-janazah padhna aur dafnana zaroori hai. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 3/210).

Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) farmati hain: Ansaar ka ek faut shudah bachchah Rasool (ﷺ) ke paas laaya gaya to Aap (ﷺ) ne us ki namaz-e-janazah padhi (An-Nasa'i: 1947). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kaate Huye 'Uzwa (Organ) Ko Dafnana

Sawal: Agar kisi ke jism ka koi 'uzwa (organ) kisi bimaari ki wajah se kaat diya jaaye to use dafnana waajib hai ya koi aur mas-alah hai? Agar koi na dafnaye to kya wo gunahgaar hoga?

Jawab: Insaani jism ke wo a'azaa jin ko zindagi me kaat kar agal kar diya gaya ho, imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek un ko qabristaan me ya paak zameen me dafan karna mustahab hai. Mustahab ko agar tarak kar diya jaaye aur chod diya jaaye to gunah nahi hoga. DNC ka mas-alah alag hoga. (Al-Majmoo': 5/254). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jal Kar Mar Jane, Car Haadisah Wagayrah Ki Wajah se Post-Mortem Ke Baad Gusl Dene Ka Hukm

Sawal: Jal kar mar jane, car haadisah wagayrah ki wajah se Post-Martem ke baad mayyit ko gusl dene ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Mayyit ko gusl dene ka matlab yeh hai ke uske pure badan pa paani baha dena, haath lagana koi zaroori nahi, aur yeh gusl farze kifayah bhi hai, aur is par namaze janazah padhne ke liye shart bhi, to jab tak is par paani bahana mumkin ho, paani bahaye warnah tayammum karaaye, lekin agar tayammum karaana bhi mumkin na ho jaise mayyit jal kar rakh ho gai ho, ya samandar ki gehrai me chali gai ho ya darinde ne mukammal kha liya ho to muta-akkhireen fukhaye shawafe' ke nazdeek bagair gusl diye bhi is par namaze Janazah padhna jaaiz hoga. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/58-59, 'Umdatul Mufti Wal-Mustafti: 1/240, Al-Majmoo':5/178, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 3/203). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Qabristaan Jana

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ke liye qabristaan jana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: 'Aurat ka qabristaan jana mamnu' aur makrooh hai. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb:2/301). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Qabristaan Me Surah Baqrah Ki Ibtidai Aur Aakhri Aayatein Padhna

Sawal: Mayyit dafan karne ke baad kya sir ki jaanib khade ho kar sureh baqarah ki ibtidai aayatein aur pair ki jaanib khade ho kar aakhri aayatein padhne ki koi fazeelat hai?

Jawab: Surah Baqrah ki shuru' ki aur aakhir ki aayatein padhna sunan baihaqi (4/93) me maujood hai lihaaza in aayaat ka padhna saabit hai. Warnah Qur-aan ki deegar aayaat padhna mustahab hai (Al-Majmoo':5/254). Mufti Fayyaz Barmare

Mayyit Ke Ghar Teen Din E'htimaam Ke Saath Khane Ka Intizaam Karna

Sawal: Aaj kal hamare elaaqe me yeh riwaaj ban gaya hai ke jis ghar me mayyit hoti hai to mayyit ke rishtedar teen din e'htimaam ke saath ghar walon ke saath saath padosi aur dost ahbaab ke liye khane ka intizam karte hain, yeh shar'an kahan tak durust hai?

Jawab: Mayyit ke ghar walon ka khana pakane aur logon ko khilane me mashgul rehna jaisa ke aaj bahut si jaghon par hota hai yeh koi shara'ee hukm aur mashru' 'amal nahi hai, aur ise sunnat samajh kar ya deen ka hissa samajh kar karna sharee'at ke hukm ki khilaf warzi hai, kyunke asal sunnat yahi hai ke Janazah me shareek hone wale baaz log khana apne saath lakar mayyit ke ghar walon ko de ya un ko apne ghar bulaye…….isi tarah khana itna ziyaadah dena mustahab hai ke un ke liye raat aur din ke liye kaafi ho jaaye, jaisa ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne Hazrat Jafar Bin Abi Taalib (Rz) ki shahadat ki khabar par farmaaya tha ke: Jafar ke ghar walon ke liye khana banao kyunke is khabar ne un ko mashgul kar diya hai. (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 998, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/263). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

QUR-AAN-E-KAREEM KE MASA-IL

'Arabi Ke 'Alaawah Rasmul Khat Me Qur-aan Ko Likhna Padhna

Sawal: Kya Qur-aan ka transliterate karna jaaiz hai? Ya'ani kya 'arabi ke 'alaawah doosri zubaan ke rasmul Khat me Qur-aan ko likhna padhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Qur-aan ko 'arabi ke 'alaawah kisi aur zubaan me likhna padhna jaaiz nahi (Al-fatawa Al-fiqhiyyatul Kubra Li-Ibn Hajar: 1/37-38, Al-Fatawa al-Hadisiyyah:164). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Kya agar koi doosri zubaan me likkha huwa Qur-aan padh raha hai to kya use rok sakte hain ke yeh sahi nahi hai?

Jawab: Likhna jaaiz nahi hai, dusra padh raha ho to us ko yeh kehte huye mana'a kiya ja sakta hai ke yeh galat tareeqah par likkha huwa Qur-aan hai. Wallahu A'alam. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Tape Recorder Ki Padhi Hui Sooratul Baqarah Ki Fazeelat

Sawal: Ghar se shayaateen ko bhagaane ke liye agar surah baqarah ki tilaawat ko tape recorder ke zariye suna jaaye to yeh fazeelat haasil hogi?

Jawab: Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya ke: shaitaan us ghar se door bhaag jaata hai jis ghar me surah baqarah ko padha jaata ho, riwaayat me padhne ki fazeelat aayi hai na ke sunne ki, lihaaza yeh fazeelat radio ya audio cassettes se sunne ke zariye haasil nahi hogi. Albatta tap recorder se Qur-aan ki tilaawat ko sunne se khair wa barkat zaroor haasil hogi aur ajr bhi milega In Sha Allah. (Muslim: 720). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Masjid Me Bahut Logon Ka Qur-aan Ki Tilaawat Karna

Sawal: Agar masjid me teen chaar aadmi tilaawate Qur-aan kar rahe hon aur un me se ek shakhs buland aawaz se tilaawat kar raha ho aur us ki aawaz doosron ke kanon me pahonch rahi ho to kya apni tilaawat chod kar doosron ki tilaawat sunna is soorat me zaroori hai? kyun ki mujhe kisi shakhs ne kaha ke jab koi tilaawat kare aur un ki tilaawat ki aawaz kanon tak pahonch rahi ho to is soorat me Qur-aan ki tilaawat sunna hum par farz hai. Kya yeh batein sahi hain?

Jawab: Yeh hukm us waqt hai jab ke hum imaam ki Iqtidaa me namaz padh rahe hon aur imaam qiraat kar raha ho to apni surah fatiha padh kar khaamosh ho jaaye aur gaur se kaan laga kar imaam ki tilaawat ko sune, isi tarah yeh hukm us waqt hai jab imaam mimbar par khutbah de raha ho, 'aam halaat me jab tamaam log tilaawat kar rahe hon to kisi ko bhi apni tilaawat band karne ki zaroorat nahi , albatta jo log faarig baithe hon unhain Qur-aan-e-kareem ki tilaawat ko gaur se sunna chahiye. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/22). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qur-aan Ki Tafseer Ya Tarjamah (Translation)

Sawal: Qur-aan ki tafseer ya tarjamah (translation) kin 'Ulama ki likhi hui padhna chahiye?

Jawab: Sahihul Aqeedah ahle Haqq 'Ulama-e-Rabbaniyeen ke zariye kiye gaye tarjamah (translation)e Qur-aan aur tafseer ko padhna chahiye jaise tafseer Ibn-e-Kaseer, Ma'aariful Qur-aan, Bayanul Qur-aan, Aasan Tarjamae Qur-aan aur Aasan Tafseer Qur-aan (Hazrat Maulana Khalid Saifullah Rahmani Sahib). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Duty Ke Dauran Qur-aan Ya Koi Deeni Kitaab Padhna

Sawal: Kya duty ke dauran Qur-aan ya koi deeni kitaab padh sakte hai? Kyunke arab mamaalik me bahut log khas kar ramazan me Qur-aan ko laptop, mobile wagayrah par khol kar padhte rehte hain?

Jawab: Agar maalik ko koi e'atiraz na ho aur wo apni zimmadari bhi puri kar raha ho to padhna jaaiz hai (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 6/19). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs maalik ki ijaazat ke bagair padhe to kya sahi hai? Agar maalik muslim ho to aksaron ko koi e'atiraz nahi hoga lekin maalik agar gair muslim ho to aksar use pasand nahi karenge!

Jawab: Agar kam jari rakhte huwe padhe masalan jo sooratein yaad hain wo padh raha ho tab to koi haraj nahi warnah mustaqil Qur-aan le kar nahi padhna chahiye, isliye ke mulazim ne apne wo auqaat us shakhs ya company ko tankhwah ke badle me de diye hain. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 6/19). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Tilaawat E Qur-aan Ke Khatam Par Sadaqallahul 'Azeem Kehna

Sawal: Kya Qur-aan ki tilaawat khatam karne ke baad Sadaqallahul 'Azeem kehna bid'at hai?

Jawab: Tilaawate Qur-aan-e-kareem ke Khatam par farziyat ya sunniyyat ka e'atiqad kiye bagair sadaqallahul Azim kehne ki gunjaaish hai balke ahle 'ilm ki ek jamaa-'at ne use aadabe tilaawat me shumaar kiya hai aur baaz ahle 'ilm ka Qur-aani aayaat zikr karne ke baad us ko likhne ka ma'amul bhi raha hai, is liye is ko mutlaqan bid'at kehna durust nahi. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/43, Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 1/431, Fatawah Darul-'Uloom). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Naujawaan Ki Qiraat Video Kisi 'Aurat Ko Bhejna

Sawal: Kya is tarah ka video jis me koi naujawan Qur-aan ki tilaawat kar raha ho kisi 'aurat ko bhejna durust hai?

Jawab: Durust nahi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1172). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Deewaaron Par Aayate Qur-aani Ko Likhna Aur Darwaazon Par Latkana

Sawal: Gharon ya masjidon ki deewaaron par Qur-aan-e-kareem ki aayaton ko likhna ya darwaazon par frame me daal kar latkane ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Qur-aan-e-kareem ka adab wa ehteraam har musalman par lazim hai, isliye bahaisiyat-e-musalman har shakhs ko aise kaamon se bachna chahiye jis me Qur-aan-e-kareem ki be adabi ho rahi ho. Ghar ya masjid ki deewaaron par ya darwaazon par Qur-aan-e-kareem ki aayaton ko likhna ya frame me daal kar uski takhti latkana shar'an koi matloob cheez nahi balke makrooh hai, lekin 'aam 'awaam is ka ehteraam nahi karte aur aadatan is ka chalan bhi hai, lihaaza Qur-aan-e-kareem ki aayaat agar badi deewaar ya bade darwaaze ya sutun ya bade frame me hon to un ke khali hisse ko bagair Wuzoo choona jaaiz hai, albatta bagair Wuzoo in aayaat ko choona ya deewaar par likhi hui in aayaat ko pase pusht daal kar un par taik lagana Qur-aane majeed ki be hurmati hone ki wajah se haraam hai. Wallahu ta'aala aalam (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 1/80, Mughniyul-Muhtaaj:1/152, Al-Fiqhu 'alal-Mazahibil-Arba'ah:1/363). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur 'Ilmi Batein - Jild Duwam

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il ki doosri jild anqareeb (soon) Insha Allah manzar-e-'aam par aane ki kagaar par hai. Pecheeda, jadeed aur nit naye masa-il par nazar-e-saani jaari hai. Yeh doosri jild Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare aur Mufti Ishaq Patel ki koshishon ka samarah hai.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

ROZA AUR SADAQA-E-FITR KE MASA-IL

Ramazan Ke Chaand Ki Gawaahi

Sawal: Kya Ramazan ke chaand ke liye bhi do (2) gawaah zaroori hain?

Jawab: Islam me aksar masa-il me do (2) gawaah zaroori hote hain lekin Ramazan ke chaand ke liye e'htiyaatan Sharee'at ne ek aadil gawaah bhi kaafi qaraar diya hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/322). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar pure 'alaqe me Ramazan ke chaand ki koi khabar na ho lekin ek (1) ya do (2) musalmaan ki gawaahi dein, kuch log unhen mu'atabar samajhte hon aur kuch log unhen mu'atabar na samjhein to kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Ramazan ke chaand ke liye ek aadil gawaah bhi kaafi hai. Mazkoorah Sawal me chunke gawaah aadil nahi hai to ab khud in do musalmaanon ko aur un par e'etimaad karne wale logon ko roza rakhna waajib hoga doosron par waajib nahi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/322). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Niyyat

Sawal: Ramazan ke farz roze ki niyyat raat me na kare to kya roza hoga?

Jawab: Agar raat me niyyat na kare to roza sahi na hoga, dil se niyyat karna farz hai, agar raat me kisi ke dil me yeh aa gaya ke kal use roza rakhna hai, isi tarah wo sehri kar raha hai is niyyat ke istihzaar ke saath ke roza rakhega to yeh niyyat ke qaa-im maqaam ho jaayega aur use zubaan se niyyat karne ki zaroorat nahi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/322). Maulana Shabbir Tambe & Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya sunnat roze ki niyyat zawal se pehle kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Sunnat rozon ki niyyat zawal se qabl karna bhi sahi hai, albatta zawal se qabl niyyat karne se sunnat roza us waqt sahi hoga jab ke subah saadiq se roze ke manafi koi baat (jaise khana, jima'a (sexual intercourse), kufr, haiz, Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding), pagalpan) na paayi jaaye warna roza sahi na hoga. (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 3/140). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shak Ki Wajah se E'htiyaatan Tees Sha'abaan Ka Roza Rakhna

Sawal: Agar koi Ramazan ke chaand par shak ki wajah se e'htiyatan tees sha'abaan ka bhi roza rakhe ya'ani Ramazan se ek din pehle to kya yeh durust hai?

Jawab: Agar tees Sha'abaan ko logon me Ramazan ka chaand nazar aane ka sirf charcha aur afwah ho ya'ani rooyate Hilaal ki mu'atabar gawaahi na ho to use yaumush-shak kehte hain aur us din sirf e'htiyaatan roza rakhna haraam hai lekin rooyate Hilaal ki mu'atabar gawaahi ho to roza rakhna waajib hai. (Al-Majmoo': 6/427). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Pandrah Sha'abaan Ke Baad Nifli Roza Rakhna

Sawal: Kya pandrah Sha'abaan ke baad nifli roza rakhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: 'Aam halaat me pandrah sha'abaan ke baad mutlaqan (bila-qaid – without restriction, gair mashrut - unconditional) nifl roza rakhna jaaiz nahi lekin aisa shakhs jo pandrah (15) sha'abaan ke pehle se roza rakhta aaraha ho ya jise peer aur jumerat ka roza rakhne ki 'aadat ho to uske liye mamnu' nahi hai, isi tarah qaza aur nazar ka roza rakhna bhi mamnu' nahi hai, hikmat is me yeh hai ke Ramazan ke farz roze jo aage aarahe hain un ki adayigi ke liye jism me quwwat barqaraar rahe kamzori na aa jaaye aur koi roza choot na jaaye (Fathul Jawwad: 1/440, Tuhfatul Ahwazi: 3/362). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Napaaki Me Sehri Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar kisi shakhs ko Ramazan ki raat me ehtilaam ho jaaye ya biwi se humbistari kare aur subah saadiq (fajr ki azaan ka waqt) qareeb ho to kya kare? pehle gusl se farig ho jaaye ya pehle sehri kare?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs Ramazan me biwi se hambistar ho jaaye ya use ehtilaam ho jaaye to afzal yahi hai ke bedaar hote hi pehle gusl se faarig ho jaaye phir sehri kare take roze ki ibtida'a paaki ke saath ho, lekin agar waqt kam ho to jism se gandagi door kar ke Wuzoo kar le phir sehri kha kar baad me gusl kar le (Kifayatun Nabeeh-Sharhut Tambeeh:2/204). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Haalat Me Cream, Tel Lagaana

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me agar jism ke kisi hisse me dard ho to us par cream ya tel lagana kaisa hai?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me cream ya tel laga sakte hain (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 1/512). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Roze Ki Haalat Me Surmah Lagaana Aur Eye Drops Daalna

Sawal: Roze me surmah lagana aur Eye Drops daalne ka hukm kya hai?

Jawab: Roze me surmah lagana aur Eye Drops daalna jaaiz hai. (Asnal-Matalib: 2/233). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ki Haalat Me Tooth Paste Se Brush Karna

Sawal: Kya roze ki haalat me Tooth Paste se brush kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Ha! Kar sakte hain, lekin is se bachna aur na karna ziyaadah behtar hai kyunke zarasi koi cheez halaq ke andar gai to roza toot jaayega. (Mausuatul Ahkaam: 602). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Roze Ki Haalat Me Injection Lena Aur Khoon Dena

Sawal: Kya roza ki haalat mien kisi mareez ko khoon dena durust hai?

Jawab: Ji ha, Khoon dena karaahat ke saath jaaiz hai, is se roza faasid (invalid) nahi hoga. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/583). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya Roza ki haalat me injection lena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me 'ilaaj ke liye injection lagwaane me koi haraj nahi hai, is se roza faasid (invalid) nahi hoga (Haashiyatul Qalyoobi: 2/71, Rauzatut-taalibeen: 2/358). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Haalat Me 'Asr Ke Baad Sona Aur Nahaana

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me 'asr ke baad sona aur nahaana kaisa hai?

Jawab: 'Asr baad sone, nahaane me koi haraj nahi hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 3/368). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ki Haalat Me Khushboo Lagaana, Miswaak Karna

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek roze ki haalat me zawal ke baad miswak karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Rozedaar ke liye zawal ke baad miswaak karna makrooh hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 2/368). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Roza ki haalat me khushboo lagaana kaisa hai?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me khushboo naa lagaana aula aur behtar hai kyunke is se tabi'at me nishat paida hota hai. (Fathul-Wahhab: 1/121). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me kisi ne makroohaat ko apnaaya jaise khushboo lagaya, zawal ke baad miswak kiya, to kya us ka roza tootega?

Jawab: Roza to nahi tootega lekin bachna behtar hai kyunke roze ke maqaasid me se ek yeh bhi hai rozedaar un tamaam umoor se ijtinab kare jin se nafs ko raahat aur farhat haasil hoti hai. (Asnal-Matalib: 1/422). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ki Haalat Me Bagair Qasd Ke Achanak Qay (Vomiting)

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me bagair qasd ke achanak qay (vomiting) ho jaaye jis ki miqdaar ek kulli se ziyaadah ho aur saath khoon ka bhi ikhraj ho to roze par kya asar padega?

Jawab: Agar khud bakhud qay ho jaaye ya'ani uske nikalne me rozedaar ke 'amal ka dakhal na ho to roze par koi asar nahi padega ya'ani roza nahi tootega is ke barkhilaf ungli wagayrah dalne ki wajah se qay ho jaaye to roza toot jaayega. (Al-Majmoo': 6/319). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Haalat Me Naak Aur Kaan Me Dawa Daalna

Sawal: Roze me naak aur kaan me dawa daalna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me naak aur kaan me dawa daalne se roza toot jaayega kyunke yeh khule suraakh hain aur khule surakh me dawa wagayrah daalne se roza toot jaata hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/156). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Me Cotton Buds Aur Nebulizer Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me kaan me Cotton Buds daalna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Kaan ke andaruni hisse me cotton buds daalne se roza toot jaayega kyunke khule huye surakh se koi bhi cheez andar jaaye to roza toot jaata hai aur kaan khule suraakh me se hai. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/327). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me Nebulizer le sakte hain ya nahi?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me Nebulizer nahi le sakte (Bushral-Kareem: 2/460). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Roze Ki Haalat Me Hijaamah (Cupping) Karwaana

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me Hijaamah (Cupping) karwaana kaisa hai?

Jawab: Makrooh hai kyunke is se badan me kamzori paida hoti hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 2/234). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ki Haalat Me Honton Par Wasline Lagaana

Sawal: Kya roze ki haalat me honton par wasline laga sakte hain?

Jawab: Basoorate 'uzr (majboori) roza ki haalat me wasline ya lipguard ka isti'emaal karaahat ke saath jaaiz hai, karaahat is wajah se ke thook ke zariye is ke asaraat ka andar jaane ka qavi imkaan rehta hai. Agar thoda sa bhi nigal le to us se roza toot jaayega (Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan:479). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Me Kunwein (Well) Ya Hauz (Tank) Me Gota (Dive) Lagana

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me kunwein (well) ya hauz (tank) me gota (drive) lagane ki soorat me paani khule surakh (kaan wagayrah) me chala jaaye to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Gota (dive) lagane par paani andar gaya to roza toot jaayega. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:3/171). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ki Haalat Me Biwi Ko Haath Lagaana, Bosah Lena

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me biwi ko haath lagaana aur bosah lena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me agar inzaal ka khatrah na ho to biwi ko haath lagaana aur bosah lena jaaiz hai lekin is ka tark aula aur behtar hai, kyunke rozedar ko shahwaton ka tark karna masnoon hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji 'Ala mazhabil Imaamish Shafi'ee:2/86). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me agar inzaal ka dar ho to biwi ko haath lagaana aur bosah lena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Aysi soorat me biwi ko bosah lena haraam hai. (Minhaajut-Talibeen 275). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar roze me biwi ko choone bosah lene ya saath letne se mani nikle to roze par kya asar hoga?

Jawab: Agar roza ki haalat me bila haayil (without barrier) biwi ko choone bosa lene ya saath letne se mani nikle to roza baatil hoga lekin agar haayil (barrier) ho chahe patla kapda hi kyun na ho to roza baatil nahi hoga, lekin yeh hukm us waqt hai jab haayil ke saath bosah lene ya choone mein mani nikalne ka qasd wa iraadah naa ho. Agar mani nikalne ke iraadah se chuye ya bosah le aur mani nikal aaye, agarche beech mein kapda wagayrah haayil aur rukawat ho, roza toot jayega kyunki yeh musht zani yaani apne hathon se sharmgah pakar kar mani nikalne mein shumar hoga jo ke roze ko faasid (invalid) kar deta hai. Yahi muta'ayyan aur rajeh qawl hai (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 3/410). (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 335). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Haalat Me Inzaal Ya Ehtilaam Hona

Sawal: Roze ki haalat me sirf kisi ko dekhne ya kuch sochne ki wajah se inzaal ho ya nind me ehtilaam ho to kya roza toot jaayega?

Jawab: Roze ki haalat me sirf kisi ko dekhne ya kuch sochne ki wajah se inzaal ho to roza baatil nahi hoga, neez ehtilaam se bhi roza baatil nahi hoga (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/75). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Haalat Me Apni Biwi Se Jima'a (sexual intercourse) Karna
