Sawal: Agar koi roza ki haalat mien apni biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) kare to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Roza ki haalat me apni biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) karna haraam hai, is se us ka roza toot jaayega, neez ek roze ke badle use do mah ke musalsal roze rakhna waajib hoga is tarah ke darmiyan me ek roza bhi na choote (Minhaajut-Talibeen: 275). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ramazanul Mubarak Me Biwi Se Humbistari Karna

Sawal: Ramazanul Mubarak me biwi se humbistari karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Ramazanul Mubarak ki raat me biwi se humbistari karna jaaiz hai, aur din me ya'ani subah saadiq se guroob-e-aaftab ke darmiyan humbistari karna haraam hai, agar koi shakhs jaan boojh kar din me humbistari kare to wo gunahgaar hoga aur us ka roza toot jaayega aur us shakhs par us roza ki qaza ke saath kaffarah bhi waajib hoga. Kaffarah yeh hai ke agar roza rakhne ki taaqat ho to musalsal do maheene ke roze rakhe, roze musalsal rakhne ka matlab yeh hai ke do mah roze mukammal hone tak darmiyan me kisi din naagah na ho, agar naagah ho gaya to phir se az sare nau dobaarah do mah ke roze rakhne zaroori honge lekin agar budhape ya beemaari ki wajah se roze rakhne ki taaqat aur qudrat na ho to phir saath (60) fuqaraa ya masaakeen ko anaaj de is tarah ke har har miskeen ko ek mudd ya'ani cheh sau (600) grams aisa anaaj de jo fitrah me diya jaata hai. (Fiqhul Manhaji: 3/115, Bahrul-Mazhab: 3/147). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ki Haalat Me Daant Ka 'ilaaj

Sawal: Kya roze ki haalat me daant ka 'ilaaj kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Daant nikaalne, root cannel, daant ki safai ya masnuyi daant lagaane se pehle is ka measurement lete waqt dawa (gel) aur paani ka isti'emaal kiya jaata hai aur in cheezon ka halaq me jaane ka qawi imkaan rehta hai aur isi tarah zayiqah daar dawa ka isti'emaal kiya jaata hai aur roze ki haalat me zayiqahdar cheez ka isti'emaal makrooh hai is liye roze ki haalat me bila zaroorat daant durustgi wala koi bhi 'amal nahi karna chahiye, agar daant ka koi bhi 'ilaaj karaaya gaya aur 'amal ke dauraan paani andar chala gaya to us se wo gunahgaar hoga aur roza bhi toot jaayega.( Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 1/474). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Ki Taraf Se Iftaari Ya Iftaari Ke Liye Raqam

Sawal: Kya Ramazanul Mubarak me gair muslim ki taraf se iftaari ya iftaari ke liye raqam qabool kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Musalmaanon ke liye ramazan me gair muslimon ki jaanib se pesh ki gai iftaari ya iftaari ki raqaam qabool karne me koi haraj nahi hai basharte ke uski kamaai (wages) haraam na ho aur haraam cheez na ho, kyunke yeh tuhfah ya hadiyah hai aur Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne kaafiron se tahayif qabool farmaye hain. Jaisa ke riwaayaton me milta hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne ek yahoodi (Jews) 'aurat se bakri ke gosht ka hadiyah qabool kiya hai, isi tarah ek yahoodi (Jews) ki da'awat bhi qabool farmaayi hai jis ne aap ko apne ghar da'awat di thi to Aap (ﷺ) ne us ke ghar ja kar khaana tanaaul farmaaya tha. (Al-'Aziz Sharhul wajeez: 1/310). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bachchon Ko Pehle Ramazan Ke Poore Roze Rakhwaana

Sawal: Bachchon ko pehle ramazan ke poore roze rakhwaana kaisa hai? Roza kab farz hota hai?

Jawab: hadees me waalidain ko is baat ka mukallaf kiya gaya hai ke bachchah jab saat (7) saal ki 'umr ko pahonch jaaye to use namaz ka hukm diya jaaye take use namaz ki 'aadat aur mashq ho jaaye, lihaaza namaz ki tarah waalidain saat (7) salah bachche ko roze rakhne ki targeeb de, use 'aadi banaaye, lekin aise chote Bachchon ko pehle ramazan ke naam se zabardasti roze rakhwaana jo roze rakhne ki sakat aur taaqat nahi rakhte yeh Sharee'at ke mizaaj ke khilaaf hai, han agar saat (7) se kam 'umr wala bachchah fitri taur par roze rakhne ki taqaat rakhta ho to use roze rakhwaane me koi haraj nahi hai. (Al-Majmoo': 6/253). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Iftaar Ke Waqt Du'aa Ki Qabooliyat

Sawal: Iftaar ke waqt saat (7) bar darood, pandrah bar teesra kalimah, das (10) bar ya wahhabu, saat bar darood padh kar du'aa mangna, phir teen (3) bar darood padh kar roza iftaar karna, Allah aap ki du'aa ko khujoor (date) khane se pehle hi qabool kar leta hai, kya yeh sahi hai?

Jawab: Qur-aan wa sunnat me is ka saboot nahi hai, albatta iftaar ke waqt du'aa qabool hoti hai yeh baat hadees se saabit hai. (Kifayatun Nabeeh-Sharhut Tambeeh:6/371). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Iftaari Me Khujoor (Date) Na Ho To

Sawal: Agar Iftaari me khujoor (date) na ho to kis cheez se roza Iftaar kare?

Jawab: Khujoor (date) se Iftaar karna sunnat hai aur agar khujoor (date) na ho to paani se Iftaar kare warnah kisi aur mithi aur sheerin cheez se (Al-Majmoo': 6/362). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Iftaar Ki Du'aa – Iftaar Karne Ke Baad Padhne

Sawal: Iftaar ki du'aa (اَللّٰهُمَّ إِنِّي لَكَ صُمْتُ وَعَلىٰ رِزْقِكَ أَفْطَرْتُ فَتَقَبَّلْ مِنِّيْ) kab padhna chahiye? Roza chodne se pehle ya baad mein?

Jawab: Iftaar karne ke baad padhna chahiye (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 3/425). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Ayyaame Haiz Me Roza Rakhna Aur Qaza

Sawal: Kya 'aurat haiz ke ayyaam me roza rakh sakti hai? Agar nahi rakh sakti phir ramazan ke rozon ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Ayyaame Haiz me 'aurat ka roza rakhna jaaiz nahi is liye ramazan me haiz ki wajah se choote huwe rozon ki qaza agle ramazan se pehle kare, agar koi qaza agle ramazan se pehle na kare to qaza ke saath fidyah bhi ada karna zaroori hoga ('Umdatus-Salik: 118). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Daurane naapaaki ki namaz mu'aaf hai lekin roze ki qaza kyun zaroori hai?

Jawab: Fuqahaye Shawaafe' ne is ki wajah yeh bayaan ki hai ke roze to in dino me chand hi choot-te hain jin ki qaza karne me dushwaari nahi aati barkhilaaf namaz ke ke wo in dino me bahut ziyaadah choot jaati hain jin ki qaza dushwaar saabit ho sakti hai, is farq ki wajah se in dino me rozon ki qaza hai namazon ki nahi (Al-Majmoo': 2/351). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Maa Baap Ke Rozon Ki Qaza

Sawal: Kya koi apne maa baap ke rozon ki qaza kar sakta hai jabke wo hayaat hon?

Jawab: Nahi kar sakta (Sharahul Muqadamatul Hazarmiyah: 1/577). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya koi apne maa baap ke rozon ki qaza kar sakta hai jab ke wo intiqaal kar gaye hon?

Jawab: Ji hain, kar sakta hai, Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ka farmaan hai: “Jo Shakhs faut ho jaaye aur us ke zimmeh roze hon to us ka wali us ki jaanib se roze rakhe” (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 1952, Saheeh Muslim: 1147). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shohar Ki Maujoodgi Me 'Aurat Ka Nifli Roza Rakhna

Sawal: Kya biwi ko shohar ki maujoodgi me nifli roza rakhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Kisi 'aurat ke liye shohar ki maujoodgi me us ki ijaazat ke bagair aise nifli roze rakhna jaaiz nahi hai jo pure saal me aate rehte hain jaise peer aur jumerat ka roza, ayyaame beez ke roze wagayrah, lekin aise nifli roze jo saal bhar me ek daf'aa aate hain jaise Aashora'a aur 9 Zilhijjah ka roza to yeh nifli roze shohar ki ijaazat ke bagair bhi jaaiz hain albatta agar shohar in rozon ko bhi rakhne se mana'a kare to 'aurat ko yeh roze rakhna jaaiz nahi (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/354, Al-Majmoo': 6/419). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hamal Ya Chote Bacheh Ki Wajah se Roza Na Rakhna

Sawal: Ramazan me hamilah 'aurat ya jis 'aurat ko bachchah paida huwa ho:

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah me niyyat wa haalaat ke hisaab se faisalah hoga.

Sawal: Hamilah 'aurat ya doodh pilaane wali bachche ki takleef ki wajah se roza na rakhe to qaza aur fidyah zaroori hai lekin apni takleef ki wajah se na rakhe to sirf qaza kiun hai?

Jawab: Bachche ki wajah se roza na rakhne par doosre ka faidah hai is liye qaza ke saath fidyah waajib hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/336). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Aurat Ka Maane'e Haiz Dawaayi Kha Kar Roza Rakhna

Sawal: Kya ramazan ke rozon ke liye 'aurat maane'e haiz dawaayi (medicine that stops menstruation) isti'emaal kar sakti hai?

Jawab: Agar 'aurat Ramazan ke Mubarak mah me roza aur deegar 'ibaadat ki fazeelat haasil karne ki niyyat se maane'e haiz dawaayi (jab ke tibbi nuqtaye nazar se bhi is me koi khatrah na ho) isti'emaal kare to is me koi haraj nahi hai. Agar mahaz yeh niyyat ho ke roze ki qaza zimmeh me na rahe to yeh munaasib nahi, garcheh roza ada ho jaayega (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/326). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Safar Me Roze Ka Hukm

Sawal: Safar me roze ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Aisa safar jis me namazen qasr karna jaaiz hai us me roza chod sakte hain, agar roza rakhne me zarar laahiq hone ka andeshah ho to tark karna afzal hai, zarar aur takleef na ho to roza rakhna afzal hai.

Abu Darda'a (Rz) bayan karte hain ke hum Ramazan ke maheene me sakht garmi ke andar Nabi (ﷺ) ke saath safar par nikle aur garmi ki wajah se apne haath sar par rakhte the, aur hum me Nabi (ﷺ) aur 'Abdullah Bin Rawahah ke 'alaawah kisi aur shakhs ka roza nahi tha (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 18945, Saheeh Muslim: 1122). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ramazan Ke Rozon Ki Qaza Par Fidyah

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs pichle Ramazan ke roze ki qaza maujoodah ramazan se pehle karna bhool jaaye to us ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Roze ki qaza bhool jaaye to aaindah use qaza ke saath fidyah dena zaroori hai, albatta bhool ki wajah se kaee (many) saal taakheer ho jaaye to fidyah ki miqdaar me izaafah na hoga. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/372). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Ramazan ke roze ki qaza me taakheer kare to fidyeh ka kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Agar Ramazan ke choote huwe roze ki qaza agle Ramazan se pehle jaan boojh kar bila kisi 'uzr ke na kare to use ek roze ka fidyah (1) Ek saal ke baad: 600 grams (2) Do saal ke baad: 1200 Grams (3) Teen saal ke baad 1800 Grams. Isi tarah har saal badhta jaayega (Minhajut Taalibeen: 278). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Roze Ke Fidyeh Me Anaaj Dena

Sawal: Kya roze ke fidyeh me wahi chaawal, gehun dena zaroori hai jo hum rozanah khaate hai?

Jawab: Sadaq-e-fitr ki tarah roze ke fidyeh me bhi darmiyaani qism ka wahi anaaj dena zaroori hai jo us 'alaqe me 'aammatan saal bhar khaya jaata ho (Nihayatuz-Zain: 188). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Roze Ka Fidyah Usi Din Iftaar Se Pehle Dena

Sawal: Kya roze ka fidyah usi din iftaar se pehle dena afzal hai?

Jawab: Behtar hai ke usi din de de (Fatawa ar-Ramali: 2/74). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ka Kaffarah

Sawal: Agar koi Shakhs Beemaari ki wajah se roze nahi rakh sakta to wo roze ka kaffarah kis tarah ada kare?

Jawab: Agar beemaari se achche hone ki ummeed ho to theek hone ke baad qaza kar le aur agar aysi beemaari hai ke sihhatmand hone ki ummeed nahi hai to har roze ke badle bataure fidyah ek mudd anaaj ya'ani 600 grams garibon me taqseem kar de. (Al_Umm: 2/113). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Aisa Beemaar jis ke thik hone ki ummeed na ho to apne rozon ka fidyah kab de? Har roze ke baad ya 'Eid ke baad ek saath pure mah ka, ya har din har roze ka Iftaar ke wqat ya Iftaar ke baad?

Jawab: Har din dena bhi sahi hai aur Ramazan ke ikhtitaam par pure mah ka ek saath dena bhi kaafi hai (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/342). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Ek Shakhs pichle Ramazan me bairooni mulk gaya aur tabi'at kharab hone ki wajah se char roze choot gaye, us ne niyyat ki ke India me jaane ke baad qaza kar lunga lekin yahan aane par na qaabile bardaasht bhook pyas ya aur kisi 'uzr ki wajah se roza nahi rakh paraha hai to us ke liye kya mas-alah hai fidyah ada kare ya kuch aur?

Jawab: Agar beemaari aysi hai ke theek hone ki ummeed ho to baad me qaza kare aur theek hone ki ummeed na ho to har roze ke badle ek mudd (600 grams) fidyah ada kare (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/339). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Roze Ki Haalat Me Biwi Se Jima'a (sexual intercourse) Karne Ka Kaffaarah

Sawal: Ramazan me roze ki haalat me apni biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne ka kya kaffaarah hai?

Jawab: Ramazan ke din roze ki haalat me apni biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) karna haraam hai, roze ka yaad hote huwe aur jaan boojh kar jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne se us ka roza toot jaayega, roze ki haalat me jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne ka kaffaarah yeh hai ke ek mo'min gulaam aazad kare, agar gulaam mayassar na ho to do mah musalsal roze rakhe, agar darmiyaan me ek roza bhi choot jaaye garcheh 'uzr ki wajah se ho to az-sare nau do mah roze rakhe, albatta haiz , Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) junoon ya mukammal din behoshi ki wajah se choot jaaye to haraj nahi, budhape ya beemaari ki wajah se is tarah musalsal roze rakhne par qaadir na ho to 60 fuqaraa ya masaakeen ko anaaj de jo zakaat ke mustahiq hon aur har ek ko ek mudd (600 grams) aisa anaaj de jo fitrah me diya jaata hai (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 3/344). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla Aur Mufti Ishaq Patel

Ayyaame Haiz Ke Rozon Ki Qaza Shawwaal Ke Saath

Sawal: Ayyaame Haiz me choote huwe Ramazan ke rozon ki niyyat ke saath Shawwaal ke rozon ki niyyat kar sakte hain ya alag se rakhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Mahe Shawwal me Ramazan ke rozon ki qaza ke saath Shawwal ke rozon ki bhi niyyat kar sakte hain magar behtar yahi hai ke pehle farz rozon ki qaza kare phir Shawwaal ke cheh (6) roze rakhe take kaamil sawaab haasil ho jaaye (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/419). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Shawwaal me Ramazan ke rozon ki qaza karne se Shawwaal ki fazeelat haasil hogi ya nahi?

Jawab: Shawwal ki fazeelat haasil hogi In Sha Allah lekin kaamil nahi hogi (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/419). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Peer Aur Jume'raat Ke Roze

Sawal: Peer aur Jume'raat ke roze ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Peer aur Jume'raat ka roza rakhna sunnat hai is ki fazeelat me Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya: Peer aur jume'raat wo din hain jin me Rabbul Aalameen ke paas a'amaal pesh kiye jate hain pas main chahata hun mera 'amal us din pesh kiya jaaye jab mai roze me hun (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 745, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/595). Maulana Muzaffar Sange

Shabe Bara-at Ka Roza

Sawal: Shabe Bara-at ke muta-'alliq roza rakhne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Shabe Bara-at ke roze ke bare mien 'Ulama ka ikhtilaaf hai, behtar baat yeh hai ke ayyaame beez ya'ani 13, 14 15 tareekh ke roze rakhe jaayein jo ke har maah sunnat hain, inhein me yeh roza bhi shaamil ho jaayega. Aur agar koi sirf 15 Sha'abaan ka roza rakhe to use mana'a na kiya jaaye, aur koi na rakhe to zabardasti na ki jaaye. Kyunke mazkoorah baala Sawal se muta-'alliq roza rakhne wali hadees za'eef hai. Ise istidlaal ke qaabil samajhne me 'Ulama ka ikhtilaaf hai (Al-Fatawa al-Kubra: 2/80, 'Umdatul-Fiqh: 3/112, Fatawa ar-Ramali: 2/79, Qamoosul-Fiqh: 4/299). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Yaume 'Aashuraa Ke Roze

Sawal: Muharram ke roze sunnat hain ya nifl?

Jawab: Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek sunnat aur nifl ek hi ma'anaa me isti'emaal hote hain, jis ke ma'anaa hain wo 'ibaadat jo Faraaiz ke 'alaawah ho lihaaza in rozon ko sunnat bhi kaha ja sakta hai aur nifl bhi (Sharhul Muslim: 4/264). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Yaume 'Aashuraa ke saath 9 Muharram ka roza kyun rakhna hai?

Jawab: 'Aashuraa ke roze ke saath 9 ya 11 ka roza rakhna mustahab hai take yahoodiyon ((Jews) ke saath mushabahat na ho. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj Ila Ma'rifati M'aaniyee Alfaazil Minhaaj: 2/183). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya 'Aashuraa ke saath Ramazan ka qaza roza rakh sakte hain? Agar han to niyyat kis tarah ki jaaye?

Jawab: 'Aashuraa ke din Ramazan ka qaza roza rakhne me koi haraj nahi hai isliye ke is din roza rakhkha jana matloob hai phir chahe wo farz roza ho ya nifl lihaaza agar farz qaza roza rakhkha jaaye to In Sha Allah 'Aashuraa ka bhi sawaab milega lekin kaamil sawaab nahi milega (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/419). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ya'ani Ramazan ke qaza roze ki niyyat karna chahiye aur dil me doosri 'Aashuraa ki bhi niyyat kare.

Jawab: 'Aashuraa ki niyyat na bhi kare tab bhi In Sha Allah 'Aashuraa ke roze ka sawaab mil jaayega (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 3/160). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Das Muharram Ke Saath 9 Muharram Ka Roza

Sawal: Huzoor (ﷺ) ne agle saal 10 Muharram ke saath 9 Muharram ko roza rakhne ka zikr kiya hai is ki wajah yeh hai ke yahoodi (Jews) wa nasara se mushabahat na ho lekin kya 11 Muharram ka roza rakhne ka hadees me ya sahabah ke 'amal se saabit hai? Kyunke kuch 'Ulama ise bhi bid'at samajhte hain?

Jawab: Hadees: Tum 'Aashuraa ka roza rakho aur is me yahoodi (Jews)yon ki mukhaalafat karo aur 'Aashuraa ke saath ek din pehle ya ek din baad ka roza rakho. (Sunan Baihaqi: 6/360)

Is riwaayat se pata chalta hai ke khud Huzoor (ﷺ) ne 9 aur 10 ya 10 aur 11 tareekh ka roza rakhne ka hukm diya hai. Aur shaayad kisi bhi maslak wale ne ise najaaiz ya makrooh nahi kaha hai (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/596). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Muslim Shareef ki hadees ki wajah se 10 Muharram ke saath 9 Muharram ka roza rakhna bhi sunnat hai, lekin kya 9 ke bajaaye 11 Muharram ka roza rakhna hadees se ya sahabah ke 'amal se saabit hai? Yeh Sawal is liye hai ke ho sakta hai baaz log ise bid'at samajhte hain.

Jawab: Musnade Ahmed (2154) aur sahi Ibne Khuzaimah (2095) wagayrah ki riwaayat me aaya hai: 'Aashuraa ke din ka roza rakho aur is silsile me yahoodi (Jews)yon ki mukhaalafat karo is tarah ke is se pehle ek din ya is ke baad ek din ka roza rakho.

Is hadees ko Allamah Ahmed Shakir Misri ne Hashiya Musnade Ahmed (2/546) me Hasan qaraar diya hai.

Aur agar bilfarz yeh riwaayat saabit na ho jaisa ke baazon ka khayaal hai tab bhi is roza ko bid'at nahi kaha ja sakta, is liye ke jo 'illat hadeese sahi se saabit hai: “Mukhaalafate Yahood” wahi 'illat 11ven roze me bhi payi ja rahi hai. Wallahu A'alam. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: 8 Muharram ko safar shuru' huwa aur 9 ko khatam ho raha hai, jis jagah 9 ko safar khatam ho raha hai wahan 10 Muharram ka waqt shuru' hota ho to is haalat me kya kiya jaaye?

Jawab: 'Aashuraa ya'ani 10 Muharram ka roza rakhe aur us ke baad aaindah kal 11 Muharram ka bhi roza rakhe, agar mumkin na ho to husoole sawaab wa fazeelat ke liye 10 Muharram ka sirf ek roza rakhna bhi kaafi hai (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 3/208). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

9 Zilhijjah / 'Arafah Ka Roza

Sawal: 9 Zilhijjah ka roza Hindustaan me kab rakhkha jaayega? Kya 8 Zilhijjah ko jab Saudi me 9 Zilhijjah ko yaume 'arafah hoga? Ya 9 Zilhijjah ko jab ke Saudi me 'Eid ka din hoga?

Jawab: Har shakhs ke liye zaroori hai ke wo jis mulk me rehta ho usi mulk ke e'etibaar se roza rakhe ya 'Eid manaye, is liye ke 'ibadat apni jagah ke e'etibaar se anjam di jati hai lihaaza usi jagah ke e'etibaar se hukm nafiz hoga, albatta behtar baat yeh hai ke 9 ke saath 8 Zilhijjah ka bhi roza rakhkha jaaye (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 3/465, Al-Majmoo': 6/275). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Hindustaan me 'arafah ka roza 9 Zilhijjah ko rakhkha jaata hai, lekin kuch log Makkah ke 'arafah ke din ka e'etibaar karte huwe 8 Zilhijjah ko roza rakhte hain agar koi ehtiyatan 8 zilhijjah ko bhi roza rakhe aur 9 zilhijjah ko bhi to kya is tarah karna sahi hai?

Jawab: Agar koi Shakhs 8 Zilhijjah ko bhi roza rakhe to koi haraj nahi balke 9 ke saath 8 ke roze ko bhi 'Ulama ne mustahab likkha hai is tarah se gar ek se 9 tak ke roze rakhe jaayein to behtar hai kyun ke hadeese paak me pure 9 roze rakhne ki bhi fazeelat aayi hai (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 3/465, Sunan At-Tirmizi: 758). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Arafah Ke Din Ramazan Ke Roze Ki Qaza

Sawal: Agar kisi ka farz roza choota ho aur wo 'arafah ke din roza rakhe to use kya niyyat karni chahiye aur kya use dono rozon ka sawab milega?

Jawab: Us shakhs ko sirf farz qaza ki niyyat karna chahiye chunke 'arafah ke din roza paya ja raha hai is liye Allah Ta'aala ki zaat se qawi ummid hai ke farz ke saath nifl ka bhi sawab haasil ho jaayega lekin kamil sawab nahi milega. Is liye behtar yahi hai ke qaza roza alag ayyam me rakhe aur 'arafah ka roza alag se sunnat ki niyyat karke rakkhe is soorat me dono ka sawab kamil taur par mil jaayega In Sha Allah (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/419). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Arafah Ka Roza – Ek Tahqeeqi Jaaizah

Ahadees me Yaume 'arafah ki badi fazeelat aayi hai ek taraf hujjaj ke liye Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ka din hai jis din Allah Ta'aala maidane 'Arafat me wuqoof karne walon par fakhr karta hai aur kasrat se unhein jahannam se rustagaari deta hai to doosri taraf 'aam musalmaanon ke liye is din roza rakhne ka hukm mila hai jo ek saal guzashtah aur ek saal aaindah ke gunahon ka kaffaarah hai.

Chunanche Abu Qatadah (Rz) se marvi hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se Siyaame 'arafah ke baare me pucha gaya to Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: yeh guzre huye aur aane wale saal ke gunaahon ka kaffaarah hai.

Is roze se muta-'alliq aaj se pehle koi ikhtilaaf nahi paya jaata tha magar aaj globalization (Media ki wajah se ek ghar aangan) ki wajah se logon ke darmiyaan yeh ikhtilaaf paida ho gaya ke 'arafah ka roza kab rakhkha jaaye?

Baaz logon ka kehna hai ke yeh roza Saudi 'Arab ke hisaab se Wuqoof-e-'Arafah wale din rakhna hai aur baaz ka kehna hai ke har mulk wala apne yahan ki taareekh se 9 zilhijjah ka roza rakhega.

Is mas-alah ko hal karne ke liye hamein dekhna hoga ke roza ke silsile me islaami ahkaam kya hain us waqt baat khud wazeh ho jaayegi. Roze se muta-'alliq islaam ke do aham qaaide hain.

Pehla Qaaidah: Ruyate Hilal ka hai ya'ani roza rakhne me chaand dekhne ka e'atibar hoga jise 'arabi me Qamari Nizaam bhi keh sakte hain. Bukhari shareef me Aap (ﷺ) ka hukm hai.

Chaand dekh kar roza rakho aur chaand ko dekh kar rozon ka ikhtitaam karo aur agar tum par chaand makhfi ho jaaye to phir Shaabaan ke tees din poore kar lo.

Yeh hadees roza se muta-'alliq 'aam hai khwah koi bhi roza ho is me yahi hukm lagega ya'ani roze me apne apne mulk ki ruyat ka e'etibaar hoga, isi wajah se dekhte hain ke ramazaan ka roza rakhne ke liye chaand dekha jaata hai na ke Saudi 'Arab ko, aur is tarah jab Iftaar kiya jaata hai to us waqt bhi chaand hi ke dubne ka intizaar kiya jaata hai.

Doosra Qaaidah: Ikhtilaafe Mataale' ka hai. Ek shahar ki ruyat qarebi un tamaam shahar walon ke liye kaafi hogi jin ka matlaa' ek ho, matlaa' ke ikhtilaaf se ek shahar ki ruyat doosre shahar ke liye nahi maani jaayegi.

Daleel: Hazrat Ummul Fazal (Rz). Hazrat Kureb (Rz) ko apne kaam ke liye Hazrat Mu'aaviyah ke paas shaam me bhejte hain, Hazrat Kureb (Rz) farmaate hain ke wahaan hum ne Ramazaan Shareef ka chaand Jumu'ah ki raat ko dekha, main apna kaam karke waapas lauta yahan meri batein 'Abdullah Bin Abbas (Rz) se ho rahi thin. Aap (Rz) ne mujh se mulk e shaam ke chaand ke bare me daryafat farmaya to me ne kaha ke wahan chaand Jumu'ah ki raat ko dekha gaya hai, Aap (Rz) ne farmaaya, tum ne khud dekha hai? Main ne khaha, ji han mai ne bhi dekha. Aur sub logon ne dekha, sub ne bil-ittifaaq roza rakhkha. Khud Hazrat Ameer Mu'aaviyah (Rz) ne bhi roza rakhkha. Aap ne farmaya theek hai, lekin hum ne to haftah ki raat chaand dekha hai, aur haftah se roza shuru' kiya hai, ab chaand ho jaane tak hum to tees roze poore karenge. Ya yeh ke chaand nazar aa jaaye. maine kaha Subhanallah! Ameere Mu'aaviyah (Rz) aur ahle shaam ka dekha huwa chaand aur roza kya aap ko kaafi nahi hai?

Aap (Rz) ne farmaaya, hargiz nahi, hamein Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne isi tarah hukm farmaaya hai. Yeh hadees Muslim 1087, Tirmizi, An-Nasa'i, Abu Dawood wagayrah me maujood hai, is hadees ko bunyaad bana kar Muhaddiseen ne jo abwaab bandhe hain is se baat aur bhi wazeh ho jaati hai.

In dono usoolon ki roshni me 'arafah ka roza bhi apne mulk ke hisaab se 9 Zilhijjah ko rakhkha jaayega. Yahi baat dalaayil ki ru se saabit hoti hai.

Agar roze se muta-'alliq ruyate hilaal ka hukm nikaal diya jaaye to roza be mana'a ho jaayega, ek din ka bhi koi roza nahi rakh sakta hai, na sehri kha sakta hai aur na hi Iftaari kar sakta hai. Aise hi ikhtilaafe mataale' ka e'etibaar na karne se musalmaanon ke roze, namaz, qurbaani, 'Eidain aur deegar ibaadaat ki anjamdahi mushkil ho jaayegi.

'Arafah Ke Roze Se Muta'lliq Ishqalaat Ka Jawab

Pehla Ishqaal: Jin logon ka kehna hai ke hadees me taareekh ka zikr nahi hai balkeh 'arafah ka lafz aaya hai aur 'arafah ka ta'alluq 'Arafat me wuqoof karne se hai is liye haji ke Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ke din hi sare musalman 'arafah ka roza rakhein. Yeh istidlaal kaee (many) wujooh se sahi nahi hai.

Pehli wajah: qaaide ki ru se roze me ruyat hilaal aur ikhtilaafe mataale' ka e'etibaar hoga jis ka zikr upar ho chuka hai. 'arafah ke roze ko is qaaide se nikaalne ke liye wazeh nass chahiye jo ke maujood nahi.

Doosri wajah: Agar 'arafah ke roze se muta'lliq baaz hadees me taareekh nahi aayi to koi haraj nahi, doosri hadees me Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se taareekh ke saath 9 Zilhijjah tak roza rakhna saabit hai. Baaz azwaaje Mutahharaat ka bayaan hai:

Rasoolullah (ﷺ) Zilhijjah ke pehle 9 din, 'Aashuraa Muharram, har maheene me teen din aur har maheene ke pehle peer aur jume'raat ko roza rakhkha karte the. (Sahih Abu Dawood: 2437)

Is hadees me 'arafah ka roza bhi daakhil hai jo ke taareekh ke saath saabit hai, is se 'arafah ke roze ki is naahiyeh se taayeed hoti hai ke use 9 Zilhijjah ko rakhkha jaayega.

Teesri wajah: Agar 'Arafaat me wuqoof se muta'lliq roza hota to 'arafah nahi 'Arafaat ka zikr hota.

Chauthi wajah: Agar yeh Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ki wajah se hota to hajiyon ke liye bhi yeh roza mashru' hota magar yeh hajiyon ke liye mashru' nahi hai.

Panchwi Wajah: Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ka ek waqt muta-'ayyan hai jo ke taqreeban zawaal ke baad se Magrib ke waqt tak hai. Yeh waqt roza ke waaste Saudi 'Arab walon ke liye bhi kaafi nahi hai kyunke roze me Subah saadiq ke waqt sehri aur niyyat karna phir ghuroobe shams pe Iftaari karna hai. Goya roze me wuqoof ka e'etibaar huwa hi nahi is me to nizaame shamsi wa qamari ka e'etibaar hua. Is bina par bhi nisbat ka hi andaazah laga sakte hain wuqoof ka nahi.

Chatti Wajah: Saudi 'Arab walon ke liye bhi 'arafah 9 Zilhijjah hi hai, wo roza rakhte huwe 'arafah ke wuqoof ko madde nazar nahi rakhte balke qamari taareekh ke hisab se 8 Zilhijjah (Yaumut Tarwiyah) se Hajj shuru' karte hain, aisa kabhi nahi ho sakta hai ke Zilhijjah ki 7 taareekh ho aur haaji mina'a jaaye ya 8 taareekh ho aur haaji 'Arafat chala jaaye. Manasike Hajj me bhi Yaumut Tarwiyah aur Yaume 'arafah taareekh ke taur par hai kyunke islaami ibaadaat me rooyate Hilaal ka bada dakhal hai. Isi cheez ki taraf Qur-aan me rahnumaayi ki gai hai.

Allah ka farman hai: log aap se hilaal ke bare me puchte hain, Aap kaho yeh logon ke liye auqaat aur Hajj ke ta'aayyun ka zariyah hai. (Sooratul Baqarah: 189)

Doosra Ishqaal: Qaa'ileene Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ka ek ishqaal yeh hai ke ahaadees me yaume 'arafah ki badi fazeelat waarid hai aur 'arafah wuqoof 'Arafat pe hai is liye 'arafah ke wuqoof pe hi yeh roza rakhkha jaaye.

Yeh baat sahi hai ke 'arafah ki badi fazeelat aayi hai, is me koi shak nahi hai magar 'arafah ke roze se muta'lliq yeh kehna ke “Is ki fazeelat ki wajah se Wuqoof-e-'Arafah par hi roza rakhna hai” galat istidlaal hai. Allah Ta'aala ne yaume 'arafah ko hujjaj ke 'alaawah puri duniya ke musalmanon ke liye ba'ise fazeelat banaya hai. Haajiyon ko Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ka sawaab milta hai jabke duniya walon ko yaume 'arafah ka roza rakhne ki wajah se sawab milta hai. Aur is baat pe hairan hone ki zaroorat nahi ke yaume 'arafah to ek hi din hai, phir apne apne mulkon ke hisaab se kaise fazeelat hogi? Tab to kayee ayyam ho jaayenge!

Is misaal se yun samjhein ke Allah Ta'aala ne lailatul Qadr ek banayi hai magar sare musalmaanon ke liye apne apne hisab se fazeelat milti hai. Saudi me ek din pehle Shabe Qadr, Hind-paak me ek din baad Shabe Qadr, raat ek hi hai aur sawaab ki ummeed har mulk wale apne apne mulk ke hisaab se Shabe Qadr me bedaar ho kar rakhte hain.

Teesara Ishqaal: Baaz log Tirmizi ki ek riwaayat se daleel pakadte hain.

Roza us din rakhkha jaaye jis din log roza rakhte hain, 'Eid-ul-Fitr aur 'Eid-ul-Adha bhi usi din manayi jaaye jab log manate hain. (Sunan At-Tirmizi:697).

Is hadees ki roshani me kehte hain ke jis din 'arafah ka roza Saudi 'Arab me rakhkha jaata hai us din sab log rakhein. Is me ittihaad hai.

Agar is hadees se aisa hi mas-alah istimbaat kiya jaaye (Jabke is me lafz 'arafah zikr hi nahi) to is ka matlab yeh bhi hoga ke poori duniya ke musalmaan ek saath ramazan ka roza rakhein balk ek saath sehri khayein, ek hi saath Iftaar kare ek hi saath aur ek hi waqt me 'Eid-ul- fitr aur 'Eid-ul- Adha ki namazen padhein zaahiri baat hai ke wuqoof 'Arafat ka roza rakhne ke qayileen is baat ko nahi maneinge to phir 'arafah ke roze par hi poori duniya ka ittihaad kyun?

Is hadees ka sahi mafhoom yeh hai ke roza aur 'Eid jamaa-'at aur logon ki aksariyat ke saath mu'atabar hai jaisa ke Shaikhul Islam Ibne Taymiyah (Rh) ne likkha hai.

Chautha Ishqaal: ek aur baat kahi jaati hai ke pehle log nahi jaan pate the ke Wuqoof-e-'Arafah kab hai isliye apne mulk ke hisab se 9 Zilhijjah ka roza rakhkha karte the is liye ma'azoor the ab zamaanah taraqqi kar gaya aur Wuqoof-e-'Arafah sab ko ma'aloom ho jaata hai is liye wo 'uzr saaqit ho gaya.

Awwalan: Aaj bhi poori duniya me har kas wa nakas ko media ki sari khabron ka 'ilm nahi ho pata, media se jude logon ko hi pata chal pata hai.

Saaniyan: Islaam ne jo aafaaqi deen diya hai wo internet aur media ka mohtaaj nahi hai. Bataure misaal yeh kahun ke internet aur media khatam ho jaaye to tab aap kya kaheinge ke abhi phir se log ma'azoor ho gaye. Yeh to machinery cheez hai chal bhi sakti hai aur kabhi is ka nizaam darham barham bhi ho sakta hai. Is ka mushaahadah kabhi kabhaar bankon aur offison me hota hai. Jab net connection gaaib rehtah hai to logon ki kya durgat hoti hai. Lekin islaam ka nizaam hamesha bagair media aur internet ke chalta raha hai aur qayaamat tak chalta rahega. Duniyawalon ko bagair internet ke qamari nizaam se ramazan ka roza, ayyaame beez, 'Aashuraa aur 'arafah ma'aloom hota rahega.

Qa'ileen Siyaame 'Arafaat Pe Chand Muhakamah

To Jawab dete hain aise logon se roza saaqit ho jaata hai. Roze ke muta'lliq usool wa a'hkaam wazeh hain, unhein baalaaye taaq rakh kar bagair suboot ke roza saaqit karwa ke logon ko bade ajr se mahroom kar dena badi na insaafi hai.

Yeh Jawab kuch hazam nahi ho pata, is Jawab ke maan lene se yeh maanna padega ke quroon oola se lekar aaj tak kisi ne 'arafah ka roza sahi nahi rakhkha siwaaye arab walon ke, jabke islaam ek aafaqi mazhab hai wo sab ke liye eksaan dastoor pesh karta hai khwah Saudi 'Arab ka ho ya doosre mulk ka, aur islaam par 'amal karne ke liye kisi media ki bhi zaroorat nahi.

'Arafah Ke Roze Se Muta'lliq Ek Aham Nuktah

New Zealand aur Saudi me 11 ghante ka farq hai, jab Saudi me 9 Zilhijjah ka sooraj nikalta hai to New Zealand me 8 Zilhijjah ka sooraj ghuroob hota hai. To kya New Zealand wale raat ko roza rakheinge? Aur agar raat guzaar kar roza rakhein to un ke yahan to Zilhijjah ki 9 taareekh hogi magar Saudi me 10 taareekh ya'ani 'Eid ka din hoga, to kya New Zealand wale 'Eid ke din roza rakhainge jo ke mamnu' hai. 'arafah ka roza samajhne ke liye yeh ek aham nuktah hai.

Khulasah me yeh kehna chahunga ke 'arafah ka roza apne mulk ke hisab se Zilhijjah ki 9 taareekh ko rakhkha jaayega.

Ayyaame Tashreeq Me Nifli Roza Rakhna

Sawal: Kya 'Eid-ul-Adha ke doosre ya teesre din nifli roza rakh sakte hain?

Jawab: 10 se 13 Zilhijjah tak roza rakhna haraam hai. (Al-Majmoo': 6/454). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi ne la ilmi me ayyaame tashreeq me nifli roza rakhkha to 'ilm hone ke baad use kya karna hoga?

Jawab: Chunke in ayyaam me roza rakhna haraam hai aur rakhe to sahi nahi hoga lihaaza agar koi shakhs la ilmi me in teen dino me roza rakhe to wo roza munaqqid nahi hoga aur 'ilm ho jaane ke baad use roza todna aur istigfar karna zaroori hai. (Al-Majmoo': 6/440). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Friday, Saturday and Sunday Ko Bagair Sabab Ke Nifli Roza

Sawal: Kya sunnat roza Jumu'ah ke din rakh sakte hai?

Jawab: Sirf Jumu'ah ya Sirf Saneechar ka roza rakhna ya sirf itwaar ka rakhna makrooh hai agar rakhna hi ho to in dino ke rozon ke saath ek din pehle ya ek din baad ka roza rakhe to makrooh nahi (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/447). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Sirf Jumu'ah, Saneechar aur itwaar ko bagair kisi sabab ke nifli roza rakhna makrooh kyun hai?

Jawab: Is liye ke saneechar aur itwaar ko yahood wa nasaara mutabarrak samajhte the aur 'Eid manate the to un ki mushabahat se bachne aur un ki mukhaalafat karne ka hamein hukm diya gaya hai (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 3/209). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jumu'ah ke din roza ki mumaana'at ki hikmat yeh hai ke Jumu'ah ka din du'aa, zikr, gusl wagayrah se paaki haasil karne, namaz ke liye jaldi jane, namaz ka intizaar karne, khutbah tawajjuh se sunne, namaz ke baad kasrat se zikr karne aur in ke 'alaawah bahut si 'ibaadaton ke karne ka din hai isi wajah se Jumu'ah ke din roza na rakhne ko mustahab qaraar diya take in wazaayif par madad haasil ho sake aur in wazaayif ko nashaat aur inshirahe qalb ke saath ada kar sake(aur bagair kisi thakan aur uktaahat ke) in wazaayif ko poora karne me lazzat mehsoos kar sake.(Sharah Muslim: 8/210, Fathul-Bari: 5/777). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Farz Roza Todne Par Hukm

Sawal: Farz roza todne par kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Jima'a (sexual intercourse) ke 'alaawah kisi aur tareeqe se roza toda jaaye to doosre dino me sirf us ki qaza karna waajib hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 339). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jaan Bachane Ke Liye Roza Todna

Sawal: Kisi andhe ki jaan bachane ke liye namaz todna jaaiz hai to kya kisi ki jaan bachane ke liye bhi roza tod sakte hain?

Jawab: Ji han, andhe shakhs isi tarah har musharraf aur masoom insaan ya haiwane mohtaram ki jaan bachane ke liye agar roza todna zaroori ho jaaye yane bagair roza tode use bachana mumkin na ho to roza todna waajib hai, ab is soorat me doosre dino me is roze ki qaza kare aur fidya de yeh waajib hai. ('Ujalatul Muhtaaj:2/544). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ramazaan Ke Rozon Ke Mutafarriq Masa-il

Sawal: Agar kisi ke ramazan ki shuru'aat Middle East me hui ho aur phir wo Hindustan me aakhari asharah me aa jaaye.

Jawab: Aadmi jis jagah jaayega us jagah ke e'etibaar se us par hukm lagega.

Sawal: Baaz log Saudi 'Ulama ke fataawah ke mutaabiq kehte hain ke islaami maheene 29 ya 30 ke hote hain 31 ka ho hi nahi sakta is liye ramazan me masalan Saudi 'Arab se Hindustan safar karne ki wajah se kisi ke roze 31 ho rahe hon to wo 31van roza na rakhe, aise logon ko kya Jawab diya jaaye?

Jawab: Allah Ta'aala ka irshad hai: tum me se jo shakhs bhi yeh mahinah paaye wo is me zaroor roza rakhe (Aasan Tarjuma-e-Qur-aan Mufti Taqee 'Usmani Sahab) is me Allah ne har us shakhs ko roze ka hukm diya hai jo ramazan ka mahinah paye, lekin Saudi 'Arab ke konse 'Ulama aisa fatwah de rahe hain? Un ke akabeer 'Ulama wa Muftiyan ka to yeh fatwah nahi hai, wo bhi yahi farmaate hain ke aysi soorat me 31van roza rakhkha jaaye. Mulahizah ho Saudi 'Arab ke saabiq Mufti-e-A'azam 'Allama Abdullah Ibne Baaz (Rh) ka fatwah (Majmoo' Fatawa Ibne Baaz: 15/155-156). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Ramazan me koi bairune Mulk (Gulf) se India ka safar kare aur safar ki wajah se roza chod de, phir India me roze us ke pure 30 din ke ho gaye kyunke bairun mulk ek din pehle roza rakhkha jaata hai. To kya safar ka choota huwa ek roza qaza kare ya nahi?

Jawab: Qaza karna zaroori hai kyunke ramazan ke maheene me roza nahi rakhkha gaya tha jo rakhna waajib tha (Al-Majmoo': 6/275). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla & Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Ek Shakhs bairun mulk me 30 roze mukammal karta hai aur 'Eid ka chaand bhi nikalta hai aur wo us raat baarah ya ek baje India ke liye rawana hota hai.

Jawab: Masa-il mazkoorah ke jawabaat is tarah hain:

Zakaatul Fitr / Sadaqa-e-Fitr ka Maqsad

Sawal: Zakaatul Fitr / Sadaqa-e-fitr ka maqsad kya hai?

Jawab: Sadaqa-e-fitr ka maqsad roze ki kamion aur kotahiyon ki talafi aur garibon ke liye 'Eid ke din khane ki sahulat dena hai. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit tullaab:2/46). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Fitrah kab waajib hota hai?

Jawab: 'Eid ka chaand nazar aane ke baad aadmi par apna aur apne Zere kafalat afraad ka fitrah waajib ho jaata hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/219). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Sadaqa-e-fitr kis par farz hai?

Jawab: Sadaqa-e-fitr har us shakhs par farz hai jis ke paas apne ahal wa ayaal ke liye 'Eid ke din ka samaan, khana, kapada, khadim wagayrah muhayya ho aur itni raqam zaa-id (excess) bach jaaye ke jis se Sadaqa-e-fitr ada kar sakta ho to us par fitrah ada krana waajib hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/220). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zakaatul Fitr Ki Miqdar

Sawal: Zakaatul Fitr ki miqdar kitani hai?

Jawab: Do (2) kilo aur char sau (400) grams anaaj hai jise 'umooman us 'alaqe me saal bhar khaya jaata ho (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/23). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zakaatul Fitr / Sadaqa-E-Fitr Ke Mustahiqeen

Sawal: Sadaqa-e-fitr ke Mustahiq kaun hain?

Jawab: Sadaqa-e-fitr ke mustahiq wahi hain jo zakaat ke mustahiq hain. ('Umdatus-Salik Wa 'Udatun-Nasik:104). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Sadaqa-e-fitr kab ada kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Sadaqa-e-fitr pure ramazan me de sakte hain (Ada karna jaaiz hai) lekin 'Eid ka chaand nazar aane ke baad se 'Eid ki namaz tak ka waqt afzal hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/23). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya 'Eid se do char din pehle Sadaqa-e-fitr de sakte hain?

Jawab: Ji han, de sakte hain. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:3/95). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sadaqa-E-Fitr 'Eid Ki Namaz Ke Baad

Sawal: Kya Sadaqa-e-fitr 'Eid ki namaz ke baad ada kar sakte hain?

Jawab: 'Eid ki namaz se pehle ada karna afzal hai. Taakheer (delay) ke bawajud Sadaqa-e-fitr ada karna zaroori hai aur itni taakheer karna ke 'Eid ki namaz bhi ho jaaye yeh imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek makrooh hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/221). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Chote Bachchon Ka Bhi Sadaqa-E-Fitr

Sawal: Kya chote Bachchon ka bhi Sadaqa-e-fitr ada karna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Sadaqa-e-fitr har us farad ki taraf se nikalna zaroori hai jise mahe ramazan ka kuch hissa mile aur Shawwal ka kuch hissa mile, lihaaza agar bachchah Shawwal ka chaand nazar aane se pehle paida huwa aur chaand ke baad tak zindah raha to us ka bhi fitrah nikalna waajib hoga. ('Umdatus-Salik:99). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zakatul Fitr Me Anaaj

Sawal: Zakatul Fitr me konsa anaaj dena chahiye?

Jawab: Zakatul Fitr me wo anaaj dena waajib hai jo umumi taur par poora saal us 'alaqe me khaya jaata ho. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/220). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sadaqa-E-Fitr Me Roti, Aata Dena

Sawal: Kya Sadaqa-e-fitr me roti ya aata de sakte hain?

Jawab: Sadaqa-e-fitr me anaaj hi dena zaroori aur shart hai, lihaaza anaaj ke badle aata, roti, sattu wagayrah ada karna kafi nahi hoga agar koi de to Sadaqa-e-fitr ada nahi hoga. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:2/119). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sadaqa-E-Fitr Me Anaaj Ke Badle Qimat Dene Ka Hukm

Sawal: Sadaqa-e-fitr me anaaj ke badle qimat dene ka hukm kya hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek Sadaqa-e-fitr me galib anaaj dena zaroori hai (Jo anaaj basti me ziyadah tar isti'emaal hota ho) galib anaaj ke 'alaawah koi doosri cheez dena kafi nahi hai yahan tak ke agar koi kisi faqir ko sadaqa-e-fitr me qimat ada karna chahe to us ki bhi ijazat nahi hai.

Albatta chunke Imaam Hanifah (Rh) ke maslak me qimat di ja sakti hai is liye baaz fuqhaa ne ahnaaf ke maslak par 'amal karte huwe qimat ada karne ki ijazat di hai, lihaaza agar kisi jagah anaaj nikalna bahut ziyadah dushwar ho to badarjah majboori qimat ada kar sakte hain. Albatta Shawafe ke maslak par 'amal karte huwe anaaj hi dene ki koshish kare. (Al-Majmoo':6/144, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/220). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Kisi Doosre Ki Taraf Se Sadaqa-E-Fitr Nikaalna

Sawal: Zere kafalat afrad ke 'alaawah kisi doosre (bhai, bahan wagayrah) ki taraf se Sadaqa-e-fitr nikaal sakte hain?

Jawab: Aadami apne Zere kafalat (Biwi ya nabaalig Bachche wagayrah) ki ijazat ke bagair un ki taraf se Sadaqa-e-fitr ada kar sakta hai, lekin aise log jo us shakhs ki kafalat me na hon to unki ijazat ke bagair Sadaqa-e-fitr ada nahi kar sakta, agar ijazat ke bagair Sadaqa-e-fitr nikaala to Sadaqa-e-fitr ada na hoga. (Al-Majmoo', Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/220). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar waalidain apne kisi bête ko hukm dein ke wo bhaiyon aur un ki kafalat me rehne walon ka Sadaqa-e-fitr ada kare to kya us ke ada karne se bhaiyon aur un ke ma'tahton ka Sadaqa-e-fitr ada ho jaayega? Aur kya koi bhi kisi ka Sadaqa-e-fitr us ki ijazat ke bagair ada kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Kisi aur ki taraf se Sadaqa-e-fitr ada karne ke liye us ki ijazat zaroori hai, bhai aur bahan agar chote hain to waalidain ki ijazat kafi hogi warnah khud un ki ijazat zaroori hai. (Al-Majmoo':6/136). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Rozon Ka Kaffarah Dene Ke Baad Fitrah Dene Ka Hukm

Sawal: Jis ka fidyah diya gaya, kya us ka fitrah dena zaroori hai?

Jawab: Roze ka kaffarah aur Sadaqa-e-fitr dono alag alag waajibat hain, in me se ek ki adayigi se doosra zimmeh se saqit nahi hoga ya'ani dono ki adaying zaroori hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/220, 234). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ijtimaa'i Zakaatul Fitr / Sadaqa-E-Fitr

Sawal: Ijtimaa'i nizaam me raqam jama'a kar ke fitrah ada karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Ijtimaa'i Nizaam me log raqam de kar zimmedaron ko chaawal khareedne ka wakeel banaate hain lihaaza is tarah ijtimaa'i nizaam me raqam jama'a kar ke fitrah ada ho jaayega, albatta yeh zaroori hai ke fitrah me aisa anaaj khareeda jaaye jo us 'alaqe me 'aammatan poora saal khaya jaata ho (Al-Majmoo':6/133-136). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ijtimaa'i zakatul fitr me us aadmi ka fitrah ada hoga jo 60 Rupaye kilo chaawal khaata hai lekin fitrah 45 Rupaye kilo de raha hai?

Jawab: Fitrah ki adaayigi me chaawal wagayrah anaaj ki isi qism ko dekha jaayega jo us 'alaqe me 'umoomi taur par poore saal khaayi ja rahi hai. Lihaaza us 'alaqe me poore saal 'umooman jis qism ka chaawal wagayrah khaya ja raha tha wahi dena waajib hoga. Chaahe sadaqah fitr ada karne wala kitna hi qeemati ya kam qeemat waala chaawal wagayrah kha raha ho (Al-Bayan:3/374). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ijtimaa'i zakaatul fitr me agar koi apne hisaab se paise jama'a kare (40, 45, 60, 70) aur phir kul afraad ke mutaabiq us jama shuda raqam me utna hi kilo chaawal khareeda jaaye jo tamaam logon me taqsim kiya jaaye to kaisa hai? Us se logon ko achchi qism ka chaawal milega, chaahe kam mile aur baantne me sahoolat hogi.

Jawab: Zakatul fitr ki raqam ada karne wala is baat ka khayal rakhe ke jis 'alaqe me fitrah waajib ho raha hai us 'alaqe me poora saal 'umoomi taur par anaaj ki jo bhi jins aur jo bhi qism khaayi ja rahi ho us ke e'etibaar se raqam nikaale, phir khareedne wala aisa anaaj khareede jo a'alaa ho ya kam se kam darmiyaani me'eyaar ka ho to in sha Allah fitrah ada ho jaayega (Al-Majmoo':6/133). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

ZAKAAT AUR SADAQAH KE MASA-IL

'Aurat Ke Isti'emaal Ke Zaiwaraat Par Zakaat

Sawal: Kya Shawaafe' ke nazdeek 'aurat ke isti'emaal ke zaiwaraat par zakaat laazim hai?

Jawab: 'Aurat ke isti'emaal ke jaaiz zaiwaraat par zakaat laazim nahi hai albatta zaiwaraat bahut ziyaadah hon ke israaf (fuzool kharchi) ki had (limit) me aa jaayein to zakaat laazim hogi (Fathul-Mu'een:113). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Isti'emaal ke mubaah zaiwaraat me had tajaawuz (violation of limit) se kya muraad hai?

Jawab: Is ki ek muta-'ayyan miqdaar nahi hai, balke 'alaqe ke 'urf wa 'aadat par munhasir hai, jis 'alaqe me 'umooman ziyaadah se ziyaadah jitni miqdaar me zaiwaraat 'auraten isti'emaal karti hai wo wahaan ki 'auraton ke liye jaaiz miqdaar hai aur is se ziyaadah hone par zakaat hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:2/23). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar kisi ke paas do teen set hon to kya yeh had se tajaawuz me shaamil hai?

Jawab: Ji had se tajaawuz us waqt hogi jabke us 'alaqe ka 'urfe 'aam is miqdaar ko israaf aur fuzool kharchi khayal karta ho to ab had tajaawuz ki soorat me zaa-id (excess) zaiwaraat agar nisaab ko pahonch rahe hon to un par zakaat lazim hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/171). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek mubaah aur jaaiz zaiwaraat pe zakaat nahi jab ke zakhere ki niyyat na ho! Kya tamam 'auratein sirf pehanne ki niyyat se zaiwaraat banati hain ya zakhere ki niyyat se? ek ek ke paas 2-3 se ziyadah set hote hain jo bahut kam isti'emaal me aate hain. Kya yeh had se tajaawuz nahi hai? Aksar 'auraton ki niyyat yeh hoti hai ke ziyaadah se ziyaadah sona bana bana ke rakho, kya pata kal kya hoga, kam se kam majboori me yeh kaam aayenge, ho sakta hai ke kuch 'auraton ki niyyat alag ho lekin 'auraton ki fitrat se pata chalta hai ke aksar 'auraton ka yahi khayaal hota hai to kya is me zakaat waajib hai?

Jawab: Bilkul waajib hogi jabke waaqayi 'auraton ki niyyat zakherah andozi ki ho (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/328). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar isti'emaal ke mubaah zaiwaraat toot jaayein (ke wo isti'emaal ke qaabil na rahe) to us par zakaat ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Isti'emaal ke mubaah zaiwaraat agar toot jaayein aur isi soorat par baaqi rakhte huye un ki islaah ka iraadah ho aur bagair pighlaye mumkin ho to zakaat waajib nahi hai lekin agar islaah ka iraadah na ho balke bagair dhaale us se aise hi sone chaandi ke tukde ke taur par rakhne ka iraadah ho ya dirham ya khazaana ka iraadah ho ya bila kisi iraade ke aise hi chod de ya islaah ke liye dobarah pighla kar dhaalne ki zaroorat ho to in tamaam sooraton me zakaat waajib hogi ab saal ka shumaar tootne ke waqt se karenge. (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/30). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla & Mufti Ishaq Patel

Phalon (Fruits) Me Aam (Mango) Par Zakaat Ka Hukm

Sawal: 'Alaqa-e-Kokan me 'umooman Haapoos aam (mango) ki paidawaar bahut ziyaadah hoti hai, jin logon ke bagaat me aam kaseer miqdaar me aate hain wo in aamon ko khud apne isti'emaal me bhi laate hain isi tarah rishtedaaron me bataur hadiyah bhi dete hain, aur baqiyyah aamon ko farokht bhi karte hain. Sharee'ate islaam me phalon par bhi zakaat waajib hai, to kya aam ke phalon me bhi zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek zakaat sirf unhi phalon par waajib hai jin phalon ko bataure gizaa khaya jaata ho aur jinhein zakhera kar ke rakhkha ja sakta ho jaise angoor aur khujoor (date). In ke 'alaawah deegar phalon me zakaat nahi hai. Albatta agar koi shakhs aam ya deegar phalon ki tijaarat kar raha ho aur us par saal guzar jaaye aur us se aane wali aamadni nisaab ke baqadr ho to is ke zimme tijaarat ki zakaat waajib hogi. Aam ka phal, angoor aur khujoor (date) ke phalon ki tarah nahi hota ke is ko bataure gizaa zakhairah karke rakhkha jaata ho. Lihaaza aam ke phalon me zakaat waajib nahi hogi (Al-Majmoo': 5/451). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ghar Banaane Ke Iraade Se Jama Shudah Raqam Par Zakaat

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs ghar banaane ke irade se rupaye jama'a karta hai aur wo raqam nisaab ko pahonchi to kya saal ke baad us raqam par zakaat farz hogi?

Jawab: Ji han, baqadre nisaab raqam par saal guzarne ke baad zakaat farz hogi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 2/22). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Flat Aur Kiraaye Par Zakaat

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs flat khareede is niyyat se ke ek do saal ke baad achchi qeemat aane par use bech dunga to kya us par bhi zakaat waajib hai?

Jawab: Ji han: us flat par bhi zakaat waajib hogi (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 3/1948). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi flat is niyyat se khareede ke use kiraaye par dunga to kya is par zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Baraahe Raast us flat par to zakaat waajib nahi hogi albatta us se aane wali aamadni agar nisaab ki miqdaar ko pahonche aur us par saal guzar jaaye to us jama aamadni par zakaat hogi (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 3/1948). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar kisi ne flat book karne ki niyyat se maalike makaan ko peshgi raqam ada kiya aur us par ek saal guzar gaya aur us makaan ka kaam abhi shuru' bhi na huwa ho to kya us par ada kardah raqam me zakaat farz hogi?

Jawab: Flat book karne wale par booking ki raqam par zakaat waajib nahi hogi, fiqah ki istilaah me is aqd ko aqde Istisna'a kaha jaata hai (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dukaan Ya Makaan Ke Deposit Par Zakaat

Sawal: Aaj kal log dukaan ya makaan kiraaye par dete waqt ek deposit bataure bharosah lete hain take agar dukaan ya makaan ke andar koi nuqsaan ho jaaye to is raqam se nuqsaan ki bharpai ki jasake to Sawal yeh hai ke kya makaan ya dukaan maalik par aur kya kiraayedaar par us ki zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Kiraayedaar makaan ya dukaan ke maalik ko jo deposit deta hai wo raqam use makaan ya dukaan ki wapasi ke waqt wapas milti hai, ya'ani makaan maalik ke paas bataure amaanat hoti hai, lihaaza nisaab ke baqadr deposit ka maal ho to amaanat ki soorat me makaan maalik par us deposit ki raqam par zakaat waajib nahi hogi aur qarz ki niyyat ho aur ek saal ho jaaye to zakaat waajib hogi lekin raqam dene wala yane kiraayedaar agar sahibe nisaab hai to use deposit par zakaat nikalna waajib hoga. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/331). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dukaan Ya Makaan Ke Peshagi Kiraayah Par Zakaat

Sawal: Aaj kal bahut sare makaan ya dukaan maalik 11 maheene ka peshgi (Advance) kiraaya lete hain jis ke 'iwaz (badle me) deposit nahi lete aur makaan maalik ko ek saath raqam milti hai jise wo apne kisi kaam me isti'emaal karta hai. Is muaamale me kis par zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Peshgi (Advance) kiraaya dene wale par zakaat nahi hai, kiraaya lene wale par zakaat us waqt waajib hogi jab ke us par saal guzar jaaye (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/326). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Builder Ka Khareedi Hui Zameen Aur Flat Par Zakaat

Sawal: Agar koi builder imaarat baandhne ki niyyat se ek zameen khareede:

Jawab: Mazkoorah sooraton me niyyat ke mutaabiq hukm hoga:

Provident Fund Par Zakaat

Sawal: Agar mulaazim ki tankhwah se har maheene Provident Fund (P.F) cut ho jaaye to kya is jama shudah raqam par zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Sarkaari ya neemsarkari ya gair sarkaari idaaron me mulaazimeen ki tankhwah me se har mah kuch raqam kaat li jaati hai jo Provident Fund (P.F) me jama hoti hai, aur idaarah use retirement ke baad us me z'iyaadati karke mulazim ko deta hai. Chunke P.F me kaati hui raqam tankhwah se kat-ti hai aur wo idaareh ke zimmeh qarz ke hukm me hai aur qarz ke wapas milne ki ummeed bhi hoti hai is liye P.F me jama shudah raqam mila kar nisaab tak pahonch rahi ho aur saal mukammal ho raha ho to is par zakaat waajib hogi (Fathul-Mu'een: 113, Tuhfatul-Bari:1/334). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Idaarah ki taraf se izaafaah shudah raqam par zakaat kab waajib hogi?

Jawab: Idaarah ki taraf se izaafaah shudah raqam par zakaat us waqt waajib hogi jab us par qabza ho jaaye aur qabze ke baad saal guzar jaaye albatta P.F me apni jama shudah raqam nisaab ko pahonch jaaye to har saal zakaat nikalna waajib hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/335). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

End Of Service (Indemnity) Par Zakaat

Sawal: Jo log bairooni mulk me mulaazamat karte hain wahan ke mazdoori qanoon (Labour Law) ke hisaab se mulaazim ko retirement ya company se nikaale jaane ke baad ya mulaazamat chod dene ke baad end of service milti hai, to kya is raqam par zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Chunke wo raqam mulaazim ki tankhwah se kaati nahi jaati balke wo raqam company ya hukoomat ki taraf se mulaazim ke liye hadiya hoti hai, jis par mulaazim ka filwaqt qabaza nahi hota, is liye us raqam par filhaal zakaat waajib nahi hogi balke jab mulaazim company chod dega ya company nikaal degi aur use end of service milegi aur us par saal guzar jaayega aur us waqt wo mulaazim sahibe nisaab bhi hoga to us raqam par zakaat waajib hogi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 265). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sone Ke Daant Par Zakaat

Sawal: Kisi ka daant toot jaaye phir wo sone ka daant lagwaye to kya us par zakaat lazim hogi?

Jawab: Sone ke lagaye huwe daant par zakaat lazim nahi hai (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 1/169). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qarz Ki Raqam Par Zakaat

Sawal: Agar kisi ne kisi shakhs ko qarz diya aur jis shakhs ko qarz diya us ki maali haalat behtar nahi hai is liye qarz dene wale ne iraadah kiya ke agar us ki haalat durust nahi hui to qarz mu'aaf kar dunga lekin dil me niyyat hai ke agar us ki maali haalat behtar hui aur us ne qarz waapas kiya to le lunga. Kya soorate mas-uoolah me us raqam par zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Dono sooraton me us par zakaat waajib hogi agarche qarz dene wala us raqam ko wapas na le lekin filhaal to wo raqam wapas lene par qaadir hai, goya ke raqam usee ki milkiyat me hai. (Fathul-Mu'een: 113). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar kisi ne kisi shakhs ko qarz diya aur jis shakhs ko qarz diya us ki maali haalat behtar nahi thi, isliye qarz dene wale ne apna qarz mu'aaf kar diya to kya soorate mas-uoolah me us raqam par zakaat waajib hogi?

Jawab: Agar us shakhs ne qarzdar ka qarz saal ki takmeel se pehle mu'aaf kar diya to us raqam par zakaat waajib nahi hogi lekin saal ki takmeel ke baad mu'aaf kar raha hai to zakaat waajib hogi. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit tullaab: 2/28). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zakaat Keh Kar Dena

Sawal: Kya zakaat ki raqam mustahiq ko dete waqt zakaat keh kar dena zaroori hai, ya bagair kuch kahe di ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Zakaat keh kar dena zaroori nahi hai, dene wale ki niyyat zakaat ada karne ki hona zaroori hai, lene wale ko is ki ittila'a ki zaroorat nahi ke yeh zakaat hai bas us ka mustahiq rehna kaafi hai taaham hadiyah keh kar nahi de sakte. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 2/204, Al-Majmoo': 6/223). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Peshgi (Advance) Zakaat Nikaalna

Sawal: Kya Peshgi (Advance) zakaat nikaalna durust hai?

Jawab: Peshgi (Advance) me zakaat de sakte hain lekin chaad sharaait paaye jaane ki soorat mein:

Pehli Shart: Maal ka maalik saal mukammal hone tak zakaat ke nisaab ka maalik rahe.

Doosri Shart: Us ka maal saal poora hone tak nisaab ki miqdaar par baqi rahe nisaab se kam na ho.

Teesri Shart: Peshgi zakaat ke maal par qabzah karne wala shakhs saal ke mukammal hone tak zakaat ka mustahiq rahe (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 3/176). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zakaat Ke Mustahiqqeen

Sawal: Zakaat ke mustahiqqeen kitne hain?

Jawab: Zakaat ke mustahiqqeen 8 log hain:

Mustahiqqeene Zakaat Me Se Har Qism Ke Teen Logon Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Agar mustahiqqeene zakaat me se har qism ke teen logon ko zakaat di jaaye to kya zakaat ada hogi?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke muta'addad (multiple) 'Ulama'-E-kibaar (Senior scholars) aur aksar muta-akkhireen ki raaye ke mutaabiq mazkoorah soorat me zakaat ada ho jaayegi (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/343). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Peshaawaraanah Faqeer Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Peshaawaraanah faqeer jo masjid ke darwaaze par khade rehte hain ya ghar par maangne aaya karte hain kya un ko zakaat di ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Zakaat to aath (8) mustahiqqeen me se kisi ko bhi di ja sakti hai, aur un hi me se faqeer bhi hai lekin agar maangane walon ka haal ma'aloom na ho ke wo haqeeqatan mustahiq hai ya nahi zakaat dena durust nahi, aur 'umooman Peshaawar faqeeron ki maali haalat achchi hoti hai, jis ki wajah se wo mustahiq ki fehrist me shaamil nahi hote. Balke Maulana Yusuf Ludhyaanwi (Rh) ne “Aap ke Masa-il aur un ka Hal” me Peshaawar faqeeron ko nifli saqadah dena bhi mamnu' likkha hai. Wallahu A'alam. (Haashiyatut Tahtaavi: 1/722). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Madaaris Ke Sufaraa Ibne Sabil (Musafir) Ko Zakaat

Sawal: Kya madaaris ke sufaraa ibne sabil (Musafir) ki fehrist me daakhil ho kar zakaat ke mustahiq qaraar payeinge?

Jawab: Madaaris ke sufaraa ko intizaamiyah (committee) mukammal safar kharch de kar bhejti hai jis se un ki safar ki saari zarooriayat poori ho jaati hai, albatta agar wo apni niji zindagi me zarooratmand hon aur zakaat ke mustahiq hon to unhain bhi zakaat di ja sakti hai (Nihaayatul Matlab: 11/561). Mufti Ishaq Patel & Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sadaat Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Ahle Bait (Sadaat) kise kehte hain? Kya Ahle bait (Sadaat) ko kisi soorat me zakaat de sakte hain?

Jawab: Ahle bait se murad banu haashim aur banu muttalib hain. Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek raje'ah qaul mumaana'at ka hai ya'ani ahle bait ko zakaat dena jaaiz nahi lekin chunke maujoodah daur me in ko baitul maal se khumus ka khumus (Paanchwe Hisse ka panchwah) nahi milta is wajah se halaate haazirah ke tahat mutaqaddimeen wa muta-akhkhireen Fuqahaa-e-Kiraam ne sadaat ko khumus ka khumus na milne ki soorat me zakaat dene ki ijaazat di hai. (Kifaayatun Nabeeh: 6/210). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Muslim Mustahiq Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Kya gair muslim mustahiq ko zakaat dena durust hai?

Jawab: Zakaat musalmaan mustahiq hi ko dena zaroori hai kyunke yeh musalmanon ka haq hai gair muslim is ka haqdaar nahi gair muslim ko dene se zakaat ada nahi hogi. (Fathul-Mu'een: 122). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Zakaat Ada Karne Ke Liye Kisi Ko Wakeel Banana

Sawal: Jis ke upar zakaat farz hui hai agar koi doosra shakhs us ki ijazat se zakaat ada kare to kya us ki zakaat ada ho jaayegi?

Jawab: Ji han: Saahibe Maal agar apni zakaat ada karne ke liye kisi ko wakeel banaye to zakaat ada ho jaayegi (Minhaajut-Talibeen: 267). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Doosre Shakhs Ka Apne Maal Se Saahibe Maal Ki Zakaat Ada Karna

Sawal: Saahibe maal ki zakaat koi aur shakhs apne maal se nikaale to kya saahibe maal ki zakaat ada ho jaayegi?

Jawab: Is shart ke saath ada ho jaayegi ke saahibe maal us shakhs ko ijaazat de phir wo shakhs saahibe maal se miqdaar jaan kar saahibe maal ki jaanib se farz zakaat ki adaayigi ki niyyat karega. (Al-bayaan: 3/364). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qistwaar Zakaat Ki Adaayigi

Sawal: Kya zakaat ki raqam saal bhar thodi thodi karke ada ki ja sakti hai ya ek hi martabah saari raqam waajibul ada hoti hai?

Jawab: Zakaat ki adayigi ka waqt aur qudrat ho jaane ke baad fauran zakaat ki raqam nikaal kar mustahiqeen tak pahonchana waajib hai warna gunahgaar hoga, kyunke mustahiqeen filwaqt us ke Mohtaaj aur zarooratmand hain lihaaza taakheer karna haraam hai, albatta kisi rishtedaar ke intizaar me ya padosi ke intizaar me ya ziyaadah zarooratmand ke intizaar me ya afzal shakhs ke intizaar me taakheer karta hai to jaaiz hoga, jabke haazireen ko ziyaadah zarar na ho lekin agar nikaala huwa maal ada karne se pehle talf (destroy / waste) ho jaaye aur barbaad ho jaaye to yeh shakhs zaamin hoga aur us ke zimme yeh zaroori hoga ke utni hi raqam dobaarah nikaal kar ada kare.(Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 2/53). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Rishtedaaron Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Aise Rishtedaar jin ka nafaqah saahibe maal par laazim na ho kya unhain zakaat di ja sakti hai? Masalan haqeeqi bhai bahan, chacha, mamu, khala, mamuzaad chachazaad phuphizaad bhai bahan wagayrah.

Jawab: Ji han: in rishtedaaron ko jabke wo mustahiq hon zakaat de sakte hain balke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke farmaan ke mutaabiq yahan dohrah ajr milega ek zakaat aur ek silahrehmi ka (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/349). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Shohar Ka Biwi Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Kya Shohar biwi ko zakaat de sakta hai?

Jawab: Chunke Biwi ka nafaqah shohar ke zimme laazim hota hai is liye shohar ke liye jaaiz nahi ke wo biwi ko miskeen ya faqeer ke madd se zakaat de lekin agar biwi maqrooz ya fi sabeelillah wagayrah sifaat me daakhil ho to is sifat ki munasibat se shohar biwi ko zakaat de sakta hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 2/65). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Biwi Ke Paas Mahar Ki Shakl Me Zaivaraat Ke Bawajood Miskeen Shohar Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs miskeen ho lekin us ki biwi ke paas mahar ki shakl me zaivaraat hon to kya us shakhs ko zakaat dena durust hai?

Jawab: Mahar ke zaivaraat 'aurat ki milkiyat hai aur shohar us ka maalik nahi lihaaza miskeen hone ki wajah se use zakaat di ja sakti hai (Fathul-Mu'een:122). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Ladki Ka Apne Mustahiq Waalidain Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Kya ladki apne gareeb wa mustahiq waalidain ko zakaat de sakti hai?

Jawab: Agar zakaat khud ladki ke maal ki ho to wo us ke mustahiq waalidain ko nahi de sakti albatta agar wo ladki apne shohar ke maal ki zakaat apne waalidain ko dena chaahe to jaaiz hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 2/65). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Biwi Ka Apne Mustahiq Shohar Ko Zakaat Dena

Sawal: Kya saahibe istita'at biwi jis par zakaat farz hui hai apne mustahiq shohar ko zakaat de sakti hai?

Jawab: Ji han, Saahibe istita'at 'aurat apne gareeb shohar ko zakaat de sakti hai, chunke shohar ka nafaqah biwi ke zimme waajib nahi hai. (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 5/210). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Mahe Ramazaan Hi Me Zakaat Ada Karna

Sawal: Kya zakaat mahe ramazaan hi me ada karna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Jab maal nisaab ko pahonche aur us par saal guzar jaaye to zakaat nikaalna farz ho jaata hai, ramazaan hi me zakaat nikaalna koi zaroori nahi balke ramazaan ho ya gair ramazaan nisaab ke baqadr maal par jab saal poora ho jaaye to usi waqt nikaalna chahiye, ramazaan ka intizaar na kare kyunke saal ki takmeel ke baad ramazaan ki fazeelat ke liye zakaat ko muakkhar (delay) karna haraam hai albatta apne sharaait ke saath bataure ta'ajil (jaldi) ramazaan me zakaat de sakte hain (Bahrul-Mazhab: 10/394, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/129). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Ijtimaa'i Taur Par Zakaat

Sawal: Zakaat ke mustahiqqeen me se 'aamil (jo zakaat wasool kare aur taqseem kare) bhi hai is ka yahi matlab hai ke zakaat ijtimaa'i taur par nikaalna afzal hai jaisa ke Huzoor (ﷺ) ke zamaane me hota tha.

Aaj hum log is ki kyun fikr nahi karte? Waise bhi har ijtimaa'i 'amal ka sawab infiraadi (individual) 'amal se ziyaadah hota hai. Jis tarah Anjuman Dardmandane Talim wa Taraqqi ne Alhamdulillah ijtimaa'i fitrah ko taqreeban poore konkan me 'aam kiya, kya ijtimaa'i zakaat ki koi shakl hai?

Jawab: Ijtimaa'i nizaam agar banaaya jaaye to ziyaadah behtar hoga. Lekin jahan maal ka muamalah hota hai wahan tohmat ka bhi andesha hota hai aur chunke rishtedaar apne gareeb rishtedaar ke halaat se ziyaadah waqif hote hain. Is daur me jitna khayal qareebi rashtedaar rakh sakta hai ijtimaa'i nizaam wale nahi rakh sakte. Wo to muta-'ayyan miqdaar hi denge, phir bhi zakaat ka kuch hissa Dardmand ya usi ke maanind deegar idaaron me jama'a karna behtar hai. Baqi raqam apne haath se dena hi behtar hoga. (ujaalatul Muhtaaj: 1/468). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sone Ki Zakaat Rupayon Ki Shakl Me Dena

Sawal: Kya sone ki zakaat rupayon ki shakl me dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Hattal imkaan sone ki zakaat sone hi ki shakl mien dena zaroori hai, albatta majboori ki soorat me aur halaat aur fuqaraa ke faaide ko peshe nazar rakhte huwe sone chaandi ki zakaat raaij sikkon aur rupayon mien nikaalna bhi jaaiz hai. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:23/298). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tankhwah Me Se Mahaanah Bachat Par Zakaat Kis Tarah Ada Kare?

Sawal: Mai ek Oil aur Gas ki badi service company me mulaazamat kar raha hun, Allah ke fazl wa karam se meri tankhwah ziyadah hai aur har apni tankhwah ka ek bada hissa bachat ke taur par jama'a karta hu aur mere paas pehle mah (maslan yakum ramazaan) me us waqt ke mutabiq nisaabe zakaat se ziyadah raqam (3 lakh) jama ho gai thi aur us ke baad mere paas maujood raqam mukhtalif maheenon me kam ziyadah hoti rahi lekin nisaabe zakaat se kam nahi hui. Nisaabe zakaat se 30 sha'abaan ko ek saal mukammal ho aur us se chand din pehle meri tankhwah mili aur un dino tankhwah se choti raqam isti'emaal hui. Aur 30 sha'abaan ko mere paas 12 lakh baqi the, jis me se kuch raqam ko chand din, isi tarah kuch raqam ko chand maheene ho chuke the.

'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke Shafi'ee mas-alah bayan kare:

Jawab: (1) Ha har maah me baqi raqam par ek saal mukammal hone ke baad zakaat ada karni hogi, agar kisi maah ki baaqi raqam me se kuch raqam kharch ho jaaye to jitni raqam is maah me baaqi rahegi aur us par saal guzar jaaye to usi par zakaat farz hogi aur phir jis waqt is raqam me izaafah hoga aur wo izaafah ke saath saal bhar baaqi rahe to zakaat farz hogi, agar baaqi raqam aakhari maah se kam ho jaaye to kam hone ke baad jitni raqam bachegi usi par zakaat hogi, albatta jo kharch ho jaaye us par zakaat na hogi.

(2) Mukammal 12 lakh par zakaat farz na hogi balke jitni raqam par saal guzara ho usi par zakaat farz hogi, albatta agar abhi mukammal 12 lakh par zakaat nikaale to darmiyani saal jis raqam par saal mukammal ho jaayega to phir dobaarah us ki zakaat nikaalne ki zaroorat na hogi.

Hazrat Ibne 'umr (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaya: “Jise koi maal haasil ho to us par koi zakaat nahi jab tak ke us par us ke maalik ke yahan ek saal na guzar jaaye.” (Rawahut Tirmizi: 631)

Allamah Rafa'ee (Rh) ka bayan hai: Bahar haal jo maal (Janwar wagayrah) darmiyan saal kharidne ke zariye ya waraasat ya hadiyah ke taur par haasil ho to us maal ko saal ke darmiyaan wale maal se nahi milaya jaayega.” (Fathul-'Aziz: 2/526). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Zakaat Ke Mutafarriq Masa-il

Sawal: Agar kisi ne kisi mustahiq ko zakaat dene ka iraadah kiya lekin wo use mil nahi raha ho phir koi aur mustahiq mile to kya use zakaat de sakte hain ya usi mustahiq ko dena hoga jis ka iraadah kiya tha?

Jawab: Zakaat waajib hone ke baad jald se jald mustahiqqeen ko dena zaroori hai. Agar kisi ne kisi mustahiq ko zakaat dene ka iraadah kiya to use dene ke liye intizaar kar sakte hain lekin agar wo na mile aur koi aur zakaat ka mustahiq mil jaaye to use bhi de sakte hain. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/349, Tahreerul-Fatawa: 2/468). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Jo log zameen ki khareed wa farokht ka kaarobar karte hain aur zameen is niyyat se khareed kar rakhte hain ke munaasib qeemat aane par farokht karenge to kya us zameen par zakaat laazim hogi?

Jawab: Agar us zameen ko tijaarat hi ki garz se khareeda gaya hai to us par saal guzarne ke baad zakaat waajib hogi (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 3/1948) lekin agar zameen ko khud ke isti'emaal ki niyyat se khareeda tha lekin baad me chal kar us ka iraadah badal gaya aur us ne use farokht karne ki niyyat ki to zakaat waajib nahi hogi kyunke mas-alah ke mutabiq usi maale tijaarat par zakaat laazim hoti hai jo ibtidah hi se khaalis tijaarat ki niyyat se khareeda gaya ho. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs bairun mulk me mulaazamat kar raha ho aur wo kisi haadise ka shikaar ya badi beemaari me mubtala (afflicted) ho kar Kauma me chala jaaye aur do teen mahinon se ziyadah hosh me na rahe us ke biwi aur bachcheh bhi wahan us ke saath hon. Bank ne us shakhs ka Debit Card block kiya kyunke wo hosh me nahi hai aur biwi ko is ki ijaazat nahi hai ke wo use isti'emaal kare. Agar us ka intiqaal huwa ya use company ne terminate kiya to use end of service bhi milegi lekin abhi us ka koi faaidah nahi hai kyunke biwi ko apne aur apne Bachchon ke ikhraajaat ke liye filwaqt maal ki zaroorat hai, kya is soorat me us ki biwi aur bachche zakaat ke mustahiq honge?

Jawab: Agar aafiyat se qarz ka intizaam ho sakta ho to behtar yahi hai ke wo qarz le kar apni zarooriyat me kharch karegi, lekin agar koi qarz na de raha ho to filwaqt zarooratmand hone ki wajah se use zakaat ki raqam dena jaaiz hoga. (Fathul Qaribul Mujeeb: 1/133). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Gazzah (City of Palestine) ke pareshan musalmanon ko zakaat de sakte hain? Wo mustahiqqeene zakaat ke kis sho'abe me aayenge?

Jawab: In me se jo zarooratmand hon unhain faqeer ya miskeen ke lihaaz se di ja sakti hai aur jo bar sare pekar hain unhain mujahideen ke e'etibaar se diya jaayega agarcha wo maldaar hon. (('Umdatus-Salik: 101-103). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Nifli Sadaqah Gair Muslim Ko Dena

Sawal: Konsa sadaqah gairon ko dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Nifli sadaqah gair muslim ko dena jaaiz hai, jo ke haqqe insaaniyyat ki bunyaad par bataure imdaad diya jaata hai (Bidayatul Muhtaaj: 2/696). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Bahut Jald Manzar-E-'Aam Par Aane Wali Kitaab 📘 "Islami Bank-Kaari: Haqiqat, Zarurat Aur Ishqalaat Ka Izaalah"

Aaj ke daur mein jab ma'eeshat ka pahiyyah soodi nizaam ke gird ghoom raha hai, aisey mein Islami Bank-kaari sirf ek mutabadil nahi balke aik shara'ee taqaza bhi hai.

Isi aham zarurat ke pehlu ko madde nazar rakhte hue Jami'aa Zia-ul-'Uloom Kundloor – Karnataka ke Shu'bah Tadreebe Iftah ke ek taalib-e- 'ilm Maulavi 'Usman Sadi Papa ko yeh mauzoo' baraye tahqeeq diya gaya tha. Unhon ne apne ustad Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Mahmood Barmare Husaini aur Maulana Jasim Mahadik ki nigrani mein is mauzoo' par gehre mutaala'aah, 'ilmi tajziye aur mustanad dalaeelon ke saath ek jaame'e maqalah tahreer kiya tha.

Yeh maqalah na sirf Islami Bank-kaari ki bunyadon aur usoolon ko wazeh karta hai balke un ishqalaat aur e'etiraazaat ka mudallal aur 'ilmi jawab bhi pesh karta hai jo 'aam taor par logon ke zehno mein paida hote hain.

Ab yeh maqalah kuch zaroori aur jadeed masa-il ke izafe ke saath, 'awaami mafaad ke pehlu ko dekhte hue, kitaabi shakal mein shaaye'e kiya jaayega taake 'aam log bhi Islami Bank-kaari ke usooolon ko samajh sakein aur is ke muta'alliq paaye janae wale ishqalaat aur galat fahmiyon ka izaalah kar sakein.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

Kuch Ahamm Tanbeehaat wa Tafseelaat

Af'aale 'Umrah Ka Naqsha'a

Ayyame Hajj Me A'amaal

Sawal: Hujjaje Kiraam ke 'alaawah deegar logon ke liye in ayyam me kon se a'amaal afzal hain?

Jawab: Is mahine ke Pehle das ayyam hame badi ahmiyat aur fazilat ke mile hain, jis ka tazkirah Qur'aan me bhi aaya hai. In ayyam me bandah aur bandi khoob Allah ke Huzoor 'ibadat karein aur khususan 'arafah ka roza rakhein to ziyadah behtar hai aur sawab ka kaam hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen:2/388). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Umrah Aur Hajj Ke Af'aal

Sawal: 'Umrah aur Hajj ke af'aal ki wazahat karein?

Jawab: 'Umrah wa hajj ke af'aal ki teen (3) Qismein hain:

Hajj Aur 'Umrah Ke Arkaan

Sawal: Hajj aur 'umrah ke arkaan ki wazahat karein?

Jawab: Hajj ke arkaan panch hain:

In arkaan me tarteeb wajib hai. Ihram sab par muqaddam hoga. Wuqoof-e-'Arafah ka Tawaaf-e-Ifazah aur halaq par muqaddam hona lazim hai aur sa'ee ka Tawaaf-e-Qudoom ya Tawaaf-e-Ifazah ke baad hona shart hai. Tawaaf wa halaq me tarteeb shart nahi (Mugniul Muhtaaj:2/285).

Wuqoof -e-'arafah ke 'alaawah baqiyah charon rukn (Ihram, Tawaaf, Sa'ee, Halaq ya Taqseer) 'umrah ke bhi arkaan hain. Mufti Ishaq Patel

Hajj Ke Wajibaat

Sawal: Hajj ke wajibaat kya hain?

Jawab: Hajj ke wajibaat panch hain:

(Al-Mohazzab:1/424). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Farz Hajj Ki Adayigi Me Awwaliyat

Sawal: Agar kisi ke paas itna maal ho jo do (2) logon ke hajj ke kharch ke liye kafi ho sakta hai to use kya karna chahiye ya wo khud apni biwi ke sath hajj kare ya budhe walidain ko hajj karaye?

Jawab: Sab se Pehle to khud us ko hajj karna chahiye kyunke sahibe istita'at hone ki wajah se khud us par hajj farz ho chuka hai neez mazeed istita'at ho to biwi ko karana chahiye kyunke shohar ke liye mustahab hai ke biwi ke sath hajj kare (Hashiyatul Iezaah: 50). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar aadmi dusra hajj kare to kya wo nifli hua?

Jawab: Dusare hajj ki agar nazar ya mannat mani thi to farz hoga warna nifl (Faizul Ilahil Malik:1/457). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khud Apni Zaat Se Pehle Walidain Ko Hajj Ke liye Bhejna

Sawal: Kya insaan khud hajj karne se Pehle walidain ko hajj ke liye bhej sakta hai?

Jawab: Hajj karna har musalman, aazad, 'aaqil, baligh aur istita'at rakhne wale par zindagi me ek baar farz hai, lihaza agar bachche par hajj farz ho chuka hai to ab wo walidain ko tarjih nahi de sakta balke Pehle khud apna hajj ada kare, albattah kisi wajib ki adayigi ke liye walidain ki i'aanat baqadre istita'at karna shara'ee taur par durust hai. (Asnal-Matalib: 1/457). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qarzah Ki Wajah Se Hajj Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar kisi ke paas itna maal ho ke wo hajj par ja sakta ho lekin us par qarzah ho. (1) Deendari ya itni ma'alumaat na hone ki wajah se bank ka qarzah liya ho, lekin mahanah qist pabandi se de raha ho aur us ki adayigi hajj khatam hone ke baad tak wajib ho. (2) Kisi rishte dar ya dost ka qarzah liya ho.

Jawab: Hajj se Pehle agar qarz ki adayigi ka waqt ho aur itna paisa na ho ke qarz ada karne ke baad hajj kare to is par filhaal hajj farz nahi hai, lekin agar koi qarz ke hote hue hajj kar le to us ka hajj sahih hoga, isi tarah agar qarz wala qarz ada karne se Pehle hajj karne ki ijazat de tab bhi koi harj nahi hoga. Hajj farz hone ke liye sahibe istita'at hona chahiye magar us shakhs par qarz ki adayigi baqi hai to goya uske paas maal hi nahi hai jab maal nahi to hajj bhi farz nahi (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/386). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Upar ke mas-aleh me bank se sood par loan le kar us ne gunah ka kam kiya hai, to kya us ka hajj ho jayega? Aur agar us ke paas itna maal hai ke wo poora qarzah ada kar sakta hai to kya use us loan se jald se jald chhutkara nahi pana chahiye? Afzal aur behtar kya hai?

Jawab: Is soorat me us ke zimme se hajj ka farizah saqit hoga lekin afzal aur behtar yahi hai ke qarz ki adayigi ke baad hajj ko jaye. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 3/408). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Do Ghar Hone Ki Soorat Me Hajj Ki Farziyat

Sawal: Agar ksi ke paas do ghar hon jin me se ek ki qimat itni hai jis se us ka farizae hajj ada ho sakta hai to kya wo hajj ke liye sahibe istita'at shumar hoga?

Jawab: Us ka dusra ghar chun ke iski zaroorat se zayid hai lihaza wo sahibe istita'at shumar hoga aur use us zayid ghar ko bech kar farizaye hajj ada karna zaroori hai (AlFiqhul Manhaji: 1/364). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Zameen Wa Ja'idaad (Property) Ki Soorat Me Hajj Ki Farziyat

Sawal: Agar kisi ke paas zameen wa jayidad ki soorat me maal hai lekin achche daam na milne ki wajah se wo us ko bechna nahi chahta to kya is soorat me hajj karna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Agar zameen wa Ja'idaad (Property) ke bechne se itna paisa milta ho jis ki wajah se us ke hajj ke saare ikhrajaat pure hote hon to is soorat me use zameen wa Ja'idaad (Property) bech kar hajj karna zaroori hoga (Al-Najmaul Wahaj:3/406). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Hajj Ke Liye Tour Walo Ka Advance Me Kimat/Rupaye Lena

Sawal: Kuch hajj tours wale 6 se 12 mahine Pehle advanced me kimat/rupaye lete hai aur hajj ke liye discounted rate dete hain. Mere khayal se us ki asal wajah yeh hai ke wo jab advance me ek sath ziyadah room kisi hotal me book karte hain to unhe rate achcha milta hai aur sath hi advance me achchi raqam milti hai jis se hotel wale ko bhi faidah hota hai aur sath hi tour wale kuch hissah apne doosre kamon me bhi iste'emaal karte hain?

Jawab: Koi haraj nahi, kyunke wo bataure ujrat raqam le raha hai to jaise chahe kar sakta hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji 'Ala mazhabil Imaamish Shafi'ee:6/141). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Umrah Wa Hajj Ke Liye Maani-E-Haiz Dawai Iste'emaal Karna

Sawal: Kya 'aurat 'umrah wa hajj ke liye maana-e-haiz (haiz ko rokni wali) dawai iste'mal kar sakti hai take pure safar me paki hasil rahe kyun ke har ek ko 'umrah wa hajj ki sa'aadat bar bar nahi milti?

Jawab: Hadise paak me Nabi Karim (ﷺ) ka irshad hai: ''yeh taba'ee aur fitri khoon Aadam ki betion ke muqaddar me likkha ja chuka hai''. Lehaza ise dawaon ke zariye rokna 'aam halat me muzir aur nuqsandeh hota hai, isi liye 'umuman atibbaa (doctors) 'auraton ko is se mana'a karte hain, 'auraton ka hajj ya 'umrah basa auqaat ayyame haiz me hota hai jis me hajj wa 'umrah ke mukammal arkaan ki adayigi nahi ho pati aur visa, ticket isi tarah tour ke aamadoraft ki tarteeb par apna koi ikhtiyar nahi hota, lihaza aise halaat me un ke liye maani-e-haiz goliyon ke khane ki ijazat hai take khawaateen mashaqqat me na padein aur un ka hajj ya 'umrah ba-husno khubi anjaam pa jaye (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/362). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hajiyon Ka Khidmat Wali Visa Le Kar Hajj Karna

Sawal: Kuch log ayyame Hajj me hajiyon ki khidmat wali visa le kar jate hain aur hajj karte hain. Kya yeh durust hai? Hajj hoga ya nahi isliye ke yeh visa sasta hota hai balke kuch tour wale khidmat ka muawazah (qimat) bhi dete hain.

Jawab: Hajiyon ka khidmat ya tijarat wali visa le kar hajj karne me koi harj nahi, jaaiz hai kyunke Allah Tabarak wa Ta'aala ne surah Baqrah me hajiyon ko hajj ke ayyam me tijarat ke sath sath hajj karne ki ijazat di hai agarche aula ya'ani behtar yahi hai ke khalis hajj ki niyyat se hajj kare aur zikar wa 'ibadat ke 'alaawah ksi aur chiz me mashgul naa hon. Chunanche Harzrat Ibne Abbas (Rz) ne bayan kiya ke Ukaaz, Majannah aur Zulmajaz zamanaye jahiliyat ke bazaar (mele) the, isliye (Islam ke baad) mausame hajj me Sahaba-e-Kiraam (Rz) ne wahan karobar ko bura samjha to aayat nazil hui ke “Tumhein is bare me koi harj nahi ke tum apne parwardeegar ke yahan se talashe ma'aash karo.” Ya'ani mausame hajj me tijarat ke liye mazkoora mandiyon me jawo aur rizqe halal ki talash karo (Saheeh Al-Bukhari:1770, Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Mayassarah: 4/253). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ayyame Haiz Me 'Umrah Ke Liye Safar Shuru' Karna

Sawal: Agar 'aurat haiz ke ayyam me 'umrah ke liye safar shuru' kare to kya kare?

Jawab: Agar 'aurat haiz ke ayyam me ho to wo usi halat me Ihram ka gusl le kar 'umrah ki niyyat kar ke makkah ke liye rawanah ho jaye, is halat me bhi 'aurat Ihram ki pabandiyon me rahegi aur phir paaki hasil ho jane ke baad 'umrah ke arkaan ada karegi (Al-Bayan Fi Mazhabil Imam Shafi'ee:4/72). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hajj Ke Liye Jhoot Ka Sahara Lena

Sawal: Ek Aadmi General Category me hone ke bawajood Backward class ka certificate bana ke mulazamat hasil karta hai. Kya is mulazamat se hasil hone wale maal se hajj kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Galat Certificate banana gunah hai kyunke yeh jhoot bhi hai aur dhokah bhi lekin chunke yeh tankhwah us ke 'amal aur mehnat ki wajah se hai aur 'amal pe ujrat ya tankhwah hasil karna jaaiz aur halal tariqah hai, isliye is ki aamadani halal hi mutasawwar hogi na ke haraam, isliye is raqam se hajj karna durust hai, albattah use apne is jhoot pe taubah karni chahiye (Jadid Fiqhi masa-il, Maulana Khalid Saifullah Rahmani Sahab). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi Government mulazim ho aur hajj karne ke liye use P.F (Provident Fund) jo har mahinah us ki tankhwah se kat-ti hai, ka iste'emaal karna zaroori ho lekin aaj kal hajj ke liye ziyadah raqam lagti hai jo P.F se nahi nikal sakte kyun ke is ki bhi ek limit hai. P.F se ziyadah raqam nikalne ke liye use jhoot ka sahara lena padta hai. Kya jhoot bol kar P.F se raqam nikal kar hajj ko jasakte hain?

Jawab: Agar zindagi me hajj farz ho gaya hai to bataure majboori jhoot ka sahara le kar P.F se paise nikal kar hajj kare kyunke wo maal usi ka apna haq hai, isliye deeni farize ki adayigi ke liye jhoot ka sahara le sakte hain lekin agar farz hajj ada kiya ho to sunnat hajj ke liye jhoot ki ijazat nahi mil sakti. (Az-Zawaajir 'an iqtirafil kabaair:2/326). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kuwait me Labour Law ke hisab se har musalman ko apne farz hajj ke liye Government ki taraf se bayees (22) din tankhwah ke sath chhutti hoti hai.

Jawab: Shart ki khilaf warzi payi jarahi hai aur jhoot bhi, isliye chhuttiyon ki tankhwah halal nahi hogi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji 'Ala mazhabil Imaamish Shafi'ee: 6/151). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zindagi Me Baar Baar Hajj Karna

Sawal: Kya zindagi me baar baar hajj karna fazilat ka bais hai?

Jawab: Sahibe Istita'at par 'umr bhar me ek bar hajj karna aur ek bar 'umrah karna farz hai, iske baad jab bhi hajj ya 'umrah kiya jayega wo nifl hoga. Hadise paak me baar baar hajj aur 'umrah karne ki badi fazilat warid hui hai, chunanche Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ne irshad farmaya: Lagataar baar-baar hajj wa 'umrah kiya karo. Beshak yeh dono (Ya'ani hajj wa 'umrah) faqar wa faqah aur gunahon ko is tarah door kar dete hain jis tarah bhatti lohe aur sone wa chandi ke mail kuchel ko door kar deti hai.

Isi tarah Aap (ﷺ) ne yeh bhi irshad farmaya ke ek 'umrah ke baad jab insan doosra 'umrah karta hai to yeh doosra 'umrah darmiyan ke gunahon ke liye kaffarah ban jata hai: (Bukhari, baab wujoobil 'Umrah wa fazliha, hadis No. 1773), albattah is hadis me gunah se murad sagirah gunah hain. Lihaza is fazilat ke hasil karne ke liye agar koi shakhs baar baar hajj wa 'umrah karna chahe to jaaiz hai. Albattah daure hazir me hukumate Saudiya ne intazami sahulat ki bina par har mulk ke liye hajiyon ki nashishtain muta-'ayyan kar rakhi hain, aur kuch sakht qanoon bhi banaye hain take har mustati musalman farizaye hajj bakhoobi anjam de sake. Lihaza is qanoon ki ri'aayat karte hue ahle sarwat aur maal daar hazrat 5 ya 6 saalon ka waqfah rakhte hue hajj ada karen to behtar hoga take tamam log is farizah ko anjam de sakein. (Fathul-Mu'een:138). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hajj Ke Safar Ke Liye Gair Muslim Se Du'aa

Sawal: Hajj ke safar ki kamiyabi ke liye gair muslim se du'aa ki darkhwast karna kaisa hai?
