Jawab: Gair muslim se du'aa ki darkhwast karna jaaiz nahi kyunke wo gairullah se du'aa karega jo bazahir gairullah ki 'ibadat ki tayeed hai jo jaaiz nahi (Hashiyatul-Jamal:2/119). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mayyit Ki Taraf Se Hajj

Sawal: Agar kisi marhoom ki hayat me us par hajj farz nahi hua tha to kya us ke rishtedar us ke intiqal ke baad us ki wasiyyat ke bagair hajj kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Ji haan, kar sakte hain bashart yeh ke un ki taraf se aisa shakhs hajj kare jis ka hajj wa 'umrah ho chuka ho to wo hajj 'umrah sahih ho jayega. Albattah mayyit ki taraf se sunnat hajj ya 'umrah karna ho to wasiyyat zaroori hai (Al-Majmoo': 7/81, AlBayan:4/52). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi mayyit apni hayat me apne maal me se hajj ki wasiyyat kare to kya us ke rishtedar par us ke intiqal ke baad us ki wasiyyat ki bina par hajj karna zaroori hai ya nahi? Shafi'ee maslak bayan farmayein?

Jawab: Agar mayyit par hajj farz ho chuka ho to khwah us ne wasiyyat ki ho ya nahi us mayyit ke rishtedaron par wajib hai ke us ke tarke (chodi hui jaaidaad) me se hajj ada karein lekin nifli hajj wa 'umrah ke liye wasiyyat zaroori hogi. Soorate mas-uoolah me marne ke baad us ke chode huye maal ke teen hisse kiye jayenge aur agar mumkin ho to tihayi hisseh se us ki wasiyyat poori ki jayegi, lekin agar ziyadah raqam lagti ho aur wariseen ki ijazat ho to us ki taraf se hajj ada kiya jayega warna nahi (Tuhfatul-Bari:2/215). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bachchon Ka Hajj Wa 'Umrah

Sawal: Kya bachchon ka hajj wa 'umrah karna sahih hai? Agar kisi bachche ne baligh hone se Pehle hajj wa 'umrah kiya hoga to kya us ka farz ada ho jayega?

Jawab: Bachchon par hajj wa 'umrah farz nahi hai, lekin agar koi bachcha hajj wa 'umrah kare to wo durust aur sahih hoga aur ajr walidain ko milega. Agar bachche ne nabaligh hone se Pehle hajj wa 'umrah kiya hoga to wo farz shumar nahi hoga balke wo nifl shumar hoga is liye baligh hone ke baad agar wo bachcha mustati (us par hajj farz) ho jaye to use dobarah farz hajj wa 'umrah karna zaroori hoga (Rawahu Muslim:1336, Al-Majmoo':7/56). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Umrah Ki Farziyyat

Sawal: Kya sahibe istitaa'at par hajj ki tarah 'umrah bhi zindagi me ek martabah farz hai?

Jawab: Ji haan, 'umrah bhi zindagi me eik baar sahibe istitaa'at par farz hai (Fiqhul-Manhaji: 2/116). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Umrah Me Taakheer (Delay)

Sawal: Kya Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdik 'Umrah farz hai, agar koi hajj ki taqat na rakh sakta ho aur 'umrah ki taqat ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Qur'aan me Allah ne hajj aur 'umrah ko Allah ke liye poora karne ka hukm farmaya hai. 'Umrah chunke hajj hi ki tarah hai, lihaza istitaa'at hone ki soorat me dono zindagi me ek martaba farz ho jate hain, albattah donon ka ek hi sath ada karna zaroori nahi hai, neez farz hone ke baad ada karne ki niyyat ho to us ko taakheer (delay) se bhi kar sakte hain to jaisi gunjaish ho usi ke e'etibar se ada kare. (Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 1/53). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi ke paas itni raqam ho ke wo 'umrah kar sakta hai to ab kya use 'umrah kar lena chahiye ya hajj ke liye darkar mazeed raqam ke jama hone ki koshish wa intizar karna chahiye?

Jawab: Koi is niyyat se taakheer (delay) kare ke kuch aur raqam jama ho jaye to hajj aur 'umrah donon ek sath ada ho jayenge to jaaiz hai (Al-Gurarul-Bahiyyah: 2/255). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Kurta Shalwar Wagayrah Pahen Kar 'Umrah Aur Hajj Karna

Sawal: Kya bina Ihram ki chadar ke (Kurta Shalwar wagayrah pahen kar) 'Umrah aur Hajj hoga?

Jawab: Hajj to sahih ho jayega lekin agar bila 'uzr sile huye kapde pahne the to gunahgar bhi hoga aur dum dena bhi hoga (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:4/102). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hajj Aur 'Umrah Ke Liye 'Aurat Ka Safed Kapda Pahenna

Sawal: Kya Hajj aur 'Umrah ke liye 'aurat ko safed kapda pahenna afzal hai? Kyunke barre sageer me yeh bahut mash'hur hai.

Jawab: Ha, 'aurat ke liye mard ki tarah safed kapde pahenna sunnat hai ('Umdatul Mufti Wal-Mustafti). Mufti Ishaq Patel

'Aurat Ka Hijab Me Hajj Aur 'Umrah

Sawal: Kya 'aurat Hijab me hajj aur 'Umrah kar sakti hai?

Jawab: 'aurat Ihram ki soorat me hatheliyon aur chehre par is tarah kapda dale rakhe ke chehre ko mas (touch) na karta ho to koi harj nahi (Fathul Qareebul Mujeeb: 1/154, Rauzatut-taalibeen:3/127). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Aaj kal 'auratain umbrella cap (Topi) isti'emaal karti hain jis se chehra dhak jata hai. Agar kapda chehre ko mas (touch) kare to 'Umrah aur hajj ka mas-alah kya hai bayan farmayein doosri baat yeh hai ke Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) aur Aap (ﷺ) ki doosri azwaaje mutahharat ka is silsile me kya 'amal tha? Unhon ne Ihram ki halat me chehra chhupane ki koshish ki thi ya nahi.

Jawab: Kisi lakdi ya is tarah ke umbrella cap ke sahare chehra par kapda is tarah latkana ke chehre ko mas na ho jaye, yeh jaaiz hai kyunke Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ka chadar ke kinare ko chehre par latkana hadis se sabit hai (Abu Dawood: 1833) bila ikhtiyar yeh kapda chehra se mas (touch) ho aur fauran hataye to fidiyah nahi. Agar 'amadan (jaan boojh kar) maskare ya ittifaqan mas hone ke baad use na hataye to fidyah wajib hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 3/128). Mufti Ishaq Patel

'Iddat Wali 'Aurat Ka Hajj Wa 'Umrah Ka Safar

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat ka hajj wa 'Umrah ka safar shuru' karne se Pehle shohar ka intiqal ho jaye, jab ke uska ticket ban chuka ho to kya wo 'aurat hajj ya 'Umrah ke safar ke liye nikal sakti hai?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah me is 'aurat ke liye hajj wa 'Umrah ka safar karna jaaiz na hoga. Balke is ka shohar ke ghar par char (4) mah dus (10) din ki 'iddat puri karna zaroori hoga (Kanzur Ragibeen:482). Mufti Ishaq Patel

'Aurat Ka Daure Hazir Me Bagair Mahram Ke Hajj Ka Safar

Sawal: Daure hazir me flight ka safar hota hai jahan tour ke sath bahut sari doosri 'auratain bhi safar me sath hoti hain un ke sath kya koi 'aurat bagair mahram ke hajj ka safar kar sakti hai?

Jawab: Agar farz hajj ho to kar sakti hai warna nahi (As-Sirajul-Wahhaj:181). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Ihram Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya mard ke liye Ihram ki halat me naaf aur ghutnon ka chhupana zaroori hai?

Jawab: Ihram hi ka mas-alah nahi balke 'aam halaat me bhi namaz ki tarah satar chupana zaroori hai, lihazah agar Ihram ki chadar se naaf dhaki na ho isi tarah ghutne ke upar ka hissah dikhaee de raha ho to na namaz durust hogi aur na tawaaf, lihaza Ihram ki chadar bandhte waqt is ka khayal rakhna zaroori hai. (Al-Muhimmaat:4/313, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:2/174). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya haalate Ihram me danton par toothpaste se brush karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Ji haan, Haalate Ihram me dant saaf karne ke liye brush aur toothpaste ka iste'emaal karna jaaiz hai kyunke is me jo khushboo hoti hai wo mamnu'aa khushboo me se nahi hai (Fiqhul 'Ibadaat: 2/219). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Daurane Ihram agar sote waqt chadar sar par odh le to kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Daurane Ihram, nind ki halat me agar chadar bila qasd wa iraadah sir par odh le to muaf hai, jaan bujh kar nahi daal sakte (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:2/133). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya haalate Ihram me mard sile huye chappal pahen sakta hai?

Jawab: Haalate Ihram me mard ke liye aise chappal ya jute pehenna jaaiz nahi jis se takhne aur pairon ki ungliyan chupi hui ho lekin agar sile huye chappal ya jute aise hon ke unko pahenne ke baad takhne aur pairon ki ungliyan khuli rahe to un ko pahenne me koi haraj nahi (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:2/133). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haalate Ihram Me gusl Lena Aur Ihram Ki Chadarein Tabdeel Karna

Sawal: Kya paseene ki wajah se daurane hajj wa 'Umrah gusl lena aur Ihram ki chadrein tabdeel karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Ji haan, Ihram ki halat me gusl lena aur Ihram ki chadrein tabdeel karna jaaiz hai. (Kitabul Umm:3/528, Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/411). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haalate Ihram Me Khushboodar Chizon Aur Iodex Ka Iste'emaal

Sawal: Kya halite Ihram me badan aur kapdon par khushboo ka iste'emaal jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Ihram ki halat me badan aur kapdon par khushboo lagana jaaiz nahi, isi tarah khushboodar tel, khushboodar saban aur khushboodar surmah wagayrah ka iste'emaal bhi haraam hai (Kifayatun-Nabih Sharhut-Tambeeh:7/185). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ihram ki niyyat karne ke baad kya khane me khushboodar chizon ka iste'emaal kar sakte hai?

Jawab: Haalate Ihram me badan , kapde aur khanon me bhi aysi khushboo ka iste'emaal haraam hai jis se khushboo maqsood hoti hai jaise mushk, Oudh, Kafur, Sandal, Zafaran, aur itar wagayrah lekin jin ashya se khushboo maqsood na ho jaise khushboodar phal, seb, limu, darchini, laung, saunf, masalaha, mehendi wagayrah to in ka iste'emaal haraam nahi, isi tarah aisa khana ke jis me khushboodar chiz ke asarat ghul kar khatam ho chuke hon to un khanon ke iste'emaal me koi haraj nahi, misaal ke taur par zafran mila hua khana haraam hai, lekin zafran ka asar bilkul khatam ho gaya ho to ab us khane ka iste'emaal jaaiz hoga (Al-Wajeez Ma'al 'Aziz:3/464). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Daurane Hajj/'Umrah Ihram ki halat me agar paun dard kar raha ho to kya iodex laga sakte hain?

Jawab: Iodex lagane me koi haraj nahi hai kyunke is me jo khushboo hoti hai use khushboo ki garz se iste'emaal nahi kiya jata (Al-Majmoo':7/357). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Tawaaf Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya bagair paaki aur wuzoo ke tawaaf kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Tawaaf me taharat shart hai aur tawaaf me wuzoo ka hona bhi zaroori hai, bagair wuzoo ke tawaaf sahih nahi hoga (Al-Majmoo':8/15). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Ka'abah ki taraf rukh karke tawaaf karna durust hai?

Jawab: Tawaaf ke dauran ka'batullah ko apne bayein kandhe ki taraf rakhna zaroori hai, is kaifiyat ke sath agar koi daurane tawaaf chehrah kabatullah ki taraf kare to koi muzaiqah nahi (Rauzatut-taalibeen:388). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Hateem ke andar tawaaf karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Tawaaf Hateem ke andar se na kare balke Hateem ke bahar se kare is ki wajah yeh hai ke Hateem kabatullah hi ka hissah hai aur tawaaf kabatullah ke bahar se karna zaroori aur shart hai. Hateem ke andar se kiya hua chkkar ya tawaaf shumaar nahi hoga. (Al-Eezah: 355). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Tawaaf me Hajre Aswad ko bosah lena zaroori hai?

Jawab: Hajre Aswad ko bosah dena zaroori nahi, balke sunnat hai, agar logon ko takleef diye bagair sahulat se mumkin ho to bosah de phir us par sajdah kare, agar yeh mumkin na ho to is ko chhu kar hathon ko bosah de, agar yeh bhi mumkin na ho to door se hathon se ya kisi chiz se isharah kare aur takbeer padhe (Tuhfatul-Bari:1/388). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Tawaaf ya Sa'ee ke dauran agar jamat khadi ho jaye to kya kare?

Jawab: Tawaaf ya Sa'ee ke dauran agar farz namaz ki jamat shuru' ho jaye to tawaaf ya sa'ee ko rok kar farz namaz ba jamat ada kare phir tawaaf ya sa'ee ko mukammal kare albattah bagair 'uzr ke tawaaf ko qata'a karna makrooh hai. (Kifayatun Nabeeh:7/421, Rauzatut-taalibeen:3/76). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Daurane Tawaaf Agar Ta'daad Me Shak

Sawal: Daurane tawaaf agar taadad me shak ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Daurane tawaaf agar taadad me shak ho to kam wale 'adad ka tasawwur kare ya'ani teen ya char huye is ka shak ho to teen tasawwur karke baqi char chakkar ada kare (Al-Majmoo':8/21). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Tawaaf Ke Dauran Moze (Socks) Pehenna

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ka tawaaf ke dauran moze (socks) pehenna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Tawaaf me bhi namaz ki tarah satar chhupana zaroori hai aur pair bhi satar me se hain. Lihaza agar kapde is qadar taweel ho ke qadam chhup sakte hon to phir moze pehenne ki zaroorat nahi hai aur agar qadam na chhupte hon to 'auratein pairon me moze pehne aur isi tarah sir ke baalon me se koi baal na dikhayein. 'aurat ka bagair moze pehne hue tawaaf karna sahih nahi hoga kyunke asal pairon ka chhupana hai (An-Nihayah:3/278). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Daurane Tawaaf Agar Bila Ikhtiyar Do Ya Char Baal Dikhaee Dein

Sawal: Daurane Tawaaf agar bila ikhtiyar do (2) ya char (4) baal dikhaee dein to kya tawaaf sahih hoga kyun ke bheed ke dhakkon ki wajah se makni ya dopatta khisak jata hai aur chand baal nazar aate hain. Yeh bahut 'auraton ke sath hota hai is soorat me kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdik namaz ki tarah tawaaf me bhi satar chhupana shart hai. Lihaza agar koi jaan bujh kar satar kholta hai to us ka tawaaf sahih nahi hoga, lekin be khayali me agar satar khul jata hai aur fauran dhank diya jaye to tawaaf sahih hoga, lihaza ab yeh 'aurat wahin thahar kar apne sir ko dhankegi aur chakkar mukammal karegi, lekin agar isi halat me ya'ani sir ya satar khule huye hone ki halat me chakkar mukammal karegi to wo chakkar shumar nahi hoga, mazeed chakkar lagana padega ya'ani aath chakkar, lekin agar bheed ki wajah se mumkin na ho to bawajah majburi “Hanafi” maslak par 'amal kiya jaye to gunjaish nikali ja sakti hai, kyunke un ke nazdik tawaaf me satar shart nahi (Al-Mo'tamad:2/244). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Tawaaf Aur Sa'ee Ke Dauran Baat Aur Video Call Karna

Sawal: Kya daurane tawaaf aur Sa'ee baat karna jaaiz hai? Kyunke kabhi kabhi family ke sath tawaaf ke ta'alluq se baat karni hoti hai, jaisa ke kitne chakkar huye, rukne yamani par du'aa ki yaad dahani, Hajre aswad aane par hath se isharah kar ke bosah lena ya kisi bhai ko kisi galat chiz par rahnumai karna wagayrah.

Jawab: Tirmizi ki riwayat me hai ke baitullah ke ird gird tawaaf karna namaz ki tarah hai magar yeh ke us me tum baat chit karte hon to jo is me baat kare to khair hi ki baat kare (Sunan At-Tirmizi:96). Imam Tirmizi (Rh) farmate hain ke ahle 'ilm Hazrat is baat ko pasand karte hain ke aadmi tawaaf me bagair zaroorat ke baat chit na kare balke Allah ka zikar kare ya ilmi baat kare (Kitabul eezah fi manasikil Hajj:143). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kaseer ta'adaad me log tawaaf aur sa'ee karte waqt video call kar ke apne rishtedaron ya doston se baat karte hain aur unhe wahan ka nazaara dikhate hain kya is tarah tawaaf wa sa'ee karna sahih hoga?

Jawab: Daurane Tawaaf aur sa'ee agar koi video call kare to tawaaf aur sa'ee fee-nafsihi sahih honge albattah duniyavi kamon me lagne ki wajah se sawab me kami hogi. Hadis me hain ke tawaaf namaz ki tarah hai, jis tarah namaz me khushu' wa khuzu', dhayan aur adab zaroori hai usi tarah tawaaf me bhi is ka khayal rakhna zaroori hai is liye fuzul chizon me na lage (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 4/145). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Daurane Tawaaf Na Mahram Ke Jism Ko Mas/Touch Hona

Sawal: Kya Haram shareef me daurane tawaaf na mahram ke jism ko mas (touch) hone se wuzoo tutega?

Jawab: Maujuda daur me a'amaale hajj wa 'Umrah ki adayigi ke mauqeh par izdiham (bheed) ki wajah se ajnabiyah 'aurat ko chhoone se bachna dushwar ho chuka hai, lihaza agar bagair qasd wa shahwat ke chhooye to wuzoo nahi tutega (Shafi'ee Fiqhi Seminar 2019). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Kisi Dusare Ki Taraf Se Tawaaf Karna

Sawal: Kya dusare ki taraf se tawaaf karna sahih hai?

Jawab: Doosre ki taraf se tawaaf karna sahih nahi hai. Albattah apne tawaaf ke dauran us ke liye du'aa kar sakte hain (Al-Fatawa al-Kubra: 2/106). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Maqame Ibrahim Ko Chhoona Aur Bosah Dena

Sawal: Maqame Ibrahim ko chhoona aur use bosah dena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Imam Nawawi (Rh) ne apni kitaab manasik me likkha hai ke maqame Ibrahim ko chhoona aur use bosah dena bid'at hai (Al-Eezah fi manasikil Hajj:1/392). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Daurane 'Umrah/Tawaaf 'Aurat Ki Mahwari Shuru' Hona

Sawal: Daurane 'Umrah agar 'aurat ki mahwari shuru' ho to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar daurane tawaaf mahwari shuru' ho to napaaki ki wajah se tawaaf sahih nahi hoga kyunke tawaaf me taharat shart hai. Ab yeh 'aurat mahfwari ke khatam hone ka intizar karegi phir az-sare nau tawaaf wagayrah karke 'Umrah mukammal karegi, tab tak Ihram ki pabandiyon me rahengi. Agar tawaaf se farig hone ke baad ayyame haiz shuru' ho rahe hon to 'aurat sa'ee, qasar (baal katna) wagayrah baqiyyah arakaane 'Umrah ada kar sakti hai, kyunke un ke liye taharat shart nahi (Al-Majmoo':8/257, Sharhe Muslim:8/134). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tawaaf Ke Baad Sunnat

Sawal: Tawaaf ke baad ki sunnat kab aur kahan padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Tawaaf ke baad maqame Ibrahim ke peeche do raka'at padhna sunnat hai, agar wahan mumkin na ho to Hateem me warna kisi bhi jagah haram me ada kare. Aur is namaz ke liye koi waqat muta-'ayyan nahi hai (I'aanatut-Taalibeen:2/340). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zam Zam Ka Paani Pine Ka Tariqa

Sawal: Zam Zam ka paani khade ho kar pina masnoon hai ya baith kar?

Jawab: Hadise Sahih me Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne khade ho kar paani pine se mana'a farmaya hai (Muslim wagayrah) lihaza maaye zam zam bhi 'ulamaye Shawafe' ke nazdik baith kar hi pina masnoon hai. Jahan tak us riwayat ka ta'alluq hai jis me maaye zam zam khade ho kar pine ka zikar hai 'Ulama-E-Kiraam ne us ki doosri taujihaat zikar farmayi hain (Hashiyatul-Jamal:2/482). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar koi zam zam ka paani haram shareef me zam zam ke kunwein ke 'alaawah kisi aur jagah piye to use baith kar pina hai ya khade rah kar?

Jawab: Zam Zam mutlaqan baith kar Bismillah padh kar aur qiblah rukh ho kr pina sunnat hai albattah khade ho kar pina jaaiz hai (Hashiyatul-Jamal:2/482). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Haalate Haiz Me Zam Zam Ke Paani Ko Haath Lagana Aur Pina

Sawal: Kya haiz ki halat me zam zam ke paani ko haath lagana aur pina sahih hai?

Jawab: Koi haraj nahi, kyunke shari-'ate mutahraharah me kahin bhi yeh baat manqool nahi hai ke zam zam ko chhoone aur pine ke liye taharat shart aur farz hai, lihaza is halat me zam zam ko chhoo bhi sakte hai aur pee bhi sakte hain. Yahan tak ke sunnat wa farz wuzoo wa gusl ki taharat me zam zam ke paani ko iste'emaal bhi kar sakte hain. Makrooh nahi. Lekin maye zam zam se istinjaa karne ko fuqhaye Kiraam ne mana'a kiya hai aur makrooh kaha hain (Al-Majmoo': 1/90, Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah: 1/47). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Safa Marwah Ki Sa'ee Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya daurane Sa'ee paaki aur wuzoo zaroori hai?

Jawab: Paaki aur wuzoo ki halat me sa'ee karna masnoon hai. Agar bagair wuzoo ke ya haiz wa janabat ya najasat lagi hoi halat me sa'ee kare tab bhi sa'ee sahih hogi (Al-'Aziz: 3/408). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya tawaaf ki tarah sa'ee me bhi iztiba'a kare?

Jawab: Sa'ee me bhi tawaaf ki tarah iztiba'a karna masnoon hai ya'ani dayan / sidha kandha khula rakhe (Al-Manhaj: 1/87). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Safa marwah ke darmiyan hari light hai wahan Hajirah (A) daudi thi phir 'auratein abhi kyun nahi daudtein?

Jawab: Baaz chizein tauqifi hoti hain ya'ani shari-'at ka aisa

hukm hai is liye hame manna hai jis me aqal ka kuch dakhal nahi agarcha wo aqal ke khilaf nahi hoti lekin hamari samajh se door hoti hai albattah 'auraton ko daudne ki mumaana'at ki ek zahiri wajah yeh hai ke shari-'at ne 'auraton ko mardon ki mushabahat ikhtiyar karne se mana'a' farmaya hai. Daudna aur taiz chalna yeh 'aurat ke haya ke khilaf hai aur chunke Hajira (As) us waqt tanha thi ab jab ke ajnabi mard bhi rehte hain is liye satar ke khulne aur zahir hone ka andeshah hai (Al-Eezah: 260). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Hajj Ya 'Umrah Karne Wala Daurane Namaz Iztiba'a Na Kare

Sawal: Daurane 'Umrah agar koi farz ya sunnat namaz ada kare to kya kandha khula rakhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Har wo tawaaf jis ke baad sa'ee karna ho to tawaaf karne wale ke liye tawaaf aur sa'ee me iztiba'a ya'ani chadar ka darmiyani hissah dahine kandhe ke niche rakhna aur chadar ke donon sire bayein kandhe par dalna is tarah ke dahina kandha khula rahe yeh masnoon hai, lekin agar is daurane namaz ka waqt ho jaye to apne donon kandhe kapde se dhank de, yahi sunnat hai, namaz se farig ho kar phir iztiba'a ki kaifiyat ikhtiyar kare (Al-Majmoo': 8/20). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Halaq Ya Taqseer Ke Masa-il

Sawal: 'Umrah aur Hajj me Halaq ya Taqseer ki kya ahmiyat hai?

Jawab: Halaq ya Taqseer Hajj wa 'Umrah ke arkaan me se hai, jis ke bagair hajj wa 'Umrah sahih nahi hota aur dum wagayrah se is ki talafi bhi nahi ki ja sakti. Neez Halaq ya Qasar donon bhi jaaiz hain. Lekin mardon ke liye halaq ya'ani ustare se baal saaf karna afzal hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:2/187). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'Aurat ke liye Halaq ya taqseer ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: 'Aurat ke liye halaq karna jaaiz nahi us ke liye sirf taqseer zaroori hai aur kam se kam teen baal paure (fingertips) ke baqadar katna farz hai jo hajj ke arkaan me se hai. (AlMajmoo': 8/204). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Halaq karate waqt khushboodar chizon ko iste'emaal kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Halaq karate waqt khushboodar saban ya khushboodar paani iste'emaal karna jaaiz nahi (Al-Bayan: 4/158). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya Halaq ya Taqseer doosron se hi karana zaroori hai?

Jawab: Sir ke baal khud ke liye maundna ya chote karna bhi jaaiz hai, doosron se karana zaroori nahi (Asnaul Mataleeb:1/504). Mufti Ishaq Patel

'Aurat Ka Baar Baar 'Umrah Karne Par Taqseer Ka Hukm

Sawal: 'aurat baar baar 'Umrah kare (Hajj ke safar me teen ya char baar ya ziyadah bhi) to kya har baar paure (fingertips) ke baqadar baal katna zaroori hai kyun ke is tarah se baal katne se baal chote honge khas kar jin 'auraton ke baal chote hote hain.

Jawab: Taqseer me kam se kam teen balon ka katna zaroori (farz) hai, pure baalon ko ek paure (fingertips) ke barabar katna sunnat hai lekin agar 'aurat sir ke kisi bhi hisse me se teen baalon ko ek paure ke barabar kate to bhi fee-nafsihi us ke baal katne ka farizah ada ho jata hai. Is liye 'aurat baar baar 'Umrah karne ka iraadah rakhe to har dafa'a kam se kam teen baal paure ke baqadar kate, har baar mukhtalif balon me se kate (Al-Eezah: 286290). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sir Par Baal Na Hon To Halaq Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Agar kisi ke sir par baal hi na ho to halaq ka kya mas-alah hoga?

Jawab: Agar sir me baal na hon to ustarah ghumana sunnat hai. Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) farmate hain ke aysi soorat me moonch ya dadhi ke chand baal katarna mujhe pasand hai take Allah ke liye balon ka katna paya jaye (Al-Eezah:388). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Agar koi 'Umrah karne ke baad ek ya do din ya kuch dinon ke baad 'Umrah kare to kya use halaq karana zaroori hoga ya nahi?

Jawab: Agar sir par Pehle se baal na uge hon ya Pehle se sir par baal hon aur 'Umrah me halaq kar chuka ho aur abhi baal nazar na aa rahe hon to ustarah sir par ghumana mustahab hai, ya'ani agar koi ustara na ghumaye to koi harj nahi. Matlab yeh ke dum dene ki zaroorat nahi. Lekin agar baal nazar aa rahe hon to phir ustarah ghumana farz hoga (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/382-383). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qiyame Makkah Ke A'amaal

Sawal: Qiyame Makkah me konsa 'amal mustahab hai?

Jawab: Makkah ke qiyam me mustahab hai ke ziyadah se ziyadah 'Umrah kare, us ke baad yeh ke ba-kasrat tawaaf kare. (Al-Majmoo':8/197) Masjide Haraam me farz namazen, sunan wa nawafil ka ehtimaam kare, neez sadqah wa khairaat jaise nek kamon me mashgul rahe (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 3/556). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya makkah mukarramah me makrooh auqaat me bhi namaz padh sakte hain?

Jawab: Makkah Mukarramah aur Haram me kisi bhi waqt koi bhi namaz (Tawaaf ki namaz ho ya koi aur namaz ho) makrooh nahi (Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/194). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bayak Waqt (Ek Saath) Kayi Janaze

Sawal: Haram me taqriban har namaz ke baad ek sath bahut janaze hote hain aise waqt me namaz padhte waqt hame kya aur kaise du'aa padhni chahiye, tariqa batayein.

Jawab: Agar ek mard ho to du'aa me (Allahummag firlahu - اَللّھُمَّ اغْفِر لَہ) aur agar ek 'aurat ho to (Allahummag firlaha - اَللّھُمَّ اغْفِر لَھَا) padhe aur agar do mard ya do 'auratein hon ya ek mard ya ek 'aurat ho to (Allahummag firlahuma - اَللّھُمَّ اغْفِر لَھُما) padhe aur agar do se ziyadah hon to (Allahummag firlahum - اَللّھُمَّ اغْفِر لَھُمْ) padhe. Matlab yeh hai ke (Lahuma - لَھُمَا) un donon ke liye. (Lahum - لَھُم) un sub ke liye (Al-'Aziz be Sharhil-Wajeez Al ma'roof bi sharhil kabeer:2/438). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imame Haram Ki Iqtidaa' Me Rasteh Me Namaz

Sawal: Kuch log apne hotel ki rehaishgah ke niche ya raste me imame haram ke peeche namaz padhte hain kyunke haram me sab logon ko jagah nahi hoti aur azaan se Pehle hi andar jane ke taqriban darwaze band kiye jate hain. Kya is tarah hotel ke niche ya raste par namaz ada karne se unhe haram ki namaz ka ya'ani 1 lakh namazon ka sawab milega?

Jawab: Agar Imam masjid me ya us se bahar ho aur muqtadi raste me ya building ke niche hon to us imam ki Iqtidaa sahih hone ke liye do shart hain:

Pehli Shart: Masjid ke aakhri kinare ya masjid ke bahar raste me banaye gaye har har do saf ke darmiyan ka faslah 300 zira' (145 Mtrs/450 Fts.) se zayid na ho ya'ani haram se bahar jo aakhri saf bani hai, us se le kar haram ke raste me jo doosri saf bani hai, un donon ke darmiyan ka faslah 450 feets se ziyadah na ho phir doosri aur teesri saf me bhi faslah isi tarah 450 feets se zayid na ho to us ke darmiyan kahan bhi namaz padhe to Iqtidaa durust hogi.

Dusari Shart: Imam aur muqtadi ke darmiyan aisa hayil na ho jo guzarne aur dekhne se roke, agar koi building hi ke kisi manzile par namaz shuru' kare to yeh kafi nahi is ke liye zaroori hai ke aysi jagah ikhtiyar kare jahan se agar imam tak chal kar jana chahe to ba'aasani jasake albattah building ke nichle hisseh (Ground floor) me haraj nahi, albattah ground floor charon taraf se mukammal band ho to namaz ke waqt masjid ki taraf ka hissah ek aadmi ke guzarne ke baqadar khula rakhna zaroori hai, warna Iqtidaa durust nahi hogi (Tuhfatul Muhtaaj: 2/315). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Maqame Tan'eem (Masjid-E-'Aayesha) Se 'Umrah

Sawal: Jab hum 'Umrah ko jate hain to 'Umrah ke baad kuch log wapas 'Umrah karne ka irade se masjide 'Aayesha (Meeqaat) jakar wahan se Ihram bandh kar niyyat karte hain aur phir 'Umrah ke baqi arkaan mukammal karte hain, ek shakhs ne mujh se kaha ke masjide 'Aayesha wahan ke rehaishi logon ke liye meeqaat hai to hame yeh baat ma'aloom karna hai ke 'Umrah ke liye asal meeqaat kahan se muqarrar hai? Jahan se hum umarah karne ke liye Ihram bandh kar sahih se 'Umrah ke faraiz ko anjaam de sakein?

Jawab: 'Umrah Masjide 'Aayesha se karna durust aur jaaiz hai, asal mas-alah yeh hai ke jo koi shakhs makkah ya hudoode haram ke andar ho to us par lazim hai ke wo 'Umrah ka Ihram bandhne ke liye Hill (Hudoode Haram se bahar) me jaye aur wahan se Ihram bandh kar aaye yahi Aimmah Arabah ka mazhab hai (Mustafad az: Al-fiqh alal mazahibil arba'ah liljazayiri: 1/618).

Aur Tan'eem bhi Hill hai jahan se Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ne Huzoor (ﷺ) ke hukm se 'Umrah ka Ihram bandha tha. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Ayyame Haiz Me Safar Aur 8 Zilhijjah Se Pehle Paaki Hasil Na Hona

Sawal: Agar 'aurat haiz ke ayyam me hajje Tamattu' ki niyyat se safar shuru' kare lekin 8 zilhijjah se Pehle paaki hasil na ho aur 'Umrah na kar paye to ab wo 'aurat kya kare?

Jawab: Agar 'aurat haiz ke ayyam me hajje Tamattu' ki niyyat se safar shuru' kare lekin 8 zilhijjah se Pehle paaki hasil na ho to 'aurat tab tak Ihram ki pabandiyon me rahegi aur phir mina rawana hone se Pehle apni niyyat ko tabdil kare aur Tamattu' ki bajaye hajje qiraan ki niyyat kare. 8 zilhijjah (Mina) se le kar ayyame tashreeq tak tamam arkaan aur wajibaat ko ada kare aur phir paaki hasil karne ke baad tawaaf ma'as sa'ee karke apne hajj ko mukammal kare aur us me 'Umrah khud bakhud hoga 'Umrah ki sirf niyyat hogi baqi hajje qiraan me 'Umrah ka koi 'amal nahi kiya jayega is soorat me us 'aurat par dum lazim hoga. (Majmoo' Fatawa wa Rasail Ibn 'Usaymin:24/144). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Daurane Hajj Khawind Ka Intiqal Ho Jaye To Hajj Aur 'iddat Ka Hukm

Sawal: Daurane hajj agar kisi ke shohar ka intiqal ho jaye to us 'aurat ke liye kya ahkaam honge? Kya wo 'aurat hajj mukammal karegi ya fauri taur par ghar wapas aa jayegi? Neez us ki 'iddat kis waqt se shumar hogi?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah me agar us 'aurat ne apna safare hajj shohar ki ijazat se shuru' kiya ho to us 'aurat ko ikhtiyar hoga, chahe hajj mukammal kare ya fauri taur par apne ghar wapas aaye. Albattah agar wo apna hajj mukammal karti hai to uski 'iddat shohar ke intiqal ke roz se hi shumar hogi. (Kanzur Ragibeen: 482, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:5/180). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Hajj Ke Ihram Ki Qismein

Sawal: Hajj (Ihram) ki kitni qismein hain?

Jawab: Hajj ki teen (3) qismein hain.

Pehle sirf 'Umrah ka Ihram bandhe aur 'Umrah ke kisi 'amal ko ada karne se qabal us par hajj ko bhi dakhil kare (Ya'ani hajj ki bhi niyyat shaamil kare) to yeh bhi sahih hai, use bhi Qiraan kahenge lekin Pehle sirf hajj ka Ihram bandhe aur baad me us par 'Umrah dakhil karna chahe to is ki ijazat na hogi, ab pehle sirf hajj mukammal karna hoga (Hilyat-ul-'Ulama:3/254). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdik kis tariqe se hajj karna afzal hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdik Ifraad ke tariqe se hajj ziyadah afzal hai lekin aaj kal jald jane wale hazrat ko hajj se Pehle Madinah ho aana hota hai is liye is par 'amal karna mushkil hota hai aur 'umuman Tamattu' par 'amal karte hain. Hajje Ifraad me agar Madeenahh ka safar na bhi ho to hajj hone tak Ihram ki pabandi lazim hogi in pabandion ke sath rehne ki himmat hoto hajje Ifraad karna afzal hai warna phir Tamattu' par 'amal kare albattah jo log ain hajj ke waqt makkah pohanch rahe hon un ko is par 'amal karna aasan aur behtar hai (Kifayatun-Nabih Sharhut-Tambeeh:7/86). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mina Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Aaj kal tours wale 7 zilhijjah ki sham ko hi minah le kar jate hain kyunke bheed/traffic na mile aur safar aasan ho, kya 8 se Pehle mina jana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: 8 Zilhijjah ko hi mina ke liye rawanah hona sunnat hai albattah agar koi kisi wajah se 7 ki sham me mina rawanah ho jaye to jaaiz hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji 'Ala mazhabil Imaamish Shafi'ee: 2/184). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya Mina mein 8 zilhijjah ki zohar se 9 zilhijjah ki fajar tak 5 namazain ada karna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Mina mein 8 zilhijjah ki zohar se 9 zilhijjah ki fajar tak 5 namazain ada karna sunnat hai, wajib aur zaroori nahi (Rauzatut-taalibeen:395). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Mina, 'arafah aur Muzdalifah mein bheed ki wajah se wuzoo ke liye ek nal se bayak waqt kayi log wuzoo karte hain, kya is tarah karna sunnat hai?

Jawab: Nal se aane wala paani maye mutlaq hai, jis se farz wuzoo wa gusl wagayrah durust hai (Minhaajut-Talibeen:1/9). Mufti Ishaq Patel

'Arafah Ke Masa-il

Sawal: 'Arafaat ke din du'aa mangne ki fazilat pure din ki hai ya sirf asar se Magrib tak hai?

Jawab: 'Arafah ke din du'aa mangne ki fazilat pure din ki hai, chunanche Huzoor (ﷺ) ka irshade Mubarak hai ke “Afzalud du'aa du'aau yaumil 'arafah” (As-Sunanul Kubra lil bayhaqi: 8391). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya tamam hajion ke liye 'arafah me qasar aur jama ya'ani (zohar aur asar ko jama'a kar ke) padhna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Haji agar shara'ee musafir (jise Hindustan ya Madinah wagayrah se makkah aaye huye 4 din mukammal na hoye hon) to qasar wa jama'a kar sakta hai ya'ani zohar asar aur Magrib 'Isha ko jama'a kare lekin shara'ee musafir na ho to donon namazon ko apne apne waqt par mukammal ada kare (Al-Majmoo':8/88). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Huzoor (ﷺ) ne 'arafah ke din zohar aur asar ko jama (qasar) kiya yeh hajj ki wajah se makhsoos hai ya safar ki wajah se? baqi sahabah ka is mu'aamle me kya 'amal tha?

Jawab: Huzoor (ﷺ) ne jo qasar kiya tha wo safar hi ki wajah se kiya tha na ke nusuk (Hajj) ki wajah se, lihaza jo shara'ee musafir hai usi ke liye sari rukhsatein rahengi ya'ani wo qasar aur jama'a kar sakta hai aur jo shara'ee musafir na ho use mukammal namaz waqt me ada karni hai. Baqi Sahaba-e-Kiraam Rizwanullahi 'Alaihim Ajma'een ke liye is silsile me Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ka hukm tha ke ay makkah ke bashindon tum apni namazon ko mukammal (bagair qasar ke) ada karo is liye ke hum to musafir qaum hain (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:2/360). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Agar kisi (Muqeem shakhs) ko 'arafah me Magrib ki namaz padhne ka waqt na mile kyunke tours wale guroob se Pehle hi bus me bithate hain take muzdalifah rawangi aasan ho. Is soorat me kya karna hoga?

Jawab: Agar shara'ee musafir na ho to 'arafah hi me Magrib padh kar nikle, albattah aaj kal bus aur safar ki pareshani ke e'etibaar se 'Arafaat me Magrib padhna dushwar ho to aisa shakhs bhi jama'a taakheer (delay) kar sakta hai ya'ani us ke liye gunjaish hai ke wo Muzdalifah mein pohanch kar Magrib aur 'Isha jama'a kare (Shafi'ee Fiqhi Seminar 2019). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Notes:

Haji Ka 'Arafat Me Intiqal Hona

Sawal: Agar haji ki 'Arafat me maut ho jaye to kya us ka hajj mukammal hoga? Ya koi doosra haji mayyit ke baqiyyah a'amaal anjam de sakta hai? Ya baad me is ki taraf se hajj badal karna hoga?

Jawab: Agar haji ki 'Arafat me wuqoof ke baad ya deegar hajj ke a'amaal (jinki dum wa kaffarah ke zariye talafi mumkin nahi) mukammal karne se Pehle maut waqe' ho jaye to yeh hajj us ki taraf se namukammal shumar kiya jayega, ya'ani baqiyyah aamal ko us ki taraf se koi naa'ib (Representative) ban kar ada nahi kar sakta, balke agar mayyit par hajj farz ho gaya tha aur us ke tarake (chodi hui raqam) wa miraas me itna maal maujood hai ke uski taraf se hajj kiya ja sakta hai, to mayyit ki taraf se hajje badal karna zaroori hoga. Mayyit ne jo bazate khud a'amaal anjam diye the us ka sawab batil na hoga wo use milega (AlMajmoo': 2/394). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Muzdalifah Mein Raat Guzarna

Sawal: Agar koi muzdalifah ki raat me na ruka to kya karna hoga?

Jawab: Muzdalifah mein aadhi raat ke baad kuch der thaharna wajib hai jis ka waqt subha sadiq tak rehta hai, agar koi muzdalifah ki raat me na ruka to use dum dena lazim hoga (Al-Eezah: 345). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Rami Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Rami ke liye kankariyan kahan se jama'a karna chahiye?

Jawab: Rami ke liye kankariyan kahin se bhi jama'a karna jaaiz hai lekin muzdalifah se lena mustahab hai. Albattah napaak jagah se aur jamarat par mari hui kankariyan lena makrooh hai (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/266). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ayyame Tashreeq (11, 12, 13 zilhijjah) me Rami ka waqt kab shuru' hota hai aur kab tak rahta hai?

Jawab: Ayyame Tashreeq me Rami / kankari marne ka waqt zawal se shuru' hota hai aur 13 zilhijjah ki guroob tak rehta hai albattah har din ki rami guroob se pehle karna behtar hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/106). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Rami karne wale ko rami ke waqt sutoon ka qasd (intention) karna chahiye ya sutoon ke ird gird gol dayereh (circle) ka, agar sutoon ka qasd karke rami kare to kya rami ada ho jayegi?

Jawab: Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ke zamane me “sutoon” nahi tha balke Aap (ﷺ) ke hukm se nishani ke taur par kuch patthar jama'a karke rakh diye gaye the. Jab haramain shareefain ki tausee' (expansion)hui to in pattharon ki jagah par sutoon banaye gaye. Lihaza rami karne ki asal jagah sutoon ke ird gird charon taraf se teen teen zira' ka gol dayirah (circle) ya'ani kunwen ki shakal wali jagah hai, lihazah isi kunwen me kankarion ka girna zaroori hai, agar koi sutoon ki niyyat se kankari mare to rami ada nahi hogi, lekin chunke 'awamun naas is qadar bareek-bini (attention to detail) se 'amal nahi karte, is liye in se darguzar kiya jayega, Shaikh Jamaal Ar-Ramili ne bhi isi ko mo'tamad kaha hai aur iski ijazat di hai ya'ani agar koi sutoon ka qasd karke bhi rami ka 'amal kare to iski yeh rami kafi samjhi jayegi is shart ke sath ke kankari gol dayirah me hi gire, jawaaz ki wajah yeh batate hain ke awamun naas jab rami karte hain to un ka maqsad wajib ki adayigi aur rami ki jagah me kankariyan phenkna hota hai na ke sutoon ko marna. (Tohtaful Muhtaaj: 4/135, Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah:1/649, I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 2/347). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Rami ke waqt agar koi kankari dayirah (circle) me na gire ya sutoon ko lag kar bahar gire to kya kare?

Jawab: Rami ki asal jagah gol dayirah (circle) ya'ani kunwen ki shakal wali jagah hai, isi kunwen me kankar girna zaroori hai, agar koi kankar dayireh me na gire to us kankari ko dobarah mare (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/277). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya raat me jamarat ki rami karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar koi din me bheed ki wajah se kankariyan na maar sake to raat me bhi maar sakta hai neez 'auraton aur budhon ke liye yahi waqt munasib hai (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/377). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya ayyame tashreeq me kisi aur ki janib se rami ada kar sakte hai?

Jawab: Aisa budha ya bimar jo jamarat tak 13 zilhijjah ki guroob tak jane ki qudrat na rakhta ho to wo doosre ko naa'ib (Representative) bana sakta hai, naa'ib ko chahiye ke Pehle wo apni teenon jamaron ki kankariyan mukammal kare phir jis ki taraf se naa'ib (Representative) bana hai us ki janib se kankari mare, albattah aaj ke halaat me use apni teenon jamaron ki kankariyan mukammal karne ke fauran baad lautna dushwar ho to apne sath sath bhi doosre ki taraf se kankari maar sakta hai (Kifayatul-Akhyaar:1/218). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Ihram Kholna / Tahallul

Sawal: Hajj aur 'Umrah me Ihram ka hukm kab khatam hota hai?

Jawab: Hajj ke Ihram kholne ko Tahallul kehte hain. Hajj me 2 Tahallul hain. Tahallul ka ta'alluq Yaumun Nahar ke 3 Aa'amaal (Rami, Halaq aur Tawaaf ma'a Sa'ee) se hai, in 3 aa'amaal me se kisi bhi do ke ada karne se Tahallule awwal hasil hoga jis ke baad biwi se jinsi ta'alluqaat aur nikah ke 'alaawah sab chizain halal ho jati hain. Mazkurah 3 aa'amaal me se baqiyah aa'amaal ke ada karne se tahallul saani bhi hasil hota hai jis ke baad sab chizain halal ho jati hain. 'Umrah me sirf ek hi tahallul hai, tawaaf, sa'ee aur Halaq ya Taqseer karne (Baal chote karane) ke baad Ihram khol sakte hain (Al-Eezah: 395-396). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya Hadi ka janwar zabah karne se Pehle hajj ka Ihram khol sakte hain?

Jawab: Hazrat Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdik hadi ka afzal waqt halaq ya qasar se Pehle hai, lekin agar koi Pehle halaq, tawaaf aur sa'ee karke halal ho jaye phir hadi ka janwar zabah kare tab bhi jaaiz hai (Al-Majmoo': 8/380). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Daurane Ihram agar koi hadisa ya rukaawat pesh aaye ya bimar ho jaye ya koi 'uzr ho jaye to Ihram kholne ki kya soorat hogi?

Jawab: 1. Agar hajj ya 'Umrah ki niyyat karte waqt is tarah shart lagaee thi ke “Agar daurane 'Umrah ya hajj me koi rukaawat pesh aayegi ya bimar ho jaunga ya 'aurat kahe ke mujhe haiz ke ayyam shuru' ho jayein to main Ihram khol dungi ya Ihram khol dunga aur qurbani ka tazkirah na kiya ho to is shart ki bina par jaise hi rukaawat pesh aayegi sirf halal hone ki niyyat se Ihram kholna jaaiz ho jayega. Lekin agar qurbani karne ka bhi tazkirah niyyat me kiya tha to sahoolat maujood ho to usi jagah qurbani kare aur yeh lazim hai, ya haram shareef rawana karne ki sahoolat ho to janwar ko haram shareef rawanah kar de.

2. Aur agar niyyat karte waqt halal hone ki shart nahi lagaee thi is soorat me agar 'Umrah ka Ihram ho to 'uzr ke khatam hone ka intizar kare phir 'Umrah ada kare, agar 'Umrah karna mayassar na ho to usi jagah bakri ya bade janwar ka satwan hissah zabah kare aur halal hone ki niyyat se halaq ya taqseer kare. Agar hajj ka Ihram ho to 'uzr ke khatam hone ka intizar kare aur waqt ho to hajj ada kare warnah 'Umrah ke arkaan ada karke bakri zabah kare warnah oont ya gaay ka satwan hissah zabah kare aur is 'alaqe ke fuqara par taqseem kare, phir halaq ya taqseer kare, yeh donon kam tahallul ki niyyat se kare, agar janwar mayassar na ho to janwar ki qimat ke baqadar anaaj ko muddon (traditional unit of measurement for dry goods like grain) me taqseem kare, phir har mud ya'ani 600 gram anaaj ke badle jahan chahe roza rakhe ya'ani masalan bees mud anaaj banta ho to bees roze rakhe aur roza rakhne se Pehle halal hone ki niyyat kare (Al-Majmoo': 8/393). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dum, Hadi, Qurbani Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Daurane Hajj fidyah kab wajib hota hai?

Jawab: Hajj ya 'Umrah me agar koi wajib 'amal chhot jaye ya haraam ka irtikaab ho jaye to is ki talafi ke liye fidyah wajib hota hai isi tarah hajje Tamattu' ya hajje qiraan me dum wajib hota hai (Kifayatul Muhtaaj ilad dimail wajibati Alal Mu'tamiri wal Hajj: 105, 169, 249, 271). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Hadi aur Qurbaani me kya farq hai?

Jawab: Hajj ke ayyam me hajje Tamattu' ya hajje Qiraan karne wala haji jo janwar zabah karta hai use hadi kehte hain aur gair haji zilhijjah ki 10 tarikh aur ayyame tashreeque me Allah ka taqarrub hasil karne ke liye jab koi janwar zabah karta hai to use qurbaani kehte hain. (Al-bayan: 4/439).

Hadi ya'ani chaupayon me se qurbani ka wo janwar jo baitullah ki taraf Allah ki raza hasil karne ke liye hanka jaye ya Tamattu' aur qiraan aur ihsaar ki wajah se wajib ho jaye. (Al-Mu'tamad: 2/461).

Hadi ki do qismein hain:

Wajib Hadi: Tamattu' aur qiraan karne wale par hadi wajib hoti hai aur wo ek bakari warnah oont ya gaay ka satwan hissah hota hai. (Kifayatul Muhtaaj: 118).

Nafali Hadi: aur wo yeh hai ke aisa shakhs jo hajje Ifraad ya 'Umrah karne wala ho aur sawab hasil karne ki niyyat se janwar zabah kare ya hajje Tamattu' ya hajje qiraan karne wala wajib hadi par zayid janwar zabah kare use nifli hadi kehte hain (Al-Mu'tamad:2/463). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Hajje Qiraan me dum wajib kyun hota hai?

Jawab: Hajje Qiraan me hajj ke a'amaal me hi 'Umrah ke aamal ko shaamil kiya jata hai is liye do 'ibadaton ko milane ki wajah se dum wajib hota hai (Al-'Aziz be Sharhil-Wajeez:3/346). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Hajje Tamattu' me dum wajib kyun hota hai?

Jawab: Hajje Tamattu' me Pehle 'Umrah ki niyyat se Ihram bandha jata hai. 'Umrah mukammal karne ke baad Ihram khol diya jata hai phir hudoode haram se hi 8 zilhijjah ko hajj ka Ihram bandha jata hai (Al-bayan: 4/439).

Is liye meeqaat se Ihram na bandhne ki wajah se dum wajib hota hai (Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah:1/654). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Hajje Tamattu' aur Qiraan ki wajah se jo dum wajib hota hai, use kab aur kahan zabah karna hai? Kya is ka gosht kha sakte hain?

Jawab: Tamattu' wa Qiraan ki wajah se wajib dum ko 'Eid ke din zabah karna afzal hai, 'Umrah ke baad aur hajj se qabal zabah karna bhi jaaiz hai lekin 'Umrah se farig hone se qabal zabah nahi kar sakte, neez is dum ko hudoode haram me zabah karna wajib / zaroori hai aur hajje Tamattu' wa Qiraan ke dum ke gosht ko poora sadqah karna zaroori hai, na hum khud kha sakte hain aur na hi ameer ko khila sakte hain (At-tambeeh: 1/70, Rauzatut-taalibeen: 3/221). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya haji apni qurbani hudoode haram ke bahar (misal ke taur par Jiddah mein) kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Hajje Tamattu' ya hajje qiraan ki wajah se jo dum wajib hota hai use hudoode haram me hi dena wajib hota hai. Qurbani jo sunnat hai wo apne 'alaqe me ya Jiddah me ya jahan par karna chahe kar sakta hai, is ka hajj se koi ta'alluq nahi hai (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/287). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Tawaaf-e-Ziyarat Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Tawaaf-e-Ziyarat kab tak karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: 'Eid ke din zawaal se Pehle Tawaaf-e-Ziyarat karna afzal hai, bagair 'uzr ke ayyame tashreeq tak muakkhar karna makrooh hai aur ayyame tashreeq se bhi muakkhar (delay) karna ziyadah makrooh hai albattah is ka waqt ta dame hayat baqi rahta hai (Hashiyatul Iezaah:387). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Agar haiza 'aurat ke upar Tawaaf-e-Ziyarat baqi ho aur us ke ghar lautne ka waqt ho jaye to kya kare?

Jawab: Agar haiza 'aurat ke zimme hajj ka farz tawaaf baqi ho aur us ke ghar wapasi ka waqt aajaye to wo 'aurat teen sooraton me se kisi ek soorat par 'amal kare.

Ayyame Tashreeq Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya takbeerate tashreeq ('Eid ke ayyam me padhi jane wali takbeerat) sunnat namaz ke baad bhi padhni chahiye?

Jawab: Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke maslak ke e'etibaar se ayyame tashreeq me jo bhi namaz padhi jati hai chahe farz namaz ho ya sunnat ho ya namaze janazah har namaz ke baad takbeerat padhna sunnat hai (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/208). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya Imam Abu Hanifah (Rh) ke nazdik bhi takbeerate tashreeq ka yahi mas-alah hai jo Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdik hai?

Jawab: Imam Abu Hanifah (Rh) ke nazdik takbeerate tashreeq padhna wajib hai jis ki shuru'aat 9 zilhijjah ki fajar se hogi aur ikhtitaam 13 zilhijjah ki asar me hoga lekin yeh takbeerat sirf farz namazon ke baaad hi padhi jaye (Badaius Sanaye: 1/195). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'auraton ke liye takbeerate tashreeq padhne ke bare me kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Mardon ki tarah 'auraton ke liye bhi yeh takbeerat masnoon hain albattah 'auratein in takbeerat ko thodi past aawaz me padhein (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/206). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hajiyon Ko Ayyame Tashreeq Me Roza Rakhna

Sawal: Kya hajiyon me se kisi ko ayyame tashreeq me roza rakhna jaaiz hai? Agar jaaiz hai to kis tarah ke hajj ka Ihram bandhne wale ke liye hai aur is me kya shart zaroori hai?

Jawab: Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne 'Eidain ki tarah ayyame tashreek ke rozon ko bhi haraam qarar diya hai. 'Aam halaat me ayyame tashreeq ke roze rakhna jaaiz nahi. Albattah aisa haji jis ne “Hajje Tamattu'” ya “Hajje Qiraan” ki niyyat ki ho aur us ke pass hadi ka janwar na ho ya janwar kharidne ke liye paise na hon, ya janwar khatam ho gaye hon, ya mil na sakein, to us shakhs ke liye “Allah” ki taraf se hukm yeh hai ke dus roze rakhe, teen hajj ke dinon me aur saat ghar laut kar. Ab yeh shakhs koshish yahi karega ke Ihram bandh kar ayyame hajj shuru' hone se Pehle ya'ani 'Arafah se Pehle hi yeh teen roze mukammal ho jayein lekin kisi wajah se ayyame hajj se Pehle roze na rakh sake to Bukhari shareef ki us hadis ki wajah se jisme Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne mutamatte' ke liye in dinon mein roze rakhne ki ijazat di hai isi tarah Imam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke qoul-e-qadeem par 'amal karte hue Ayyam-e-Tashreeq ke teen dinon mein yeh farz roze rakh sakta hai ya'ani jaaiz hai. Is soorat-e-haal mein yeh dus (10) roze hadi ya'ani dum ka badal hon jayeinge.

(Hadi ya'ani Hajje Tamattu' ya Qiraan ki niyyat karne ki wajah se “Dum” ya'ani janwar zabah karna zaroori ho jata hai use hadi kehte hain).

(Sahihul jame': 6964, Wad dare Qutani: 2/157, Al-Muhazzab: 1/347, Al-Majmoo':6/443, Al-Majmoo': 6/442, Al-Majmoo':6/445). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tawaafe Wada'a Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Haiz wa Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) wali 'aurat ke liye tawaaf wada'a ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Haiz wa Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) wali 'aurat par tawaafe wada'a wajib nahi hai lekin use masjide Haraam ke darwaze ke bahar khadi ho kar du'aa kar ke rawanah hona mustahab hai (As-Sirajul-Wahhaj: 164). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Jis par tawaafe wada'a wajib ho aur wo use chhod de to kya karna hoga?

Jawab: Jis par tawaafe wada'a wajib ho aur wo use chhod de chahe jaan bujh kar ya galati se, har haal me is par dum wajib hoga. Agar yeh shakhs 81 KM pohanchne se Pehle hi wapas aa kar tawaaf kar le to ab us par dum wajib nahi hoga (As-Sirajul-Wahhaj: 164). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Haji Ko Dawat Karna

Sawal: Hamare yahan hajj karne wale ko hajj ke liye rawana hone se Pehle dawat dete hain, is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Hajj ko jane se Pehle dawat dene ka riwaj hamare yahan 'aam hai lekin asal me hajj se wapas aane ke baad haji ko dawat karna mustahab hai. (Al-Majmoo': 4/400). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Muslimon Ko Zam Zam Aur Khujoor (Date) Dene Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya 'Umrah se laya hua khujoor (date) aur zam zam ka paani sath me kaam karne wale ya apne 'alaqe me gair muslim doston ko de sakte hain?

Jawab: Ji haan, gair muslim sathi ko 'Umrah se laya hua khujoor (date) aur zam zam ka paani de sakte hain. Is me koi harj nahi. Allah se du'aa bhi kare ke Allah ta'aala in Mubarak chizon ki barkat se is gair muslim bhai ko iman ki daulat se nawaze. Sahih hadis me hai ke zam zam me shifa hai aur kufr se badh kar koi ruhaani bimari nahi (Kitabul-Fatawa – Maulana Khalid Saifullah: 4/84, Fatawa ash-Shabakatul-Islamiyyah: 11/121). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

QURBAANI AUR 'AQEEQAH KE MASA-IL

Qurbaani Ke Din Sab Se Mahboob 'Amal

Sawal: Qurbaani ke din sab se mahboob 'amal kya hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani ke din sab se mahboob 'amal Allah ke nazdeek khoon bahana hai, is liye 'Ulama ne likkha hai sab se afzal 7 bakriyan, phir unt, phir bail, phir mendha, phir bakri, phir unt me shirkat, phir bail me shirkat (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 1493, Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/208-212). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Janwar Ke Rang Aur Zabah Ke Waqt Me Afzaliyat

Sawal: Qurbaani ke janwar ke liye rang me afzaliyat kya hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani ke janwar ke rang ke liye safed afzal hai (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/212). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Qurbaani me zabah ke waqt me afzaliyat kya hai?

Jawab: Afzal waqt 'Eid ke din namaze 'Eid ke baad zabah karna hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/212). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sahibe Qurbaani Ke Liye Masnoon 'Amal

Sawal: Jis ki Qurbaani ho us ke liye das (10) din pehle (1 Zilhijjah se) baal, naakhun kaantne se kyun mana'a kiya gaya hai?

Jawab: Take magfirat aur najaat jism ke pure a'azaa ko haasil ho jaaye (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/209). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Jis ki Qurbaani hai us ne galati se apne naakhun kaat diye to kya us ki Qurbaani ho sakti hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani ke mauqa'a par badan ki koi cheez alag na karna sunnat hai. Is se asal Qurbaani par koi farq na padega. (Al-Majmoo': 8/386). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar ghar ka zimmedaar apni Qurbaani kar raha ho to kya us ke saare ghar walon ko baal aur naakhun nahi kaantna chahiye?

Jawab: Jis ki taraf se Qurbaani hai us ke liye hi sunnat hai. Saare gharwalon ke liye yeh hukm nahi hai. (Al-Majmoo': 8/391). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar hamara qurbaani ka iraadah na ho aur hum sunnat ke taur par hamare naakhun aur baal na kaatein, kya aisa kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Koi faidah nahi hoga kyunke baal aur nakhun wagayrah kaatna yeh sirf usi ke liye masnoon hai jo Qurbaani karne ka iraadah rakhta ho, jin ka iraadah na ho unhain nakhun na tarashne ka ajar nahi milega un ke liye to saaf karna hi masnoon hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 8/132). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Kisi Aur Ki Taraf Se Qurbaani

Sawal: Kya hum, kisi aur ki taraf se Qurbaani kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Kisi aur ki taraf se Qurbaani karna jaaiz hai lekin us shakhs ki ijaazat zaroori hai jis ki taraf se Qurbaani ki jaarahi ho, bagair ijaazat ke Qurbaani sahi nahi hogi (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 6/137). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya bhai, bahan ya kisi aur khooni rishtedaar ki Qurbaani ke liye bhi ijaazat zaroori hai?

Jawab: Ji ha: apne aur apne ahl-o-'ayaal ke 'alaawah kisi aur ki taraf se Qurbaani karna ho to us ki ijaazat zaroori hai. Bagair ijaazat Qurbaani sahi nahi hogi (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/215). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ke Janwar Ki Nazar

Sawal: Agar koi ek maah pehle jaanwar khareed kar laaye aur khidmat kare to kya wo nazar ho jaayegi aur poora gosht sadaqah karna hoga. Bagair niyyat ke nazar kaise ho sakti hai jabke har 'amal ka daaromadaar niyyat par hai?

Jawab: Jaanwar ki Qurbaani karna waajib yeh us soorat me hoga jab ke koi shakhs jaanwar ko Qurbaani ke liye muta-'ayyan kare ya'ani yeh kahe ke yeh mere Qurbaani ka jaanwar hai ya yeh kahe ke yeh mera jaanwar Qurbaani ke liye rakhkha huwa hai. Agarcha baaz 'Ulama-e-kiraam, 'awaam ki jahaalat ki wajaah se us ko nazar ka nahi maante phir bhi us se bachna zaroori hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/209). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Muta-'ayyan karne se wo nazar ka kaise hota hai. Samjhaiye.

Jawab: Muta-'ayyan karne ki wajaah se wo nazar ho jati hai. Nazar ki taarif yahi hai ke kisi cheez ko apne upar waajib aur zaroori qaraar dena. Ahkamaat me har jagah sirf dil ka iraadah hi kaafi nahi hota balke kuch ahkaam aise hain jahan zubaan ke izhaar par hukm ka daaromadaar hota hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/245). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Hadi ke jaanwar par Sahabah 'alaamaat lagate the, to kya nazar hui?

Jawab: Nahi! Balke hadi ke jaanwar par 'alaamaat is liye hoti thien take kuffaar un ko na chedein aur chori na karein aur hadi baaz wajohat ki bina par waajib hoti hai ya'ani wo poora jaanwar sadaqah karna hota hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj ilaa sharhil Minhaaj:3/362). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Qurbaani Ka Zindah Bakra 300 Rupaye Fi Kilo Se Bechna

Sawal: Aaj kal Qurbaani ke bakre zindah wazan karke 300 rupaye fi kilo grams se bech rahe hain. Kya is tarah karna durust hai?

Jawab: In Sha Allah Jaaiz hoga bashart yeh ke wazan karne ke baad jaanwar ke jism ko dekh kar andaazah lagaye ke andaazan itna gosht hoga, phir is andaaze ke mutabiq poore jaanwar ka mu'aamalah kare take dhokah na ho (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 13/289-290). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Chote Bachche Ki Taraf Se Qurbaani Karna

Sawal: Kya ek baap apne chote bachche ki taraf se Qurbaani kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani baalig, 'aaqil, aur istita'at rakhne wale musalman par masnoon hai lihaaza nabaalig bachche par Qurbaani zaroori nahi hai albatta agar us ka wali jaise bachche ka waalid ya daada wagayrah us bachche ki taraf se Qurbaani karna chaahe to apni zaati maal se Qurbaani kar sakta hai aur Qurbaani durust ho jaayengi. (Al-Fatawa al-Kubra:4/255). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bade Jaanwar Me Qurbaani Wa 'Aqeeqah Shaamil Karna

Sawal: Kya ek hi jaanwar par Qurbaani aur 'Aqeeqah ek saath kar sakte hain? Misal ke taur par teen hisse Qurbaani ke aur char hisse 'Aqeeqah ke.

Jawab: Bade janwar (Unt, gaye, bail, bhains) me Qurbaani wa 'Aqeeqah ko shaamil kar sakte hain (Al-Majmoo': 8/429). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Qarzdaar Par Qurbaani

Sawal: Jis par qarz ho to kya wo Qurbaani kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Qarz ki adayigi ek waajib 'amal hai aur Qurbaani ek masnoon 'amal hai. Lihaaza agar kisi par qarz ho aur qarz ki adaayigi ka waqt ho gaya ho neez us ke paas maujood raqam agar Qurbaani ke liye kharch kare to qarz ki adaayigi ke liye doosri raqam maujood na ho to us soorat me Qurbaani karna durust nahi hai. Balke us raqam se qarz ada karna zaroori hai. Ha agar qarz ki adaayigi ka koi ma'qool nazm maujood ho ya qarz muta'ayyanah waqt ke andar ada karna mumkin ho to phir qarz ki us raqam se qurbaani karne me koi haraj nahi, balke us ke liye sunnat yeh hai ke wo pehle Qurbaani kare phir jitni jald ho sake qarz ki raqam ada kare. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 4/113, Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah: 1/538). Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare

Sawal: Agar qarz itna ho jitna Qurbaani ka kharch to kya karna chahiye?

Jawab: pehle apne qarz ko ada karne ki koshish karni chahiye. Qarz ke muta-'alliq yeh baat yaad rahe ke qarz hona rukaawat nahi hai balke doosre ka haq hai. Agar qarz wala apna haq baad me lene par raazi ho to koi haraj nahi hai. (Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah: 1/538, Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 4/113). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Marhoom Ki Taraf Se Qurbaani

Sawal: Agar kisi shakhs ne kisi marhoom ki taraf se us ki wasiyyat ke bagair Qurbaani ki to kya wo Qurbaani ho jaayegi?

Jawab: Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek mare huwe shakhs ki taraf se wasiyyat ke bagair Qurbaani karna durust nahi. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:6/137). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Huzoor (ﷺ) Ki Taraf Se Qurbaani

Sawal: Kya Huzoor (ﷺ) ki taraf se Qurbaani ki ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ki taraf se Qurbaani nahi ho sakti (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj Ila Sharhil Minhaaj:8/144). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi Huzoor (ﷺ) ki taraf se Qurbaani kare to us jaanwar ke gosht ka kya hukm rahega?

Jawab: Agar koi Huzoor (ﷺ) ki taraf se Qurbaani kare to us ke gosht ko mukammal sadaqah karna zaroori nahi hai balke khud bhi use kha sakte hain, is liye ke Shawaafe' ke nazdeek mashhoor qaul ke mutabiq mayyit ki taraf se Qurbaani sahi hone ke liye us ki wasiyyat zaroori hai, agar us ki wasiyyat hai to mukammal gosht sadaqah karna waajib hai aur agar wasiyyat nahi hai to us ki taraf se Qurbaani sahi nahi hai, is ke bawajood agar koi Qurbaani kare to use khud bhi kha sakte hain aur sadaqah bhi kar sakte hain, lihaaza Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ki taraf se hamein Qurbaani ki wasiyyat na hone ki wajaah se Aap (ﷺ) ke haq me Qurbaani sahi nahi hai. Jaise ke fuqahaa ki ibaaraton se pata chalta hai. (Minhaajut-Talibeen: 321, Kifayatun Nabeeh-Sharhut Tambeeh:8/60, Al-Majmoo': 8/406, Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah: 4/256, Islaami Sawal wa Jawab). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Ek Bakre Me Ghar Ke Sab Logon Ki Qurbaani

Sawal: ek shakhs ek bakra apne aur apne ahl-o-'ayaal ki taraf se zabah karta hai to is soorat me kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek ek bakre ki Qurbaani ek hi shakhs ki taraf se ada hogi neez zabah ke baad us Qurbaani ke ajr wa sawaab me ahle khana aur doosron ko bhi shaamil kare to sab ko sawaab bhi milega. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/208-209). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya hanafi maslak ke hisaab se ek bakre par pure khaandanwaalon ki taraf se Qurbaani kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Shawafe' ki tarah ahnaaf ke nazdeek bhi ek bakra ki Qurbaani ek hi shakhs ki taraf se ada hogi pure khaandanwaalon ko Qurbaani me shareek nahi kar sakte. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya ghar ka sarbarah apne khaandan ki taraf se ek bakra Qurbaani ka de sakta hai? Ya wo Qurbaani sirf usi ki Qurbaani kehlaayegi.

Jawab: Ghar walon me se agar koi ek Qurbaani kare to wo Qurbaani usi ki taraf se hogi aur tamaam ghar waalon ki taraf se sunnat ka haq ada ho jaayega ya'ani Qurbaani ka mutaalabah gharwaalon me se doosron se saaqit ho jaayega is e'etibaar se yeh Qurbaani sunnat alal kifaaya hogi. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 7/119, Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 8/211, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 8/126, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 7/111). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar ek ghar me ek se ziyadah kamaane wale hain lekin ek saath rehte hain ya'ani jo aulad waalidain par munhasir nahi hain to kya sab ki taraf se ek bakra kaafi hoga?

Jawab: Aulad jab baalig ho jaati hai aur wo maa'zur wa majboor na ho to us ki zimmedaari baap ki nahi hoti is liye un ko apne taur par apni haisiyat se Qurbaani karna chaahiye. Lekin gharwaalon me se koi ek ada kare to tamam logon ki taraf se kaafi ho jaayegi aur doosron se Qurbaani karne ka mutaalabah nahi kiya jaayega lekin sawaab sirf Qurbaani karne wale ko hi milega. Magar yeh ke Qurbaani karne wala ahle khana ko bhi sawaab me shaamil kare to ab sawaab me tamaam shaamil ho jaayenge. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 8/126, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 7/111). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Gair muqallideen kehte hain ke ek bakre me ghar ke sab logon ki taraf se Qurbaani ho jaati hai? Is ki daleel kiya hai?

Jawab: Sunan Ibne Maajah me Siddiqua 'Aayesha (Rz) aur Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) se marvi hai farmaate hain ke jab Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) Qurbaani karne ka iraadah farmaate to wo bade bade, mote taaze, seengwale, chitkabade, Khassi mendhe khareedte phir un me se ek ko apni ummat ki taraf se zabah karte aur doosre ko Aap (ﷺ) apni taraf se aur Aale Muhammad (ﷺ) ki taraf se zabah karte isi tarah Hazrat Abu Ayyub Ansaari (Rz) se kisi ne poocha ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke daure mas'ood me Qurbaani kaise huwa karti thi us ke Jawab me Aap (Rz) ne farmaaya ke ek shakhs ek hi bakri apni taraf se aur apne ahl-o-'ayaal ki taraf se Qurbaani me zabah karta tha phir wo khud bhi us me se khata tha aur doosron ko bhi khilaata tha (Sunan At-Tirmizi:1505) is jaisi hadison ko buniyad bana kar gair muqallideen is baat ke qaail (manne wale) ho gaye hain ke ek bakre ki Qurbaani poore khaandan waalon ki taraf se ada ho jaayegi jabke fuqhaye Shawafe' aur deegar fuqhaye Kiraam is ki taaweel karte hain ke is se murad ahle khana ko sawaab me shaamil karna hai. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ek Hi Saal Me Do Bar Qurbaani Karna

Sawal: Kya koi aadmi ek hi saal me do Qurbaani kar sakta hai ek bairun me jahaan kama raha hai afzaliyat ki wajaah se aur doosre apne mulk me jahaan gharwaale Qurbaani kar rahe hain?

Jawab: Qurbaani ek 'ibaadat hai aur sawaab ki cheez hai is liye ek se ziyaadah baar bhi kiya jaaye to bhi koi haraj nahi hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 3/228). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Bhains Par Qurbaani

Sawal: Gaye bail par pabandi ki wajaah se bhains par Qurbaani hogi ke nahi kyunke kuch log is ke gosht ko makrooh samajhte hain?

Jawab: Bhains par Qurbaani sahi hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/210). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Qurbaani Ke Jaanwar Ke Liye Bakre Ki 'Umr (Age)

Sawal: Shawafe' ke yahan Qurbaani ke liye mendha aur bakre ki 'umr (Age) kya hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke yahan Qurbaani ke liye mendha ek saal ka aur bakra do saal ka hona zaroori hai (Tuhfatul-Bari:3/210). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kuwait me ek saal ke bakre Qurbaani ke liye beche ja rahe hain kya baaqi maslak me bakre ki 'umr ek saal hai?

Jawab: Imaam Malik (Rh) aur Abu Hanifah (Rh) ke nazdeek ek saal ka bakra kaafi hota hai. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:5/82-83). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Do (2) saal se ziyaadah 'umr wale bakre ki nishaani kiya hai? Kya do daant hona is ki sahi daleel hai?

Jawab: Do daant ka nikalna yeh zaahiri nishaani ho sakti hai aur is ka taa'lluq tajrabe se hota hai is liye jinhen is ka tajrabah ho un ki baat ka e'etibaar kiya jaayega lekin daant nikle bagair do saal ho to kaafi hai (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:5/83, Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/210). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Note: Asal aur dalail ke aitibar se mazboot qaul yahi hai ke Shari'at ki janib se muta'ayyan kardah janwaron ki umar ka khayal rakh kar qurbani ki jaye. Agar kisi wajah se matloobah umar ke janwar mayassar na hon to bade janwar mein shirkat kar li jaye ya mendhe ke zariye qurbani ki sunnat ada ki jaye. Agar yeh bhi dushwar ho jaye aur mumkin na ho to aakhri darje mein Shawafe'e ke qaul-e-marjooh (daleelon ke aitibar se kamzor qaul) ke mutabiq do saal se kam umar ke bakre ki qurbani ki gunjaish hai.

Qurbaani Ke Liye Langdhaapan Wala Jaanwar

Sawal: Agar kisi ne qurbaani ke liye bakra khareeda magar Qurbaani se pehle us ke paun me langdhaapan aa jaaye to kya langdhaapan wala jaanwar qurbaani ke liye kaafi hoga?

Jawab: imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek agar Qurbaani ke jaanwar mein langdhaapan thoda ho ya'ani chalne me apne saathiyon se peeche na rah jaata ho to aisa langdha jaanwar Qurbaani me jaaiz hoga warna nahi. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:15/80). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kami Ya Kharabi Wale Jaanwar Per Qurbaani

Sawal: Agar kisi ne qurbaani ka jaanwar khareeda, baad me us me kuch naqs (kami ya kharabi) paai jaaye jis se wo jaanwar qurbaani ke layiq na rahe phir wo doosra jaanwar la kar Qurbaani kar le to phir us pehle jaanwar ka kya kare?

Jawab: Sunnat Qurbaani ki niyyat se jab jaanwar khareeda gaya to ab wo jaanwar muta-'ayyan nahi huwa ya'ani us jaanwar ki Qurbaani zaroori nahi, phir jab us me 'ayb aur naqs paida ho gaya to wo Qurbaani me jaaiz nahi huwa to ab us ke saath jo bhi mu'aamalah kiya jaaye jaaiz hoga ya'ani kaat kar khud kha le ya use bech de wagayrah (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/213). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Andha, Haamilah Aur Khujli Wale Jaanwar Par Qurbaani

Sawal: Naabina (Andha) jaanwar par Qurbaani karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Naabina jaanwar par Qurbaani karna durust nahi hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 3/195). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Sawal: Jis jaanwar ko khaarish (Khujli) ki beemaari laahiq ho gai ho us par Qurbaani karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Jis jaanwar ko khaarish ki beemaari ho gai ho us par Qurbaani karna durust nahi hai. (Asnal-Matalib: 1/535). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Sawal: Haamilah jaanwar par Qurbaani karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Haamilah jaanwar par Qurbaani karna durust nahi hai. (Fatawa ar-Ramali: 4/69). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Bachcha Jani Hui Gaay, Bakri Par Qurbaani Aur 'Aqeeqah

Sawal: Kya Qurbaani aur 'Aqeeqah ke liye bachcha jani hui gaay, bakri ya isi tarah koi muannas jaanwar jis par Qurbaani jaaiz hai isti'emaal kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Bilkul jaaiz hai, bashart yeh ke us me koi aisa 'ayb paida na ho jo Qurbaani ke liye maane'e (rukuwat) ho. (Bushral-Kareem: 1/698). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kaashtkaari Me Isti'emaal Hone Wale Bail Par Qurbaani Aur 'Aqeeqah

Sawal: Kya kaashtkaari ke liye isti'emaal hone wale bail, Qurbaani aur 'Aqeeqah ke liye isti'emaal kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Bilkul jaaiz hai, bashart yeh ke us me koi aisa 'ayb paida na ho jo Qurbaani ke liye maane'e (rukuwat) ho. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/210). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jaanwaron Ki Khassi Karna Aur Us Par Qurbaani

Sawal: Kya janwaron ki Khassi (Shahwate Nafsaaniyah ko khatam) karana jaaiz hai? Neez kya khassi kiye huye jaanwar par Qurbaani jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Aise janwar jin ka gosht khana halaal hai un ke gosht ko behtar aur badhaane ki niyyat se un ke bachpan me un ki khassi karna jaaiz hai. Lekin badi 'umr me chunke Khusiyah (Kapoore) sakht ho jaate hain, to is 'umr me agar jaanwar par khassi banane ka 'amal kiya jaaye to use shadeed mashaqqat laahiq hoti hai. Is liye fuqhaa-e-Kiraam ke nazdeek jaanwaron ko badi 'umr me khassi karaana haraam aur najaaiz 'amal hai, is ke baawajood us ka gosht khaana halaal hoga aur zakhm bilkul theek ho chuka ho to is par Qurbaani bhi jaaiz aur sahi hogi (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/211). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bade Jaanwar Par Saat Logon Ki Qurbaani

Sawal: Bade jaanwar par saat logon ki Qurbaani hone ki daleel hadees se kya hai?

Jawab: Hazrat Jaabir (Rz) farmaate hain hum ne sulah hudaibiyah wale saal Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ke saath jaanwar zabah kiye. Gaay saat logon ki taraf se au oont saat aadamiyon ki taraf se (Muslim).

Imam Baihaqi (Rh) farmaate hain ke “Hum tak Hazrat Ali (Rz), Hazrat Huzaifah (Rz), Hazrat Abu Mas'ood Ansari (Rz) aur Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) se riwaayat pahonchi hai ke in sab ne kaha, gaay par saat aadmiyon ki taraf se Qurbaani hoti hai.” (Al-Majmoo': 8/399). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Oont (Camel) par kitne logon ki Qurbaani hoti hai? Kyunke mai ne kisi kitaab me das (10) ka padha is liye tahqeeq kar raha hu.

Jawab: Ek riwaayat me oont par das logon ki Qurbaani ka tazkirah hai, magar wo marjooh ya mu'azal hai, aksar riwaayat me oont par saat logon ki taraf se Qurbaani ka tazkirah hai. (Kifaayatul akhyaar fi halli gaayatil ikhtisaar: 1/5). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Hissah De Kar Qurbaani Karna

Sawal: Kya hissa de kar Qurbaani karna durust hai?

Jawab: Hissa de kar Qurbaani karna durust hai. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb=Tuhfatul-Habib 'Ala Sharhil-Khateeb:4/333). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya bade jaanwar me hissa liya ja sakta hai?

Jawab: 'Eid ke din Allah Ta'aala ke nazdeek bande ka mahboob 'amal khoon bahana hai is wajaah se 'Ulama-e-kiraam ne farmaaya bade jaanwar me shirkat se behtar chote jaanwar ki Qurbaani hai, taake khoon khoob bahe lekin bade jaanwar me shirkat bhi jaaiz hai aur hadees me is ka suboot hai is liye 'Ulama ne jo tarteeb likhi hai pehle saat bakriyan phir oont men shirkat phir gaay me shirkat (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj Ila Sharhil Minhaaj:8/134). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Doosri Jagah Qurbaani Karna

Sawal: Kya doosri jagah Qurbaani karana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani apne 'alaqe me karna behtar hai aur doosri jagah karana bhi jaaiz hai. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen 'ala Halli Alfazi Fathil-Mu'een:2/380). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Haraam Kamaai Se Qurbaani Me Hissa Lena

Sawal: Koi shakhs haraam kamaai se Qurbaani ke jaanwar me doosre logon ke saath shareek ho jaaye to kya is soorat mein us ki aur doosre shurakaa ki Qurbaani durust ho jaayegi ya nahi?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke yahan agar Qurbaani ke hissedaaron me se koi hissadaar deegar shurakaa ke saath haraam kamaai ke zariye shareek ho to use Qurbaani ka sawaab nahi milega albatta deegar shurakaa ki Qurbaani durust hogi aur unhein apne Qurbaani ka sawaab bhi milega. Allah Ta'aala ka irshaad hai: koi bojh uthaane wala doosre ka bojh nahi uthaayega.

Is aayat se ma'aloom huwa ke agar ek jaanwar ki Qurbaani me kayi log shaamil hain aur ek shakhs ki kamaai haraam ki hai to us ke sabab doosron ki Qurbaani zaaye'a na hogi, balke jis ka maal haraam ka hai wahi shakhs sawaab se mahroom hoga. Mufti Ishaq Patel

Qurbaani Ka Waqt

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Qurbaani ka waqt 13 Zilhijjah ke guroob tak hai. Is ki daleel kya hai? Gair muqallideen ke yahan kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Gair Muqallideen ke nazdeek bhi 13 Zilhijjah ki Magrib tak Qurbaani karna masnoon hai. hadees me hai ke Nabi (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: Tamam ayyame tashreeque zabah ke din hain, is riwaayat ki wajaah se imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek 13 Zilhijjah ki Magrib tak Qurbaani karna jaaiz aur masnoon hai (As-Sunanul Kubra lil bayhaqi:9/295, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 8/136). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ahnaaf ke nazdeek Qurbaani ka waqt 12 Zilhijjah ki 'asrtak hai wo kis bunyaad par hai?

Jawab: Hazrat Ali (Rz) aur Anas (Rz) ki riwaayat hai us me 'Eid ke baad do din ka tazkirah hai (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 5/93). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi Middle East me kaam kar raha hai us ki 'Eid aaj hui lekin wo Qurbaani India ke chauthe din (13 Zilhijjah) me India me karaye jo ke middle east ke hisaab se Qurbaani ke ayyam me se nahi hai to kya us ki Qurbaani sahi hogi?

Jawab: Behtar yeh hai ke Qurbaani ke liye muakkil aur wakeel dono ke waqt ki ri'aayat ki jaaye taaham agar wakeel aur muakkil ke waqt me mu'afaqat (baraabari) dushwar ho to wakeel ke waqt ka e'etibaar hoga, lihaaza Soorate mas-uoolah me Qurbaani ada hogi. (Tajaawiz: Baabat doosra Shafi'ee fiqahi seminar: 2020, Zamemah Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/463). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Raat Me Qurbaani Karna

Sawal: Kya raat me Qurbaani karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Raat me Qurbaani karna makrooh hai, lekin agar roshani ka achcha intizaam ho, is tarah se ke jaanwar ka halaq ya'ani halaq ki wo jagaah jahan zabah karna hai wo waazeh ho to aysi soorat me makrooh nahi hai, albatta behtar yahi hai ke din me Qurbaani ki jaaye take sahi waqt me gurabaa tak Qurbaani ka gosht pahuanch sake. (Asnal-Matalib:1/537). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Eid Ki Namaz Se Pehle Qurbaani Ka Jaanwar Zabah Karna

Sawal: Kya 'Eid ki namaz se pehle Qurbaani ka jaanwar zabah kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek Qurbaani ke sahi hone ke liye Tulu'-e-Aaftab aur makrooh waqt guzar jaane ke baad itne waqt ka guzarna kaafi hai jis me 2 rak'at namaze 'Eid aur khutbe diye jaayein. Lihaaza agar koi shakhs itna waqt guzarne ke baad Qurbaani karta hai to us ki Qurbaani sahi hogi agarcha namaze 'Eid se pehle ho. (Tuhfatul-Bari:3/212). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zabah Me Do Ragon Ka Kaatana

Sawal: Zabah me kin ragon ka katna zaroori hai jis ke baad jaanwar halaal ho jaata hai?

Jawab: Jis haiwaan ko baaqaidah zabah kar sakte hon(khwah paaltu ho ya wahshi) us ke hulqoom (Saans ki naali) aur maree (Gizaa ki naali) ko mukammal katna zaroori hai, agar in ka kuch hissa bhi katne se rah jaaye ya kuch hissa katne ke baad jaanwar harkate mazbuh (post-slaughter movements) ki haalat ko pahonch jaaye phir kuch taakheer se baqiyyah hissa kaate to jaanwar halaal na hoga, agar kuch hissa katne ke baad churi uthaye aur fauran dobaarah phere ya churi gir jaaye aur fauran utha kar zabah mukammal kare, ya dhaar kam hone ki wajaah se wo churi rakh kar fauran doosri churi se zabah mukammal kare to jaanwar halaal hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ke Gosht Ke Hisse

Sawal: Qurbaani ke gosht ke silsile me kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Afzal aur behtar yeh hai ke chand luqme bataure tabarruk khaye aur baqiyyah gosht sadaqah kare. Agar mazeed khaane ka iraadah hoto afzal yeh hai ke gosht ko teen hisson me taqseem kare. ek hissa apne liye doosra hissa rishtadaaron aur dost wa ahbaab me hadiyah kar dein aur teesara hissa gurabaa wa masaakeen me sadaqah kar dein. Chunanche “Al-Mabsoot” me imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ka yeh qaul naql kiya gaya hai ke wo farmaate hain: main yeh pasand karta hun ke Qurbaani karne wala ek tihaayi gosht me se khaye aur zakherah karke rakhe aur baqiyah doosra tihaayi gosht hadiyah kar de aur teesra tihaayi hissa sadaqah kar de. (Al-Mabasut). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Qurbaani ka poora gosht apne paas roke rakhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs sunnat Qurbaani kar raha ho to aysi Qurbaani ke gosht me se thoda gosht jis par gosht ka itlaaq hota hai fuqaraa wa masaakeen par sadaqah karna waajib hai, is ke baad sunnat Qurbaani ka baqiyah gosht apne paas roke rakhna jaaiz hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/214). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ka Gosht Teen Din Se Ziyaadah Zakherah Karna

Sawal: Kya Qurbaani ka gosht teen din se ziyaadah gharon me jama'a kar ke rakhna aur khana jaaiz nahi? Kyunke baaz log kehte hain ke hadeese paak me Huzoor (ﷺ) ne qurbaani ke gosht ki zakherah andozi se mana'a farmaaya hai?

Jawab: Islam ke ibtidaayi daur me Sahaba-e-Kiraam (Rz) ki khastah haali ki wajaah se Nabi Paak (ﷺ) ne Qurbaani ka gosht teen din se ziyaadah khane se mana'a farmaya tha, phir jab haalaat saazgaar hoye to Aap(ﷺ) ne is paabandi ko khatam kiya aur khane aur zakherah karne ki ijaazat de di. Lihaaza Qurbaani ke gosht ko ayyame tashreeq ke baad bhi jama'a kar ke rakh sakte hain, aur kha sakte hai. (Al-Majmoo' Sharhul-Muhazzab: 8/418).

Chunanche sahi Bukhari shareef me hadeese mubarak hai: Sayyiduna Salmah bin Akwa'a (Rz) se riwaayat hai Aap ne farmaya ke Hazrat Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya: Jo shakhs Qurbaani kare to teesre din us ke ghar me Qurbaani ka kuch hissa bhi bachaa na rahe, jab doosre saal Qurbaani ka marhala aaya to Hazraate Sahaba-e-Kiraam (Rz) ne arz kiya: Ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ)! Kya hum is saal bhi Qurbaani ka gosht guzashtah saal ki tarah isti'emaal kare? Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaya: Qurbaani ka gosht tum khao, doosron ko khilao aur zakherah kar lo: kyunke guzashtah saal log mashaqqat me the isi liye main ne iraadah kiya ke tum ayyame Qurbaani mein un ki madad karo. (Saheehul Bukhaari: 5569).

Isi tarah Saheeh Muslim me hai: Hazrat Abu Musa Ash'ari (Rz) kehte hain ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Mai ne tumhain qabron ki ziyaarat se mana'a kiya tha to ab tum us ki ziyaarat kiya karo aur mai ne Qurbaani ke gosht ko teen din se ziyaadah rakhne se mana'a kiya tha to ab jab tak ji chahe rakho, aur main ne tumhain mashk ke 'alaawah kisi aur bartan me nabeez Peene se mana'a kiya tha, to sare bartanon me peewo(magar) kisi nashah'aawar cheez ko mat peena” (Saheeh Muslim: 977). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ka Gosht Farokht Karne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Qurbaani ka gosht farokht karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Jis shakhs ne Qurbaani ki hai us ke liye Qurbaani ke jaanwar me se koi cheez bechna jaaiz nahi hai. Albatta Qurbaani karne wala kisi faqeer ko gosht de to faqeer ke liye apne gosht me se bechna jaaiz hai. (Al-Majmoo': 8/420). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Ek Ghar Me Do Ya Us Se Ziyaadah Qurbaani Ke Bakre

Sawal: Agar ek ghar me do ya do se ziyaadah Qurbaani ke bakre hon.

Jawab: Har jaanwar me se kuch gosht sadaqah karna waajib aur zaroori hai. lihaaza muta'addad bakron ki ek saath qurbani karne ki soorat me agar kisi bakre ka kuch gosht sadaqa na kiya jaaye to qurbaani to ho jayegi lekin gosht ki utni miqdar uske zimme qarz rahegi, jis gosht ko apni milkiyat se ya kharid kar sadqah karna zaruri hoga. (Al-Minhajul-Qaweem Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah: 1/309). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar ek ghar me do ya us se ziyaadah Qurbaani ke bakre hon aur un me se ek do saal ka ho aur baqi ek saal ya ek saal se kam ho. Kya in chote bakron ka gosht kha sakte hain?

Jawab: Mas-alah gosht khane ka nahi hai gosht to kha sakte hain lekin imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek aise bakre par Qurbaani sahi nahi hogi. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/210). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Qurbaani Ka Gosht Gairon Ko Khilaana

Sawal: Gair muslim ko Qurbaani ka gosht dene ka Shawafe' ke nazdeek kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani 'ibaadat hai aur 'ibaadat ka ta'alluq musalmaanon se hota hai. Is liye imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Qurbaani ka gosht gairon ko kisi tarah bhi dena durust nahi hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Shawafe' ke nazdeek Qurbaani ka gosht kisi gair muslim ko dena ya khilaana wagayrah mana'a hai. Isi tarah koi gareeb khud ko sadaqah me mila huwa ya amir hadiyah me mila huwa gosht kafeer ko bech ya khila nahi sakta aur na hadiyah me de sakta hai. Jaise ke Allama Ibne Hajar haitami (Rh) ka bayan hai.

(Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 9/363). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Sawal: Kya Hanafi maslak me gair muslimon ko Qurbaani ka gosht dene ki ijazat hai? Isi tarah kya sadaqah kisi gair muslim ko de sakte hain? Kya Qurbaani ka gosht sadaqah karna nifli sadaqah me aata hai ya farz?

Jawab: Ji ha. Ahnaaf ke nazdeek Qurbaani ka gosht gair muslim ko dena durust hai. Aise hi nifli sadaqah gair ko dena sahi hai. Qurbaani me se kuch gosht sadaqah karna waajib hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Ahnaaf ke yahan Qurbaani ka gosht gairon ko de sakte hain to Shafi'ee me kyun nahi? Ahnaaf ke nazdeek sahi hai is ki kya daleel hai?

Jawab: Ahnaaf ki mash'hoore zamanah Kitabul Fatawa Al Hindiyyah me likkha hai ke Qurbaani ke gosht me se zimmi (kaafir) ko diya ja sakta hai aur Shawafe' ke nazdeek mumaana'at ki wajaah upar guzar chuki hai. (Kitabul Fatawa Al Hindiyyah:5/300). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar gair muslim ghar me naukar hai aur wo apne ghar me hi khana khata hai to kya use ghar me paka huwa Qurbaani ka gosht khane de sakte hain?

Jawab: Kisi bhi soorat me kaafir ko Qurbaani ka gosht dena sahi nahi. Agar use khilana hi ho to alag se doosra gosht paka kar khila de. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/215). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi sunnat Qurbaani ka gosht gair muslim shakhs ko khilaye phir yeh baat us ke 'ilm me aaye ke Shawafe' ke nazdeek Qurbaani ka gosht gair muslim ko dena haraam hai, ab is soorat me uski talaafi ki soorat kya hogi? Kya utna hi gosht khareed kar sadaqah kiya jaaye ya us ki qeemat sadaqah ki jaaye ya sirf taubah wa istigfaar kaafi hoga?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah ke mutaabiq agar koi la ilmi me gair muslim ko Qurbaani ka gosht de to gair muslim par zaroori hai ke wo us gosht ki qeemat musalman fuqaraa par taqseem kare, agar gair muslim taqsim nahi karta hai to behtar hai ke jis ne gosht diya hai wahi taqseem kare jaise ke sahibe Bujayrimi ke kalam se is ki wazaahat hoti hai. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb:5/397) neez dekhiye: Haashiyatut Tirmizi:6/647, Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab:8/218 Mufti Ishaq Patel

Bakre Ki Ojhdi Dafnaana Ya Gair Ko Dena

Sawal: Aksar log Qurbaani ke bakre ki ojhdi (pait ka cover) dafnaate hain. To kya use gair muslim ko de sakte hain? Agar hum ne use dafnaane ki niyyat se gaddhe me daal diya lekin hamare yahan kaam karne wala gair use nikaal kar saaf kar ke le jaaye to kya kare?

Jawab: Ojhdi khana jaaiz hai. Agar pasand na ho to kisi musalman bhai ko hadiya kar de, gair muslim ko dena jaaiz nahi, isi tarah ojhdi ya chamde ko gaddhe me daal kar zaae'a bhi na kare kyunke Qurbaani ke ajzaa me se kisi juz ko talf (destroy / waste) karna aur barbaad karna jaaiz nahi hai. (Asnal-Matalib). Lekin agar koi lene ke liye aamaadah na ho to dafanane ki gunjaish hai. Dafnaane ke baad lailmi me koi nikaal kar le jaaye to aap ka zimma us se bari hai ya'ani aap per us ki koi zimmedari nahi. (Asnal-Matalib). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ka Jaanwar Mar Jaaye Ya Bhaag Jaaye

Sawal: Agar kisi ne Qurbaani ka jaanwar khareed liya aur wo mar jaaye ya bhag jaaye ya use koi beemaari ho jaaye to kya kare? Kya us par naya janwar la kar Qurbaani karna waajib hoga? Agar haisiyat ke bawajood doosra janwar na laye aur Qurbaani na kare to kya wo gunahgar hoga?

Jawab: Sunnat Qurbaani ki niyyat se jab janwar kharida gaya to ab wo janwar muta-'ayyan nahi huwa ya'ani us janwar ki Qurbaani zaroori nahi, phir jab us me 'ayb aur nuqs paida ho gaya to wo Qurbaani me jaaiz nahi hua. Ab us ke saath jo bhi mu'aamalah kiya jaaye jaaiz hoga. Ya'ani kaat kar khud kha jaaye ya use bech de neez haisiyat hone ke bawajood doosre janwar par Qurbaani na kare tab bhi gunahgar nahi hoga. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 9/356). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kaan Me Bataure Nishaani Billa Punch Kiye Huwe Jaanwar Par Qurbaani

Sawal: Government scheme me liye huwe bakre ke kaan me bataur nishaani billa punch kar ke lagaaya huwa hota hai, kya is tarah ka bakra Qurbaani ke liye jaaiz hoga?

Jawab: Agar punch karne ki wajaah se kaan ka kuch hissa alag ho jaata ho to Qurbaani me kaafi nahi hoga. Warnah kaafi ho jaayega albatta baad az tahqeeq yaqeeni taur par yeh baat wazeh ho gai hai ke billa punch karne ki maujoodah soorat me kaan ka koi bhi hissa zaae'a nahi hota lihaaza aise jaanwar ki Qurbaani sahi hogi. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/211). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ki Bajaaye Us Ki Qeemat Atiyyah Ya Sadaqah Karna
