Sawal: Maujoodah haalaat me “Lock Down” ki wajah se aksar log ma'aashi e'etibaar se khastah ho gaye hain, kayi saalon se mera yeh ma'amul chala aa raha hai ke mai apne qurbaani ke saath saath apne ahle khana me se har har fard ki taraf se ek ek bakre ki Qurbaani karta hun, mera Sawal yeh hai ke kya mai is saal Qurbaani ke ayyaam me ghar waalon ki taraf se qurbaani na karte huye wahi raqam masaajid wa madaaris aur gurabaa wa masaakeen me atiyyah ya sadaqah kar doon to aisa karna jaaiz hoga aur kya is tarah karne se Qurbaani ka sawaab haasil ho jaayega?

Jawab: Ayyaame Qurbaani me Allah Ta'aala ke nazdeek jaanwaron ko zabah karne aur khoon bahaane se behtar koi 'amal nahi, Qurbaani ek mustaqil 'ibaadat hai aur saqadah wa atiyyah karna ek alag 'ibaadat, Qurbaani karne ke bajaaye us ki qeemat sadaqah wa atiyyah kar dene se Qurbaani ka ajr nahi milega aur yeh Qurbaani ka badal bhi nahi ho sakta.

Shawafe' ke nazdeek aisa musalmaan ke jiske paas 10 Zilhijjah se 13 Zilhijjah tak apne masaarif aur zarooriyaat se zaa-id (excess) itna maal ho ke wo Qurbaani kar sakta ho to us ke liye Qurbaani karna sunnate Muakadah hai, lihaaza use tark karna aur Qurbaani na karna makrooh hai, isi wajaah se Qurbaani karna nifli sadaqah karne se afzal hai.

Agar ek shakhs par kayi logon ka nafaqah waajib ho to un par Qurbaani sunnat Alal kifaayah hai ya'ani un me se koi ek anjaam de to baqiyyah se us ka mutaalabah saaqit ho jaayega, is soorat me Qurbaani ek ki taraf se ada hogi, albatta Qurbaani karne wala shakhs sawaab me deegar ahle khaana wagayrah ko shareek kar sakta hai. Is e'etibaar se agar kisi shakhs ka yeh ma'amul ho ke wo in ayyaam me apni qurbaani ke 'alaawah apne ahle khaana ki taraf se bhi doosre janwar par qurbani karta ho to maujoodah ahwaal mein is shakhs ka apne ghar ke tamaam afraad ki taraf se alag alag qurbani karne se behtar hai ke ghar ke ek fard ki taraf se qurbani kare aur mazeed raqam se zarooratmandon ki zaroorat ko poora kiya jaaye to In Sha Allah Ta'aala qurbaani ki sunnat par bhi 'amal hoga aur infaaq fi sabilillah ke hukm par bhi 'amal hoga. Wallahu A'alam (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/208-209). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qurbaani Ke Mutafarriq Masa-il

Sawal: Agar kisi ne apne ghar me ek bail pala, us ke walid ka iraadah tha ke is bail par Qurbaani karenge, ab un ke walid ka intiqaal ho chuka aur aaj ke halaat me bail par Qurbaani hukoomat ne pabandi kar di hai, ab is haal me kya kare? Kya us ko farokht karke bakra khareede? Agar walid ki taraf se bail par Qurbaani ki gai to kya poora ka poora sadaqah karna hai ya walid ke hisse ka sadaqah karna hai?

Jawab: Qurbaani ki niyyat se kisi jaanwar ko palna ya Qurbaani ke liye kisi jaanwar ko pehle se khas karna 'Ulama ne ise nazar ki Qurbaani maani hai jo waajib hai aur poora sadaqah karna hota hai lekin 'awaam chunke is mas'ale se waqif nahi hoti is liye baaz 'Ulama ne is ko sunnat hi mana'a hai aur sunnat hone ki soorat me agar Qurbaani na bhi kare to us ka zimmeh qaza wagayrah bhi nahi hoti ab jabke bail ki Qurbaani par paabandi hai is liye is ko bech kar bakra khareed kar Qurbaani kare to behtar hoga (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/209). Wallahu A'alamu bis sawaab. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Mera room partner naumuslim hai. In Sha Allah India me Qurbaani kar raha hai aur us ke rishteydaar saare gair muslim hain ab rishteydaar ka hissa kya karna hai?

Jawab: Gair Muslim rishteydaaron ko Qurbaani ka gosht dena jaaiz nahi, yeh sirf musalmaanon ka haq hai jaise zakaat kaafiron ko dena jaaiz nahi, kyunke yeh musalmaanon ka haq hai to ab rishteydaaron ka hissa 'aam musalmaanon aur gurabaa wa fuqaraa par sarf kar de. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/215). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Agar Qurbaani Ke Liye Do Saal Ka Bakra Na Mile

Sawal: Agar Qurbaani ke liye do saal ka bakra na mile to kya karna chahiye?

Jawab: Qurbaani ke liye jin jaanwaron ko sharee'at ne mashru'a kiya hai un me se ek bakra aur bakri bhi hai, aur bakra aur bakri ki 'umr ke silsile me nasirul hadees imaam Shafi'ee ka nuqta-e-nazar ahaadees ki roshni me yeh hai ke bakre ki 'umr do saal ya us se ziyadah hona chaahiye, is liye ke hazrat Jaabir (Rz) ne farmaaya ke Tum sirf Musinnah hi ki Qurbaani karo agar tum ko koi dushwaari laahiq ho to tum mendhe ka ek saalah bachchah zabah karo (Muslim: 1963). Hazrat Baraa Bin Aazib (Rz) se marvi hai ke Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) ne 'Eid ki namaz se qabl Qurbaani ki to Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne un se farmaaya ke tum ne gosht ke liye bakri zabah ki (Qurbani nahi hui) Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) ne farmaaya mere paas ek saalah bakri hai to Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya us ki Qurbaani karo. Yeh teri taraf se kaafi hai tere baad kisi ke liye kaafi na hoga (Abu Dawood: 2801).

Imam 'Umrani (Rh) in dalaayil ki roshni me farmate hain ke Qurbaani ke liye oont, gaay, bail, bakra, bakri, mendha, mendhi saniyyah (2 saal) hona zaroori hai. Ya'ani oont paanch saal ka gaaye bail bakra bakri do saal ka aur mendha ek saal ka hona zaroori hai is liye ke Aap (ﷺ) ne Hazrat Abu Burdah (Rz) ko ek saalah bakri zabah karne ki rukhsat di thi aur yeh farmaya tha ke tumhaare baad kisi ke liye jaaiz na hoga. Lihaaza kisi ne ek saalah bakri par Qurbaani ki to asal qaul ke mutaabiq Qurbaani durust nahi hogi (Al-Bayan: 4/415). Lekin maujoodah zamaanah me do saalah bakre ka ba-kasrat dastiyaab na hone ki binaa par log pareshaan nazar aate hain, aur basa auqaat bakron ke taajir hazraat bhi 'umr ke batlane me jhoot aur fareb se kaam lete hain, aysi soorate haal me hamein Qur-aan wa hadees aur us se makhuz fiqhe islaami se roshni haasil karne ki zaroorat hai. Sab se pehle to hamein is baat par gaur karna hai ke kya jis bakre ki 'umr ko le kar hum pareshaan hain, wo sirf is wajaah se ke us par Qurbaani karna afzal hai. Ya is ke muqaabale me koi aur jaanwar afzal hai, chunaanche Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) se marvi hai ke “Mai ne Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ko yeh farmaate huwe suna ke kya hi behtar hai ek saalah mendhe ki Qurbaani. Pas log mendhon par toot pade. (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 1499) is hadees ke zariye fuqahaa ne istidlaal kiya hai ke bakre ke muqaabale me mendha afzal hai. Neez Aap (ﷺ) ne do mendhon ke zariye Qurbaani ki. (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 5554).

Mazkoorah hadees se yeh baat saabit hui ke mendhe ko Aap (ﷺ) ne afzal qaraar diya hai, chunaanche imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) farmaate hain ke mendha, bakre ke muqaabale me afzal hai (Al-Umm: 3/583). imaam Mavardi (Rh) farmaate hain ke afzal to oont hai aur bakre bakri ke muqabale me mendha afzal hai (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 15/77). Jab mendha afzal hai, aur sharee'at ki muqarrarkardah 'umr ke mutaabiq mendhe dastiyaab bhi hain to phir afzaliyat ko chod kar sirf umdah gosht ko maqsad bana kar gair afzaliyat ko ikhtiyaar karne aur us ki 'umr ke taa'lluq se bhi shak wa shubah me mubtala (afflicted) rah kar Qurbaani ki chandah kuch bhi zaroorat nahi, albatta agar market me mendhe dastiyaab nahi hain aur un ka husool bhi mumkin na ho aur kisi aur jaanwar par Qurbaani mumkin na ho to badarjahe majboori do saal se kam 'umr bakre wa bakri par Qurbaani ki gunjaish hai. Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare

'Aqeeqah Ke Masa-il

Sawal: 'Aqeeqah me bakra kitne saal ka hona chahiye?

Jawab: 'Aqeeqah ke liye jaanwar se muta-'alliq wahi ahkaam hain jo Qurbaani ke jaanwaron me hain. imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek 'Aqeeqah me bhi Qurbaani ki tarah do saal ka bakra hona zaroori hai (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/217). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: 'Aqeeqah karna kahan afzal hai bairun me jahan family ke saath kama raha hai ya apne mulk mein?

Jawab: Jahan family ke saath rehta ho wahaan kare (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/218). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya bhai ya bahan apne bhai ki aulad ka 'Aqeeqah kar sakte hain?

Jawab: 'Aqeeqah karna sunnat hai aur yeh baap ke zimme hota hai lekin agar baap ki istita'at aur taaqat na ho to us ke rishtedaar us ki ijaazat se us ki aulad ka 'Aqeeqah kar sakte hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/210). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Qurbaani ke bar'aks 'Aqeeqah ka gosht maaldaar ko diya jaaye to wo jaise chahe tasarruf kar sakta hai, 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke is ka khulaasah kare?

Jawab: Tamam ahkaam me jo hukm Qurbaani ka hai wahi hukm 'Aqeeqah ka bhi hota hai kuch masa-il me 'Aqeeqah ke ahkaam thode se alag ho jate hain jaise:

Zabah Karne Ka Sahi Tareeqa

Sawal: Kya murgi, bakra wagayrah zabah karne ki koi khas du'aa hai? Agar koi musalmaan sahi tareeqe se murgi zabah kare lekin du'aa ya Bismillah na padhe to kya wo zabah halaal hoga?

Jawab: Jaanwar ke zabah karne ki mustaqil koi du'aa nahi hai, albatta kuch cheezein mustahab hain jaise taujeeh padhna, daroode paak padhna aur Bismillah padhna. Shawafe' ke nazdeek zabihah ke halaal hone ke liye zabah karne wale ka musalmaan hona zaroori hai, Bismillah padhna zaroori nahi. Lihaaza koi musalmaan Bismillah padhe bagair zabah kare to zabah kiya huwa jaanwar halaal hoga. Lekin chunke deegar masaalik me bismillah padhna zaroori hai is liye un ki ri'aayat me ehtiyaatan bismillah padhna chahiye yeh mustahab 'amal hai. (Al-Iqna': 2/592, Al-Bayaan: 4/451, Al-Majmoo': 10/221). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Masheeni Zabeeha

Sawal: Kya Masheeni zabeeha halaal hota hai?

Jawab: Masheeni zabeeha me agar jaanwar ko zabah bhi masheen ke zariye se kiya jaaye to is qism ka masheeni zabeeha haraam aur murdaar hoga, albatta masheen ke us hisse me jahan jaanwar zabah hota hai musalmaan ya kitaabi ko khadha kiya jaaye aur musalmaan ya kitaabi shara'ee tareeqa se jaanwar ko zabah kare aur baaqi sara kaam masheen se ho aur mundarijah zail sharaait ka bhi lihaaz rakhkha jaaye to is soorat me masheeni zabeeha halaal hoga.

Mundarijah baala sharaait me se koi ek shart bhi na payi jaaye to masheen se zabah hone wala jaanwar murdaar aur haraam hoga. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 3/251).

(Al-Majmoo': 9/75, Al-Waseet: 7/143, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 3/44, Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 4/2761, Al-Waseet: 7/41). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

TIJAARAT WA MULAZAMAT KE MASA-IL

Cheezon Ke Daam Kam Hon Us Waqt Zakheerah Karna

Sawal: Bazaar me kisi cheez ka daam kam ho us waqt use ziyadah miqdaar me khareed kar zakheerah kare aur jab daam badh jaaye tab beche. Kya sharee'at me is ki ijaazat hai?

Jawab: Sharee'at me maal zakheerah karne se mana'a kiya gaya hai. 'Ulama ne is ka matlab yeh likkha hai ke jab bazaar me us maal ki qillat ho aur 'awaam ko us ki zaroorat ho to mana'a hai. Lekin agar bazaar me maal bahut ho aur koi us waqt maal ka zakheera kare aur baad me ziyadah raqam se beche to koi haraj nahi hoga (Sunan At-Tirmizi:1267, Fatawa an-Nawawi). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sone Ki Sone Ke Saath Len Den

Sawal: Kya sone ka sone ke saath len den jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Sone ki sone ke saath khareed wa farokht sahi hone ke liye teen sharaait hain: (1) Sona dono taraf barabar ho. (2) Mu'aamalah naqd ho Udhar na ho. (3) Juda hone se pehle hi har ek apne sone par qabza kar le (Asnal-Matalib: 2/22). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar koi sone ke dukaan par pooraana sona bech kar naya sona lene jaaye to us ka sahi tariqaah kya hai?

Jawab: Sone ki sone ke saath khareed wa farokht sahi hone ke liye teen sharaait hain jis me se ek shart yeh hai ke “Sona dono taraf baraabar ho.”

'Aam taur par log puraane sone ka wazan karke naya sona lete hain aur phir us ke wazan ke mutaabiq qeemat me jo farq ho dete ya lete hain, yeh jaaiz nahi hai. Misaal ke taur par 100 grams sona dukandaar ko de kar us ke badle naya sona kam wa besh 80 grams liya jaata hai yeh tareeqah durust nahi, albatta us ke liye jaaiz tareeqaye kaar yeh hai ke awwalan puraane sone ko rupayon ke badle me becha jaaye ya'ani puraane sone ki qeemat haath me li jaaye phir un rupayon se naya sona khareeda jaaye. Tuhfatul-Bari me yeh mas-alah tafseel ke saath likkha hai (Fiqhul Manhaji: 6/94). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Haraam Maal Se Tijaarat

Sawal: Agar kisi shakhs ke bare me yaqeen hai ke us ka poora ka poora maal haraam hai to aise shakhs se tijaarati ya kisi qism ka maali mu'aamalah karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Aise shakhs se kisi bhi qism ka len den karna jaaiz nahi (Al-Bayaan: 2/22). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar saamne wale ke maal ke haraam hone ka shak ho is wajaah se ke kuch hissa halal maal se kama raha ho aur kuch haraam tijarat se in sooraton me kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Aise shakhs se len den karna aur us ki da'awat, hadiyah wagayrah qabool karna makrooh hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 7/337). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Jaaiz hone ki wajaah kya hai? Kyun ke halaal ki bahut ahmiyat hai?

Jawab: Us ke maal me halaal maujood hai, is liye jaaiz hai, agar yeh jaaiz na ho to log dunya me mu'aamalaat kis tarah kar paayenge? Aksar logon ke maal me kam wa besh haraam maal mila huwa hai, lekin yeh baat malhooz rahe ke agar koi aysi cheez de jo yaqeeni taur par us ne haraam tareeqe se li hai to us ka lena jaaiz nahi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 7/337, Asnal-Matalib: 2/227). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar koi musalmaan jahaalat ya be deeni ki wajaah se haraam tareeqe se maal kamaaye lekin 'ilm ho jaane ke baad apne us 'amal se taubah kare aur usi haasil shudah mukammal maale Haraam se halaal tijaarat shuru' kare to kya is soorat me ab us halaal tijaarat se kamaaya huwa maal halaal hoga?

Jawab: Wo maal jis ka Husool aise tareeqe se huwa ho jo Sharee'at ki ru se najaaiz aur galat hai, to wo shakhs us maal ka maalik nahi hoga. Aise maal ka hukm yeh hai ke use asal maalik ke hawaale kiya jaaye us waqt jabke maalik ka 'ilm ho lekin agar saahibe maal ka pata lagaane se aajiz ho jaaye to us maal ke asal maalik ki taraf se sadaqah karne ka qasd aur niyyat kar ke kaare khair me kharch kar de, yeh waajib hai. Yeh shakhs jis ke qabze me filhaal maale haraam hai, us maal se kisi bhi tareeqe se intifa (faaidah) haasil nahi kar sakta. Lihaaza soorate mas-uoolah me us shakhs ko is baat ki ijaazat nahi hogi ke wo is maale haraam ko tijaarat wagayrah me lagaaye, kyunke wo is ka maal nahi hai. Lekin is ke baawajood agar wo is maale haraam se tijaarat wagayrah karta hai to us se aane wali aamadni us ke liye halaal hogi. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 10/7945, Al-Majmoo': 14/374, Asnal-Matalib: 2/357). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muzaarabat (Ek Ka Maal Doosre Ki Mehnat)

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs apni kuch raqam is shart ke saath kisi ke hawaale kare ke wo us raqam ke zariye tijaarat kare aur nafa'a dono ke darmiyan rahe to kya yeh shar'an durust hai?

Jawab: Ji han! Use muzaarabat kehte hain ke saahibe maal apna maal kisi 'aamil ke hawaale kare jo usko tijaarat me lagaaye aur us se haasil hone wala nafa'a dono ke darmiyaan taqseem ho. Lekin is me dono ke darmiyaan taqseem hone wale nafa'a ki sharah ibtida hi me waazeh hona zaroori hai (Al-Iqna': 2/341). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Gair Muslimon Se Tijaarat Me Shirkat

Sawal: Kya gair muslimon ke saath bataure shirkat tijaarat jaaiz hai, jabke is tijaarat me ziyadah haq usi gair muslim ka ho, ya'ani faislah lene ka asal ikhtiyaar usi ke haath me ho?

Jawab: Aqde shirkat ke sahi hone ke liye shareek ka musalman hona shart nahi, lekin daurane mu'aamalah koi faasid (invalid) shart lagaayi jaaye to mu'aamalah faasid ho jaayega, mazkoorah soorat mei chunke faislah lene ka bunyaadi ikhtiyaar kisi ek ko diya gaya ya'ani doosre shareek ko tasarruf ka mukammal ikhtiyaar nahi diya gaya is sharte faasid ki wajaah se shirkhat ka mazkoorah mu'aamalah sahi nahi hoga. (Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 1/626). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Neelaami (Auction)

Sawal: Neelaami (Auction) ki Sharee'at me kya haisiyat hai?

Jawab: Agar koi sharte faasid (invalid) naa paayi jaaye to jaaiz hai, basoorate neelaami bai karna bai (kharido farokht) sahi ki ek qism hai. hadees shareef aur kutube fiqh wa fataawah me is ka jawaaz mazkoor hai (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 13/126). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya darakhton par phal aane se pehle neelaami (Auction) karna durust hai?

Jawab: Darakhton par phal aane se pehle neelaami (Auction) karna najaaiz aur baatil hai, jis ki hadees me mumaana'at aayi hai (Saheeh Al-Bukhari, Al-Majmoo': 11/410). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Qabristaan ya isi tarah gaon ki jamaa-'at ke aam (mango) ke darakhton ke phalon ki phoolon ke aane ke baad (Aam lagne se pehle) neelaami karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Hadeese paak me dhoke ka mu'aamalah aur bai (kharido farokht) karne ki mumaana'at aayi hai, chunke aam (mango) ke darakhton par bahut sare phool lagte hain jis me se kaseer ta'adaad me phool jal kar ya hawa ki wajaah se jhadh kar gir jaate hain is liye haqeeqat me kitne aam aayenge is ka andaazah nahi lagaaya ja sakta ya'ani dhoke ka andeshah hota hai is liye yeh jaaiz nahi. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 4/145). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mineral Water Ki Tijaarat

Sawal: Kya Mineral water ki tijaarat kar sakte hain? Kyunke yeh Allah ki ek bahut badi ni'emat hai jo har jaandaar ke zindah rahne ke liye bunyadi zaroorat hai.

Jawab: Paani jab tak asal jagah ya'ani 'aam talaab ya 'aam kunwen ya 'aam Borewell wagayrah me ho, to koi us ka maalik nahi hota, har shakhs use iste'emaal kar sakta hai, kisi ko roka nahi ja sakta lekin agar kisi shakhs ne kisi tadbeer se paani apne qabze aur milkiyat me le liya, masalan talaab se kisi ne Can ya Tanker me bhar liya, ya Borewell se nikaal kar Tank (Tanki) ya Hauz me bhar liya isi tarah wo kunwen ya Borewell us ki zaati milkiyat me ho to wo shakhs us paani ka maalik hai. Ab us ke liye us paani ka farokht karna durust hoga. Lihaaza in sharton ke saath Mineral water ki tijaarat shar'an jaaiz hogi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 5/312). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zam Zam Ka Paani Bechna

Sawal: Kya zam zam ka paani bechna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Zam Zam ke kunwen se jab paani baahar nikaal liya jaaye aur apne qabze aur milkiyat me le liya jaaye to ab use bechna jaaiz hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 5/312, Fatawa ash-Shabakatul-Islamiyyah: 12/475). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jaanwaron Ke Khilawnon Ki Tijaarat

Sawal: Kya plastic, rabber, kapde wagayrah ke bane huwe jaanwaron (Bhaalu, sher aur Billi wagayrah) ke khilawnon ki tijaarat karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Bhalu, sher aur billi wagayrah jaanwaron ki shakl ke jo khilawne market me farokht hote hain, aise khilawnon ki khareed wa farokht durust na hogi, musalmaanon ko aise kaarobaar se bachna chaahiye. (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 3/568). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Billi (Cat) Ki Khareed Wa Farokht

Sawal: Kya Shar'an Billi (Cat) ko paalna aur us ki khareed wa farokht karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Hazrat Abdur Rehman Bin Sakhar (Rz) ka laqab Abu Hurairah isi wajah se pada tha ke wo billi ke bachche ko hamesha apne paas rakhte the, isi tarah Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke yeh napaak nahi hai yeh to tumhaare paas aane jaane waalon me se hai lihaaza is ke paalne me aur khareed wa farokht karne me koi haraj nahi. (Tirmiz Shareef: 92, Al-Majmoo': 9/229). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Baaz log is hadees ko buniyaad bana kar ke jis me Hazrat Jaabir (Rz) farmaate hain ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne kutte aur billi ki qeemat lene se mana'a kiya hai mutlaqan billi ki khareed wa farokht ko najaaiz qaraar dete hain is ki haqeeqat kya hai maslake Shafi'ee me?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Billi ki khareed wa farokht karna jaaiz hai. Muslim Shareef me billi ki qeemat ko jo mana'a kiya gaya hai us se jangli billi muraad hai jo insaanon se unsiyat ya Mohabbat nahi karti aur wo nafa'a bakhsh bhi nahi hoti is maqsad se koi jangli billi paalta bhi nahi aur unsiyat to paltu billi se hoti hai lihaaza paltu billi khareedna bechana dono jaaiz hai kyunke yeh paak jaanwar hai, aur nafa'a bakhsh bhi, lihaaza us ki khareed wa farokht jaaiz hai. (Saheehul Jaame'e: 6950, Al-Majmoo': 9/230). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muqarrarah Nafa'a Par Maal Lagaana

Sawal: Kya kisi company me ya kisi dost ki tijaarat me maahaanah muqarrarah nafa'a par maal lagana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Muqarrarah Nafa'a par kisi bhi tijaarat par maal lagaana jaaiz nahi hai. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 3/515). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Andaazeh Se Nafa'a

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs apne dost ki tijaarat me maal lagaaye, dost ka taamiraati kaarobaar (Construction Business) hai aur jis me aksar nafa'a hi hota hai, us ka dost har maheene hisaab karta aur apne andaaze se use har maheena nafa'a deta hai jo taqreeban haqeeqi nafa'a ke qareeb hota hai. Kya is tarah karna durust hai?

Jawab: Shirkat me nafa'a ka ta'ayyun lagaayi gai raqam ke tanaasub se hona zaroori hai, ya'ani agar nafa'a huwa to is nafa'a ka 40% fulaan shareek ka hoga jabke is shareekekaar ka asal maal me 40% laga ho aur doosre ka 20% aur teesre ka 40% jabke bittarteeb dono shareek ka raasul maal / asal muddal (principal amount) 20 aur 40 feesad maal laga ho, aur nuqsaan ki soorat me bhi usi tanaasub se nuqsaan bhi bardaasht karna padega, mahaz andaaze se raqam muqarrar karke nafa'a dena durust nahi (Al-Mo'atamad: 3/312). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khareedaar Se Kitna Munaafa Lena Chaahiye

Sawal: Islami Sharee'at ke mutaabiq khareedaar se kitna munaafa lena jaaiz hai? Kyunke bahut saari tijaaraton me nafa'a ki sharah ziyadah hoti hai?

Jawab: Sharee'at me nafa'a lene ki koi had (limit) muta-'ayyan nahi hai, insaani aur akhlaaqi qadron ki ri'aayat ke saath jitna chaahe nafa'a le sakte hain basharte ke jhoot na bolein aur gaahak ko dhokah na dein, albatta giza ka buhraan aur kami ho to is ki qeemat badha kr bechna ya kisi ki majboori se faidaah utha kar koi bhi cheez itne ziyadah nafa'a par bechna jis ko 'urf me “Gabane faahish” samjha jaata ho, yeh murawwat ke khilaaf hai, is se bachna behtar hai, aap apne yahaan ka 'urf dekh kar khud faisla kar len ke is kaam me lage huwe imaandaar taajiron ke yahan yeh gabane faahish (severe price manipulation) hai ya nahi. Kisi ki majboori se faaida utha kar bahut ziyadah nafa'a lene se bachna behtar hai. (Fatawa ash-Shabakatul-Islamiyyah: 12/1028). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tax Se Bachne Ke Liye Bagair Bill Ke Khareed Wa Farokht

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Income tax wagayrah se bachne ke liye bill ke bagair khareed wa farokht karta hai lekin wo apni poori zakaat ada karta hai. Kya aisa karna sahi hai?

Jawab: Baaz tax hukoomat ki taraf se zaalimaanah hote hain ya'ani is ka koi faaidah us aadmi ko nahi hota jaise income tax, sales tax, ya custom duty wagayrah. Income tax ek nawaajibi tax hai, aur nawaajib had (limit) tak wasool kiya jaata hai. Lihaaza shar'an is tarah khareed wa farokht karna jaaiz hai. (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband: 147365). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Expired Saamaan Bechna

Sawal: Kya expired cosmetic saamaan jaise shampoo, saaban, toothpaste, body spray wagayrah bechna jaaiz hai? Yeh saamaan sasta milta hai aur bahut se logon ka kahna hai ke expiry ke baad bhi 3 maah (months) cosmetic saamaan istemaal kar sakte hain kyunke har company ihtiyaatan expiry taareekh kam likhti hai.

Jawab: Insaani istemaal wali khane peene ki ashya par un ke istemaal aur baratne ki jo aakhri taareekh likhi hoti hai, tibbi e'etibaar se ehtiyaat is me hai ke wo cheezein is se pehle istemaal kar li jaayein warnah tayyarshudah khane, peene ya istemaal wa baratne ki cheezain muta-'ayyanah taareekh ke baad istemaal karne se nuqsaan ka andeshaah rahta hai. Market me jo log expired cheezain farokht karte Hain wo ya to bhole bhaale khareedaaron ko dhokah dete hain ya kisi tadbeer se un par doosri taareekhein daal dete hain aur dono hi sooraton me jhoot aur dhokah dahi paya jaata hai, is liye logon ko dhokah de kar aur amali taur se 'ayb ko zaahir kar diya jaaye aur munaasib daam par farokht kiya jaaye to yeh durust ho sakta hai lekin chunke qanooni e'etibaar se expired cheezon ki khareed wa farokht mana'a hai, is liye is tarah ki cheezein farokht karne se bachna zaroori hai. (Nihaayatul Mutlab: 5/283). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kaam Karne Se Pehle Mazdoori Dena

Sawal: Kya kisi peshaawar mazdoor ko kisi tay shudah kaam ki ujrat us kaam ke karne se pehle de dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Mazdoor ko kaam mukammal hone se pehle mazdoori dena bila karaahat jaaiz hai. (Fathur-Rahmaan: 682). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shirkat Me Makaan Khareedna

Sawal: Kya kisi ke saath shirkat me makaan khareedna jaaiz hai? Aadhi qeemat ek shakhs ada kare aur aadhi dosra?

Jawab: Shirkat me makaan khareedna aur deegar halaal kaarobaar karna shar'an jaaiz hai. (Fiqhul Manhaji: 7/58). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qiston Par Internet Ki Line Lena

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs Internet ki line leta hai to use do saal tak har maheene me 20 deenaar qist dena padega. Is package ke badle do saal tak use har maheene 500 GB internet milega. Use do sim cards meleinge, ek router/wireless network ke liye aur ek zaa-id (excess) sim card jahan chahe istemaal kare, is package ke badle use Ipad ya iphone muft milega, agar bahut achcha ipad ya iphone chaahiye to use shuru'aat me 29 Kuwaiti Dinar (KD) zaa-id (excess) dena padega. 'umooman ipad iphone ki qeemat 100, 200 KD ke beech me hoti hai jo use is internet scheme se muft ya bahut sasta mil raha hai. Kya is tarah internet ki line lena aur saath mei ipade ya iphone lena durust hai agar wo shakhs waqt par bil bhare aur is par koi sood na lage?

Jawab: Haraj nahi basharte ke soodi len den na ho (Al-Majmoo': 9/145). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Upar ke mas-alah me kisi ne e'atiraaz kiya ke udhaar lena sahi nahi wo bhi do saal ke liye is ki wazaahat kare.

Jawab: Udhaar lenden Sharee'at me jaaiz hai. Shart yeh hai ke dono ek saath udhaar na ho ya'ani cheez aur uski qeemat ya to cheez udhaar ho ya paise udhaar ho. Lihaaza mazkoorah baala mas-alah me cheez mil rahi hai, paise udhaar hai to jaaiz hoga. Is ke bar'aks taajir agar iphone deta hai to yeh us ki taraf se gift hoga jaise Big Bazaar me gaahak ko mutawajjah karne ke liye tel ke dabbe ke saath kabhi saaban ya shaker di jaati hai, lihaaza asal paise company to intenet line ke le rahi hai. Wallahu A'alam (Bahrul-Mazhab: 4/357). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qiston (Installemnt) Par Sona (Gold) Khareedna

Sawal: Gold ki dukaan me ek scheme ke tahat gaahak ko har maheene ek muta-'ayyan qist (Installment) deni padhti hai (masalan 25,50,100 Kuwaiti Dinar) jis din wo maheene ki qist (Installment) ada karega us din gold ki qeemat ke hisaab se us ke account me utni miqdaar gold ki likh di jati hai isi tarah har maheene gold ki qeemat ke hisaab se us ke account me gold ki miqdaar likh di jati hai. ek saal mukammal hone ke baad jitni miqdaar gold ki account me likh di jaati hai, utna gold use mil jaata hai, chahe us waqt gold ki qeemat ziyadah ho ya kam ho aur saath me use ek qist ke baraabar kupan in'aam me diya jaata hai, chahe wo use Marketing charges dene me istemaal kare ya us qeemat se us din ke gold ki qeemat ke hisaab se gold khareede.

Agar zaiwar ka wazan ziyadah hoto zaa-id (excess) wazan ke badle use us din ke gold ki qeemat ke hisaab se qeemat deni hogi neez scheme beech me band kar sakta hai, scheme jab band karega us waqt jitni gold amount me register hoga utna le sakta hai lekin in'aami kupan nahi milega. Kya is tarah mu'aamalah karna jaaiz hoga?

Jawab: Mazkoorah mu'aamalah jaaiz hai, kyunke currency ya rupayon ke badle sone ya chaandi ko udhaar khareedna aur bechna jaaiz hai, basharte ke rupayon paison aur sona chaandi me se kisi ek par majlis e aqd hi me qabza kar liya gaya ho.

(Kupan ki soorat me dukaandar ki taraf se) in'aam ki peshkash karna Sharee'at ki ru se jaaiz hai yeh saamaan ki khareedaari par logon ko ubhaarne aur ragbat dilaane ke liye dukaandaar ki taraf se ek tarah ka atiyyah hai, Hazrat Mufti Taqee Sahab 'Usmani (MZA) farmaate hain: “Bande ke khayaal me yeh in'aam “z'iyaadat fil mabee (woh izafee cheez ya faidah jo farokht ki gai cheez ke saath shaamil ho)” nahi hai, balke ek hiba mubtadiya ka ek taraf wa'ada hai, baaye'e (seller) aur mushtari (buyer) me se koi bhi use “z'iyaadat fil mabee” nahi samajhta, na 'urfe 'aam me use “z'iyaadat fil mabee” samajha jaata hai aur shar'an bhi yeh zaroori nahi hai ke bai taam hone ke baad jab bhi baaye' mushtari ko koi cheez bila qeemat de to use “z'iyaadat fil mabee” qaraar diya jaaye. (Fatawa 'Usmani: 3/257, Kitaabul Buyoo'a: Zakariya Deoband, Saheeh Muslim: 1585, Al-Majmoo': 13/4). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Government Contract Ke Liye Rishwat Dena

Sawal: Ek Shakhs ki Construction Company hai, use itne private contract milte hain jis ke munaafa se uska guzara hota hai. Kya aisa shakhs governemt ke bade contract jo ke lakhon ya karodon rupayon me hote hain, un ko haasil karne ke liye rishwat de sakte hai? Kyunke Hindustaan me bina rishwat ke government ka contract milna taqreeban namumkin hota hai?

Jawab: Islam me rishwat lena dena dono najaaiz hai, lekin apna waajib haq haasil karne ke liye bawaqte zaroorat jab rishwat lena laazimi ho jaaye to dena jaaiz hai, mazkoorah soorat me Tender lene wale ko qawi yaqeen ho ke is kaam ko wo amaanatdaari se karega dosre shakhs ke haathon chala jaaye to wo khayaanat kar sakta hai to is soorat me hukoomat ko bade khasaare se bachaane ki niyyat se rishwat de kar tender haasil karne ki gunjaish mil sakti hai warna nahi. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 16/283). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ko Tijaarat Ke Muaamale Me Ameer Banaana

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ko tijaarat ke muaamale me ameer banaana durust hai?

Jawab: Shar'an jaaiz to hai magar 'aurat naaqisul aql hoti hai is liye behtar hai ke use zimmedaar na banaaye (Al-Majmoo': 18/300). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Lottery Nikaalna

Sawal: Lottery nikaalna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Lottery nikaalna juwa hai, jo islaam me haraam hai. Yeh shaitaani 'amal hai, Qur-aane majeed ne juwa se mana'a kiya hai. Lottery me bade bade in'aamat ka laalach de kar lakhon ticket farokht kiye jate hain aur ticketon ke zariye jo raqam jam'a hoti hai, in me se qura'a andaazi ke zariye chand lakh rupaye taqseem kar diye jaate hain, lihaaza yeh logon ke saath zulm hai aur mahaz ittifaaqi amr par is ki taqseem hoti hai, jo najaaiz hai (Al-Azeez: 4/67). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kisi Bhi Muaamale Me Feesadi (%) Se Commission Lena

Sawal: Kya kisi bhi muaamale me feesadi (%) se commission lena jaaiz hai? Madaris ke safeer ko commission dena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Kisi bhi muaamale me feesadi (%) ki nisbat se 'iwaz (badle me) muqarrar karna sahi nahi hai, misaal ke taur par mukhtalif idaaron me chanda jama'a karne waalon ke liye un ki jamaashudah raqam se feesadi ke e'etibaar se 'iwaz (badle me) dena, wakeel ko case jitaane par munaafa ki qeemat par feesad ke e'etibaar se 'iwaz (badle me) dena, isi tarah madaaris ke safeer ko jamaashuda raqam par feesad ke hisaab se mu'aavazah dena wagayrah, albatta is ka jaaiz tareeqah yeh hai ke kaam shuru' karne se pehle hi dono ke ittifaaq se ek mu'aavazah muta-'ayyan kiya jaaye. Lihaaza 'amal anjam dene ke baad wo shakhs is mu'aavazah ka mustahiq hoga, madaaris ke safeer ke liye bhi yahi hukm hoga. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 878). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

International Hotel Ke Front Desk Pe Naukari Karna

Sawal: Agar koi international hotel ke front desk (Reception) pe kaam karta hai lekin us hotel me sharaab bikti ho to kya wo naukari us ke liye sahi hai? Kya us ki rozi halaal hogi? 'Ulama se guzaarish hai upar ke mas-alah ka hal bataayein.

Jawab: Agar us shakhs ko sharaab ke len den me ya sharaab ke bill ke len den me ya hisaab rakhne me shaamil na hona pade to mere naaqis khayaal me is ki kamaai halaal hogi lekin us ke liye behtar yeh hai ke wo koi dosra zariye maash ikhtiyaar kare. Wallahu A'alam. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Agar sharaab pilaana us ka kaam na ho isi tarah us hotel me sharaab ke 'alaawah aur halaal aur pakeezah cheez bechi ja rahi ho jin ki aamdani (Revenue) sharaab ki aamdani se ziyadah ho to koi haraj nahi. Yeh jaaiz hone ke e'etibaar se hai lekin taqwah baharhaal ehtiyaat baratne me hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Oil Aur Gas Ki Badi Service Companiyon Ke Saath Kaam Karna

Sawal: Kya oil aur gas ki badi service companiyon (Baker Hughes, Schlumberger, Newpark, Halliburton) ke saath kaam karna jaaiz hai? In companiyon me kisi na kisi darjah me soodi len den hota hai.

Jawab: Soodi mu'aamalah aur soodi len den Qur-aan wa hadees ki ru se haraam hai. Neez islam me jis tarah sood lena haraam wa najaaiz hai, usi tarah sood dena bhi haraam wa najaaiz hai aur ahaadeese mubaarakah me dono par wa'eedein waarid hui hain, (Sooratul Baqarah: 275-278, Sunan At-Tirmizi: 1206) soodi mu'aamalah dunya wa aakhirat ki tabaahi aur barbaadi zillat aur ruswaayi ka sabab hai, is ke 'alaawah kuch nahi, 'umooman jo log sood par qarz le kar kaarobaar karte hain sari zindagi sood se picha chudhaane me guzar jati hai, balke ba-kasrat aise waaqiyaat pesh aate hain ke sarmaaya manaafa sab se haath dhona pad jaata hai. Is liye company ka soodi mu'aahadah (agreement) karna aur sood ki bunyaad par qarzah lena najaaiz aur haraam hai. Lekin agar company ka kaarobaar (business) jaaiz aur halaal umoor par mushtamil ho aur aap ke supurd kaam bhi jaaiz ho to aap ke liye is company me mulaazamat karna aur us company se apne kaam ki tankhwah lena jaaiz hai. Faqat Wallahu A'alam (Darul Iftah'a: Jami'ah Al 'Uloomul Islamiyyah Binnori Town). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Oil Field Me Land Ya Offshore (Samandar) Rig Par Kaam Karna

Sawal: Ek shakhs oil field me land ya offshore (samandar ke andar) rig par kaam karta hai, wahaan jaane ke baad wo kam se kam 28 din ya 28 dino se ziyadah rahtah hai, phir chuttiyan guzaarne ke liye apne watan laut aata hai, rig par kaam karne ke dino me wo Jumu'ah ki namaz ada nahi kar sakta kyunke maslake Shafi'ee ke mutaabiq Jumu'ah ke sahi hone ke sharaait wahaan paaye nahi jate, jabke Jumu'ah ki namaz farze ain hai, Sawal yeh hai ke kya is soorat me aysi jag'hon par naukari karna jaaiz hai? Jahan namaze Jumu'ah faut ho jaati ho aur kya is naukari se haasil hone waali aamdani halaal hogi?

Jawab: Sahi Ahaaddees mei haraam maal se ijtinaab aur halaal rozi talaash karne aur haasil karne ki targeeb di gai hai. Chunke ahl-o-'ayaal ka naan wa nafqa shohar par farz hai, is bina par ek qaul ke mutaabiq rozi haasil karne ke liye ghar se baahar nikalna bhi waajib hai aur farz, lihaaza halaal rozi ki talaash me musalsal safar karna, ya aise maqaam par rahna naaguzeer ho jaaye, jahan Jumu'ah ke sahi hone ke sharaait paaye na jaate hon to koi haraj nahi. Aysi jagaah par us shakhs ka naukari karna bhi sahi aur jaaiz hai aur us naukari se haasil hone wali aamdani bhi halaal hai. (Al-Mustadrak alas Saheehain: 2166, Nihaayatul Matlab:15/522). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Mai kuwait me oil field me land rig par kaam karta hoon wahaan jaane ke baad kam se kam 8 din rahta hoon jahan Jumu'ah ke sharaait nahi paaye jaate. 20-25 misri aur Pakistaani log mil kar Jumu'ah ki namaz ada karte hain kyunke imaam Abu Haneefah ke nazdeek Jumu'ah ke liye imaam ke 'alaawah teen logon ka hona shart hai is liye mai bhi un ke saath Jumu'ah ada karta hoon ho sakta hai ke Allah qabool kare hattah ke imaamat bhi mai karta hoon, lekin imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ki taqleed ki wajaah se namaze Jumu'ah ke baad mai ehtiyaatan zuhr ki chaar rak'at ada karta hoon kya is tarah ada ki huee Jumu'ah ki namaz kisi bhi imaam ke nazdeek durust hogi kyun ke wahaan basti ya shahar nahi paaya jata?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah me Aimmaa-e-Arba'ah (Rh) me se kisi ke nazdeek bhi Jumu'ah sahi nahi hogi, is liye ke imaam Shafi'ee aur imaam Ahmad ke nazdeek Jumu'ah sahi hone ke liye chaalees logon ka hona zaroori hai, neez imaam Shafi'ee ke nazdeek gaon ya shahar ke muqeem hazaraat se Jumu'ah mun'aqid hoti hai aur imaam Abu Haneefah (rh) ke nazdeek shahar me Jumu'ah qaa-im ki jaaye aur kam se kam chaar logon ka hona zaroori hai aur Imaam Malik (rh) ke nazdeek basti ka hona shart hai jahaan logon ke darmiyaan khareed wa farokht huwa karti ho, koi khaas 'adad zaroori nahi hai. Magar teen chaar logon se sahi nahi hogi, jaise ke imaam Nawawi (rh) ka bayan hai. (Al-Majmoo': 4/422). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Part Time Karna

Sawal: Agar koi baironi mulk me naukari kar raha hai, kya wo apni duty ke baad part time kar sakta hai ek shakhs ne kaha ke hum ne wahaan ke Labour Law ko qabool kiya ke hum isi ke liye kaam karenge, phir dosra kaam kaise kar sakte hain? Mere khayaal se agar wo jis kaam / zimmedaari ki tankhwah le raha hai wo waqt poora aur kaam poora kare to baaqi wo apne guzaare ke liye kuch alag kaam kare to kya haraj hai?

Jawab: Bazaahir aaj kal jo is tarah ka qaanoon labour logon par laga kar un se dastakhat li jaati hai, yeh zabardasti aur zaalimaanah hai is liye agar koi dosra kaam bhi kare to is me koi haraj nahi. Jitne ghante ki us ki duty hai wo karne ke baad wo aazad hai, is par paabandi lagaane ka kisi ko haq nahi. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

School Me Principal Ka Uhdah Qabool Karne Ka Hukm Jabke Wahan Kuch Gair Shara'ee umoor Anjaam Diye Ja Rahe Hon

Sawal: Mai sarkaari school me 22 saal se ustaad hun aur society mujhe ab principal banaana chaahti hai. Mas-alah yeh hai ke school ke asaatazah waqtan fawaqtan bank ka soodi qarz lete rahte hain. Bank in qarz khwahon se bataure waseeqah (guarantee) aur e'etimaad aur kuch cheezon ki zaroori ma'alumaat haasil karne ke liye school management se kuch dastaavezaat ka mutaalabah karti hai, lihaaza principal ko teen khutoot jari karna laazim ho jaata hai jis me ek to mulaazamat ka khat (Employment letter) dosra tankhwah ka certificate (Salary Certificate) aur teesra Undertaking jis me yeh wazaahat hoti hai ke is ki tankhaw itni hai jis me se aap (bank) 40% tak qist ki raqam (Installment) kaat sakte hain. Yeh to haqeeqat bayani hoti hai, lekin is ke 'alaawah kuch gair shara'ee umoor bhi anjaam dene hote hain masalan saalaana Ijtima (Annual Gathering) jis me naach gaana hota hai, mardo zan ka ikhtilaat hota hai wagayrah lekin is mansab par rahte huye deegar kaarekhair bhi wajood me aa sakte hai aur kisi had (limit) tak khilaafe shara'a umoor par rok bhi lagaayi ja sakti hai. Kya Soorate mas-uoolah me principal ke is mansab ko qabool karna mere liye jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Soodi qarz ka mu'aamalah jo hazraat kar rahe hain ho sakta hai un me se kuch majboor hon aur kuch bila zaroorat hi le rahe hon, lihaaza yeh log khud is ke zimmedaar honge aur 'indallah muakhazah (Allah key waha pakad yaa saza) bhi inhin se hoga. Zimmedaar hone ki haisiyat se qaanoonan aap ma'azoor hain, aur jaisa ke aap ne wazaahat ki hai ke aap ki zimmedaari qabool karne ke baad dheere dheere khilaafe shara'a kaam band bhi ho sakte hain. To yeh shar'an mamdooh wa matloob cheez hai. Lihaaza aap achche azm aur tawakkul alalallah is mansab ko qabool kar sakte hain. Wallahu A'alam.

Chunaanche Allah Ta'aala ka paak irshaad hai ke koi bojh uthane waali jaan doosre ka bojh nahi uthaayegi ya'ani koi bojh uthaane waali jaan doosri jaan ka bojh nahi uthaayegi aur koi doosre ke gunah par pakda nahi jaayega.

Hazrat Anas Bin Maalik (Rz) se riwaayat hai, Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: “kuch log neki ki chaabiyan aur buraayi ke taale hote hain aur kuch log buraayi ki chabiyan aur neki ke taale hote hain. Us shakhs ko Mubaarak ho jis ke haath me Allah ne neki ki chaabiyan de dein aur us shakhs ke liye halaakat hai jis ke haath me Allah ne buraayi ki chaabiyan de dein.” (Kitaabus Sunnah: 297). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Jaaiz Haqq Haasil Karne Ke Liye Jhoot Ka Sahaara

Sawal: Mai ek National Oil Company me mulaazamat karta hun jis me har saal April me tankhwah (Salary Increment) hota hai, jis ki miqdar guzashtah saal ki kaarkardagi (Performance) par munhasir hoti hai jo ke 6.25% se le kar 8.33% hoti hai. Aksar tashkhish (Evaluation) me deri hoti hai lekin jab bhi milta hai, baqaaya (Arrears) se ya'ani April se milta hai. Mujhe meri guzashta saal (last year) ki kaarkardagi ki wajah se 8.33% izaafah mila tha jo ke manzoorshudah (Approved) hui thi jise mere dost ne apni aankhon se dekha tha, mujhe is se behtar mulaazamat ki peshkash (Job Offer) mili thi aur is izaafah (Salary Increament) ke pataa chalne ke baad mai ne Isteefah (Resignation) de diya kyunke mujhe yaqeen ho gaya ke is July ke maheene me mujhe 4 maheene ka izaafah milega. Lekin mu'aahadah (agreement) mohkamah (Contract Department) ne is par nazarsaani ke liye bheja aur kaha ke sirf 10% logon ko 8.33% de sakte hain, baqi logon ko 7.5% ya 6.25% diya jaaye, jaise hi main ne isteefah de diya, company ne mera izafah 8.33 se 0% kar diya.

Yeh to meri guzashtah saal ki mehnat par mila huwa izafah (Salary Increament) tha aur taakheer (delay) hone ki wajah se ab tak nahi mila tha, isi company ke ek sho'abah (Departmet) ke logon ko June ki tankhwah (Salary) me hi April se izafah (Salary Increament) mila.

Company join karte waqt saamaan ke Cargo ke liye taqreeban do lakh pachchees hazaar rupaye milte hain aur aksar log jhoota bil de kar use haasil karte hain lekin mai ne jhoota bill nahi banaaya is liye mujhe 1 bhi rupaya nahi mila kyunke bill shart hai. Company se isteefah dene ke baad bhi apna saamaan Cargo karne ke liye company taqreeban 2.25 lakh rupaye deti hai jis ke liye bill shart hai. Is waqt mai haqeeqat me samaan Cargo ke zariye bhejunga, kya mai haqeeqi / asal bill (Actual Bill) me 1.25 lakhs rupaye ka izaafah kar ke apne haq ka izaafah (Salary Increament) le sakta hun jo ke company ne mahaz mere isteefah dene ki wajah se cheen liya hai.

Increament haqeeqat me performance par munhasir hota hai lekin Reporting Manager ke haath me hai ke kis ko kitna dena hai? Jis ji sharah mukhtalif hoti hai jaise ke 8.33%, 7.5%, aur 6.25%. KOC taqreeban logon ko Increament milta hai, agar kisi ko na mila to is ki koi wajaah hoti hai jaise ke 1 saal mukammal nahi huwa ho ya Performance kharaab ho baaqi Reporting Manager ka aakhari faislah hoga, mujhe meri guzashtah saal ki kaarkardagi ki wajaah se 8.33% izaafah mila tha jo ke manzoorshudah (Approved) hui thi, jise mere dost ne apni aankhon se dekha tha lekin jaise hi mai ne isteefah de diya, company ne mera izafah 8.33% se 0% kar diya.

Jawab: Increament mulaazim ka ek haq hai jo hasbe zaabatah company ko dena chahiye. Soorate mas-uoolah me mulaazim ke istifah dene ki wajaah se us ka increament rok diya gaya jo ke galat hai jab ke mulaazim ne qanooni aur akhlaaqui taqaaze poore kiye hain is liye mulaazim apne is increament ko haasil karne ka haq rakhta hai, jis ko haasil karne ke liye wo koi bhi jaaiz zariya ikhtiyaar kar sakta hai albatta jhoot bol kar ya jhoota bill bana kar wasool karna jaaiz nahi taaham agar mulaazim ne aisa kar liya to increament ki raqam jaaiz hogi albatta galat tareeqa ikhtiyaar karne ka gunah hoga. (Jami'ah Darut-Taqwa Chobarchee Park Lahore, 20 Zilhijjah 1440/22 August 2019). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Naukari Ke Liye Jhoot Ka Sahaara Lena

Sawal: Ek aadmi kisi field me do teen saal ka tajrabah lene ke baad bairun mulk me kaam ke liye wo class join karta hai magar fail hone ki wajaah se kaheen aur private college se paise de kar asli certificate banaata hai aur is pe is ko kaam (Job) bhi milta hai aur apne ghar waalon ke m'aash ka zariyah banta hai, ab bahut saal ho gaye ab is ke tajrabah ki qadar hai certificate ki nahi. Kya is ki kamaai haraam hui agar hui to abhi kya kare?

Jawab: Aap ke andar salaahiyat aur isti'edaad hai aur aap us kaam ko qabool karke umdah tareeqah par anjaam dein to milne wali tankhwah par agarcha najaaiz ya haraam hone ka hukm to na hoga albatta naqli certificate lagaana gunah hai is liye ke yeh khilaafe qaanoon hone ke saath kizb wa khuda'a(dhoka) bhi hai is liye jaali kaam se ijtinaab kare. Wallahu A'alam (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya jhoota certificate banaane ki wajaah se use gunaah hoga?

Jawab: Ji gunaah to hoga, use chaahiye ke wo taubah kare. Kyunke yeh baat Rasoolullah (ﷺ) se saabit hai ke aap ne farmaya: “Jis ne hum se bad diyaanati (Dhokah) kiya, us ka hum se koi ta'alluq nahi.” (Saheeh Muslim: 102, Saheeh Ibne Hibban: 5559, Al-Majmoo': 12/31). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ek banda engineer hai jis ne Diploma kiya hai agar wo degree ka certificate haasil karega to use badi company me achchi tankhwah milegi aur aaj kal diploma ko koi ahmiyat nahi hai to kya kare? Aur kaam bhi janta hai ke is ko sirf certificate dikhaana hai kaam me koi tabdeeli nahi hogi kaam me mahir hai kya achchi tankhwah aur company ke liye aisa kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Agar pehle waali tankhwah me aafiyat se guzaarah ho raha hai to mahaz aishparasti aur dunya ki hawas me jhoot aur dhoke ka sahaara nahi lena chaahiye, chunaanche Hazrat Maulana Khalid Saifullah Rahmani Sahab tahreer farmaate hain ke “Galat certificate banaana gunah hai” kyunke yeh jhoot bhi hai aur dhoka bhi lekin chunke yeh ujrate 'amal aur mehnat ka mu'aawazah hai aur 'amal par ujrat haasil karna jaaiz wa halaal tareeqah hai is liye is ki aamdani halaal hi mutasawwar hogi na ke haraam, albatta use apne is jhoot par taubah wa istigfaar karte rehna chaahiye (Kitabul-Fatawa – Maulana Khalid Saifullah:5/394). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ek aadmi General Category mien hone ke baawajood Backward class ka certificate bana ke mulaazamat haasil karta hai, kya yeh maal us ke liye halaal hai ya haraam? Kya is mulaazamat se haasil hone waale maal se hajj kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Galat certificate banaana gunah hai kyunke yeh jhoot bhi hai aur dhokah bhi, lekin chunke yeh tankhwah us ke 'amal aur mehnat ki wajaah se hai aur 'amal par ujrat / tankhwah haasil karna jaaiz wa halaal tareeqa hai, is liye is ki aamdani halaal hi maqsood hogi na ke haraam, is liye is raqam se hajj karna durust hai, albatta ise apne us jhoot pe taubah karna chahiye. (Jadeed Fiqhi Masa-il – Maulana Khalid Saifullah). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Share Market: Ek Tahqeeqi Jaaizah

Chaar sharton ke saath shares ki khareed wa farokht jaaiz hai, lihaaza agar kisi shakhs ko Stock Market se shares khareedne ho to us ko un shares ki khareedaari ke liye chaar sharaait ka lihaaz rakhna zaroori hai.

Pehli Shart yeh hai ke wo company haraam kaarobaar me mulavvas na ho, masalan wo soodi bank na ho, sood aur qimaar (juwwa) par mabni insurances company na ho, sharaab ke kaarobaar me mulavvas na ho, ya un ke 'alaawah doosre haraam kaam karne waali company na ho, aysi company ke shares lena kisi haal me jaaiz nahi, na ibtida'a jaari (Float) hone ke waqt lena jaaiz hai aur na hi Stock Market se lena jaaiz hai.

Doosri Shart yeh hai ke is company ke tamaam asaase aur imlaak sayyaal asaason (Liquid Assets) ya'ani naqd raqam ki shakl me na ho balke is company ne kuch Fixed Assets haasil kar liye hon, masalan us ne building bana li ho, ya zameen khareed li ho, lihaaza agar is company ka koi Fixed Assets ujood me nahi aaya balke is ke tamam asaase abhi sayyaal (Liquid) ya'ani naqd raqam ki shakl me hain to is soorat me is company ke shares ko Face value se kam ya ziyadah (Above Par or Below par) me farokht karna jaaiz nahi balke baraabar saraabar khareedna zaroori hai warnah yeh sood ho jaayega.

Teesri Shart samajhne se pehle is baat ko jaanna zaroori hai ke aaj jitni companiyan is waqt qaa-im hain, in me se aksar companiyan aysi hain un ka bunyaadi karobar haraam to nahi hai, maslan textile company, automobile companiyan wagayrah lekin shaayad hi koi company aysi hogi jo kisi na kisi soodi kaarobar me mulavvas na ho, yeh companiyan do tareeqe se soodi kaarobaar me mulavvas hoti hain.

Pehla tareeqa yeh hai ke yeh companiyan fund badhaane ke liye bank se sood par qarz leti hain aur qarz se apna kaam chalaati hain. Doosra tareeqa yeh hai ke company ke paas jo zaa-id (excess) aur faazil (surplus) raqam hoti hai wo soodi account me rakhwaati hain aur is par wo bank se sood haasil karti hain, wo sood bhi un ki aamdani ka ek hissa hota hai. Lihaaza agar koi shakhs yeh chaahe ke mai aysi company ke shares khareedun jo kisi bhi tareeqe se kisi soodi kaarobaar me mulavvas na ho to yeh bahut mushkil hai. Ab Sawal yeh hai ke phir to kisi company ke shares ki khareed wa farokht bhi jaaiz nahi honi chaahiye?

Aysi companiyon ke bare me maujoodah daur ke 'Ulama-E-Kiraam ki rayein mukhtalif hain. 'Ulama ki ek jamaa-'at ka kehna hai ke chunke yeh companiyan haraam kaamon me mulavvas hain. Ab chahe tanaasub ke lihaaz se wo haraam kaam thoda hai lekin chunke haraam kaam kar rahi hain lihaaza ek musalmaan ke liye jaaiz nahi ke wo us company ke saath haraam kaam me hissadaar bane, is liye ke jab us ne shares khareed liya to wo us ke kaarobaar me shareek ho gaya. Aur kaarobaar ka ek shareek doosre shareek ka wakeel aur agent hai, ab goya ke shareholder in ko kaam ke liye agent bana raha hai ke tum soodi qarze aur soodi aamdani bhi haasil karo, is liye in 'Ulama ke nazdeek kisi company ke shares us waqt tak jaaiz nahi jab tak yeh itminaan na ho jaaye ke yeh company na sood leti hai na sood deti hai.

'Ulama-E-Kiraam ki doosri jamaa-'at ka yeh kehna hai ke agarcha in companiyon me yeh kharaabi (soodi len den) payi jati hai lekin is ke bawajood agar company ka bunyaadi kaarobaar majmooyi taur par halaal hai to do sharton ke saath us company ke shares lene ki gunjaaish hai.

Mufti Taqee 'Usmani (MZA) farmate hain ke Hakeemul Ummat Hazrat Maulana Ashraf Ali Sahab Thanvi (Rh) aur mere walid Hazrat Mufti Mohammed Shafi'e Sahab (Rh) ka yahi mauqaf hai aur in dono hazraat ki ittiba'a me bhi is mauqaf ko durust samjhta ho, wo do sharten yeh hain:

Pehli Shart yeh hai ke wo shareholder us company ke andar soodi kaarobaar ke khilaaf aawaz zaroor uthaye agarcha us ki aawaz mustarad (Overrule) ho jaaye aur mere nazdeek aawaz uthaane ka behtar tareeqa yeh hai ke company ki jo salana meeting (Annual General Meeting) hoti hai is me yeh aawaz uthaaye ke hum soodi len den ko durust nahi samajhte, soodi len den par raazi nahi hain, is liye is ko band kiya jaaye ab zaahir hai ke maujoodah haalaat me yeh aawaz naqqaar khane me toti ki aawaz hogi aur yaqeenan us ki yeh aawaz mustarad (Overrule) hogi, lekin jab wo yeh aawaz uthayega to Hazrat Thanvi (Rh) ke qaul ke mutaabiq aysi soorat me wo insaan apani zimmedaari poori ada kar deta hai.

Chauthi shart, jo haqeeqat me teesri shart ka ek hissa hai, wo yeh hai ke jab munafa'a (Dividend) taqseem ho, to wo shakhs Income Statement ke zariye yeh ma'aloom kare ke aamdani ka kitna feesad hissa soodi Deposit se haasil huwa hai, masalan farz keejiye ke us company ka kul aamdani ka 5% soodi deposite me raqam rakhwaane se haasil huwa hai, to ab wo shakhs nafa'a ka 5% hissa sadaqah karde.

Lihaaza company ka asal kaarobaar halaal hai lekin saath me wo company bank se soodi qarze leti hai ya apni zaa-id (excess) raqam soodi account me rakh kar us par sood haasil karti hai to is soorat me in mazkoora baala sharton par 'amal kar liya jaaye to phir aysi companiyon ke shares ki khareed wa farokht ki gunjaaish hai, aur mai samajhta hun ke yeh jawaaz ka mauqaf mu'atadil (balanced position) aur islami usoolon ke mutabiq hai, logon ke liye sahoolat ka raasta faraham karta hai.

Upar ki tafseel se yeh ma'aloom huwa ke shares ki khareed wa farokht ke jawaaz ke liye kul chaar sharten hogayein:

In chaar sharton ke saath shares ki khareed wa farokht jaaiz hai. (Fiqhi Maqaalaat-jild awwal, Mufti Muhammad Taqee 'Usmani: 144-151).

MAKAAN AUR DUKAAN KE MASA-IL

Daurane Ta'meer Apna Book Kiya Huwa Flat Bechna

Sawal: Kya ek shakhs jis ne kisi aysi imaarat me flat book kiya hai jis ki ta'meer ka kaam abhi mukammal nahi huwa hai kya dauraane ta'meer wo apna flat kisi aur ko bech sakta hai?

Jawab: Agar abhi building ta'meer hi nahi hui hai to khareedne waale shakhs ke liye us ka bechna jaaiz nahi hai kyunke jo cheez bechi jaaye us ka filjumla maujood hona zaroori hai, albatta agar us ki chat padh chuki ho aur us ke khareede huwe flats ki jo satah hogi khwah zameen ho ya koi chat wo maujood ho, diwarein aur makaan se muta-'alliq doosri zarooriyat maujood na hon to bahaalate maujoodah us ki qeemat muqarrar ho is ke lihaaz se farokht karna jaaiz hai is liye ke is had tak makaan wajood mien aa chuka hai. (Kitabul-Fatawa – Maulana Khalid Saifullah:5/272). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Makaan Ya Dukaan Ki Deposit Ko Isti'emaal Karna

Sawal: Aaj kal log dukaan ya makaan kiraaye par dete waqt deposit lete hain take agar dukaan ya makaan ke andar koi nuqsaan ho jaaye to us me se raqam isti'emaal kar ke durust kiya jaaye. Kya makaan ya dukaan maalik is deposit ko isti'emaal kar sakta hai?

Jawab: Kiraayedaar makaan ya dukaan maalik ko jo deposit deta hai wo raqam makaan maalik ke paas amaanat hoti hai. Is liye deposit dene se pehle mu'aahadah (agreement) (Agreement) me ya kam se kam zabaani is ka zikr kare ke yeh raqam bataure amaanat hai ya qarz? Agar bataure qarz mu'aamalah huwa hai to use dukaan ya makaan maalik isti'emaal kar sakta hai, lekin agar bataure amaanat mu'aamalah huwa hai to kiraayedaar ki ijaazat se makaan maalik isti'emaal kar sakta hai. (Nihaayatul matlab fi diraayatil mazhab: 11/385). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bhaari Deposit par Flat

Sawal: Bhari deposit par flat lena kaisa hai? Kuch log Mumbai me jaise flat kiraaye par dete hain is se mahaanah kiraaye ki raqam ki bajaaye ek badi raqam (Deposit) lekar mahaanah kiraya nahi lete, baad me ek muddat ke baad us ki wo raqam waapas kar di jati hai. Is se rehne wale ko kiraaya masalan 20 hazaar na dete huwe ek badi raqam jaise 5 lakh ya 10 lakh rupaye dene hote hain jo use ek saal ya muqarrarah waqt par waapas milte hain, kya yeh jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Hadeese paak me waarid huwa hai aur taqreeban tamaam 'Ulama ke nazdeek yahi usul hai ke “Har wo qarz jiski wajaah se qarz dene wala us ka nafa'a uthaaye to wo sood hai aur sood haraam hai.

Soorate mas-uoolah me chunke bhaari deposit dene waala qarz ke badle us ke ghar ki rehaaish se nafa'a haasil kar raha hai aur waqte muqarrarah par di hui raqam poori wasool bhi kar raha hai to yeh qarz se faidah uthana hua. Lihaaza yeh mu'aamalah jaaiz nahi hoga. (Tajaviz: Babat Doosra Shafi'ee fiqahi Seminaar: 2020).

Is ki jaaiz shakl yeh hai ke do alag alag mu'aamalah kiye jaayein ek mu'aamalah qarz ka kiya jaaye masalan Zaid, amr ko 10 lakh rupaye qarz de raha hai aur Indattalab (on demand) Amr, Zaid ko wo raqam waapas karega, aur doosra mu'aamalah kiraaye ka tay ho jaaye is me maalike makaan wa dukaan ko ikhtiyaar hai ke wo jis qeemat me chahe kiraayeh par de. (Majmoo'a Talkhees e maqaalaat, Doosra Shafi'ee fiqhi Seminar). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ghar Farokht Karne Par Mu'aamalah

Sawal: Hamari haisiyat maalike makaan ki taraf se wakeel ki hai aur hum ne ek shakhs se us ke ghar ke farokht karne par is tarah mu'aamalah tay kiya hai ke hum maalike makaan ke makaan ko 8 lakh me farokht karenge jis ke baad maalik hamein ek muta'ayyanah ujrat dega, ab hum maalike makaan ko ittila'a kiye bagair gaahak ko uski qeemat 10 lakh bata kar zaa-id (excess) do lakh rupaye hamare liye rakhte hain to kya hamaara yeh mu'aamalah shar'an durust hai?

Jawab: Aisa mu'aamalah karna durust nahi, han agar mu'aamalah karne waala shakhs maalike makaan ki taraf se wakeel na ban kar baraahe raast maalike makaan se udhaar mu'aamalah tay kare masalan ek ya do maah ki mohlat le aur aath (8) lakh me maalike makaan se khareed le phir das lakh me sauda tay kare phir aath lakh udhaar rakhi hui raqam ada kare to durust hai. (Al-Majmoo': 10/90). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ghar Ki Iftitaah Ke Mauqa'a Par Da'awat Aur Qur-Aan Khwani

Sawal: Naye ghar ki iftitaah ke mauqa'a par 'umooman jo riwaaj chal pada hai ke logon ko da'awat de kar Qur-aan khwani karwayi jaati hai aur in ki ziyafat ka bhi intizaam kiya jaata hai, kya yeh shar'an durust hai?

Jawab: Agar bagair rasam aur bagair kisi cheez ke iltizaam ke barkat ke liye khud Qur-aan padhe aur doosron se phadhwaaye to durust hai aur jahaan tak ziyaafat ka mas-alah hai to ghar Allah ki ek ni'emat hai lihaaza is ni'emat ki khushi mien da'awat ki jaaye to haraj nahi (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/404). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

SOOD, BANK, FINANCE & INSURANCE KE MASA-IL

Bank Aur Insurance Companiyon Se Mu'aamalaat Ki

Hurmat Ke Silsile Me Tamheedi Kalimaat

Ju ju zamane me jiddat aa rahi hai usi ke saath saath har cheez ke andar jiddat aur tabdeeli aa rahi hai, is haqeeqat ke peshe nazar soodi kaarobaar aur juye ki shakl me bhi khaasi tabdeeli aa gai hai, lekin in shaklon ke bunyaadi anaasir ko dekha jaaye to in ki asliyat khul kar samne aa jati hai.

Murawwaja insurance ki tamaam companiyon ka mu'aamalah bhi bank ke kaarobaar ki tarah ek soodi mu'aamalah hai, aur bankon ki buniyaad sood par hai, aur sood ka len den haraam aur gunah hai, isi buniyaadi wajah ki bina par bankon se baahami ta'aaun ko 'Ulama-e-kiraam doosre ki mu'aawanat karne se mana'a kiya hai (Surah Maaidah:2) bank aur insurance company ke darmiyaan sirf shakl wa soorat ka farq hai, insurance ke andar “Sood” ke saath “Juwa” bhi paaya jaata hai, aur Islam me yeh dono haraam hain, in ki hurmat Qur-aane Kareem ki waazeh aur qata'ee Nusoos se saabit hai, (Surah Maaidah: 90) Kisi zamane ki koi maslihat is haraam ko halaal nahi kar sakti.

Insurance me “Sood” is e'etibaar se hai ke haadisah ki soorat me jamaashudah raqam se zaa-id (excess) raqam milti hai aur zaa-id (excess) raqam sood hai, aur Juwa is e'etibaar se hai ke baaz sooraton me agar haadisah wagayrah na ho to jama shudah raqam bhi waapas nahi milti, insurance company is raqam ki maalik ban jati hai, is soorat me yeh Izaa'ate maal bhi huwa aur naahaq doosron ka maal baatil tareeqa se khaana bhi huwa jo az ruye shara'a haraam hai. (Surah Baqarah: 188).

Is tarah is me jahaalat aur garar (Dhokah) bhi paaya jaata hai, aur jahaalat aur garar waale mu'aamalah ko Sharee'at ne faasid (invalid) qaraar diya hai, (Saheeh Muslim: 1/1513) lihaaza kisi bhi qism ka insurance karna aur karaana aur insurance company ka member banna shar'an naa jaaiz aur haraam hai.

Sood Aur Soodi Nizaam

Sawal: Sood ki ta'areef kya hai?

Jawab: Fiqhi Istilaah me Ribaa (Sood) ka mafhoom hai, ivaze makhsoos par aisa aqd jo bawaqte aqd shara'ee me'eyaar me ma'aloomaatutt tamaasul (known similarity) na ho ya dono badal ya ek badal me taakheer (delay) ho. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 2/53).

Sood ki ta'areef fuqahae kiraam yeh karte hain ke kayli (Naapi jaane wali) ya wazani cheez ke tabaadalah me dono fareeq me se kisi ek ko aysi z'iyaadati ka milna jo 'iwaz (badle me) se khaali ho aur aqd me mashroot ho. (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband), At-Tawqeef 'ala Muhimmaatit-Ta'aareef: 173). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Aaj Kal har jagah bank aur soodi kaarobaar hai, kya maujoodah daur me sood ke bagair kaarobaar mumkin hai?

Jawab: Bilaashubah mumkin hai, bashart yeh ke maal me zaroorat se ziyadah izaafah ki hirs wa tama'a na rakhi jaaye, albatta kuch dushwaari aur tangi zaroor aayegi, tamaam tar e'htiyaat ke baawajood bhi ma'mooli (ordinary) sood ka hissa kaarobaar me shaamil ho jaaye is se taubah wa istigfaar karta rahe. hadeese paak me hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne irshaad faramaaya: Yaqeenan logon par ek waqt aisa zaroor aayega ke koi bhi bagair sood khaaye na bach sakega. Aur agar khaaya nahi to kam se kam us ka dhuwaan (smoke) ya gard to pahonch hi jaayega. (Musnade Ahmed). Is liye haraam ko haraam samajh kar us se bachne ki poori koshish karna farz hai, albatta majboori ki soorat me jo sood shaamil ho jaaye use gurabaa wa masakeen me sarf kare aur us se laaziman istigfaar karta rahe. Aur majboori ki haalat ko Allah behtar jaanta hai Allah ko dhokah nahi diya ja sakta, (Surah Ankabut). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bank Account Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya Bank me account kholna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: 'Aam haalaat me bank account kholna jaaiz nahi, kyunke is me soodi kaarobaar hota hai, lekin agar shadeed zaroorat pesh aa jaaye to Saving Account ke bajaaye Current Account me apna khata khulwaaye kyunke current Account me soodi mu'aamalah nahi hota aur jo khidmaat bank faraaham karta hai un ka charge le leta hai yeh jaaiz hai us me account khulwane me koi gunah nahi. Yaa bank se Locker le kar is me apni raqam mahfooz rakhain kyunke is soorat me bhi aadmi soodi len den se kisi had (limit) tak bach jaayega. Isi tarah Saving Account bhi majbooran khulwa sakte hain lekin jo sood ki raqam mile use nikaal kar gurabaa par sadaqah kare. Fixed Account me raqam jama'a karna jaaiz nahi haraam hai aur izaafi milne wali raqam sood hai jis ka sadaqah karna waajib hai. Wallahu Ta'aala A'alam (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Bank ke Saving Account me jamaashudah sood ki raqam ka kya karna chaahiye?

Jawab: Soodi Account se haasil hone waale soodi munafe'e ke bare me hukm yeh hai ke use kisi mustahiqqe zakaat ko sawaab ki niyyat ke bagair de di jaaye lihaaza aysi raqam ko zaati isti'emaal me ya kisi ko hadiyah dene me isti'emaal karna shar'an durust nahi (Darul Iftah, Karachi). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar Bank me chaar account hain aur chaaron me sood ke paise hain to kya chaaron account se sood ki raqam na nikalte huye agar wo raqam apne Pocket Balance me se diye to chal sakta hai?

Jawab: Ji ha! Account me maujood “Soodi Raqam” ka hisaab karke agar apne paas maujood raqam me se utni raqam sood ki raqam ki niyyat se sadaqah kar de to zimmah faarig ho jaayega (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bank Ko Kiraaye Par Gaadi, Gaala Aur Flat Dena

Sawal: Kya bank ko kiraaye par gaadi dena jaaiz hai? Agar koi de to kya kiraaya hamare liye halaal hoga?

Jawab: Islam me kisi bhi qism ka soodi kaarobaar karna ya soodi kaarobaar me mu'aawanat karna haraam hai, chunke bank me soodi kaarobaar hota hai is liye kiraaye par gaadi dena jaaiz nahi hoga, aur us se aane waala kiraaya bhi halaal nahi hoga. (Sooratul Maaidah: 2, Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 11/26, Jawahirul-'Uqood: 1/210). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Bank ko Flat ya ATM Machine ke liye dukaan kiraaye par dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Bank me soodi kaarobaar hota hai aur islam me soodi len den aur soodi len den par mu'aawanat haraam hai. Aur Imame Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek kisi naajaaiz kaam me madad karna bhi haraam hai, is liye bank ko flat ya dukaan kiraaye par dena jaaiz nahi hai. (Sooratul Maaidah: 2, Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 11/26, Jawahirul-'Uqood: 1/210). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sood Ki Raqam Ki Taqseem

Sawal: Kya sood ke paise bagair sawaab ki niyaat se aise logon ko de sakte hain jo bahut mohtaaj hon chahe rishtedaar hon ya gaonwaalon me se koi ho ya phir kisi teesre insaan ke zariye se de sakte hain ke lene waale ko pata na chale ke kis ne diye hain kyun ke bank me jama rakhne ka koi faaidah nahi?

Jawab: Sood ki raqam bagair sawaab ki niyyat se gareebon aur mohtaajon me taqseem karna chaahiye, chahe wo rishtedaar hon ya aur koi ho koi haraj nahi. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya sood ke paise rishtedaar ko khud bhi de sakte hain ya kisi teesre shakhs ke zariye dena chaahiye aur lene wale ko muttale'e (inform) karna zaroori hai ke yeh sood ke paise hain.

Jawab: Khud bhi de sakta hai ya kisi aur ke zariye se bhi de sakta hai aur samne waale ko batana bhi koi zaroori nahi albatta yeh laazim hai ke lene waala Mohtaaj ho. (Al-Majmoo': 9/351). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sood Ki Raqam Masjid Aur Madrasah Ke Liye Chandah Dena

Sawal: Kya sood ki raqam bila niyyate sawaab Masjid wa madrase ke liye bataure chandah de sakte hain?

Jawab: Masjid wa madrasa ki ta'meer me ya imaam asaatazah wa mulaazimeen ki tankhwahon me soodi raqam dena jaaiz nahi. Soodi raqam ke asal mustahiq masaakeen aur fuqaraa hain lihaaza in logon ko bila niyyate sawaab de sakte hain. (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bank Service Charges Ke Liye Sood Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Kya bank se katne wale service charges jaise ATM Fees, RTGS, SMS Alert wagayrah ki adaayigi ke liye sood ka isti'emaal kar sakte hain?

Jawab: ATM Fees, RTGS, SMS Alert wagayrah me jo raqam bank wasool karti hai, wo raqam bank apne haqqe mehnat aur ujrat ke taur par leti hai is liye un cheezon ki fix me apne milkiyat ki raqam ka isti'emaal kare sood ki raqam ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz nahi kyun ke sood ki raqam ke aap maalik nahi hain. (Tuhfatul Habeeb 'Ala Sharhil Khateeb: 3/455). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Soodi Qarz Se Gaadi Khareedna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs gaadi khareedna chaahta hai lekin us ke liye darkaar raqam is ke paas maujood na hone ki wajah se use aise shakhs ke paas se qarz lena pade jo soodi qarz deta ho to kya is ke paas se qarz lena durust hai?

Jawab: Soodi len den waale mu'aamalaat shar'an durust nahi hai (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

India Me Income Tax Ki Adaayigi Ke Liye Sood (Interest) Ki Raqam

Sawal: Kya sood (Interest) ki raqam India me Income Tax bharne ke liye isti'emaal ki ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Income Tax ek naawaajibi tax hai, aur naawaajib had (limit) tak wasool kiya jaata hai, is liye agar bank me kisi majboori ke tahat aysi scheme me raqam rakhni pade jis me sood haasil hota hai, aur sood mil gaya to is sood se income tax ada kiya ja sakta hai, bashart yeh ke bank ya wo idaarah sarkari hi ho, kyun ke maale haraam ka hukm yahi hai ke use asal maalik ko pahunchaaya jaaye aur sarkar se maale haraam haasil ho aur usi ko pohcha diya gaya taham khas is maqsad ke liye ke sood haasil hoga aur us se income tax ada karenge, bank me raqam rakhna durust nahi hai. Wazeh ho ke yeh ijaazat badarjah zaroorat w majboori aur maujoodah badhte huwe naajaaiz tax ke maahol ko samne rakh kar hai, warnah yeh raqam dar asal sood ki hai jo haraam hai, lihaaza 'aam tex jaise baldiyah (Municipality) wagayrah ya is tarah ke doosre mu'aamaleh ke liye bank ki sood ko isti'emaal karne ka heelah ikhtiyaar karna durust nahi hoga. (Ma'khooz: Kitabul-Fatawa – Maulana Khalid Saifullah Jild number 5). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Badi badi companiyan tankhwah me se hi income tax kaat kar tankhwah deti hai, kya wo zaati bank account me jama shudah sood ki raqam ke baare me yeh niyyat kar sakta hai ke agar me ne khud income tax bharaa hota to is sood se bharaa hota aur is income tax ki miqdaar ke baraabar sood nikaal kar apne liye isti'emaal kar sakta hai? Kyunke asal me sood ki raqam bank me jama'a hoti hai aur income tax company doosre paison se deti hai.

Jawab: Ji ha! Is tarah niyyat karne se sood ki raqam income tax ke naam par ada ho jaayegi aur aap ke liye income tax ke baqadr soodi raqam ka isti'emaal jaaiz ho jaayega. Income tax me wahi sood ki raqam di ja sakti hai jo hukoomat ki taraf se haasil ho, private bank se haasilshudah soodi raqam ko hukoomat income tax me dene se aap bariuz zimmeh nahi honge. Wallahu ta'aala aalam. (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Credit Card Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Kya Hindustan me credit Card ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Waazeh rahe ke kisi muaamale ke halaal wa haraam hone ka daaromadaar darhaqeeqat wo mu'aahadah (agreement), sharaait aur qawaayid hote hain jo fareeqain ke darmiyaan tay paate hain, credit card lene waala credit card jaari karne waale idaaron ke saath mu'aahadah (agreement) karta hai ke: agar muqarrarah muddat me li jaane waali raqam waapas na kar saka to ek muta-'ayyan sharah ke saath jurmaaneh ke naam par sood ada karunga. Jis tarah sood ka lena aur dena haraam hai usi tarah us ka mu'aahadah (agreement) karna bhi shar'an naajaaiz aur haraam hai. Is bunyaad par agar credit card lene waala li gai raqam bilfarz muqarrarah muddat me waapas bhi karde to mu'aahadah (agreement) ke soodi hone ki wajah se usooli taur par credit card ka isti'emaal naajaaiz hai. (Saheeh Al-Bukhari wa Muslim me hai: har wo shart jo Allah ta'aala ki kitaab me na ho baatil hai, agarcha aysi sau (100) sharten hon….Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504) aur agar muqarrarah muddat ke baad sood ke saath raqam waapas karta hai to us ke naajaaiz hone me kisi qism ka shubah nahi hai, is liye adaayigi ki soorat koi bhi ho us se aur us ke isti'emaal se qata'a nazar nafse mu'aahadah (agreement) ke soodi hone ki wajah se credit card banwaana hi jaaiz nahi hai. Faqat Wallahu A'alam. (Darul Iftah'a: Jami'ah Al 'Uloomul Islamiyyah Binnori Town, Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kuwait me NBK (National Bank of Kuwait), achchi tankhwah ke liye ek credit card deti hai jis ke bahut saare faaide hote hain, jaise bahut saare shops, restaurant, flight wa hotel bookings wagayrah me discount milta hai, dunya ke aksar Airports par muft me Lounge Services milti hai jis me flight se pehle khana kha sakte hain aur aaraam bhi kar sakte hain. Is ki saalaana 50 Kuwaiti Dinar (KD) fees hai aur bill aane ke baad ek maheene ke andar adaayigi (payments) karne par koi sood bhi nahi lagta lekin agar waqt muqarrarah par adaayigi na kare to sood ke saath izaafah karna hoga neez saal me credit card ka kam se kam isti'emaal 5500 KD ke zariye kiye to saalaana fees 50 KD bhi waapas milti hai. Kya is credit card ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz hoga?

Jawab: Jis tarah sood lena aur dena haraam hai usi tarah us ka mu'aahadah (agreement) karna bhi shar'an naajaaiz aur haraam hai. Is bunyad par agar credit card lene waala li gai raqam bilfarz muqarrarah muddat me waapas bhi kar de to mu'aahadah (agreement) ke soodi hone ki wajah se usooli taur par credit card ka isti'emaal naajaaiz hai. Saheeh Al-Bukhari wa Muslim me hai “Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: Aakhir logon ko kya ho gaya hai ke wo aysi sharten lagaate hain jo Allah Ta'aala ki kitaab me nahi hain, aur har wo shart jo Allah Ta'aala ki kitaab me na ho baatil hai, agarche aysi sau (100) sharten hon.” Aur agar muqarrarah muddat ke baad sood ke saath raqam waapas karta hai to is ke naajaaiz hone me kisi qism ka shubah nahi hai; is liye adaayigi ki soorat koi bhi ho us se aur us ke isti'emaal se qata'a nazar nafse mu'aahadah (agreement) ke soodi hone ki wajah se credit card banwana hi jaaiz nahi hai. Faqat Wallahu A'alam. (Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 5/354, Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ghar Ki Ta'meer Ke Liye Soodi Qarz

Sawal: Mai Maa'Sha Allah kamaata hun lekin mujhe ghar ta'meer karne ke liye paison ki sakht zaroorat hai jab ke ghar ta'meer karne ka kharch 60,00000/- rupaye hai. Mere paas ek musht itna rupaya maujood nahi hai. Main bank se ja kar loan le sakta hu aur mujhe milega bhi, magar mai ne aaj tak bank se loan nahi liya kyunke mai jaanta hun ke loan lena haraam hai, haalaanke mai har saal income tax bhi bharta hun to aysi soorat me kya karun? Kya bank se loan le kar income tax aur interest bharun ya kya karun.

Jawab: Maujoodah bankon se ghar khareedne ke liye qarzah (House Loan) lena shar'an jaaiz nahi hai, zaroorat ke liye kisi rishtedaar ya shanaasa shakhs se qarz liya ja sakta hai, aur agar koi bandobast na ho to Allah Ta'aala par tawakkul karte huwe qanaa'at karna chaahiye, ya'ani kam se kam kharch me aisa ghar ta'meer karayein jis mein hamari saari laazimi zarooriyaat hon. Yeh naajaaiz aur naamunaasib is wajah se hai, kyunke qarz lene ke baad us qarz ki adaayigi ka bhi to intizaam karna hoga. Lihaaza ek naaqis ray yeh hai ke soodi qarz ki adaayigi ka waqt aane par jaise mehnat karke halaal raqam se adaayigi ka intizaam aap karoge, to abhi se hi koshish kar li jaaye take soodi qarz lene ki hoi nobat na aaye.

Soodi qarz lene ke baad me adaayigi me halaal ka ehtimaam na ho payega to yeh gunah dar gunah ho jaayega. Kahin is se ziyadah pareshaani na ho jaaye aur soodi qarzah utaarne ke liye phir soodi qarzah lene ka silsilah na karna pad jaaye.

Qabile Yaqeen baat to yeh hai ke aadmi agar haraam se bachne ke liye himmat buland rakhe aur Allah Ta'aala par bharosah rakhe to Allah ki taraf se madad hoti hai, aur allah ta'aala aysi jagah se bandobast karte hain jo us ke waham wa gumaan me bhi nahi hoti.

Pas dunya janti hai aur hamein bhi janna chaahiye ke is kaayinaat me maali e'etibaar se mukhtalif haisiyat ke log aabaad hain, sab ek jaise nahi hain, is liye shara'ee usoolon ke mutaabiq hamaara nazariyah yeh hona chaahiye ke hum maali haisiyat aur maali mura'aat me apne se kamtar logon ko dekhein apne se upar walon ko na dekhein doosra yeh ke apni zarooriyaat aur ta'ayyushaat ki tashkhees bhi kar lein mazeed yeh ke hum sab asbaab wa mata'a ki faraawani ke bawajood ma'aashi se ziyadah ma'aadi ya'ani aakhirat ki taraf lautne waale insaan hain, aakhirat ka khayaal aur dhyan bhi rakhein to hamara bojh kam ho jaayega, In Sha Allah.

Irshaade Baariee Ta'aala hai: Aur jo Allah se darta hai Allah us ke liye kushaadgi paida kar deta hai aur us ko wahan se rizq deta hai jahan se us ka gumaan bhi nahi hota aur jo Allah par tawakkul karta hai to wo use kaafi hai, be shak Allah apna kaam poora karne waala hai, be shak Allah ne har cheez ka ek andaazah rakhkha hai.” (Surah Talaq:3)

Hadeese Mubarak me hai: Hazrat Jabir (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne sood khane (lene) waale par sood khilane (dene) wale par soodi len den likhne waale par aur us ke gawaahon par sabhi par la'anat farmaayi hai neez Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke yeh sab (Asal gunah me baraabar hain). Wallahu Ta'aala A'alam wa 'ilmuhu atamm (Albani: Ta 1420, Saheehul Jaame'e: 5090). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Business Ke Liye Soodi Qarzah

Sawal: Kya kisi shakhs ka bank ya koi Finance Institute se qarzah le ke apna Business shuru' karna sahi hai?

Jawab: Bank se loan lene me sood dena pاaya jaata hai, aur jis tarah sood lena haraam hai aise hi bila zaroorat sood dena nاajaaiz hai. hadees me soodi len den karne waalon par la'anat aayi hai is liye bagair majboori aur sakht zaroorat ke soodi mu'aamalah karna jaaiz nahi hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi shakhs ka business pehle se chote paimaane par hai lekin wo business badhaana chaahta hai aur income tax ki adaayigi se bachna bhi chaahta hai to kya is garz se wo soodi qarz le sakta hai?

Jawab: Islam me jis tarah sood lena haraam hai usi tarah sood dena bhi haraam hai, ahaaddees me sood lene waale aur sood dene waale dono par la'anat aayi hai, is liye kaarobaar ki taraqqi ya koi naya kaarobaar shuru' karne ke liye bank se soodi qarz lena hargiz jaaiz nahi, sakht haraam hai, albatta agar kisi shakhs ke paas kaarobaar ki taraqqi ya koi naya kaarobaar shuru' karne ke liye deegar ikhraajaat se khaali matloobah raqam maujood hai lekin wo income tax ada kardah nahi hai, ya'ani hukoomat ki nazar me gair qanooni hai, jis ki bina par kaarobaar ki taraqqi ya koi naya kaarobaar shuru' karne ke liye agar bank se loan na liya jaaye to income tax ke hawaale se mukhtalif pareshaaniyon ka khatrah hai to aysi soorat me mahaz income tax ke masa-il se bachne ke liye zaahiri taur par bank se loan lene ki gunjaish hogi, kyun ke yahan bank se soodi qarz kisi faaide ki garz se nahi liya ja raha hai, balke sirf income tax ke masa-il se bachne ke liye ya'ani daf'ey zulm (stopping an act of injustice) ke liye, is liye is soorat me In sha Allah gunah na hoga (Dekhiye: Fatawa Nizamiyyah Indorwiyyah: 1/233-234) lekin is soorat me bhi behtar yeh hai ke loan ki raqam kahin utha kar rakh di jaaye, kaarobaar me isti'emaal na ki jaaye aur pehle se maujood apni halaal raqam hi kaarobaar me lagaai jaaye.

Is soorat me bank loan lene waale ko chaahiye ke agar qiston ke intizaar ke bagair jald az jald bank loan ada kiya ja sakta ho to ada kare, is me taakheer na kare. Aur agar kisi ke paas tijaarat ko farog dene ke liye matloobah raqam nahi hai to mahaz business badhaane ki garz se bank se soodi qarz ka mu'aamalah karna islam me sakht haraam hai. Lihaaza apne paas maujood halaal raqam ke zariye hi tijaarat ko farog de aur isi par iktifaa kare. Wallahu Ta'aala A'alam. (As saheeh li Muslim: 2/72, Darul Ifta, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

English Medium Taaleem Ke Liye Shahar Me Flat Khareedne Ke Liye Soodi Qarz

Sawal: Aaj kal bahut saare log apne Bachchon ko achchi English Medium taaleem ke liye apne gaon ko chod kar shahar me muntaqil ho rahe hain, isi bahane se flat khareedne ke liye bank se soodi qarz le rahe hain, yeh kis had (limit) tak jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Bachchon ko English medium me padhaane ke naam par shahar me flat khareedne ke liye soodi qarz ka mu'aamalah karna sahi nahi hai. Kiraayeh se makaan le kar bhi yeh zaroorat poori ki ja sakti hai kyunke Alla Ta'aala ne sood khaane aur khilaane waale dono ko sakht azaab aur la'anat ki dhamki di hai, is liye apne aap ko un logon me shaamil karne se bachao (Sooratul Baqarah: 278) isi tarah Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne la'anat bhi farmaayi hai: Sood khaane waale aur khilaane waale par. (Saheeh Muslim: 1597, Al-Majmoo': 13/173). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Insurance Company Me Kaam Karne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Insurance company me mulaazamat karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Chunke insurance ki bunyaad sood aur qimaar (juwwa) par qaa-im hai aur Qur-aane majeed ne dono ko naajaaiz aur haraam kaha hai. Neez ek hadees me Hazrate Jabir (Rz) se marvi hai ke Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne sood lene, sood dene, sood likhne aur us ki gawaahi dene waalon par la'anat farmaayi hai aur farmaaya ke wo sab gunah me baraabar hain (Rawahul Muslim Mishkaat: 244). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Hindustaani income tax Act Assi C (Indian Income Tax Act Section 80C) ke mutaabiq kuch mutual fund scheme me sarmaayaakari ki soorat me hukoomat ki jaanib se ek laakh pachaas hazar (150000/-) Rupaye par income tax ki choot milti hai to kya naajaaiz aur gair waajibi income tax bachane ki niyyat se bankon ke Tax Saving Mutual Fund Scheme me invest karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Hindustaan aur aksar mulkon me jo mutual funds qaa-im hain un me shara'ee ahkaam ki ri'aayat nahi paayi jati, (kyunke is me qimaar (juwwa), juwa, sood aur jahaalat paayi jaati hai) is liye is fund me surmaayah kaari jaaiz nahi (Jadeed Maliyati Idaray – Maulana Khalid Saifullah: 87).

Albatta apni halaal aur jaaiz raqam bachaane ki niyyat ho aur mutual fund me sarmaayaakaari karne se income tax me kisi had (limit) tak katauti ho sakti ho aur tax bachaane ki koi doosri be gubaar aur saaf suthri shakl na ho to 'Ulama-E-Kiraam ne aysi majboori ki soorat me LIC ki tarah mutual funds me sarmaayaah kaari ki bhi gunjaaish di hai. Albatta is baat ka khayaal rakhein ke aap ne mutual fund scheme me jis qadar raqam jama ki hai, muddat mukammal hone ke baad aap ke liye isi jama shuda raqam ke baqadr lene ki gunjaish hogi, zaa-id (excess) izaafi raqam lena jaaiz na hoga balkeh is ka bila niyyate sawaab sadaqah karna waajib hoga. (Al-Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504, Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 5/354, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 7/25-26, Kitabul-Fatawa – Maulana Khalid Saifullah: 10/85-86). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Finance Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Finance ke bare me kya khayaal hai? Is ka Jawab tafseel se diya jaaye taake sab ko tasalli ho jaaye kyunke aksar log is me mubtala (afflicted) hain, 'awaam ka chodo khwaas bhi Business ke liye finance ka isti'emaal karte hain khusoosan kitne log badi badi gadiyan finance par lete hain aur jab koi samjhaane jaata hai to log 'Ulama ke naam le kar misaal dete hain ke unhonne bhi kiya. Mera maqsad 'Ulama par tanqeed nahi hai, lekin haqeeqat me zaroorat hai ke logon ko is ka sahi 'ilm pahonche. Agar is ki ijaazat hai to kin majbooriyon me hai?

Jawab: Jaise sood ka lena haraam hai, usi tarah kisi shadeed majboori ke bagair sood ka dena bhi haraam hai, bank jo is tarah ki ashya'a me khareedi ka wastah banta hai, wo zaahir hai ke aap ki madad ke jazbeh se yeh kaam nahi karta, balke us ka maqsad sood haasil karna hota hai, is liye agar is tarah qarz faraaham kiya jaaye ya gaadi farokht ki jaye ke itni qeemat asal hogi aur itni qeemat interest ki to yeh soorat jaaiz nahi hai, albatta aaj kal baaz companiyon ne sifar sood ki scheme bhi nikaali hain, ya'ani gaadi ki ek hi qeemat muta-'ayyan hoti hai, jo naqd se ziyadah hoti hai, company shuru' me apne nafa'a ki raqam wasool kar leti hai aur showroom ki asal qeemat ko aqsaat par taqseem kar deti hai, baad me koi qist ada nahi hoti to raqam badhayi nahi jati hai, balke gaadi zabt kar li jaati hai, aur use farokht kar ke company apni baqi maandah qeemat wasool kar leti hai, aur jo paise bach jate hain use wapas kar deti hai, yeh soorat shar'an jaaiz hai aur sood ke dayereh (circle) me nahi hoti hai. (Kitabul-Fatawa – Maulana Khalid Saifullah: Jild no.10). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar hum second hand gaadi (vehicle) kisi aur se lein aur us se aisa mu'aamalah kare ke is ko har maheene hum ek raqam dein jaise bank me dete hain lekin car ki asal qeemat se ziyadah me saudah ho jaaye to kiya is tarah karna durust hoga?

Jawab: Qistwaar mu'aamalah karne me koi haraj nahi hai jab ke aapasi razaamandi se ho isi tarah naqd aur udhaar karne me agar qeemat me bhi farq padta ho to koi haraj nahi jab ke naqd aur udhaar ki qeemat muta-'ayyan ki jaaye. Jaise agar gaadi/car ka maalik kahe agar naqd raqam dete ho to yeh gaadi das hazaar me aur agar udhaar aur qistwaar do to baarah hazaar me to is me koi haraj nahi hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe
