Sawal: Agar kisi ne loan par car li thi par kisi wajah se wo raqam ada nahi kar saka ab wo car bechna chaahta hai aur abhi chaubees hafte baaqi hain kya aysi car khareed sakte hain?

Jawab: Nahi, kyunke is soorat me khareedne wala soodi mu'aamalah me is ka shareek ho jaayega. (Surah Maaidah: 2, Saheeh Muslim: 1598). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar bank se muaamalah tay ho jaaye ke Installment par aap ko car itne me mil jaayegi aur installment na bharne ki soorat me bank sood (Interest) charge karengi. Agar hum bank se installment par car lein aur waqt par installment bharein aur koi sood na lage to kya hum bank se mu'aamalah kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Chunke aqd aur mu'aamalah karte waqt sood ki shart lagaee jaati hai jo baatil shart hai, is shart ki wajah se yeh mu'aamalah baatil ho jaayega. (Al-Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504, Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 5/354). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Jab hum car lete hain to car bank ke naam par hoti hai aur jab hum tayshudah raqam bharte hain tab wo car hamaare naam par transfer hoti hai, aaj ki taareekh me Indian bank aur aksar Financial Institute sirf aur sirf sood par kaam karte hain. Jab hum ghar ya gaadi ke liye loan lete hain to jo installment bank ki taraf se hota hai us me internet ka feesad bhi hota hai aur agar installment waqt par na bharah jaaye to bank jurmaanah (Penalty) lagaati hai. Yeh saari sharaait bank ya finance waale Brochure me bataate hain, kya hum bank se mu'aamalah kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Jis tarah sood ka lena aur dena haraam hai usi tarah us ka mu'aahadah (agreement) karna bhi shar'an haraam hai. Saheeh Al-Bukhari wa muslim me hai “Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: Aakhir logon ko kya ho gaya hai ke wo aysi sharten lagaate hain jo Allah Ta'aala ki kitaab me nahi hain, aur har wo shart jo Allah Ta'aala ki kitaab me na ho baatil hai, agarcha sau (100) sharten hi kyun na hon” Lihaaza bank ke saath is tarah ke mu'aamalaat karna sahi nahi hai. (Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 5/354). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Yahan kuwait me islaami bank hain jo Profit (Muraabaha) ke naam par car installment par dilaati hai, hum koi car loan par khareedne jaate hain to Islamic bank us car ki qeemat ada karti hai phir wo bank hum se maahaanah taur par ziyadah paise lete hain. Yahan bank use profit kehti hai. Sawal yeh hai ki waaqayi me yeh profit hai ya sood? Agar hum wo raqam do saal me ada karte hain to installment alag, teen saal me ada kare to installment alag. In ka profit of margin (Profit Margin) tabdeel hota hai. Kya yeh ribaa (sood) hai? Ya phir jaaiz tareeqah hai? Kya is tarah car installment par le sakte hain?

Jawab: Aap ke puche gaye Sawal ke mutaabiq bank awwalan car company se car khareedti hai phir apna munafa'a rakh kar ek qeemat muta-'ayyan karke qist par farokht karti hai. Lihaazah yeh mu'aamalah sahi hoga is shart ke saath ke bank mu'aahadah (agreement) ke waqt koi khilaafe Sharee'at shart na lagaye. (Al-Gurarul-Bahiyyah: 2/439, Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 5/354). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Koi cheez bazaar me se us ki qeemat 30 se 32 hazaar hai wahi cheez online 25 se 27 hazaar me milti ho lekin koi ek hazaar rupaye sood laga kar farokht karta hai (EMI ke hisaab se) phir bhi bazaar ki qeemat se kam ho to is bare me kya raay hai?

Jawab: Yeh mu'aamalah sood ka nahi lagta hai har ek ko apni cheez kam aur ziyadah paison me bechne ka ikhtiyaar hai, har jagah ek hi qeemat ho koi zaroori nahi hai. Ab lene waale ka ikhtiyar hai. Qeemat kam hoti hai par qist (EMI) jaise teen (3) ya cheh (6) ya baarah (12) maheene par alag alag sood lagaate hain. Udhaar aur naqd ka mu'aamalah pehle tay kare jo qiston me ada karna hoti hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Pehle hi tay ki gai raqam ho to koi haraj nahi? Khwah use Interest/Sood ka naam de?

Jawab: Wo interest nahi hota jaise meri cheez hai mujhe ikhtiyar hai, udhaar aur naqd me qeemat ke farq ke saath bechne ki. Aap chaahe to khareedo ya mat kareedo, aap par koi zabardasti nahi. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Lekin mera Sawal yeh hai ke bechne wale ke computer me ya registered me zaa-id (excess) paise ke liye interest hi column hota hai. Wo log zaa-id (excess) raqam jo lete hain interest ke naam se hi lete hain?

Jawab: Dekhiye aadmi ko apna maal apne ikhtiyar se bechne ka haq hota hai, chaahe wo udhaar aur naqd me farq kare, baraabari ke saath bechen ya naqd me ziyadah aur udhaar me kam lekin yeh saari cheezein muaamale ki ibtidah hi me tay hona chaahiye taake baad me ikhtilaaf na ho. Naam ki tabdeeli se farq nahi hota. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Beshak har ek ko ikhtiyar hai ke apna maal kisi bhi qeemat par bechen usi qeemat ya ziyadah qeemat me bashart yeh ke saamne waala raazi ho, agar yeh infiraadi (individual) hai to baat thik hai lekin yahan in logon ka yeh business hi hai aur kitne log musalmaanon ko galat kaamon me daalne ke liye saazish karte hain. Agar wo chaahe to use processing fees ke taur par bhi zaa-id (excess) qeemat le sakte the lekin un ka asal maqsad musalmaanon ko sood me mubtalah karna bhi ho sakta hai, is tarah sood waali cheez ko sahi kehne se log us ka galat isti'emaal karenge kyunke aaj aise bhi musalmaan hain jo bank ka sood khaate hain aur daleel me yeh kehte hain ke us waqt ki raqam ki aaj, do chaar saal baad yeh qeemat hai mehngaayi ke badhne ki wajah se ya koi aur wajah. Sood ke naam se dil me dar to laga rahega, ek cheez yeh bhi dhyaan me rakhni hogi ke agar qist waqt par ada na kiya jaaye to sood lagega kya?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs apni cheez ki qeemat naqd farokht karne ki soorat me ek rakhe aur udhaar farokht karne ki soorat me doosri rakhe ya'ani izaafah kare to Shawaafe' ke nazdeek yeh jaaiz hai bashart yeh ke wo alag alag mu'aamalaat hon ya'ani naqd farokht karne ka mu'aamalah alag kare aur udhaar farokht alag, ek hi aqd me dono mu'aamalaat jaaiz nahi bai baatil ho jaayegi. Pichle Jawab mai mazkoora mu'aamaleh me is baat ko dekhna bhi zaroori hai ke aqd ke waqt bechne waale ne kya shart lagaayi hai, agar yeh shart lagaayi thi ke koi qist (EMI) ada na kare to company baad me jurmaaneh ke taur par khareedar se izaafi raqam legi to aqd ke waqt baatil shart ki wajah se yeh mu'aamalah sahi nahi hoga, aur agar jurmaanah wasool karne ki koi shart na ho ya'ani sifar feesad soodi scheme ho to yeh mu'aamalah jaaiz hoga warnah nahi. (Al-Badrul Muneer: 6/496, Mukhtasarul bazzar: 1/530, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 6/37, Al-Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504, Al-Majmoo': 13/173, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 5/354). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

GST Ki Raqam Sood Se Ada Karna

Sawal: Kya sood ki raqam se GST (Goods and Services Tax) ada kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Jis soorat me GST me di jaane waali raqam saarif ya taajir ko waapas milne ki ummeed na ho aur wo raqam bilwasta ya bila waastah sarkar ko pahonchti ho to is me “Radd ilal-maalik” ke wasool par hukoomati idaaron se haasil shudah sood ki raqam dene ki gunjaish hai.

Dukaandar saarif se GST ke naam par muta-'ayyan raqam wasool kar le to is raqam ko hukoomat tak pahonchana laazim hai use kisi tadbeer se apne liye rokna durust nahi hai. (Satraawah fiqhih Ijtima'a idaaratul Mabaahisul Fiqhiyya, Jamiyatul 'Ulama Hind, Banglore, Karnatak- August 2020). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hospital Ki Ta'meer Me Haraam Aur Sood Ki Raqam Isti'emaal Karna

Sawal: Daure haazir me bade bade private Hospital gairon ke hain aur unhonne 'ilaaj ko ek tijaarat bana ke rakhkha hai. Kuch muslim doctor apni taraf se gareebon ke 'ilaaj ke liye madad karte hain. Albatta badi beemaariyon ka 'ilaaj bade Hospital hi me hota hai jahaan wo muslim doctor bhi itni safaarish nahi kar pate jis ki wajah se 'aam insaan achcha 'ilaaj nahi kar pate, agar koi tanzeem ya koi muslim doctor is niyyat se Hospital banaana chaahta hai jahaan 'aam aadmi ka kam kharch me achcha 'ilaaj ho to kya aise Hospital ki ta'meer me haraam aur sood ki raqam isti'emaal kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Behtareen tareeqah to yahi hai ke Hospital aur us ki zaroorat ke liye halaal aur paakizah maal isti'emaal kare, awwalan to is ki koshish karna chaahiye lekin sood ke 'alaawah raqam itni na ho jis se mukammal ta'meer ho sake to is maal ko zaae'a karne ki bajaaye is ko Hospital ki ta'meer me isti'emaal ki gunjaaish hai, kyunke 'Ulama-e-kiraam ne sood ki raqam ko rifaahi kaamon me kharch karne ki ijaazat di hai aur Hospital bhi rifaahi umoor me dakhil hai. (Al-Bukhaari: 2561, Muslim: 1504, Al-Majmoo' Sharhul-Muhazzab-An-Nawawi: 9/351). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur 'Ilmi Batein - Jild Duwam

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il ki doosri jild anqareeb (soon) Insha Allah manzar-e-'aam par aane ki kagaar par hai. Pecheeda, jadeed aur nit naye masa-il par nazar-e-saani jaari hai. Yeh doosri jild Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare aur Mufti Ishaq Patel ki koshishon ka samarah hai.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

QARZ KE MASA-IL

Qarz Ke Len Den Ka Shara'ee Tareeqah

Sawal: Qarz ke len den ka shara'ee tareeqah kya hai?

Jawab: Qarz ke len den ke sahi hone ke liye chand umoor ka lihaaz rakhna zaroori hai jin ke bagair yeh mu'aamalah sahi nahi hoga balke faasid (invalid) ho jaayega.

Jo cheez bataure qarz di ja rahi hai bawaqte qarz us ki miqdaar ma'aloom ho, naap, wazan, 'adad ya paimaayish ke zariye, taake is ka badal lautaana mumkin ho, isi tarah wo cheez kisi aur jins ke saath makhloot aur mili hui na ho kyunke aysi soorat me us ka badal lautaana mushkil hoga khusoosan us waqt jab ke makhloot ashya'a ki infiraadi (individual) miqdaar na ma'aloom ho, lihaaza gehun aur jaw ka makhloot (mixture) paani mila dudh wagayrah qarz dena jaaiz nahi. Qarz dene waala jab chaahe aur jis waqt bhi chaahe qarz ki waapasi ka mutaalabah kar sakta hai chaahe koi muddat muta-'ayyan ki gai ho ya na ki gai ho. Har aysi shart se ke jis me qarz dene waale ka kuch faaida ho aur jo aqd ke muwaafiq (suitable) wa munaasib (appropriate) na ho to is shart ki wajah se qarz ka mu'aamalah faasid (invalid) ho jaayega. Jaise: lautaate waqt muta-'ayyan miqdaar se ziyadah ki shart, isi tarah 'aybdaar cheez de kar sahi saalim cheez lautaane ki shart, isi tarah is shart par qarzah de ke qarz lene waala apni fulaan cheez us ke haath farokht kare wagayrah. Albatta bagair shart aur 'urf ke qarz lene waala kuch izaafah ya hadiyah pesh kare to us ka hukm yeh hai ke agar qarz ki waapasi se qabl is tarah ka izaafah wagayrah pesh kare to is se ehtiyaat baratna (na lena) afzal hai, agar qarz se qabl bhi in dono ke darmiyaan is tarah hadiyah wagayrah ka ma'amool tha to haraj nahi isi tarah qarz dene waala maqrooz ke hadiyeh ki mukaafaat kare aur badlah de to karaahat khatam hogi. Qarz ki adaayigi ke baad maqrooz hadiyah wagayrah de to haraj nahi, qarz dene waale ko is ka lena makrooh nahi balke Aap (ﷺ) ke qaul wa 'amal ki ittiba'a me yeh 'amal mustahab hai lekin yeh tafseel us waqt hai jab ke logon ka 'urf qarz ke lautaane me izaafah ya hadiyah wagayrah ka na ho isi tarah maqrooz is ka 'aadi aur is silsile me maaruf na hon warnah phir mazkoorah izaafah ya hadiyah wagayrah ka qabool karna makrooh hoga. (Tuhfatul-Bari :2/70-74). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Maqrooz Ka Apni Razaamandi Se Izaafe Ki Peshkash

Sawal: Ek shakhs das laakh udhaar mang raha hai aur das maheene ki mohlat dene par ek laakh izaafi dene ke liye razaamand hai. Mujhe ma'aloom karna hai ke yeh izaafi raqam sood to nahi, wo apni khushi se de raha hai to kya yeh jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Wazan ki janewali ya paimane se naapi jaane wali ajnaas aur saamaan (Rupiya, paisa/currency) me do aadmiyon ka is tarah mu'aamalah karna ke ek ko zaa-id (excess) dena padhta ho, sood kehlata hai aur use islaam me haraam aur gunahe kabeerah qaraar diya gaya hai. Albatta agar qarz ki adaayigi ke waqt apni khushi se asal qarz se kuch zaa-id (excess) dena chaahe to yeh jaaiz hai aur aisa karna Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke 'amal se bhi saabit hai. Lekin is ke liye shart yeh hai ke fareeqain ke darmiyaan is zaa-id (excess) ke muaamale me pehle koi baat tay na ki gai ho.

Soorate mas-uoolah me maqrooz agarche apni marzi se zaa-id (excess) raqam dene ki peshkash kar raha hai magar chunke yeh z'iyaadati pehle se tay ki jaarahi hai is liye jaaiz nahi hai. Agar is silsile me pehle kuch tay na kiya jaaye aur maqrooz asal raqam ki adaayigi karte huye apni marzi se thoda ya ziyadah izaafah kar le to aisa izaafah lene me koi haraj nahi, taaham pehle se tay ki gai z'iyaadati haraam hai. (I'aanatul Taalibeen: 3/87). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qarz Ka Galat Isti'emaal

Sawal: Agar kisi ne kisi aise shakhs ko qarzah diya jis ne us raqam ka galat isti'emaal kiya masalan sharaab noshi ya juwa wagayrah me lagaaya to ab kya is ki wajah se qarzah dene waala gunahgaar hoga?

Jawab: Chunke denewaala qarz khwah ki niyyat aur us ke 'amal se waqif nahi tha lihaaza wo gunahgaar nahi hoga. Albatta use chaahiye ke apne qarz ki waapasi ka mutaalbah kare. Agar pehle se hi 'ilm ho ke wo galat isti'emaal ke liye maang raha hai to qarz dena mamnu' hai. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 3/80). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

NIKAH AUR TALAAQ KE MASA-IL

Mangni Ki Rasm Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Aaj kal hamare yahaan mangni karne ka riwaaj 'aam hai jis me do khaandaanon ki razaamandi hoti hai, tarafain ki jaanib se len den ka tabaadalah hota hai. Shar'an is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Mangni ya paigaame Nikah ka iraadah karne waale ki taraf se ek iltimaas aur darkhwast hoti hai. Shar'an is ka hukm yeh hai ke agar 'aurat kisi ke nikah me ya kisi ki 'iddat me na ho to is ko saraahatan bhi paigaame nikah dena jaaiz aur halaal hai aur ta'reezan bhi. (Ta'reez ke ma'ne hain isharah aur kinaayah me kalaam karna, ya'ani apne kalaam me aysi cheez ko le aana ke jo apne maqsad par bhi dalaalat ki salaahiyat rakhti ho aur gair maqsood par bhi magar jaanibe maqsood ki taraf is ki rahnumaayi ziyadah mukammal aur ziyadah raajeh (ziyadah sahi) ho, ya'ani yeh kehna ke tum bahut khoobsoorat ho, tum jaisi 'auraton ki mu'aashare ko bahut zaroorat hai, Allah Ta'aala sab behtar karenge, tum zarra baraabar fiqr mat karo wagayrah, agar samajhdaar hogi to samajh jaayegi ke yeh shakhs mujh me ragbat rakhta hai aur 'iddat ke baad nikah karega.)

Aysi 'aurat jo pehle hi se kisi ke nikah me ho ya talaaqe raj'ee ki 'iddat guzaar rahi ho ya us par kisi aur ka paigaam aa chuka ho aur 'aurat ki taraf se koi Jawab diya gaya ho to aysi 'aurat ko na to saraahatan paigaame nikah dena jaaiz hai aur na hi ta'reez w isharah kinaayah me yane paigaame nikah dena haraam hai.

Agar 'aurat wafaat ki 'iddat guzaar rahi ho ya talaaqe baayin ya khula'a ya faskh-e-nikah (cancellation or dissolution of marriage) ki 'iddat guzaar rahi ho to aysi 'aurat ko sirf isharah aur kinaayah me paigaame nikah dena jaaiz hai saraahatan paigaame nikah dena jaaiz nahi.

Mangni ki haisiyat mahaz wa'adaye nikah ki hoti hai, lihaaza mangni ke bagair bhi nikah durust hai, neez mangni karne ko zaroori samjhna bhi galat hai. Isi tarah is mauke ki munaasabat se len den ka mu'aamalah karna, mehfilein sajaana, poore gaon (village) ko jama'a karna, aatish bazi karna, nachna gaana, yeh sab khilaafe shara'a a'amaal hain, jin se bachna zaroori hai. (Minhaajut-Talibeen: 1/205, Fathul-Wahhab: 2/40, Bukhaari: 2140, Wal Lafz lahu wa Muslim: 1413). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Do Behnon Ko Ek Saath Nikah Me Rakhna

Sawal: Kya ek saath do behnon ko nikah me rakh sakte hain?

Jawab: Do behnon ko ek saath nikah me nahi rakhkha ja sakta. Allah Ta'aala ka irshaad hai: “Aur Haraam qaraar diya gaya hai ke tum do behnon ko (Nikah mein) jama'a karo.” Albatta ek ke intiqaal ke baad us ki bahan se nikah kar sakte hai. (Al-Majmoo': 16/226). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Saali (Biwi Ki Bahan) Ki Beti Se Nikah

Sawal: Kya saali (Biwi ki bahan) ki beti se nikah jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Biwi ki maujoodgi me saali ki beti ya'ani biwi ki bhaanji se nikah jaaiz nahi. Jaisa ke hadeese mubarak me hai ke Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaya: “Na to 'aurat aur us ki phuphi ko ek nikah me jama kiya ja sakta hai, aur na 'aurat aur us ki khalah ko.” (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 5109) lekin biwi faut ho jaaye ya use talaaq di jaaye aur talaaq ke baad 'iddat guzar jaaye to phir biwi ki bahan ya bhaanji se nikah karna halaal ho jaayega. (Al-Majmoo': 16/223). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Baap Shareek Aur Maa Shareek Bahan Se Nikah

Sawal: Allaati (baap shareek) aur Akhyaafi (Maa shareek) bahan se nikah ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Islam me behnon se nikah karna haraam hai, bahan chaahe haqeeqi ho ya razaayi (dudh shareek) ho ya akhyafi (maa shareek) ho ya allaati (baap shareek) ho in ke saath nikah hamesha ke liye haraam hai. (Surah Nisaa: 23, Al-Majmoo': 16/215). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haalate Haiz Me Nikah

Sawal: Kya haalate haiz me nikah karna durust hai?

Jawab: Nikah ke sahi hone ke liye shart yeh hai ke nikah ke waqt 'aurat kisi doosre shakhs ki 'iddat me na ho. Lihaaza haiz ki haalat me nikah sahi hoga. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 11/172). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Naamard (Impotent) Se Shadi Karne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya 'aurat aise shakhs se nikah kar sakti hai jo mubaasharat (sexual intercourse) karne ke laayiq na ho ya'ani naamard (impotent) ho aur kya nikah karne ke baad 'aurat ko faskh-e-nikah (cancellation or dissolution of marriage) ka haq baaqi rahega?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek wati aur jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne ka haq shohar ka hota hai, phir bhi us ki yeh akhlaaqi zimmedaari hai ke waqtan fawaqtan mubaasharat (sexual intercourse) karke use paakdaaman rakhe, mazkoorah baala mas-aleh me 'aurat ka is baat ko janne ke baawajood ke hone wala shohar naamard hai, nikah karna jaaiz hai, albatta 'aurat ko yeh ikhtiyaar baaqi rahega ke jab chaahe qazi ke paas ja kar nikah faskh kar sakti hai. (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 5/45). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bank Me Kaam Karne Waali Ladki Se Shaadi Karna

Sawal: Bank me kaam karne waali ladki se shaadi karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Fi NafsihI us ladki ke saath nikah karna to durust hai, nikah ke baad nufqah ki zimmedaari to shohar par hoti hai lihaaza shohar us ka kharch bardaasht karega aur use naukri chodne par majboor karega kyunke bank ki naukri karna shar'an jaaiz nahi (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 4/107). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shohar Ka Jismaani 'Ayb Chupa Kar Kiye Gaye Nikah Ka Hukm

Sawal: Shohar ka jismaani 'ayb chupa kar kiye gaye nikah ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Agar waqa'ee dhoke me rakh kar 'aurat ka nikah aise ma'ayoob mard se karaaya gaya ho to us 'ayb ke rehte huye huqooqe zaujiyat ada na kar sakta ho to qaaziye Sharee'at ke paas ja kar us ke 'uyoob ko saabit karna zaroori hoga phir qazi sahib us ka nikah faskh karenge. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/114, Nihaayatul Matlab: 15/486). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Miyan Biwi Ikhatte Islam Layein To Saabiqa Nikah Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar miyan biwi ikhatte Islam layein to un ke nikah ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Mazkoorah soorat me un ka nikah barqaraar rahega, islam lane ke baad dobaarah nikah karne ki zaroorat nahi. (Al-Waseet fil Mazhab: 5/131). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Zaujain (Married Couple) Me Se Koi Ek Murtad Ho Jaaye

Sawal: Biwi agar kafir aur murtad ho jaaye to nikah ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Biwi aur shohar me se koi ek ya dono islam ko chod kar murtad ho jaaye to dekha jaayega ke islam ko chodna nikah ke baad pehli martabah ki sohbat se pehle huwa hai ya baad me agar sohbat se pehle murtad hui hai to usi waqt nikah faskh ho jaayega aur agar sohbat ke baad murtad hui ho to 'aurat ki 'iddat ke mukammal hone tak yane teen tuhar (teen paaki ke ayyam tak) intizaar kiya jaaye ga aur 'iddat mukammal hone se pehle agar wo eemaan qabool karti hai to shohar ke nikah me barqaraar rahegi. Agar 'iddat me eemaan na laye to teen tuhar mukammal hote hi shohar se us ka nikah toot jaayega. Ab us se jismaani ta'alluqaat banaaye rakhna haraam hoga aur zina shumaar hoga aur us ke nateeje me hone waali aulaad ka nasab us shakhs se nahi judega…. Is ke baad agar wo eemaan qabool karti hai aur saabiqah (former) shohar se milne ki mutamanni hai to az sare nau nikah karna zaroori hoga. (Al-Gaayah fi Ikhtisaarin Nihaayah: 5/187). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Wali Ki Ijaazat Ke Bagair (Ghar Se Bhaag Kar) Shaadi

Sawal: Kya Shafi'ee maslak ke e'etibaar se wali ki ijaazat ke bagair (ghar se bhaag kar) shaadi karne se nikah sahi hota hai?

Jawab: Shafi'ee maslak ke e'etibaar se nikah sahi nahi hoga (Al-Muhazzab fi Fiqhil-Imam ash-Shafi'ee: 2/426). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Ghar se bhaag kar shaadi karne se nikah sahi nahi hota hai is ki daleel kiya hai? Mai ne suna hai ke Hanafi me nikah ho jaata hai! Baaqi maslak ka kya?

Jawab: Hadees me hai ke wali ke bagair nikah nahi (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 1102). Ahnaaf ke nazdeek chand sharaait ke saath ho jaata hai. (Al-Mabsoot: 5/10). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Ahnaaf ke nazdeek daleel kya hai? Aaj kal bhaag kar shaadi waale qisse bahut ho rahe hain. Kuch Shafi'ee naujawaan aisa karke Deoband ke fatwe ka hawaalah dete hain ke nikah ho jaata hai?

Jawab: Maslake Shafi'ee me wali ki ijaazat ke bagair nikah nahi hota, albatta ahnaaf ke nazdeek chand sharaait ke saath nikah ho jaata hai, in ke nazdeek jab ladka ya ladki baalig ho jaaye to baap dada ki wilaayate ijbaar (compulsory guardianship) us par se khatam ho jaati hai yane wo apni marzi se nikah kar sakte hai agarche wali ki ijaazat aur marzi se nikah karna behtar aur pasandeedah hai. Muslim Shareef ki riwaayat me hai:

اَلْاَيِّمُ اَحَقُّ بِنَفْسِهَا مِنْ وَلِيِّهَا... اِلىٰ آخِرهِ

Is riwaat me Al-Ayyimu (اَلْاَيِّمُ) ka lafz aaya hai jis ka matlab hai bagair shohar waali baaliga ladki khwah baakirah ho ya sayyibah wali ki ijaazat zaroori nahi chunaanche daure risaalat me Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke paas ek 'aurat aayi aur kehne lagi mere baap ne mera nikah ek aise shakhs se kara diya hai jo mujhe pasand nahi to Huzoor Paak (ﷺ) ne us nikah ko khatam kiya aur farmaaya tumhara nikah nahi huwa jaao jis se chaaho nikah kar lo (Muslim:1421, Badayius Sanaaye'a: 2/248). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Doosre Nikah Ke Liye Biwi Ki Ijaazat Shart Nahi

Sawal: Sharee'ate mutahharah ne ek mard ko chaar nikah ki ijaazat di hai to kya ek se zaa-id nikah ke liye pehli biwi se ijaazat shart hai?

Jawab: Doosre nikah ke liye pehli biwi se ijaazat shart nahi (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 2036). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Ahle Kitaab (Jews, Christian) Ki 'Auraton Se Nikah

Sawal: Kya ahle kitaab (Yahoodi - Jews, Isa'ee - Christian) Ladki se nikah karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Irshaade Baarie ta'aala hai: Ahle kitaab ki paak daman 'auratein bhi tumhare liye halaal ki gai hain (Al-Maaidah:5) Shawafe' ke nazdeek isi aayate Kareema ki wajah se ahle kitaab , 'isaa'ee (Christian) ya yahoodi (Jews) 'auraton se nikah karne ko jaaiz aur halaal kaha gaya hai, is shart ke paaye jaane ki soorat mien ke us shakhs ko is baat ka 'ilm ho ke jis kitaabi 'aurat se wo nikah karna chaahta hai us ke aaba'a wa ajdaad (Baap, dada, pardada upar tak) 'isaa'ee ya yahoodi mazhab me us ke mansookh hone se pehle daakhil huye the lihaazah yahoodiyat (Judaism) aur 'isaaiyat (Christianity) mansookh hone ke baad agar koi yahoodi ya 'isaa'ee mazhab ikhtiyaar karta hai to wo ahle kitaab me shumaar nahi hoga aur un ki 'auraton se nikah karna bhi jaaiz nahi.

Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) farmaate hain ke musalmaan mard ke liye ahle kitaab (yahoodi - Jews ya nasaara - christian) ki aazaad 'auraton se nikah karna halaal hai kyunke Allah Ta'aala ne bagair istisna'a ke in 'auraton ko halaal kiya hai, lekin meri dili khwahish hai aur mai chaahta hun ke koi muslim mard in kitaabi 'auraton se nikah na kare.

Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke is qaul ki taayid Hazrat Umare Farooq (Rz) ke is mashhur waqiya se hoti hai ke jab in ko ittila'a mili ki Hazrat Huzaifah Bin Yamaan (Rz) ne Madaayin pahonch kar ek yahoodi (Jews) 'aurat se nikah kar liya hai to Aap ne khat likh kar un ko talaaq dene ka hukm diya. Hazrat Huzaifah (Rz) ne Jawab me likkha ke kya wo mere liye haraam hai? To dobaarah Hazrat 'umr (Rz) ne is mazmoon ka khat Hazrat Huzaifah (Rz) ko tehrir farmaya: “Aap ko qasam deta hun ke mera yeh khat apne haath se rakhne se pehle hi us ko talaaq de kar aazaad kar do kyunke mujhe yeh khatrah hai ke doosre musalmaan bhi aap ki Iqtidaa na kare aur ahle zimmeh ahle kitaab ki 'auraton ko un ke husn wa Jamaal ki wajah se musalmaan 'auraton par tarjeeh dene lagein to musalman 'auraton ke liye is se badi museebat kya hogi (Al-Talkhisul Habeer: 3/1199, Al-Maaidah: 5, Mughniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/312, Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 9/6655, Kitaabul Umm: 5/7). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Nikah-E-Muta'aa / Muta-'ayyan Muddat Ke Liye Nikah

Sawal: Kya islam me nikahe muta'aa/ Muta-'ayyan Muddat ke liye (Short Term Contract Marriage) jaaiz hai jis me mahar aur nikah ki muddat ko muta-'ayyan kar ke mard aur 'aurat mu'aahadah (agreement) par dastakhat karte hain aur muddat khatam hone ke baad dono juda ho jaate hain?

Jawab: Islam ke ibtidaayi daur me Muta'aa (Short Term Contract Marriage) jaaiz tha, lekin jab Qur-aan me nikah, talaaq aur 'iddat ke ahkaam mustaqillan naazil huye to is ke haraam hone ka hukm diya gaya, logon me yeh mashhoor hai ke Hazrat 'umr (Rz) ne muta'aa ko haraam kiya, is ki koi haqeeqat nahi hai kyunke sahi ahaadees se saabit hai ke “Muta'aa” ko Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) hi ne qayaamat tak ke liye haraam qaraar diya hai.

Hazrat Ali (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne gazwah Khaibar ke din “Muta'aa ke nikah” se aur “Paltu/desi gadhon” ka gosht khane se mana'a kiya (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 4216). (Al-Umm: 8/276, Ibne Maajah: 1963, Saheeh Bukhaari: 4216). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bewa 'Aurat Ka Nikah Karaane Ka Haq

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat ke shohar ka intiqaal ho jaane ke baad us ke susraal waale us ka nikah kisi aur jagah karaana chaahe aur us ke maike waale kisi aur jagah to aise waqt kis ki baat ko tarjeeh di jaayegi?

Jawab: Aysi ladki ka nikah khud us ki ijaazat wa ikhtiyaar par munhasir hoga na us me maa baap ke ikhtiyaar ko koi dakhal hoga na susraal waalon ko (Muslim: 1421). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Biwi Ya Shohar Ke Intiqaal Ke Baad Nikah Ka Baaqi Rehna

Sawal: Kya shohar ya biwi me se kisi ki wafaat par nikah Toot jaata hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek maut waqe'e hone ki wajah se nikah nahi toot-taa (Fathul-'Aziz: 8/249). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Kya shohar ke intiqaal ke baad 'aurat ke liye us ka sasur (Shohar ka baap) mahram hi shumaar hoga? Isi tarah kya biwi ke intiqaal ke baad saas (Biwi ki maa) us mard ke haq me mahram hi shumaar hogi?

Jawab: Ji ha! Yeh dono hamesha ke liye mahram hain, chaahe shohar ya biwi ka intiqaal ho jaaye ya talaaq waqe'e ho jaaye (Surah Nisaa:23, Bahrul-Mazhab: 9/196). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Jannat Me Momin 'Auraton Ka Nikah

Sawal: Jannat mien momin mard ko hoor milegi to momin 'auraton ko kya milega?

Jawab: Momin 'aurat agar shaadishudah thi tab to use us ka apna shohar milega jab ke wo ahle jannat se ho warna aur kisi momin mard se us ka nikah kar diya jaayega. Aur yeh Sawal momin 'auraton ko kya milega? To is silsile me ahle jannat ke bare me yeh aayate Qur-aani peshe nazar rehni chaahiye: “To koi bhi nahi janta ke un ke liye kya kuch in ki aankhon ki thandak chupa kar rakhi gai hai.” (Surah Sajdah: 17). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Zina Ke Sabab Haamilah 'Aurat Se Zaani Ka Nikah

Sawal: Agar koi ladka kisi ladki se zina kare aur us zina ke nateejah me 'aurat haamilah ho jaaye to kya us zaani ladke ka us zaaniyah haamilah ladki se nikah durust hai?

Jawab: Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) aur Imaam Abu Hanifah (Rh) aur imaam Muhammad Rahimahumullah ke nazdeek aysi 'aurat se nikah karna jaaiz hai jo zina ke sabab haamilah hui ho chaahe zaani khud kare ya koi doosra shakhs kare… kyunke zina ke paani ke liye koi hurmat nahi hoti.

Is daleel se ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke: Bachchah Saahibe Bistar ke liye hai (Ya'ani jis ki biwi ya laundi hogi bachchah usi ko milega) aur zaani ke liye patthar hai. (Fathul-Bari: 12/32, Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 16/272). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya zaaniyah se nikah karne ke baad humbistari karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Shawafe' ke nazdeek jaaiz hai. Kyunke zina ke paani ke liye koi hurmat nahi hain. (Asnal-Matalib: 3/148). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Zina ke nateeje me paida hone waale bachche ka kya hukm hai jabke us ka nikah usi zaani se huwa ho?

Jawab: Us ka nasab maa se aur maa ke rishtedaaron se judega aur wo bachcha un ka waaris hoga. Zaani se us bachche ka koi rishtah aur ta'alluq nahi rahega. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 9/218). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zina Ke Hamal Ko Chupa Kar Kisi Se Nikah Karna

Sawal: Agar koi ladki kisi se zina kare aur us ko hamal bhi theher jaaye aur hamal thaher jaane ke baad ladki apni badnaami ke dar se kisi ko bataaye bagair kisi aur ladke ke saath nikah kar leti hai to kya yeh nikah durust hoga?

Jawab: Surah Baqarah me Allah Ta'aala ka paak irshaad hai ke: Agar wo 'auratein Allah aur Aakhirat ke din par eemaan rakhti hain to un ke liye halaal nahi (Haraam hai) ke jo bachcha Allah Ta'aala ne un ke rahamon me paida kar diya hai use chupaye (Surah Baqarah: 228).

Zaaniyah ka apne hamal ko chupana ek jurme 'azeem aur bada gunah hai aur yeh aayat daleel hai is baat par ke is 'aurat ka eemaan Allah Ta'aala ki zaate Aqdas aur aakhirat ke din par kamzor hai. Uska nateejah yeh hoga ke aaindah bhi yeh 'aurat nikah ke baad khayaanat karegi aur paraaye mardon se zina ka irtikaab karegi aur bachche ki nisbat saahibe firaash (Shohar) ki taraf karegi. Isi tarah is khayaanat ke nateeje me be shumaar jaraaim (crimes) aur mafaasid (harmful consequences) ujood me aayenge jaise ek naajaaiz bachcha ki nisbat ek aise shakhs ke nisbat se jud jaayegi jo us ka nahi, jiske sabab wo bachcha ek paraaye mard ka waaris banega goya doosron ke maal par daaka daalne waala shumaar hoga. Isi tarah us shakhs ke mahram rishtadaar 'auraton se ikhtilaat hoga jo ke paraaye mard ke liye haraam hai. 'aurat ke ek jhoot aur khayaanat ki wajah se yeh aur in jaise bahut saare mafaasid aur jaraaim (crimes) ujood me aayenge. Allah Ta'aala ka khauf rakh kar agar wo 'aurat shuru' me apne jurm ka iqraar karke taubah karti to itne saare mafaasid ujood me na aate.

Is ke baawajood agar koi shakhs us 'aurat se nikah karta hai to Shawafe' ke nazdeek us ka nikah sahi hoga aur us se wati aur sohbat karna bhi halaal hoga, kyunke zina ke paani ke liye koi hurmat nahi hoti. Albatta wo hamal jo ke zina ka hai, wo naajaaiz bachcha kehlayega aur us bachche ki nisbat us ki maa se judegi, shohar se nahi. Shohar ke liye zaroori hai ke us bachche ko apni waraasat se be dakhal kare, is ka tareeqah yeh hai ke shohar qaazi ki maujoodgi me is bachche ki nafi kare ke yeh bachcha zina ka hai mera nahi. Agar nafi nahi karega to wo bachcha shohar ke nisbat se jud jaayega, kyunke shar'an bachche ki nisbat saahibe firaash hi ki taraf hoti hai. (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer: 2/218). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Da'awat Aur Waleemah

Sawal: Agar kisi ke ghar me ladki ki shaadi hai aur nikah bhi ladki ka hai aur saahibe istitaa'at bhi hai to ladki ki taraf se rishtedaaron, padosiyon aur dost ahbaab ko nikah ke baad khane ki da'awat dena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Nikah ka e'alaan karne ka hukm hai isliye kuch logon ko bulaaye ya'ani da'awat de to koi haraj nahi, jahan tak khaane ki da'awat ka mas-alah hai to waleemah to shohar ke zimmeh hai, lekin 'aam si da'awat aur khushi ke mauqa'a ki munaasabat se ho to aur baat hai lekin zaroori samajh kar ya riwaaj ke taur par ho to munaasib nahi. (Kitabul Fiqh - Maulana Khalid Saifullah: 4/417). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar shohar door hai jahaan rishtadaaron aur doston ko lejaana mumkin nahi hai aur waleemah dena hai to agar ladki ki taraf se nikah ki da'awat de to kaisa hai?

Jawab: Az rooye Sharee'at waleemah khilaana shohar ke liye mashru' wa masnoon hai. (Bukhaari: 5167), albatta door se aaye huye mehmaanon ke liye khaane ka intizaam dulhan ka wali karta hai to koi muzaaiqah nahi hai, chaahe thoda ho, neez agar wali ya khud dulhan dulhe ki ijaazat se waleemah kare to shohar ki taraf se sunnat ada hogi. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 3/555). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Waleemah Ki Dawat Qabool Karna

Sawal: Aaj kal shahron me aksar nikah aur waleemah ki taqreeb / program me mardon aur 'auraton ke liye common stage aur khaane ka program hota hai, kya aise program ki da'awat ko qabool karna zaroori hai?

Jawab: Waleemah ki da'awat qabool karna waajib hai lekin aysi taqreeb jahan mardon aur 'auraton ka ikhtilaat ho, gaana bajana, tasweer kashi, video graphy, sharaab noshi ki jaa rahi ho aur Sharee'at ke ahkaam ki khilaafwarzi ho rahi ho wahan waleemah ki da'awat qabool karna waajib nahi balke aysi taqreeb me shirkat karna gunah hai (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 3/555). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Talaaq Aur Talaaq Dene Ka Ahsan Tareeqa

Sawal: Islam me talaaq ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Jab zaujain me naa ittifaaqi ho jaaye to rishtadaaron aur badon ko beech me le kar sulah ki koshish karna chaahiye kyunke Allah ke nazdeek talaaq naapasandeedah 'amal hai. Lekin jab naaittifaaqi is darjah badh jaaye ke nibah ki koi soorat na rahe aur hudoode ilaahi ki sahi paabandi na ho paaye to mard ke liye shar'an is ki ijaazat hai ke 'aurat ko talaaq dede, ya'ani islahe haal ki garz se talaaq de dena jaaiz aur mubaah hai (Surah Baqara: 229, Al-Umm: 6/457). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Islam me talaaq dene ka ahsan tareeqah kya hai?

Jawab: Islam me talaaq dene ka ahsan tareeqa yeh hai ke use tuhar (paaki ki haalat) me jis me mard ne 'aurat se sohbat na ki ho ek talaaq de aur faislah sahi ho to teen tuhar (teen pakiyon) ke khatam hone tak is par qaayim rahe warnah 'iddat me rujoo'u ke zariye talaafi kar le phir agar nafs dobaarah sabiqah haalat ki taraf lautaaye aur wahi baat is par gaalib aaye to phir doosri talaaq de phir jaanch le ke kya kaifiyat pesh aati hai agar nibah ho sakta ho to 'iddat me rujoo'u kare us ke baad bhi nihab na ho to teesri talaaq de de, teesri talaaq ke baad ab rujoo'u nahi kar sakta aur us ke saath nikah ka I'aadah bhi nahi kar sakta jab tak ke wo kisi doosre ke nikah me jaaye aur wo us ke saath dukhool aur jima'a (sexual intercourse) kare aur nikah ke baad aaindah wahan se judayi ho tab hi us ke liye halaal hogi ya'ani doosre shohar se 'iddat puri hone ke baad us ke saath dobaarah nikah kar sakta hai.(Al-Bayaan: 10/81, Bukhaari: 5317, Muslim: 1433). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Talaaq Dene Ke Baad Rujoo'u Aur Dobaarah Nikah

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs apni biwi ko ek ya do talaaq de aur phir rujoo'u kare ya 'iddat mukammal hone ke baad dobaarah isi tarah kare to ab shohar ko kitni talaaq ka haq baaqi rahega?

Jawab: Agar shohar ne ek talaaq di hogi to do talaaq ka haq baaqi rahega aur agar do talaaq di hogi to ek talaaq ka haq baaqi rahega. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 4/141, Surah Baqarah: 229). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar shohar apni biwi ko ek ya do talaaq de aur use waapas apne paas rakhna chaahta ho to kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah ke mutaabiq agar kisi ne ek ya do talaaqe raja'ee (revocable divorce) di ho aur 'iddat me hi is 'aurat ko apne paas rakhna chaahta ho to us se zubaani rujoo'u kar ke apne paas rakh sakta hai, nikah ki zaroorat nahi hai, aur agar 'iddat poori hone ke baad apne paas rakhne ka iraadah ho jaaye to naya nikah karna padega, nikah ke bagair wo 'aurat halaal nahi hogi. (Al-Bayaan: 225, Bukhaari: 5258).

Tambeeh: Haamilah 'aurat ki 'iddat bachcha janne tak aur gair haamilah 'aurat ki 'iddat 'umooman teen tuhar (teen paaki ke zamanon) tak hoti hai. (Surah Baqarah: 228, Surah Talaq: 4). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Ek Hi Majlis Me Teen Talaaq Ka Mas-alah

Sawal: Kya chaaron imaamon ke nazdeek teen talaaq ka mas-alah ek jaisa hai kyunke kuch 'Ulama kehte hain ke ek hi majlis me teen baar talaaq kehne se wo sirf ek talaaq shumaar hogi lekin imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek yeh teen talaaq hogi? Baraye karam is ki wazaahat kare.

Jawab: Chaaron Imaamon ke nazdeek aysi soorat me (Ek majlis me teen baar talaaq kehne se) teen talaaq waqe'e ho jati hai. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 9/1927). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Dauraane Haiz Talaaq Ka Hukm

Sawal: Dauraane Haiz talaaq ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Haiz ki haalat me talaaq dena yeh talaaqe bida'ee kehlaata hai, yeh haraam aur naajaaiz hai, albatta talaaq waqe'e ho jaati hai (Fathul-'Aziz: 8/481). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Jabran (Zabardasti) Talaaq

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek jabran (zabardasti) talaaq ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Jabarn ya'ani shohar ko marne wagayrah ki dhamki de kar talaaq dene par zabardasti ki jaaye aur shohar us dhamki ki wajah se talaaq de to talaaq waqe'e nahi hoti. (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 6/75, Ibne Maajah: 2046). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Dukhool Se Pehle Talaaq Ki Soorat Me Mahar

Sawal: Kya talaaq me aysi koi soorat hai jis me shohar ko sirf aadhi mahar ada karni padti ho?

Jawab: Agar jismaani ta'alluqaat qaayim hone se pehle talaaq waqe'e ho aur aqde nikah me mahar muqarrar ki gai ho to 'aurat aadhe mahar ki mustahiq hoti hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/238, Surah Baqarah: 237). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Khula'a Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Khula'a kise kehte hain?

Jawab: Khula'a ke lugavi maane nikalne aur utarne ke hain. Shar'an shohar ki taraf se lautne waale maqsood 'iwaz (badle) ke muqaabale me talaaq ke kisi lafz ya khula'a ke lafz se zaujain ke maabain judaai ko khula'a kehteh hain (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 4/128). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar 'aurat apne shohar se khula'a le to us ki 'iddat ka kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Khula'a lene par 'iddat ka wahi mas-alah hai jo talaaq ka hai. Haiz waali 'aurat ki 'iddat jo haamilah na ho teen tuhar (paaki) aur agar 'aurat haamilah ho to us ki 'iddat waz'e hamal (bachcha janne tak) aur bagair haiz waali ya'ani nabaaliga ya boodhi 'aurat ki 'iddat qamari teen maheene hai. (Surah Talaq: 4, Bidayatul Muhtaaj fi sharhil Minhaaj: 3/202). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya 'aurat khula'a lene ke baad apne shohar ke paas waapas ja sakti hai?

Jawab: Agar Khula'a ke waqt shohar ne ek ya do talaaq di hogi to wo 'aurat pehle shohar se dobaarah nikah kar sakti hai agarcha wo nikah 'iddat me hi kare is liye ke is soorat me 'iddat ko mukammal karna zaroori nahi hai lekin agar shohar ne teen talaaq di hogi to wo 'aurat ke pehle shohar ke nikah me waapas nahi aa sakti jab tak ke us 'aurat ka nikah kisi aur se ho phir sohbat bhi ho aur phir doosre shohar se judaai ho jaaye phir 'iddat guzaarne ke baad wo pehle shohar ke nikah me aa sakti hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 7/378). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'Aurat agar apne shohar se khula'a ka mutaalabah kare to shohar ko kya karna chaahiye?

Jawab: Agar 'aurat khula'a ka mutaalabah kare to shohar ko mu'aamalah ko hal karneki aur suljhaane ki koshish karni chaahiye lekin agar wo kisi wajah se inkaar kare to shohar use khula'a de kar aazaad kar de. (Surah Baqarah: 229, Al-Tadreeb: 3/188). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ki 'Iddat Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Islam me 'iddat ko mashru' (lawful/legitimate) kyun kiya gaya?

Jawab: 'iddat ko mashru' karne ki wajah pehle shohar ke paani (Mani) ki hifaazat karna hoti hai, aur raham ka pehle shohar ki mani se khaali hona batlana hota hai, kyunke agar bagair 'iddat shaadi karaadi jaaye to mani khalat malat ho jaayegi aur pata nahi chalega ke bachcha shohar ka hai ya doosre ka! Lihaaza agar zindagi me ek baar bhi sohbat hui ho to talaaq ke baad 'iddat (teen paakiyan) guzaarna zaroori hai agarche talaaq 20 saal ke baad kyun na de raha ho aur in bees saalon me ek baar ke 'alaawah kabhi bhi sohbat na kiya ho kyunke ab is soorat me yeh kaha jaayega ke yeh hukm “Ta'abbudi” hai, ya'ani Allah Ta'aala ki taraf se aaya huwa hukm hai, jis me wajah aur hikmat dekhi nahi jati. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 4/158, Surah Talaq:1). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Talaaq waqe'e hone ke baad 'aurat ki 'iddat ka kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Haiz waali 'aurat ki 'iddat jis ke saath dukhool kiya gaya ho aur jo haamilah na ho teen tuhar (paaki) aur agar 'aurat haamilah ho to uski waz'e hamal (bachcha janne tak) aur bagair haiz waali ya'ani nabaaliga ya boodhi 'aurat ki 'iddat qamari teen maheene hai. (Al-Ubaab: 3/290-291, Surah Baqarah: 228, Surah Taubah: 4). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat ko haiz aane se ek din pehle talaaq hui to kya us ek din ki paaki ko bhi teen tuhar me shumaar kiya jaayega?

Jawab: Ji han us paaki ko bhi teen tuhar me se ek shumaar kiya jaayega garcha wo paaki sirf ek hi din mili ho. (Al-Ubaab: 3/290). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Iddat Ke Bagair Nikah

Sawal: Agar koi shohar biwi ko talaaq de ya biwi khula'a le aur in ke darmiyan sohbat / humbistari na hui ho to kiya aysi mutallaqah ke liye bhi 'iddat laazimi hai?

Jawab: Nikah ke baad agar shohar ne ek baar bhi sohbat nahi kiya tha aur khula'a de raha hai ya talaaqe baayin de raha hai (ya'ani ek ya do ya teen talaaq jis ke baad shohar zubaani rujoo'u karke biwi ko dobarah nikah me na le sakta ho) to bagair 'iddat guzaare wo 'aurat kisi aur se fauran shaadi kar sakti hai kyun ke gair madkhool biha 'aurat (jis 'aurat ke saath nikah ke baad sohabat hi na hui ho) ke liye 'iddat nahi hoti. (Surah Ahzaab: 49, Al-Gaayah fi Ikhtisaarin Nihaayah: 6/135). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Daurane 'Iddat Mulaazamat Ke Liye Ghar Se Bahar Nikalna

Sawal: Agar koi 'aurat khula'a le ya talaaq shudah ho aur wo 'aurat mulaazamat bhi karti thi kya wo 'aurat dauraane 'iddat mulaazamat ke liye ghar se baahar nikal sakti hai?

Jawab: 'Aurat ka daurane 'iddat ghar se baahar nikalna jaaiz nahi jab tak ke koi majboori na ho. Fuqahaaye Kiraam hadees ki roshani me farmaate hain ke agar kisi 'aurat ka nafaqah shohar par waajib na ho aur wo 'aurat apna aur apne Bachchon ka naan wa nafaqah khud bardaasht kar rahi ho neez us ka naan wa nafaqah bardaasht karne waala koi doosra bhi maujood na ho to dauraane 'iddat us ke liye zarooratan ghar se nikalna jaaiz hai lihaaza zarooratan mulazamat ke liye ghar se baahar nikal sakti hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 5/106, Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/144).

Hazrat Jaabir Bin 'Abdullah (Rz) farmaate hain ke meri khaala ko talaaq di gai jab us ne apne khujoor (date) ke phalon ko kaatna chaaha to ek aadmi ne ghar se nikalne par unhain daanta to wo Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ki khidmat me haazir hui to Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya tum apni khajorein todo, ho sakta hai ke tum us me se sadaqah karo ya aur koi nek kaam karo. (Muslim: 1483). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

IZDIWAAJI ZINDAGI KE MASA-IL

Biwi Se Humbistari Karna

Sawal: Paaki ki haalat me biwi se kab humbistari karna masnoon hai aur tibbi e'etibaar se mufeed hai?

Jawab: Taqalluf (Mashaqqat) ke saath sohbat ki ifraat (z'iyaadati) nuqsaandeh hai. Baaz hazraat ne chaar raaton me se ek raat jima'a (sexual intercourse) karna masnoon qaraar diya hai, albatta agar biwi ke jazbaat ki wajah se us ki iffat wa paakdaamani ke liye mazeed jima'a (sexual intercourse) ki zaroorat ho to is ka khayal rakhna chaahiye sahar ke waqt sohbat karna hadees ki ittiba'a me mandoob (Mustahab) hai. (Ihyaau uloommiddeen: 2/50, Tuhfatul-Bari). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haalate Haiz Aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) Me Biwi Se Humbistari Karna

Sawal: Shohar ka haalate haiz aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) me apni biwi se humbistari karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Shohar ko biwi ke badan se har tarah ka intifa'a wa istimta'a (Lutfandozi) jaaiz hai, sirf dubur (pichli sharamagah) me dukhool karna haraam hai, isi tarah haiz aur Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki haalat me humbistari karna ghutne aur naaf ke darmiyaani hisseh se lutf andoz hona haraam hai is ke 'alaawah mukammal badan se har tarah ka intifa'a jaaiz hai (Ash-Sharhul-Kabeer: 2/410, Surah Baqarah: 222, Sunan At-Tirmizi: 144). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Safar Se Waapasi Par Biwi Se Jima'a (sexual intercourse)

Sawal: Safar se waapasi par biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Safar se waapasi par biwi ke saath jima'a (sexual intercourse) karna sunnat hai jaisa ke ahaadees me zikr hai, agar din me waapasi ho aur tanhaayi ka mauqa'a ho to din me biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) kare warnah raat mien (I'aanatul Taalibeen: 3/388). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kisi 'Aurat Par Nazar Pade Aur Wo Achchi Lage

Sawal: Agar kisi 'aurat par nazar pade aur wo achchi lage to islaam kya ta'aleem deta hai?

Jawab: Agar kisi 'aurat par nazar pad jaaye aur wo achchi lage to use chaahiye ke apni biwi ke saath jaakar sohbat kare jaisa ke hadees me maujood hai. “Jab tum me se kisi ko koi 'aurat achchi lage aur us ke dil me jaaguzein (baith jana, sama jana) ho jaaye to wo apni biwi ka rukh kare aur us se sobat kare, bilaashubah yeh 'amal us kaifiyat ko door kar dega jo us ke dil me (paida hui) hai.” (Muslim: 1403, Sharhul Muslim Lin Nawawi: 9/178). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sohbat Ke Waqt Kisi Doosri 'Aurat Ka Tasawwur Karna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs apni biwi ke saath sohbat ke waqt kisi doosri 'aurat ka tasawwur kare to kya yeh jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Apni biwi ke saath sohbat ke waqt kisi doosri 'aurat ke mahaasin (Khubsoorati) ka tasawwur karna, yahan tak ke yu khayaal kare ke us 'aurat se sohbat kar raha hai to kayi mutakhkhireen Muhaqqiqeen masalan Ibnul farkaah, Jamaalul islaam (rh), Ibnul Bazari (rh), imaam Suyooti (rh) wagayrah ne ise jaaiz qaraar diya hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 7/205). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haamilah Biwi Se Humbistari Karna

Sawal: Haamilah Biwi se humbistari karna kaisa 'amal hai?

Jawab: Sharee'ate Mutahharah me haamilah biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne ki koi mumaana'at waarid nahi hui hai, lihaazah haamilah biwi se humbistari karna jaaiz hoga jab tak ke biwi ko humbistari karne ki wajah se takleef laahiq na ho aur hamal ko nuqsaan pahonchne ka andeshah na ho, albatta biwi ki pichli sharmagah me watii karna isi tarah haiz wa Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding) ki haalat mein jima'a (sexual intercourse) wa humbistari karna haraam hai. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 4/47). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khaawind Aur Biwi Ka Barhanah Sona

Sawal: Kya Islaam me khaawind aur biwi ka barhanah sona jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Khaawind aur biwi ka barhanah sona jaaiz hai, isi tarah us ke poore jism ko dekhna bhi jaaiz hai, siwaaye sharmagah ke ke use dekhna makrooh hai. (Fathul-Bari: 1/364). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Biwi Ke Haath Se Apni Mani Nikaalna

Sawal: Kya biwi ke haath se mani nikaalna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Biwi ke haath se istimna'a (apni mani nikaalna) jaaiz hai lekin apne ya kisi doosre ke haath se haraam hai khwah zina ka andeshah ho (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 2/360). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Biwi Ki Pichli Sharmagah Me Dukhool Karne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Biwi ke pichli sharmagah me dukhool karne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Biwi ki pichli sharmagah me dukhool karna haraam aur gunahe kabeerah hai, chahe haiz ke dino me ho ya 'aam dino main, Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne aisa kaam karne waale par la'anat ki hai aur farmaaya “Aisa shakhs mal'oon hai jo 'aurat ki pichli sharmagah me jima'a (sexual intercourse) kare” (Al-Majmoo':16/416, Musnade Ahmed: 10206). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Biwi Se Chaar Maheene Door Rehna

Sawal: Kya Islam me biwi se char maheene door rehna haraam hai? Bairoon mulk mulazamat ke liye jaane ka shar'an kya hukm hoga kyunke biwi se ek se teen saal door rehna padta hai?

Jawab: Jaaiz kamaai ki garz se bairoon mulk jana mana'a nahi hai, lekin biwi ke huqooq ki adaayigi bhi zaroori hai, watii (humbistari) karna shohar ka haq hai, lihaaza jab tak ke wo watii na karne ki qasam nahi khaata, agarchah salha saal bhi watii na kare wo gunahgaar nahi hoga, lekin biwi se sohbat karna zaroori hai take us ki izzat wa naamus mehfooz rahe (At-Tahzeeb fil Fiqhish Shafi'ee Lil Bagavi: 5/469). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Biwi Kisi Aur Ke Saath Bhaag Jaaye Aur Waapas Aaye

Sawal: Agar biwi kisi ajnabi mard ke saath baagh jaaye aur kuch dino ke baad waapas aaye aur kahe ke mujh se galati ho gai. Ab Sawal yeh hai ke is 'amal ki wajah se us ka nikah toot jaayega ya barqaraar rahega?

Jawab: Shaadishudah 'aurat ka kisi ajnabi shakhs ke saath bhaag jana najaaiz wa haraam 'amal hai aur gunahe kabeerah hai, is ke is 'amal se nikah to nahi tootega lekin shohar ke liye munaasib yahi hai ke wo us ke 'uyoob ki pardah poshi karte huye use talaaq dede. Ab dekha jayrga ke agar us 'aurat ne yeh kaam halaal samajh kar kiya hoga to wo kafir aur murtad ho jaayegi aur us par murtad waale ahkaam jari ho jaayenge, aur agar wo is 'amal ko haraam samajhti thi phir bhi us ne yeh qadam uthaya to taubah wa istigfaar kar le Allah Ta'aala gunahon ko mu'aaf karne waale hain. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Nau Mah (9th Month) Ki Haamilah Se Jima'a (Sexual Intercourse) – Fawaaid Wa Nuqsaanaat

Sawal: Kya nau maah ki haamilah biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) karna makrooh hai?

Jawab: Haiz, Nifaas (Postpartum Bleeding), ihram aur I'etikaf ke 'alaawah kisi bhi waqt biwi se humbistari karna haraam ya makrooh nahi. Albatta tabeebe haaziq (Maahir aur tajrabah kaar Doctor) is waqt watii karne ko nuqsaandah bataaye to us ki baat ka e'etibaar karte huye e'htiyaat karna zaroori hoga. (Asnal-Matalib: 3/186). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya Haamilah biwi se humbistari karne se 'aurat ko koi nuqsaan pahonchta hai ya bachcha jald paida ho jaata hai?

Jawab: Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne to pait me paaye jaane wale bachche ko kheti se mushaabahat (resembling) di hai, aur aadmi ki mani ko us paani se mushaabahat (resembling) di hai jo kheti ko sairaab karta hai. Is hadees se ma'aloom huwa ke dauraane hamal jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne se janeen (pait me maujood bachcha) ki jismaani sihhat me behtari aati hai aur us ki khilqat ke naqaayis khatam hote hain. Bilkul isi tarah jis tarah kheti ko paani lagaane se fasal sar sabz wa shaadaab hoti hai. Lihaaza dauraane hamal apni biwi se jima'a (sexual intercourse) karna ma'ayoob nahi balke mustahsan (achcha) amr hai aur yeh janeen ke liye mufeed bhi. Ibne qayyim (rh) kehte hain, “Imaam Ahmed (rh) farmaate hain ke watii bachche ki samaa'at aur basaarat me izaafeh ka baayis banti hai.”

Asal ke e'etibaar se yahi hai ke hamal ki haalat me jima'a (sexual intercourse) karne se na to 'aurat ko aur na hi bachche ko koi nuqsaan pahonchta hai, aur hamal ke nateejeh me paida hone waale asaraat ke e'etibaar se hoga jo mukhtalif hote hain, aur use maahir lady doctor hi bataayegi. (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 19/353). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shaadi Shudah 'Aurat Ke Liye Mehndi Lagaane Ka Hukm

Sawal: Shaadi shudah 'aurat ke liye mehndi lagaane ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: 'Aurat ke liye jaaiz aur mustahsan aur mustahab hai ke wo apne shohar ke liye zeenat ikhtiyaar kare isi liye shaadishudah 'aurat ke liye apne shohar ki maujoodgi me uski ijaazat se mukammal haath aur pair ko mehndi lagaana masnoon qaraar diya gaya hai lekin mehndi cone wagayrah se design karna masnoon nahi, aur shohar ki ijaazat se jaaiz hoga aur ijaazat ke bagair design wa naqsh wa nigaari haraam hai. Kisi bhi 'aurat ko kisi ajnabi mard ke haath se design nikalwana shadeed haraam hai. (Al-Majmoo': 3/140). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Zaujain (Married Couple) Me Tanaazu'a Ki Ek Soorat

Sawal: Agar miyan biwi me shohar ke kisi galat kaam ki wajah se jhagdha ho gaya aur biwi maa baap ke ghar chali gai. Biwi ne waapas aane ke liye shart rakhi hai ke “pehle tamaam jaayidaad (Property) us ke naam karna hogi.” Aysi soorat me kya karna hoga?

Jawab: Ek deendar aqalmand aadmi shohar ki taraf se aur ek biwi ki taraf se bhej kar aapas me islaah karane ki fikr ki jaaye jaayidaad naam par karaana koi zaroori nahi yeh bejaa mutaalabah hai. (Al-Majmoo': 16/451). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'AURATON KE MUTAFARRIQ MASA-IL

Gair Shaadi Shudah 'Aurat Ke Liye Mehndi Lagane Ka Hukm

Sawal: Gair shaadi shudah 'aurat ke liye mehndi lagaana ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Gair shaadi shudah 'aurat ke haq me mutlaqan mehndi lagaana makrooh hai aur agar fitnah ka andeshah ho to us waqt mehndi lagaana haraam hai. Neez gair shaadi shuda 'aurat ke liye haath pair me naqsh wa nigaari karna aur design nikaalna mutlaqan haraam hai. Shaadi shudah aur gair shaadi shudah ko kisi ajnabi mard ke haath se design nikalwaana shadeed haraam hai. (Al-Majmoo': 16/451). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Naujawaan Ladki Ka Shara'ee Masa-il Ma'aloom Karne Ke Liye 'Aalim Se Baat Karna

Sawal: Kya shar'an naujawaan ladki ke liye kisi 'aalim se shara'ee masa-il ma'aloom karne ke liye baat karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Behtar hai ke kisi mahram ke waaste se 'Ulama se raabitah kare, agar mumkin na ho to badarjah Majboori Sharee'at ke hudood ki ri'aayat ke saath baraaheraast bhi baat kar sakti hai. (Al-Bayaan: 11/188). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Mard Ka 'Aurat Ko Salaam Karna

Sawal: Mard ka 'aurat ko salaam karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Mard ka apni mahram 'auraton ko salaam karne me ibtidah karna masnoon aur matloob hai aur Jawab dena waajib hai isi tarah gair mahram budhi 'aurat ko salaam karna jaaiz hai lekin agar 'aurat ajnabiyah ho aur fitne me mubtalah hone ka andeshah ho to mard ka ajnabiyah ko salaam karna jaaiz nahi phir bhi mard salaam kare to 'aurat ko Jawab dena waajib nahi agar ajnabiyah 'aurat mard ko salaam kare aur fitnah ka andesha ho to wo Jawab ki mustahiq nahi hogi mard ke liye makrooh hai ke us ko salaam ka Jawab de (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 9/299). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Saahibe Nisbat Buzurg Se Bay'at

Sawal: Kya 'aurat kisi saahibe nisbat buzurg se bay'at kar sakti hai?

Jawab: 'Aurat ke liye Sharee'at ke hudood ki ri'aayat ke saath kisi buzurg se bay'at karna jaaiz hai. Albatta bay'at ke liye musaafaha mamnu' hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj fi Sharhil-Minhaaj wa Hawaashi Ash Sharwaani Wa 'Abbaadi: 3//56). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Aurat Ka Mulaazamat Karna

Sawal: Kya 'aurat apne naan wa nafaqah ke liye mulaazamat kar sakti hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek waalidain ke zimmeh apne Bachchon ka naan wa nafaqah teen sharton me se kisi ek shart ke paaye jaane ki soorat me waajib hota hai. (1) bachche kam 'umr hone ke saath saath apaahij (disabled) hon. (2) aulaad tangdast hone ke saath saath apaahij hon. (3) aulaad tangdast hone ke saath saath majnoon hon. Lihaaza wo ladka ya ladki jo sahi saalim hon aur baalig ho jaayein to waalid ke zimmeh un ka nafaqah waajib nahi yeh khud mehnat mazdoori karke apne kharchon ka intizaam karenge. Isi wajah se 'aurat ka mulaazamat karna kuch sharaait ke saath jaaiz hoga.

(1) Koi kafaalat karne waala na ho, apna kharch khud sanbhalti ho. (2) safar ki zaroorat padti ho to mahram ke saath safar mumkin ho. (3) jis jagah mulaazamat karti ho wahan ajnabi mardon se saabiqah (former) na padta ho. (4) Parde ke ehtimaam ke saath mulaazamat mumkin ho. (Al-Ahzaab: 33, Sooratun Noor: 31, Saheehul Jaame'e: 765, Al-Majmoo': 18/300). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar 'aurat ka mulaazamat ke dauran ajnabi mardon ke saath ikhtilaat ho raha ho to kya us 'aurat ki tankhwah halaal hogi?

Jawab: Tankhwah ka koi mas-alah nahi, wo halaal hogi, albatta ajnabi mardon ke saath uthna baithna ya safar karna sahi nahi hai. Us ko is se bachna chaahiye, chahe wo mulaazamat karti ho ya na karti ho. (Majmoo' Fatawa Ibne Baaz: 4/293). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Tanha 'Aurat Ka Safar Karna

Sawal: Tanha 'aurat kitni doori tak safar kar sakti hai?

Jawab: hadeese paak me is baat ki saraahat maujood hai ke 'aurat ke liye bagair mahram safar karna jaaiz nahi, chaahe wo safar kam ho ya ziyadah. Albatta 'aurat apne hi gaon me ya hudoode iqaamat mein, rishtadaron ke paas ya bazaar wagayrah me hudoode Sharee'at me reh kar itni masaafat tak ja sakti hai jis par safar ka itlaaq na hota ho ((Saheeh Muslim: 827, Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim:9/103, Saheeh Ibne Hibban: 2732, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 4/25). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Ghar Me I'etikaf Karna

Sawal: Kya imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek 'aurat ka ghar me i'etikaf karna bid'at hai ya I'etikaf karne ki ijaazat hai?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke qadeem qaul ke mutaabiq 'aurat ghar me I'etikaf kar sakti hai lekin jadeed qaul ke mutaabiq 'aurat ka ghar me I'etikaf sahi nahi hai aur isi par fatwah hai. Albatta imaam Abu Hanifah (Rh) ke nazdeek 'aurat ka ghar me I'etikaf sahi hai (Al-Majmoo': 60/480). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla aur Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

'Aurat Ka Baal Kaatna

Sawal: Kya 'aurat apne shohar ki khaatir zeenat ikhtiyaar karne ke liye sar ke baalon ka kuch hissa kaat sakti hai?

Jawab: Is shart ke saath jaaiz hoga ke us ke baal mardon ke baalon ke mushaabah na ho jaayein. Isi tarah kuffaar 'auraton aur zaaniyah (badkaar 'aurat) ke mushaabah bhi na ho jaaye. (Ash-Sharhul-Kabeer: 597). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Jab kisi 'aurat ke baal jhadh rahe hon aur baalon ki tarteeb sahi na ho ya'ani kuch chote aur kuch lambe to kya aysi soorat me wo 'aurat baal kaat sakti hai? Kuch logon se suna hai ke use kaatne se dobaarah achche ghane aur lambe baal aate hain?

Jawab: Ji han. Is maqsad se kaat sakti hai lekin shart yeh hai ke mardon ke baalon ke mushaabah na ho jaayein isi tarah zaaniyah aur kuffar 'auraton ke mushaabah bhi na ho jaaye. (Fathur-Rahmaan fi -Sharhi Zubad Ibne Raslaan: 1/159). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Apne Bhanwon Ke Baal Katwaane Ka Hukm

Sawal: 'aurat ka apne bhanwon (Eye Brow) ke baal katwaane ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Beemaari ya kisi ashadd zaroorat ke bagair 'aam halaat me Bhanwon ke baal bareek karna ya ukhaadna ya katwaana mard wa khawaateen dono ke haq me haraam aur najaaiz hai. Fuqahaa aur muhaddiseen kiraam farmaate hain ke hadeese paak me Allah Ta'aala aur us ke Rasool (ﷺ) ki taraf se mutanammisaat par la'anat bheji gai hai is se muraad wo 'auratein hain jo apne bhanwon ke baal ukhedti hain, aur Allah Ta'aala ka ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ka kisi khaas kaam ke karne par la'anat ki wa'eed sunaana us kaam ke haraam hone aur bade gunaahon me se hone ki 'alaamat hoti hai. Is liye fuqahaa Rahimahullah ne bila zaroorate shadeedah mard wa 'aurat dono ke haq me bhanwon ke baal ukhedne ya ukhadwaane ko najaaiz qaraar diya hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/531). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya biwi shohar ki khushi ke liye bhanwon ke baal nikaal sakti hai?

Jawab: 'Aam halaat me to haraam hai, kyunke ahaadeese mubaarakah me aysi 'auraton par la'anat bheji gai hai jo bhanwon ke baal bareek karti hain ya karwaati hain. Lekin agar bhanwon ke baal bahut ziyadah phail gaye hon, aur 'aam banaawat se tajaawuz kar gaye hon jis ki wajah se shohar ko nagaawar ho rahi ho, ya baal aankhon ke ander jaarahe hon to is 'uzr ki bunyad par hazraate 'Ulama-e-kiraam ne itni had (limit) tak taraashne ki gunjaayish di hai ke wo 'aam fitri haalat par aa jaayein (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhil-Islami: 1/401) Wallahu Ta'aala A'alam (Sunane Abu Dawood: 4170). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ladkiyon Ka Bataure Air Hostess Kaam Karna

Sawal: Kya ladkiyon ka naan wa nafaqah ki justajoo me Air Hostess ka kaam karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Chunke Air Hostess 'aurat mustaqil safar me rehti hai, aur be pardagi w chust libaas ke saath ajnabi mardon ke saath ikhtilaat hota hai, neez un ke saath un ka koi mahram bhi nahi hota jabke bagair mahram safar karna bhi 'auraton ke liye jaaiz nahi to yeh safar kaise jaaiz hoga, lihaaza in wujoohat ke peshe nazar 'auraton ke liye air hostess ki naukari karna jaaiz nahi hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj fi Sharhil-Minhaaj wa Hawaashi Ash Sharwaani Wa 'Abbaadi: 9/111). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ka Samandar Ke Kinaare Tayraaki Karna

Sawal: Kya 'auraton ka samandar ke kinaare tayraaki karne ke liye jana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Jaaiz nahi, kyunke islam se pehle zamaanae jaahiliyat me 'auratein be pardah ghoomti phirti aur apne badan aur libaas ki zaibaayish ka 'alaaniyah muzaharah karti thien is bad akhlaqi aur be-hayaai par muqaddas islam ne rok lagaai aur us ne 'auraton ko hukm diya ke gharon me thahre rahe aur zamaanae jaahiliyat ki tarah bahar nikal kar husn wa Jamaal ki numaayish karti na phirein (Surah Ahzaab: 33) Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne bhi 'auraton ko ghar me rahne ki targeeb di aur ghar me rahne par ubhaara hai. Lihaaza jin jaghon me be-hayaai, be pardagi, uryaaniyat aur ba-kasrat buraayiyan payi jati hon aysi jaghon me jana jaaiz nahi. (Al-Gurarul-Bahiyyah: 1/347, Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:3/181). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton ka Ladies Swimming Pool jaane ka shara'ee hukm

Sawal: Shahar ki badi rihaayishi colony me 'auraton ke liye ladies swimming pool hote hain jis me mardon ko ijaazat nahi hoti yahan 'auraton ke jaane ka shara'ee hukm kya hai?

Jawab: Chand (some) sharton ke saath Kuwait ke muftiyane Kiraam ki jamaa-'at ne is ki ijaazat di hai. (1) Swimming pool khaalis 'auraton ke liye ho. (2) Mardon ki nazar se ojhal ho is tarah ke upar mukammal chatt ho aur chaaron taraf diwaarein hon aur mardon ka daakhila mamnu' ho. (3) 'auraton ko tayraaki sikhaane waali 'aurat ho jo is baat par bhi ma'amoor ho ke 'auraton ki nighrani karegi aur khilaafe Sharee'at kaam na hone degi. (4) Tayraaki ke makhsoos kapde 'auratein apne ghar hi se pehen kar aayein. (5) Kapde aise hon jo mukammal jism ko chupaane waale hon ya kam se kam ghutne aur sine aur in ke darmiyaan waale hisse ko chupaane waale hon. (6) Itne tangh na hon ke jism ke nasheb wa faraaz zaahir hon balke dheele hon, aur itne baareek bhi na hon ke kapde ke ander se jism ki rangat nazar aaye. (7) 'auratein doosri 'auraton ke samne na to barhanah hon aur na hi libaas tabdeel karein. (Lajnatul Fatawa Bi-Wazaaratil Auqaaf Wash-shu'oonil-Islaamiyah Al-Kuwait). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ka Motorcycle Aur Four-Wheeler (Car, Jeep Wagayrah) Chalaana

Sawal: Kya 'auraton ko motorcycle chalana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Gaadi chalaana asal waz'a me mardon ka kaam hai mardon ke saath mushaabahat (resembling) ki wajah se 'aam haalaat me 'auraton ke liye gaadi chalaana jaaiz nahi hai. Kyunke is se fitnah ka darwaazah khulega aur Sharee'at ki jaanib se mana'a kardah cheezein wajood me aayegi, bilkhusoos naa-mahram mardon se ikhtilaat, bepardagi, ishqbaazi wagayrah. Albatta shadeed zaroorat ho to zaroorat ke baqadr mahram mard ki maujoodgi me driving karne ki ijazat hogi. (Muktabas Az Deeni Masa-il Aur Un Ka Hal, Bukhari: 5885, Bukhari: 1862, Muslim:1341, Fatawa ash-Shabakatul-Islamiyyah:13/16403). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'Auraton ke four-wheeler (Car, jeep wagayrah) chalaane ke silsile me kya hukm hai? Jabke arab mamaalik me is ki ijaazat hai.

Jawab: 'Auraton ke driving karne ki hillat wa hurmat se muta'alliq koi hukm kitaab wa sunnat me maujood nahi kyunke us daur me motor car ya bike jaisi sawaariyan nahi thien, albatta Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) aur Sahaabah-e-Kiraam Rizwanullahi 'Alaihim Ajma'een se le kar aaj tak shurafa wa saaliheen ka yahi tareeqa aur 'amal chala aa raha hai ke sawaari ke janwaron ki q'iyaadat mard hazraat karte the aur 'auratein peeche haudaj au kajaawah ('auraton ke liye sawaari par baithne ka chota sa kamrah) me baith kar safar kiya karti thien. Yahi wo tareeqa tha aur hai ke jis se mardon ka qaayid aur haakim hona samajh me aata tha aur isi me 'auraton ki izzat aur ehteraam bhi tha. Agar 'aurat driving karna shuru' kar de to un ka waqaar aur izzat khatam ho jaayegi, aur khilaafe shara'a (against islamic principles) umoor ki murtakib bhi ho sakti hai, masalan raaste me gadi ki kharaabi ki soorat me in ko mukhtalif qism ki pareshaaniyon ka saamna karna pad sakta hai, saath hi saath gair mahram mardon se ikhtilaat aur mel milaap, mechanic aur rehguzar aur police ki soorat me wagayrah. Lihaaza 'auraton ke liye driving karne ki sirf zaroorate shadeedah hi ke waqt ijaazat hogi warna nahi (Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Suyuti: 1/60). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'aurat ke liye zaroorat ke waqt jab us ka mahram na ho kisi ajnabi ke saath safar karne se behtar khud driving karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Sharee'ate Mutahhirah ne 'auraton ko parde me rehne aur apne gharon ko laazim pakadne ka hukm diya hai, aur bagair mahram safar karne se mana'a kiya hai. 'auratein agar driving karna shuru' kar de to qawi imkaanat hain ke be shumaar khilaafe shara'a (against islamic principles) umoor wajood me aayenge. Neez Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) aur aslaafe ummat ke daur se lekar aaj tak shurafa aur saaliheen ka yahi ma'amol raha hai ke sawaari ke janwaron ki q'iyaadat aur un ki bag dor aur lagaam mardon hi ke haathon mein raha karti thi. Lihaaza shadeed zaroorat ke waqt hi 'aurat ke liye driving karne ki ijaazat hogi warna nahi. (Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Suyuti: 1/60). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ka Chehre Par Bleaching Karna

Sawal: Aaj kal 'auratein chehre par Bleaching karti hain jis se chehre par maujood baal sunhare rang ke ho jate hain, kya shar'an is ki ijaazat hai?

Jawab: Agar is 'amal se sirf baalon ki rangat me tabdeeli aati hai aur baalon par is ki parat jama nahi hoti to is me haraj nahi hai aur agar parat jama hoti ho to durust nahi hai kyunke is se Wuzoo ka paani baalon tak nahi pahonchega. Saath saath yeh baat bhi zahan nasheen rahe ke yeh zeenat mahaz shohar ki khatir kare to jaaiz hoga warna nahi. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:2/283, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/407, Rauzatut-taalibeen:1/175,). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ka Beauty Parlor Me Waxing Karna

Sawal: Kya 'aurat Beauty Parlor me ja kar Waxing ke zariye hathon aur pairon ke baal nikaal sakti hai?

Jawab: Aankhon ke upar bhanwon ke baal katna aur baarik karna, apne baalon me kisi insaan ke baal jod kar lambe karna khubsoorati paida karne ke liye daanton ke beech me lakeerein banaana wagayrah jo Allah Ta'aala ki takhleeq me tabdeeli ka baayis banti hai, jaaiz nahi. Albatta satr ka khayaal rakhte huye khud mahaz shohar ko khush karne aur use apni taraf maayil karne ke liye haath pair ke baalon ki waxing kar sakti hai lekin is kaam ko chodna behtar hai kyunke islam saadgi ki ta'aleem deta hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:3/103, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:1/407). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Auraton Ka Heel Waale Sandle Pehenna

Sawal: Kisi 'aalim ne bayan me kaha hai ke heel waale sandal pehenna galat hai, 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke is ka khulaasah kare.

Jawab: High heel sandal pehenne se khawaateen ka na sirf qad uncha hota hai balke wo numaayan bhi hoti hain aur un ki zaibaaish logon ke nazar me aati hain aur log us ki taraf maayil wa mutawajjah hote hain jo islam me mamnu' hai. Kyunke hadeese paak me aysi 'auraton par la'anat bheji gai hai jo paraaye mardon ko apni taraf maayil karne waali ho. Chunaanche Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: “Dozakh waalon ki do qismein aysi hain ke jinhein main ne nahi dekha, ek qism to un logon ki hai ke jin ke paas bailon ki dumon ki tarah kode honge jis se wo logon ko maarte phirenge aur doosri qism un 'auraton ki hai jo libaas pehenne ke bawajood nangi hogi. Wo mardon ko apni taraf maayil karegi aur khud mardon ki taraf maayil hogi, in 'auraton ke sar bukhti oonton ki tarah ek taraf jhuke huwe honge wo 'auratein jannat me daakhil nahi hogi aur na hi jannat ki khushboo pa sakeingi haalanke jannat ki khushboo itni masaafat (ya'ani door) se mehsoos ki jaasakti hai.” (Saheeh Muslim:2128) Isi tarah islam ne 'auraton ko yeh bhi hukm diya hai ke wo apni zaib wa zeenat ko chupaaye. Chunanche Allah Tabarak wa Ta'aala ka irshaad hai: Aur Aye Nabi (ﷺ) aap eemaan waliyon se keh do ke apni nigaahein neeche rakhein aur apni ismat ki hifaazat karein aur apni zeenat ko chupayein…………… aur apne paon zameen par zor se na maarein ke in ka makhfi zaiwar ma'aloom ho jaaye aur aye musalmaanon! Tum sab Allah ke samne taubah karo taake tum najaat paao (Surah Noor:31) Isi tarah is qism ke chappal ya joote pehenna tibbi e'etibaar se koi nafa'a bakhsh nahi. In wujoohaat ke pesh-e-nazar yeh hukm lagaana munaasib hoga ke high heel waale sandal ya chappal pehenna jaaiz nahi chunanche Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband me hai “oonchi yedhi waali jootiyaan mahaz fashion hain, un me koi qabile zikr faaidah nahi hai; is liye baher soorat oonchi yediyon waali jootiyon se parhez karna chaahiye.” (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Jismaani Warzish Ke Liye Gym Khaana Jaana

Sawal: Kya 'auraton ka jismaani warzish ke liye aise Gym khaana me jaana shar'an jaaiz hai jo makhsoos 'auraton hi ke liye banaaya gaya ho? Kyunke 'auraton ke ziyadah motaape ki wajah se Harmons ka tawaazun bighad jaata hai jis se haiz/mahwaari waqt par nahi aati aur wo mazeed motaape aur doosri beemaariyon ka sabab banti hai.

Jawab: Jismaani warzish sirf jaaiz hi nahi balke ek matloob cheez hai kyunke yeh sihhat ke liye mufeed saabit hoti hai lekin 'aurat ka jismaani warzish ke liye Gym ya Fitness Center jaana fitnah se khaali nahi is wajah se is ki ijaazat nahi, lihaaza behtar yahi hai ke wazan kam karne ke liye 'auratein apne gharon me hi wazan kam karne ki tadbeerein ikhtiyaar karein. (Fatawa Jami'ah Husainiyyah 'Arabiyyah Shrivardhan, Darul Iftah Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ko Mashriqi Raqs (Belly Dance) Sikha Kar Wazan Kam Karne Se Muta'alliq Hukm

Sawal: Jadeed Sciency Tahqeeqaat ke muta'alliq yeh baat saamne aayi hai ke khawaateen agar rozaana Belly Dance / Mashriqi Raqs ki mashq kare to is tareeqe ko apna kar wo apna kaafi wazan kam kar sakti hain. Mera Sawal yeh hai ke agar music ka isti'emaal kiye bagair ghar hi me reh kar 'aurat belly dance kare, to kya bataure 'ilaaj sihhat ko barqaraar rakhne ke liye shar'an is ki ijaazat hogi?

Jawab: Nachna, gaana aur moseeky yeh hamaari islaami tahzeeb nahi hai aur az rooye shara'a (according to islamic law) haraam hai, aur haraam asbaabe 'ilaaj ko ikhtiyaar karne se Sharee'at ne mana'a kiya hai.

Bukhaari wa Muslim ki riwayaat me waazeh taur par Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ka yeh irshaad maujood hai jis ka khulaasah yeh hai ke, Allah tabarak wa Ta'aala ne har marz ke liye us ka 'ilaaj yaqeeni taur par utaara hai, lihaaza hukm diya gaya hai ke hum kisi bhi beemaari ka 'ilaaj haraam cheezon me talaash na karein.

Mukhtalif gizaayi Diets, lambi masaafat tak subah wa sham chalna, halki phulki warzish aur deegar halaal dawaaon ka isti'emaal, yeh aysi halaal aur mauzoon cheezein aur tareeqe hain ke jin ke zariye hum apna gair zaroori wazan basahoolat kam kar sakte hain. Lihaazah wazan kam karne ke liye belly dance sikhna ya karna shar'an bilkul jaaiz nahi.

Chunancha Sayyiduna Abu Hurairah (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: Allah Ta'aala ne koi aysi bimaari naazil nahi farmaayi jis ki shifa na utaari ho (Bukhaari:5678).

Albatta is baat ka khayaal rakhna zaroori hai ke 'ilaaj me haraam cheezaon se ijtinaab kiya jaaye jaisa ke hadeese paak me hai: Beshak Allah Ta'aala ne bimaari aur dawa ko paida farmaaya hai, pas 'ilaaj karo magar haraam se 'ilaaj na karo. (Saheehul Jaame'e:1762, Al-Majmoo':5/106-107). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bewah 'Aurat Ka Zaiwaraat Aur Rangeen Wa Munaqqash Kapde Pehenna

Sawal: Kya islaam me bewah 'aurat sone (Gold) wagayrah ke zaiwaraat aur rangeen wa munaqqash kapde pehen sakti hai?

Jawab: Bilkul Pehen sakti hai, Sahaabiyaat Rizwanullahi 'Alaihinn Ajma'een se saabit hai. Bewah sone (Gold) ke zaiwaraat bhi pehen sakti hai aur rangeen kapde bhi, bas shart yeh hai ke wo 'iddat me na ho (Al-Bayaan:3/301). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aurat Ka Khushboo Laga Kar Baahar Jaana

Sawal: Jo 'aurat khushboo laga kar logon ke paas se guzre to wo zaaniyah (badkaar) hai. (Sunan At-Tirmizi:2786). 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke is ka khulaasah kare mera matlab hai ke kya koi 'aurat baahar jate waqt khushboo nahi laga sakti? Kya 'aurat sirf apne shohar ki maujoodgi me khushboo lagaaye?

Jawab: 'auraton ke liye phailne wali khushboo laga kar baahar nikalna jis se log un ki taraf mutawajjah hon najaaiz hai, aysi 'aurat gunahgaar hai is 'amal se ijtinaab zaroori hai, Abu Dawood shareef ki hadees me hai: “Hazrat Abu Moosa Ash'ari (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya har aankh zinaakaar hai (jab wo aysi 'aurat ki taraf dekhe jise dekhna haraam ho) aur 'aurat jab khushboo laga kar majlis ke paas se guzre (mardon ki majlis ke paas se) to wo bhi aysi aysi hai ya'ani wo bhi zaaniyah hai (Sabab ke darje me wo apne aap ko zina ke liye pesh karne waali hogi is tarah se ke us ne mardon ko aamaadah kiya ke wo us ki taraf nazare bad se dekhein aur yeh dekhna nazar ka zina hai aur basa auqaat nazare bad ke baad mu'aamalah yahan tak pahonchta hai ke bad nazari karne waala haqeeqee zina me mubtalah ho jaata hai) (Al-Majmoo':4/199). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

WILAADAT AUR US KE BAAD KE MASA-IL

Wilaadat Ke Baad Kaan Me Azaan Aur Iqaamat

Sawal: Bachcha paida hone ke baad us ke seedhe kaan me azaan aur bayein kaan mein iqaamat kyun padhi jaati hai?

Jawab: Asal baat yeh hai ke hamein Allah aur Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke hukmon me 'illat (wajah) talaash nahi karna chaahiye balke Allah aur Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ka hukm hone ki wajah se us par 'amal karna chaahiye. Waise to azaan aur iqaamat ki hikmat (maslihat) 'Ulama ne yeh bayan farmaayi hai ke sab se pehle bachche ke kaanon me tauheed ki aawaz pahonche. Jo is ke liye In sha Allah eemaan par baaqi rahne aur eemaan par khaatime ka sabab banega. 'aurat ke zariye kaan me azaan aur iqaamat kehna bhi jaaiz hai. (Tuhfatul Maulood bi ahkaamil maulood Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 8/231). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Nau-Maulood Ke Kaan Me 'Aurat Ka Azaan Dena

Sawal: Kya nau-maulood ke kaan me 'aurat azaan de sakti hai?

Jawab: Nau-maulud ke kaan me 'aurat ka azaan dena kaafi hai. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 8/231, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 8/149). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Tahneek Ki Hikmat

Sawal: Tahneek ke waqt khujoor (date) chaba kar bachche ke muh me kyun daalte hain?

Jawab: Tahneek ki hikmat yeh bataayi gai hai ke tahneek ki wajah se bachche ke akhlaaq bhi tahneek karne waale ki tarah achche banein. Is liye nek aur saaleh aadmi ke zariye karaana mustahab hai. 'aurat ke zariye tahneek karaana bhi mustahab hai. (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 5467, Rauzatut-taalibeen: 2/501). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Bachcha ki paidayish ke baad ladke ko mard ke zariye tahneek karaana aur ladki ko 'aurat ke zariye tahneek karaane se muta'alliq kitaabon me koi baat milti hai?

Jawab: Ahaadeese mubarakah me is baat ka suboot milta hai ke hazraate Sahaabah wa sahaabiyat razi allahu anhum ajma'een apne naumaulood bachche ko Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ki khidmat me tahneek karaane ke liye le jaaya karte thien phir Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) khujoor (date) se tahneek karaate aur bachche ke liye barkat ki du'aa farmaate, lihaaza behtar yahi hai ke kisi nek mard se tahneek kara le. Agar mard maujood na ho to tahneek ka yeh 'amal nek 'aurat ke zariye bhi sahi hoga. Albatta is baat ki saraahat kahein nahi mili ke ladke ko mard tahneek karaale aur ladki ko 'aurat. Wallahu A'alam. (Al-Majmoo': 8/443, (Saheeh Bukhaari: 3903). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar bachche ke paida hone ke baad koi deendar aur parhezgaar shakhs maujood na ho to kya teen din ke baad ya 'Aqeeqah ke pehle bachcha ko tahneek kara sakte hain?

Jawab: Tahneek ka asal waqt to fauran wilaadat ke baad hai, ya'ani dudh wagayrah pilaane se pehle lihaaza teen din ke baad tahneek ka koi matlab nahi wo to 'aam gizaa ke hukm me hoga. (Bukhaari: 3909, Muslim: 2146). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bachchon Ko Dudh Pilaane Ki Zimmedaari

Sawal: “Bachchon ko dudh pilaane ki zimmedaari maa ki nahi balke baap ke zimma hai ke bachche ke liye daayah (dudh pilaane waali) ka intizaam kare.”

Is buniyad par aaj kal ki padhi likhi (dunyaavi ta'aleem yaftah) 'auratein is ka galat isti'emaal karti hain ke yeh hamaare zimma nahi hai aur baqiyah masa-il nahi batatein! Huzoor (ﷺ) ka bhi hawalah deti hain ke unhonne apni maa ka nahi balke daayi Haleemah ka doodh piya. 'Ulama is ta'alluq se rehbari farmayein.

Jawab: Hamare maslak (Shafi'ee) me wilaadat ke fauran baad 'aurat ke seene se sab se pehle jo doodh nikalta hai use pilaana waajib hai jise 'arabi me “Al-Libaa” kehte hain. Aur hamari zubaan me “Cheek” kehte hain ya peusi ka dudh, yeh doodh bachcha ki paidaayish ke baad aata hai aur taqreeban do se paanch dino tak rehta hai. Is doodh me waafir miqdaar me lahmiyaat hote hain. Gizaayiyat ke saath saath is me amraaz se muqaabala karne ki salaahiyat bhi hoti hai, is ke 'alaawah yeh jarasimkash bhi hota hai. Is ke muta'alliq kaha jaata hai ke agar yeh na pilaaya jaaye to bachchah 'aam taur par mar jaata hai, ya is ka jism taaqatwar nahi ban pata kamzor rehta hai. (Asnal-Matalib: 3//445, Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 1/201).

Phir yeh peusi ka dudh (cheek) pilaane ke baad dekha jaayega.

Aur durust baat yeh hai ke Huzoor (ﷺ) ko kuch din un ki maa ne doodh pilaaya tha phir daayah ke paas diya tha, daayah ke paas dene me ek hikmat yeh thi ke dihaat ki fiza khushgawaar hoti thi to yeh bachchah sihhatmand rehta tha. Doosri hikmat yeh thi ke dihaat ki zabaan faseeh wa baleeg ya'ani 'arabi qawaayid ke mutaabiq hoti thi to bachpan hi se bachcha ki zabaan shastah wa shaayistah ho jaaya karti thi. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Daurane Hamal, Colostrum Nikaal Kar Freezer Me Rakh Kar Bachche Ki Paidaayish Ke Waqt Pilaana

Sawal: Science ki tahqeeq ke mutaabiq 'aam taur par hamal ke 16, 22 hafton ke darmiyan doodh ki paidaawaar shuru' hoti hai, is marhale me banne wale doodh ko Colostrum kaha jaata hai. Yeh ek zard rang ka gaadha doodh hota hai jo Calories aur beemaariyon se ladne wale anti-bodies se bharpoor hota hai jo bachche ki paidaayish ke waqt pehli khuraak ke taur par kaam karega. Colostrum ko kamre ke darja haraarat par chaar ghante tak zakhairah kiya ja sakta hai. Yeh refridgerator me chaar din tak reh sakta hai. Pumpshudah colostrum ko cheh maah tak (Behtareen me'eyaar ke liye) ya 12 maah tak freezer me rakh sakte hain. Dauraane hamal 'aurat ke pistaan se colostrum nikaal kar freezer me rakh kar bachche ki paidaayish ke waqt pilaane ka shara'ee hukm kya hoga?

Jawab: Yeh wahi doodh hai jo aksar wa beshtar aur umoomam paidaayish ke fauran baad maa ke pistaanon se nikalta hai aur kabhi kabhaar wilaadat se kuch maah pehle bhi nikalna shuru' ho jaata hai jise 'arabi me “Al-Liba'a” kehte hai.

Wilaadat ke baad jab yeh nikalta hai to is ki miqdaar bahut kam hoti hai, aur nikalne ki muddat bhi kam hoti hai, ya'ani taqreeban paanch dino tak aata rehta hai. Chunke in ayyaam me bachche ka maidah bahut hi chota hota hai, to itni miqdaar is bachche ke liye kaafi ho jati hai.

Maahir atibbaa (Doctor) ki tahqeeq yeh hai ke yeh doodh bachche ki jaan bachaane ke liye Allah Ta'aala ki taraf se milne waala ek 'azeem tuhfah hai, kyunke is ko piye bagair 'umooman bachcha zindah nahi reh pata. Isi wajah se hamare fuqahaa ne bachche ki maa par is doodh ko pilaana waajib qaraar diya hai, maahir atibbaa ki tahqeeq ke mutaabiq wilaadat ke fauran baad nikalne waale doodh me jo sifaat hoti hain wo wilaadat se pehle maa ke haalate hamal ke aakhari ayyam me hi pistanon me maujood doodh me paida ho jati hain, agar use bachche ki wilaadat se pehle maa us doodh ko nikaal le aur mahfooz karke freezer me rakhe take wilaadat ke baad kaafi muddat tak kaam aa sake to is soorat me is doodh me koi bigaad paida na huwa ho aur tibbi e'etibaar se is me koi zarar na ho to us ke pilaane me koi haraj nahi . isliye ke jis haamilah 'aurat ka hamal me bachcha ko dudh pilaana jaaiz hai, isi tarah wilaadat se qabl jab yeh doodh nikalta hai to us ko nikaal kar freezer me mahfooz karna aur wilaadat ke waqt bachche ko pilaana bhi jaaiz hai.

(Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 2/267, Fiqhul Islaam Sharh Buloogul Maraam: 7/76, Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 5/16. Asnal-Matalib: 3/44. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla & Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare

Milk Bank Ke Shara'ee Ahkaam

Daure Haazir me blood bank ki tarah 'auraton ke doodh ke “Milk Bank” qaayim hona shuru' hogaye hain yeh bank 'auraton se doodh khareed kar us ko jama'a karti hain phir us doodh ko bechti hain taake maa ya murziah (Foster Mother) ke doodh ke badle bachche us doodh se giza haasil kare. Is zimn me chand Sawalaat dar pesh hain.

Sawal (1): Bachche ka aisa doodh peene ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab (1): Haalate iztiraari me bachche ko milk bank ka doodh pilaane ki gunjaayish hai. Bagair kisi 'uzr ke doodh pilaane se bachna behtar hai. (Al-Mawsoo'atut-Tibbiyyah Al Fiqhiyyah: 487).

Sawal (2): Kya bachche aur jis 'aurat ka doodh hai us me hurmat paida hogi? Kya yeh 'aurat us bachche ki razaayi maa ban jaayegi aur yeh bachcha us 'aurat ki doosri aulad se nikaah na kar sakega?
