Jawab (2): Hurmate Razaa'at ke saabit hone ke liye baraahe raast 'aurat ke than se doodh peena zaroori nahi hai, lihaaza agar razaa'at ke sharaait ke saath kisi bachche ko milk bank se kisi makhsoos 'aurat ka doodh pilaaya jaaye to hurmate razaa'at saabit hogi, aur wo razaayi maa banegi phir un par razaa'at ke tamaam ahkaam jaari honge, phir us 'aurat ke doosri aulaad se nikah wagayrah jaaiz nahi hogi.

Is ke bar'aks agar kisi khaas 'aurat ka doodh na pilaaya gaya ho, balke muta'addad 'auraton ka jama shuda doodh pilaaya gaya ho to aysi soorat me in tamaam 'auraton se hurmate razaa'at saabit karna dushwaar hoga is liye ke doodh jama'a karne waali 'auraton ki ta'een mushkil hai ab chunke doodh pilaane waali 'aurat ka 'ilm nahi hai, lihaaza hurmate razaa'at saabit nahi hogi. (Al-Mawsoo'atut-Tibbiyyah Al Fiqhiyyah: 488).

Sawal (3): Kya 'auraton ke doodh ki khareed wa farokht jaaiz hai?

Jawab (3): Doodh chunke halaal cheez hai, insaani giza bhi hai, to 'aurat ke liye bagair kisi hurmat ke doodh ki bay'e wa shira'a jaaiz hai.

(Al-Majmoo':9/214).

Sawal (4): Us makhloot doodh (yeh doodh basa auqaat kayi 'auraton ka makhloot doodh hota hai) peene se kya asaraat murattab honge?

Jawab (4): Is me do tarah ke nuqsaan ka andeshah hai, pehla yeh ke makhloot doodh me kaafirah, faahishah, faasiqah 'aurat ka doodh bhi ho sakta hai jo ke akhlaaque wa aadaat me fasaad ka sabab ban sakta hai, aur bachche ki zindagi me shirk, zina, fahhaashi ke asaraat murattab ho sakte hain.

'Alaawah azeen doosra nuqsaan yeh hai ke 'aurat kisi aysi muta'addi (contagious) marz me mubtala (afflicted) ho sakti hai jo bachche ke andar muntaqil hone ka khadshah hota hai, lihaaza 'aurat ko hattal Maqdoor apna hi doodh pilaane ka ehtimaam karna chaahiye. Sirf haalate iztiraari me hi aise cheezon ko isti'emaal kare. (Tauzeehul Ahkaam min buloogil Maraam: 3/6). Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare

Bachche Ka Naam Rakhna

Sawal: Bachche ka naam rakhne ka sab se ziyadah haq kis ka hota hai? Maa, baap ya dada?

Jawab: pehle baap phir dada (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 8/230). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Wilaadat ke baad bachche ka kab naam rakhna chaahiye?

Jawab: Agar 'Aqeeqah karna ho to saatwein din naam rakhna sunnat hai warna wilaadat hi ke din ya saatwein din se pehle aur saatwein din ke baad bhi naam rakhna jaaiz hai. (Al-Majmoo': 8/336). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Aqeeqah Ka Din Aur Jagah

Sawal: Bachche ki wilaadat ke baad 'Aqeeqah kab karna chaahiye?

Jawab: 'Aqeeqah saatwein din afzal hai, agar nahi kar paaye to jab bhi kare saatwein din ke e'etibaar se kare ya'ani dino ke e'etibaar se saatwan din ho ya'ani yaume paidaayish se ek roz pehle kare masalan peer ko paidah huwa ho to itwaar ko 'Aqeeqah kare. (Sharhul-Muhazzab: 8/322). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: 'Aqeeqah kahan karna chaahiye, asal basti me ya jahan wo abhi muqeem ho?

Jawab: 'Aqeeqah jahan rehte hon wahan karna banisbat khaas mulk ke behtar hai. (Bahrul-Mazhab: 4/211). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Wilaadat Ke Baad Baal Mundwaana

Sawal: Wilaadat ke baad baal mundawaane ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Agar 'Aqeeqah karna ho to 'Aqeeqah ke baad saatwein din hi baal mundwaana sunnat hai aur is ke wazan ke baraabar sona ya chaandi sadaqah karna hai aur baalon ko dafan kar dena hai (Al-Majmoo': 8/324, Al-Bayan:4/467). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Wilaadat ke baad aksar Bachchon ke sar par bahut kam baal hote hain aur un ka wazan bhi bahut thoda hota hai jis ki wajah se itna kam miqdaar me sona dena aasan nahi to phir kya kare?

Jawab: Afzal yahi hai ke sona ya chaandi sadaqah kare lekin miqdaar ki kami ki wajah se majboori me is ki qeemat ke baraabar rupaye bhi sadaqah kar sakte hain. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji 'Ala mazhabil Imaamish Shafi'ee:3/60, Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 23/298). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Naumaulood Bachche Ke Baal Gair Muslim Ke Hathon Mundwaana

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs apne nau maulud bachche ke baal kisi gair muslim naayi ke hathon mundawaaye to kya yeh shar'an durust hai?

Jawab: Ji han gair muslim naayi ke haathon baal mundwaane me koi haraj nahi hai. (Al-Mu'tamad:3/214). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Khatnah Aur Kati Hui Chamdi

Sawal: Khatnah kab kare aur kati hui chamdi ka kya kare?

Jawab: Saatwein din us ka khatnah kare aur kati hui chamdi ko dafan kare. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:6/33). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bachchon Ko Pyaar Se Doosre Naam Se Bulaana

Sawal: Kya apne Bachchon ko pyaar se doosre naam se bula sakte hain? Jaise 'Amrah ko Ammu, Nusaibah ko Nushshu…

Jawab: Pyaar se is tarah bulaane me koi haraj nahi. Huzoor (ﷺ) kabhi kabhi Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) ko “Ya Aa'ish” keh ke pukaarte the. (Saheeh Al-Bukhari:3768). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Chote Bachchon Ke Gaal Par Kaala Daag (Teeka) Lagaana

Sawal: Baaz log chote Bachchon ke gaal par kaala daag (teeka) lagaate hain ya kaali choodiyan ya kaala dhagaa pehnaate hain is garz se ke un ko nazare bad na lage kya yeh durust hai?

Jawab: Nazare bad se hifaazat ke liye kuch log yeh mazkoorah aqeedah rakhte hain jo saraasar islaami ta'aleemaat aur tawakkul ke khilaaf hai aur daure jaahiliyat ke rusoom mien se hai…Janabe Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne hajjatul Wada'a ke mauqa'a par irshaad farmaaya ke “Aagah raho! Jaahiliyat ka har kaam mai apne in dono qadmon ke neeche dafan kar raha hun.” (Muslim: 2137).

Lihaaza in cheezon se bachna zaroori hai. Bachchon ko nazare bad se bachaane ke liye Mu'awwazatain (Surah "FALAQ & NAAS") aur masnoon du'aawon ka ehtimaam karna nafa'a bakhsh hoga. Jaisa ke Hazraat Hasnain (Rz) par Aap (ﷺ) yeh kalimaat padh kar dum kiya karte the:

أُعِيْذُ كُمَا بِكَلِمَاتِ اللّٰهِ التَّامَّةِ مِنْ كُلِّ شَيْطَانٍ وَّهَامَّةٍوَّمِنْ كُلِّ عَيْنٍ لَامَّةٍ

(Sunane Abu Dawood: 4737).

Albatta Ibne Qayyim (Rh) ne apni kitaab Zaadul Ma'aad mein, Imaam Baghavi (Rh) ki sharhus Sunnah se aur Imaam Khattabi (Rh) ki Gareebul hadees se Hazrat Usmaan Gani (Rz) ka ek asar naqal kiya hai, Hazrat Usmaan Gani (Rz) ne ek khubsoorat bachcheh ko dekha jo nazare bad se muta'assir tha, to irshaad farmaaya ke is ke thuddi me kaala nishaan laga do take use nazar bad na lage. (Zaadul Ma'aad:4/159).

Mumkin hai Wallahu A'alam ke jab Hazrat Usmaan (Rz) ne is bachche ko dekha to samajh gaye honge ke is ko nazare bad lagne ki wajah us ki khoobsoorati hai, to chehre ko daagdaar karne ka hukm diya take us bachche par kisi ki zahar aalood nazar na pade, lihaaza is maqsad ke tahat sirf kala daag is kaifiyat se lagane ki gunjaayish mil sakti hai ke chehra kuch waqt ke liye badnuma ho jaaye, aur waqti taur par logon ki buri nazar se mehfooz ho jaaye.

Waazeh rahe ke maujoodah daur me bachche ke rukhsaar par ya peshaani par isi maqsad ke tahat jis andaaz me teeka lagaaya jaata hai wo to bachche ke husn ko mazeed nikhaarta hai, to is daag lagaane ka jo faidah haasil hona chaahiye tha wo haasil nahi ho pata, lihaaza ab yeh zeenat ke qabeel ki ek cheez ho gai hai. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bahut Jald Manzar-E-'Aam Par Aane Wali Kitaab 📘 "Islami Bank-Kaari: Haqiqat, Zarurat Aur Ishqalaat Ka Izaalah"

Aaj ke daur mein jab ma'eeshat ka pahiyyah soodi nizaam ke gird ghoom raha hai, aisey mein Islami Bank-kaari sirf ek mutabadil nahi balke aik shara'ee taqaza bhi hai.

Isi aham zarurat ke pehlu ko madde nazar rakhte hue Jami'aa Zia-ul-'Uloom Kundloor – Karnataka ke Shu'bah Tadreebe Iftah ke ek taalib-e- 'ilm Maulavi 'Usman Sadi Papa ko yeh mauzoo' baraye tahqeeq diya gaya tha. Unhon ne apne ustad Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Mahmood Barmare Husaini aur Maulana Jasim Mahadik ki nigrani mein is mauzoo' par gehre mutaala'aah, 'ilmi tajziye aur mustanad dalaeelon ke saath ek jaame'e maqalah tahreer kiya tha.

Yeh maqalah na sirf Islami Bank-kaari ki bunyadon aur usoolon ko wazeh karta hai balke un ishqalaat aur e'etiraazaat ka mudallal aur 'ilmi jawab bhi pesh karta hai jo 'aam taor par logon ke zehno mein paida hote hain.

Ab yeh maqalah kuch zaroori aur jadeed masa-il ke izafe ke saath, 'awaami mafaad ke pehlu ko dekhte hue, kitaabi shakal mein shaaye'e kiya jaayega taake 'aam log bhi Islami Bank-kaari ke usooolon ko samajh sakein aur is ke muta'alliq paaye janae wale ishqalaat aur galat fahmiyon ka izaalah kar sakein.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

LIBAAS WA ZAIWAR KE MASA-IL

Mard, 'Aurat Ka Satr

Sawal: Kya mard ke satar me naaf ko dhankna zaroori hai? Neez 'aurat ka shara'ee satar kya hai?

Jawab: Mard ka shara'ee satar naaf aur ghutne ke darmiyaan waala hissa hai, naaf aur ghutna satar nahi lekin kaamil satar ke husool ke liye naaf aur ghutne ka baa'z hissa chupaana waajib hai, neez aazaad 'aurat ka satr namaz me aur ajnabi mard ki maujoodgi me mukammal jism hai siwaaye chehre aur hatheli ke zaahir wa batin ke (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/125). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ke liye us ke apne ghar me jahan us ke mahram hi rehte hon mukammal satr me rehna zaroori hai? 'umooman hota yeh hai ke 'auratein apne ghar me sar par dupatte ka ehtimaam nahi kartein ya baa'z auqaat nisf aasteen ki qamees pehenti hain kya shar'an is ki gunjaayish hai?

Jawab: Mahram mardon aur shohar ki maujoodgi me bagair dupattah ke ya nisf aasteen waali qameez me rehene me koi haraj nahi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/126) Mualavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Kya mard 3/4th (teen chauthaai) trouser pehen sakte hain? Aur kya badon ke saamne pehen kar jaa sakte hain? Yeh trousers ghutnon ke niche hoti hai.

Jawab: Hadees me mard momin ka satar naaf se ghutne tak bayaan kiya gaya hai agar ghutne se neeche rakhe to takhnon se upar hona chaahiye. Teen chauthaai taqreeban itna hi hota hai is liye jaaiz to hai lekin aysi patloonein sunnat samajh kar ya islaami tareeqa samajh kar nahi peheni jati balke fashion aur style ya Magribi tahzeeb samajh kar pehni jaati hai jo shar'an makrooh aur naapasandeedah hai. (Kifayatun Nabeeh-Sharhut Tambeeh:2/460, Al-Majmoo': 4/454). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Lungi Pehenna

Sawal: Kya lungi pehenna hadees se saabit hai?

Jawab: Ji ha, hadees se saabit hai, Dasyon Beesyon hadeeson me Aap (ﷺ) ka “Izaar” pehenna aaya huwa hai aur Izaar ka Tarjamah (translation) “Lungi” hai. (Al-Qamoosul Waheed Urdu, Saheeh Al-Bukhari:3829/1843/1545). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Ladkiyon Ka Jins Pents, T-Shirt Aur Shirt Pehenna

Sawal: Kya ladkiyon ka jins pents T-shirt aur shirt pehenna sahi hai?

Jawab: Yeh libaas mardon ke liye khaas hai aur Nabi (ﷺ) ne 'auraton ko mardon ki mushaabahat (resembling) karne se mana'a farmaaya hai aur in par la'naat farmaayi hai is liye 'auraton ko aysi jeens aur t-shirt pehenna sahi nahi hai jis se mardon ki mushaabahat (resembling) laazim aaye doosri baat yeh hai ke is me satar mukammal nahi hota. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 1/521). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kuch logon ki yeh soch hoti hai ke choti bachchiyon ko jeens pehnaane me koi haraj nahi albatta badi hone ke baad nahi pehnaayenge. Is ke bare me 'Ulama ki kya rai hai?

Jawab: Insan Sharee'at ka paband baalig hone ke baad hota hai, gunah aur sawaab ka mu'aamalah mukallaf hone ke baad hoga, lihaaza choti bachchiyon ko jeens pehnaane me koi haraj nahi jaisa ke chote ladkon ko sone ke zaiwar pehnaane ki 'Ulama ne ijaazat di hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 3/172) Lekin agar shuru' se hi in cheezon se bacha jaaye aur parhaiz kiya jaaye to ziyaadah behtar hoga. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Jandaar Ki Tasweer Waale Kapde Bachchon Ko Pehnaana

Sawal: Kya kisi jandaar ki tasweer waale kapde Bachchon ko pehnaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Jaaiz nahi, aise kapde khareedne, pehenne se bachna bahut zaroori hai. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 7/383, Al-Wasit Fil Mazhab:5/277, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:4/407). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bachchon Ke Kapde Kisi Aur Ko Dena

Sawal: Chote Bachchon ke kapde jo puraane ho gaye hon ya un ke badan par raast na aarahe hon kya kisi aur ko dena sahi hai? Baaz log kehte hain ke kapdon ke zariye sahar kiya jaata hai is liye kisi ko boseedah (phate-puraane) kapde nahi dena chahiye is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Boseedah (phate-puraane) kapde kisi aur ko dene me koi haraj ki baat nahi hai. Aysi be asal aur ehtimaali baaton ki wajah se neki ke kaam se rukna nahi chaahiye (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/198). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Mard Ke Liye Angothi Pehenna

Sawal: Kya mardon ke liye sone ya deegar dhaat ki angothi pehenna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Muslim mard ke liye sone ki angothi pehenna haraam hai, albatta jo angothi sone ke 'alaawah deegar dhaat se banayi gai ho jaise chaandi, platimum wagayrah to mard ke liye is ka pehenna jaaiz hai, khwah wo kitni hi qeemati dhaat se banaayi hui ho. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen:2/243). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: kya angothi me patthar daal kar pehenna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Angothi me patthar aur nageenah wagayrah daal kar pehenna jaaiz hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji:1/512). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mardon Ke Liye Gale Me Chain Pehenne Ka Hukm

Sawal: Mardon ke liye gale me chain pehenne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Mardon ke liye siwaaye angothi ke aur kisi zaiwar ka pehenna khwah chaandi hi kyun na ho jaaiz nahi. Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne sirf chaandi ki angothi pehenne ki ijaazat di hai. is ke 'alaawah gale aur haathon me chain pehenne me 'auraton aur deegar mazaahib ke saath mushaabahat (resembling) hai, aur 'auraton ki mushaabahat karne waale par to Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne la'anat farmaayi hai. Isi liye mardon ka aise zaiwaraat pehenna khawah wo kisi bhi cheez ke bane hon bilkul jaaiz nahi. (Al-Majmoo':6/32). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mardon Ka 22 Carat Gold Plated Ghadhiyan Pehenna

Sawal: Mardon ka 22 carat gold plated ghadhiyan pehenna kaisa hai? Kya yeh bhi sone ke daayire me aayega? haalaanke is par sirf sone ka paani chadhaaya jaata hai.

Jawab: Agar itna sone ka paani ho us ke aag par tapaane se sona nikaale to is ko isti'emaal karna haraam hai warnah nahi, taaham paani chadhaana mamnu' hai. ('Ujalatul Muhtaaj:1/485). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya 'umooman ghadhi jo Gold Plated hoti hain un ke tapaane se sona nikaalne ki gunjaayish hoti hai?

Jawab: Maahir sonaar ki tahqeeq ke mutaabiq jawaaz ya adme jawaaz ka hukm hoga. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ghadhi ki qeemat se bhi to andaazah lagaaya ja sakta hai ke us se sona niklega ya nahi?

Jawab: Kisi cheez par koi hukm lagaane ke liye yaqeeni 'ilm hona zaroori hai, shak aur andaazah kafi nahi. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Aurat Ka Mangalsutr Pehenna

Sawal: Kya 'aurat ka mangalsutr pehenna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Mangal Sutr aaj kal zaiwar ki tarah isti'emaal hota hai, jise khas taur par shaadi shudah Hindu 'auratien pehennti hain, agar us ke pehenne se koi faasid (invalid) aqeedah waabastah na ho sirf zaiwar ki niyyat se pehenna chaahe to pehen sakte hain koi muzaaiqah (haraj) nahi lekin chunke is ki wajah se kisi na kisi darjah me Hindu 'auraton se mushaabahat (resembling) laazim aati hai, is liye pehanna makrooh hai. (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband:147701). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Rangeen Burqah Pehenna

Sawal: Aaj kal bahut saari 'auratein Modern rangeen burqah pehen rahi hain, kya kaale rang ke 'alaawah doosre rang ka burqah pehenna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Allah Tabarak wa Ta'aala ne 'auraton ko pardah karne aur hijaab lene ka hukm diya hai. Namaz ki haalat me 'auraton ka satar chehre aur hatheliyon ko chod kar mukammal jism hai aur namaz ke 'alaawah ajnabiyon ke samne 'auraton ko apna mukammal jism chupaana farz hai lihaaza 'auratein apna jism chupaane ke liye kisi bhi qism ka aur kisi bhi rang ka kapda isti'emaal kar sakti hain.

Sharee'at me hijaab ke liye kisi makhsoos rang ya kapde ki shart nahi albatta 'auraton ke liye aise rang ka kapda jaaiz nahi jo rang mardon ke liye khaas ho, ya mardon ko 'auraton ki taraf maayil karne waala ho kyunke hadeese paak me aysi 'auraton par la'anat bheji gai hai jo mardon ki mushaabahat (resembling) ikhtiyaar karti hain.

Isi tarah 'auraton ke liye jaaiz nahi ke wo aisa burqah pehne jo naqsh wa nighaar se muzayyan aur aisa jaazibe nazar ho jo naamahramon ko us ki taraf dekhne par barangekhta kare/ubhaare.

Burqah ke liye zarooori ausaaf yeh hain ke wo saada ho aur gaadha aur gehre rang ka ho aur is qadar dheela dhaala ho jise pehenne ke baad jism ya andar ka zanaanah libaas nazar na aaye aur a'azaa ki sakht wa hay'at bhi zaahir na ho.

Bahut saari 'auratein siyah rang ka burqah pehenti hain wo is wajah se nahi ke yeh waajib hai, balke is wajah se ke kala rang zaib wa zeenat se door hota hai, aur is baat ka bhi suboot aaya hai ke Sahaba-e-Kiraam ki azwaaje mutaharaat siyah libaas pehenti thien, chunaanche Abu Dawood shareef ki riwaayat me hai ke jab yeh aayat (يُدْنِينَ عَلَيْهِنَّ مِنْ جَلابِيبِهِنَّ) – (Latka lein wo apne upar apni chaadarein) nazil hui to ansar ki 'auratein is haalat me kala libaas pehene huwe ghar se niklein goya un ke saron par kale kawwe baithe hon.

Algarz yeh baat hamare zahnon me rehna chaahiye ke islaami hijaab, 'auraton ka waqaar hai, khawaateen me jo burqah pehenne ka riwaaj huwa hai yeh daure nubuwwat ki pakeezaa khawaateene islaam ke 'amal se hi makhooz hai, burqah bhi isi chaadar ke qaayim maqaam hai jis ka tazkirah Qur-aan-e-kareem me lafze jilbaab se kiya gaya hai, albatta pehle zamaane me burqah kaale rang ke dheele dhaale huwa karte the aur khawaateen isi qism ke burqaun ko pasand karti thien jo pure badan ko bhi chupaalete the aur 'aurat ki sharaafat ko bhi zaahir karte the, lekin aaj ke daur ka ek bada alamiyah yeh hai ke market me aise burqe aagaye hain jin se burqah ka maqsad hi faut ho raha hai, kapda itna baareek ke jis se andar tak ka libaas nazar aata hai, size itna tang ke jis se jism ki saakht bhi zaahir hoti ha, rang birange dhaagon ke phool, design, rangon chamkiyon aur motiyon wagayrah se muzayyan bade bade qeemati aur jaazibe nazar burqe aaj market me dastiyaab hain, jin ke pehenne se kabhi burqah ka maqsad haasil nahi ho sakta. Jo bajaaye hayaa ke be-hayaai ko zaahir karte hain aur saath hi bahut si khawaateen poora chehrah ya aadha chehrah khula rakhti hain aur burqah pehenne ke baawajood sar ke baal burqah ke bahar daale rakhti hain aur baaz khawaateen nose piece is tarah lagati hain ke peshaani, bhawein aur naak ka hissa khula rehta hai, jo khuli hui be hijaabi aur be gairati hai. (Al-Bukhaari: 5885, Abu Dawood: 4097, Gaayatul-Maraam Saheeh: 197, Sunane Abu Dawood: 4101, Al-Majmoo': 3/170-171). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bahut Jald Manzar-E-'Aam Par Aane Wali Kitaab 📘 "Islami Bank-Kaari: Haqiqat, Zarurat Aur Ishqalaat Ka Izaalah"

Aaj ke daur mein jab ma'eeshat ka pahiyyah soodi nizaam ke gird ghoom raha hai, aisey mein Islami Bank-kaari sirf ek mutabadil nahi balke aik shara'ee taqaza bhi hai.

Isi aham zarurat ke pehlu ko madde nazar rakhte hue Jami'aa Zia-ul-'Uloom Kundloor – Karnataka ke Shu'bah Tadreebe Iftah ke ek taalib-e- 'ilm Maulavi 'Usman Sadi Papa ko yeh mauzoo' baraye tahqeeq diya gaya tha. Unhon ne apne ustad Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Mahmood Barmare Husaini aur Maulana Jasim Mahadik ki nigrani mein is mauzoo' par gehre mutaala'aah, 'ilmi tajziye aur mustanad dalaeelon ke saath ek jaame'e maqalah tahreer kiya tha.

Yeh maqalah na sirf Islami Bank-kaari ki bunyadon aur usoolon ko wazeh karta hai balke un ishqalaat aur e'etiraazaat ka mudallal aur 'ilmi jawab bhi pesh karta hai jo 'aam taor par logon ke zehno mein paida hote hain.

Ab yeh maqalah kuch zaroori aur jadeed masa-il ke izafe ke saath, 'awaami mafaad ke pehlu ko dekhte hue, kitaabi shakal mein shaaye'e kiya jaayega taake 'aam log bhi Islami Bank-kaari ke usooolon ko samajh sakein aur is ke muta'alliq paaye janae wale ishqalaat aur galat fahmiyon ka izaalah kar sakein.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

WIRASAT KE MASA-IL

Maut Se Pehle Jaayidaad Ko Waarison Me Taqseem Karna

Sawal: Kya apni maut se pehle apni jaayidaad ko waarison me taqseem karna afzal hai ya marne ke baad Sharee'at ke hisaab se taqseem karna afzal hai?

Jawab: Dono bhi sooratein jaaiz hain, albatta zindagi me agar taqseem kar raha hai to apni tamaam aulaad me baraabari karna sunnat aur afzal hai (Saheeh Al-Bukhari:2587). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Waraasat Ki Taqseem Me Har Ek Ko Hissa Dena

Sawal: Mai ne ek hanafi 'aalim se suna ke waraasat ki taqseem me har ek ko hissa dena zaroori hai, chaahe wo na le ya mu'aaf kare, hissa lene ke baad wo kisi aur ko de. Mai ne un se kaha ke Shafi'ee maslak me agar koi mu'aaf kare to hissa dena zaroori nahi hai. 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke sahi Shafi'ee mas-alah ki wazaahat keejiye.

Jawab: Shafi'ee maslak ke mutaabiq koi bhi waaris apna hissa chod sakta hai. Ya'ani mu'aaf kar sakta hai. Le kar dena koi zaroori nahi hai. (Kitab Taqseeme Miraas az Muhammad Ayyub Nadavi Shafi'ee: 103)

Hamare 'alaqe me behnon aur betiyon ka waraasat me se hissa na lene ka riwaaj 'aam hai. Agar koi mutaalabah kare bhi to mu'aashareh me us ko achchi nigah se nahi dekha jaata to wo zaroorat mand aur chahte huwe bhi mangne se sharmaati hai. (Ahmed: 23605) hadees me hai kisi ka bhi maal us ki dili razaamandi ke bagair lena jaaiz nahi. Is liye behtar hai ke haalaate haazirah ke peshe nazar pehle us ko us ka haq diya jaaye phir us ka ikhtiyaar hai chaahe wo le ya apne bhaayiyon ko de. Is soorat me khud mukhtaari aur razaamandi ke saath mu'aaf karna ya lena zaahir hoga. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Hamare yahan bade bade deendaar gharaane bhi behnon aur betiyon ko waraasat me hissa nahi dete, haalaanke sab ko pata hai ke is ki kya ahmiyat hai phir bhi 'awaam ke saath khawaas bhi is ko nazar andaaz karte hain. Hamare yahan agar zameen farokht ki gai to behnon ko hissa dete hain lekin baqi cheezon me main ne aaj tak nahi dekha hai. Kya 'Ulama is ka khulaasah karenge ke kin kin cheezon me waraasat ki taqseem zaroori hai? Jaise maal, zameen, sona wagayrah.

Jawab: Marte waqt choti badi jo cheez bhi marne waale ki milkiyat me hogi wo tarkah kehlaayega aur un saari cheezon me waarison ka hissa hoga (Taqseeme Miraas:30) waraasat ke masa-il par 'amal na karne ke asbaab me se ek sabab 'ilm ka na hona bhi hai. Is tarah bandon ke huqooq ki ahmiyat na jaanna bhi hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya ghar me bhi bahan ka hissa hoga? Do ghar ho to alag baat hai lekin ek hi ghar rehne ke liye ho to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Ghar ki qeemat lagaaye aur us ke e'etibaar se behnon ka hissa de. (Taqseeme Meeraas: 30). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Dada Hayaat Ho To Bahu Aur Pote Ki Waraasat Ka Mas-alah

Sawal: Agar walid hayaat ho, jaayidaad sirf unhi ke naam par ho aur kisi ko hissa taqseem nahi kiye gaye hon aur un ke bête ka intiqaal ho jaaye to

Jawab: (1) Bete ki aulaad agar 'aaqil wa baalig ho aur ma'azoor na ho to in ki kafaalat ki zimmedaari kisi par waajib nahi hai aur agar naabaalig ya paagal ya ma'azoor ho to us ke dada par us ki zimmedaari hogi (Tuhfatul-Bari:3/147,144).

(2) Bahu ka waraasat me hissa nahi hota hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari:3/268).

(3) Baap ke intiqaal ke waqt marhoom bête ki aulaad maujood ho to phir mutawaffa bête ki aulaad ka hissa nahi hoga albatta un ke liye baap wasiyyat kar sakta hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj Ala Ma'rifati Ma'aani Alfazil Minhaaj:3/309). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Baap ke intiqaal se pehle baqi doosre bhi bachche intiqaal ho jaayein to?

Jawab: To phir waraasat pote potiyon ko milegi. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Aysi Soorat me (Baap ke intiqaal se pehle sab bachche bhi intiqaal ho) to bahu ko bhi hissa milega kya? Ya sirf pote potiyon ko?

Jawab: Bahu ka waraasat me kabhi bhi koi hissa nahi. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Pehle Shohar Ke Intiqaal Ke Baad Jaayidaad Me Hissah

Sawal: pehle shohar ke intiqaal ke baad us 'aurat ne doosra nikah kiya ab poochna yeh hai ke kya us 'aurat ko pehle shohar ke “Tarkah” (Chode huye maal) me se hissa milega? Agar milega to kitna?

Jawab: Sharee'ate Mutahharah me marne waale shakhs ki biwi ko “Tarkah” (Chode huye maal) me hissa muta-'ayyan hai. Agar us shohar ki aulaad ho to aathwa (1/8th) hissa milega aur aulaad na ho to chautha (1/4th) hissah. (Al-Majmoo' Sharhul-Muhazzab: 16/71). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Waraasat Ke Mutafarriq Masa-il

Sawal: Agar kisi ne apni mehnat ki kamaai se flat/ghar liya lekin wo flat us ne apne waalid ke naam kiya agar baap ka intiqaal huwa to wo flat waraasat ki taqseem me aayega? Ya'ani kya is me se us ke maa, bhai aur bahan ka hissa hoga ya wo us ke akele ka haq hoga?

Jawab: Agar waalid sahib ke naam par sirf register kiya hai, un ko diya nahi (Gift nahi kiya) to wo us ka khud ka hi hai, lihaaza us mein waraasat jaari na hogi. Aur agar waalid sahab ko de diya hai to wo un ka ho gaya, ab us me waraasat jaari hogi aur sab waarison ko bhi hissa milega. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: KOC ka ek muslim Engineer, Brain Hemorrhage se pichle 2-3 maheene se Coma me hai. Us ki biwi aur bachche yahan par hain, bank ne engineer ka Debit card block kiya kyun ke wo hosh me nahi hai aur biwi ko is ki ijaazat nahi hai neez use KOC se End of Service bhi milegi lekin abhi us ka koi faayidah nahi, agar intiqaal huwa to us ke waalid ko milegi kyunke us ne Beneficiary me waalid ka naam daala hai. Kya is soorat me us ki biwi aur aulad bhi waraasat me hissedar honge?

Jawab: Beneficiary me kisi ka naam indaraaj karaana to yeh ek wasiyyat hai magar chunke wasiyyat baap ke naam par hai aur usool yeh hai ke Sharee'at ne miraas me jin ka hissa muqarrar kiya hai un ke haq me wasiyyat durust nahi hoti aur Sharee'at me baap ka hissa muqarrar hai, lihaaza wasiyyat baatil hogi aur is maal me baap ke saath saath us ki biwi aur aulaad bhi hisse dar honge. Chunke filwaqt biwi ke naan aur nufqe ki zimmedaari shohar par waajib hai isliye biwi baqadre zaroorat qarz legi aur yeh qarz shohar ke zimme waajib samjha jaayega. Ab agar us ka intiqaal hota hai to aysi soorat me sab se pehle is maal (End of Service) me se us ki tajheez wa takfeen aur us qarz ki adaayigi ki jaayegi jo us ki biwi ne liya tha, phir jo us me se bach jaaye wo us ke tamaam warasaa me taqseem kiya jaayega jis me us ki biwi aur aulaad bhi shaamil hain. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji Ala Mazhabe Imaam Shafi'ee: 5/56). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

GIZA KE MASA-IL

Murgi Ki Jild Khaana

Sawal: Kya murgi ki jild khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Ji han, murgi ki jild khaane mein koi haraj nahi hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 3/20). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Khaane Ya Peene Ki Cheezon Mein Chiuntiyan

Sawal: Agar kisi khaane ya peene ki cheez mein chiuntiyan hon jin ko nikaalna kisi had tak mushkil ho, to kya un ko is khaane ki cheez ke saath khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar shahad (honey) wagayrah mein makhkhi ya chiunti ho to use khaana jaaiz nahi agar ek makhkhi chiunti ya shahad (honey) ki makhkhi kisi cheez mein gir kar us ke ajzaa is mein gal jaayein to use khaana haraam nahi kyunke in ki hurmat gandagi ki wajah se thi aur yeh gandah shumaar na hoga. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 3/225) Mufti Ishaque Patel

Mor (Peacock) Khaana

Sawal: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek Mor (peacock) khaane ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek mor (peacock) khaana shar'an haraam aur naajaaiz hai. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 2/726, Minhaajut-Talibeen:1/322). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Mor khaana haraam aur naa jaaiz hai lekin Imaam Abu Haneefah (Rh) ke nazdeek jaaiz hai is ki kya wajah hai?

Jawab: Jaanwaron aur parindon ke haraam hone ki wujoohaat mein ek wajah un ke gosht ka khabees hona hai. chunke ahle arab mor ke gosht ko khabees samajhte hain khaate nahi is liye haraam hoga. (Minhaajut-Talibeen:1/322).

Imaam Abu Haneefah (Rh) ke nazdeek parindon ke haraam hone ke liye ek wajah panjon ka mazboot hona jis se wo shikaar kare, doosri wajah mara aur sada huwa gosht khaane waala hona. Chunke mor mein yeh khaslatein nahi payi jaatein is liye wo halaal hoga. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khusyatain Khaana

Sawal: Kya Khusyatain khaana Haraam hai?

Jawab: Khusyatain khaana makrooh hai haraam nahi hai. (Al-Majmoo':9/70). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Ojh (Ojhadi) Khaana

Sawal: Ojh (Ojhadi) khaana Shafi'ee ke nazdeek jaaiz hai ya naajaaiz?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek ojh (ojhadi) khaana jaaiz hai. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 10/57). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Imaam Abu Haneefah (Rh) ke nazdeek Ojh (Ojhadi) khaana makrooh hai? Is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Imaam Abu Haneefah (Rh) ke nazdeek Ojh (Ojhadi) khaana Makrooh nahein hai balke jaaiz hai. (Fatawa Rahimiyyah: 10/81). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Paani Ke Jaanwaron Ke Khaane Ka Hukm

Sawal: Shawaafe' ke yaha Jheengaa (Prawns) khaane ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek jheengaa (Prawns) khaana bila karaahat jaaiz hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 3/68). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Is hadees ka khulaasah kare jis mein Huzoor (ﷺ) ne kaha tha ke samandar ke paani ka har murdah halaal hai.

Jawab: Sahaabi ne Huzoor (ﷺ) se poocha ke hum samandar ka safar karte hain, hamaare saath meetha paani bahut thoda hota hai, agar hum meethe paani se wuzoo karte hain to pyaase rah jaayenge, to kya hum samandar ke paani se wuzoo kar sakte hain?

Sahaabi ne sawal is liye kiya tha kyunke wuzoo to saaf aur be 'ayb paani se kiya jaata hai, (samandar ke paani mein to badbu bhi hoti hai aur maza bhi bigda hota hai) to Huzoor (ﷺ) ne jawab mein irshaad farmaaya ke “Samandar ka paani paak hai aur is ka mara huwa jaanwar halaal hai” yeh sawal to pucha nahein gaya tha phir bhi Huzoor (ﷺ) ne ilmi baat bataayi ke khaana kam pad jaaye to samandar par tairta huwa jaanwar (machli) bagair zabah kiye huye kha sakte ho, wo murdaar nahein hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Badi Machli ke pet se agar choti machli nikal aaye to kya us choti machli ko khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Ji han, wo choti machli bhi khaayi ja sakti hai, lekin shart yeh hai ke machli ke me'de mein rehne ki wajah se us ki apni asli haalat mutagayyar (badli hui) na ho ya'ani wo saheeh saalim ho lihaaza agar koi tagayyur paida ho gaya ho to wo najis (Naapaak) shumaar hogi aur khaana jaaiz nahein hoga (Al-Gurarul-Bahiyyah:1/47). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Machli Khaane Ke Baad Doodh Ya Us Se Bani Cheez Khaana

Sawal: Machli (fish) khaane ke baad doodh ya us se bani cheez nahein khaana chaahiye kya yeh hadees se saabit hai?

Jawab: Hadees mein to aisa kuch nahein hai, albattah tibbi e'etibaar se mana'a kiya jata hai. (Daarul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Har Maheene 3 Din Subah Ke Waqt Shahad (honey) Chakhna

Sawal: Jo shakhas har maheene 3 din subah ke waqt shahad (honey) chakhega to use koi badi beemaari nahein hogi (Ibne Maajah: 3450). Kya is ko mahinah ke ibtidaayi dinon mein karna hai? Ya koi bhi 3 din? Subah ke waqt se kya muraad hai? Subah saadiq ya Tulu'-e-Aaftab ke baad?

Jawab: Teen din se koi bhi 3 din muraad hain shuru' ke hona zaroori nahein. Subah se muraad subah saadiq se zawaal ke pehle ka waqt kehlaata hai lekin khaali pet shahad (honey) khaana ziyadah asar karta hai.

Imaame Suyuti (Rh) ne " Al la-aaliul masnu'ah" me ek Hadees naqal ki hai us hadees se bhi is ki tayeed hoti hai, lekin wo hadees ya to za'eef hai ya mawzoo' jis mein farmaaya gaya hai ke jo shakhs har maheene 3 din nihaar munh (empty stomach) shahad (honey) piyega wo badi beemaariyon jayse laqwah, juzaam (leprosy) aur bars (vitiligo) se mahfooz rahenga. (AL-la-aaliul masnu'ah:2/344, As silsilatuz za'eefah:763). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Bars (vitiligo) aur juzaam (leprosy) kon si beemaariyan hain aur un se mahfooz rehne ka wazeefah kya hai?

Jawab: Juzaam (leprosy) ek bahut buri beemaari hai jis mein insaan ke hath pair surkh (Red) ho jaate hain phir siyaah (Black) ho jaate hain phir kat kat kar jhadh jaate hain ya'ani bikhar jaate hain.

Bars (vitiligo) bhi ek bahut buri beemaari hai jis mein insaan ke jild mein khoon ka dauraan band ho jaata hai jis ki wajah se jism par safed (white) daag pad jaate hain aur kuch hi saalon mein poora jism daagon se bhar jaata hai. In donon beemaariyon ka asar zaahiri jism par bhi hota hai aur dimaag par bhi, isi wajah se in beemaariyon se hadees mein panah maangi gai hai, chunaanche Huzoore Paak (ﷺ) yeh du'aa padha karte the.: Aye Allah! Mein teri panaah mein aata hun mujhe panah deejiye meri hifaazat farmaaye bars (vitiligo) se junoon se aur juzaam se aur har qism ki buri beemaariyon se (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:15/129). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ghode (Horse) Ke Gosht Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya ghode ka gosht khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek ghode ka gosht khaana halaal hai (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:15/142) jaisa ke riwaayaton se saabit hai chunaanche Hazrat Jaabir Bin 'Abdullah (Rz) ne bayaan kiya ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne gazwaye khaibar ke mauqe par desi aur paaltu gadhon (donkeys) ka gosht khaane se mana'a kiya aur ghodon ka gosht khaane ki ijaazat di. (Bukhaari:5524) Isi tarah Hazrat Abu Bakr Siddeeque (Rz) ki saahibzaadi Hazrat Asma'a (Rz) farmaati hain ke: Hum ne Madinah mein Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ke daure Mas'ood mein ghoda zabah kiya aur phir use khaaya. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:2/723). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Teetar Khaane Ka Shar'an Hukm

Sawal: Teetar khaane ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek aise parinde khaana jaaiz nahi jo mazboot panje rakhte hon aur jin ki madad se wo apne shikaar ko pakad kar khaate hon, in ke 'alaawah deegar parindon ko khaana jaaiz hai, lihaaza teetar khaana jaaiz hoga (Rauzatut-taalibeen:2/726). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Chai, Coffee Aur Cold Drink Mein Caffeine Ke Isti'emaal Ki Shara'ee Haisiyat

Sawal: Caffeine jo ke chai, coffee aur kuch cold drink jaise pepsi wagayrah mein paayi jaati hai, is mein ek qism ka nashah hai, to is soorat mein chai, coffee wagayrah ki shar'ee haisiyat kya hai?

Jawab: Hazrat Umme-Qays Binte Hanee (R.A.) farmatei hain ke maine Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se suna ke Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya tum log us oode hindi (Kust) ka isti'emaal kiya karo kyunke is mein saat beemaariyon ka 'ilaaj hai, jise halaq ke dard mein naak mein daala jata hai, aur pasli ke dard mein chabaayi jaati hai. (Saheeh Bukhaari: 5692)

Mazkoorah hadees mein Oode Hindi ke isti'emaal karne ki targeeb di gai hai. Is ke zail mein shaariheen ne is ke bahut se fawaayid likhe hain, in mein se ek yeh bhi hai ke wo quwwate baah ko badhaata hai. (Fathul-Bari bisharhi Saheeh Bukhaari: 13/27) aur badan mein nashaat wa phurti ka sabab hai.

Rahi baat Caffeine ke isti'emaal karne ki to wo bhi do tarah ki dastiyaab hoti hai, ek qudrati taur par aur doosra masnooyi taur par dastiyaab hoti hai. In donon mein tameez bhi bahut mushkil amr hai. Mazeed donon ka asar bhi yaksaan hota hai, daure haazir ke 'Ulama ka kehna hai ke yeh munasshit (Nashaat paida karne waali cheez) hai, na ke muskir aur caffeine ko muskir waali hadees ke daayire mein nahi laaya ja sakta hai kyun ke sukr ki wajah se paida hone waali alaamatein is mein mafqood (milna mushkil - missing) hain lihaaza caffeine ko qata'ee taur par haraam nahi kaha ja sakta hai. Albattah upar mazkoora hadees ki tashreeh mein shaariheen ne quwwate baah ke liye Oode hindi ka isti'emaal karne ka zikr kiya hai, jis ka maqsad jism mein nashaat aur shahwat ko ubhaarna rehtah hai. Aur yeh jism ko taqviyat bhi pohnchaata hai. Lihaaza aise munasshit (energetic) ashyaa ka isti'emaal durust hai. Lekin e'etidaal ke saath isti'emaal karna ziyadah munaasib hai. Maulavi Safwan Mangalori, Nazar farmoodah: Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare

Kisi Khaane Ki Cheez Ko Haleem Kehna

Sawal: Kya Haleem kehna galat hai? Kyunke Haleem Allah ka ek sifaati naam hai aur hum ne use khaane ki ek cheez ka naam rakh diya jabke hamaare khaane ki is dish ka Saheeh naam daleem hai.

Jawab: Bekaar ki baat hai is dish ka naam agar Urdu mein Haleem pad gaya hai to is mein 'arabi ke ma'anaa thopne ki kya zaroorat hai? Aysi pareshaani to alfaaz mein ayegi. Misaal ke taur par mere bête ka naam mai ne Anas rakhkha to kisi ne tawajjuh dilaayi ke English mein is lafz (ANUS) ke kharab ma'anaa hain to kya ab wo naam chod diya jaaye? Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Kutta Kaata Huwa Jaanwar Khaana

Sawal: Kisi jaanwar ke pair ko kutte ne kaata to kya us ko khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar kisi jaanwar ko kutta kaat le to us jaanwar ke gosht ko khaane aur na khaane ki 3 sooratein hain.

Kaate huye hisse ko us waqt khaana jaaiz hai jab ke us ko saat martabah dhoya jaaye aur us mein bhi ek martabah mitti se dhoya jaaye behtar hai ke us hisse ke gosht ko phenk diya jaaye. (Al-Majmoo': 9/91). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sharaab Noshi (Beer Bar) Restaurant Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya aise Restaurant mein khaana jaaiz hai jis mein sharaab noshi (Beer Bar) ho?

Jawab: Sharaab noshi waale restaurant mein khaana jaaiz nahein hai, kyunke is soorat mein gunaah par mu'aawanat aur madad laazim aayegi jo haraam hai (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 7/334) Mufti Faiz Ahmed Bin Aadam Mulla.

Sawal: Agar kisi Restaurant mein sharab noshi (Beer Bar) ho lekin family ke liye alag room ho jis mein sharaab noshi mamnu' ho to kya aise hotal mein khaana khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Islam mein sharaab peena, pilaana aur us ta'alluq se koi bhi madad karna haraam hai. Sharaab noshi waale restaurant mein khaane se parhez kare agarcha family ke liye alag room ho kyunke sharaab noshi waale hotel gairon ke hi hote hain aur mumkin hai ke baawarchi khaana (Kitchen) mein isti'emaal hone waale bartan, plates aur glass idhar ke udhar ho jaayein aur saheeh tareeqe se saaf na hoye hon neez gunaah par muaavanat bhi hai, lihaaza aise hotalon mein jaane se gurez kare (Ash-Sharhul-Kabeer: 8/348). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar kisi Restaurant mein sharaab noshi (Beer Bar) ho lekin is mein Vegetarians ke liye alag room ho jis mein sharaab noshi mamnu' ho aur jis ka baawarchi khaana (Kitchen) bhi alag ho kyunke gairon mein bhi kuch mazaahib mein gosht (Non-Vegetarians) khaana aur isi tarah sharaab noshi ki ijaazat nahein hoti. Restaurant maalik ko mukhtalif customer ko muta'assir karne ke liye aisa karna padta hai jis se restaurant ko bhi faaidah hota hai. Kya aise restaurant ke Vegetarians room mein khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Aysi jaghein jahaan khilaafe sharee'at kaam hote hon 'aam halaat mein wahan jaana jaaiz nahein, kyunke Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne hamein yeh hukm diya hai ke tum mein se jo shakhs Khilaafe Sharee'at kaam dekhe to apne haathon se is ki islaah kare aur agar taaqat na rakhta ho to zabaan se is ka radd kare aur agar is ki bhi taaqat na rakhtaa ho to dil se is ko bura jaane aur yeh eemaan ka kamzor tareen darjah hai. (Saheeh Muslim: 49) Kisi buraayi ko dil se bura samajhne ka taqaazah yeh hai ke us jagah se ijtinaab karein. Aur door rahe lihaaza ma'siyat aur gunaah ki jagah baithna Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke hukm ki khilaaf warzi hai. Isi liye aise hotalon mein jaana 'aam haalaat mein jaaiz nahein. Albattah zaroorat aur majboori ke ahkaam juda honge, ya'ani iztiraari haalat mein jaane ki gunjaayish hogi is shart ke saath ke veg waale sho'abe mein khaana khaaye. (Saheeh Muslim: 49, Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Subuki:1/25). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Ek Shakhs jis ka ek restaurant kuwait mein hai aur doosra Mumbai mein hai, jis mein sharaab noshi aur fahhaashi wagayrah bhi hoti hai, wo shakhs kuwait mein maujood us ke restaurant ke zariye ziyadah munaafah haasil karta hai jise Mumbai ke restaurant ko chalaane mein isti'emaal karta hai, aysi soorate haal mein kya us ke Kuwait ke restaurant mein khaana durust hai?

Jawab: Kuwait ke restaurant mein khane mein haraj nahein. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

GAIR MUSLIM & UN KE TEHWAARON KE MASA-IL

Gair Muslim Ko Jazaakallahu Khairan Kehna

Sawal: Kya gair muslim ko Jazaakallahu Khairan bol sakte hai?

Jawab: Hadees me mutlaq ehsaan ki makafaat (Badla) zikr hai aur yeh bhi hai ke agar kuch nahi de sakte ho to du'aa hi de do aur “Khairan” me niyyat hidaayat aur Deene Islam ki ho to koi haraj nahi balke behter hai, lihaaza gair muslim ko jazaakallahu Khairan kehna jaaiz hai (I'aanatut-Taalibeen:4/221). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Muslimon Se Dosti

Sawal: Kya gair muslimon se dostaanah ta'alluqaat rakhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Gair Muslimon se musalmon ke ta'alluqaat teen qism ke honge. (1) Muwalaat ya'ani dosti (2) Muwaasaat ya'ani Ghamkhwari. (3) Mu'aamalaat

in me se mu'aamalaat aur muwaasaat to jaaiz hai, yeh to riwaayaat se bhi saabit hai. Albatta un se dostaanah ta'alluqaat ko Qur-aan paak me sarahatan mana'a kiya gaya hai. (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah:12/253). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Namaste Ya Namaskaar Kehna

Sawal: Wedon me Namaste ke mane “Aap ke samne jhuk kar salaam karna” hai aur Namaskaar bhi Namaste ke hum ma'anaa hai lekin aaj kal poori dunya me hindi bolne waalon ke darmiyaan is ka mazhabi pasmanzar ojhal hai aur sirf tahzeebi riwaayat ke taur par lafz raayij hai ya'ani Namaste ya namaskaar ka lafzi matlab “Aap ko salaam” hai. Kya Namaste ya namaskaar kehne ki gunjaayish nikalti hai? Neez agar gairon ne Namaste ya namaskaar kaha to is ke Jawab me kya kehna chaahiye?

Jawab: “Namaste ya Namaskaar” yeh dono Sanskrit zabaan ke alfaaz hain, jo hinduwon me salaam ke taur par bole jaate hain. “Bharat kosh” hindi lugat me is ke lafzi ma'anaa “ehteraam” se kiya gaya hai. Aur urdu ki mash'hoor wa ma'roof lugat “Fairozul lughaat” me is ka ma'anaa “hath jod kar ta'azeem karna” se kiya gaya hai (Fairozul Lughaat:1379). Lihaaza Soorate mas-uoolah me mulaaqaat ke waqt hinduwon ke makhsoos kalimaat “Namaste ya Namaskaar” kehna ek musalman ke liye jaaiz nahi hai. Fi zamaanah agar yeh kalimaat un ke maz'habi pasmanzar ke saath khas na bhi hon tab bhi chunke yeh kalimaat un ke makhsoos tahzeeb ka hissa hain aur Islam ke aqeeda'e tauheed ke khilaaf hai isliye un ke khehne ki gunjaayish nahi hai. Gair muslimon ke saath office ya phone par mulaqaat ke dauran “Aadaab Arz” “Assalmu ala manittba'al Huda” ya koi aisa lafz isti'emaal kiya ja sakta hai jo gair muslimon ka mazhabi shiaar na ho aur na hi us ke andar shirkiyah ma'aani paye jaate hon.

Agar gairon ne Namaste ya namaskaar kaha to us ke Jawab me Good Morning, Afternoon ya Evening kahe. (Sunane Abu Dawood: 4031, Raddul Mukhtar: 1/646, Fatawa Rahimiyyah: 10/126, Mulakhas Az Darul Iftah'a: Jami'ah Al 'Uloomul Islamiyyah Binnori Town). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gairon Ki 'Iyaadat Karna

Sawal: Kya gairon ki 'iyaadat karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Gairon ki 'iyaadat karna jaaiz hai jaisa ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke 'amal se saabit hai.

Hazrat Anas (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke ek yahoodi (Jews) gulaam Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ki khidmat kiya karta tha wo bimaar ho gaya to Aap (ﷺ) us ki 'iyaadat ke liye tashreef le gaye aur us se kaha ke tum islam qabool kar lo to wo musalman ho gaya (Saheeh Al-Bukhari). (Al-Majmoo': 5/112). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Muslim Naukar Ya Gair Muslim Logon Se Muta'alliq Hukm

Sawal: Gharon me kaam karne waale gair muslim naukar ya 'aam gair muslim logon se muta'alliq shar'an kya hukm hai jabke wo hamaare baithne ki jagah baithein ya hamare bartan me khaein, kyunke baaz log use galat aur ma'ayoob samajhte hain?

Jawab: Gair Muslim fi nafsihi naapaak nahi hota, agar us ke jism wagayrah par koi naapaaki ho tabhi us par naapaaki ka hukm lagta hai warnah nahi, aur Qur-aan paak me jo gair muslimon ko naapaak kaha gaya hai, us se kufriyah wa shirkiyah e'etiqaad ki naapaaki muraad hai, zaahiri naapaaki murad nahi hai. Is liye kisi gair muslim ke hamaare nashiston par baithne aur hamare bartanon me peene se wo jagah ya wo bartan naapaak nahi hota. Bashart yeh ke gair muslim ke haath aur muh aur jism ya kapde par koi naapaaki na lagi ho (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah:1/431). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Muslim Padosi Ya Dost Ki Taraf Se Prasaad Lena

Sawal: Kya gair muslim padosi ya dost ki taraf se Prasaad ke taur par aaya huwa hadiyah lena jaaiz hai? Phir agar koi le le to kya use isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai? Agar nahi to is ka masraf kya hoga?

Jawab: Prasaad ke taur par di gai cheez agar butaon par chadhaave ke liye pesh ki gai thi phir use taqseem kiya gaya tha to is ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz nahi, lihaaza use qabool na kare aur agar wo cheez chadhaawe ki nahi thi to 'aam hadiyah ke hukm me hogi, lihaazah isti'emaal karna jaaiz hoga. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 5/538, Al-Maaidaah: 3, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 3/45). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kaafiron Se Tahaayif Qabool Karne Aur Un Ke Ghar Jakar Khana Khaane Ka Shara'ee Hukm

Sawal: Kaafiron se tahaayif qabool karna isi tarah un ke ghar ja kar khaana khaane ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Bunyaadi taur par gair muslim se ta'leefe Qalb (Dil khush karne ke liye) aur Islam ki taraf raaghib karne ke liye tahaayif qabool karna aur un ke ghar khaana khaana chand sharton par jaaiz hai:

Gair Muslim Bhaiyon Ko Un Ke Tehwaar Par Mubarakbaadi Aur Salaamati Ki Du'aa Dena

Sawal: Kya gair muslim bhaiyon ko un ke tehwaar ke mauqa'a par Mubarakbaadi pesh karna aur salaamati ki du'aa dena durust hai?

Jawab: Gair Muslimon ko un ke kaafiraanah aur mushrikaanah tehwaaron ke mawaaqe'e ki munaasabat se mubarakbaad pesh karna aur salaamati ki du'aa dena shar'an jaaiz nahi, kyunke rahmat wa salaamati ki du'aa ka haqdaar to sirf musalmaan hi hai, kaafir us du'aa ka mustahiq nahi, isi tarah un ke tehwaar chunke butparasti, poojaapaat aur kufr wa shirk par mabni hote hain is liye un ke tehwaaron par khushi ka izhar karna, un me shareek hona aur un ko mubarakbaad dena jaaiz nahi, mazeed yeh ke us me kaafiron se mushaabahat (resembling) aur un ke kufr wa shirk se razaamandi ki kaifiyat paida hoti hai aur unhein is baat ka ehsaas hone lagta hai ke waaqa'ee wo achcha kaam kar rahe hain aur zaahir hai ke aise mushrikaanah aqaaid me un ki hoslah afzaayi karna kayse / kyunkar jaaiz aur durust ho sakta hai. Lihaaza, gair muslimon ke mazhabi tehwaar par unhein Mubarakbaad dene se bachna waajib hai. Lekin “Yahdeekumullah” jaise du'aaiyyah kalimaat keh sakte hain. (Ahkaamu Ahliz zimmah: 1/441, Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 2/103). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gairon Ke Tehwaaron (Ganpati, Diwaali Wagayrah) Me Chanda Dena

Sawal: Ganpati, diwaali, isi tarah gairon ke doosre tehwaaron me hindu tanzeemon se munsalik log dukaanon aur housing society me chanda lene aate hain, agar un ko chanda dene se mana'a kiya jaaye to baad me target (nishaanah) bana kar jaan wa maal ko nuqsaan pahonchaane me koi kasar nahi chodte, aise halaat me hamein kya karna chaahiye? Kya hum un ko bank se haasil shuda soodi raqam de sakte hain?

Jawab: Halaat bahut nazuk hon aur inkar karne ki gunjaayish na ho to aysi soorat me gunah se bachne ke liye aur apni jaan wa maal ko bachaane ke liye ek heelah ikhtiyaar kar sakte hain, wo yeh ke jo shakhs rupaya lene aaya hai, us shakhs ki niyyat kar ke ya'ani us aadmi ko sadaqah de raha hun yeh niyyat karke raqam us ke hawaale kar do. Ganpati ya kisi tehwaar ki ya kisi tanzeem ki niyyat na karo, to yeh shakhsi sadaqah hoga jo ke gair muslim ko bhi dena jaaiz hai. Is me sood ki raqam nahi de sakte. (Sooratul Insaan: 8, Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 2363, Al-Muslim:2244, Al-Fiqhul Islaami Wa Adillatuh:3/2058, Sooratul Hajj:78, Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Suyuti:1/7). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Siyaasi Mafaad Ya Majboori Ki Wajah Se Mandir Ya Charch Ko Paise Dena

Sawal: Kya koi musalman apne siyasi mafaad ya majboori ki wajah se mandir ya charch ko paise de sakta hai?

Jawab: Mandir gairon ka mazhabi shi'aar hai, musalmaanon ko bilqasd is me maali t'aaun karna jaaiz nahi. Lekin agar aysi koi majboori ki soorat pesh aaye to us waqt us tanzeem ke gair muslim zimmedaaron me se jo shakhs saamne aaye to bazaate khud us shakhs ko nifli sadqah ki niyyat se paise de diye jaayein to jaaiz hoga, wo chaahega to apni taraf se mandir me laga dega ya apne liye rakh lega, lekin dete waqt mandir ka qasd bilkul na kare. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili:7/5116, Sunan At-Tirmizi: 2395, Al-Bayaan:3/452). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sharaab Waale Dastarkhwan Par Khaana Khaana

Sawal: Agar kuch aise doston ke saath khaana khaane ka ittifaaq ho jo dastarkhwan par sharaab bhi piyein to kya un ke saath ek hi dastarkhwan par baith kar khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Jaaiz nahi kyun ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne aise dastarkhwan par khaana khaane se mana'a farmaaya hai jahan sharaab pi jaati ho. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj:7/378, Sahih At-Targibu wat-Tarheebu:172, Mu'ajamul Ausat:1/186). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Islam Laane Se Pehle Haraam Tareeqah Se Kamaaye Huye Maal Ka Hukm

Sawal: Agar koi Shakhs islaam laye to us ke maal ka kya hukm hoga jo batareeqe haraam kamaya ho?

Jawab: Wo maal us ke liye halaal hai basharte ke us maal par qabzah kar chuka ho, aur jis maal par abhi qabzah na huwa ho (masalan sharaab becha ho aur us ki qeemat udhaar ho) to islaam lane ke baad wo maal lena jaaiz nahi. (Kitabul Umm: Baab hukm ahluz zimmah:4/223). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Gairon Ke Tehwaaron Me Mili Chuttiyon Ki Tankhwah

Sawal: Diwali, Ganesh utsav, Dashera aur Chrismas jaise gairon ke in tehwaaron me kaseer ta'adaad me hukoomat ki taraf se ta'ateelaat milti hain, is me hamaare musalmaan bhaiyon ko bhi chuttiyan milti hain. Kya gairo ke tehwaaron me mili chuttiyon ki tankhwah halaal hai?

Jawab: Ji ha, Halaal hai. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Gair Muslim Naukar Ko Un Ke Tehwaar Par Paise Dena

Sawal: Agar hamaare ghar me koi gair muslim 'aurat hai to kya un ke kisi tehwaar par un ko alag se paise de sakte hain?

Jawab: Jo cheez Sharee'at ke khilaaf ho barahe rast us ke liye paise na de baaqi aise dene me koi haraj nahi hoga. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami Wa Adillatuh:7/5116). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

KHEL KOOD KE MASA-IL

Cricket Khelna

Sawal: Cricket khelna aur cricket T.V par dekhna ya online score dekhna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Fuqahaa-e-Kiraam ne zakheraye ahaadees ko samne rakh kar chand usool murattab kiye hain, jin par gaur karne se maujoodah zamaane ke tamaam khelon ka hukm ma'aloom ho jaata hai, wo usool darja zail hain:

Lihaaza agar cricket me mazkoorah baala kharabiyan paayi jaayein ya'ani us me mashgool ho kar waalidain ke huqooq me kotaahi aur shara'ee Faraaiz aur waajibaat me kotaahi aur gaflat barti jaati ho ya is me gair shara'ee umoor ka irtikaab kiya jaata ho, masalan mardon aur 'auraton ka ikhtilaat, mosiki aur juwa wagayrah ya tasweer saazi ho ya use mahaz lahwo la'ib (amusements and play) ke liye khela jaata ho to yeh jaaiz nahi.

Albatta agar is me mazkoorah kharaabiyan na paayi jaayein balke use badan ki warzish sihhat aur tandrusti baaqi rakhne ke liye ya kam se kam tabee'at ki thakaawat door karne ke liye khela jaaye aur us me gulu na kiya jaaye neez isi ko mashgalah na banaaya jaaye, waajibaat aur Faraaiz aur zaroori kaamon me is se haraj na pade to phir jismaani warzish ki had (limit) tak cricket khelne ki gunjaayish hogi. T.V par cricket dekhna jaaiz nahi, online score dekhne mien agar kisi haraam ka irtikaab na ho to muzaaiqah nahi (Jamiyatul Uloom al-Islamiyah Karachi). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Cricket Tournament Mun'aqid Karna Ya Is Me Khelna

Sawal: Kya Cricket Tournament mun'aqid karna ya is me khelna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Sharee'ate Mutahharah me muqaabalah ke andar dono ya tamaam teemon ki jaanib se maali shart lagaana juwa aur qimaar (juwwa) (juwwa bazi) ke hukm me hai aur juwa w qimaar (juwwa) shar'an naajaaiz aur haraam hain, lihaaza is se ehtiraaz (bachna) laazim hai. (As-Sirajul-Wahhaj:548). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Carrom Board Khelna

Sawal: Carrom Board khelna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Sharee'ate Mutahharah ne aise khelon ki haosalah afzaayi ki hai jin khelon se jismaani faaidah hota ho jaise tayraaki, teer andaazi, ghudhsawaari wagayrah aur un khelon ki hoslah shikni ki hai jis me lagne se waqt ka ziya'a (waste) hota ho aur aise khelon ko naajaaiz qaraar diya hai jin ki wajah se gunaahon ka irtiqaab laazim aata ho ya wo khel fussaaq wa fujjaar ke khel hon jaise taash, nard, carrom board wagayrah chunke carrom board me koi dunyavi manfa'at bhi nahi paayi jaati neez basa auqaat us me is darjah inhimaak (bahut ziyadah mashgooliyat) hota hai ke namaz aur deegar umoore deen se bhi insaan gaafil ho jaata hai, lihaaza is ka khelna jaaiz nahi. (Jamiyatul Uloom al-Islamiyah Karachi). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shatranj (Chess) Khelne Ka Shar'an Hukm

Sawal: Kya shatranj (Chess) khelna shar'an jaaiz hai?

Jawab: imaam Nawawi (Rh) farmaate hain ke Shawaafe' ke nazdeek sahi qaul yahi hai ke shatranj (Chess) khelna makrooh hai, jabke us ke saath juwa ya koi fahash kaam shaamil na ho, ya us ki wajah se namaz zaae'e (missed) na ho, warnah shatranj khelna haraam hoga. Wallahu A'alam. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 11/225, Al-Bayaan: 13/287). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Taash (Playing Cards) Aur Ludo Khelna

Sawal: Kya hadees me Ludo khelne se mana'a kiya gaya hai?

Jawab: Hadees me jis khel se mana'a kiya gaya hai, us ka naam “Nard” ya “Nard Shair” hai jaisa ke Saheeh Muslim me hai. Ke jis ne “Nard” khela to us ne goya khinzir aur us ke khoon me apna haath duboya. “Nard” dar-asal Aap (ﷺ) ke zamaane me ahle faaras ka ek khel tha jise hum Indoor Games me shaamil kar sakte hain lihaaza is daur me agar Nard ka naam “Ludo” pad gaya ho to use khelna bhi naajaaiz hoga. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Kya taash aur ludo khelna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Juwe aur shart ke bagair namaz wagayrah ka khayal rakhte huye bhi taash aur ludo khelna apne aap ko laaya'ani (bekaar) aur fuzool kaamon me mashgool rakhna hai, zaahir hai ke is se dunya ya deen ka koi mafaad bhi wabastah nahi is liye aise khel me masroof rehna makrooh hai, ijtinaab karna chaahiye chunanche fataawah Rasheediyah me hai ke: taash ya ludo khelna bahut bura hai aur agar is par haar jeet ki shart ho to juwa hai jo bilkul haraam hai. Gunahe kabeerah se bachna har musalmaan par farz hai. Kamiyaab momin ki shaan Allah Ta'aala ne Qur-aan-e-kareem me yun bayaan ki hai (Kaamyab momineen wo hain jo lahwo la'ib (amusements and play) se e'araaz karte hain) Hazrat Shaikhul Hind (Rh) likhte hain ya'ani kamiyaab momineen wo hain jo khud to lahwo la'ib (amusements and play) me masroof nahi hote balke agar koi aur shakhs masroof ho to us se bhi e'araaz karte hain. (Darul Iftaha: Jami'ah Al 'Uloomul Islamiyyah Binnori Town). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mobile, Computer Par Cartoon Ke Video Game Khelna

Sawal: Mobile, computer ya Tablet par cartoon ke video games khelna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Jis game me tasweer ho us game ki hurmat ki wajah se aur juwe ki wajah se khelna jaaiz nahi hai. Is ke 'alaawah deegar game agarchah us me juwa na ho, waqt ke ziya'a (waste) ki wajah se in ko khelna makrooh hai. (An-Najmul Wahhaaj: 7/383). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

PUBG Khelna

Sawal: Maujoodah daur me hamaare darmiyan ek mobile game bahut ziyadah mashhoor wa ma'roof hai, jis ka naam PUBG (Player Unknowns Battle Grounds) hai. Khel me ek sau tak khilaadi cartoon ki shakl me parachute ke zariye jazire par uterte hain aur doosron ko marne ke liye hathiyaron aur saaz wa samaan ke liye ghotalah karte hain jabke khud ko halaak karne se bachte hain. Khel ke naqshe ka dastiyaab mahfooz 'alaqa waqt ke saath size me kam hota jaata hai, jo zindah bach jaane waale khilaadiyon ko muqaabalon par majboor karne ke liye sakht ilaaqon me muntaqil hota hai. Aakhiri khilaadi ya team asal round jitaati hai. Jis se jeetne waala bandah bahut khushi mehsoos karta hai. Kya yeh PUBG game Sharee'ate Islam ki nazar me jaaiz hai ya naajaaiz?

Jawab: PUBG Game kaee (many) tarah ke gayr shara'ee wa deegar mafaasid (harmful consequences) par mushtamil hai is ke khelne me na koi deeni aur na hi koi dunyaavi faaidah hai masalan jismaani warzish wagayrah, yeh mahaz lahwo la'ib (amusements and play) aur waqt guzaari ke liye khela jaata hai jo laaya'ani kaam hai aur is ko khelne waale 'aam taur par is qadar 'aadi ho jate hain ke phir unhein is ka nashah ho jaata hai aur aisa inhimaak hota hai ke wo kayi deeni balke dunyavi umoor se bhi gaafil ho jaate hain, Sharee'ate mutahharah aise laya'ani lahwo la'ib (amusements and play) par mushtamil khel ki ijaazat nahi deti, is me mazeed shara'ee qabaahat yeh bhi hai ke jaandaar ki tasaawir par mushtamil hoti hai. Neez mushahadah yeh hai ke jo log is game ko baar baar khelte hain un ka zahan manfi hone lagta hai aur game ki tarah wo waqa'ee dunya me bhi maar dhaadh wagayrah ke kaam sochte hain jis ke bahut se waqiaat wuqu'a pazeer huye hain lihaaza PUBG aur is jaise deegar game khelna shar'an jaaiz nahi hai. (Darul Iftah'a: Jami'ah Al 'Uloomul Islamiyyah Binnori Town). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

MUTA'ADDI (CONTAGIOUS) BEEMAARIYON KE MASA-IL

Wabaayi Amraaz Ki Bunyaadi Wujoohaat Aur A'amaal

Sawal: Jaan Lewa wabaayi amraaz jaise ke Corona Virus jo bahut tezi se puri dunya me phail raha hai is ki bunyaadi wujoohat aur us moqah par musalmaanon ko kya karna chaahiye?

Jawab: Jaan lewa wabaayi amraaz ki wajah huqooqul 'ibaad me kotaahi karna aur Allah ke azaab se be khauf ho kar e'elaaniyah gunah ki jur'at karna aur is par israar karna hai kyunke jab koi qaum deeni ahkaam mein ijtimaa'i taur par gaflat ka shikaar ho jaati hai to Allah unhein mukhtalif tareeqon se aazmayishon me mubtalah karta hai taake log Allah Ta'aala ki taraf mutawajjah ho jaayein aur gunaahon se sachchi pakki taubah karke Allah ta'aala ki itaa'at karne waale ho jaayein unhein aazmaayishon mein se yeh wabaayi marz Corona Virus bhi hai.

Islam me sab se pehle waali 'umoomi wabaa (pandemic) Hazrat 'umr (Rz) ke daure Khilaafat me aayi jis ko “Taa'oone Amwaas” kaha jaata hai. Is Taa'oon (plague) me jahan 'umoomi amwaat huyein wahein shaam ke governer Badari Sahaabi Hazrat Abu Ubaidah Bin Jarrah (Rz) bhi us me shaheed ho gaye. Un ki wafaat ke baad banne waale governer Hazrat Mu'aaz (Rz) ne sab se pehli naseehat jo logon ko ki wo sachchi taubah ki naseehat thi, us ke baad logon ke huqooq ki taraf mutawajjah karte huye qarz khwa ko qarz ada karne ka hukm diya aur qata'a ta'alluq karne waalon ko apne bhaayiyon se mil kar musaafaha karne ki talqeen ki, in waaqi'aat ki roshani me yeh ma'aloom hota hai ke wabaa (pandemic) door karne mein mu'aamalaat wa mu'aasharati a'amaal ki islaah ka bhi bahut bada dakhal hai. Albatta zaahiri asbaab ke darje me muta'addi (contagious) beemaari ke virus se bachne ke liye ehtiyaatan logon se musaafaha na kare balke sirf salaam par iktifaa kare, isi tarah kasrat se sadaqah wa khairaat kare aur darj zail masnoon duwaon ka bhi ehtimaam kare.

''اَللّٰهُمَّ إِنِّیْ أَعُوْذُبِکَ مِنَ الْبَرَصِ، وَالْجُنُوْنِ، وَالْجُذَامِ، وَمِنْ سَیِّءِ الاَسْقَامِ''

“Aye Allah! Beshak mai panah maangta hun tujh se phal behri (sufed daag) aur paagal pan aur kodh aur intaahayi muhlik beemaariyon se.

''اَللّٰھُمَّ عَافِنِیْ فِیْ بَدَنِیْ اَللّٰھُمَّ عَافِنِیْ فِیْ سَمْعِیْ اَللّٰھُمَ عَافِنِیْ فِیْ بَصَرِیْ لَآ اِلَہَ اِلَّااَنْتَ''

“Aye Allah! Mere badan, meri samaa'at aur meri aankhon me mujhe aafiyat de, aap ke siwa koi ma'abood nahi. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Juzaam (Leprosy) Ki Beemaari Waale Shakhs Ke Saath Khaana

Sawal: Kya Majzoom (Juzaam / leprosy ki beemaari waale shakhs) ke saath khaana kha sakte hain?

Jawab: Majzoom shakhs ke saath khaana khaane ka daaromadaar us shakhs ki eemaani kaifiyat par munhasir hoga, qaviyyul eemaan aur qaviyyul Aqeedah shakhs ke liye gunjaayish hogi lekin jin logon ka eemaan aur aqeedah kamzor ho un ke liye ehtiyaat baratna hi behtar aur zaroori hai chunaache ek martabah Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke paas ek majzoom (Juzaam / leprosy ki beemaari me mubtala (afflicted) shakhs aaya ya'ani aysi beemaari waala shakhs ke jis beemaari ke muta'alliq mashhoor tha ke yeh phailti hai) aaya to Aap (ﷺ) ne us ke saath baith kar khaana tanaaul farmaya jis se ma'aloom huwa ke beemaar aadmi se yaqeeni taur par beemaari lag jaane ka e'etiqaad durust nahi hai is liye majzoom ke saath khaana kha sakte hain, albatta ehtiyaat behtar hai kyunke ahadees me muta'addi (contagious) samjhi jaane waali beemaariyon me mubtala (afflicted) logon se door rehne ka hukm diya gaya hai. (Fathul-Bari: 10/159). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muta'addi (Contagious) Beemaari Phail Jaaye To Ehtiyaati Tadaabir Ikhtiyaar Karna

Sawal: Agar kisi 'alaqe me koi wabaa / muta'addi Beemaari (Contagious Disease) masalan taa'oon (plague) wagayrah phail jaaye to ehtiyaati tadaabeer ikhtiyaar karne ke silsile me Sharee'ate Islam ka nuqtahe nazar kya hai?

Jawab: Bukhaari shareef me Hazrat Saad aur Hazrat Abdul Rahman bin Auf (Rz) se manqool hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: Jab tum kisi sar zameen me taa'oon (plague) ki ittila'a paao to is sar zameen me daakhil na ho, agar jahan tum ho waheen taa'oon (plague) phoot pade aur phail jaaye to us maqaam se baahar na jaao (Bukhaari: 2/852).

Jab asbaab ke darjeh me in amraaz ka muta'addi (contagious) hona saabit hai to sihhate 'aammah ki hifaazat ke liye ehtiyaati tadaabeer ko ikhtiyaar karna az qabeele waajibaat hai.

Is liye koi is wabaa se bhaagna chaahta ho to yeh mana'a hai, lekin agar kisi ki niyyat taa'oon (plague) se bhagna na ho balke zaroorat ki wajah se wabaa zadah 'alaqe se baahar jaana ho to jaaiz hai, isi tarah Doctor ya koi ahle khair taa'oon (plague) zadah logon ko tibbi imdaad aur raahat pahonchaane ki garz se taa'oon (plague) zadah 'alaqe me daakhil ho to yeh na sirf jaaiz hoga balke baayise ajr bhi hoga. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:15/131).

Muta'addi (Phailne waali) beemaariyon se bachne ke liye ehtiyaati tadaabeer isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai, taaham yeh aqeedah na ho ke aysi jagah me jaane se ya rehne se ya aise shakhs ke qurb se beemaari ka lagna yaqeeni hai. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muta'addi Beemaari Phelne Par Logon Se Musaafaha Na Karna

Sawal: Agar ksii beemaari ka virus ho jaaye aur wo phailna shuru' ho jaaye jaisa ke aaj kal corona virus (COVID-19) china se shuru' ho kar poori dunya me phail chuka hai. Yeh ek muta'addi (contagious) beemaari (Contagious Diseases) hai, kya is se bachne ke liye logon se musaafaha na karna durust hai?

Jawab: Koi bhi beemaari Allah Ta'aala ki mashiyyat ke bagair fi nafsihi muta'addi (contagious) nahi hoti balke jab Allah Ta'alaa is me muta'addi hone ki taaseer daal dete hain us waqt wo doosre ki taraf muntaqil ho jaati hai, hadeeson me muta'addi samjhi jaane waali beemaariyon me mubtala (afflicted) logon aur jaanwaron se ehtiyaat karne ka hukm diya hai.

Huzoor (ﷺ) ne farmaya “na koi beemaari muta'addi hai aur na koi bad shugooni hai, aur na hi Ullu manhoos hai aur no maahe safar aur majzoom se aise bhaago jaise tum sher se bhaagte ho.”

Muslim ki riwaayat me hai ke wafde saqeef me ek majzoom shakhs the wo bhi deegar logon ke saath Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ke haath par bay'at karne aa rahe the to Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne un ki taraf yeh paigaam bheja ke “hum ne tumhaari bay'at kar li hai, lihaaza tum waapas laut jaao.”Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne majzoom se haath milaaye bagair hi use bay'at kar liya jis se ma'aloom hota hai ke ehtiyaat ke darjah me beemaari ke zaahiri asbaab se bachna jaaiz hai, taake agar koi ikhtilaat ke baad ba-mashiyyate khudaawandi (by the will of Allah) beemaar ho gaya, to is ka e'etiqaad kharaab na ho. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:15/131).

Isi tarah hadees me hai ke “Mareez oonton ka maalik apne oont, tandurust oonton waale ke oont me na chode.” ek aur hadees me Huzoor (ﷺ) ne farmaaya. “Juzaam zadah (leprosy patient) mareezon par ziyadah der tak nazar na daala karo.” (At-tareekhul Awsat Lil Bukhari:2/55).

In ahaadees ki roshani me agar muta'addi (contagious) beemaari ka virus ho jaaye aur wo phailna shuru' ho jaaye to is se bachne ke liye ehtiyaatan logon se musaafaha na karne aur sirf salaam par iktifa karne me koi haraj nahi. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj:4/218). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Ke Difa'a Me Alcohol Based Sanitizer Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Corona virus se hifaazat ki khaatir haath dhone ke liye ek tarah ka sayyaal Maaddah (Sanitizer) isti'emaal kiya jaata jis me kuch nisbat Alcohol bhi hota hai is tarah ki masnoo'aat isti'emaal karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Mazhabe Islam me Nasha paida karne waali cheezon ka isti'emaal karna mamnu' aur haraam hai, albatta baaz sooraton me masalan 'ilaaj wagayrah ki garz se islaam ne chand sharaait ke saath is tarah ki cheezon ke isti'emaal ki ijaazat bhi di hai. Alcohol bhi isi qabeel se hai maahireen ke mutaabiq alcohol ki baaz qismein khujoor (date) aur angoor ke 'alaawah shakar, jaw, ganna, chuqandar aur jawaar wagayrah ke daanon se tayyar ki jaati hai jo Ethyl Alcohol ke naam se jaani jaati hai is ka faaidah yeh hota hai ke yeh adviyaat (Medicines) aur mashroobaat (Beverages) ki mee'aad khatam (Expiry date) tak kharab hone se hifaazat karta hai. Neez agar angoor aur khujoor (date) se bana huwa alcohol kisi dawa me maujood ho to chunke is ki miqdaar bhi 'aam taur par asal maaddah me Mustahlik (Expendable Material) ki si hoti hai. Lihaaza corona virus ke mauqa'a par jo hand sanitizer ka isti'emaal ho raha hai wo bhi ek qism ki dawa hai is liye is ke isti'emaal karne me koi haraj nahi. (Kitaabul Umm: 6/156, Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 4/158, Takmilatu Fathil Mulhim: 5/342-343). Mufti Sawood Mujawar Nadvi Aur Maulana Suhail Manipuri

Corona Virus Ko Dafa'a Karne Ke Liye Azaan Dena

Sawal: Corona virus ko dafa'a karne ke liye azaan dene ki shara'ee haisiyat kiya hai?

Jawab: Wabaa (pandemic) ke khaatimah ke hawaale se baaz fuqahaye Shawaafe' ne baaz naagahaani aafaat aur shadeed masaayib wa pareshaani se najaat ke liye azaan dene ki ijaazat di hai, lihaaza Soorate mas-uoolah me azaan dene ki gunjaayish hogi. (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 1/267). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muta'addi (Contagious) Beemaariyon Se Bachne Ke Liye Ehtiyaatan Mask Pehen Kar Namaz Padhna

Sawal: Corona virus ya is jaisi deegar muta'addi (contagious) beemaariyon se bachne ke liye ehtiyaatan mask pehen kar namaz padhne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Namaz ke durust hone ke liye deegar sharaait ke saath saath satar ka chupaana bhi shart hai aur sajde ke durust hone ke liye saat a'azaa ka zameen par lagna shart hai, saath saath yeh bhi shart hai ke peshaani khuli rahe. Agar peshaani kisi cheez se chupi hui hogi to wo sajdah sahi nahi hoga lihaaza namaz durust nahi hogi. Ab dekha jaayega ke namazi ne kis qism ka “Mask” pehen rakhkha hai. Agar aisa mask pehen rakhkha hai jis se sirf naak aur muh dhaka huwa hai to namaz durust hogi, aur agar aisa mask pehen rakhkha hai jo peshaani ko bhi chupaane waala hai, to namaz durusut nahi hogi (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj:1/509). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Ke Phailne Ki Wajah Se Juma Aur Baajama'at Namazon Ka Hukm

Sawal: Corona virus ke phailne ki wajah se khaleeji mamaalik (Gulf Countries) ki hukoomat ki jaanib se juma aur baajama'at namazon par paabandi laga di gai hai to aise mauqe'e par kya kare?

Jawab: Allah Ta'aala ki ta'seer se muta'addi (contagious) hone waali beemaariyon ki bina par masaajid ko band karna aur masjid me baajamaa-'at namaz padhne par paabandi lagaana shar'an jaaiz nahi hai. Lekin agar hukoomat ki jaanib se Jumu'ah aur baajamaa'at namazon par paabandi lagaa di gai ho to awwalan baa asar shakhs ke zariye us ke bahaali ki koshish kare. Koshish ke baawajood paabandi na hati to aysi soorat me ghar hi me biwi aur bachchon ko saath le kar ba jamaa'at namaz ka ehtimaam kare. Yahi hukm Jumu'ah ka bhi hoga ke hukoomat ki jaanib se Jumu'ah par paabandi aayid kar di gai ho to apne ghar hi me zuhr ki 4 rak'at ke saath ada kare, ghar me Jumu'ah nahi padh sakte kyunke Hazrat imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek Jumu'ah mun'aqid hone ke liye deegar sharaait ke saath saath ek shart challis (40) afraad ka hona bhi hai. Chalis (40) afraad se kam log jama hon to Jumu'ah ki namaz munaqqid nahi hogi albatta agar kahin Jumu'ah padhi ja rahi ho jahan shareek afraad 40 se kam hon to un ke saath Jumu'ah ki niyyat se Jumu'ah ada kare phir ehtiyaatan apni zuhr ki namaz padh le. (Al-Majmoo':4/493). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Ki Wajah Se Safon Me Faaslah Rakhna

Sawal: Aaj kal masaajid me corona virus ki wajah se ek hi saff me namazi ek doosre se kuch faasle par khade rehte hain kya is tarah safon me ziyadah khaali jagah rakhne se jamaa'at ki fazeelat haasil hogi?

Jawab: Safon ko mukammal karna, mil kar khada hona aur darmiyan mein khaali jagah na chodna ya'ani saff bandi karna ek mustaqil sunnat 'amal hai, jis ki Hazrat Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne badi taakeed farmaayi hai.

Shawaafe' ke nazdeek safon ko durust karna qareeb qareeb khade hona ek sidh me khade hona aur jab tak agli saf mukammal na ho pichli saff me khade na hona yeh sab mustahab a'amaal hain, jinhein tark karna makrooh hai, lekin is mustahab 'amal ko tark karne ki soorat me jamaa'at ka sawaab faut nahi hoga.

Lihaaza maujoodah daur me darpesh halaat ki wajah se hukoomat ki taraf se jaari kardah hidaayat ke peshenazar safon ki durustagi aur takmil waali sunnat faut ho jaaye tab bhi jamaa'at ka sawaab haasil ho jaayega. (Sharhul-Muqaddamatul-Hazramiyyah: 1/362, Bushral-Kareem).

Lekin jab haalaat sazgaar ho jaayein to is kaifiyat se namaz na padhein balke safbandi ka khaas khayaal rakhein kyunke hadeese paak me waarid huwa hai ke Hazrat Naumaan Bin Basheer (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) hamaari safon ko is qadar seedha aur baraabar karaate the goya ke un ke zariye aap teeron ko seedha karenge yahaan tak ke aap ko khayaal ho gaya ke ab hum log samajh gaye (ke hum ko kis tarah baraabar khada hona chaahiye) is ke baad ek din aisa huwa ke Aap (ﷺ) baahar tashreef laye aur namaz padhaane ke liye apni jagah par khade bhi ho gaye, yahaan tak ke qareeb tha ke Aap (ﷺ) takbeer keh ke namaz shuru' farma dein ke Aap (ﷺ) ki nigah ek shakhs par padi jis ka seenah saf se kuch aage nikla huwa tha Aap (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke: Allah ke bando! Apni safon ko seedha aur bilkul baraabar karo warnah Allah Ta'aala tumhaare rukh ek doosre ke mukhaalif kar dega (Saheeh Bukhaari: 717, Saheeh Muslim:436).

Is hadees ke raavi Hazrat Nauman Bin Basheer (Rz) ka matlab yeh hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) hamaari safon ko is qadar seedhi aur baraabar karne ki koshish farmaate the ke hum me se koi soot baraabar bhi aage ya peeche na ho, yahan tak ke taweel muddat ki is musalsal koshish aur tarbiyat ke baad Aap (ﷺ) ko itminaan ho gaya ke hum ko yeh baat samajh aa gai, lekin is ke baad jab ek din Aap (ﷺ) ne is mu'aamalah me ek aadmi ki kotaahi dekhi to bade jalaal ke andaaz me farmaaya ke: Allah ke bando! Mai tum ko aagahi deta hun ke agar safon ko baraabar aur sheedha karne me tum be parwahi aur kotaahi karoge to Allah Ta'aala is ki saza me tumhare rukh ek doosre se mukhtalif kar dega, ya'ani tumhaari wahdat (unity) aur ijtimaa'iyat (collectivism) parah parah (scattering into pieces) kar di jaayegi aur tum me phoot pad jaayegi jo ummaton aur qaumon ke liye is dunya me sau (100) azabon ka ek azaab hai. Safon ko baraabar aur seedha karne me kotaahi aur gaflat par baahami ikhtilaaf aur phoot ki wa'eed muta'addad hadeeson me waarid hui hai aur bilaashubah is qusoor aur is ki is saza me khas munaasabat hai. Afsos bahut si doosri cheezon ki tarah is muaamale mein bhi kotaahi khas kar baaz ilaaqon me bahut 'aam ho chuki hai. (M'aariful Hadees. Hazrat Maulana Manzoor Naumani Sahab, Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 1/493). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gharon Me Ziyadah Saamaan Ka Stock Karna

Sawal: Corona virus ke phailne ki wajah se tamaam logon me ek khauf aur dahshat si chaayi hui hai, taqreeban Arab mamaalik ne Airport bhi band kar diye hain, ab logon ko darr hai ke roz marrah ke saamaan ka stock khatam ho jaayega phir zarooriyaate zindagi ka saamaan muhayyaa (available) nahi ho paayega to kya aysi soorat me gharon me ziyadah saamaan ka stock kar ke rakhna jaaiz hoga?

Jawab: Apni zaroorat ke mutaabiq saamaan khareed kar ghar par rakhna jaaiz hai taake baar baar super market me jaane ki zaroorat pesh na aaye kyunke 'aam taur par wahan bheed bhaad hoti hai aur log ehtiyaat nahi karte jis ki wajah se beemaari phailne ka andeshah hota hai lihaaza ek baargi saamaan ka zakhairah apne paas la kar rakh lein baar baar bazaar ke chakkar lagaane se guraiz kare. Hadeese paak me jis zakhairah andozi ko mamnu' aur gunah kaha gaya hai is se muraad wahi zakhairah andozi hai jis se logon ki gizayi ajnaas me qillat paida ho jaaye aur wo pareshaani ka shikaar ho jaayein. Ahl-o-'ayaal ke nafaqah aur zarooriyaat ke liye gallaah wagayrah ka zakhairah karna mamnu' nahi. Ibne Arsalaan (Rh) farmaate hain ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) apne ahl-o-'ayaal ke liye saal bhar lagne waali giza jama'a karke rakhkha karte the, isi tarah Sahaabi e Rasool Hazrat ma'amar (Rz) aur Hazrat Saeed Bin Musayyib (Rh) bhi tel, gehu, aur isi tarah ki cheezon ki zakhairah andozi karte the. (Al-Majmoo': 13/47, (Fathul-'Aziz: 8/216). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Lock Down Ki Wajah Se Maal Ki Peshgi (Advance) Zakaat Nikaalna

Sawal: Corona Virus ki wabaa (pandemic) ke phailne ki wajah se mulk bhar me Lock Down kiya gaya hai, jis ki wajah se rozaanah mehnat mazdoori karke kamaane aur khaane waalon ko bahut hi pareshaani ka saamna karna pad raha hai, haalat yahan tak pahonch chuki hai ke rozmarrah ki zarooriyaate zindagi bhi mayassar nahi ho rahi hai. 'Aam halaate zindagi me in me se aksar log zakaat ke mustahiq nahi hote, lekin ab wo log mustahiqqeene zakaat ki fehrist me daakhil ho chuke hain, Sawal yeh hai ke kya inhein zakaat di ja sakti hai? Aur chunke logon ki ta'daad bahut ziyadah hai aur haalaat kab durust honge koi nahi janta, to kya aysi soorat mein apne maal ki peshgi (Advance) zakaat nikaal kar inhein de sakte hain?

Jawab: Achanak Lock Down ke hukm ki wajah se bahut saare log naukari ke saath saath apni rozi roti se bhi mahroom ho gaye hain aur naubat iztiraari haalat tak pahonch chuki hai, aur yeh log zakaat ke mustahiq ho chuke hain. Lihaaza ahle khair hazraat ki yeh akhlaaqi zimmedaari hai ke wo sadqaat wa 'atiyyaat se un ka ta'aaun kare, maal par saal mukammal ho chuka ho to us ki zakaat nikaal kar ada kare isi tarah nisaab ke baqadr maal par saal mukammal na huwa ho balke jaari ho to chand sharaait ke saath us maal ki peshgi zakaat bhi nikaal kar ada kar sakte hain. Masalan: kisi shakhs ka ma'amul ho ke wo har saal Ramazan ke awaayil me zakaat nikaalta ho to ab us ke saal ki takmeel Ramazanul Mubarak ke shuru' hote hi ho jaayegi, lihaaza jin logon ne Ramazan 1440 ki ibtida'a me zakaat ada ki thi un ke nisaab ke baqadr maal par saal ki takmeel Ramazaan 1441 ke shuru' hote hi ho jaayegi, lihaaza agar yeh log sha'abaan 1441 me zakaat nikaalein to yeh jaari saal ki peshgi zakaat shumaar hogi jis ki takmeel ek maah baad Ramazaan me hone waali hai, to ehtiyaatan is maale zakaat me se un logon ko dein jo maujoodah waqt me Lock Down ki wajah se zaroorat mand aur mustahiq ho gaye hain, is ke 'alaawah dino me un ki yeh haalat aur kaifiyat nahi hoti, phir awaayil Ramazaan 1441 ke baad nisaab ke baqadr maal par jab doosra saal shuru' ho jaayega to us maal ki bhi peshgi zakaat nikaal sakte hain, ab us saal ki zakaat un logon ko dein jin ke muta-'alliq gaalib gumaan yeh ho ke yeh log saal ki takmeel tak mustahiqqe zakaat rahenge, Wallahu A'alam.

Peshgi (Advance) zakaat mundarijah zail sharaait ke paaye jaane ki soorat me nikaal kar ada karna jaaiz hai.

Yeh baat to hum sabhi ke 'ilm me hogi ke asal haqdaar to musalmaan hi hain, lekin zarooratmandon me ek bada tabqah gair muslim biraadahraane watan ka bhi hai, lihaaza waqt ke taqaaze ke peshe nazar apne nafali sadaqaat wa 'atiyyaat se in ka bhi ta'aaun karte rahen. (Al-Bayaan: 3/378). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Ke Phailne Ki Wajah Se 'Umrah Wa Hajj Visa Band Karna

Sawal: Corona Virus ke phailne ki wajah se Saudi Hukoomat ne 'Umrah wa Hajj visa band kiye hain, is bare me shara'ee hukm kya hai?

Jawab: Muta'addi (Phailne waali) beemaari masalan corona virus se bachne aur is ke phailaav ko rokne ke liye tamaam zaroori ehtiyaati tadaabeer isti'emaal karna tawakkul ke manaafi nahi balke shar'an jaaiz hai jaisa ke mukhtalif ahaadees me is ka suboot milta hai, chunaanche Tirmizi shareef me Hazrat Anas (Rz) se marvi hai ke ek shakhs Nabi Paak (ﷺ) ki khidmat me haazir huwa aur poocha ke Ay Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) mai is ootni ko baandhun phir Allah par tawakkul karun ya use yun hi aazaad chod dun aur tawakkul karun to Aap (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya ke use baandho phir Allah par tawakkul karo. (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 2517).

Taareekh me chand aise Shawaahid milte hain jin se pata chala hai ke taqreeban 40 dafah aise haalaat aaye jin me hajj ada na ho saka aur kayi baar khana-e-Ka'abah haajiyon ke liye band raha, is ki kayi wujoohaat thien jin me Baitullah par hamle se le kar siyaasi jhaghde , wabaa (pandemic), sailaab chor aur dakuwon ka haajiyon ke qaafilon par hamla karna aur mausam ki kharaabi shaamil hai, har saal haajiyon ke hajj se faraagat ke baad taaqreeban ek maheene tak 'Umrah ki visa nahi di jaati taake saaf safaayi aur bahaali (Maintenance) ka kaam kiya ja sake. Chunke corona virus bahut tezi se poori dunya me phail chuka hai lihaaza is ko rokne ke liye hajj wa 'Umrah ke silsile me Saudi 'Arab ki hukoomat ki taraf se jari kardah tamaam hidaayaat par 'amal karna zaroori hai. (Nihayatul-Muhtaaj Ila Sharhil Minhaaj: 6/63). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Se Faut Shuda Shakhs Ke Liye Shaheed Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya corona virus se faut shudah shakhs shaheed kehlaayega? Aur kya dunya me is ke saath shaheedon jaisa mu'aamalah kiya jaayega?

Jawab: Wabaayi Amraaz jaise taa'oon (plague) ya maujoodah daur me corona virus jaisi beemaariyon me musalmaanon me se jo intiqaal kar jaaye to wo ukhravi e'etibaar se shaheed kehlaayega, ya'ani aakhirat me use shahaadat ka darjah milega albatta dunya me us ke saath shaheedon jaisa mu'aamalah nahi kiya jaayega, ya'ani use gusl bhi diya jaayega aur kafan bhi. (Al-Majmoo':5/364). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Ke Sabab Wafaat Paane Waale Ke Liye Gusl, Namaze Janazah Aur Tadfeen Ka Hukm

Sawal: Corona Virus ki wajah se agar kisi ki maut hoti hai to hukoomat is laash ko sandooq ya plastic cover me lapet kar deti hai is soorat me use kholne ki bilkul ijaazat nahi hoti balke isi haalat me dafan karne ka hukm hota hai taake virus ka phailav na ho, is soorat me gusl, namaze Janazah aur dafan ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Muslim mayyit ko gusl dena, kafan pehnaana, namaze Janazah padhna aur dafan kar dena sab farze Kifaayah hai. Chand log mil kar bhi in kaamon ko anjaam dein to tamaam logon se yeh farz saaqit ho jaayega. Asah qaul ke e'etibaar se mayyit ke gusl me niyyat karna waajib nahi, lihaaza agar koi gair muslim musalman mayyit ke poore jism par napaaki dur karne ke baad ek dafah is tarah paani bahaye ke poora jism tar batar ho jaaye aur aise baareek kapde par se bahaye jo paani ko jism tak pahonchaane ke liye maane'e (rukaawat) na ho to yeh bhi kaafi hai. Gusle Janazah me mayyit ke jism ko choona koi zaroori nahi, lihaaza agar koi shakhs mayyit ko kisi aalah ki madad se paani me duba kar bahar nikaale ya door se pipe ya fawwaare ki madad se spray paani bahaye to yeh bhi kaafi hai. 'Aam haalaat me plastic cover ke upar se gusl dena kaafi nahi samjha jaayega ya'ani gusl shumaar nahi hoga, aur bagair gusl diye namaze Janazah durust nahi hogi. Lekin agar mayyit ko bagair gusl diye dafnaaya gaya to mayyit ke ehteraam ki wajah se qabr ko dobaarah khoda nahi jaayega. Maujoodah haalaat me ehtiyaati tadaabeer ko peshe nazar rakhte huye hukoomat ki jaanib se sakht taakeed hoti hai ke is marz corona ki wajah se mare huye shakhs ko is cover se bilkul baahar na nikaala jaaye jis me use moher band kiya gaya hai, balke isi haalat me dafnaaya jaaye , to aysi soorat me dekha jaayega ke upar se paani bahaane ki ijaazat hai ya nahi agar upar se paani bahaane ki ijazat di gai hai to mas-alae jabeerah (Patti par paani se masah karne) par qiyaas karte huye plastic cover ke upar se paani bahaana ya geele haath se poore jism par masah karna is tarah se ke koi jagah paani lagne se choot na jaaye to yeh bhi kaafi samjha jaayega, lekin agar is ki bhi ijaazat na ho to bataure majboori isi haalat me namaze janazah padh kar dafnaaya ja sakta hai, kyunke muta-akhkhireen fuqhaaye Shawaafe' ke nazdeek gusl dena mumkin na ho to bagair gusl diye bhi mayyit par namaze Janazah padhne ka jawaaz milta hai, jaisa ke koi doob jaaye ya jal kar rakh ho jaaye to is par bagair gusl diye namaze Janazah padh sakte hain.

Phir maujoodah haalaat me hukoomat ki jaanib se paabandi ki bina par chand afraad mil kar namaze Janazah padh lein to yeh kaafi hoga aur baaqi hazraat se fareezah saaqit ho jaayega, deegar log bhi namaz padhne ka iraadah rakhte hon aur wo usi shahar ke rehnewaale hon aur koi rok tok na ho to qabr par ja kar namaz padhna yeh zaroori hai, lihaaza un logon ke liye namaze gaayibaanah ada karna jaaiz nahi, lekin agar hukoomat ki taraf se qabr par haaziri ko mana'a kiya gaya ho to 'uzr ki wajah se namaze gaayibaanah bhi padh sakte hain.
