Mayyit ko dafan karne ke liye itna gehra gadha khodna waajib hai jo mayyit ki badbu ko chupaane waala ho, 'aam haalaat me bila wajah sandooq me mayyit ko rakh kar dafnaana makrooh hai, lekin wahan koi 'uzr ho jaise gaddhe me paani jama hota ho ya zameen geeli ho ya koi 'uzr ho to makrooh nahi, albatta is soorat mein is baat ka ehtimaam kare ke pehle hi se mayyit ko sandooq me daahini karwat par litaaye aur qabr me utaare ya sandooq ko tircha karke qabr me utaare taake mayyit ka rukh qiblah ki taraf ho jaaye kyunke mayyit ka rukh qiblah ki taraf karna waajib hai. Dafnaane ke baad pata chala ke mayyit ko qiblah rukh litaaya nahi gaya tha to is ke sadhne se pehle dobaarah qabr khod kar qiblah rukh karna waajib hai.

Agar yeh mumkin na ho to isi haalat me chod dein jaisa ke ek qaul Imaam Nawawi (rh) ne “Rauzatut-taalibeen” me mashhoor Shafi'ee faqeeh “Qazi Abut Tayyib” (Rh) se naql kiya hai jise unhon ne apni kitaab (Al-Mujarrad) me likkha hai ke Mayyit ka rukh qiblah ki taraf karna sunnat hai, agar mayyit ko qiblah rukh litaaya na gaya ho aur dafnaane ke baad 'ilm ho jaaye to qabr khod kar qiblah rukh karna mustahab hai waajib nahi.

Tamaam musalmaanon ki yeh akhlaaqui aur mazhabi zimmadari hai ke Hifzaane Sihhat ki khaatir gusl se lekar qabristaan me dafnaane tak hukoomat ki taraf se jaari ki gai tamaam ehtiyaati tadaabeer masalan Medical Safety Kit ko ikhtiyaar kare, khud bhi mehfooz rahe auron ko bhi mehfooz rakhein. (Rauzatut-taalibeen:2/135, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/485, Minhaajut-Talibeen: 1/117). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Corona Virus Ki Wajah Se Jumu'ah Wa Jamaa'at Ke Liye Masaajid Band Kar Dena: Ek Tahqeeqi Jaayizah

Sawal: Corona Virus (COVID-19) ke phailne ki wajah se Khaleeji Mamaalik (Gulf Countries) ne baajamaa'at namazon par paabandi laga di aur masaajid band kar di, hatta ke Jumu'ah ke liye bhi masaajid band ki hain, is bare me shara'ee hukm kya hai?

Jawab: Corona Virus ki beemaari (COVID-19) ek muta'addi (Contagious Disease) phailne waali beemaari hai. Is beemaari ki pehli baar shanaakhat 2019 me wasati Cheen ke subah “Hubei” ke darul Hukoomat “Wuhan” me hui thi, aur ab soorate haal yeh hai ke yeh wabaa (pandemic) saare aalam me phail chuki hai.

'Aam 'Alamaat:

Bukhaar, khaansi, saans ki takleef, patthon me dard (muscular pain), Balgam (Sputum), is'haal (Diarrhea) aur gale ki sozish wagayrah hain.

Yeh Aur In Jaisi Wabaa Aur Beemaariyon Ki Bunyaadi Wujoohaat:

Yeh wabayein aur beemaariyan darhaqeeqat insaanon ke gunaahon aur naafarmaaniyon ki wajah se aati hain. Chunaanche Allah Ta'aala ka irshaad hai. “Aur jo bhi museebat tum ko pahonchti hai wo tumhare hathon ki kamaai hai.” (Sooratus Shu'ra: 30).

Beemaariyon Ke Muta'addi (contagious) Hone Ke Silsile Me Sharee'ate Islaam Ka Mauqaf:

Islam me Hifzaane Sihhat ki khaas ahmiyat hai, sihhat wa tandrusti ke tahaffuz aur is ki baqa ke liye islaam nihaayat qeemati usool wa zawaabit pesh karta hai lekin qaza aur qadr se jab koi beemaar ho jaata hai ya wabaayi marz phail jaata hai to is me bhi ehtiyaati tadaabeer ki hidaayaat maujood hain. Chunaanche Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ka irshaad hai: “Beemaar oont ko tandurust oont ke paas na le jaao.” (Saheehul Bukhaari).

Ek doosri hadees me Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke: Juzaami (leprosy wale) shakhs se is tarah bhaago jis tarah sher se bhagte ho.” (Saheehul Bukhaari) Juzaamiyon (leprosy patients) ki beemaari se bachne ke liye Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ne in se ek neze ke faasle se baat cheet karne ki bhi taakeed farmaayi hai. Hadees me hai ke “Kodhiyon ki taraf ziyadah der tak mat dekha karo aur in se is tarah baat karo ke tumhaare aur un ke darmiyaan ek nezah ka faasla rahe (Musnade Ahmed).

Isliye ke jab aadmi baat karta hai to us ke muh se thook ke cheente nikalte hain jis me beemaari ke kaafi jaraasim maujood hote hain yeh jab mukhaatab ke upar padenge to mukhaatab ko bhi beemaari me mubtala (afflicted) kar sakte hain. Isi tarah beemaari ke phailne se bachaaw ke liye taa'oon (plague) zadah 'alaqe me jaane aur wahan maujood hon to us jagah se nikalne se mana'a kiya gaya hai.

Hadees me hai ke “Jab tumhein ma'aloom ho ke kisi jagah taa'oon (plague) hai to wahan mat jaao aur jahan tum ho wahan agar taa'oon (plague) phail jaaye to use chod kar mat jaao.” (Saheeh Bukhaari wa Muslim).

In tamaam ahaadees se yeh baat saabit hoti hai ke baaz beemaariyan Allah Ta'aala ki ta'seer se muta'addi (contagious) hoti hain jin se ehtiyaat baratna zaroori hai.

Hazrat imaam Nawawi (Rh) farmaate hain ke is hadees me Allah Ta'aala ki mashiyyat aur faislah ke tahat jin cheezon se bil-'umoom nuqsaan pahonchta hai un se bachne ki ta'aleem di gai hai. Yahi jumhoor 'Ulama ka maslak hai aur isi ko ikhtiyaar kiya jana chaahiye. (Sharah Muslim).

Chunaanche Aap (ﷺ) ne in jaisi hadeeson me muta'addi samjhi jaane waali beemaariyon mein mubtala (afflicted) logon aur jaanwaro se ehtiyaat karne ka hukm diya, taake agar koi ikhtilaat ke baad ba-mashiyyate Khudaawandi (by the will of Allah) beemaar hogaya to is ka e'etiqaad kharab na ho.

Saheeh Bukhaari ki hadees “La Adwa” “koi beemaari kisi ek se doosri ko nahi lagti.” (Saheeh Bukhaari). Se muraad qata'ee yeh nahi ke beemaari ka ek se doosre ki taraf muntaqil hona koi haqeeqat nahi rakhta, balke is hadees ki muraad ko “Riyaazus Saaliheen” ki sharah daleelul Faaliheen me likkha huwa hai ke baaz beemaariyan jo muta'addi (infectious) samjhi jaati hain to is hadees me un ke muta'addi hone ka inkaar nahi balke sirf aqeede ki durusti ki taraf tawajjuh dilaayi gai hai ke beemaari lagne me asal dakhal Allah ki mashiyyat (marzi) ka hai, agar Allah ki mashiyyat ho to bandah beemaar hoga warnah nahi, beemaari aur wabaa (pandemic) me khud yeh ta'seer nahi ke kisi ko ja lage.

Imam Nawawi (Rh) farmaate hain is hadees me jahiliyat ke us aqeedah wa khayaal ki tardeed hai (ya'ani is aqeede ko galat thehraaya gaya hai) ke log yeh samajhte the ke beemaariyon ke phailne me Allah ka koi 'amal dakhal nahi hai balke wo apne taur par khud hi phailti rehti hai (Sharhe Muslim) ya'ani zamaanae Jaahiliyat me baaz beemaariyon mein logon ka yeh e'etiqaad tha ke jab koi tandrust aadmi kisi beemaar shakhs ke paas jaata hai, to us ki beemaari tandurust aadmi me khud bakhud muntaqil ho jaati hai aur Allah Ta'aala ki taqdeer aur mashiyyat ka is me koi dakhal nahi hota, to Aap alaihis salaam ne “la adwa” jaisi ahaadees me isi baatil aqeede ki tardeed farmaayi hai, jis ka khulasah yeh hai ke koi bhi beemaari Allah Ta'aala ki mashiyyat ke bagair fi nafsihi muta'addi (contagious) nahi hoti; balke jab Allah Ta'aala is me muta'addi hone ki ta'seer daal dete hain us waqt wo doosre ki taraf muntaqil ho jaati hai, haasile kalaam yeh hai ke hadees me marz ke muta'addi hone ki nafi nahi hai.

In Wabaayi Amraaz Se Najaat Haasil Karne Ka Tariqaekaar:

Jaisa ke hum jaan chuke hain ke insaanon par aayi hui musibatein un ki kartooton aur bad aamaliyon ka nateejah hoti hain, to un ko door karne ka ek hi raastah hoga, aur wo yeh ke insaan apne gunaahon se taubah wa istigfaar kare aur Allah Ta'aala ki khushnudi haasil kare.

Chunaanche Allah Tabarak wa Ta'aala ne Qur-aane majeed me mukhtalif jaghon par pareshaaniyon aur museebaton se najaat ka haasil ruju'a ilallah, istigfaar aur sabr wa namaz ko qaraar diya hai, Irshaade Baarie Ta'aala hai: “Ay Musalmaanon! Madad lo sabr aur namaz se, beshak Allah sabr karne waalon ke saath hai.” (Al-Baqarah).

Ista'eenu Bis Sabri was Salaat (اِسْتَعِيْنُوْا بِالصَّبْرِ وَالصَّلوٰة), is aayate kareemah me yeh hidaayat hai ke insaan ki tamaam hawaayij wa zarooriyaat ke poora karne aur tamaam aafaat wa masaayib aur takaaleef ko door karne ka nuskhae ikseer do ju'zwon se murakkab hai ek sabr doosre namaz aur is nuskhah ke tamaam hawaayij aur tamaam masaayib ke liye 'aam hone ki taraf Qur-aane 'azeem ne is tarah se isharah kar diya hai ke ista'eenu ko 'aam choda hai koi khaas cheez zikr nahi farmaayi ke falan kaam me in dono cheezon se madad haasil karo, is se ma'aloom huwa ke yeh do cheezein aysi hain ke in se insaan ki har zaroorat me madad haasil ki jaasakti hai.

Sabr ke asli ma'anaa apne nafs ko rokne aur us par qaabu paane ke hain ya'ani apne nafs ko haraam wa naajaaiz cheezon se rokna, nafs ko taa'aat wa ibaadaat ki paabandi par majboor karna aur masaayib wa aafaat par sabr karna ya'ani jo museebat aagayi us ko Allah Ta'aala ki taraf se samajhna aur us ke sawaab ka ummeedwaar hona.

Surah Nuh me Allah Ta'aala ka Irshaad hai: Tum apne rab se magfirat talab karo, bilaashubah wo bahut bakhashne waala hai.

Ibne Murdavaih ne Hazrat Salman (Rz) se riwaayat kiya ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaaya istigfaar ki kasrat karo. Bilaashubah Allah Ta'aala ne tum ko istigfaar ki ta'aleem is liye di ke wo tumhaari magfirat karna chaahta hai.

Hazrat Ali Murtaza (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke mujh se Abu Bakr (Rz) ne bayan farmaaya (Jo bilaashubah saadiq wa siddeeque hai) ke mai ne Rasoolullah (ﷺ) se suna, Aap (ﷺ) farmaate the: Jis shakhs se koi gunaah ho jaaye phir wo uth kar wuzoo kare, phir namaz padhe, phir Allah se magfirat aur mu'aafi talab kare to Allah Ta'aala us ko mu'aaf farma hi deta hai.” (Jame'a Tirmizi).

Muta'addi (Contagious) Beemaariyon Se Bachne Ke Liye Ehtiyaati Tadaabeer Ikhtiyaar Karna:

Huzoor (ﷺ) ne choot chaat ke zariye phailne waale amraaz ya'ani muta'addi amraaz se ehtiyaat baratne aur parhez karne ki taakeed ki hai. Is silsile me muta'addad ahaadees kutube hadees me maujood hain, jin me se chand ka tazkirah upar guzar chuka, isi tarah “Muslim Shareef” ki riwaayat me hain ke wafde saqeef me ek Majzoom shakhs the, wo bhi deegar logon ke saath Huzur-e-Akram (ﷺ) ke haath par bay'at karne aarahe the to Nabi (ﷺ) ne un ki taraf yeh paigam bheja ke “Hum ne tumhari bay'at kar li hai, lihaaza tum waapas laut jaao.”

Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne majzoom se haath milaaye bagair hi bay'at kar liya jis se ma'aloom hota hai ke ehtiyaat ke darje me beemaari ke zaahiri asbaab se bachna jaaiz hai taake agar koi ikhtilaat ke baad ba-mashiyyate khudaawandi (by the will of Allah) beemaar ho gaya to us ka e'etiqaad kharaab na ho.

Baajamaa'at Namaz Ki Ahmiyat:

Ahaadees me baajamaa'at namaz ki bahut ahmiyat wa fazeelat aayi hai. Bajamaa'at namazen padhna aur bilkhusoos Jumu'ah ki namaz padhna yeh sha'aaire islaam (symbols/rituals of Islam) me se hai.

Hazrat 'Abdullah bin 'umar (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaaya baajamaa'at namaz padhna akele namaz padhne ke muqaabale me sattaayis darjah ziyadah fazeelat rakhta hai. (Saheeh Bukhaari wa saheeh Muslim).

Hazrat Abu Dardah (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke: kisi basti me ya jangal me teen aadmi ho aur wo namaze baajamaa'at na padhte hon to un par shaitaan yaqeenan qaabu paalega, lihaaza tum jamaa'at ki paabandi ko apne uooper laazim kar lo, kyunke bhediya usi bhed ko luqmah banaata hai jo galle se alag door rehti hai. (Musnade Ahmed, Sunane Abu Dawood, Sunane An-Nasa'i).

Allah Ta'aala ke raaste me dushman ke saath jihaad ke dauran bhi jamaa-'at ki namaz saaqit nahi hoti thi, balke maidaane jang me dushman ke hamle ka khatrah ho to bhi Sharee'at ne salatul Khauf ki soorat me jamaa'at ke ehtimaam ki targeeb di hai.

Masjid Me Baajamaa'at Namaz Padhne Ki Ahmiyat:

Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz) farmaate hain ke ek naabina sahaabi Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ki khidmat me haazir huye, kahne lage, Ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ)! Mere pas koi aadmi nahi jo mujhe masjid me laaye. Yeh keh kar unhon ne namaz ghar par padhne ki rukhsat chaahi. Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne unhein rukhsat de di lekin jab wo waapas hone lage to unhon ne phir bulaaya aur poocha kya tum azaan sunte ho? Unhon ne arz kiya han Ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ)! Aap (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya: to phir masjid me aakar hi namaz padha karo. (Saheeh Muslim).

Masjid Me Baajamaa'at Namaz Na Padhne Par Wa'eed:

Ek hadees me hai ke “Huzoor (ﷺ) irshaad farmaate hain ke mera dil chaahta hai ke chand naujawaanon se kahun ke wo bahut sa eendhan ikhatta kar ke laayein phir mai un logon ke paas jaaun jo bila 'uzr ke gharon mein namaz padh lete hain aur jaakar un ke gharon ko jalaadun. (Saheehul Bukhaari).

Bajamaa'at Masjid Me Namaz Na Padhne Ka 'Uzr:

Hazrat 'Abdullah bin Abbas (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke: Jo shakhs namaze baajamaa'at ke liye Mu'azzin ki pukaar sune aur us ki ta'bedaari karne se ya'ani jamaa'at me shareek hone se koi waaqayi uzr us ke liye maane'e (rukaawat) na ho, (aur us ke baawajood wo jamaa'at me na aaye balke alag hi apni namaz padh le) to us ki wo namaz Allah ke yahan qabool nahi hogi. Baaz Sahaabah (Rz) ne arz kiya ke: Hazrat (ﷺ) waaqai 'uzr kya ho sakta hai? Aap (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya: Jaan wa maal ka khauf ya marz (Sunane Abu Dawood, Sunan Dare Qutni).

Masaajid Ko Is Tarah Mukammal Band Kar Dena Ke Jamaa'at Aur Jumu'ah Ki Namaz Bhi Na Ho…:

Kisi bhi aafat, khauf, hamlah ke waqt aur Allah Ta'aala ki ta'seer se muta'addi (contagious) hone waali beemaariyon ki bina par masaajid ko bilkulliyah band karna aur masjid me bajamaa'at namaz padhne par paabandi lagaana shar'an jaaiz nahi hai. Maidaane jang me dushman ke hamle ka khatrah ho to bhi Sharee'at ne salatul Khauf ki soorat me jamaa'at ke ehtimaam ki targeeb di hai. Aur rooye zameen par maujood jaghon me Allah Ta'aala ke yahan sab se pasandeedah jaghein masaajid hain, yeh Allah ke ghar hain, Allah ki rahmat wa barkaat ke nuzool ka mauqa'ah wa mazhar hain, masaayib wa aafaat me ummat ko chaahiye ke wo namaz aur du'aa ke zariye Allah ta'aala se madad maange aur Allah ki taraf ruju'a ho.

Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) aur Hazrat 'umar farooque (Rz) ne taa'oon (plague) ka marz phail jaane par masaajid ko band kar dene, Jumu'ah aur jamaa'at ke tarak karne ki hidaayat nahi farmaayi.

Corona Virus Ka Phailaw:

Corona Virus ka marz bunyaadi taur par muta'assir shakhs ke saath rabt zabt ke zariye phailta hai jis waqt wo khaanste hain ya cheenkte hain. Isi tarah yeh virus us waqt bhi phailta hai jab koi shakhs kisi aysi satah ya cheez ko choo leta hai jis par virus ho, phir isi haal me apne hathon se apni aankhon ya naak ya muh ko chu leta hai.

Chunke jis tarah tezi se yeh virus poori duniya me phail raha hai aur is ki abhi tak koi mu'atabar wa muassir vaccine ya 'ilaaj dastiyaab nahi huwa hai, is 'uzr ke peshenazar itni rukhsat to zaroor di jaayegi ke jis 'alaqe me corona virus ya is jaisi koi muta'addi (contagious) beemaari ya wabaa 'aam ho jaaye to us 'alaqe ke logon se taakeedan yeh kaha jaaye ke chand din aap hazraat apne gharon me hi bajaamaa'at namaz ka ehtimaam kare, phir jab beemaari zaayil ho jaaye to dobaarah masjidon me namaze baajamaa'at ka ehtimaam kare, masjid ko weeran chodna munaasib nahi is taur par ke na to azaan ho rahi hai aur na hi namaz, balke mohalle ke chand log jaise imaam wa Mu'azzin aur khaadim wagayrah masjid me haazir ho kar azaan aur namaze baajamaa'at ka ehtimaam kare, jamaa'at ke saath namaz padhna farze kifaayah hai is tarah ke shi'aar zaahir ho, aur masjid me ada karna afzal hai. Agar baajamaa'at namaz poore mohalle waalon ne tark kar di to poora mohallah gunahgaar hoga. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Nazar Saani Kardah: Hazrat Maulana Nisaar Saheb Daroge, Kuwait

'ILMI BATEIN

Khaana Khaane Waale Shakhs Ko Salaam Karna

Sawal: Kya kisi aise shakhs ko salaam karna durust hai, jo khaana kha raha ho, neez jab aise shakhs ko salaam kiya jaaye to kya ise khaana khaate huye salaam ka Jawab dena zaroori hai?

Jawab: Khaana khaane waale shakhs ko salaam karna masnoon nahi hai, aur na hi luqmah muh me hone ki soorat me Jawab dena waajib hai, albatta khaane waale shakhs ko luqmah nigalne ke baad doosra luqmah muh me rakhne se pehle salaam karna masnoon hai aur is soorat me salaam ka Jawab dena waajib hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj ma'al Hawaashi: 9/227). Mufti Ishaq Patel

Nahaar Muh Paani

Sawal: “Jis ne nahaar muh paani piya us ki taaqat kam ho jaayegi”. Kya yeh sahi hadees hai?

Jawab: Ji nahi, Zayeef riwaayat hai. (Majmauz Zawaayid:5/86). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Hijaama (Cupping) Ki Ahmiyat

Sawal: Deene Islaam me Hijaama (Cupping) ki kya ahmiyat hai?

Jawab: Bukhaari shareef me hadees hai Huzoor (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke “Agar tumhaari dawaaon me koi bhalayi hai to shahad (honey) ke peene me hai aur pachna (Hijaama) lagwaane me hai aur aag se daagne me hai lekin mai aag se daag kar 'ilaaj ko pasand nahi karta.

Hijaama ko English me Cupping kehte hain aur kokani me Faasani kehte hain. Cupping se bazaahir Hijaamah ka faaidah haasil ho jaata hai lekin daure risaalat me hijaama ki jo shakl thi yeh is tarah nahi hai. (Sharh Bulugul Maram li Atiyyah Saalim: 3/148). Mufti Mahmood Mahadik

Bilaawajah Aundha (Pet Ke Bal) Letna

Sawal: Mai ne kisi kitaab me padha tha ke ladkon ko aundha (pet ke bal) na sulaaye aur ladkiyon ko chit (peeth ke bal) na sulaye is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Bilaawajah aundha letna naapasandeedah hai, ahaadees me aysi haalat me letne se mana'a kiya gaya hai. Is me ladke ladki me koi farq nahi hai, sab ke liye yaksan hukm hai. Tirmizi ki hadees me hai ke letne ki is haalat ko Allah naapasand karte hain. (Al-Majmoo': 1/186). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Naakhun Kaatne Ke Aadab

Sawal: Kya Shafi'ee aur Hanafi me naakhun kaatne ke aadab alag hain?

Jawab: hadees me naakhun kaatne ki koi tarteeb mazkoor aur saabit nahi jis tarah chaahe kaat sakte hain naakhun kaatna masnoon wa matloob hai. (Al-Majmoo': 1/186). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

'Aashuraa Ke Din Apne Ahl-O-'Ayaal Par Rizq Me Wus'at (Capacity)

Sawal: “Jo shakhs 'Aashuraa ke din apne ahl-o-'ayaal (Gharwaalon) par rizq me wus'at (capacity) (Kushaadgi) karega Allah Ta'aala poore saal is ke rizq me barkat farmaayeinge.” Is hadees ke mutabiq sirf ahl-o-'ayaal ke khaane par kharch karna murad hai ya doosri cheezein bhi shaamil hain jaise kapda, zaib wa zeenat ke liye sona zaiwarat wagayrah bhi?

Jawab: Bazaahir khaana peena hi muraad hai kyunke rozaana ka kharch khaana aur peena hi hai. Kapda to mausam ke e'etibaar se, makaan ek baar diya jaata hai aur zaiwaraat ki zimmedaari nahi hai. (Mir'aatul Mafateeh: 6/362). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Allah Ki Qasam Kha Kar Nazar Maanna

Sawal: Agar koi Allah ki qasam kha kar nazar maane aur yeh kahe ke mai fulaan kaam karunga aur wo kaam poora nahi kiya to us ko kya fidyah dena hoga?

Jawab: Agar wus'at (capacity) ho to das miskeenon ko khaana khilaaye ya unhein kapde pehnaaye ya momin gulaam aazad kare agar yeh mayassar na ho to teen din roze rakhega. (As-Sirajul-Wahhaj: 552). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dawa Khate Waqt “Huwash Shaafi” Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Kya dawa khaane ki makhsoos du'aa hai? Dawa khaate waqt, “Huwash-Shaafi” kehna logon me mash'hoor hai, is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Nabi (ﷺ) se dawa khaane ki koi makhsoos du'aa saabit nahi hai. Albatta har khaane peene ke shuru' me Bismillah kehna masnoon hai is me saari ashyaa dakhil hain.

Hazrat 'Aayesha (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: Jab tum me se koi khaana khaaye to pehle Bismillah kahe. Agar khaane ke shuru' me Bismillah kehna bhool jaaye to “Bismillahi awwalahu wa aakhirahu (بسمِ اللهِ أوَّلَهُ وآخِرَهُ)” (Is khaane ka aagaz aur ikhtitaam Allah ke naam ke saath karta hu) kahe.

Chunke momin hone ki wajah se musalmaanon ka bunyaadi 'Aqeedah yahi hota hai ke dar haqeeqat dawaayi me koi shifa nahi hoti, shifa to min jaanibillah hoti hai, lihaaza agar koi musalmaan dawa khaate waqt khalisatan apne is aqeede ka izhaar karte huye “Huwash.Shaafee” kehta hai to koi muzaayiqah nahi. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bijli Chamakne Ya Kadakne Par Darood Shareef Padhne Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Bijli chamakne ya kadakne par darood shareef padhne ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Allah jab naaraaz hote hain to bijli ki kadak ke zariye daraate hain, Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) se bijli chamakne ya kadakne par darood shareef padhna saabit nahi lekin is waqt Allah ki tasbeeh bayaan karna Qur-aan wa hadees se saabit hai.

Hazrat 'Abdullah bin Zubair (Rz) se marvi hai ke “wo jis waqt kadak sunte to guftagu chod dete aur kehte:

"سُبْحَانَ الَّذِي يُسَبِّحُ الرَّعْدُ بِحَمْدِهِ وَالْمَلَائِكَةُ مِنْ خِيفَتِهِ"

“Mera Rub paak hai ke kadak Hamd ke saath is ki tasbeeh karti hai aur farishtey us ke khauf se tasbeeh bayaan karte hain,” (Khulaasatul Ahkaam lin Nawawi: 2/888) phir 'Abdullah bin zubair kehte the yeh ahle zamin ke liye sakht wa'eed hai.”

Rasoolullah (ﷺ) jab kadak ki aawaz sunte to yeh du'aa padhte the.

" أللّٰهُمَّ لاَ تَقْتُلْنَا بِغَضَبِك، وَلاَ تُهْلِكْنَا بِعَذابِك، وَعَافِنَا قَبْلَ ذٰلِك "

(Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bismillahir Rahmaanir Raheem (بِسْمِ اللّٰهِ الرَّحْمٰنِ الرَّحِيْمْ) Ki Jagah 786 Likhna

Sawal: Kya Bismillahir Rahmaanir Raheem ki jagah 786 likkha ja sakta hai? Kya 786 Bismillah ka ni'amul Badal ho sakta hai?

Jawab: 786 Bismillahir Rahmanir Raheem ka 'adad hai is 'adad se Bismillahir Rahmanir Raheem ki taraf isharah kiya jaata hai, yeh Bismillahir Rahmanir Raheem ka badal nahi hai aur na hi is me tasmiyah likhne ka sawaab hai, is liye asal to yahi hai ke khat ya kisi aur cheez ke likhne ki ibtida Bismillahir Rahmanir Raheem se ki jaaye. Bismillah shareef ke fazaayil aur barakaat mukhtalif riwaayaat se wazeh hain is liye 786 likhne se bandah in tamaam barakaat se mahroom rehta hai. Musalmaamon ko chaahiye ke har maqaam par jahan tasmiyah likhna chaahiye abjad ke aa'daad isti'emaal nahi karne chaahiye. Maazi me tamaam mufassireen, muhaddiseen, fuqahaa aur 'Ulama mukammal tasmiyah likhte the aur is par tamaam ahle islaam ka 'amal jaari hai. (Tuhfatul-Bari: 1/85). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Huzoor (ﷺ) Aur Allah Ko Khwab Me Dekhna

Sawal: Huzoor (ﷺ) ka khwab me nazar aana sahi aur sach hota hai? Lekin kya Allah ko bhi khwab me dekha ja sakta hai is ke muta'alliq Qur-aan ya kisi hadees me tazkirah ho to baraaye meharbaani rehbari farmaayein?

Jawab: Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ko khwab mein agar kisi ne dekha to us ne Aap (ﷺ) ko hi dekha, shaitan Aap (ﷺ) ki soorat me nahi aa sakta, yeh baat sahi hadees me aayi hui hai. Magar is tarah ki koi baat Allah Ta'aala ko khwab me dekhne se muta'alliq kisi hadees me nazar se nahi guzri albatta Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne ek hadees me Allah Ta'aala ko dekhne ki baat irshaad farmaayi hai, 'Ulama ne is ko Khwab par Mahmool kiya hai. Is se pata chalta hai ke Allah ta'aala ko khwab me dekhna mumkin hai chunaanche imaam Nawawi (rh) ne naqal kiya hai ke tamaam 'Ulama is par muttafiq hain ke Allah Ta'aala ko khwab me dekhna durust aur sahi hai. (Sharhu Muslim:15/25). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Gair Muqallid aysi cheezon par e'atiraaz karte hain?

Jawab: Gair Muqallid ko na maanna ho to na maane! Is mas-alah par eemaan ka daromadaar to hai nahi! Waise Allah Ta'aala ko khwab me dekhne ki baat imaam Ahmad jaise Imaamul Muhaddiseen ke bare me aayi hui hai balke gair muqallidon ke mu'atabar 'Aalim Shaikh Ibne baaz bhi is ko mumkin likh chuke hain aur un se pehle Shaikhul Islaam imaam Ibne Taymiyah bhi yahi baat likh chuke hain. (Mulaahazah ho: Majmoo' Fatawa Ibne Baaz: 6/367-369). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Hadees-E Marfu'a Aur Hadees Mauqoof

Sawal: Aksar ahaadeese mubarakah ki nisbat Huzoor (ﷺ) ki taraf hoti hai, lekin kuch ahaadees aysi bhi hain jin ki nisbat Hazrat Ali (Rz) ki taraf hoti hai aysi ahaadees ko kya kaheinge?

Jawab: Jis hadees ki nisbat Nabi (ﷺ) ki taraf ho us ko hadees-e Marfu'a kehte hain aur jis hadees ki sanad Sahaabi par khatam hoti ho us hadees ko Hadees-e Mauqoof kehte hain. (Manhajun Naqd fi Uloomul Hadees: 325-326). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Aqwaale Zarreen

Sawal: Kya Aqwaale Zarreen deen ki baat hai? Ya'ani use deen ki baat samajh kar bataana sahi hai?

Jawab: Aqwaale Zarreen ke ma'anaa hote hain qeemati ya'ani achchi baatein aur Sharee'at ne achhchi baaton ka hukm diya hai aur buri baaton se roka hai aur Sharee'at ne koi ta'ayeen nahi farmaayi lekin hum us ko Allah aur Rasool (ﷺ) ki taraf mansoob nahi kar sakte (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband: 51061). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Deen Me Guloo Mat Karo

Sawal: Qur-aan ke irshaad “Deen me Guloo mat karo” se kya muraad hai? Kya yeh jo hum choti choti batein puchte hain wo guloo me daakhil hain?

Jawab: Jo baatein ma'aloom na hon use janne walon se pooch kar ma'aloom karna zaroori hai is liye choti choti baat pooch pooch kar 'amal karna chaahiye isi se hamaari islah hogi, isko guloo kehna sahi nahi hai chunke Sharee'at par 'amal zaroori hai to 'amal se muta'alliq masa-il ko ma'aloom karna bhi zaroori hai, choti choti baat 'Ulama se poochna ain Sharee'at ke hukm par 'amal hai. (Nihayatuz-Zain: 7/1). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Islaam Ki Nazar Me Majboori

Sawal: Islaam ki nazar me majboori kise kehte hai aur kis waqt soodi len den ya haraam cheezon ko qabool karne ki ijaazat di hai? Kyunke aksar log apni nafsaani khwahishaat ko poora karne ke liye majboori kehte hain?

Jawab: Zaroorat aur majboori ki ta'areef: Hayaate Insaani ki paanch Maslihatein. (1) Tahaffuze nafs (2) Tahaffuze Deen (3) Tahaffuze Maal (4) Tahaffuze Nasl (5) Tahaffuze Aql me se kisi par bhi shadeed zad pade to use zaroorat aur majboori kaha jaata hai, majboori aur zaroorat ke mu'atabar hone ke liye shart yeh hai ke: (1) Kisi baat ke ijaazat dene ki wajah se isi darjah ya is se bade mafsade ki naubat na aati ho. (2) zaroorat ke zariye agar kisi ke shakhsi mafsadah ko door kiya jaaye to yeh bhi zaroori hai ke yeh doosre shakhs ke liye isi darjah ya is se ziyadah mafaasid (harmful consequences) ka zari'aah na ban jaaye. (3) zarooratan us shakhs ko ijaazat di ja sakti hai jis ka koi mubaah mutabaadil maujood na ho. Masalan fuqahaa ne az raahe 'ilaaj sharaab ke isti'emaal ki ijaazat di hai lekin is waqt jab ke is maqsad ke liye doosri dawa maujood na ho. (4) jo Moharramaat wa mamnu'aat zaroorat ke waqt jaaiz qaraar di jaati hain un ka mahaz zaroorat ke waqt hi isti'emaal kiya jaaye. Is ko fuqahaa ne is tarah ta'abeer kiya hai: jo cheezein az raahe zaroorat jaaiz qaraar di gai hon wo baqadre zaroorat hi jaaiz rehti hain. (5) Zaroorat ke naam par koi Nas bilkul hi be asar ho jaaye yeh durust nahi, haan yeh zaroor hai ke zaroorat ki bina par nas ke 'umoom me takhsees ki ja sakti hai. (6) zaroorat ke mu'atabar hone ke liye zaroori hai ke wo filhaal maujood ho mahaz imkaan ke darjah me na ho (Qaamoosul Fiqh: 4). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hazrat Fatima (Rz) Ko Fatimatuz Zahra Ka Laqab

Sawal: Hazrat Fatima (Rz) ko Fatimatuz Zahra kyun kaha jaata hai aur yeh laqab kis ne diya?

Jawab: Hazrat Fatima (Rz) ko Haiz ka khoon nahi aata tha is e'etibaar se wo zahiran bhi paak thien aur baatin se to paak thien hi isi wajah se unhein Fatimatuz zahra ka laqab mila tha. (At-Tafseerul Muneer: 3/224). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Imaam Abu Haneefah (Rh) Ka Janazah Jail Se Utha

Sawal: Kal ek shakhs ne mujhe kaha ke use kisi ne kaha ke imaam Abu Haneefah (rh) ka Janazah jail se utha, use us aadmi ne kitaab ka bhi hawaalah diya tha. Kya yeh sach hai? 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke is ka khulaasa karen.

Jawab: Jab Abbaasi Khaleefa Abu Ja'far Mansoor ne imaam Abu Haneefah (rh) ko “Qaziul Quzaat” ka 'uhdah (post / position) qabool karne ke liye kaha tha to imaam saahib ne be niyaazi ka izhaar kiya aur use thukra diya to usi gusseh me khaleefah mansoor ne Aap (Rh) ko qaid me daala phir zahar de kar maar diya, lihaaza qaid khaana ki saoobaton hi me aap ka intiqaal huwa tha. (Maahnaamah Darul-'Uloom Shumaarah Baarah Vol-96, Safarul Muzaffar 1434/December 2012). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hazrat Abu Bakr (Rz) Ka Bête Ko Talaaq Dene Ka Hukm

Sawal: Kya Hazrat Abu Bakr (Rz) ne apne bête ko jab Allah ke raste me nikalne ke liye kaha to unhon ne kaha ke biwi se muhabbat beech me aati hai to abu Bakr (Rz) ne kaha ke biwi ko talaaq do lekin Allah ke deen ko phailana zaroori hai, yeh suna huwa Khulasa hai. Is ki tahqeeq kya hai?

Jawab: Yeh Qissah is tarah nahi hai balke Allama Ibne Abdul Barr ke bayaan ke mutaabiq Hazrat Abu Bakr (Rz) ke bête ki biwi bahut khoobsoorat thi aur unhein us se bahut mohabbat thi to Hazrat Abu Bakr ne farmaaya ke tum is ko talaaq de do kyunke tum is ki wajah se gazwah (jihaad) me nahi jaate ho. Unhon ne talaaq nahi di balke jihaad me chale gaye. Phir Hazrat Abu Bakr (Rz) ne bhi dobaarah talaaq dene ko nahi kaha. Aur us jihaad me wo saahibzaade shaheed ho gaye. (Al-Tamheed Ibne Abdul Barr:33/405). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Iblees Bahut Bada 'Aalim Ya Aabid

Sawal: Aisa kyun kehte hain ke Iblees bahut bada 'aalim tha?

Jawab: Is ka 'aalim hona mash'hoor nahi balke Aabid hona mash'hoor hai agar cheezon ki ma'aloomaat ya jaankaari muraad ho to wo ja'al saazi aur makkaari wa fitnah parwari me maahir hai (Aalamul Jin wash Shayaateen: 1/48). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Lauhe Qur-Aani Padhna, Yaad Karna

Sawal: Kya Lauhe Qur-aani (Qur-aan ke wo alfaz jin ke ma'anaa pata nahi jaise Alif_Laam_Meem, Qaaf, Ha_Meem, Ain_Seen_Qaaf) padhna ya yaad karna bid'at hai?

Jawab: Chunke wo Qur-aan hi hai is liye is ko padhna aur yaad karna ajr aur sawaab hi hai is me koi haraj nahi. Agar is se koi aur nazariyah muta'alliq ho to aur baat hogi. (Al-Kifayah fi at-Tafseer bil-Masur wa ad-Dirayah: 2/28). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Rajab Maheene Ki Du'aa Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Rajab maheenah ki du'aa ki kya haqeeqat hai?

(اَللّٰهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَنَا فِى رَجَبَ وَ شَعْبَانَ وَ بَلِّغْنَا رَمَضَان) - (Allahumma baarik lana fi rajab wa sha'abaan wa balligna Ramazaan).

Jawab: Zayeef to hai, magar fazaayil se muta'alliq hai is liye haraj nahi. (Al-Jamiu's-Sageer wa Ziyadatuhu: 1/9875). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Saheeh Muslim Me Ek Bhi Hadees Zayeef Nahi

Sawal: Kya saheeh muslim me ek bhi hadees zayeef nahi hai? Agar hai to kon si?

Jawab: Saheeh muslim me koi zayeef hadees nahi hai (Muqaddimatu Ibne-as-salah: 24). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Qur-Aan Me Makkah Madinah Se Ziyadah Misr Ka Tazkirah

Sawal: Ek misri dost keh raha tha ke Allah ne Qur-aan me makkah Madinah se ziyadah Misr ka tazkirah kiya hai. Is ne yeh bhi bataaya ke misr ka tazkirah alag alag andaaz me bhi hai. 'Ulama rehbari farmaayein.

Jawab: Qur-aan me sab se ziyadah qisse Hazrat Musa (A) ke hain jo fir'aun ke baare mein hai chunke is ka ta'alluq misr se tha is wajah se misr ka tazkirah ziyadah hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Social Media Par Hazrat Ali (Rz) Ke Naam Se Beshumaar Galat Aqwaal

Sawal: Kya kutube ahaadees me hazrat Ali (Rz) se sab se ziyadah riwaayatein manqool hain kyunke hazrat Ali (Rz) ke naam se bahut aqwaal social media par dekhne ko milte hain jo deegar Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) ki taraf mansoob nahi hote baraaye karam wazaahat keejiye.

Jawab: Kutube ahaadees me kasrat se riwaayaat karne waale Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) me se chand ek naam sare fehrist hain jaise ke Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Rz), Hazrat 'Abdullah Bin 'umar (Rz), Hazrat Anas (Rz), Hazrat Siddeeqah 'Aayesha (Rz), Hazrat 'Abdullah Bin Mas'uood (Rz), Hazrat 'Abdullah bin Abbas (Rz) aur Hazrat Jaabir Bin 'Abdullah (Rz) wagayrah aham hain. Shi'aa Hazraat ne hazrat Ali (rz) ki aqeedat me guloo kiya aur be shumaar galat aqwaal in ki taraf mansoob kiye jin ki haqeeqat kizb bayaani hai isi wajah se social media par hazrat Ali Karramallahu waj'hahu ki taraf kasrat se aqwaal mansoob milte hain is liye Hazrat Ali (Rz) ki taraf mansoob har qaul aankh band kar ke qabool karna nahi chaahiye balke tahqeeq karna chaahiye agar mu'atabar sanad se is ka suboot ho to lena chaahiye warna nahi (Darul Iftah, Darul-'Uloom Deoband). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Qur-Aan Me Pardeh Ka Hukm

Sawal: Mera ek dost keh raha tha ke Qur-aan me likkha hai ke sirf baalon ko chupaaya karo, chehre ka koi pardah nahi hai isliye burqah me niqaab koi zaroori nahi hai, main ne use kaha ke yeh konsa naya Qur-aan laya hai, is ke ma'anaa aur tashreeh ko samajhna sab ke bas ki baat nahi. Meharbaani kar ke rehbari farmaayein.

Jawab: Qur-aan me sirf baalon ka hi chupaana mazkoor nahi hai balke poore jism ko chupaane ka hukm diya gaya hai chunaanche Allah Ta'aala ka irshaade Mubaarak hai:

'' يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُّ قُل لِّأَزْوَاجِكَ وَبَنَاتِكَ وَنِسَاءِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ يُدْنِينَ عَلَيْهِنَّ مِن جَلَابِيبِهِنَّ ''

"Ae Nabi! Apni biwiyon aur betiyon aur momin 'auraton se keh do ke woh apne upar apni chadrein latka liya karein."

Aur doosri jagah Surah Ahzaab me irshaad farmaaya:

'' وَإِذَا سَأَلْتُمُوهُنَّ مَتَاعًا فَاسْأَلُوهُنَّ مِن وَرَاءِ حِجَابٍ ''

"Aur jab tum Nabi ki biwiyon se koi cheez maango to parde ke peechhe se maango."

Aur kaee (many) riwaayatein hain tashreeh ke liye Ma'aariful Qur-aan surah Noor aur Al-ahzaab ki tafseer mulaahazah ho. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Kya 10 Muharram Ko Qayaamat Qaayim Hogi?

Sawal: Hamaare yahan logon me mash'hoor hai ke qayaamat Jumu'ah ke din 10 Muharram ko qaayim hogi, is ki shar'an kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Jumu'ah ke din qayaamat qaayim hogi yeh to saheeh hadees me maujood hai, lekin 10 Muharram ko qaayim hogi yeh baat kisi sahi hadees me maujood nahi hai. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Watan Se Muhabbat Karna Eemaan Ka Hissa: Hadees Ka Khulaasa

Sawal: Aap (ﷺ) ki taraf nisbat karte huye ek hadees mash'hoor hai: “Hubbul Watani Minal Eemaan” kya yeh hadees saheeh hai?

Jawab: Is hadees ko muhaddiseen wa shurraahe hadees ne mauzu'a qaraar diya hai agarcha is ke ma'anaa saheeh hai. Lekin Aap (ﷺ) se is tarah koi riwaayat manqool nahi hai lihaaza aap (ﷺ) ki taraf nisbat karte huye is hadees ko bayaan karna qat'an durust nahi, behtar aur durust baat yahi hai ke is ko bataur muhaavarah isti'emaal kiya jaaye aur hadees ki taraf nisbat karne se bacha jaaye. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Ta'veez Ke Jawaaz Par Dalaayil Tahaffuze Ahle Sunnat Wal Jamaa'at

Sawal: Kya Sharee'ate mutahharah me ta'veez ka jawaaz milta hai?

Jawab: (1) Hazrat 'Abdullah Bin Amar (Rz) ka ta'veez likh kar dena. (Abu Dawood: 2/543).

(2) Hazrat 'Abdullah Bin Abbas (Rz) Bachche ki paidaayish ke liye 2 aayate qur'aani likh kar dete the ke un ko dho kar mareezah (beemaar 'aurat) ko pilaado. (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/24).

(3) Hazrat Ayesha (Rz) is ko mana'a nahi karti thi ke paani me ta'veez daal kar wo paani mareez par chidka jaaye. (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/24).

(4) Hazrat Mujaahid (Rh) is me koi haraj na jaante the ke qur'aani aayaat likh kar darne waale mareez ko pila di jaaye (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/24).

(5) Hazrat Sa'eed Bin Zubair (Rz) jo bhi aata us ko ta'veez likh dete the. (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/24).

(6) Hajjaaj Ibne Aswad (Rh) kehte hain ke mai ne makkah mukarramah ke mufti Hazrat 'Ataa (Rh) se is bare me pucha to farmaaya ke hum ne to nahi suna ke koi is ko makrooh kehta ho.

(7) Abu Asma (rh) kehte hain ke maine Hazrat Sa'eed bin Musayyib (Rh) se ta'veez ke baare me poocha to farmaaya: koi haraj nahi jab chamde me ho. (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/31).

(8) Hazrat 'Ataa Bin Abi Rabaah (rh) se poocha gaya ke 'aurat ke gale me ta'veez hai, jab haiz aaye to kya kare? Farmaaya: agar chamde par likkha ho to use utaar de aur agar chaandi me band ho to chaahe utaare chaahe na utaare. (Musannaf Ibn abi shaibah: 8/31).

(9) Madinah Munawwarah ke jalilul qadar taba'ee Hazrat Mujahid (Rh) tavizh likh kar logon par latkate (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/32).

(10) Imaam bakr (rh) is me koi haraj nahi samajhte the ke chamde par qur'aan likh kar latka liya jaaye. (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/32).

(11) Hazrat Zahhaq (rh), Allah ki kitaab se likh kar ta'veez banaane me koi haraj nahi samajhte the jabke raf'yeh haajat aur gusl ke waqt utaar le. (Musannaf Ibne Abi Shaibah: 8/33).

(12) Imaam Muhammad (Rh) farmaate hain ke hum is mas-alah ko maante hai jab ke wo dum ya ta'veez Allah ke zikr ya Allah ki kitaab se ho aur yahi imaam Abu Haneefah (Rh) ka farman hai. (Kitaabul Aasaar: 377).

Note: Ruqiya aur ta'veez teen sharton ke saath jaaiz hai: (1) yeh ke wo aayaate Qur-aaniya ya asmaaul Husna wagayrah par mushtamil ho. (2) 'Arabi ya is ke 'alaawah aysi zubaan mein ho jis ki muraad samajh me aa sake. (3) yeh aqeedah ho ke yeh ta'veez muassir biz zaat nahi hai. (is ki haisiyat sirf asbaab ki hai) muassir biz zaat sirf Allah rabbul Izzat hai. Jawaaz ke qaaileen (manne walon) me hazraat Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) me se hazrat 'Abdullah Bin Amr Bin Aas (Rz) hain aur taabayeen me se Hazrat Mujaahid (rh) aur Hazrat 'Ataa (rh) wagayrah hain. Aur aaj bhi is par logon ka 'amal hai. Wazeh rahe ke ta'veez me jis tarah padh kar dum karna jaaiz hai isi tarah Qur-aani aayaat wagayrah ko kisi kaagaz par likh kar is ko bandhna bhi jaaiz hai. Jahan tak in ahaadees ka ta'alluq hai jin me tameemah ko latkaane par shirk ka hukm mazkoor hai to yeh is soorat par mahmool hai jab ke is ta'veez ko naafe'e aur Zaarr samjha jaaye jaisa ke log zamaanae jaahiliyyat me e'etiqaad rakhte the ya mumaana'at waali in ahaadees se muraad aise ta'aaveez hain jin me alfaaze shirkiyah ya aise alfaaz hon jin ke ma'aani ma'aloom na hon. (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband).

SOCIAL MEDIA KE POSTON KI HAQEEQAT

Pandrah Ramazaan Ko Jumu'ah Waali Hadees Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Pandrah Ramazaan ko Jumu'ah waqe'e ho to ek hadees bahut phailaayi jaa rahi hai ke is din ek khatarnaak aasmaani aafat aayegi, is hadees ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: yeh hadees man ghadhat hai aur aisa pehli baar nahi ho raha hai ke Jumu'ah ko 15 Ramazaan ho, aise kayi Ramazaan guzar chuke hain, is saal whatsApp ki wajah se is ko khoob badhaawa mil raha hai. Saudi 'Arab ke saabiq Mufti-e-A'azam 'Allama Abdullah Ibne Baaz (Rh) ne bhi is hadees ko man ghadhat kaha hai. Shaikh Ibne Baaz (Rh) farmaate hain: “Yeh be bunyaad riwaayat hai, jhoot aur baatil hai, musalmaanon par kayi aise saal aa chuke hain jin me pandrah ramazaan Jumu'ah ke din waqe'e huwa, magar is ke baawajood Alhamdulillah cheekh wagayrah umoor kuch bhi pesh nahi aaye jo is jhoot shakhs ne bayaan kiye hain. Isi se har waaqif shakhs samajh sakta hai ke is galat hadees ko phailaana jaaiz nahi hai. Balke is ko phaadh dena, zaaye'a kar dena aur is ke baatil hone par mutanabbah karna waajib hai aur yeh baat to har ek janta hai ke Allah se tamaam ahwaal me darna zaroori hai. Aur yeh bhi laazim hai ke aadmi taa dame hayaat Allah ki mana'a kardah cheezon se parhez karta rahe.” (Majmoo' Fatawa Ibne Baaz:26/339-341). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

27 Rajab Ke Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Agar meri taraf se koi galati ho ya meri taraf se aap ko dukh pahuncha. Ya mai ne jaane anjane me aap ki geebat, chugli, bugz rakhkha ho to baraaye meharbaani mujhe Allah ki raza ki khaatir mu'aaf kar dijiye. Kyunke 27 Rajab aa gai hai aur is din naamae aamal badal diye jaayenge. Zubaan se zaroor ek baar keh do ke mu'aaf kar diya.” Kya is tarah ke paigaamat whatsApp par forward kar sakte hain? Kya yeh saheeh hai?

Jawab: Ji nahi, is ki koi haqeeqat nahi. 27 Rajab ki kuch fazeelat nahi hai Is liye is se munaasabat rakhna saheeh nahi hai. Lekin takleef pahonchaane par mu'aafi mangne se huqooqul 'ibaad se baraa'at hogi aur hashr ki girift se hifaazat bhi hogi. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla aur Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Kya Shifa Dozakh Ki Ghati Hai?

Sawal: “Shifa'a aur Shafa” me farq. “Shifa'a” ka matlab hai sihhat, jab ke “Shafa” naam hai dozakh ki ek ghaati ka jis se dozakh khud bhi din me 70 baar panaah maangti hai. Aksar log kehte hain “Allah Aap ko Shafa de” yeh du'aa nahi balke bad du'aa hai. Hamein kehna chahiye ke Allah Aap ko Shifa'a de. Kisi ne yeh post bheja hai. Kya shafa dozakh ki ghaati hai?

Jawab: Jahannam ki koi waadi shafa naam ki hai ab tak kahin padha nahi. Mera apna khayaal yeh hai ke message banaane waale ko galat fehmi hui hai. Aayate Shareefah “Shafa Hufratim Minan Naar” se jis ka tarjama hai: Jahannam ke gadhe ke kinaare. Shafa ka tarjuma hai “Kinaarah” to is se samajh liya ke shafa jahannam ka hissa hai. Agar yahi baat hai to yeh galat samajh hai. Wallahu A'alam. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Huzoor (ﷺ) Azaan Dete To – Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Is post ki kya haqeeqat hai?

“Allah ko Azaan bahut pasand thi magar Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne kabhi azaan nahi di thi kyunke agar Aap (ﷺ) azaan dete to kaayinaat ki har cheez masjid me aa jaati aur nizaame kaayinaat ruk jaata.”

Jawab: Is ki koi haqeeqat nahi hai, balke Huzoor (ﷺ) ke ek dafah azaan dene ka tazakirah hadees ki kitaabon me aaya hai. (Sunan At-Tirmizi: 411). Yeh alag baat hai ke shaariheene hadees ka is me ikhtilaaf hai ke wahan par azaan dena muraad hai ya azaan ka hukm dena? 'Ulama aur muhaddiseen ki ek jamaa'at kehti hai ke Aap (ﷺ) ne is ek martabah azaan di hai. Lihaaza yeh jo baat likhi hai ke Huzoor (ﷺ) azaan dete to aisa ho jaata yeh bilkul be asal baat hai. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Du'aa Ki Qabooliyat Waale Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Hamare kisi Bhai ne ek message bheja hai jis me ek du'aa hai. Yeh du'aa kuch alag lagi mujhe. “Ya Allah! Mere wo gunah mu'aaf farma jo meri du'aaon ko qabool nahi hone dete.” 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke hamein bataayein ki kya aysi du'aa mangna saheeh hai?

Jawab: Waise to gunahgaar hona hi du'aa ke qabool na hone ka sabab banta hai lekin baaz gunaah khusoosan du'aa ke radd hone ka sabab bante hain jaise qata'a rahmi. Isliye har gunah se mu'aafi mangna chaahiye. (At-Tahbeer li eezaahi Ma'aaniyit Taiseer: 4/43). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Ladki Ki Zindagi Me Du'aa Ki Qabooliyat Ke Chaar Auqaat Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Aaj kal khusoosan 'auraton me ek message bahut phail raha hai, is ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Ladki ki zindagi me chaar dafa'a aisa waqt aata hai jab wo jo du'aa maange qabool hoti hai.

Jawab: Bilkul galat aur be asal baat hai, is ka koi suboot nahi hai. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Riyadh (Najd) Fitnon Ki Jagah – Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: 'Ulama is post ki wazaahat kare.

Riyadh (Najd) Fitnon ki jagah hai. Hazrat 'Abdullah Ibne 'umar (Rz) se Hazrat Imaam Bukhari ne yeh hadees naqal ki hai ke ek din Huzoor (ﷺ) ne shaam aur yaman ke liye du'aa farmaayi jis ke alfaaz yeh hain: “Khuda wand! Hamaare shaam aur yaman me barkat naazil farma (du'aa karte waqt Najd (jis ko aaj Riyadh kehte hain) ke kuch log baithe huye the) unhon ne arz kiya aur hamare Najd (Riyadh) me ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ! is par Huzoor (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya: Khuda wand! Hamaare Shaam aur yaman par barkat naazil farma. Phir dobaarah Najd ke logon ne arz kiya aur hamare Najd me Ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ) raavi ka bayaan hai ke teesri martabah me Huzoor (ﷺ) ne farmaaya wo zalzalon aur fitnon ki jagah hai aur wahan se shaitaan ki seeng niklengi. (Bukhaari shareef, jild – 2, Safha No. 1051) (Muslim Shareef Baab – Kitaabul Fitan jild – 1 Safha No. 394).

Jawab: Yeh hadees Bukhaari Abwaab Istisqaa'a me Maujood hai. Lekin naql karne waale ne kayi Tahreefaat (Changes) ki hain.

Sone Se Pehle Hajj Karo – Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: Sone se pehle Hajj karo, padhne ke liye sirf do minutes lagenge.

Ek din Hazrat Muhammad (ﷺ) ne Hazrat Ali (Rz) se farmaaya ke “Ali sone se pehle panch cheezein karo.

Hazrat Ali (Rz) ne farmaaya: Ya Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ! yeh to namumkin hai. Yeh main kis tarah kar sakta hun? Tab Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaaya:

Jitna ho sake ise forward keejiye taake jitney log bhi is 'amal ko shuru' kareinge aap ko is ka ajr ata hoga. In Sha Allah. Aameen.

Kya upar ki post saheeh hai?

Jawab: Is riwaayat ka hadees ki kitaabon me koi wajood nahi hai. Muhaddise 'Asr Shaikhul hadees Hazrat Maulana Yunus Sahab Jaonpuri (Rh) ne bhi Tasreeh farmaayi hai ke yeh hadees kahein nahi mili. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

10 Muharram Ke Post Ki Haqeeqat

Sawal: 10 Muharram ke din insaan ki har du'aa, taubah qabool hogi kyunke is din Hazrat Aadam (As) ki taubah qabool hui thi.

Hazrat Musa (As) ki du'aa qabool hui thi, Hazrat Isa (As) upar utha liye gaye the, Hazrat Yunus (As) machli ke pait se nikaale gaye the, hazrat Muhammad Mustafa (ﷺ) ne faaraan ki choti par nubuwwat ka e'elaan isi taareekh ko kiya. 10 Muharram ko Nuh (As) ki kashti tufaan se paar aayi thi. Isi taareekh ko Ibrahim (As) aatishe Namrood se baahar nikle the, isi taareekh ko Ayyub (As) ko keedon se najaat mili, isi taareekh ko islaam ko bachaane ke liye mere Nabi (ﷺ) ke nawaase Hazrat Imaame Husain (Rz) ne karbala ki zameen par apne aal ki aur apni Qurbaani di, aur isi taareekh ko qayaamat bhi aayegi is din Allah se apne gunaahon ki mu'aafi mangein aur jo du'aa mange mujhe bhi please shaamil kar lein. Allah Ta'aala qabool farmaayeinge.

Kya upar ki post saheeh hai?

Jawab: Upar ki post me Aashora'a ke bare me jo taareekhi baatein kahi gai hain. Un me se aksar galat hain jo man ghadat riwaayat me aayi hui hain. Sirf do teen baatein waarid hain:

Aur Nawaasa-e-Rasool Hazrat Husain (Rz) ki shahaadat is din pesh aane ka is ki fazeelat se koi ta'alluq nahi hai. 'Aashuraa ke din du'aa qabool hone se muta'alliq koi baat mai ne kahein nahi padhi. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Kya Qayaamat Se Pehle Boodha Bachchah Paida Hoga?

Sawal: Ek post ka khulasa karna hai, kya soorah Muzammil me qayaamat se pehle boodha bachchah paida hoga is ka zikr hai?

Jawab: Bilkul Nahi. Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

MUTAFARRIQ MASA-IL

'Ibaadat Ke Liye Kisi Doosre Ko Tarjee'h Dena

Sawal: Kya 'ibaadat ke liye kisi doosre ko apne aap par tarjeeh de sakte hain?

Jawab: 'ibaadat ke liye kisi doosre ko apne aap par tarjeeh dene ki do qismein hain:

Du'aa Karte Waqt Haath Uthaana

Sawal: Kya du'aa karte waqt haath uthaana zaroori hai?

Jawab: Du'aa me haath uthaana waajib aur zaroori nahi balke masnoon hai yeh aajizi aur inkisaari ki nishaani hai aur Aap (ﷺ) aur Hazraate Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) se haath uthaana saabit hai. (Al-Majmoo':3/469). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Daanon Ki Tasbeeh Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Kya dhaage daanon ki tasbeeh ka isti'emaal jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Behtar aur sunnat yahi hai ke tasbeehaat wa takbeerat wagayrah apne haathon hi par shumaar kare lekin agar niyyat achchi ho aur riyaakaari jaisi galat niyyat na ho to daanon ki tasbeeh ka isti'emaal bila karaahat jaaiz hai (Al-Minhatu fis Subhati-Lis Suyuti:30). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Masjid Ki Ta'meer Ke Liye Gair Muslim Ka Halaal Maal

Sawal: Kya Masjid ki ta'meer ke liye gair muslim ka halaal maal isti'emaal kiya ja sakta hai?

Jawab: Ta'meere Masjid ke liye gair muslimon ka halaal maal isti'emaal karne me koi haraj nahi hai jabke is se kisi mafsade ka ehtimaal na ho. (Majmoo'ul Fatawa Ibne Taymiyah: 17/499). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Masjid Ke Liye Waqf Kardah Aur Rakhi Hui Cheezon Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kya Masjid ke liye waqf kardah cheez ko bechna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Asal ko baaqi rakhte huye is ke samaraat ko bechna jaaiz hai, jaise kisi ne darakht waqf kiya to ab us darakht ko bechna jaaiz nahi albatta us ke phal bechna jaaiz hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 5/28). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Masjid ki zameen ko bechne ka kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Masjid ki zameen agar waqf ki hui ho to bechna jaaiz nahi. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 5/28). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya masjid me rakhkha huwa Qur-aan tilaawat ke liye ghar le jaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Masjid me rakhe huye Qur-aan aksar log waqf ki niyyat se rakhte hai taake musalli Hazraat use masjid me padhein aur dene waalon ko sawaab milta rahe, waqf ki hui cheez ko baahar le jana jaaiz nahi hai.

Agar masjid ke zimmedaar, masjid committee ke maal se Qur-aan ya koi deeni kitaab khareed kar masjid me rakkhe aur niyyat 'aam nafa'a pohnchaane ki ho to un ki ijaazat se Qur-aan ya deeni kitaab ghar par kuch der ke liye le jaana jaaiz hoga, lekin mutaala'aah karne ke baad apni zimmedaari par waapas le aana zaroori hoga. (Majmoo' Fatawa wa Rasail Ibn 'Usaymin: 31/14). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar masjid ke liye koi cheez (A.C, Mic wagayrah) atiyyah kiya gaya ho aur wo kharaab ho jaaye to kya kiya jaaye?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs masjid ke liye atiyyah de aur wo cheez kharaab ho jaaye to use durust karne ki koshish ki jaaye taake asal baaqi rahe, agar durust karna mumkin na ho to masjid ke zimmedaar ya mutawalli hazraat use bech kar aane waali raqam masjid ke kisi bhi kaam me isti'emaal kar sakte hai lekin agar waqf ki hui cheez ho to mas-alah alag hoga. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 5/358). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Masjid ke liye waqf kardah pankha kharaab ho jaaye to kya use bechna jaaiz hai? Agar bechna jaaiz hai to aane waali raqam kahan isti'emaal kare?

Jawab: Waqf kardah pankhe ko agar durust kar ke isti'emaal karna mumkin ho to durust kar ke isti'emaal kare kyunke waqf ki hui cheez ko bechna jaaiz nahi. Agar durust karna mumkin na ho to bataure majboori use bech diya jaaye taake waqf ka maal zaaye'a na ho lekin bechne ke baad aane waali raqam se doosra pankha khareedein ya pankhe ki raqam me isti'emaal kare. Isi tarah tamam waqf ki hui cheezon ka hukm hoga (Al-Gurarul-Bahiyyah: 3/386). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bachcha Goad Lena (Adoption)

Sawal: Kya kisi bachche ko goad lena (Adoption) jaaiz hai? Agar koi kisi ko paale to alag baat hai lekin naam par register karna kaisa hai? Kyunke bahut pehle suna tha yeh jaaiz nahi hai kyunke Qur-aan ki aayat ke baare me jo Zaid bin Haarisah aur Aap (ﷺ) ke ta'alluq se hai. Is me mana'a hai.

Jawab: Goad lena jaaiz hai albatta us bachche ki nisbat ya'ani us bachche ke naam ke aage us ke baap hi ka naam lage ga, goad lene waale ka nahi yeh naajaaiz aur haraam hai. Islam ke aane se pehle log goad liye huye bachche ko haqeeqi bachche ki tarah samajhte the is wajah se log Hazrat Zaid (Rz) ko Zaid Bin Muhammad kehte the. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 34/209). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kisi aur ka naam lagaana haraam hai ya nahi? Agar pehle se wo mahram ho to parde ka kya hukm hoga? Waraasat ka kya mas-alah hai?

Jawab: Kisi doosre ka bataur baap ke naam lagana haraam hai, naamahram hone ki wajah se pardah zaroori hoga. Aur wo us ka waaris nahi hoga. Agar wo us ko zindagi me kuch tuhfah de ya wasiyyat kare to maalik ban sakta hai. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:34/209, Al-Fiqihul Manhaji: 4/47). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Bachchon Ke Daant Aate Waqt Kaala Dhaagah (Tilismati) Pehnaana

Sawal: Chemist me Bachchon ke daant aate waqt pehenne ke liye ek kaala dhaagah (Tilisimati dora) aata hai. Yeh dhaagah bahut se muslim log bhi apne Bachchon ko pehnaate hain aur chemist ke muslim maalikaan bhi use apni dukaan me rakhte hain. Haqeeqat me is kaale dhaage ka tibbi e'etibaar se daant se koi waasta nahi, aur mai ne ek doctor se bhi tasdeeq ki, unhon ne kaha ke logon ko bewakoof bana rahe hain. 'Ulama se guzaarish hai ke is ka khulaasah kare ke kya yeh pehenna saheeh hai?

Jawab: Sharee'ate mutahharah me is dhaage se muta'alliq kahin bhi koi hukm saabit nahi aur tibbi e'etibaar se bhi is me koi nafa'a nahi hai, aur agar darhaqeeqat yeh jaadu ya mantar kiya huwa dhaaga ho jis me gairullah ki madad li jaati hai jaisa ke us ke naam (Tilisimaati dora) se wazeh hai, to yeh shirk hai aur Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne shirk se bachne aur door rehne ka hukm diya hai. Lihaaza shar'an is ka khareedna, pehenna ya pehnaana jaaiz nahi hoga. (Saheehul Bukhaari: 5764) (Al-Majmoo': 9/66). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Choti Betiyon Ke Baal Kaatna

Sawal: Kitni maayein apni choti betiyon ke baal katati hain kyunke lambe aur achche baal hon? Kya is niyyat se ijaazat hogi?

Jawab: Is niyyat se choti bachchiyon ke baalon ko tarashna jaaiz hoga (Ash-Sharhul-Kabeer: 3/425). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Beti ke baal kaafi lambe hon aur maa apni tabee'at ki naasaazi ki wajah se un ko chote karna chaahti ho to kya apni beti ke baal katarwa sakti hai?

Jawab: Baal katwaane ki mukhtalif wujoohaat ho sakti hain. Mardon ki mushaabahat (resembling) ho to haraam hai, gairon ki mushaabahat (resembling) bhi mana'a hai, fashion bhi galat hai lekin koi majboori ho to zaroorat ke mutaabiq badarjah majboori beti ke baal katarwa sakti hai. (Al-Bayaan: 4/341). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Lekin majboori me kis 'umr tak beti ke baal katarwa sakte hain? 'umr ki koi khaas bandish jaise sinne bulugat? Aur wo bhi majboori ki haalat me aur katarwaana bhi is liye hai ke kaafi lambe hain banaane me taqleef hoti hai is liye sirf thodi si lambaayi kam karna maqsad hai, bilkul chote nahi karna!

Jawab: Majboori ke liye kisi 'umr ki qaid nahi hoti. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Chote Bachchon Ko Behlaane Phuslaane Ke Liye Jhoot Ka Sahaara Lena

Sawal: Kya chote bachchon ko behlaane phuslaane ke liye jhoot ka sahaara le sakte hain? Jab wo bahut ro rahe hon aur unhein samjhaana mushkil ho kyunke wo naasamajh hote hain.

Jawab: Nahi, is tarah karna saheeh nahi hai. hadees me is (jhoot) se mana'a kiya gaya hai aur yeh gunaah me daakhil hoga. (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah:34/208). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Bachchon Ko Convent School Me Dakhil Karna

Sawal: Kya a'ala ta'aleem ki gharaz se Bachchon ko convent school me daakhil karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Musalmaan ke liye a'ala ta'aleem doosre number par honi chaahiye, sab se muqaddam cheez deen wa eemaan aur aqeede ki hifaazat hai. Convent school me 'isaaiyat ki ta'aleem di jati hai is liye is tarah ke schoolon se apne Bachchon ko bachaana zaroori hai jin me khilaafe shara'a baaton ki ta'aleem di jaati ho ya khilaafe shara'a aa'amaal karwaaye jaate hon. (Darul-'Uloom Deoband: 18614). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Choti Bachchi Ka School Me Tairaaki (Swimming) Seekhna

Sawal: Kya choti bachchi jis ki 'umr paanch se aath saal ho use school me tairaaki seekhne ke liye bhejna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar fitnah ka andeshah na ho to jaaiz hai. (Al-Muqaddimah fi Fiqhil 'Asr: 1/529). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Khaalah Zaad Saas (Biwi Ki Sagi Khaala) Se Pardah

Sawal: Kya Khaala zaad saas (Biwi ki sagi khaala) ka shumaar mahram 'auraton me hota hai?

Jawab: Biwi ki sagi khaalah na mahram hai aur us se pardah zaroori hai. (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 4/28). Mufti Saeed Ghare

Baalon Ka Qaza'a (Kuch Baal Mundwaana Aur Kuch Chod Dena)

Sawal: Baaz naujawaanaan sir ke baal kuch kam kuch ziyadah katwaate hain kya shar'an is ki gunjaayish hai?

Jawab: Riwaayaat me hai ke Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne qaza'a se mana'a farmaya aur qaza'a ka matlab hai ke bachche ke kuch baal mundwaana aur kuch chod dena, lihaaza yeh makrooh hai. Albatta agar baal is tarah kaate hain jis me gairon ki (yahood wa nasaara ki) mushaabahat (resembling) ho rahi ho to yeh haraam hoga, kyunke Rasoolullah (ﷺ) ne gairon ki mushaabahat (resembling) se shiddat se mana'a farmaaya hai. (Saheehul Bukhaari: 5921, Al-Majmoo': 1/296). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Mard Ka Seene Ke Baal Nikaalna

Sawal: Kya mard seene ke baal nikaal sakta hai, kya hum ko is ki ijaazat hai?

Jawab: Nikaal sakta hai magar Khilaafe Aula hai (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 18/100). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Baalon Ko Kaala Karna

Sawal: Kya baalon ko kaala karna jaaiz hai? Kya is se namaz saheeh ho jaayegi?

Jawab: Baalon ko kaala karna jaaiz nahi hai. Baalon par kaala khizaab sirf jihaad me jaaiz hai taake dushmanon par ru'ab pade. Doosri baat yeh hai ke kaale pan ki wajah se agar baalon par cover chadhta ho to phir baalon ko paani na lagne ki wajah se na gusl saheeh hoga aur na Wuzoo, agar Wuzoo saheeh na ho to namaz bhi saheeh nahi hogi is liye ke Wuzoo namaz ke liye shart hai. (Al-Majmoo':1/294). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Daadhi Ki Miqdaar, Halaq Karna Ya Kaatna

Sawal: Shafi'ee 'Ulama ke nazdeek daadhi ka kya mas-alah hai? Aur daadhi ki miqdaar kitni honi chaahiye?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek daadhi rakhna sunnat hai. Is ki koi had (limit) aur muta'ayyan miqdaar nahi hai. Balke daadhi ko apne haal par chod dena chaahiye is ka kaatna makrooh hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj:9/376). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Kya daadhi rakhna sunnate muakkadah hai? Agar hai to kya is ko na rakhne waala aisa hi gunahgaar hai jaise farz namaz ki sunnate muakkadah waala? Ya is ki aur farz ki sunnat ko chodne waalon ke liye alag alag gunaah hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek daadhi ka halaq karna shadeed makrooh hai aur ek musht se kam rakh kar kaatna makrooh hai. Lihaaza agar koi kaatata ho to gunahgaar nahi hoga lekin yeh naa munaasib baat hogi ke koi musalmaan apna chehrah uswaye Rasoolullah (ﷺ) aur tareeqaye Sahaabah Rizwanullahi 'Alaihim aur aslaafe ummat ke khilaaf rakhe. (Al-Majmoo' Sharhe Muhazzab:1/290). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bosah, Musaafaha Aur Mu'aanaqah Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Kisi ke peshaani haath ya rukhsaar pe bosah lena kaisa hai?

Jawab: Nek logon ki peshaani ya haath ka bosah lena jaaiz hai, albatta is me kisi qism ki buri niyyat ka dakhal na ho (Al-Majmoo': 4/636). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Musaafaha ka masnoon tareeqah kya hai?

Jawab: Shawaafe' ke nazdeek musaafaha daahine haath se karna masnoon hai. (Al-Majmoo' Sharhe Muhazzab:1/384). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Mu'aanaqah ka asal tareeqa kya hai? Neez mu'aanaqah ek baar karna masnoon hai ya teen baar?

Jawab: Mu'aanaqah ka ma'anaa gale milna hai, lihaaza is ka asal tareeqa gale se gale ka milna hai, neez ek ya teen martabah mu'aanaqah karne ki koi takhsees kisi riwaayat me nahi hai, ek martabah mu'aanaqah karna bhi kaafi hai (Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 25/157). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Jaandaar Ki Tasweer Waale Message Kisi Ko Bhejna

Sawal: Kya kisi jaandaar ki tasweer waale message, social media ke zariye kisi ko bhejna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Jaandaar ki tasweer ke baare me ahaadees me sakht wa'eed waarid hai, is liye is se bachna chaahiye (Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 3/105). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Ehsaan Ke Badle Me Sirf Jazaakallah Kehna

Sawal: Kya kisi shakhs ke ehsaan ke badle me sirf jazakallah kehna kafi hai ya poora jazaakallahu khairan kehna chaahiye kyunke mujhe kisi ne kaha ke jazaakallah ka matlab hai Allah aap ko jaza de achchi de ya buri de yeh waazeh nahi hai is liye sirf jazaakallah kehna saheeh nahi.

Jawab: Sirf Jazaakallah kehne me koi haraj nahi, is ka matlab bhi “Achcha badla” dena hi hoga. Qur-aan me wasayajzillaahush shaakireen aur is ka matlab hai “Allah shukr guzaaron ko achchi jaza deinge.” (Al-Majmoo':4/643). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Jamaahee (Yawn) Aane Par A'uoozubillaahi Padhna

Sawal: Kya Jamaahee (yawn) aane par A'uoozubillaahi padhna saheeh hai? Kyunke kisi ne mujhe toka ke yeh padhna galat hai. 'Ulama se rahnumaayi ki darkhwast hai.

Jawab: A'uoozubillaah (أَعُوذُ بِاللّٰهِ) padhna kisi hadees me nahi aaya hai, lekin sahee hadees me aaya hai ke Jamaahee (yawn) shaitaan ki taraf se hoti hai (Bukhaari wa muslim) is e'etibaar se A'uoozubillaahi minash shaitaanirr Rajeem padhne me koi haraj nahi. Shaikh Ibne Baaz (Rh) ne bhi “Haraj nahi” kaha hai. (Fatawa Nur 'ali ad-Darb). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Agar kisi ko Jamaahee (yawn) aa rahi ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Saheeh hadees me aaya hai ke Jamaahee (yawn) shaitaan ki taraf se hoti hai aur jahan tak ho sake rokne ka hukm hai. Jamaahee (yawn) ke waqt haa…haa… kehne se mana'a kiya gaya hai ke is soorat me shaitaan pait me daakhil ho kar hansta hai. (Al-Majmoo':4/642). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Magrib Baad Ya Raat Ke Waqt Naakhun Nikaalna

Sawal: Kya Magrib baad ya raat ke waqt naakhun nikaalna mamnu' hai? Neez Jumu'ah ke roz naakhun nikaalna sunnat hai?

Jawab: Raat ke waqt naakhun nikaalne me koi mumaana'at nahi hai, neez 'Ulama ne Jumu'ah ke din ke mustahab a'amaal me naakhun nikaalna bhi likkha hai. (Ad-deenul Khaalis:1/179). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Islaami Naye Saal Ki Mubaarakbaad Dena

Sawal: Kya Islaami naye saal ki mubaarakbaad dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Musaafaha ke saath 'Eid ki mubaarakbaadi dena isi tarah islaami saal ki aur maheene ki mubaarakbaadi dena jaaiz hai. (Tuhfatul-Muhtaaj: 3/56). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

New Year Ke Masa-il

Sawal: New Year ki raat ko celebrate karna kaisa hai?

Jawab: New Year ki raat ko celebrate karna to gairon ki mushaabahat (resembling) hai is liye jaaiz nahi hai. (Fatawa Ibnus-Salah: 2/473). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar gair hamein new year ki mubaarakbaad dein to kya bataure majboori use Jawab me Happy new year kehna saheeh hai?

Jawab: Kuffar ko Christmas ya new year ke jashn ya un ke kisi bhi mazhabi tehwaar ki mubaarakbaad dena jaaiz nahi (Ahkaamu Ahliz zimmah: 1/441). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Birthday Manaana Aur Wish / Du'aa Karna

Sawal: Kya Islaam me birthday manaana aur wish ya du'aa karna jaaiz hai? Kyunke aksar aaj kal dekhne me aata hai ke achche achche deendaar log bhi birthday ki Mubaarakbaad aur du'aayein dete rehte hain. Kya is ki deen me koi gunzaayish hai?

Jawab: Birthday ki Sharee'at me koi asal nahi hai yeh galat hai aur gairon ka tareeqa hai is liye is se bachna chaahiye (Darul-'Uloom Deoband: 37002). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Jis ki birthday hai us ke liye us din khusoosan du'aayein mangna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Birthday manaana hi galat hai isliye is ki munaasabat se jo cheez ki jaaye wo bhi galat hi hogi. Chaahe du'aa ho ya aur koi cheez ho. Mazhabi shi'aar ho ya kisi qaum ki khaas pehchaan ho. Yeh aaj 'aam ho gaya hai lekin Sharee'at ke khilaaf hai aur Allah ke naafarmaan logon ka tareeqah hai is liye is se bachna chaahiye (Darul-'Uloom Deoband: 37002). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Giri Padi Cheez Ka Mas-alah

Sawal: Gire huye paise mil jaayein to kya in ko isti'emaal kar sakte hain? Phir is ka kya karein?

Jawab: Kisi gair mamlook jagah me gire pade paise ya koi cheez jo bilkul haqeer aur ma'mooli (ordinary) ho ke aadatan aur 'alaqe aur waqt ke 'urf ke mutaabiq is ke gum hone par koi fikr aur koi justaju nahi karta masalan ek luqmah, ek khujoor (date) wagayrah to uthaane waala bagair kisi e'elaan ke hi is ka maalik ban jaaye to fauran wo khud isti'emaal kar sakta hai lekin agar maalik zaahir ho aur kahe ke mai ne is cheez ki taraf se e'eraaz (avoidance) nahi barta to use lautaana aur talf (destroy / waste) ho to is ka badal lautaana waajib hai lekin gire huye rupayon ki kuch qeemat aur ahmiyat ho ya'ani gum ho jaane par itni raqam ki talaash aur justaju hoti ho to uthaane waale par is ka e'elaan waajib hai, sirf hifaazat ki niyyat se uthaaye ya hifaazat ke baad khud maalik banne ki niyyat se, dono sooraton me e'elaan waajib hai. E'elaan ek saal tak karna waajib hoga is tarah se ke ibtidaa'an ek hafte tak subha wa shaam do waqt e'elaan kare phir doosre hafte me din me ek baar phir paanch hafte tak har hafte me ek ya do bar phir har maah ek baar e'elaan kare yeh e'elaan 'aam maqaamaat, bazaar ya masjid ke darwaaze par aur jahaan par log jama rehte hain, albatta jahaan wo rupaye mile hain wahan ziyadah e'elaan kare kyunke us jagaah mustahiq ke talaash karne ka ziyadah imkaan hai, laazimi muddat tak e'laaan karne ke baawajood maalik na mile aur wo rupaye bhi baaqi hon to jise wo luqtah mila hai wo us cheez ka maalik ban sakta hai in alfaaz ke saath ke “Mai ne yeh rupaye apni milkiyat me le liya” ab maalik banne ke baad un rupayon ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai phir kabhi kisi ne aakar un rupayon ki milkiyat ka da'awaa kiya to us se tamaam ausaaf daryaafat kar le, tamaam ausaaf bataane ke baad uthaane waale ko gaalib gumaan ho ke yeh sachcha hai to wo rupaye us ke supurd kar de, yeh waajib hai. (Mulakhkhas AZ Tuhfatul-Bari). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Badi Beemaari Ko Chupaane Ke Liye Jhoot Bolna

Sawal: Agar rishtedaari me kisi ko badi beemaari (Misaal ke taur par cancer, AIDS wagayrah) ho aur log is beemaari ke muta'alliq poochein to kya jhoot bol kar is ki asal beemaari chupa sakte hain? Aksar log cancer jaisi beemaari chupaate hain. Is ki kuch wujoohaat hain! Mu'aashareh me use buri nazar se dekha jaata hai aur logon ke eemaan me kami ki wajah se ruj'haan hai ke yeh beemaari la 'ilaaj hai halaankeh Allah ne har beemaari ka 'ilaaj rakhkha hai kuch log aise mareezon se dooriyan banaaye rakhte hain?

Jawab: Kitaab wa sunnat ki roshni me Shawaafe' ke nazdeek yeh mas-alah hai ke agar koi shakhs kisi beemaari me mubtalah ho jaaye to us ke liye sabr karna mustahab hai aur Allah Ta'aala par tawakkul aur bharosah kar ke 'ilaaj na karaana jaaiz hai aur yeh bhi mustahab hai ke 'ilaaj aur dawa kare aur yeh afzal hai, jhoot bolna to haraam hai aur gunaahe kabeerah hai. Albatta kisi heeleh se beemaari ko chupa sakte hain, masalan nek niyyati ke saath ya'ani jis me shikwa'h shikaayat ka andaaz na ho du'aa ki darkhwast aur 'ilaaj se muta'alliq ma'aloomat ke liye is beemaari se lagi hui doosri takaaleef ka zikr kare, 'umooman cancer ke marz me mubtala (afflicted) shakhs intahaayi darjah ke dard me mubtala (afflicted) rehta hai wagayrah to isi ka tazkirah kar de khud bakhud wo beemaari chup jaayegi jise aap chupana chaahte hain, lekin jhoot ka sahaara na lein. Wallahu A'alam. (Al-Majmoo': 5/106, Tuhfatul-Bari: 10/115). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

India Me Income Tax Ki Adaayegi Ke Liye Jhoot Ka Sahaara

Sawal: Agar koi thoda income tax bachaane me jhoot ka sahaara lena chaahe to kya yeh saheeh hai? Aap ke Jawab dene se pehle aap ko income tax ke bare me kuch ma'aloomat dena chaahta hun.

Beshak jhoot bolna galat hai lekin kya yeh hukoomat ki z'iyaadati nahi hai jo itna income tax leti hai? Aysi soorat me agar koi income tax ada karta hai lekin jhoot ka sahaara le kar kuch raqam bachaata hai to kya saheeh hai?

Jawab: Zakaat yeh maali tax nahi hai balke yeh ek 'ibaadat hai jaise namaz 'ibaadat hai to usi tarah zakaat ko bhi maali 'ibaadat kehte hain. Asal hukmaraani Allah ki hai aur Sharee'at ne jahan doosron ki baat maanne ka hukm diya hai hum wahan itna hi maan sakte hain jitni Sharee'at ne ijaazat di hai aur wo cheez Sharee'at ke khilaaf bhi na ho. Baaz mauqon par 'Ulama ne kitab wa sunnat ki Roshni me majboori ki soorat me dil ki karaahat ke saath kuch rukhsat di hai, is liye ke deen aasan hai is me koi tangi nahi. Yaqeenan baaz tax hukoomat ki taraf se gair zaroori hote hain ya'ani is ka koi faaidah us aadmi ko nahi hota jaise income tax, sales tax ya custom duty wagayrah. 'Ulama ne in taxon se bachne ke liye kisi tarkeeb ki ijaazat ya in ki adaayegi ke liye sood ki raqam bhi isti'emaal karne ki ijaazat di hai, baharhaal aisa koi tareeqah apnaaya jaaye jis se hukoomat ki khuli khilaaf warzi se bhi bacha jaaye aur apna mas-alah bhi hal ho jaaye haraj nahi. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Jaaiz Haqq Haasil Karne Ke Liye Bataure Majboori Rishwat Dena

Sawal: Kya apna jaaiz haqq haasil karne ke liye bataure majboori rishwat de sakte hain?

Jawab: Ahaadeese mubaarakah me rishwat dene waale aur rishwat lene waale dono par Allah aur us ke Rasool (ﷺ) ki la'anat waarid hui hai. Rishwat lena to kisi soorat me jaaiz nahi, lekin apna jaaiz haqq haasil karne ke waaste rishwat dene ko Fuqahaa-e-Kiraam ne ijaazat di hai. (Fatawa As-Subuki: 1/204). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Vote Haasil Karne Ke Liye Rupaye, Ijtimaa'i Khaana Khilaana Ya Safar Kharch Ka Kiraayah Dena

Sawal: Hamaare 'alaqe me election se pehle election ki munaasabat se chunaaw me khada rehne waala ummeedwaar ya wo party jis ke liye yeh chunaaw lad raha hai vote haasil karne ke liye fardan fardan rupaye dete hain, ya party ki soorat me ijtimaa'i khaana khilaate hain, ya safar kharch ke naam par aamadoraft ka kiraayah, naashtah paani wagayrah dete hain, taake wo murawwat me isi party ko vote de, kya shar'an in paison ya in sahooliyaat ko lena, ya wo khaana khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Kisi bhi naam se koi rupiyah ya koi maal le kar kisi ko vote dena ya dilwaana ya kisi ke liye raah hamwaar karwaana naajaaiz hai aur yeh raqam lena aur us ki bunyaad par vote dena dono haraam hain. Kyunke election ki munaasabat se diye jaane wale rupaye aur khaana rishwat ke hukm me aata hai, aur rishwat lena, dena, ya us ki dalaali karna sab haraam aur naajaaiz hai.

Rishwat ki mazammat aur us ke lene aur dene waalon par Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne badi sakht wa'eedein batayi hain, Hazrat 'Abdullah Bin 'umar (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: “Rishwat lene aur dene waale par Allah ki la'anat barasti hai.”

Ek doosri hadees me Hazrat 'Abdullah Bin 'umar (Rz) se hi marvi hai ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaya ke “Rishwat lene aur dene waale dono hi dozakh me jaayenge.”

Islam ki nazar me jis tarah rishwat lene aur dene waala mal'oon aur dozakhi hai usi tarah is mu'aamaleh ki dalaali karne waala bhi hadees-e Rasool ki roshni mien Mal'oon hai.

Chunaancheh Sahaabiye Rasool Hazrat Saubaan (Rz) aur Hazrat 'Abdullah Ibne 'umar (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke “Rasool (ﷺ) ne rishwat lene aur dene waale aur rishwat ki dalaali karne waale sab par la'anat farmaayi hai.” (Fatawa As-Subuki: 1/204, Akhrajahu Abu Dawood: 3580, Sunan At-Tirmizi: 1337, Ibne Maajah: 2313, Al-Mu'jamul Ausat: 2/295, Kashful Khifa'a: 2/186. Sanaduhu Hasanun, Al-Mustadrak alas Saheehain: 7264, Al-Muhazzab: 3/381). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hukoomat Ke Bill Me Chori

Sawal: Jo log income tax nahi bharte, ghar me electric board ki tarteeb karte hain jis se light bill bahut kam aata hai, paani ki chori, kayi taraf se hukoomat ka nuqsaan ho raha hai jis me kayi log shaamil hain. Yeh kis had (limit) tak theek hai? Is mauzu'a par bhi roshni daaliye?

Jawab: Sawwal me jo cheezein poochi gai wo saheeh aur jaaiz nahi hai balke yeh dhokah dahi hai, Sharee'at is ki ijaazat nahi deti balke yeh gunaah hai is liye is se bachna chaahiye. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Hukoomat ke bijli meter ko kayi log setting kar ke bill kam karwaate hain. Kya yeh saheeh hai?

Jawab: Yeh haraam hai. Yeh dhokah dena hai aur Sharee'at me dhokah dena mana'a hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Naarangi (Orange) Rang Ka Ration Card

Sawal: Aaj kal Alhamdulillah aksar logon ki tankhwah theek hoti hai phir bhi kayi log naarangi (Orange) rang ka ration card apni hukoomat se chapwa kar subsidy waala ration card lete hain jis se doosre gareeb logon ka nuqsaan hota hai, kya is tarah ka card banwaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar koi shakhs naarangi (Orange) rang ke ration card ka mustahiq na ho to is ke liye is card ka isti'emaal karna saheeh nahi hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Note Bandi Ke Dauran, Kam Wa Besh Raqam Lena

Sawal: Note bandi ke dauran paanch saw ya hazaar ki pooraani note de kar is ke badle kisi agent (Dalaal) se chaar saw ya naw saw ya kam wa besh raqam lena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Note bandi ho jaane ke baad ab un pooraani noton ki haisiyat kaagaz ki tarah hoti hai lihaaza kami beshi ke saath in ka tabaadalah jaaiz hoga. (Al-Umm Lish Shafi'ee: 3/29). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sona (Gold) Par Custom Duty Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Indian Custom Rule ke mutaabiq bairooni mamaalik (Foreign Country) se aane waale mard ko 50000 rupaye aur 'aurat ko 100000 rupaye ka sona (Gold) laane ki ijaazat hai, wo bhi kam se kam 6 maheene se ziyadah biarooni mulk me waqt gazaarah ho is had (limit) se upar sona ho to taqreeban 36% Custom Duty bharna padti hai.

Jawab: Hukoomat baahar se cheezein laane aur le jaane par jo paabandi lagaati hai is ka maqsad hota hai ke mulki maal ka khasaara aur nuqsaan na ho, lihaaza bagair custom bhare laana mubaah to hai lekin hukoomati qaanoon ki khilaaf warzi hai aur qaanoon shikni jurm hai. Albatta agar hukoomat ki taraf se zaiwaraat ki shakl me ziyadah sona laane ki ijaazat ho to koi haraj nahi. Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Jo log zaiwarat ki shakl me sona Hindustan me laate hain un ka asal maqsad yeh hota hai ke khaleej (Gulf) ka sona Hindustan ke banisbat ziyadah behtar hota hai aur thoda sasta bhi to saal me thoda sa sona bashakle zaiwaraat apne isti'emaal ke liye laane me koi bhi insaan galat nahi samajhta aur na hi is me koi hukoomat ko dhokah dena samajhta hai kyunke yeh business ki niyyat nahi hoti aur bagair custom bhare zaiwaraat le aate hain aur miqdaar bhi ek had (limit) me hoti hai jise custom officer pehenne ke zaiwaraat samajh ke muft chod dete hain. Mere khayaal se aksar logon ki yahi soch hoti hai. Kya is tarah karna saheeh hai?

Jawab: Is me koi haraj nahi hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Parindon (Kabootar, Tota Wagayrah) Ko Paalne Ka Shar'an Hukm

Sawal: Parindon (Kabootar, Tota wagayrah) ko paalne ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Parindon ko pinjre me ya aazaad rakh kar paalna jaaiz hai jab ke in parindon ka khaane peene aur har tarah se khayaal rakhkha jaaye lekin haq talafi aur khayaal na rakhne ki soorat me in parindon ko paalna jaaiz nahi hai aur is soorat me marne par yeh shakhs la'anat aur sakht wa'eed ka mustahiq hoga.

Hadeese paak me aata hai ke Hazrat Anas (Rz) farmaate hain ke un ke bhai Abu Umair ne ek parindah paal rakhkha tha, Rasoolullah (ﷺ) jab tashrif laate to farmaate Ay Abu Umair! Nugair (Parindah) ka kya huwa? Wo bachchah us parindah se khela karta tha. (Saheeh Al-Bukhari: 6203).

Mazkoorah baala hadees se istidlaal karte huye Allamah Ibne Hajar Asqalaani (Rh) farmaate hain ke is hadees se parindah ko pinjre wagayrah me roke rakhne ka jawaaz ma'aloom hota hai. (Fathul-Bari: 10/584). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Islaam Me Kutta Paalna Aur Kutton Ko Maarna

Sawal: Islaam me kutta paalna kaisa hai?

Jawab: Bila Zaroorat kutta paalna islaam me naapasandeedah hai. Allah ke Rasool (ﷺ) ka irshaad hai ke farishte aise ghar me daakhil nahi hote jis me kutta ho (Bukhaari) is e'etibaar se kutta paalna achcha kaam nahi hai. Lekin baaz zarooriyaat ke liye gunjaayish di gai hai, chunaanche ahaadees me shikaar ke liye, kheti aur bakriyon ki hifaazat ke liye kutta paalne ki ijaazat di gai hai. (Bukhaari wa Muslim) is ke 'alaawah kutta be barkati ka sabab hai. ek hadees ka mafhoom hai ke jo be zaroorat kutta paalta hai to rozaana us ke ajr me kami waaqe hoti rehti hai. (Bukhaari). Maulavi 'Abdullah Khateeb

Sawal: Hamaari Basti me kuch roz se aawaarah kutte biphar (Gusse me bharna, gazabnaak hona) gaye hain aur jhund ki shakl me logon par aur Bachchon aur khawaateen par hamlah aawar ho rahe hain, aur in par gurraate aur daraate hain, jis ki wajah se rahgeer bhi khaufzadah hain aur bachche bhi maktab wa school jaane ke liye darte hain, kya in haalaat me kutton ko maarna jaaiz hai? Aur kya in ko maarne ke liye zaher wagayrah ka isti'emaal kiya ja sakta hai?

Jawab: Agar kutta kaatne waala ho ya har kas wa naakas par gurraata ho ya hamlah aawar ho ya kisi aur soorat me eezaa ka sabab bane to aise moozi aur takleef dene waale kutte ko maarna jaaiz hai, albatta maarne ke liye aisa tareeqah ikhtiyaar kiya jaaye jis me kam se kam takleef ho, lihaaza use zaher de kar maarna bhi durust hai. Jaanwar ko tadpa aur tarsa kar maarne se deen me mana'a kiya gaya hai, lekin agar wo kisi ko takleef na de raha ho to khwah makhwah use qatl karna jaaiz nahi. Chunaanche Hazrat Shaddaad bin Aus (Rz) se riwaayat hai ke Rasool (ﷺ) ne farmaaya: “Beshak Allah Ta'aala ne har kaam ko achche tareeqe se karna zaroori qaraar diya hai, pas jab tum qatl karo to achche tareeqe se qatl karo aur jab (jaanwar) zabah karo to achche tareeqe se zabah karo, lihaaza yeh zaroori hai ke tum me se koi bhi shakhs (jo jaanwar ko zabah karna chaahta ho) apni churi ko khoob tez kar le aur zabah kiye jaane waale jaanwar ko aaram pahunchaaye.” (Saheeh Muslim:1955).

Ammi 'Aayesha (Rz) farmaati hain ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya ke paanch cheezein faasiq hain unhein haram aur gair haram me qatal kiya jaaye: Saanp aur siyaah wa safed kawwa, chuha, cheel aur Kaatne waala kutta (Saheeh Muslim: 1198, Al-Majmoo': 9/235). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

4 September Ko Hijaab Day Ki Kya Haqeeqat

Sawal: 4 September ko Hijaab Day ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Is ki koi asal nahi hai mahaz jahaalat hai. Mufti Mahmood Mahaadik

Chori Ki Hui Cheez Hadiyah Me Isti'emaal Karna

Sawal: Agar koi shakhs kisi ko koi cheez hadiyah de aur baad me pata chala ke wo cheez to kisi ki churaayi hui thi to kya is hadiya ko isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Is cheez ko isti'emaal karna jaaiz nahi ya to use waapas kare ya agar is ke maalik ka pata ho to use de de ya aakhari darjah me use kisi gareeb ko sadaqah kare. (Al-Majmoo':9/351). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Khudkushi Ke Masa-il

Sawal: Islaam me khudkushi haraam hai. Lekin kya wo musalmaan jannat me jaayega jis ne khudkushi ki?

Jawab: Jannat se wahi shakhs mahroom hoga jis ki maut kufr aur shirk par hui ho is ke 'alaawah jitne gunaah hain wo Allah ki maslihat ke tahat hain chaahe mu'aaf kare ya azaab de aur gunaah ki wajah se aadmi eemaan se khaarij nahi hota is liye wo bhi In sha Allah jannat me jaayega. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya khudkushi karne waale ki namaze Janazah padhna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Khudkushi apni jagah garche ek fe'ele haraam hai lekin khudkushi karne waale ki namaze Janazah padhi jaayegi. Aur is ka yeh 'amale bad Allah ke hawaale hoga. (Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 1/150 aur 7/47). Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Ek post tha “Khudkushi Sakht gunaah hai”. Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya, “Jo apna gala ghot kar marta hai wo jahannam me apna gala ghotata rahega aur jo shakhs bhaala (nezah) chubho kar apni jaan deta hai wo jahannam me apne aap ko bhaala marta rahega.” (Bukhaari-kitaabul Janaayiz: 1365, 5778).

Hadees-e Qud'si me hai ke Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne farmaaya Allah Ta'aala irshaad farmaata hai “Mere bande ne apni jaan mujh se pehle khud li isliye maine us par jannat Haraam kar di.” (Bukhaari, Hawala saabiq 1364, 3463).

Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne khudkushi (suicide) karne waale ki namaze janazah nahi padhi. (Saheeh Muslim: 978).

Is liye 'Ulama ko chaahiye ke aise logon ki namaze Janazah me shareek na hon taake baaqi logon ko ibrat haasil ho.

Is post ki kya haqeeqat hai?

Jawab: Khudkushi karne waala agar khudkushi karne ko jaaiz samajhta tha to goya us ne kufr kiya jiski wajah se hameshah jahannam me jalega lihaaza murtad hone ki wajah se ab us par namaze Janazah nahi padheinge lekin agar is 'amal ko haraam samajhta tha phir bhi khudkushi ki ho to aisa shakhs musalmaan hi samjha jaayega aur wo faasiq shumaar hoga aur us par namaze Janazah padhi jaayegi agarcha us ne gunaahe kabeerah kiya hai. Kyunke shirk ke 'alaawah har gunaah ko Allah Ta'aala mu'aaf karenge aur bil-aakhir wo jannat me jaayega. Yahi hukm Imaam Malik, imaam Abu Hanifah (Rh) aur imaam Shafi'ee (rh) ka hai. Hadees-e mubaarakah me jo yeh baat mazkoor hai ke khudkushi karne waale par Huzoor (ﷺ) ne namaze Janazah nahi padhi wo is wajah se taake aaindah koi shakhs aise khilaafe Sharee'at kaam karne ki jur'at na kare, albatta Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) ko mana'a bhi nahi kiya to Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) ne us ki namaze Janazah padhi. (Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 1/150, Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 7/47). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Istitaa'at Ke Baawajood Zaroorat Mand Ki Mukammal Haajat Puri Na Karna

Sawal: Agar koi zaroorat mand apni beti ki shaadi ke liye 80000 ki madad mange. Agar istitaa'at hone ke baawajood 80000 ki bajaaye sirf 15000 ki madad kare to kya wo gunaahgaar hoga?

Jawab: Aap jis maal ke maalik hain aap ko mukammal ikhtiyaar hai is amr me ke aap kisi ko ziyadah dein ya kisi ko kam, lihaaza mazkoorah soorat me aap gunaahgaar nahi honge In Sha Allah. Kyunke Qur-aan wa hadees me muta'addad jaghon par e'etidaal ke saath maal kharch karne ki targeeb di gai hai, ya'ani yeh hukm diya gaya hai ke insaan apne maal me na to bukhl kare aur na hi israaf aur fuzool kharchi. Chunaanche surah an'aam me Allah Ta'aala irshaad fermaate hain: (Jab darakht phal de) to un ke phalon se khao aur un phalon me fuqaraa ka jo haq waajib hai ('Ushr) wo un ki kataayi ke din diya karo aur had se tajaawuz na karo, beshak Allah had se tajaawuz karne waalon ko naapasand farmaate hai. (Al-an'aam: 141).

Is aayat ka ma'anaa yeh hai ke sadaqah wa khairaat karne aur 'ushr (1/10th – daswan hissa zakaat) ada karne me bhi had se tajaawuz mat karo, aisa na ho ke aaj tum had (limit) se ziyadah maal sadaqah kar do aur kal tum khud zaroorat mand ho jao.

Isi tarah surah Isra'a me Allah Ta'aala irshaad farmaate hain ke: Apna haath apni gardan se bandha huwa na rakh aur na use bilkul hi khol de ke phir malaamat (blame) kiya huwaa darmaandah (helpless) baith jaaye. (Surah Israa:29).

Is aayate Kareemah me Allah Ta'aala ne maal kharch karne ka adab bayaan kiya hai aur wo yeh hai ke insaan itna bhi bukhl na kare ke apni aur apne ahl-o-'ayaal ki zarooriyaat par bhi kharch na kare aur itni bhi fuzool kharchi na kare ke apni wus'at (capacity) aur gunjaayish dekhe bagair hi be daregh kharch karta rahe. Bukhl ka nateejah yeh hoga ke insaan qaabile malaamat wa nadaamat qaraar payega aur fuzool kharchi ke nateeje me mah'soor (Thaka haara aur pachtaane waala) ho jaayega. Fuzool kharchi karne waala bhi bil-aakhir khaali haath ho kar baith jaata hai.

Isi tarah surah Furqaan me Allah Ta'aala irshaad fermaate hain: Aur (Rehmaan ke bande wo hain) jo kharch karte waqt bhi na to israaf karte hain na bakheeli, balke in dono ke darmiyaan mu'atadil tareeqe par kharch karte hain (Surah Furqaan: 67, (Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:3/30). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Dirilis Ertugrul Ghaazi Naami Serial Dekhna

Sawal: Turki hukoomat ke zere ehtimaam Dirilis Ertugrul naami serial jo ke poori dunya me bahut mash'hoor hai aur yeh khilaafate Usmaaniyah ki taareekh par mabni hai.
