Ab yeh urdu tarjamah (translation) ke saath Television par nashr ho raha hai, aaj kal Asia ke musalmaanon me ise khoob dekha bhi ja raha hai aur saraaha bhi jaa raha hai, 'awaam ke saath saath baaz khawaas bhi is serial ke dekhne ke bahut 'aadi ho gaye hain, kya Sharee'at aise serial dekhne ki ijaazat deti hai?

Jawab: Mazkoorah Drama ho ya koi Drama ya serial ya film dekhna shar'an jaaiz nahi hai. Yeh sharaab ke mutaraadif hai, jis ka nafa'a kam aur nuqsaan ziyadah aur nuqsaan to nafa'a par gaalib aur badha huwa hai, is drame ko dekhne ki 'aadat dheere dheere filmbeeni ki taraf le jaati hai jo ke haraam hai. Neez is me waqt ka ziya'a (waste) bhi hai aur laaya'ani kaam bhi, jis se bachne ki hadeese paak me targeeb di gai hai aur husne islaam ki 'alaamat batlaayi gai hai. Chunke 'umooman T.V par aane waale program moseeqi naach gaane, bad nighaahi ke asbaab se khaali nahi hote, lihaaza is qism ke programon ke dekhne ki targeeb dena yeh fahash cheez ko aur gunaah wa bad akhlaaqi ko phailaane aur gunaah par madad karne ke qabeel se hone ki wajah se jaaiz nahi.

Agar shaayiqeen Hazaraat Khilaafate Usmaaniah se muta'alliq taareekhi kitaabon ka mutaala'ah kare to yeh nafa'a bakhsh hoga. (Surah Noor: 30, Surah Noor: 19, Sunan At-Tirmizi:2317, Ibne Maajah: 3976, Mas-alatus Sima'a:407, Takhreejul Ihyaa lil 'Iraaqi:2/336). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Asal Maalik Ki Ijaazat Ke Bagair Ashya'a Ki Taqseem

Sawal: Mere ek dost ne kiraaye par Laundry chalaane ko li bahut maheenon se band thi. pehle jo use chalaata tha wo bhaag gaya. Abhi laundry me bahut saare kapde, estari wagayrah padi hui hain. Filhaal wo shakhs laundry chala raha hai lekin use puraane laundry waale ke kapde nahi chaahiye. Naya laundry waala un kapdon ko phenk raha hai aur jo achche kapde hain wo taqseem kar raha hai. Ziyadah tar kapde jubbah hain. Laundry chalaane waala gair muslim hone ki wajah se kuch jubbah us ne hamein bhi diye. Kya in kapdon ko hum le sakte hain?

Jawab: Laundry aur dhobi ke paas diye jaane waale kapde dhobi ke paas amaanat hote hain, aur amaanat rakhi hui cheez saahibe amaanat ya'ani asal maalik ko lautaana zaroori hai agar wo maujood ho, warnah haakime waqt ke hawaale kare. Chunke laundry ka naya maalik pehle se rakhi hui in cheezon ka maalik nahi hai, isliye naye maalik ko yeh jaaiz nahi ke yeh cheezein kisi ko beche ya yun hi kisi ko muft me de aur lene waale ko yeh cheezen na to khareed kar lena jaaiz hai aur na hi muft mein. (Al-Iqna' lil-Mawardi: 1/113, Kitaabul Umm: 7/102, Al-Majmoo': 15/96). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Haosalah Afzaayi Ke Liye Taaliyan Bajaana

Sawal: Islaam me kisi ki haosalah afzaayi ke liye taaliyan bajaana kaisa hai?

Jawab: Islaam me bagair kisi haajat ke sirf kisi ki haosalah afzaayi ke liye taaliyan bajaana makrooh hai kyunke taaliyan bajaane me gairon se mushaabahat (resembling) aur 'auraton ke saath tashabbuh bhi paaya jaata hai aur zamaanae jaahiliyyat ki 'aadat bhi hai is liye is se ehtiraaz karna chaahiye lekin kisi zaroorat ke mauqa'ah par masalan kisi door khade shakhs ko mutawajjah karne ki garz se taali bajaaye to mubaah hoga. Lihaazah kisi achche kaam par haosalah afzaayi ke liye taali ke bajaaye Allahu Akbar ya Subhaanallah kehna behtar hai. (Hawaashi ash Sharwaani wa ibnul Qasim al 'Abbaadi:2/150, Haashiyataa Al-Qalyubi wa 'Umairah: 1/534, Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 2/164). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Yoga Aur Suryah Namaskaar Karna

Sawal: Kya Yoga aur Suryah Namaskaar karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Yoga se muta'alliq tahqeeq me yeh baat saamne aayi hai ke is ki do bunyaadi baatein seedhi kufr aur hindu mazhab se ja milti hain. ek is ka maqsad geeta ki ta'aleem hai. Doosre yoga par rishiyon aur aachaariyon ka mukammal control hai. Bataure 'ibaadat is ki mashq ki jaati hai, isi tarah yoga ke ushlokon mien tauheed, risaalat, kitaabullah aur youme aakhirat ka inkaar hai. Makhlooq ka khaaliq me Hulool kar jaane ka aqeedah hai. Kuffaar ke saath mushaabahat (resembling) hai. Is tahqeeq ki bunyaad par yeh baat wazeh ho jaati hai ke yoga hindu tahzeeb ke aqaaid ka aham hissa hai, aur barhami tahzeeb aur shirkiyah umoor par mushtamil hai, isi liye islaam me yoga aur suryah namaskaar karna jaaiz nahi.

Isi tarah Taajdaare Madinah Hazrat Muhammad (ﷺ) ne teen baaton se mana'a farmaaya: Tashabbuh Bil Kuffaar, Tashabbuh bin Nisaa, Tashabbuh Bir Rijaal. Ya'ani koi momin kaafir ki mushaabahat (resembling) ikhtiyaar na kare, koi mard kisi 'aurat ki mushaabahat (resembling) ikhtiyaar na kare aur koi 'aurat kisi mard ki mushaabahat (resembling) ikhtiyaar na kare. Ummul Mu'amineen Hazrat Aayeshah Siddeequa (Rz) ne ek dafa'ah ek 'aurat ko dekha jis ki chaadar me saleeb ke naqsh wa nigaar bane huye the, dekhte hi daanta ke yeh chaadar utaar do, Huzoor (ﷺ) aise kapdon ko dekhte to phaad dalte.

Qur-aan-e-kareem me Allah Paak ki targeeb wa tarheeb, Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ke irshaadaat aur Sahaabah-e-Kiraam (Rz) ka 'amal is baat ki taraf musheer hai ke kuffaar ke taur tareeqe rasm wa riwaaj, makhsoos mazhabi style wa andaaz ki taqleed karna aur kuffaar se bhari asal nav'iyyat ko bagair zaayil kiye kisi ki payravi (follow) karna kuffaar ki mushaabahat (resembling) ikhtiyaar karna hai, jis ki islaam me mumaana'at hai aur islaam is ki kabhi bhi ijaazat nahi deta. Lihaaza musalmaanon ko yoga aur suryah namaskaar jaise hinduwaanah rasm wa riwaaj aur taur tareeqe se kulli taur par ijtinaab karna chaahiye. (Saheeh Ibne Maajah: 1557. Hasan Saheeh, An-Nawawi: 676, Fatawa an-Nawawi: 61. Saheehatun Mash'hoorah, Ibnul 'Arabi: 543, Ahkaamul Qur-aan Lish Shafi'ee: 1/387.Saheeh, Gayatul Maraam: 142, Saheeh). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il aur 'Ilmi Batein - Jild Duwam

Muntakhab Shafi'ee Masa-il ki doosri jild anqareeb (soon) Insha Allah manzar-e-'aam par aane ki kagaar par hai. Pecheeda, jadeed aur nit naye masa-il par nazar-e-saani jaari hai. Yeh doosri jild Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla, Mufti Fayyaz Ahmed Barmare aur Mufti Ishaq Patel ki koshishon ka samarah hai.

Zair-e-tabaa'at: Maktabah Dare Arqam, Lower Tudil, Mahad, Maharashtra

📞 6361759091 / 9028286370

MEDICAL AUR 'ILAAJ KE MASA-IL

Hum Jins Muslim Doctor Se 'Ilaaj

Sawal: Agar koi musalmaan mard ya 'aurat beemaar ho jaaye to apne marz ki tashkhees aur 'ilaaj ke liye shar'an kis ke paas jaana laazimi hai?

Jawab: 'Ilaaj ke liye sab se pehle hum jins muslim doctor ko talaash karna zaroori hai ya'ani 'aurat apne 'ilaaj ke liye muslim 'aurat ko talaash kare aur mard apne 'ilaaj ke liye muslim mard ko talaash kare, chunaanche Allamah Khateeb Sharbeeni farmaate hain:

Mard doctor ke hote huye koi 'aurat kisi mard ka 'ilaaj na kare is ke bar'aks ('aurat doctor ke hote huye koi mard doctor kisi 'aurat ka 'ilaaj na kare) isi tarah koi kaafir mard doctor, muslim mard doctor ke hote huye kisi musalmaan mard ka 'ilaaj na kare aur koi kaafirah 'aurat doctor, muslim 'aurat doctor ke hote huye kisi muslim 'aurat ka 'ilaaj na kare. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb: 3/328). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Hum Jins Gair Muslim Doctor Se 'Ilaaj

Sawal: Kya Muslim mard wa 'aurat ko apne jins ka muslim doctor na mile to kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Talaash ke baawajood agar koi hum jins muslim doctor na mile to hum jins gair muslim doctor se apna 'ilaaj karaane ki gunjaayish hai, ya'ani 'aurat apne 'ilaaj ke liye gair muslim 'aurat ke paas jaaye aur mard apne 'ilaaj ke liye gair muslim mard ke pas jaaye.

Chunaanche Allamah Sulaimaan Jamal (Rh) farmaate hain ke muslim 'aurat ke 'ilaaj ke liye gair muslim 'aurat doctor, muslim mard doctor par muqaddam hogi ya'ani muslim hum jins doctor ke na paaye jaane ki soorat me gair muslim hum jins doctor ko muslim gair jins doctor par muqaddam kareinge.

(Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 4/127, Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb: 3/280). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Gair Jins Muslim Doctor Se 'Ilaaj

Sawal: Kisi Marz ke 'ilaaj ke liye apne jins ka doctor na mile, na muslim aur na kaafir to aysi soorat me kya kare?

Jawab: Agar hum jins doctor maujood na ho to gair jins muslim doctor ko talaash kare aur us se apna 'ilaaj karwaaye chunaanche Allamah Khateeb Sharbeeni farmaate hain ke Mard 'aurat ka 'ilaaj kar sakta hai, isi tarah 'aurat mard ka 'ilaaj kar sakti hai albatta mahram ya shohar ya bharosaamand 'aurat ki maujoodgi me ho, is shart ke saath koi hum jins doctor na mile, aur musalmaan doctor ki maujoodgi me gair muslim doctor se 'ilaaj na karwaaye. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/215). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gair Jins Gair Muslim Doctor Se 'Ilaaj

Sawal: Agar kisi marz ke 'ilaaj ke liye na to hum jins doctor dastiyaab ho aur na hi gair jins muslim doctor to aysi soorat me kya kare?

Jawab: Hum jins doctor ki adame maujoodgi me gair jins muslim doctor ko talaash kare agar koi gair jins muslim doctor bhi maujood na ho to badarjah majboori gair jins muslim doctor se apna 'ilaaj karwaana jaaiz hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/216). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Doctor Ya Tabeeb Ka Tanhaayi Me Kisi 'Aurat Ka 'Ilaaj Karna

Sawal: Aaj Kal hum dekhte hain ke doctaron ka khud ka alag cabin hota hai aur is tarah tayyar kiya jaata hai ke andar mukammal tanhaayi hoti hai taake mareez ke satr par kisi ki nazar na pade. Sawal yeh hai ke kya koi mard doctor kisi 'aurat ka ya koi 'aurat doctor kisi mard ka tanhaayi me 'ilaaj kar sakti hai?

Jawab: Doctaron ke liye zaroori hain ke gair jins ka 'ilaaj tanhaayi me na kare is liye ke Hazrat Nabiye Kareem (ﷺ) ne irshaad farmaaya: koi mard kisi ajnabi 'aurat ke saath khalwat (Tanhaayi) ikhtiyaar na kare magar yeh ke us 'aurat ke saath us ka mahram ho. Lihaaza laazimi aur zaroori hai ke 'aurat ke saath koi mahram ya shohar ya kam se kam koi mu'atamad 'aurat ho.

Chunaanche Allama Khateeb Sharbeeni (Rh) farmaate hain: Zaroorat aur haajat ke waqt doctor ke liye gair jins ka 'ilaaj karna jaaiz hai, is shart ke saath ke yeh 'ilaaj mahram ya shohar ya kisi mu'atamad 'aurat ki maujoodgi me ho. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/215). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Gairon Se Yarqaan Ka 'ilaaj

Sawal: Kya gairon ke paas kaamin (peeliya) utaar sakte hain?

Jawab: Agar mantar ke zariye utaara jaaye to jaaiz nahi lekin agar sirf dawa ke zariye ho to koi haraj nahi. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Abhi hamein kaise pata chalega ke saamne waala gair muslim kaamin utarne ke liye kon sa tareeqa ikhtiyaar karta hai? Dawa ke saath muh ke muh me bhi wo mantar padh raha hoga. Yeh ek shak wa shubah me daalne waali cheez hai, agar saheeh 'ilm na ho to kya kare?

Jawab: Mantar ka ta'alluq padhne se hota hai aur is ka ehsaas ho hi jaata hai agar muaamalah yeh ho ke bagair mantar ke koi bhi sirf dawa se 'ilaaj karta hi nahi to phir un ke paas nahi jaana chaahiye. Balke agar koi muslim bhi aise mantar ke zariye 'ilaaj kare jis ka matlab ma'aloom na ho ya wo Sharee'at ke khilaaf ho to muslim ke paas bhi nahi jaana chaahiye. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Kya Koi Beemaari La-'Ilaaj Hoti Hai?

Sawal: Kya Koi beemaari la-'ilaaj hoti hai?

Jawab: Imaam Nawawi (rh) Muslim Shareef ki sharah me likhte hain ke Allah Rabbul Izzat bada Raheem wa Kareem hai, agar wo apne bandon ko mukhtalif beemaariyon me mubtalah farmaata hai to in takaaleef par unhein jazaa bhi deta hai ya un ke gunaahon ka kaffaarah banaata hai aur us ke saath saath us ne apne rahmate kaamilah se in tamaam beemaariyon se shifa ke tareeqe aur dawaayein bhi ata farmaayi hain.

Marz aur 'ilaaj: Atibaa'a ke nazdeek jisme insaani ka apni taba'ee haalat se nikal jaana marz kehlaata hai, aur is ko apni asli haalat me lautaana 'ilaaj kehlaata hai aur jism ko apni taba'ee haalat par qaayim rakhna Hifze Sihhat hai, jo gizaa aur jism se muta'alliq doosri cheezon ki durusti ke bagair mumkin nahi aur agar jism ki haalat bigad jaaye to marz ko khatam karne waali muwaafiq dawa ke zariye hi is ko behtar kiya jaa sakta hai. (Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 14/192)

Jaisa ke Muslim Shareef ki hadees me hai: Har Beemaari ki dawa hai, jab dawa beemaari ke muwaafiq (mutaabiq) ho jaati hain to Allah ke hukm se mareez beemaari se shifaayaab ho jaata hai. (Saheeh Muslim: 2204).

'Aam taur par doctors bahut se amraaz ke muta'alliq keh dete hain ke yeh marz la 'ilaaj hai, lekin mazkoorah baala hadeese paak se yeh baat wazeh ho gai ke Allah Ta'aala ne har marz ki dawa paida farmaayi hai, pas jab mareez ko marz ke muwaafiq (mutaabiq) dawa mayassar aa jaayegi to Allah ke izn se shifa bhi naseeb ho jaayegi, aur jo bahut se log 'ilaaj ke baawajood sihhatmand nahi hote, is ki wajah yeh nahi ke us marz ki dawa nahi hai balke wo us marz ki dawa aur tareeqae 'ilaaj se naa waaqifiyat ki bina par sihhat mand nahi hote. (Sharhun-Nawawi 'ala Muslim: 14/192). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Napaak Cheez Ko Bataure 'Ilaaj

Sawal: Kya napaak cheez ko bataure 'ilaaj khaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Imaam Shafi'ee (Rh) ke nazdeek napaak cheez se 'ilaaj karna jaaiz nahi, han agar is beemaari ke 'ilaaj ke liye koi aur paak dawa badal ke taur par maujood na ho to napaak cheez ko bataure dawa isti'emaal kiya ja sakta hai (Al-Majmoo': 9/50, Qawaa'idul Ahkaam fi Islaahil Anaam: 1/132). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

'Ilaaj Ke Liye Sperm Test (Maadda-E-Tauleed) Ki Jaanch Karwaana

Sawal: Kya 'ilaaj ke liye Sperm Test (Maadda-e-Tauleed ki jaanch) karwaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Agar kisi marz ya zaroorat ki wajah se mustanad aur deendaar doctor tauleedi salaahiyat jaanchne aur us ke 'ilaaj ke liye is test ka mashwarah de to is ki gunjaayish hai magar maadda-e-tauleed ke ikhraaj ke liye jaaiz tareeqah ka isti'emaal karna hoga agar jima'a (sexual intercourse) se mani ke husool ke baad is ke jaraasim ke marjaane ka andeshah ho to biwi ke haath se yeh 'amal karke madda-e-manaviyah haasil kar lene ki gunjaayish hogi. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 7/212, Fathul-Mu'een: 1/482). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mard Wa 'Aurat Ka Operation Ke Zariye Nas Bandi Karaana

Sawal: Kya mard wa 'aurat ka operation ke zariye nasbandi karaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Nasbandi, mard wa 'aurat ki quwwate tauleed ya'ani aulaad paida karne ki salaahiyat ko daayimi taur par khatm kar dena Qur-aan wa hadees ki ru se ek gair islaami tareeqae kaar hai aur is ke naajaaiz wa haraam hone me sabhon ka ittifaaq hai, isi tarah aisa operation karaa lena jis se wati par qudrat na rahe ya aulaad paida hone ki salaahiyat hi khatm ho jaaye hargiz jaaiz nahi balke sakht gunaah hai. Mazeed yeh ke koi aysi dawa ya tadbeer ikhtiyaar karna ke kasrate aulaad ke khauf se paidaayish ko mahdood karna nizaame khuda wandi me dakhal andaazi hai (I'aanatut-Taalibeen: 2/289). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Agar koi shadeed zaroorat ho to kya nas bandi ki ijaazat hogi? Aajkal bahut saare aise waaqiaat pesh aa rahe hain jis me kisi beemaari ki wajah se jawaan 'auraton ki bachcha daani (Hamal ki thaili) nikaali jaarahi hai.

Jawab: Fuqahaa ne kisi bhi tareeqe se mukammal taur par nasbandi ko haraam qaraar diya hai lekin agar koi shadeed zaroorat ho masalan bachchah hone se maa ki jaan ko khatrah ho maa ki bachchah daani me koi beemaari ho aur mu'atabar muslim doctor kahe to bataure majboori nasbandi ki ijaazat hogi lekin 'auratein is ka galat isti'emaal na kare warnah gunaah hoga. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 3/558). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Copper T Ke Zariye Ek Muddat Tak Hamal Rokna

Sawal: Kya Copper T ke zariye ek muddat tak ke liye hamal ko rokna jaaiz hai? Aur kya is haalat me 'aurat ka gusl saheeh hoga? Copper T ek aalah hai, jise 'aurat ki sharmgaah me daakhil kiya jaata hai jis se kuch saalon ke liye Hamal (Pregnancy) nahi theherta is ki muddat mukhtalif (3, 5 aur 10 saal) hoti hai, is ko waapas nikaalne ke baad hamal theher jaata hai.

Jawab: 'Aurat itni kamzor ho ke bojh ko bardaasht na kar sakti ho ya pehla bachcha ayyaame razaa'at me ho aur hamal thaharne (stay) ki wajah se is bachche ke liye maa ka doodh muzir saabit ho jis se fitri taur par is ke badan wa mizaaj me zu'af aur kamzori paida ho sakti ho ya bachche ki paidaayish maa ki jismaani wa dimaagi sihhat ya us ki zindagi ke liye khatarnaak ho aur wo khatrah waaqayi ya zanne gaalib ke darjeh me ho ya do muslim maahir tabeeb is ka mashwara dein to shakhsi ya infiraadi (individual) taur par jis kisi ko yeh a'azaar hon ya in jaise a'azaar hon to waqti taur par is ke liye copper T lagwaane ya hamal ko rokne ki koi tadbeer ikhtiyaar karne ki gunjaayish ho sakti hai. 'uzr ke khatam hone ke baad is ke liye bhi hamal ko rokne ki tadbeer ka yeh tareeqah mamnu' hoga. Is qism ke a'azaar ke 'alaawah kisi aur shakl me hamal rokne ki tadaabeer ikhtiyaar karna shar'an durust nahi (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 7/221, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 8/443). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hamal Ke Liye Test Tube Baby Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Kya hamal ke liye Test Tube Baby ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Test Tube Baby ke zariye tauleed ki 2 shaklein hain.

Sawal: Ek shakhs ka nikah huwa lekin biwi ke baanj hone ki wajah se aulaad nahi hui, is liye us shakhs ne ek bewah se nikah kiya jis ko pehle shohar se aulaad thi, isi wajah se ab wo dobaarah bachche janna nahi chaahti hai, to kya us shakhs ke liye yeh jaaiz hai ke us doosri biwi ka baizah lekar apni mani khalat malat karke Test Tube Baby waale masnooyi tareeqe se pehli biwi ke raham me daakhil kiya jaaye aur aulaad ka husool kiya jaaye? Agar yeh tareeqa saheeh hai to is bachche ki maa kon shumaar hogi? Kya wo 'aurat jis ka baizah hai ya wo 'aurat jis ne nau maah tak pait me us ki parwarish ki, use jana aur use doodh pilaaya?

Jawab: Aulaad ke husool ke liye Test Tube Baby ka yeh tareeqa shar'an haraam aur mamnu' hai kyunke mazkoorah soorat me bachcha ka nasab maa ki taraf se khalat malat (mix-up) ho jaayega aur nau (9) maah pait me bachche ka bojh uthaane aur janne ki takleef bardaasht karne ke baawajood yeh 'aurat us ki maa nahi kehlaayegi to is soorat me waalidah hona zaye'a ho jaayega.

Lihaaza agar kisi ne anjaane me is tareeqa e kaar se aulaad ka husool kiya hai to is bachche ki maa wahi 'aurat kehlaayegi jis ka baizah hai. Wallahu A'alam. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 7/5100, Qaraaraatul Majma'il Fiqhil Islaami: 159-161, Islam web/Al-Idaaratul Aamma Lil-auqaaf). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

D & C- Dilation And Curettage: Hamal Gira Dena

Sawal: Agar kisi 'uzr ki wajah se D & C (hamal gira dena) karni pade to kya us gosht ke hisse ko dafnaana chaahiye aur kin haalaat me D & C kara sakte hain aur is ke sharaait kya hain?

Jawab: Allah Ta'aala ka irshaad hain ke tum apni aulaad ko faqr wa faaqah ke khauf se katl na karo (Surah An'aam: 151)

Allama Nawawi (Rh) farmaate hain: 'Ulama ka is baat par ittifaaq hai ke ruh chaar maheene ke baad phunki jaati hai (sharhu saheehi Muslim: 6/146)

Allama Karaabesi (rh) farmaate hain ke maine Abu Bakr Bin Abu Saeed Alfuraati se is shakhs ke muta'alliq poocha jo apni baandi ko aisa mashroob pilaaye jis se us ka hamal saaqit (fallen) ho jaaye to Abu Bakr ne farmaaya ke jab hamal nutfah ya jame huwe khoon ki soorat me ho to is ko gira sakte hai. (Haashiyatul Jamal 'Ala Sharhi Manhajit-tullab: 8/625).

'Aam haalaat me hamal thahar jaane ke baad 120 din ke andar 'aam haalaat me hamal ko saaqit karaana aur giraana shar'an jaaiz nahi albatta chand aise qavi a'azaar hain jin se 'uzr waale fard ko a'zaa'a ki takhleeq se qabl ya'ani 120 din ke andar isqaate hamal (hamal giraane) ki ijaazat ho sakti hai. Aise a'azaar teen hain:

In teenon a'azaar me chaar maah se qabl hamal saaqit karaane ki gunjaayish hai. Saaqit shudah gosht ke us lothare ko kapde me lapetkar dafan kiya jaayega.

Chaar maah baad in a'azaar ki bunyaad par bhi hamal saaqit karaane ki ijaazat hargis na hogi. Kyunke is haalat me bachcha ke a'azaa'a banna shuru' ho jaate hain aur is me jaan pad jaati hai aur wo nafs e mohtaram ke hukm me ho jaata hai jis ki baqa aur hifaazat ka kaam karna misle zindah aadmi ke waajib aur zaroori hai. (Qaraaraatul Majma'il Fiqhil Islaami: 123, Al-Mawsoo'atul-Fiqhiyyatul-Kuwaitiyyah: 4/128). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sonography Me Bachche Me Naqs Ya 'Ayb Ki Wajah se Isqaate Hamal

Sawal: Aaj kal doctor mukhtalif hafton ke baad haamilah 'auraton ko sonography karne ka hukm dete hain taake bachche ki growth ka pata chale aur bachche me koi naqs ya 'ayb ho to saamne aaye, agar doctor ne bachche me naqs ya 'ayb ki wajah se bachcha giraane ki raay di kyun ke bachcha ke mustaqbil ki zindagi me baayise mashaqqat ho sakta hai to kya mazkoorah soorat ki bina par isqaate hamal jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Soorate mas-uoolah me isqaate hamal jaaiz nahi kyunke ek hi soorat me fuqahaa ne isqaate hamal ki ijaazat di hai is taur par ke agar bachchah pait me rehne ki wajah se waalidah ki zindagi ko khatrah ho to aysi soorat me faqat isqaat ki ijaazat hai is ke 'alaawah isqaat ki ijaazat nahi hogi, sonography ya doctor wagayrah jo khabar dete hain wo zanni hoti hai basa auqaat hamal ke ahwaal tabdeel ho jaate hain aur atibbaa hazraat is ko 'ayb shumaar karte hain. Allah tabaarak wa ta'aala ke saath husne zan rakhna momin ke liye waajib hai.

Waaqa'ee kisi 'uzr ke baad agar isqaate hamal rooh phunkne ke pehle ya'ani chaar maah se pehle karaaya jaaye to is ki karaahat ke saath gunjaayish hai aur agar hamal ko mukammal qamari chaar maah ho chuke hain to isqaate hamal haraam hoga. (Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb: 3/360, Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 21/251). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Internal Sonography

Sawal: Andarooni sonography (Internal Sonography) me ek rod ka isti'emaal kiya jaata hai jise 'aurat ki sharmagah me daakhil (insert) kar ke rod ko mukhtalif position me ghuma kar andar ki cheezon ka scan kiya jaata hai, misaal ke taur par Hamal (Pregnancy) me bachche ki growth aur condition kaisi hai?

Mumbai me internal sonography ke aksar specialist mard doctor hote hain wo bhi gair muslim hote hain, gair muslim doctor khud us rod ko 'aurat ke sharmagah me daakhil karte hain jo muslim mard doctor hain un ke yahan doctor ke aane se pehle nurse rod ko daakhil karti hai phir doctor use scaning ke liye alag alag position me ghumaate hain.

Mere khayaal se 'aurat ko thodi si to sharam mehsoos hi hogi ke ek na mahram us ki sharmagah me rod daakhil kar ke use scan kar raha hai lekin majboori hoti hai.

Doctor ka kehna hai ke position ka idhar udhar karna sirf specialist ka kaam hai taake saheeh report mile. India ke 'alaawah middle east me bhi internal sonography specialist mard hote hain.

Mumbai me khawaateen internal sonography specialist hain lekin bade bade doctors un ki report tasleem nahi karte, unhein un ke hi expert se report chaahiye!

Mere itna khul kar sawalaat karne par koi galat gumaan mat karna. Mera asal maqsad yeh hai ke logon ko is baat ka 'ilm ho jaaye ke middle east jaise muslim mulkon me bhi internal sonography ke aksar specialist mard hain aur hamaari maa behnon ko majboori me in doctors ke zariye internal sonography karni padti hai.

Yeh to zaahir hai ke koi shakhs is baat ko gawaarah nahi karta ke us ki maa bahan ya biwi ki sharmagah ko koi naa-mahram dekhe! 'Ulama-e-kiraam se guzaarish hai rehbari farmaayein.

Jawab: Agar kisi mareezah 'aurat ki internal sonography ya deegar 'ilaaj ke liye lady doctor na mile to kisi muslim mard doctor ko talaash kare agar koi muslim doctor bhi maujood na ho to badarjah majboori gair muslim mard doctor se apna 'ilaaj karwaana jaaiz hai. (Mugniyul-Muhtaaj: 4/216).

Albatta chand sharaait ke saath muslim Khawaateen agar khawaateen se muta'alliq tibbi uloom aur 'amal e jarraahi aur operation isi tarah internal sonography ki ta'aleem haasil kare to yeh mubaah hi nahi balke farz e kifaayah ke darje me hai, kyunke is baat me bilkul bhi do raay nahi hai ke muslim mu'aashareh ko khaatoon muaalij aur lady doctors ki ashadd zaroorat hai jo ke musalmaan khawaateen ka check up kare aur un ka 'ilaaj w mu'aalajah kare. bahut se musalmaan aise hain jinhein apni biwi beti ya bahan ka check up karwaane ki zaroorat hoti hai aur unhein koi lady doctor mayassar nahi hoti, sirf mard doctor hi milte hain is liye musalmaan ladkiyon ka is 'azeem sho'aba me jaana farz e kifaayah ke darjah me hai, lihaaza usooli taur par internal sonography seekhne ka iraadah rakhne waali aur deegar medical ki taalibaat ke liye zaroori hain ke wo aise idaaron me ta'aleem haasil kare jahan mardon aur 'auraton ka ikhtilaat na ho aur aise idaareh me jaayein jahan par sanjeedah ilmi maahol ho aur shara'ee pardeh ka ehtimaam ho sake isi tarah faraayiz e islaam me se kisi bhi farz ki adaayigi ke liye koi rok tok na ho. (Al-Majmoo': 1/26, Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb: 1/49). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Heart Transfer

Sawal: Kya Heart Transfer karna jaaiz hai? Isi tarah deegar a'azaa'a ya'ani Kidney, aankh wagayrah ka lagaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Fiqhi seminar me mubaahisah ke baad jo baat tay hui wo yeh ke marne waale ki maut se pehle ijaazat ho aur marne ke baad waariseen raazi bhi hon to us shakhs ka 'uzwa (body part) isti'emaal kar sakte hain warnah nahi (Jadeed Fiqhi Masa-il – Maulana Khalid Saifullah: 5). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Hair Transplantation Ke Zariye Sar Ke Baal Lagaana

Sawal: Kuch log sar ke baal jhadh jaane ki soorat me Hair Transplantation ke zariye apne sar me baal ugate hain, kya shar'an is tarah ki paiwandkaari saheeh hai?

Jawab: Kisi shakhs ke jism ya sar ke baal nikaal kar usi shakhs ke sar ke doosre hisse me My Hair Growth Technology ke zariye paiwandkaari kar ke ugaana jaaiz hai, bashart yeh ke is 'amal se nafa'a ke imkaanaat ziyadah hon nuqsaan ke muqaabalah mein, isi tarah is paiwandkaari ka asal maqsad zaayil huye baalon ko dobaarah ugaana aur apni saabiqah (former) shakl par laana ho wagayrah to is tarah ki paiwandkaari ki shar'an gunjaayish hai, lekin kisi doosre shakhs ke baal lekar paiwandkaari karna shar'an jaaiz nahi. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami wa Adillatuhu liz-Zuhaili: 7/5124). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Gelatin Se Bani Dawa Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Aaj kal bahut saari dawaaon me gelatin ka isti'emaal hota hai, jo paudon ya aksar jaanwaron ke jild (chamdi) aur haddiyon se banta hai, hamein is ka 'ilm nahi hota ke jaanwar haraam hai ya halaal jaanwar ko saheeh tareeqah se halaal kiya huwa tha ya nahi to kya Gelatin se bani dawaa ka isti'emaal karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Jo Gelatin paudon aur halaal jaanwaron ko shara'ee tareeqeh se zabah kiya jaata hai aur un ajzaa se banta hai us ke isti'emaal ke jawaaz me koi shak nahi hai albatta jo Gelatin haraam ya gair mazbooh jaanwaron ke ajzaa se banta hai is silsile me baaz ahle 'ilm ka kehna hain ke gelatin banaane ke liye asal cheez ko jin maraahil se guzaara jaata hai to is se is ki haqeeqat badal jati hain aur chunke naapaak cheez ki haqeeqat badal jaaye to 'umoome balva ki wajah se is ke isti'emaal me koi haraj nahi hai.

Agar gair mazbooh ho to Shawaafe' ke nazdeek agar is ke qaayim maqaam koi dawaa na ho to aysi soorat me isti'emaal ki ijaazat hogi aur doosri dawaa ho to is ke isti'emaal ki ijaazat nahi hogi (Al-Majmoo':5/50). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Wazan Kam Karwaane Ke Liye Surgery Karwaana

Sawal: Medical reports ke mutaabiq motaapa aur zaa-id (excess) wazan insaan ke sihhat par manfi asaraat murattab karta hai, aur is ki wajah se insaan ko mukhtalif beemaariyon ka saamna bhi karna padta hai aur basa auqaat is marz me mubtala (afflicted) ho kar maut bhi waqe'e ho jaati hai. To kya wazan kam karwaane aur zaa-id wa nuqsaandah charbi ko jism se nikaalne ke liye operation ya surgery karwa sakte hain?

Jawab: Agar khaane peene me ehtiyaat aur mufeed wa munaasib adviyaat wa gizaayein aur riyaazat wa warzish wagayrah se wazan kam na ho raha ho isi tarah koi galat wa naamunaasib niyyat bhi na ho to apni jaan aur sihhat ko bachaane ki khaatir surgery karaane ki gunjaayish mil sakti hai. Wallahu A'alam. (Al-Fiqhul Islaami Wa Adillatuh: 7/5205). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Khoon Dena (Blood Donation)

Sawal: Kya khoon dena (Blood Donation) jaaiz hai? Kisi jaanne waale ko dena aur blood donation camp me dene ka kya hukm hoga?

Jawab: Agar doctor ki raay me mareez ko khoon ki zaroorat ho to aise mareez ko bagair qeemat liye khoon dena saheeh hai. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya qeemat le kar khoon (Blood) dena jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Khoon de kar is ke 'iwaz (badle me) rupaye lena haraam hai is par 'Ulama ka ittifaaq hai kyunke Abu Juhaifah (Rz) se riwaayat saheeh Bukhaari mein mazkoor hai ke “Huzoor (ﷺ) ne khoon ki qeemat se mana'a kiya.” (Saheeh Bukhaari: 5945, Fathul-Bari Ibne Hajar: 4/427). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Sawal: Khoon bechna jaaiz nahi to kya zaroorat ke waqt khoon khareed sakte hain?

Jawab: Allah Ta'aala ne insaan ko mukarram aur musharraf banaaya hai, isi wajah se us ke jism ke kisi bhi hisse ko bechna jaaiz nahi, khoon me mazeed ek wasf yeh hai ke wo naapaak hota hai, is wajah se bhi use bechna jaaiz nahi. Guzashtah adwaar me beemaari ka 'ilaaj khoon nikaal kar kiya jaata tha. Ab daur aisa aaya hai ke atibbaa beemaari ka 'ilaaj khoon chadha kar karte hain. Maujoodah daur me khoon ka atiyyah insaanon ki ek aham zaroorat ban gaya hai. Lihaazah beemaari zakhmi insaanon ki jaan bachaane ke liye nek niyyati ke saath aur Allah Ta'aala se ajr ki ummeed rakhte huye khoon ka atiyyah dena zaroori hai aur us ke 'iwaz (badle me) rupaye paise lena jaaiz nahi. Jab khoon ki zaroorat ho aur koi muft khoon dene waala na ho to 'iwaz (badle me) dekar khoon khareedna ya khoon de kar khoon lena bhi jaaiz hai. Is soorat me khoon ke 'iwaz (badle me) rupaye paisa lene waale par gunaah hoga, dene waale par nahi. (Sooratul Isra'a: 70, Sooratul Hajj: 78, Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Subuki: 1/45, Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Suyuti: 1/83). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Chote Bachchon Ko Polio Teeke (Polio Vaccines) Ya'ani Injection Lagaana

Sawal: Chote Bachchon ko jo polio teeke ya'ani injection lagaaye jaate hain is ke baare me kuch logon ka kehna hai ke wo nahi lagwaane chaahiye kyunke is ki tayyari me haraam jaanwaron ke a'azaa ka isti'emaal kiya jaata hai?

Jawab: Yeh baat muhaqqaq nahi balke afwaah hai. Polio ke qatron se muta'alliq musalmaan atibbaa hazraat jin se hum ne raabitah kiya yahi kehte hain ke polio ke qatre polio ke jaraaseem se bachaw ke liye pilaaye jaate hain aur shara'ee nuqtaye nazar se kisi bhi halaal dawaa ya gizaa ke zariye beemaari se bachaaw ke liye ehtiyaati tadaabeer ikhtiyaar karna aur 'ilaaj karna jaaiz aur mustahab hai. Isi tarah Allah Ta'aala ke qaza wa qadr se raazi reh kar tawakkulan Alallah sabr karna, 'ilaaj na karna aur polio dose na pilaana yeh bhi jaaiz aur mustahab hai. Lihaaza jab tak kisi mustanad zariye se polio ke qatron me naapaak cheez ki aamezish ka 'ilm na ho us waqt tak is ko pilaane ka shar'an yahi hukm hai ke use pilaana fi nafsihi jaaiz hai. (Sooratut Talaaq: 3, Sunane Abu Dawood: 3874, Al-Muhazzab: 1/235, Al-Majmoo': 5/106, Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Suyuti: 1/60). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shadeed Takleef Me Mubtala (afflicted) La-'ilaaj Mareez Ko Maut Ka Injection (Death Injection) Lagwaana

Sawal: Agar koi shadeed beemaar ho aur doctor 'ilaaj karne se qaasir hon, jaise AIDS ya Cancer wagayrah to kya aysi shadeed takleef ki haalat me marne ke liye injection (Death injection) lagwaana jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Islaam ka tasawwur yeh hai ke insaan apni jaan ka maalik nahi. Is ka jism us ke haathon me Allah Ta'aala ki amaanat hai jis ki hifaazat is ka fareeza hai, Huzoore Paak (ﷺ) ne farmaaya ke beemaariyon ka 'ilaaj karwaaya karo. Chunaanche budhaape ke 'alaawah saari beemaariyan wo hain jin ka 'ilaaj Allah Ta'aala ne paida farma diya hai. Lihaaza la-'ilaaj beemaari ya shiddate takleef ki wajah se maut ka injection lagwa kar maut qabool karna khudkushi hai aur yeh haraam hai. Khudkushi karne se Allah Ta'aala ne mana'a farmaaya hai. Irshaade Baarie Ta'aala hai: Aur apni jaanon ko halaak mat karo. (An-Nisa'a). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

JADEED FIQHI MASA-IL

Tasweerkashi (Photography) Ki Shara'ee Haisiyat

Sawal: Tasweerkashi ka shar'an kya hukm hai? Aaj kal mobile ke zariye tasweerkashi ka bahut riwaaj pad chuka hai. Is ke hawaale se kya hukm hai? Agar kisi ko is se mana'a kiya jaaye to aage se yeh Jawab milta hai ke 'Ulama-e-kiraam bhi to T.V par aate hain ya video banwaate hain ya tasweer khichwaate hain?

Jawab: Tasweerkashi naajaaiz wa haraam hai. Riwaaj pad jaane se ya T.V par 'Ulama ki tasweer aa jaane se hukm e shara'ee nahi badal jaata, Darul-'Uloom Deoband aur Barre Sageer ke taqreeban tamaam Darul Ifta'a tasweerkashi ke naajaaiz wa haraam hone ka fatwa dete chale aaye hain aur ab bhi yahi fatwa dete hain, albatta shara'ee zaroorat aur iztiraar ki haalat ke ahkaam aur honge is mas'ale par tafseeli bahas “chand aham 'asri masa-il” naami kitab me hai jo net par bhi dastiyaab hai aap us ka mutaalaya kar sakte hain. (Fatawa Darul-'Uloom Deoband, Saheehul Bukhaari: 5951, Saheeh Muslim: 2109, Nihaayatul Matalab:13/190, (Al-Ashbahu wan-Nazairu lis-Subuki: 1/49). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mard Ka Jismaani Warzish Ke Liye Gym Jana

Sawal: Kya Mard ka Gym Khaana me jismaani warzish karna shar'an jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Jismaani warzish sirf jaaiz hi nahi balke ek matloob cheez hai kyunke yeh sihhat ki hifaazat aur umdagi ka zariya hai, hadeese paak me marvi hai ke taaqatwar momin Allah ke nazdeek kamzor momin se ziyadah pasandeedah hai. (Saheeh Muslim: 6774). Albatta is me niyyat achchi ho, koi buri niyyat jaise dikhlaawa wagayrah na ho. Neez warzish karte waqt is baat ka bhi khayaal rakhkha jaaye ke aise libaas ka isti'emaal na kiya jaaye jis se mukamaml satar na chup sake aur 'auraton ke ikhtilaat se ehtiraaz kiya jaaye. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Marne Ke Baad Badan Ka 'Uzwa (Body Part) Donate Karna

Sawal: Kya marne ke baad badan ka koi 'uzwa (Body part) donate kar sakte hain? Kya jeete ji 'uzwa donate karne ki wasiyyat kar sakte hain?

Jawab: Fiqhi seminar me mubaahaseh (debate) ke baad jo baat tay hui wo yeh ke marne waale ki maut se pehle ijaazat ho aur marne ke baad waariseen raazi bhi hon to us shakhs ka 'uzwa isti'emaal kar sakte hain warnah nahi (Jadeed Fiqhi Masa-il Jild No. 5 – Maulana Khalid Saifullah: 5). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Mardon, 'Auraton Aur Bachchon Ka Water Park Jaane Ka Shara'ee Hukm

Sawal: Mardon, 'auraton aur Bachchon ka water park jaane ka shar'an kya hukm hai?

Jawab: Taqreeban tamaam hi water park mardon aur 'auraton dono ke liye 'aam (common) hote hain jis ki wajah se mardon aur 'auraton ka ikhtilaat hota hai aur yeh haraam hai. Mazed yeh ke water park me entry karne ke baad wahan ke qawaaneen ki paasdaari wa paabandi karna bhi zaroori hoti hai, jaisa ke wahan ke Rides me Nylon aur Polyester ke kapde pehenna. Yeh is liye zaroori hota hain taake in ki madad se Slides par phisalne ke liye aasaani ho. Yeh kapde paani me bheegne ke baad jism ko chipakte hain jis se jism ke nasheb wa faraaz zaahir hote hain. Aksar gair muslim mard aur khawaateen satar ka khayaal nahi rakhte yahan tak ke basa auqaat 'auraton ke a'azaaye makhsoosah ya'ne private parts bhi dikhaee dete hain jo mardon ke liye muharrike shahwat ho sakte hain aur gunaah me mubtalah hone ka zariya bhi. In tamaam wujoohaat ki bina par mardon aur 'auraton ka water park jaana jaaiz nahi. Bachchon ke waalidain ki zimmedaari hai aur in par farz hai ke bachpan hi se un ke dil wa dimaag me qabaahat aur buraayi bitha dein aur aysi jaghon me jaane se mana'a kare jahan khilaafe shara'a (against islamic principles) umoor anjaam diye jaate hain, taake baalig hone ke baad wo in jaisi fahhaashi se bache rahein. Chunaanche Allah Ta'aala Surah Ahzaab me irshaad farmaate hain: Ay paigamber! Keh deejiye apni biwiyon aur saahib zaadiyon se aur doosre musalmaanon ki biwiyon se ke latka liya kare apne upar thodi chaadarein. Is aayat me ghar se baahar nikalne ke zaabite ki ta'aleem hai ke jab (nikalna) kisi safar wagayrah ki zaroorat se waqe'e ho, us waqt bhi be hijaab mat ho, balke apni chaader ka pallah apne chehre par latka lein taake chehrah kisi ko nazar na aaye. Surah A'araaf me Allah Ta'aala ka irshaad hai “Aye Nabi (ﷺ) tum farmaao, mere rab ne to zaahiri baatini be-hayaaiyan aur gunaah aur naahaq z'iyaadati ko haraam qaraar diya hai. Isi tarah hadees-e mubarak me hai ke “'aurat chupaane ki aur parde ki cheez hai, jab 'aurat ghar se nikalti hain to shaitaan use nigah me rakhta hai (Taake us ke zariye mardon ko fitne me mubtala (afflicted) kare). Faqat Wallahu A'alam. (Sooratul Ahzaab: 59, Sooratul A'araaf: 33, At-Targibu wat-Tarheebu: 1/181, At-Tabarani: 9/208 – 8914, (Fathul-'Aziz: 3/97). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

WhatsApp Group Par Salaam Ka Jawab Dena

Sawal: Aaj kal bahut se log whatsApp Group me salaam likh kar ya voice message wagayrah ke zariye bhejte hain is ka Jawab zabaan se dena kaafi hai ya use likh kar bhejna zaroori hai.

Jawab: Salaam ka Jawab dena waajib hai agar koi shakhs kisi jamaa'at ko salaam kare to un me se kisi ek ka Jawab dena tamaam ki taraf se kaafi hai lekin agar koi ek bhi Jawab na de to saare gunahgaar honge.

Isi tarah whatsApp group me agar koi salaam kare to ek ka Jawab dena kaafi ho jaayega, agar koi shakhs kisi ko salaam likh kar bhej de aur wo log padh kar is par muttale'e ho jaayein to un par (lafzan) zabaan se Jawab dena kaafi hai likh kar bhejna zaroori nahi. (Rauzatut-taalibeen: 10/226, Fathul-Mu'een: 1/597). Mufti Fauzan Murudkar

Machchar Maarne Ke Liye Barqi Bat (Electric Bat) Ka Isti'emaal

Sawal: Kya machchar maarne ke liye barqi bat (Electric Bat) aur Mortin Liquid aur paste control ka isti'emaal saheeh hai? jabke khaleeji mamaalik me cockroach ko paste control se maara jaata hai?

Jawab: Machchar ko maarne ke liye zehreli dawaa aur electronic machine ka isti'emaal jaaiz hai aur zaroorat ke mawqe'e par moozi (harmful) ashyaa ko is tarah maarne ki shar'an ijaazat hai kyunke machchar wagayrah ke maarne ke mazkoorah barqi aaleh me bijli hoti hai aag nahi hoti hai aur machchar isi bijli ke jhatke se marta hai. (Fatawa al-Lajnatud-Daimah: 26/192). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Cigarette Aur Shisha (Hookah) Peena

Sawal: Kya Cigarette peena haraam hai?

Jawab: Jaan aur sihhat yeh insaan ke paas Allah ki ni'emat aur amaanat hai aur Allah ne jaan aur sihhat ki hifaazat ka hukm diya hai. Is liye Sharee'at ne khudkushi karne ko haraam qaraar diya is liye ke jaan jab aap ki nahi hai to us ko kaise khatam kar sakte hain balke wo amaanat hai jis ki hifaazat karna insaan ke zimmeh zaroori hai. Isi tarah aysi koi cheez isti'emaal nahi kar sakte jis se sihhat ka zarar ho aur sihhat khatam ho jaaye. Aur doctor ka kehna hain cigarette, guthka aur tambaaku (tobacco) yeh sihhat ke liye yaqeeni taur par nuqsaandah hain is liye baaz 'Ulama ke nazdeek haraam hai aur baaz ne use makrooh kaha hai. Is liye ke Allah farmaate hain ke apne haathon apne aap ko halaakat me mat dalo. Maulana Shabbir Tambe

Sawal: Kya Shisha (Hookah) peena shar'an jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Har wo cheez jo jisme insaani ke liye muzir ho shar'an is ka khaana, peena aur khaane peene ke liye bechna aur khareedna naajaaiz hai. Bazaar me do tarah ke Shisha / Hookeh paaye jate hain ek to wo qism hoti hai jis me nashah aawar cheezon ka isti'emaal hota hai aur doosri qism wo hoti hai jis me nashah aawar cheez nahi daali jaati balke zaayikedaar kimiyaayi maaddah (chemical substance) daala jaata hai. Atibbaa aur mohaqqiqeen ki tahqeeqaat ke mutaabiq dono hi qism ke hookeh aur Shisha insaani jism aur sihhat par bahut hi bura asar daalte hain, tahqeeq se yeh baat bhi saabit hui hai ke is ke isti'emaal se jigar, phephdon ka cancer ho jaata hai, andhaapan ke khadshaat badh jaate hain, isi tarah yeh cheezein isti'emaal karne waalon ko apna 'aadi bhi banaate hain. Isi tarah is me israaf, fuzoolkharchi aur maal ka ziya'a (waste) bhi hai. Is ke 'alaawah is me aur bhi mazarraat hain jis ka tafseeli bayaan herbal Shisha se muta'alliq Sawal ke Jawab me mulaahazah keejiye. Lihaazah az rooye shara'a (according to islamic law) Shisha ya hookah peena jaaiz nahi. (Al-Majmoo': 20/121, Al-Fiqhul-Manhaji: 3/84, Al-Nisa'a: 29, Al-Baqarah: 195, Asnal-Matalib: 1/569, Hashiyatul-Bujayrmi 'Alal-Khateeb: 4/328, Nihayatul-Muhtaaj: 2/160). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Shisha peena, yeh cigarette, tambaaku (tobacco) noshi ke muqaabale me koi ziyadah mehfooz nahi hai. Shisha me ek khaas taur par tashkeel diye gaye tambaaku (tobacco) ko garam kiya jaata hai jis ka dhuwaan (smoke) paani se guzarta hai aur phir ek rubber ki nail ke zariye muh me jaata hai. Shisha ka paani tambaaku (tobacco) ke zehreele ajzaa ko filter nahi karta. Cigarette noshi karne waalon ke muqaabale, Shisha me ziyadah tambaaku (tobacco) ander liya jaata hai. Tahqeeq se pata chalta hain ke insaan is se bahut se khatraat ka shikaar ho sakta hai.

https://www.emro.who.int/tfi/know-the-truth/waterpipe-tobacco-use.html

https://www.emro.who.int/tfi/know-the-truth/waterpipe-tobacco-use.html

Herbal Flavored Hookah Peena

Sawal: Kya tambaaku (tobacco) se aazad (Tobacco Free) Herbal Flavored Hookah peena jaaiz hai?

Shisha ka isti'emaal ek aalami ruj'haan ban gaya hai, jis me mukhtalif masnu'aat (products) ka isti'emaal kiya jaata hai. Sihhat se muta'alliq saarif (Health Conscious User) ke liye aaj kal Shisha me isti'emaal hone waala ek makhsoos qism “tambaaku (tobacco) free / Herbal “mitha aur zaayikedaar masnuaat (Product) mutabaadil ke taur par marketing me bahut mash'hoor hai, ishtihaaraat ka dawa hota hai ke is ke isti'emaal se saarifeen ko tambaaku (tobacco) ke nuqsaandah asaraat ke bagair hi zaayiqah se lutf andozi ho sakti hai.

Ek tahqeeq me riwaayati tambaaku (tobacco) par mabni aur tambaaku se paak (Tobacco Free) masnuaat ka isti'emaal karte huye tambaaku (tobacco) noshi ke paani ke pipes se zahreele paidaawaar ka muwaazanah (comparison) kiya gaya. Ek insaani naqli water pipe cigarette noshi ki machine (Human Mimic Waterpipe Smoking Machine) ka isti'emaal 31 insaani shurakaa ke kashsh / Puffing ke silsile ko naqal karne ke liye kiya gaya jinhone control shudaah tibbi tarteeb me (Double Blind Ad Libitum) cigarette noshi session mukammal kiye: ek baar tambaaku (tobacco) par mabni product ke saath aur ek bar zaayiqah ke saath. Mumaasil tambaaku (tobacco) se paak masnuaat. Nataayij ke iqdaamaat me Carbon Monoxide, Nitric Oxide, gair mustahkam Volatile Aldehydes, Nicotine, Tar aur poli cyclic Aromatic Hydrocarbons shaamil hain. Paani ke pipe ki dono sooraton se nikalne waale dhuwein (smoke) me kaafi miqdaar me zehreele maadde maujood the. Dono sooraton ke nateeje me sirf Nicotine ki paidaawaar numaayan taur par mukhtalif thi. Yeh nataayij ishtihaari paigaamaat se mutasaadim (barkhilaaf) hain ke “Herber Shisha Masnuaat”, tambaaku (tobacco) Shisha ke liye sihhat mand mutabaadil hain.

Tehqeeq ki buniyad par, tambaaku (tobacco) ki bunyaad par Shisha aur “herbal” Shisha ke mutaala'aah se pata chalta hain ke dono ka dhuwaan (smoke), zahreele agenton par mushtamil hota hai jis me Carbon Monoxide, Nitric Oxide, Volatile Aldehydes, Nicotine, Tar aur Polycyclic Aromatic Hydrocarbons shaamil hain jo cancer, dil ki beemaari aur phephdon ki beemaari ke khatraat ko badhaane ke liye mash'hoor hain.

https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/articles/PMC3407543

Jawab: Halaal cheezon ki do qismein hoti hain ek to wo jo jisme insaani ke liye muzir hoti hai aur doosri wo jo mufeed saabit hoti hai. Lihaaza wo cheezein jo mufeed hon un ka isti'emaal jaaiz hai. Aur jo jisme insaani ke liye nuqsaan pohchaane waali hon in ka isti'emaal jaaiz nahi aur in cheezon ke isti'emaal se bachna waajib hai. Aap ke zariye pesh ki gai mazkoorah is tahqeeq se yeh baat bilkul wazeh ho gai hai ke “Harbal Shisha / Hookah” ka isti'emaal karna naajaaiz hai. Mufti Khaalid Saifullah Rahmani Hafizahullah ke saamne jab herbal Shisha se muta'alliq tahqeeq pesh ki gai to unhon ne bhi is ke isti'emaal ko naajaaiz kaha. Wallahu A'alam. (Al-Nisa'a: 29, Al-Baqarah: 195, (Al-A'araaf: 32) (Al-Muhazzab: 1/455). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla

Bitcoin, Crypto Currency Ki Tijaarat

Sawal: Kya Bitcoin ya kisi aur Crypto Currency ki tijaarat karna jaaiz hai?

Jawab: Dunya me jo mukhtalif currenciyan raayij hain, wo fi nafsihi maal nahi hain, wo mahaz kaagaz ka tukda hai, in me jo maaliyat (finance) ya 'urfi samaniyyat (market value) paayi jati hai, wo do wajah se hai, ek to is wajah se ke in ke peeche mulk ki iqtisaadi (economic) policy kaar farma hoti hai, isi liye mulk ki iqtisaadi taraqqi aur inhitaat ka currency ki value par asar padta hai, ya'ani iqtisaad hi ki wajah se mulk ki currency ki value ghatti badhti hai. Aur doosri wajah yeh hai ke har mulk 'awaam ke liye apni currency ka zaamin wa zimmedaar hota hai, yahi wajah hai ke jab koi currency band karta hai to currency mahaz kaagaz ka tukda ban kar reh jaati hai aur is ki koi value ya haisiyat baaqi nahi rehti.

Bitcoin ya koi Digital currency, mahaz farzi currency hai, is me haqeeqi currency ke bunyaadi ausaaf (characteristic) wa sharaait bilkul nahi paaye jaate. Aur aaj kal bitcoin ya digital currency ki khareed wa farokht ke naam se net par jo kaarobaar chal raha hai wo mahaz dhokah hai, is me haqeeqat me koi mabee'e (sold item) wagayrah nahi hoti aur na hi is kaarobaar me bay'e ke jawaaz ki shara'ee sharten paayi jaati hain, balke dar haqeeqat yeh forex trading ki tarah sood aur juwe ki shakl hai.

RBI (Reserve Bank of India) ne bhi digital currency jaise Bitcoin par concern zaahir ki hai ke wo mumkinah maaliyaati, qanooni, customer ki hifaazat aur security se muta'alliq khatrraat me izaafah kar sakte hain.

Jadeed Fiqhi Masa-il ke maahir Mufti Taqee 'Usmani aur deegar muftiyan aur Darul-'Uloom Deoband ne bhi Bitcoin ko jaaiz nahi kaha hai.

Is liye bitcoin ya kisi bhi digital currency ki khareed wa farokht ki shakl me internet par chalne waala kaarobaar shar'an Halaal wa jaaiz nahi hai. (Al-Majmoo' Sharhul-Muhazzab: 13/28, At-Tazkirah Fil Fiqhush Shafi'ee Li Ibnil Mulaqqin: 1/62, Al-Haawi al-Kabeer:5/79, Kitaabul Buyoo'a). Mufti Faiz Ahmed Mulla
